001.01	śrī-gaṇeśāya namaḥ
001.02	nyāya-vārtike
001.03	prathamaḥ adhyāyaḥ/
001.04	yad akṣapādaḥ pravaraḥ munīnām
001.05	śamāya śāstram jagataḥ jagāda/
001.06	ku-tārkika-ajñāna-nivṛtti-hetuḥ
001.07	kāriṣyate tasya mayā nibandhaḥ//
001.08	pramāṇa-ādi-padārtha-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti etad śāstrasya ādi-
001.09	ḥūtram/ tasya abhisambandha-vākyam pramāṇataḥ artha-pratipatti^i iti evam ādi
001.10	tasya anusandhāna-vākyam śāstrasya puruṣa-śreyaḥ abhidhāyakatvāt. śāstraṃ
001.11	punar pramāṇa-ādi-vācaka-pada-samūhaḥ vyūha-viśiṣṭaḥ padam punar varṇa-samūhaḥ
001.12	pada-samūhaḥ śāstram ḥūtra-samūhaḥ prakaraṇam prakaraṇa-samūhaḥ āhnikam āhnika-
002.01	samūhaḥ adhyāyaḥ pañca-adhyāyaḥ śāstram. tad padārthāḥ pramāṇa-ādyaḥ tasya
002.02	śāstrasya padānām arthāḥ pramāṇa-ādyaḥ ṣoḍaśa-ātmānaḥ tad śāstram puruṣa-
002.03	śreyaḥ abhidhatte/ pratyakṣa-anumāna-anadhigata-vastu-tattva-anvākhyānam śāstra-
002.04	dharmaḥ tāsya viṣayaḥ pratyakṣa-anumāna-anadhigata-vastu-tattvaḥ ādhyātmaka-
002.05	śakti-sampad-yuktaḥ antevāsin^ḥ/ puruṣaḥ punar caturdhā bhidyate pratipannaḥ apratipannaḥ
002.06	sandigdhaḥ viparyastaḥ ca iti/ tatra pratipannaḥ pratipādayitṛ^ḥ/
002.07	itare sāpekṣāḥ santaḥ pratipādyāḥ. te yadā indriya-artha-sannikarśam apekṣante
002.08	tadā pratyakṣena  yadā liṅga-darśana-smṛti-ādi apekṣante tadā anumānena  yadā
002.09	punar upadeśam apekṣante tadā śāstram pravartate/ śreyaḥ punar sukham ahita-ni-
002.10	vṛttiḥ ca/ tad śreyaḥ bhidyamānam dvedhā vyavatiṣṭate. dṛṣṭa-adṛṣṭa-bhedena . dṛṣṭam
002.11	sukham adṛṣṭam ahita-nivṛttiḥ/ ahita-nivṛttiḥ api ātyantikī anātyantikī
002.12	ca/ anātyantikī kaṇṭa-ādi^aḥ   duḥkha-sādhanasya parihārena / ātyantikī
002.13	punar ekaviṃśati-prebheda-bhinna-duḥkha-hānyā/ ekaviṃśati-prabheda-bhinnaṃ punar duḥkham
002.14	śarīram ṣaḍ-indriyāṇi ṣaḍ-viṣayāḥ ṣaḍ-buddhyaḥ sukham duḥkham ca iti/ śarīram
002.15	duḥkha-āyatana-tvāt duḥkham/ indriyāṇi viṣayāḥ buddhyaḥ ca tad-sādhana-bhāvāt
002.16	/ sukham duḥkha-anuṣaṅgāt/ duḥkham svarūpataḥ iti/ tasya hāniḥ dharma-
002.17	adharma-sādhana-parityāgena / anutpannayoḥ dharma-adharmayoḥ anutpādena  utpannayoḥ
002.18	ca upabhogāt prakṣayena  iti/ puruṣāḥ rāga-ādi-mantaḥ vīta-rāgāḥ ca/ tatra
002.19	rāgaḥ viṣaya-abhiṣaṅga-lakṣaṇaḥ sa eṣām asti te rāga-ādi-mantaḥ/
002.20	vairāgyaṃ punar bhoga-anabhiṣaṅga-lakṣaṇam tad yeṣām asit te vīta-rāgāḥ/
002.21	pravṛtti^aḥ   dvaividhyam puruṣa-bheda-anuvidhānāt/ teṣām puruṣāṇām pravṛttyaḥ tāḥ
003.01	puruṣa-bhedam anuvidhīyamānāḥ ubhaya-rūpāḥ bhavanti/ vīta-rāga-pravṛttiḥ
003.02	ekadhā/ tatra yā vīta-rāgāṇām pravṛttiḥ sā khalu eka-rūpā aniṣṭa-
003.03	pratiṣedha-arthā aniṣṭam hāsyāmaḥ iti eva te pravartante/ na punar eṣām
003.04	kva cit abhiṣvaṅgaḥ asti/ rāga-ādi-mat-pravṛttiḥ tu dvi-rūpā/ ye tu rāga-ādi-
003.05	mantaḥ teṣām yāḥ pravṛttyaḥ  tāḥ dvi-vidhā bhavanti/ iṣṭa-aniṣṭa-viṣaya-adhigama-
003.06	pratiṣedha-arthāḥ/ iṣṭam āpsyāmi iti saktaḥ pravartate/ aniṣṭam hāsyāmi
003.07	iti dveṣāt nivartate/ rāga-ādi-mat-pravṛtti^aḥ   api dvaividhyam bhavati samartha-
003.08	asamartha-bhedāt/ yā khalu rāga-ādi-mat-pravṛttiḥ sā samarthā asamarthā ca
003.09	bhavati/ iṣṭam āpsyāmi iti pravartamānaḥ yadā prāpnoti tadā samarthā/
003.10	aniṣṭam hāsyāmi iti pravartamānaḥ yadā jahāti tadā samarthā yadā
003.11	viparyayaḥ tadā asamarthā iti/ tataḥ punar pravṛtti-dvaividhyam pramāṇasya arthavat-
003.12	anarthaka-tvāt/ pramāṇam tāvat artha-paricchedakam pramāṇa-sāmānyāt pramāṇa-
003.13	pratirūpakam api pramāṇam iti upacaryate/ kim punar pramāṇa-sāmānyam
003.14	pramāṇa-ābhāsasya sāmānya-paricchedaka-tvam pramāṇena  api sāmānyam paricchidyate
003.15	pramāṇa-ābhāsena  api/ saḥ ayam pramātṛ^ḥ yadā pramāṇena  avadhārya
003.16	pravartate tadā asya pravṛttiḥ samarthā bhavati yadā punar pramāṇa-ābhāsena
003.17	artham avadhārya pravartate tadā asamarthā tasyāḥ punar arthavat-tvam pramāṇataḥ
003.18	artha-pratipatti^i  iti ādi-bhāṣyam/ paraspara-apekṣi-tvāt ubhaya-asiddhiḥ iti cet/
003.19	na/ anādi-tvāt/ yadi pramāṇataḥ artha-pratipatti^i  pravṛtti-sāmarthyam yadi
003.20	vā pravṛtti-sāmarthyāt pramāṇataḥ artha-pratipattiḥ kim pūrvam kim paścāt iti
003.21	vācyam/ yadi tāvat pramāṇataḥ pūrvam artha-pratipattiḥ pravṛtti-sāmarthyam
003.22	antareṇa kim iti pratipadyate/ atha pūrvam pravṛtti-sāmarthyam anavadhārya arthe
004.01	kim iti pravartate tasmāt pravṛtti^aḥ   pramāṇataḥ artha-pratipatti^aḥ   vā para-apara-
004.02	bhāvaḥ na kalpate iti/ tad ca na evam kasmāt anādi-tvāt anādiḥ ayam
004.03	saṃsāraḥ iti pūrva-abhyasta-sūtre pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ/ ādimati ca saṃsāre
004.04	eṣaḥ doṣaḥ kim pūrvam pramāṇataḥ artha-pratipattiḥ āhosvit pravṛtti-sāmarthyam
004.05	iti/ pramāṇa-pravṛtti^oḥ vā bala-abala-jijñāsāyām ubhaya-sāmarthya-pratipādana-
004.06	artham vākyam lokaḥ khalu pravartamānaḥ pramāṇena  artham avadhārya pravartate saḥ ca
004.07	tathā-pravartamānaḥ phalam upalabhate/ tatra idam cintyate/ kim pramāṇataḥ
004.08	artha-pratipattiḥ samarthā āhosvit pravṛttiḥ iti/ ubhayam samartham
004.09	tad-nāntarīyaka-tvāt tasya phalasya asya ca arthasya upadarśana-artham vākyam
004.10	iti/ loka-vṛtta-anuvādaḥ vā sarvaḥ pramātṛ^ḥ pramāṇena  artham avadhārya
004.11	pravartamānaḥ phalam upalabhate iti loka-vṛttim tad-vākyena  anūdyate/ heya-
004.12	hāna-upāya-adhigantavya-bhedāt catvāri artha-padāni iti/ heyam duḥkham tad-hetuḥ ca
004.13	duḥkham uktam hetuḥ avidyā-tṛṣṇā^i dharma-adharmau iti/ hānam tattva-jñānam/
004.14	tad punar yathā-artha-avasthita-pada-artha-adhigatiḥ tad ca pramāṇam/ upāyaḥ śāstram
004.15	tad api uktam/ adhigantavyaḥ apavargaḥ saḥ punar ātyantikaḥ duḥkha-abhāvaḥ/
004.16	etasmin ca catur-varge pramāṇasya prādhānya-pradaśana-artham ca iti/ katham punar anena
004.17	vākyena  pramāṇa-ādi-catur-vargaḥ pradarśyate iti/ pramāṇa-prameya-adhigatayaḥ
004.18	śruti-gamyāḥ pramātṛ^ḥ tad-nāntarīyaka-tvāt/ na hi pramātṛ^am antareṇa
004.19	pramāṇam sambhavati tad idam vākyam avayavaśaḥ upanyasya varṇyate/ tatra
004.20	pramāṇataḥ iti tasiḥ-vacana-vibhakti-vyāpti-darśana-arthaḥ/ pramāṇataḥ iti iyam
004.21	nimitta-pañcamī/ asya abhidhānam vacana-vyāpti-artham vibhakti-vyāpti-artham ca
004.22	katham punar pañcamī-vyatirekena  tasiḥ labhyate labhyate iti āha/ ādi-ādi^i  upasaṃkhyānāt
005.01	iti/ tad kim siddham bhavati vacana-vyāptyā saṃplavaḥ vyavasthā
005.02	ca/ pramāṇena  pramāṇābhyām pramāṇaiḥ iti saṃplavaḥ gamyate pramāṇena  eva iti
005.03	vyavasthā vibhakti-vyāptyā hetu-karaṇa-bhāvaḥ/ pramāṇāt artha-adhigatiḥ bhavati
005.04	iti hetu-tvam pramāṇena  artham sādhayati iti sādhakatama-tvāt karaṇa-arthaḥ
005.05	iti/ saṃplava-anupapattiḥ viśiṣṭa-viṣaya-tvāt iti cet/ na/ anabhyupagamāt/
005.06	syāt matiḥ eṣā viśiṣṭa-viṣayāṇi pramāṇāni/ viśeṣa-viṣayam pratyakṣam
005.07	sāmānya-viṣayam anumānam iti/ etad ca adhigantavyam yad sāmānyam viśeṣaḥ
005.08	ca na ca sāmānya-viṣayam pratyakṣam na jātu anumānam viśeṣa-viṣayam iti/
005.09	etad ca na anabhupagamāt/ na tāvat pramāṇa-dvayam pratipadyāmahe na
005.10	viṣaya-dvayam na api asaṃkaram/ kim kāraṇam/ pramāṇāni tāvat catvāri
005.11	viṣayaḥ sāmānya-viśeṣa-tadvat-bhedāt tredhā/ saṃkaraḥ api ekasya sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ
005.12	adhigati^aḥ   indriya-vat iti/ indriyam khalu artha-prakāśaka-tvāt pramāṇam tasya
005.13	vyavasthā saṃkaraḥ ca vyavasthā gandha-ādiṣu saṃkaraḥ pṛthivī-ādiṣu dvi-indriya-
005.14	grāhya-tvāt/ sattāyām guṇatve ca sārvendriyam jñānam iti/ adhigatatvāt
005.15	vaiyarthyam iti cet/ na/ anyathā tad-adhigati^aḥ  / syāt
005.16	matam yadi saṃkīryeran pramāṇāni nanu ekena  pramāṇena  adhigate arthe dvitīyam
005.17	pramāṇam vyartham āpadyeta/ adhigatam ca artham adhigamayatṛ^ḥ pramāṇena  piṣṭam
005.18	piṣṭam syāt/ na/ anyathā adhigati^aḥ   na brūmaḥ yathā pratyakṣena  arthaḥ adhigamyate
005.19	tathā anumāna-ādibhiḥ api iti/ anyathā pratyakṣena  indriya-sambaddhaḥ/ indriya-
005.20	sambaddhaḥ anumānena / saṃjñā-saṃjñi-saṃbandha-pratipattiḥ upamānena  / śabda-
006.01	ullekhena   āgamena   iti/ viṣaya-antare vyavasthā-darśanāt ca/ na ca sarvasmin arthe
006.02	saṃplavaḥ asti iti ataḥ na vaiyarthyam iti/ tasmāt vyavasthitam etad tasiḥ-vacana-
006.03	vibhakti-vyāpti-jñāpana-arthaḥ iti pramāṇa-svarūpa-avadhāraṇam kartavyam/ kim punar
006.04	pramāṇasya pramāṇatvam kim ca uktam pramāṇam iti upalabdhi-hetuḥ pramāṇam upalabdhi-
006.05	hetu-tvam pramāṇatvam yad upalabdhi-nimittam tad pramāṇam iti/ samāna-tvāt
006.06	alakṣaṇam iti cet yadi upalabdhi-hetuḥ pramāṇam nanu pramātṛ-prameyayoḥ api
006.07	upalabdhi-hetu-tvāt pramāṇatvam prasajyeta viśeṣaḥ vā vaktavyaḥ iti/ ayam
006.08	viśeṣaḥ pramāṇe pramātṛ-prameyayoḥ carita-artha-tvāt pramāṇe pramātṛ^ḥ prameyam
006.09	ca carita-artham/ acarita-artham ca pramāṇam ataḥ tad eva upalabdhi-sādhanam iti/
006.10	akaraṇā pramāṇa-utpattiḥ iti cet yadi pramātṛ-prameyābhyām pramāṇam
006.11	janyate akaraṇā tarhi pramāṇa-utpattiḥ prāpnoti/ na/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣasya
006.12	karaṇa-bhāvāt na akaraṇā pramāṇa-utpattiḥ pramāṇa-utpatti^i  indriya-
006.13	artha-sannikarṣam apekṣamāṇābhyām pramātṛ-prameyābhyām pramāṇam janyate iti
006.14	na akaraṇa-pramāṇa-utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ/ yadi tarhi pramāṇa-utpatti^i  indriya-artha-
006.15	sannikarṣam apekṣete pramātṛ-prameye atha tarhi indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpattau
006.16	kim apekṣete iti tasmin api indriyam iti evam anādiḥ kartṛ-karaṇa-bhāvaḥ yad yad
006.17	paścāt bhavati tad tad pūrva-bhāvinam apekṣate iti anādiḥ kartṛ-karaṇa-bhāvaḥ
006.18	draṣṭavyaḥ iti/ yadi pramātṛ-prameyābhyām sadbhyām pramāṇam janyate tataḥ
006.19	pramāṇam antareṇa pramātṛ-prameya-bhāvasya asiddha-tvāt pramātṛtvam prameyatvam ca
006.20	anupapannam iti/ katham pramāṇam iti yathā kāraka-śabdaḥ ayam tathā pramātṛ^ḥ
007.01	prameyam iti/ na ca kāraka-śabdāḥ kriyām antareṇa ātmānam labhante
007.02	iti. na ca dravya-mātram kārakam na ca kriyā-mātram iti/ kāraka-
007.03	śabdaḥ hi pravartamānaḥ kriyā-sādhane kriyā-viśeṣa-yukte pravartate/
007.04	pramātṛ-prameya-śabdau ca kāraka-śabdau tau antareṇa kriyām na prvartayetām
007.05	iti/ na/ pācaka-ādi-śabda-vat tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvāt na brūmaḥ kriyā-
007.06	sambandhena   eva kāraka-śabdāḥ pravartante iti api tu tri-kāla-viṣayāḥ ete
007.07	yadi kriyā-sambandha-nimittāḥ syuḥ na kriyā-sambandham antareṇa pravarteran
007.08	kriyā-sambandham antareṇa tu pravartante kim punar nimittam sāmarthyam tad ca
007.09	triṣu api kāla^su asti iti/ tasmāt upapannam pramātṛ-prameyābhyām pramāṇam
007.10	janyate iti tad upalabdhi-sādhanam iti/ sādhakatama-tvāt vā na prasaṅgaḥ/
007.11	na ca ayam prasaṅgaḥ asti pramātṛ^ḥ ca prameyam ca upalabdhi^aḥ   nimitta-tvāt
007.12	pramāṇam prasajyate iti/ kasmāt sādhakatama-tvāt sādhakatamam pramāṇam
007.13	na tu pramātṛ-prameye/ kaḥ khalu sādhakatama-arthaḥ sādhakatamam pramāṇam iti
007.14	kevalam vākyam abhidhīyate na arthaḥ iti/ bhāva-abhāvayoḥ tadvattā na
007.15	pramātari prameye vā asati pramā bhavati sati tu bhavati na punar
007.16	sati bhavati eva pramāṇe tu sati bhavantī bhavati eva saḥ ayam atiśayaḥ
007.17	sādhakatamatvam ucyate/ yadvān vā pramimīte saḥ atiśayaḥ kiṃvān pramimīte
007.18	pramāṇavān pramimīte pramāṇe sati pramimīte na asati iti
007.19	satoḥ vā akartṛ-tvam yad-abhāvāt/ yasya ca abhāvāt pramātṛ-prameye na
007.20	pramām kurutaḥ saḥ atiśayaḥ saṃyoga-vat carama-bhāvitā vā yathā vā
007.21	saṃyogaḥ paścāt-bhāvin^ḥ dravya-śaktiḥ bhavati tathā pramāṇam carama-bhāvin pramātṛ^ḥ-
007.22	prameyayoḥ pramā-śaktiḥ bhavati paścāt-bhāvaḥ atiśayaḥ/ pratipatti^aḥ   ānantaryam
008.01	vā/ yad vā pramāṇa-anantaram pratipatti^aḥ   janman saḥ ca ayam atiśayaḥ iti/
008.02	asādhāraṇa-kāraṇatā vā pramātṛ^ḥ tāvat sādhāraṇam kāraṇam sarva-pratipattīnām
008.03	prameyam api aśeṣa-puruṣa-sādhāraṇa-tvāt tathā-bhūtam pramāṇam tu asādhāraṇa-
008.04	kāraṇa-tvāt pradhānam/ prādhānyāt ca sādhakatama-tvena   abhidhīyate
008.05	iti/ pramā-kāraṇa-saṃyoga-viśeṣaka-tvam vā yaḥ vā pramā-kāraṇam saṃyogaḥ
008.06	tasya pramāṇam anugrahe vartamānam atiśaya-śabda-vācyam/ artha-grahaṇam pramāṇa-
008.07	viṣaya-pratipatti-niṣedha-artham yataḥ na pramāṇa-viṣayā pratipattiḥ puruṣam
008.08	iti-kartavyatāyām prayojayati kim tu arthasya tathā-bhāva-viṣayā yadā ayam
008.09	arthasya tathā-bhāvam pratipadyate atha pravartate iti/ upekṣaṇīya-viṣaya-
008.10	pratiṣedha-artham vā na hi ūpekṣaṇīya-viṣaya-pratipattiḥ puruṣam iti-kartavyatāyām
008.11	prayojayati kim tu arthasya sukha-duḥkha-hetu-bhāva-viṣayā yadā ayam arthasya
008.12	sukha-duḥkha-hetu-bhāvam pratipadyate atha pravartate iti pramāṇa-agrahaṇam gamyamāna-
008.13	tvāt iti cet/ na/ pramāṇa-viśeṣa-jñāpana-artha-tvāt/ artha-pratipattau
008.14	pravṛtti-sāmarthyāt iti abhidhīyamāne gamyate eva etad pramāṇataḥ iti na hi
008.15	kā cit pratipattiḥ pramāṇam antareṇa bhavati/ tad ca na evam pramāṇa-viśeṣa-
008.16	jñāpana-artha-tvāt asti ayam pramāṇa-śabdaḥ pramāṇe vartamāne asti ca pramāṇa-
008.17	sāmānyāt pramāṇa-ābhāse api iti/ tathā iha pramāṇena   pratipattiḥ sā
008.18	pravṛtti-sāmarthyam pratipādayati pramāṇam na itarat iti/ na ca pramāṇa-
008.19	grahaṇam antareṇa pañcamī-abhidhānam labhyate/ tena ca prayojanam ataḥ ca
008.20	pramāṇa-grahaṇam kṛtam iti/ artha-grahaṇena   sukha-duḥkha-hetu-tvena   sarva-saṃgrahaḥ
008.21	aryamāṇa-tvāt/ sarvaḥ pramāṇa-ādiḥ arthaḥ sukha-duḥkha-hetuḥ sarvaḥ ca aryate aryamāṇa-
009.01	tvāt arthaḥ iti na vyāghātāt avivakṣita-tvāt ca/ sarvaḥ pramāṇa-ādiḥ arthaḥ
009.02	sukha-duḥkha-hetuḥ iti bruvatā heya-ādi-bhedāt catvāri artha-padāni iti vyāhanyeta/
009.03	na ca pramāṇa-ādi-hānam śakyam kartum na ca pramāṇa-ādi-hanam vivakṣitam
009.04	tasmāt avyākhyānam etad kim tu sukha-duḥkha-tvena   tad-hetu-tvena   ca yāvatām
009.05	saṃgrahaḥ te abhidhīyante adhikārāt na ca saṃvidaḥ grahaṇam anadhikārāt
009.06	akarma-tvāt na saṃvid-heyā asukha-duḥkha-tvāt atad-hetu-tvāt ca na api aryate
009.07	phala-tvāt saḥ ayam pramāṇa-arthaḥ aparisaṃkhyeyaḥ iyān ayam pramāṇa-viṣayaḥ iti
009.08	na parisaṃkhyātum śakyam ānantyāt na ubhayasya api parisaṃkhyāta-tvāt/
009.09	etāvān ayam pramāṇa-arthaḥ yad sukha-duḥkhe tad hetuḥ ca iti tad ca ubhayam parisaṃkhyātam
009.10	ataḥ na yuktam ānantyāt na parisaṃkhyātum śakyam prāṇabhṛt-bhedasya aparisaṃkhyeya-
009.11	tvāt aparisaṃkhyeyaḥ pramāṇa-arthaḥ iti asambandham uktam iti/ na artha-śabdasya
009.12	prayojana-vāci-tvāt pramāṇa-arthaḥ aparisaṃkhyeyaḥ iti/ pramāṇe prayojanam
009.13	aparisaṃkhyeyam iti ayam vākya-arthaḥ katham saḥ eva arthaḥ keṣām cit sukha-hetuḥ
009.14	bhavati keṣām cit duḥkha-hetuḥ iti/ arthavati ca samarthe pramāṇe arthavanti
009.15	samarthāni iti/ anyatamatva-arthaḥ sādhakatama-arthaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ prakaraṇāt
009.16	prakaraṇam hi catur-varge pramāṇam pradhānam iti varṇyate/ yadi punar yathā
009.17	śruti-vākyam arthavat syāt kim pramāṇa-sāmarthye varṇitam syāt tasmāt yathā-uktam
009.18	nyāyam iti/ pramātṛ^ḥ svatantraḥ kim punar svātantryam kāraka-phala-upabhoktṛ-
009.19	tvam yasmāt kārakāṇām phalena   ayam abhisambaddhyate/ tad-samavāyaḥ
009.20	vā yad vā aśeṣa-kāraka-niṣpādyāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ puruṣaḥ āśrayaḥ bhavati
009.21	tad prayoktṛtvam itarā aprayojyatā vā/ yad vā paridṛṣṭa-sāmarthyāni kāraka-
009.22	cakrāṇi prayuṅkte taiḥ ca na prayujyate/ tattva-parisamāptiḥ viniyoga-yogyatā
010.01	upekṣyatā vā yā asau avisaṃvāditā sāvadhāritasya arthasya viniyoga-
010.02	yogyatā sukha-sādhanam vā duḥkha-sādhanam vā ayam arthaḥ iti/ jñātvā viniyogaḥ
010.03	samāptiḥ tad-prāptiḥ tad-pratiṣedhah ca/ yad vā na sukha-sādhanam na duḥkha-sādhanam
010.04	iti upekṣeti kim punar tad sat-asatī tad tasya bhāvaḥ tattvam iti atra
010.05	tad vācyam yasya bhāvaḥ iti sat-asatī tad pramāṇa-viṣaya-tvena   adhikārāt
010.06	sat-asatī pramāṇa-viṣayau iti adhikṛtam tasmāt sat-asatī tad iti tad-bhāvaḥ
010.07	sat-asattvam sat-asatoḥ pramāṇa-viṣayatā tad-pratiṣedhaḥ ca tayoḥ khalu sat-asatoḥ
010.08	bhāvaḥ dvirūpaḥ vidhīyamānaḥ pratiṣidhyamānaḥ ca yad ekatra vidhīyate
010.09	tad anyatra pratiṣidhyate tad yathā gandhavatī pṛthivī agandhā āpaḥ iti/
010.10	pramāṇa-viṣaya-tvāt sat-asat-bhāva-viśeṣa-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet/ na/ anaikāntāt
010.11	tatra bhavet eṣā buddhiḥ yadi pramāṇena   sat-asatī gamyete tataḥ pramāṇa-
010.12	viṣaya-tvāt ubhayoḥ aviśeṣaḥ prāptaḥ tad ca na anaikāntāt sarvam idam go-ghaṭa-ādi-
010.13	dravya-jātam pramāṇa-gamyam bhedavat ca tasmāt anekāntaḥ ayam iti svatantra-paratantra-
010.14	upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-kāraṇa-bhāvāt ca viśeṣaḥ sat khalu pramāṇasya ālambanam
010.15	svatantram bhavati asat tu paratantram pratiṣedha-mukhena   pratipadyate pradīpa-vat iti
010.16	yathā pradīpaḥ apavaraka-ādi-sthitam ghaṭa-ādikam artham pratipādayati tadvat
010.17	asat api pratipādayati/ na hi asat-pratipatti^i  upāya-antaram āsthīyate
010.18	dṛśyamāne hi ghaṭa-ādike arthe na anena samāna-jātīyam dṛśya-antaram asti yadi
010.19	abhaviṣyat idam iva adrakṣyata na ca dṛśyate tasmāt darśana-abhāvāt na asti iti gamyate/
010.20	evam pramāṇena   api sati pramīyamāṇe na anena samāna-jātīyam prameya-antaram
010.21	`asti yadi abhaviṣyat idam iva amāsyata na ca pramīyate tasmāt māna-abhāvāt
011.01	na asti iti tad evam sataḥ prakāśakam pramāṇam asat api prakāśayati tatra
011.02	svātantryena   asat-bhedāḥ na prakāśante iti na ucyante catur-varga-anantarbhāvāt vā
011.03	bhāva-prapañcavat abhāva-prapañcaḥ api uddiṣṭaḥ veditavyaḥ iti bhāva-upadeśāt abhāva-
011.04	prapañcaḥ uddhiṣṭaḥ bhavati iti ataḥ ca na ucyante iti/
011.05	[1-1-1]pramāṇa-prameya-saṃśaya-prayojana-dṛṣṭānta-avayava-tarka-
011.06	nirṇaya-vāda-jalpa-vitaṇḍā-hetvābhāsa-chala-jāti-nigrahasthānām
011.07	tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ
011.08	tatra khalu ṣoḍaśadhā vyūḍham upadekṣyate iti/ vyūhaḥ saṃkṣepaḥ/ te
011.09	ete sat-bhedāḥ iti sūtram/ sarva-pada-artha-pradhānaḥ samāsaḥ dvandvaḥ iti
011.10	kim uktam bhavati/ sarve ete pramāṇa-ādyaḥ  vijñeyāḥ iti/ anyathā
011.11	hi samāna-antara-parigrahāt viśeṣaṇa-tvena   upayuktānām pramāṇa-ādīnām avijñeyatā
011.12	syāt yathā-vacanam vigrahaḥ yad eva nirdeśe vacana-bheda-upādāne prayojanam
011.13	tad iha api draṣṭavyam/ pramāṇa-ādīnām tattvam iti śaiṣikī ṣaṣṭhī/ kaḥ
011.14	punar śeṣaḥ kārakāṇām avivakṣā śeṣaḥ iti/ yatra na kārakam kāraka-arthaḥ
011.15	vā vivakṣyate saḥ śeṣaḥ tad yathā brāhmaṇasya kamaṇḍaluḥ iti/ tattvasya pramāṇa-
011.16	ādibhyaḥ anya-ananya-tve doṣaḥ yadi tāvat pramāṇa-ādi-vyatiriktam tattvam
011.17	na pramāṇa-ādi-adhigamāt niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti prāptam/ kim kāraṇam
011.18	tattva-viśeṣaṇa-tvena   upādānāt rāja-puruṣa-ānayana-kriyā-vat iti/ atha abhedaḥ
011.19	tattva-grahaṇa-anarthakyam yadi pramāṇa-ādi-vyatiriktam tattvam na pratipadyase evam
011.20	tarhi tattva-grahaṇam anarthakam sampadyate na ubhayathā api adoṣāt iti eke/ yadi tāvat
011.21	pramāṇa-ādi-vyatiriktam tattvam syāt tasya asvatantra-tvāt pramāṇa-ādyaḥ  api
011.22	gamyante it kuṇḍe badara-vṛtti-vat iti yathā kuṇḍe badarāṇām vṛttiḥ iti
012.01	bhāve deśite bhāvasya asvatantra-tvāt kuṇḍe badarāṇi vartante iti uktam
012.02	bhavati/ tathā iha api tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti pramāṇa-ādīnām
012.03	grahaṇam bhāvasya asvatantra-tvāt iti/ abhede api iṣu-sthiti-vat tad-bhāva-pratiṣedhaḥ
012.04	na artha-antaram iti na pramāṇa-ādi-mātram ucyate api tu artha-antaram pratiṣidhyate/
012.05	yathā iṣu^aḥ   sthitiḥ iti na iṣu-mātram sthitiḥ api tu gatimat artha-antaram
012.06	na bhavati iti/ tad na/ an-artha-antara-tva-siddhi^aḥ   tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasa-
012.07	adhigamaḥ iti tattvam jñāyamānam karman sampadyate niḥśreyasam adhigamyamānam
012.08	karman bhavati iti/ kim punar tattvam kim vā niḥśreyasam iti/
012.09	tattvam padārthānām yathā-avasthita-ātma-pratyaya-utpatti-nimitta-tvam yaḥ yathā-
012.10	avasthitaḥ padārthaḥ saḥ tathā-bhūta-pratyaya-utpatti-nimittam bhavati yad tad
012.11	tattvam/ niḥśreyasam punar dṛṣṭa-adṛṣṭa-bhedāt dvedhā bhavati/ tatra pramāṇa-ādi-
012.12	padārtha-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam dṛṣṭam na hi kaḥ cit padārthaḥ jñāyamānaḥ
012.13	hāna-upādāna-upekṣā-buddhi-nimittam na bhavati iti evam ca kṛtvā sarve padārthāḥ
012.14	jñeyatayā upakṣipyante iti/ param tu niḥśreyasam ātma-ādi^aḥ   tattva-jñānāt bhavati
012.15	dṛṣṭam pramāṇa-ādi-parijñānāt adṛṣṭam punar ātma-ādi^aḥ   prameyasya parijñānāt iti
012.16	na pramāṇam asti/ na na asti arthasya tathā-bhāvāt arthaḥ eva ayam tathā-bhūtaḥ
012.17	yad ātma-ādi^aḥ   prameyasya tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam adhigamyate/ yadā hi ayam
012.18	ātma-ādi prameyam viparyayena   adhyavasitaḥ bhavati/ atha saṃsāram na ativartate
012.19	iti enam ca artham dvitīya-sūtre pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ/ yadi punar
012.20	pramāṇa-ādi-padārtha-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam syāt na mokṣyamāṇāḥ mokṣāya ghaṭeran
013.01	na hi kasya cit kva cit tattva-jñānam na asti iti tasmāt ātma-ādi eva
013.02	prameyam mumukṣuṇā jñeyam iti/ pṛthak-upadeśāt cac/ yadi pramāṇa-ādi eva prameyam
013.03	syāt tasya ca parijñānāt niḥśreyasam bhavet/ na ātma-ādi prameyam pṛthak-upadṛṣṭam
013.04	syāt/ prameya-artha-avadhāraṇa-arthāyām ca uttara-sūtra-prakriyāyām prameyasya
013.05	vihita-tvāt ādyena   sūtrena   akuśalaḥ sūtra-kāraḥ syāt/ tad ca etad
013.06	uttara-sūtrena   anūdyate iti bhāṣyam/ heya-hāna-upāya-adhigantavya-bhedāt catuvāri
013.07	artha-padāni samyac-buddhvā niḥśreyasam adhigacchati iti/ heyam duḥkham tasya
013.08	nirvartakam avidyā-tṛṣṇā^i dharma-adharmau iti/ hānam tattva-jñānam tasya upāyaḥ
013.09	śāstram/ adhigantavyaḥ mokṣaḥ/ etāni catvāri artha-padāni sarvāsu adhyātma-
013.10	vidyāsu sarva-ācāryaiḥ varṇyante iti/ saṃśaya-ādi-agrahaṇam pṛthak prameya-antar-
013.11	bhāvāt iti cet/ na/ vidyā-prasthāna-bheda-jñāpana-artha-tvāt iti/ saṃśaya-ādayaḥ
013.12	prameye antar-bhavati iti pṛthak te na vaktavyāḥ/ na/ vidyā-prasthāna-bheda-
013.13	jñāpana-artha-tvāt/ catasraḥ imāḥ vidyāḥ bhavanti tāḥ ca pṛthak-prasthānāḥ
013.14	agni-hotra-havana-ādi-prasthānā trayī/ hala-śakaṭa-ādi-prasthānā vārtā/ svāmya-
013.15	mātya-bheda-anuvidhāyinī daṇḍa-nītiḥ/ saṃśaya-ādi-bheda-anuvidhāyinī ānvīkṣikī/
013.16	tasyāḥ saṃśaya-ādi-prasthānam antareṇa ātma-vidyā-mātram iyam syāt
013.17	tataḥ kim syāt adhyātma-vidyā-mātra-tvāt upaniṣad-vidyā-vat trayyām eva antarbhāvaḥ
013.18	iti catuṣṭvam nivartate tasmāt pṛthak gṛhyante iti/ tatra
013.19	saṃśayaḥ tāvat vastu-svarūpa-anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ/ anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ ca
013.20	pratyayaḥ ca iti vyāhanyate/ na vyāghātaḥ svarūpa-avadhāraṇāt svarūpam asya
013.21	avadhāryate asti me saṃśaya-jñānam iti/ vastu-svarūpam tu na anena paricchidyate/
014.01	tad ubhayam anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ ca pratyayaḥ ca iti/ saḥ katham nyāyasya
014.02	aṅgam bhavati iti/ yasmāt na anupalabdhe na nirṇīte nyāyaḥ pravartate/ upalabdhaḥ
014.03	anirṇītaḥ ca iti vyāhatam/ yadi upalabdhaḥ na anirṇītaḥ atha anirṇītaḥ
014.04	na upalabdhaḥ upalabdhaḥ anirṇītaḥ ca iti vyāhatam/ na asti vyāhataḥ
014.05	sāmānyena   upalabdhaḥ viśeṣataḥ anirṇītaḥ iti/ evam api yathā upalabdhaḥ
014.06	tathā anirṇītaḥ iti vyāghāta-anivṛttiḥ na anivṛttiḥ yathā tathā iti
014.07	vyapadeśāt sarvathā nirṇīte yathā nirṇītaḥ tathā upalabdhaḥ iti anarthakam syāt/
014.08	tasmāt sāmānyataḥ upalabdhaḥ viśeṣataḥ anirṇītaḥ iti/ saḥ ca ayam
014.09	saṃśayaḥ evam-bhūtaḥ prameye antarbhūtaḥ evam artham pṛthak ucyate/ atha prayojanam/
014.10	kim punar prayojanam iti/ yena prayuktaḥ pravartate tat prayojanam iti
014.11	laukikaḥ ayam arthaḥ/ kena prayujyate/ dharma-artha-kāma-mokṣaiḥ iti ke cit/
014.12	vayam tu paśyāmaḥ sukha-duḥkha-āpti-hānibhyām prayujyate iti/ sukha-duḥkha-
014.13	sādhana-bhāvāt tu sarve arthāḥ cetanam prayojayanti iti/ tad idam prayojanam
014.14	nyāyasya āśrayaḥ kaḥ āśraya-arthaḥ ādhāra-arthaḥ tāvat na bhavati/ upakārakatvam
014.15	āśraya-arthaḥ tad-mūla-tvāt parīkṣā-vidhi^aḥ  / prayojane sati parīkṣyate iti
014.16	prayojanam parīkṣām pravartayati iti/ kā punar iyam parīkṣā nyāyaḥ kaḥ
014.17	punar ayam nyāyaḥ/ pramāṇaiḥ artha-parīkṣaṇam nyāyaḥ/ kim uktam bhavati/ samasta-
014.18	pramāṇa-vyāpārāt artha-adhigatiḥ nyāyaḥ iti/ na ekaikam pramāṇam artha-pariccheda-hetu-
014.19	bhāvena   vyavatiṣṭhamānam nyāyaḥ iti ucyate kim tu samastāni/ saḥ ayam vipratipanna-
014.20	puruṣa-pratipādaka-tvāt paramaḥ nyāyaḥ iti vakṣyāmaḥ/ pratyakṣa-
014.21	āgama-āśritam anumānam pratyakṣa-āgama-āśritam iti pratyakṣa-āgama-avirodhin yadi
014.22	hi anumāna-adhigataḥ arthaḥ pratyakṣa-āgamābhyām anusandhīyate atha sphuṭataraḥ
015.01	pratyayaḥ bhavati/ yatra punar etāni pramāṇāni itaretara-apratisaṃhitāni
015.02	vyāvṛttāni prayujyante/ nyāya-viplavaḥ asau lābha-pūja-ākhyāti-kāmaiḥ tīrtha-
015.03	pratirūpakaḥ pravādaḥ varṇyate/ yad punar anumānam pratyakṣa-āgama-virodhin saḥ
015.04	nyāya-ābhāsaḥ iti/ pratyakṣa-viruddham tāvat vahniḥ anuṣṇaḥ kṛtaka-tvāt ghaṭa-ādi
015.05	vat kaḥ punar asya anumānasya virodhaḥ anumāna-aviṣaye prayogaḥ/
015.06	na ayam anumānasya viṣaye prayogaḥ na ayam anumānasya viṣayaḥ yasmin
015.07	viṣaye etad prayujyate saḥ pratyakṣena   apahataḥ iti/ apare punar aśrāvaṇaḥ śabdaḥ
015.08	pratyakṣa-virodham varṇayanti taiḥ tu na pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ jñātaḥ
015.09	na anumānasya viṣayaḥ iti/ kim kāraṇam indriya-vṛttīnām atīndriya-tvāt
015.10	śrāvaṇatvam ca indriya-vṛttiḥ sā katham pratyakṣā bhavati āgama-viruddham śuci
015.11	nara-śiras-kapālam prāṇi-aṅga-tvāt śaṅkha-śukti-vat iti katham idam āgama-viruddham
015.12	śuci nara-śiras-kapālam iti bruvatā śuci-arthaḥ vācyaḥ kim uktam bhavati
015.13	śuci iti/ yadi spraṣṭṛ^aḥ  pratyavāya-abhāvaḥ saḥ kasya iti vācyam/ yadi ātmanaḥ
015.14	iti brūyāt tad-āgama-artha-anuṣṭhāna-tātparyena   avasthānād evam etad/
015.15	atha trayī-vidām iti brūyāt trayī-abhyupagamāt virodhaḥ iti vācyam/
015.16	śuci nara-śiras-kapālam iti kaḥ arthaḥ viśeṣa-vidhānam etad viśeṣa-
015.17	vidhānam hi śeṣa-niṣedha-viṣayam yadi śuci nara-śiras-kapālam iti
015.18	kim aśuci iti vācyam/ atha sarvam eva śuci iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ na asti sarvasya
015.19	pakṣī-kṛta-tvāt iti/ atha anumāna-viruddham kasmāt anumānam na bhavati
015.20	ekasmin anumāna-dvaya-samāveśa-asambhavāt na virodhaḥ/ na hi anvaya-vyatireka-
015.21	sampanne anumāne ekasmin arthe samāviśataḥ tasmāt na anumāna-viruddham/
016.01	pratyakṣa-virodhin api tarhi na prāpnoti/ na na prāpnoti anvaya-vyatireka-sampannasya
016.02	pratyakṣena   bādhita-tvāt/ atha ūpamāna-viruddham kasmāt na bhavati na upamāna-
016.03	viruddham pūrva-pramāṇa-virodha-anuvidhānāt/ upamāna-virodhaḥ pūrva-pramāṇa-
016.04	anuvidhāyin^ḥ āgama-āhita-saṃskāra-smṛti-apekṣam ca sārūpya-jñānam upamānam
016.05	iti vakṣyāmaḥ/ pratyakṣa-āgamayoḥ virodhāt uktam tad iti/ tatra vāda-jalpau
016.06	saprayojanau iti bhāṣyam/ tasya kutaḥ utthānam tena anena prayojanena
016.07	sarve prāṇinaḥ sarvāṇi karmāṇi sarvāh ca vidhyāḥ vyāptāḥ iti tatra
016.08	vāda-jalpau kim-prayojanau iti yuktaḥ vicāraḥ iti āha/ tatra vāda-jalpau
016.09	saprayojanau iti/ tasmin nyāya-ābhāsaḥ iti/ vitaṇḍā tu parīkṣyate
016.10	saprayojanā viṣprayojanā vā iti/ eke tāvat varṇayanti niṣprayojanā
016.11	dūṣaṇa-mātra-tvāt/ tad ca na evam na dūṣaṇa-mātram vitaṇḍā kim tu abhyupetya
016.12	pakṣam yaḥ na sthāpayati saḥ vaitaṇḍikaḥ ucyate/ atha pakṣam api na
016.13	pratipadyate unmatta-vat upekṣaṇīyaḥ syāt/ atha para-pakṣa-pratiṣedha-jñāpanam
016.14	prayojanam iti tādṛk eva etad/ etasmin api catur-varge cet pratipadyate
016.15	saḥ asya pakṣaḥ catur-vargaḥ bhāṣye uktaḥ/ atha na pratipadyate pūrvavat
016.16	upekṣaṇīyaḥ/ pratipakṣa-sthāpana-ahīnam ca vākyam vitaṇḍā iti ucyate/ tasya
016.17	yadi artham pratipadyate saḥ asya pakṣaḥ/ atha na pratipadyate pūrvavat-doṣaḥ
016.18	iti uktam prayojanam iti/ pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ arthaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ kim uktam bhavati/
016.19	laukika-parīkṣakāṇām darśana-avighāta-hetuḥ iti/ evam ca ātma-ādi-vypāptiḥ
016.20	iti darśana-avighāta-hetu-tvena   dṛṣṭānte varṇyamāne ātma-ādi vyāptam
016.21	bhavati/ pratyakṣa-ādi-viṣaya-tāyām ca ātma-ādi tyaktam bhavati/ tataḥ ca udāharaṇa-
016.22	sūtram vyāhanyeta/ saḥ ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ prameyam upalabdhi-viṣaya-tvāt tasya
017.01	pṛthak-upadeśaḥ nyāyasya tad-mūla-tvāt sati tasmin anumāna-āgamau syātām asati
017.02	tu na syātām iti pūrvam pratyakṣa-dṛṣṭam artham anumimate/ pūrvam jñātam ca artham
017.03	parasmai ācakṣate nāstikasya ca vyāghātaḥ abhyupagame anabhyupagame vā
017.04	tad uktam bhāṣye iti/ abhyupagama-vyavasthā siddhāntaḥ/ abhyupagamaḥ
017.05	idam ittham-bhūtam vā iti/ idam iti sāmānyataḥ ittham-bhūtam iti viśeṣataḥ
017.06	tasya vyavasthā idam sāṃkhya^su idam yoga^su iti/ sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ tarhi na
017.07	siddhāntaḥ avyavasthānāt na hi ayam kva cit vyavatiṣṭhate iti/ tad ca na yaḥ ayam
017.08	sarvaiḥ abhyupagamaḥ iyam evam vyavasthā tasya prameye antarbhṛtasya pṛthak-vacanam
017.09	vāda-ādi-viṣaya-tvāt siddhānta-bhede hi vāda-jalpa-vitaṇḍāḥ pravartante iti/
017.10	atha avayavāḥ vākya-ekadeśāḥ kim punar vākyam pūrva-pada-smṛti-apekṣaḥ antya-
017.11	pada-pratyayaḥ smṛti-anugrahena   pratisandhīyamānaḥ viśeṣa-pratipatti-hetuḥ vākyam
017.12	tasya bhāgāḥ ekadeśāḥ iti/ te kiyantaḥ yāvadbhiḥ siddhiḥ parisamāpyate
017.13	iti/ kā punar iyam siddhiḥ padārthasya tathatā kā parisamāptiḥ
017.14	viśeṣa-pratyayaḥ te samākhyā-śabdaiḥ abhidhīyante pratijñā-ādyaḥ  iti tatra āgamaḥ
017.15	pratijñā iti na yuktam āgamasya tattva-vyavacchedaka-tvāt pratijñā-arthasya ca
017.16	pratipādya-tvāt āgama-adhigatasya pratipādya-tvāt āgamaḥ pratijñā iti na
017.17	doṣaḥ yaḥ eva arthaḥ āgamena adhigataḥ tam eva parasmai ācaṣṭe iti āgamaḥ
017.18	pratijñā iti ucyate/ evam liṅga-darśana-mātre hetu-upacārāt hetuḥ anumānam iti/
017.19	yad tu dvitīyam liṅga-darśanam tad-sambandha-smṛti-vyakti-hetu-bhāvāt hetuḥ iti ucyate/
017.20	smṛti-viṣayasya pratyakṣataḥ punar upadarśanāt udāharaṇam pratyakṣam yasmāt pūrva-anubhūtam
017.21	artham smarati smṛtam ca viṣayam udāharaṇa-tvena   ādatte/ tena pūrva-anubhava-
018.01	prasiddham anuvidhīyamānam pratyakṣam iva pratyakṣam iti/ kaḥ punar upamāna-arthaḥ
018.02	avipratipattiḥ yathā pratyakṣe na vipratipadyate evam udāharaṇe api iti/
018.03	yathā tathā iti upamāna-ekadeśe upamāna-upacārāt upamānam upanayaḥ iti/
018.04	upamānam khalu yathā tathā iti upadeśa-upayoge sati pratyakṣa-āgama-smṛti-pūrvakam
018.05	sārūpya-jñānam upamānam iti tatra upamāna-ekadeśe upamānam iti upacaranti/
018.06	sarveṣām eka-artha-samavāye sāmarthya-pradarśanam nigamanam iti/ kaḥ punar
018.07	eka-artha-samavāyaḥ eka-vākya-adhyāropaḥ kim punar sāmarthyam itaretara-
018.08	sampratyāyita-artha-apekṣi-tvam/ etad ca vakṣyāmah iti/ nigamyate anena
018.09	pratijñā-ādyaḥ  eka-arthena   sambadhyante iti/ saḥ ayam paramaḥ nyāyaḥ iti/
018.10	kaḥ punar parama-arthaḥ vipratipanna-puruṣa-pratipādaka-tvam ekaikaśah pramāṇāni
018.11	pravṛttāni na vipratipannam puruṣam pratipādayanti vākya-bhāva-āpannāni
018.12	punar vipratipannam ataḥ ayam paramaḥ iti/ kim punar avayavāh pramāṇa-antaram uta
018.13	teṣu antarbhavanti iti/ kim ca ataḥ yadi pramāṇa-antaram parisaṃkhyānam ayuktam atha
018.14	teṣu eva antarbhavanti pṛthak-abhidhāna-anarthakyam avayavānām iti/ na pramāṇa-
018.15	antaram iti brūmaḥ/ saṃhatānām eṣām vākya-paratantrāṇām vipratipanna-
018.16	puruṣa-pratipādaka-tvena   pṛthak-upādānam iti/ te ete avayavāh pṛthak-bhavantaḥ
018.17	vāda-jalpa-vitaṇḍānām pravṛtti-hetu^āḥ bhavanti/ tattva-vyavasthāyāḥ ca āśrayāḥ
018.18	bhavanti iti/ kaḥ āśraya-arthaḥ/ viśeṣa-pratipādaka-tvam iti/ tarkaḥ na
018.19	pramāṇa-saṃgṛhītaḥ na pramāṇa-antaram aparicchedaka-tvāt/ pramāṇam paricchedakam
018.20	na tarkaḥ/ tasmāt na pramāṇam na pramāṇa-antaram api ataḥ eva/
018.21	pramāṇa-viṣaya-vibhāgāt tu pramāṇānām anugrāhakaḥ/ yaḥ pramāṇānām viṣayaḥ
018.22	tam vibhajate/ kaḥ punar vibhāgaḥ yukta-ayukta-vicāraḥ idam yuktam idam
019.01	ayuktam iti/ yad tatra yuktam bhavati tad anujānāti na tu avadhārayati
019.02	anavadhāraṇāt pramāṇa-antaram na bhavati/ tasya udāharaṇam bhāṣye/ karma-
019.03	nimittam janman iti/ katham punar karma-nimittam janman bhedavat-tvāt/ kaḥ
019.04	punar bhedaḥ sugatiḥ durgatiḥ ca iti/ sugati^i  devaḥ manuṣyaḥ iti manuṣyatve
019.05	puṃs itaraḥ iti puṃstve brāhmaṇaḥ anyaḥ iti brāhmaṇatve paṭu-indriyaḥ
019.06	mṛdu-indriyaḥ iti paṭu-indriya-tāyām ucca-abhijanaḥ nīca-abhijanaḥ iti
019.07	ucca-abhijana-tāyām sakalaḥ niṣkalaḥ iti sākalye vidvān mūrkhaḥ iti
019.08	vidvat-tāyām samāśvāsin^ḥ paritrastaḥ iti samāśvāse vaśin^ḥ para-āyattaḥ
019.09	iti/ durgati^i  api tiryac-nārakaḥ iti nārakatve api kuṭa-śālmalyām ayaḥ-
019.10	kumbhyām iti/ tiryac-tāyām go^ḥ itaraḥ iti/ saḥ ayam bhedaḥ anekam avasthitam
019.11	anityam aneka-dravyam pratyātma-niyatam nimittam antareṇa na yuktaḥ kim kāraṇam
019.12	pṛthivī-ādīnām sarva-puruṣa-sādhāraṇyāt pṛthivī-ādi-gatasya ca niyama-
019.13	hetu^aḥ   abhāvāt sarvam sarva-atmakam prasajyeta/ dṛṣṭaḥ ca niyama-bhedaḥ tasmāt
019.14	karman niyāmakam iti/ saḥ ayam ittham-bhūtaḥ tarkaḥ upalabdhi-viṣaya-tvāt
019.15	prameyam/ nirṇayaḥ tattva-jñānam pramāṇānām phalam kadā punar nirṇayaḥ pramāṇānām
019.16	phalam iti/ yadā vastu-antara-pariccheda-hetu-tvena   na upādīyate tadā
019.17	phalam yadā tena paricchinatti tadā pramāṇam iti na vyavatiṣṭhate
019.18	pramāṇa-phala-bhāvaḥ/ etad ca vakṣyāmaḥ/ prameyatā ca tulā-prāmāṇya-
019.19	vat iti etasmin sūtre nimitta-dvaya-samāveśāt ekam vastu dvi-śabda-
019.20	vācyam iti/ tau etau tarka-nirṇayau loka-yātrām udvahataḥ iti buddhvā
019.21	buddhvā lokaḥ pravartamānaḥ tarka-nirṇayābhyām heyam jahāti upādeyam
020.01	ca upādatte asya antarbhāvaḥ pramāṇa^su prameya^su vā yadā phalam tadā prameyam
020.02	yadā tena paricchinatti tadā pramāṇam iti/ vādaḥ nānā-pravakṛkaḥ
020.03	iti/ siddhānta-bheda-anuvidhānāt nānā-vaktṛkaḥ iti pratyadhikaraṇa-sādhanaḥ
020.04	anyatara-adhikaraṇa-nirṇaya-avasānaḥ iti/ adhikaraṇam adhikaraṇam prati
020.05	pratyadhikaraṇam asya sādhanam iti kim uktam bhavati/ ubhābhyām sādhanam
020.06	vaktavyam iti/ anyatarasmin adhikaraṇe nirṇayaḥ/ etad ca vakṣyāmaḥ/
020.07	saḥ ayam kim-rūpaḥ vādaḥ vākya-samūhaḥ iti āha/ nanu ca pramāṇa-tarka-
020.08	sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti śrūyate pramāṇāni ca jñāna-ātmakāni indriya-artha-
020.09	sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam iti evam ādinā prakramena   pramāṇāni jñāna-
020.10	ātmakāni iti uktam tarkaḥ ca ūhaḥ saḥ ca ūhaḥ jñāna-ātmakaḥ eva tasmāt vākya-samūhaḥ
020.11	vādaḥ iti ayuktam/ na/ pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhasya anyathā-vyākhyānāt/
020.12	tad ca sūtram varṇayiṣyantaḥ vakṣyāmaḥ/ tad-viśeṣau jalpa-vitaṇḍe iti/
020.13	kaḥ punar viśeṣaḥ aṅga-adhikyam aṅga-hāniḥ ca iti/ chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-
020.14	prayogāt adhikaḥ jalpaḥ saḥ pratipakṣasya apāna-ahīnaḥ tu vitaṇḍā etāvatā
020.15	viśeṣena   kathā-mārga-bhedaḥ iti viṣaya-bhedāt ca bhedaḥ śiṣya-ādi-viṣayaḥ vādaḥ
020.16	śiṣyamāṇa-viṣaye jalpa-vitaṇḍe iti/ anyatama-liṅga-dharma-anuvidhānena
020.17	pravartamānāḥ ahetu^āḥ santaḥ hetuvat ābhāsante iti hetvābhāsāḥ te ca
020.18	nigrahasthānam nigrahasthāna-prāptānām punar eṣām pṛthak-upadeśaḥ vāde deśanīya-
020.19	tvāt iti bhāṣyam/ yasmāt kila ete vāde deśyante ataḥ pṛthak upadiśyante
020.20	na ubhayathā api asambandhāt na vāde deśanīyatvam pṛthak-upadeśa-
020.21	avinābhāvin pṛthak-upadeśaḥ vā vāde deśanīyatva-avinābhāvin iti/ yadi
020.22	tāvat yaḥ pṛthak upadiśyate saḥ vāde deśyate iti sarve padārthāḥ vāde
021.01	deśanīyāḥ prasajyante iti/ kim kāraṇam sarve pṛthak-uapdiṣṭāḥ iti/
021.02	atha vāde deśyante te pṛthak upadiśyante iti/ tad api na anekāntāt/
021.03	na tāvat nigrahasthāna^bhyaḥ nyūna-adhikayoḥ pṛthak-upadeśaḥ atha ca deśyete
021.04	vāde tasmāt vāde deśanīya-tvāt eṣām pṛthak-upadeśaḥ iti yat kim cit etad/
021.05	etad eva tu nyāyam pṛthak-upadeśa-prayojanam vidyā-prasthāna-bheda-jñāpana-artha-tvāt
021.06	iti/ atha kasmāt nigrahasthāna-bhāva-āpannānām hetvābhāsānām pṛthak-upadeśaḥ
021.07	iti/ pramāṇa-sāmānyāt pṛthak-upadeśaḥ iti/ atha kasmāt vāde
021.08	deśyante pramāṇa-sāmānyāt/ pramāṇāni vāde abhidhīyante pramāṇa-
021.09	sāmānyam ca hetvābhāse asti iti/ ataḥ pramāṇa-sāmānyāt vāde abhidhīynate
021.10	iti/ atha vāde nigrahasthānāni kāni cit santi kāni cit na iti
021.11	kim ayam padārthānām niyogaḥ/ atha tathā-bhūtānām anvākhyānam iti kim
021.12	ca ataḥ yadi tāvat niyogaḥ evam bhavatā evam mā bhavatā iti evam padārthān anuyuñjānaḥ
021.13	bhavān upekṣaṇīyaḥ/ atha svarūpataḥ eva avasthitāḥ padārthāh tān anujānāti
021.14	bhavān kasmāt nigrahasthānāni kāni cit vāde bhavanti kāni cit na iti
021.15	vaktavyam na lakṣaṇa-paratantra-tvāt artha-tathā-bhāvasya iti brūmaḥ/ na padārthāḥ vaktṛ-
021.16	niyogam anuvidhīyante/ yathā tu bhavanti tathā-bhūtāḥ eva arthāḥ anvākhyāyante/
021.17	saḥ ayam lakṣaṇa-paratantra-tvam ācakṣāṇaḥ na abhyākhyeyaḥ padārthāḥ
021.18	bhavatā niyujyante iti vādasya śiṣya-ādi-viṣaya-tvāt/ na ca śiṣya-ādibhiḥ
021.19	saha vādam kurvāṇena   api apratibha-ādi-deśanā kāryā tāvat asya abhidheyam
021.20	yāvat asau bodhitaḥ bhavati iti/ pramāṇa-pratirūpaka-tvāt hetvābhāsānām
021.21	avirodhaḥ iti/ kim kāraṇam bhrānti^aḥ   puruṣa-dharma-tvāt puruṣa-dhramaḥ eva
021.22	bhrāntiḥ iti/ saḥ ayam bhrāntyā pramāṇam bruvan pramāṇa-ābhāsam api brūte
022.01	ataḥ asya nigrahaḥ iti/ kaḥ punar śiṣya-ācāryayoḥ vivakṣita-
022.02	artha-apratipādaka-tvam/ nyūna-adhika-apasiddhāntānām vāde katham avatāraḥ
022.03	pramāṇa-sāmānyāt eva/ nyūnasya api pramāṇa-sāmānyam adhikasya api/
022.04	avayava-vihīnatā avayava-adhikyam vākyasya bhrāntyā bhavati iti asya avatāraḥ/
022.05	alpa-vitaṇḍayoḥ tu nigrahasthānāni iti vijigīṣubhiḥ jalpa-vitaṇḍābhyām
022.06	pratyavastheyam/ tasya yathā-sambhavam nigrahasthānāni vaktavyāni iti
022.07	na upekṣaṇīyaḥ/ chala-jāti-nigrahasthānām sva-vākye parivarjanam para-vākye
022.08	paryanuyogaḥ parijñāna-artham eva kevalam/ chala-jāti-nigrahasthānāni
022.09	svayam na prayoktavyāni jāti^aḥ   ca svayam sukaraḥ prayogaḥ iti/ na/ vyāghātāt
022.10	sva-vākye parivarjanam sukaraḥ ca prayogaḥ iti vyāhatam yadi sukaraḥ prayogaḥ
022.11	na sva-vākye parivarjanam atha sva-vākye parivarjanam na sukaraḥ prayogaḥ
022.12	iti vyāghātaḥ na vyāghātaḥ praśna-apākaraṇa-artha-tvāt/ svayam ca sukaraḥ
022.13	prayogaḥ iti kim uktam bhavati/ pareṇa jāti^i  prayuktāyām praśnikān
022.14	bravīti jātiḥ anena prayuktā iti te enam paryanuyuñjīran katham jātiḥ katamā
022.15	jātiḥ ataḥ jāti-abhijñaḥ śaknoti vaktum evaṃ jātiḥ iyam jātiḥ iti/ evam
022.16	ca sukarah prayogaḥ iti/ tasmāt ete saṃśaya-ādyaḥ  padārthāḥ pramāṇe prameye
022.17	ca antarbhavantaḥ vidyā-prathāna-bheda-jñāpana-artham pṛthak-upadiṣṭāḥ/ sā iyam
022.18	ānvīkṣikī nyāya-vidyā pramāṇa-ādibhiḥ padārthaiḥ vibhajyamānā pradīpaḥ
022.19	sarva-vidyānām bhavati prakāśaka-tvāt pradīpa-vat pramāṇa-ādi-pratipāditam
022.20	artham itarāḥ vidyāḥ pratipadyante iti/ kim itarāsu vidyāsu pramāṇa-ādīni
023.01	na santi na santi iti āha/ katham na santi anadhikārāt/
023.02	na tāḥ vidyāḥ pramāṇa-ādi-parijñānena   adhikriyante iti pramāṇa-ādi-
023.03	prakāśite tu arthe tāḥ pravartante iti/ upāyaḥ sarva-karmaṇām iti/ prakāśitānām
023.04	karaṇāt upāyaḥ yasmāt pramāṇa-ādi-prakāśitam artham itarāḥ vidyāḥ
023.05	kurvanti/ āśrayaḥ sarva-dharmāṇām iti/ sarva-vidyā-upakāraka-tvāt āśrayaḥ/
023.06	sarvāsām vidyānām iyam upakaroti upakāri-tvāt āśrayaḥ rāja-bhṛtya-vat iti/
023.07	tad idam tattva-jñānam niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ ca yathā-vidyam veditavyam iti/
023.08	sarvāsu vidyāsu tattva-jñānam asti niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ ca iti/ trayyām tāvat
023.09	kim tattva-jñānam kaḥ ca niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti/ tattva-jñānam tāvat agni-
023.10	hotra-ādi-sādhanānām sva-āgata-ādi-parijñānam anupahata-ādi-parijñānam ca/
023.11	niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ api svarga-prāptiḥ tathā hi atra svargaḥ phalam śrūyate iti/
023.12	atha vārtāyām kim tattva-jñānam kaḥ ca niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti/ bhūmi-ādi-
023.13	parijñānam tattva-jñānam bhūmiḥ kaṇṭaka-ādi-anupahatā iti etad tattva-jñānam kṛṣi-ādi-adhigamaḥ
023.14	ca niḥśreyasam iti tad-phala-tvāt/ daṇḍa-nītyām kim tattva-jñānam kaḥ ca
023.15	niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti/ sāma-dāna-daṇḍa-bhedānām yathā-kālam yathā-deśam
023.16	yathā-śakti viniyogaḥ tattva-jñānam niḥśreyasam pṛthivī-jayaḥ iti/ iha
023.17	tu adhyātma-vidyāyām ātma-jñānam tattva-jñānam niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ apavarga-prāptiḥ
023.18	iti/ yad punar pramāṇa-ādi-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam na tad vivakṣitam mada-māna-
023.19	ādi-nimitta-tvāt vāda-ādi-parijñānasya na abhisambandhaḥ niḥśreyasena   iti ke cit/
023.20	na ca anabhisambaddham poratipādayati iti ayuktam uktam pramāṇa-ādi-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam
024.01	iti/ na/ sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt/ sūtra-artham na vyajñāsīḥ yad evam
024.02	pratyapatthāḥ/ kaḥ evam sūtra-artham varṇayati pramāṇa-ādi-padārtha-tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam
024.03	iti/ api tu yad parijñānāt niḥśreyasam tad uttara-sūtre vakṣyāmaḥ/
024.04	uktam ca ātma-ādi^aḥ   prameyasya tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasa-adhigamaḥ iti/ yad ca idam
024.05	ucyate mada-māna-ādi-nimittam vāda-ādyaḥ  iti/ etad ca na/ asati
024.06	bhāvāt sati ca abhāvāt/ asati vāda-ādi-jñāne rāga-ādyaḥ  sanaka-
024.07	ādīnām bhavanti sati ca tattva-vidām na bhavanti tasmāt na vāda-ādi-
024.08	parijñānam rāga-ādīnām nimittam iti//
024.09	na tattva-parijñānāt apavargaḥ ubhayathā doṣāt/ yadi tattva-jñāna-
024.10	anantaram eva apavargaḥ syāt tadvatām avasthānam na syāt/ tadvatām ca avasthānam
024.11	dṛṣṭam kutaḥ śāstra-sampradāya avicchedāt sampradāyaḥ nāma śiṣya-upādhyāya-
024.12	sambandhasya avicchedena   śāstra-prāptiḥ/ yadi tattva-jñānāt apavargaḥ syāt/
024.13	śāstra-sampradāyaḥ vicchidyeta/ yasya yadā tattva-jṇnānam vṛttam saḥ tadā eva
024.14	apavṛktaḥ iti/ atha utpanna-tattva-jñānaḥ avatiṣṭhate na tattva-jñānam kāraṇam
024.15	sati abhāvāt/ sati api tattva-jñāne yasya abhāvāt na apavṛjyate saḥ anyaḥ arthaḥ
024.16	iti/ kāraṇam hi tad bhavati yasmin sati yad bhavati yasmin ca asati yad na
024.17	bhavati/ anadhigata-artha-pūrvam vā śāstram syāt/ atha tattva-jñāna-anantaram
024.18	apavarge vāta-putrīyam śāstram syāt na tattva-dṛk-pūrvakam iti/ na/
024.19	niḥśreyasasya parāpara-bhedāt/ yad tāvat aparam niḥśreyasam tad tattva-jñāna-
024.20	anantaram eva bhavati/ tathā ca uktam/ jīvan eva hi vidvān saṃharṣa-āyāsābhyām
025.01	vimucyate iti/ ayam śāstra-arthaḥ iti/ param ca niḥśreyasam
025.02	tattva-jñānāt kramena   bhavati krama-pratipādana-artham ca idam sūtram duḥkha janma-
025.03	pravṛtti-doṣa-mithyā-jñānānām iti evam ādi//
025.04	[1-1-2]duḥkha-janma-pravṛtti-doṣa-mithyā-jñānānām uttara-uttara-apāye
025.05	tad-anantara-apāyāt apavargaḥ
025.06	etad sambandhena eva arthavat padārthaḥ tu vaktavyaḥ tatra ātma-ādi-apavarga-paryantam prameyam
025.07	jñeyam/ tatra mithyā-jñānam aneka-prakārakam vartate/ kaḥ vṛtti-arthaḥ viṣaya-
025.08	arthaḥ/ tatra ayam bhedaḥ/ tatra ātmani tāvat na asti iti/ ātman^ḥ tāvat
025.09	astitvena   pramāṇa-sāmarthyāt adhigataḥ/ astitvena   adhigate na asti iti vijñānam
025.10	mithyā-pratyayaḥ/ tasya anupapattiḥ sat-asatoḥ sārūpya-abhāvāt iti cet/
025.11	na/ pramāṇa-gamyatā-upapatti^aḥ  / na hi sat-asatī sarūpe yataḥ asat-sāmyāt
025.12	asat-dharma-āropena   ātmani mithyā-pratyayaḥ syāt iti/ tad ca na evam pramāṇa-
025.13	gamyatā-upapatti^aḥ  / sat-asatoḥ pramāṇa-gamyatvam sārūpyam kriyā-guṇa-vyapadeśaḥ tad-
025.14	rahitatā ca viśeṣaḥ saḥ ayam asat-dharmān sati ātmani āropya viparyeti
025.15	na asti ātman^ḥ iti/ evam anātmani śarīre ātman^ḥ iti/ kim punar anātmanaḥ
025.16	śarīra-ādi^aḥ   ātmanā sādharmyam yataḥ etad bhavati anātmani ātman^ḥ iti/ ahaṅkāra-
025.17	viṣaya-tvam sārūpyam/ icchā-ādi-ādhāra-anādhāra-tā iti viśeṣaḥ/
025.18	yathā eva ātma-ahaṅkārasya viṣayaḥ tathā śarīra-ādyaḥ  api/ katham punar śarīre
025.19	ahaṅkāraḥ śarīra-vācaka-śabda-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt śarīre ahaṅkāraḥ
025.20	gauraḥ aham iti ādi/ icchā-ādi-ādhāraḥ ātman^ḥ na śarīra-ādyaḥ  iti viśeṣaḥ/
026.01	etad ca upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ/ saḥ ayam ātma-dharmān icchā-ādīn śarīra-ādiṣu
026.02	adhyāropya viparyeti aham ete iti/ evam sāmānya-viśeṣa-parijñāne sati
026.03	tad-viparīta-dharma-adhyāropena   viparyayaḥ sarvatra bhavati/ kaḥ punar ayam viparyayaḥ
026.04	atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ te ime duḥkha-ādayaḥ
026.05	mithyā-jñāna-paryavasānāḥ avicchedena   pravartamānāḥ saṃsāraḥ iti/ kaḥ
026.06	punar ayam saṃsāraḥ duḥkha-ādīnām kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ/ saḥ ca anādiḥ/ pūrva-
026.07	apara-kāla-aniyamāt/ na ca śakyam vaktum pūrvam duḥkha-ādyaḥ  paścāt mithyā-
026.08	jñāna-utpattiḥ iti pūrvam vā mithyā-jñānam paścāt duḥkha-ādyaḥ  iti na śakyam
026.09	vaktum iti/ yadā tu tattva-jñānāt mithyā-jñānam apaiti/ katham apāyaḥ samāna-
026.10	viṣaye tayoḥ virodhāt/ yasmāt mithyā-jñānam tattva-jñānam ca ekasmin
026.11	viṣaye vruddhyete vastunaḥ dvairūpya-asambhavāt na hi ekam vastu dvirūpam bhavati/
026.12	tasmāt mithyā-jñānam tattva-jñānena   nivartyate iti/ katham punar pūrva-utpannam
026.13	mithyā-jñānam paścāt utpadyamānena   tattva-jñānena   nivartyate mithyā-jñānasya
026.14	asahāya-tvāt mithyā-jñānam asahāyamataḥ nivartyate samyac-jñānasya ca
026.15	viṣayaḥ sahāyin^ḥ bhavati kasmāt tathātvena   avasthānāt tathā-bhūtaḥ asau
026.16	viṣayaḥ yathā tatra tattva-jñānam iti/ pramāṇa-antara-anugrahāt ca āgama-anumāna-
026.17	ādi-pramāṇam tattva-jñānasya sahāyaḥ bhavati/ yadā hi ayam anumāna-āgamayoḥ
026.18	pratisaṃhitayoḥ viṣayam bhāvayati samāhitaḥ ananya-manāḥ cintayati tataḥ
026.19	asya vipacyamāne dhyāne viviktāyām dhyāna-bhāvanāyām tasmin arthe tattva-
026.20	pratibodhin jñānam pratyakṣam utpadyate iti/ saḥ ayam āgama-anumāna-pratyakṣāṇām
026.21	viṣayam pratipadyamānaḥ tattvam etad iti pratipadyate/ tattva-pratipatti^aḥ   asya
027.01	mithyā-jñānam nivartate iti/ nivṛtte ca mithyā-jñāne na punar tad-viṣayam
027.02	mithyā-jñānam utpadyate iti virodhāt iti uktam/ kaḥ punar virodhaḥ
027.03	saha-asambhavaḥ mithyā-jñāna-abhāve rāga-ādyaḥ  apayanti iti kārya-kāraṇa-
027.04	bhāvāt eva kāraṇam mithyā-jñānam kāryāḥ rāga-ādyaḥ  na ca kāraṇam vinā
027.05	kāryam bhavitum arhati/ ye tāvat anutpannāḥ rāga-ādyaḥ  te kāraṇa-
027.06	abhāvāt mā bhūvan/ ye tu utpannāḥ te katham teṣām api vairāgyāt nivṛttiḥ/
027.07	kim punar vairāgyam bhoga-anabhiṣvaṅga-lakṣaṇam iti uktam asaktiḥ ca saktim bādhate
027.08	tad punar vairāgyam katham bhavati tattva-jñānāt viṣaya-doṣa-darśanāt iti/ doṣa-
027.09	abhāve pravṛtti-abhāvaḥ/ yadā asya samyac-jñānavataḥ doṣāḥ apayanti atha
027.10	doṣa-abhāve pravṛttiḥ nivartate iti/ kā punar iyam pravṛttiḥ/ dharma-adharmau
027.11	janma-sādhana-tvāt na asmin sūtre kriyā pravṛtti-śabdena   ucyate api tu
027.12	dharma-adharmau tad-sādhana-bhāvāt/ janma-sādhanam dharma-adharmau na pravṛttiḥ
027.13	kriyāyāḥ kṣaṇika-tvāt/ dharma-adharmayoḥ tu pravṛtti-śabdaḥ pravṛtti-sādhana-
027.14	tvāt iti/ yau tāvat anāgatau dharma-adharmau tayoḥ doṣa-abhāvāt abhāvah yuktaḥ/
027.15	yau tau vartamānau tayoḥ na yuktaḥ kim kāraṇam nirdoṣasya api bhāvāt
027.16	nirdoṣaḥ api ayam avatiṣṭhate dharma-adharmayoḥ ca phalam bhuṅkte iti/ na anāgatayoḥ
027.17	sādhana-abhāvena   iṣṭa-tvāt/ na brūmaḥ yau vartamānau dharma-adharmau tau doṣa-
027.18	abhāvāt na bhavataḥ iti/ api tu yau anāgatau tau kāraṇa-abhāvāt na
027.19	utpadyete iti/ vartamānayoḥ punar kārya-avasānāt prakṣayaḥ yau tau
027.20	vartamānau dharma-adharmau tayoḥ yad āvasānikam kāryam tena vyāvṛttiḥ pravṛtti-
027.21	abhāve janma-apāyaḥ atra api dharma-adharmayoḥ vyāvṛttayoḥ yad anyat śarīram tad na
027.22	bhavati na punar vartamānam na bhavati iti/ atha vartamānasya katham nivṛttiḥ
028.01	saṃskāra-kṣayāt yāvat asya sthiti-hetuḥ saṃskāraḥ dharma-adharma-samākhyātaḥ
028.02	avatiṣṭhate tāvat avatiṣṭhate iti/ janma-abhāve duḥkha-abhāvaḥ iti nirāyatanasya
028.03	anutpatti^aḥ   na hi nirāyatanam duḥkham utpattum arhati/ etad ca tad
028.04	āhuḥ yāvat ātmani dharma-adharmau tāvat āyuḥ śarīram indriyam viṣayāḥ ca iti
028.05	saḥ ayam mithyā-jñāna-ādi-kalāpena   sarvataḥ viyuktaḥ vimuktaḥ iti ucyate/
028.06	tattva-jñānam tu khalu mithyā-jñāna-viparyayena   vyākhyātam/ svarūpataḥ tu yathā
028.07	vyavasthita-padārtha-adhigatiḥ iti ucyate/ kasmāt punar ayam hātṛ^ḥ sukha-duḥkhe
028.08	jahāti na punar sukham ādāya duḥkham jahāti iti/ viveka-hānasya
028.09	aśakya-tvāt/ na hi viveka-hānam śakyam kartum ataḥ sukham upabhoktu-kāmena
028.10	duḥkham api bhoktavyam/ duḥkham vā jihāsatā sukham api hātavyam saviṣa-anna-
028.11	vat iti sukham duḥkha-anuṣaktam anādeyam iti/ anuṣaṅgaḥ avinābhāvaḥ yatra ekam
028.12	tatra itarad iti/ samāna-nimitta-tā vā anuṣaṅgaḥ yāni eva sukha-sādhanāni
028.13	tāni eva duḥkha-sādhanāni iti/ samāna-ādhāra-tā vā anuṣaṅgaḥ yatra sukham tatra
028.14	duḥkham iti/ samāna-upalabhya-tā vā anuṣaṅgaḥ yena sukham upalabhyate tena
028.15	duḥkham api iti//
028.16	trividhā ca asya śāstrasya pravṛttiḥ iti bhāṣyam/ pravṛtti^aḥ   traividhyam
028.17	kutaḥ arthasya tathā-bhāvāt na amī padārthāḥ vaktṛā tredhā vyavasthāpyante
028.18	evam bhavata iti/ kim tu artham evam ayam tathā-bhūtaḥ yena tredhā bhavati iti/
028.19	nāma-dheyena padārtha-mātra-abhidhānam uddeśaḥ iti ayuktam ghrāṇa-ādi-sūtra-vyāghātāt/
028.20	ghrāṇa-rasana-cakṣus-tvak-śrotrāṇi indriyāṇi iti lakṣaṇam uktam/ na ca idam nāma-
029.01	dheyena   uddheśa-mātrāt bhidhyate mātra-grahaṇa-sāmarthyāt aprasaṅgaḥ padārtha-mātra-abhidhānam
029.02	iti uktam na ca ghrāṇa-ādīnām padārtha-abhidhāna-mātra-tvam kāraka-śabda-tvāt
029.03	kāraka-śabdāḥ ete ghrāṇa-ādi iti tasmāt na uddeśa-prasaṅgaḥ/ uddiṣṭasya vibhāgaḥ
029.04	dvedhā bhavati/ lakṣitasya alakṣitasya/ lakṣitasya chala-ādi^aḥ   alakṣitasya
029.05	pramāṇa-ādi^aḥ   iti/ atha uddiṣṭa-vibhāga-dvāreṇa sūtram/ pratyakṣa-anumāna-
029.06	upamāna-śabdāḥ pramāṇāni iti//
029.07	[1-1-3]pratyakṣa-anumāna-upamāna-śabdāḥ pramāṇāni
029.08	sūtra-arthaḥ nigadena   eva vyākhyātaḥ/ uddiṣṭa-vibhāga-anarthakyam/ vyāghātāt/
029.09	trividhā ca asya śāstrasya pravṛttiḥ iti uktam uddiṣṭa-vibhāgaḥ ca na
029.10	trividhāyām śāstra-pravṛtti^i  antarbhavati iti/ tasmāt uddiṣṭa-vibhāgaḥ na
029.11	yuktaḥ/ na/ uddiṣṭa-vibhāgasya uddeśe eva antarbhāvāt/ uddiṣṭa-vibhāgaḥ
029.12	uddeśe eva antarbhavati iti/ kasmāt/ lakṣaṇa-sāmānyāt/ samānam lakṣaṇam
029.13	nāma-dheyena   padārtha-abhidhānam uddeśaḥ iti/ kim punar vibhāgena   prayojanam/
029.14	niyamaḥ/ yadi pratyakṣa-anumāna-upamāna-śabdāḥ pramāṇāni iti vibhāgaḥ na
029.15	kriyeta niyamaḥ na gamyeta catvāri eva poramāṇāni iti/ lakṣaṇataḥ catuṣṭva-
029.16	adhigatiḥ iti cet/ na/ lakṣaṇasya itara-vyavaccheda-hetu-tvāt/ syāt eṣā buddhiḥ
029.17	lakṣaṇataḥ pramāṇa-catuṣṭvam gamyate iti/ yasmāt caturṇām lakṣaṇam uktam iti/
029.18	na/ lakṣaṇasya itara-vyavaccheda-hetu-tvāt/ lakṣaṇam khalu lakṣyam samāna-
029.19	asamāna-jātīya^bhyaḥ vyavacchinatti niyamam tu na śknoti kartum anya-artha-
030.01	tvāt iti/ anya-asambhavasya tataḥ anadhigati^aḥ  / na hi lakṣaṇataḥ anya-asambhavaḥ
030.02	adhigamyate/ tataḥ caturṇām anabhidhāne caturṇām lakṣaṇa-upadeśāt saṃśayaḥ
030.03	syāt/ kim vidyamānāni na lakṣitāni āhosvit avidyamānāni iti/
030.04	tasmāt saṃśaya-nivṛtti-artham yuktaḥ vibhāga-uddeśaḥ iti/ akṣasya akṣasya
030.05	prativiṣayam vṛttiḥ pratyakṣam iti/ ayam ca sūtra-vivakṣitāyām avyayībhāvaḥ
030.06	samāsaḥ/ anyathā tu vastu-nirdeśaḥ na samāsaḥ samāse hi akṣasya iti
030.07	ṣaṣṭhī na śrūyate/ kaḥ punar ayam samāsaḥ/ prādi-samāsaḥ ayam draṣṭavyaḥ prati
030.08	gatam akṣam pratyakṣam iti yathā upagataḥ gobhiḥ upaguḥ iti/ evam anumāna-ādiṣu
030.09	draṣṭavyam/ matena   liṅgena   liṅginoḥ arthasya paścāt mānam anumānam iti
030.10	na yuktam/ phala-abhāvāt/ etasmin vyākhyāne aphalam anumānam
030.11	iti/ kim kāraṇam/ arthasya mita-tvāt/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/ mitena
030.12	liṅgena   arthasya paścāt mānam bhavati yataḥ iti arthaḥ/ bhavatu vā ayam
030.13	arthaḥ lauṅgikī pratipattiḥ anumānam iti/ nanu ca phala-abhāvaḥ doṣaḥ
030.14	uktaḥ/ na doṣaḥ/ hāna-upādāna-upekṣā-buddhīnām phala-tvāt/ sarvam ca
030.15	pramāṇam sva-viṣayam prati bhāva-sādhanam pramitiḥ pramāṇam iti/ viṣaya-
030.16	antaram prati karaṇa-sādhanam pramīyate anena iti pramāṇam/ yadi
030.17	bhāva-sādhanaḥ pramāṇa-śabdaḥ kim phalam viṣayasya adhigata-tvāt/ uktam phalam
030.18	hāna-ādi-buddhyaḥ  iti/ jñāte tad-bhāvāt/ jñāte khalu arthe tridhā
030.19	buddhiḥ bhavati heyaḥ vā upādeyaḥ vā upekṣaṇīyaḥ vā iti/ ke cit tu sannikarṣam
030.20	eva pratyakṣam varṇayanti/ na tad nyāyam pramāṇa-abhāvāt/ sannikarṣaḥ eva
030.21	pramāṇam iti na pramāṇam asti/ ubhayam tu yuktam paricchedaka-tvāt/
030.22	ubhayam paricchedakam sannikarṣaḥ jñānam ca/ ekānta-vādinaḥ tu doṣaḥ iti/
031.01	sāmīpyamānam upamānam iti na yuktam/ upamānasya anyathā-vyākhyānāt/
031.02	samākhyā-sambandha-pratipattiḥ upamāna-arthaḥ iti uktam idānīm
031.03	tu ucyate sāmīpyamānam upamānam iti vyāghātaḥ na asti vyāghātaḥ sāmīpyamānena
031.04	samākhyā-sambandha-pratipatti^aḥ   iṣṭa-tvāt/ yasmāt ayam upayukta-upamānaḥ
031.05	go-darśin^ḥ āgama-pratyaya-āhita-smṛti-pūrvakam gavā sādṛśyam pratyakṣena   pratipadya
031.06	asya gavaya-śabdaḥ saṃjñā iti saṃjñā-saṃjñi-sambandham pratipadyate tasmāt na vyāghātaḥ
031.07	iti/ śabda-viṣayā pratipattiḥ śābda-pramāṇam phalam tad eva/ ke cit tu krama-
031.08	prayojanam varṇayanti/ ādi^i  pratyakṣa-grahaṇam prādhānyāt/ tad-anantaram
031.09	anumānam tad-pūrvaka-tvāt/ tad-anantaram upamānam tad-sādharmyāt/ ante śabdaḥ
031.10	mahā-viṣaya-tvāt/ tad ca ayuktam iti apare manyante/ krama-abhidhānasya nyāya-
031.11	prāpta-tvāt/ nyāya-prāptam krama-abhidhānam krama-vṛtti-tvāt vācaḥ iti/ mahā-
031.12	viṣaya-tvāt ca śabdasya ādi^i  abhidhāna-prasaṅgaḥ iti/ etad ca ayuktam/ krama-
031.13	vṛtti-tvāt vācaḥ ayugapad-abhidhānam yuktam pūrva-apara-abhidhāne tu na sādhanam
031.14	etad/ tasmāt anyaḥ nyāyaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ sa ca ayam nyāyaḥ pratyakṣam pūrvam
031.15	prādhānyāt iti/ mahā-viṣaya-tvāt ādi^i  śabda-upadeśah iti/ na/ ubhayam
031.16	mahā-viṣayam śabdaḥ pratyakṣam ca/ katham iti/ pratyakṣena   api sāmānya-
031.17	viśeṣa-tadvatām grahaṇam śabdena   api/ tatra kim śabdasya ādi^i  upadeśaḥ bhavatu/
031.18	āhosvit pratyakṣasya iti/ pratyakṣasya iti yuktam/ kim kāraṇam/ sarva-
031.19	pramāṇānām pratyakṣa-pūrvaka-tvāt iti/ kim punar etāni pramāṇāni saṃplavante
031.20	atha pratiprameyam vyavatiṣṭhante iti/ ubhayathā darśanam tad ca pramāṇataḥ
032.01	iti atra eva darśitam/ sā iyam pramitiḥ/ pratyakṣa-parā iti/ pratyakṣena   adhigate arthe
032.02	ākāṅkṣa-abhāvāt tad-parā/ yathā ayam laukikaḥ amutra agniḥ iti āpta-
032.03	upadeśāt agnim pratyāhita-pratyayaḥ tam deśam gacchati/ pratyāsīdat punar dhūma-
032.04	viśeṣaṇam dhūma-aṅga-tvena   vyavasthitam huta-bhujam pratipadyate/ āsannataraḥ tu
032.05	idānīm indriya-artha-sannikarśāt agni-pratyayam karoti tadā nirākāṅkṣaḥ bhavati
032.06	iti ataḥ pradhānam pratyakṣam iti/ yatra saṃplavaḥ tatra evam/ yatra punar vyavasthā tatra
032.07	guṇa-pradhānatā na cintyate iti/ iti tri-sūtrī-vārttikam//
032.08	atha vibhaktānām lakṣaṇa-vacanam iti tatra pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam ucyate/
032.09	indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti ādi sūtram
032.10	[1-1-4]indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam avyapadeśyam avyabhicārin
032.11	vyavasāya-ātmakam pratyakṣam
032.12	sūtra-arthaḥ samāna-asamāna-jātīya-viśeṣaka-tvam/ atha padānām arthaḥ
032.13	kaḥ/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti/ indriyena   arthasya sannikarṣāt yad
032.14	utpadyate jñānam tad pratyakṣam iti/ indriyāṇi punar vakṣyamāṇāni/
032.15	arthaḥ ca/ sannikarṣaḥ punar ṣoḍhā bhidyate/ saṃyogaḥ saṃyukta-samavāyaḥ saṃyukta-
032.16	samaveta-samavāyaḥ samavāyaḥ samaveta-samavāyaḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvaḥ ca iti/
032.17	tatra cakṣus indriyam rūpavān ghaṭa-adiḥ arthaḥ/ tena sannikarṣaḥ saṃyogaḥ tayoḥ
032.18	dravya-svabhāva-tvāt/ adravyena   ca tad-gata-rūpa-ādinā saṃyukta-samavāyaḥ/
032.19	yasmāt cakṣuṣā saṃyukte dravye rūpa-ādi vartate iti/ vṛttiḥ tu samavāyaḥ/
032.20	rūpa-ādi-vṛttinā sāmānyena   saṃyukta-samaveta-samavāyaḥ sannikarṣaḥ/ evam
033.01	ghrāṇa-ādiṣu gandhavat-ādi-dravyena   saṃyogaḥ tad-samaveta^su gandha-ādiṣu saṃyukta-
033.02	samavāyaḥ tad-vṛttiṣu ca sāmānya-ādiṣu saṃyukta-samaveta-samavāyaḥ/ śabde
033.03	samavāyaḥ/ ayam khalu śabdaḥ saṃyoga-vibhāga-yoniḥ ādyaḥ tatra katamaḥ
033.04	śabdaḥ saṃyoga-yoniḥ katamaḥ ca vibhāga-yoniḥ iti/ ākāśa-guṇa-tvāt
033.05	śabdasya ākāśa-vṛttiḥ saṃyogaḥ kāraṇam guṇa-karmasu ārabdhavya^su sāpekṣaḥ iti
033.06	sāpekṣaḥ na nirapekṣaḥ iti/ kim apekṣate/ bherī-ākāśa-saṃyogaḥ tāvat
033.07	śabdasya kāraṇam tasya anugrāhakaḥ bherī-daṇḍa-saṃyogaḥ daṇḍa-gata-vega-apekṣaḥ
033.08	iti/ yadi punar bherī-daṇḍa-saṃyogaḥ eva śabdasya kāraṇam syāt vyadhikaraṇaḥ
033.09	saṃyogaḥ śabda-kāraṇam iti sarvatra utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ/ vibhāgāt śabdaḥ
033.10	vaṃśa-dala-vibhāga-anugṛhītāt vaṃśa-dala-ākāśa-vibhāgāt śabdaḥ iti/ saḥ
033.11	utpannaḥ śabdaḥ sarva-diśkāni śabda-antarāṇi karoti tāni api pratyekam
033.12	śabda-antarāṇi tāvat yāvat karṇa-śaṣkulī-mati ākāśa-deśe iti/ yaḥ
033.13	karṇa-śaṣkulīmati ākāśa-deśe samavaiti saḥ samavāyāt upalabhyate tad-gata^su
033.14	ca sāmānya^su samaveta-samavāyāt/ samavāye ca abhāve ca viśeṣaṇa-
033.15	viśeṣya-bhāvāt iti/ saḥ ayam sannikarṣa-śabdaḥ saṃyoga-samavāya-viśeṣaṇa-
033.16	viśeṣya-bhāva-vyāpaka-tvāt upāttaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam sannikarṣaḥ pratyakṣasya kāraṇam
033.17	bhavati iti lakṣaṇa-tvena   ucyate/ yadi indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ pratyakṣasya kāraṇa-
033.18	tvāt upadiśyate atyalpam idam ucyate anyāni api bahūni santi tāni api vaktavyāni/
033.19	tad yathā/ ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ indriya-manas-saṃyogaḥ viṣaya-prakāśa-saṃyogaḥ
033.20	viṣaya-stham rūpam viṣaya-saṃyogi-stham ca mahattvam aneka-dravyavattvam upalabdhi-
033.21	phalaḥ saṃskāraḥ iti/ kasmāt tad-bhāve bhāvāt tad-abhāve ca abhāvāt iti/
034.01	tad yadi kāraṇa-bhāvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-grahaṇam etāni api kāraṇāni
034.02	vaktavyāni/ na vaktavyāni/ na idam kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham sūtram api tu
034.03	samāna-asamāna-jātīya-viśeṣaṇa-artham/ yad pratyakṣasya asādhāraṇam kāraṇam
034.04	tad abhidhīyate na punar sādhāraṇam kāraṇam nivartyate iti/ indriya-manas-
034.05	saṃyogaḥ tarhi asādharaṇa-tvāt apasaṃkhyeyaḥ na hi ayam anumāna-ādi-jñānānām kāraṇam
034.06	bhavati/ na anena eva tasya ukta-tvāt/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-grahaṇena   indriya-manas-
034.07	saṃyogaḥ uktaḥ veditavyaḥ/ kim kāraṇam ubhayoḥ asādhāraṇa-tvāt na ca
034.08	yāvat asādhāraṇam kāraṇam tāvat sarvam abhidhātavyam iti arthaḥ/ api tu anyatarena
034.09	api bhidyate iti anyatara-upādānam/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-grahaṇam
034.10	vā viśeṣaka-tvāt/ yad indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt upajāyate vijñānam tasya
034.11	anyatarat viśeṣakam bhavati/ indriyam arthaḥ vā tad-viśeṣakam tena vyapadeśāt
034.12	yasmāt idam jñānam indriyena   vā vyapadiśyate arthena   vā rūpa-vijñānam
034.13	iti cakṣus-vijñānam iti vā/ na punar indriya-manas-saṃyogena   vyapadiśyate
034.14	na hi bhavati tu upālambane jñāne manas-jñānam iti/ yadā tu ātma-manas-
034.15	saṃyogāt mānasyaḥ buddhyaḥ  pravartante tadā ātmanā vyapadiśyante manasā ca/
034.16	yad ca asādhāraṇam tad vyapadeśa-bhāj bhavati/ tad yathā/ ṛtu-ādi-kāraṇa-
034.17	sannidhānāt prādurbhavan aṅkuraḥ nartu-ādibhiḥ vyapadiśyate api tu asādhāraṇena
034.18	bījena   vyapadiśyate yava-aṅkuraḥ iti tathā iha api iti adoṣaḥ/ indriya-
034.19	manas-saṃyogasya vā agrahaṇam bhede abhedāt/ yasmāt pratyakṣa-jñāna-bhede indriya-
034.20	manas-saṃyogaḥ na bhidyate iti/ yadi abhedāt agrahaṇam indriya-artha-sannikarṣasya
034.21	api tarhi agrahaṇam/ eka-indriya-grāhya^su abhedāt iti prāptam yathā śuklaḥ
034.22	go^ḥ gacchati iti/ na vaktavyam/ ukta-uttara-tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etad/ na idam
035.01	kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham sūtram iti/ anabhyupagamāt ca/ indriya-manas-saṃyogasya
035.02	vā agrahaṇam samāna-tvāt/ kena samāna-tvāt iti vācyam/ ātma-manas-
035.03	saṃyogena   samāna-tvāt/ kim punar tad vyapadeśa-abhāvaḥ iti uktam/ atīindriya-
035.04	ādhāra-tā vā sāmānyam yathā ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ atīndriya-ādhāraḥ evam
035.05	indriya-manas-saṃyogaḥ api iti/ viṣaya-avṛtti-tvam vā/ yathā ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ
035.06	viṣaya-avṛttiḥ tathā indriya-manas-saṃyogaḥ api iti/ manas-vṛtti-tvam vā/ yathā
035.07	vā ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ manas-vṛttiḥ evam indriya-manas-saṃyogaḥ api iti/ tasmāt
035.08	indriya-manas-saṃyogasya ātma-manas-saṃyogena   sāmānyāt anyatara-abhidhānena   vā
035.09	carita-artha-tvāt anabhidhānam iti/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti ayuktam/
035.10	indriyasya aprāpya-kāri-tvāt/ aprāpya-kāriṇī cakṣus-śrotre iti eke/
035.11	tatra ca nyāyam bruvate/ aprāpya-kārin cakṣus sāntara-grahaṇāt pṛthutara-
035.12	grahaṇāt ca iti/ sāntarasya arthasya viprakṛṣṭa-deśa-avasthitasya grahaṇam dṛṣṭam/
035.13	na ca cakṣuṣaḥ tena arthena   prāptiḥ asti/ bhūta-viśeṣasya indriya-bhāvāt/ yaḥ
035.14	eva ayam kṛṣṇa-sāra-lakṣaṇaḥ bhūta-viśeṣaḥ saḥ bāhya-bhūta-viśeṣa-prasāda-anugṛhītaḥ
035.15	tad-tṛṣṇā-pūrvaka-karma-apekṣaḥ cakṣus iti ucyate/ tathā ca uktam/ rūpa-upalabdhi-
035.16	saṃvartakena   karmaṇā tad-tṛṣṇā-pūrvakena   cakṣus abhinirvṛttamataḥ rūpa-upalabdhi^aḥ
035.17	kāraṇam bhavati iti/ evam śeṣa^su/ na ca bhūta-viśeṣa-anugṛhītasya golakasya
035.18	prāptiḥ viṣayena   asti/ tasmāt sāntara-grahaṇāt aprāpya-kārin iti/ apare
035.19	tu sāntara-grahaṇam hetum varṇayanti/ na hi prāpya-kāriṣu ghrāṇa-ādiṣu sāntaraḥ
035.20	iti grahaṇam dṛṣṭam dṛṣṭam tu cakṣuṣi/ pṛthutara-grahaṇāt ca rāṣṭra-vana-ādi-rūpam ca
035.21	mahat upalabhyate/ na cākṣṇoḥ tathā pṛthu-avabhāsaḥ iti yuktam/ diś-deśa-vyapadeśāt
035.22	ca yadi prāpya-kārin cakṣus bhavet diś-deśa-vyapadeśah na syāt na hi
036.01	prāpya-kāriṣu ghrāṇa-ādiṣu tad bhavati sannikṛṣṭa-viprakṛṣṭayoḥ tulya-kāla-grahaṇāt
036.02	ca/ yad khalu gatimat bhavati tad tām gatim abhindat sannikṛṣṭam āśu
036.03	prāpnoti viprakṛṣṭam cirena  / śākhā-candramasoḥ tulya-kāla-grahaṇam dṛṣṭam
036.04	tasmāt aprāpya-kārin iti/ yad tāvat sāntara-grahaṇāt iti/ tad ayuktam
036.05	vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ   sāntara-grahaṇam iti kaḥ arthaḥ kim tāvat sāntarasya aprāptasya
036.06	grahaṇam iti ayam arthaḥ/ āhosvit saha antarena   grahaṇam sāntara-grahaṇam iti/
036.07	astu tāvat pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ aprāptasya grahaṇam sāntara-grahaṇam iti/ nanu ayam
036.08	pratijñā-arthaḥ eva tataḥ ca hetu-abhāvaḥ kim kāraṇam hetu-arthasya pratijñā-arthena
036.09	ākṣipta-tvāt/ etad uktam bhavati/ aprāpya-kārin cakṣus aprāpya-grahaṇāt iti
036.10	na pratijñā-arthāt bhidyate/ atha brūṣe saha antarena   grahaṇam iti/ kim
036.11	tad-antaram nāma yad cakṣuṣā viṣayena   saha upalabhyate iti/ kim ākāśam
036.12	abhāvaḥ dravya-antaram vā/ yadi ākāśam tad na cakṣuṣaḥ viṣayaḥ/ na hi ākāśam
036.13	cakṣuṣā gṛhyate arūpi-tvāt vāyu-ādi-vat/ atha rūpa-dravyam antara-śabda-
036.14	vācyam tasya vyavadhāyaka-tvāt tena saha grahaṇam upapannam/ atha abhāvaḥ
036.15	antara-śabda-vācyaḥ saḥ svatantraḥ cakṣus-viṣayaḥ na bhavati iti tena saha upalabdhau
036.16	anaikāntikam/ na ca anyā gatiḥ asti/ tasmāt śūnyam abhidhānam sāntara-
036.17	grahaṇāt iti yaiḥ api vidvisyamānaiḥ sāntara-grahaṇāt iti asya sāntaraḥ
036.18	iti grahaṇam iti etad vyākhyānam kriyate/ tad api ayuktam/ sāntaraḥ iti
036.19	grahaṇasya anya-nimitta-tvāt/ anyathā etad sāntaraḥ iti grahaṇam bhavati/
036.20	śarīra-avadhi-nimitta-tvāt/ śarīram avadhim kṛtvā sāntara-nirantare bhavataḥ
036.21	na punar indriya-prāpti-aprāpti-nimitte bhavataḥ yatra śarīram indriyam ca ubhayam
036.22	arthena   sambadhyate tatra nirantaraḥ iti grahaṇam bhavati/ yatra punar indriya-
037.01	mātram sambadhyate tatra sāntaraḥ iti/ tasmāt sāntaraḥ iti grahaṇasya anya-
037.02	nimitta-tvāt na sāntaram iti grahaṇāt aprāpya-kāri-tā sidhyati iti/ yad api
037.03	pṛthutara-grahaṇāt it tad api ayuktam/ sambandha-mātrena   mahat-aṇu^oḥ grahaṇāt/
037.04	sambandha-mātrena   eva mahati vāṇi^i  ca viṣaya-bheda-anuvidhāyin^ḥ pratyayaḥ upajāyate
037.05	tasmāt adeśyam etad api iti/ yad punar etad uktam diś-deśa-vyapadeśāt iti
037.06	tad api śarīra-avadhi-nimitta-tvāt pratyuktam/ yatra indiryam śarīram ca arthena
037.07	sambadhyate tatra diś-deśa-vyapadeśaḥ na bhavati/ dūra-antika-anuvidhānam vā/
037.08	yatra tu indriyam eva kevaolam sambadhyate tatra śarīram avadhim kṛtvā saṃyukta-saṃyoga-
037.09	alpīyastvam bhūyastvam vā apekṣyamāṇasya diś-deśa-pratyayāḥ sannikṛṣṭa-viprakṛṣṭa-
037.10	pratyayāḥ ca bhavanti/ yad punar etad śākhā-candramasoḥ tulya-kāla-grahaṇāt
037.11	iti/ tad api na/ anabhyupagamāt/ kaḥ hi svastha-ātman^ḥ śākhā-
037.12	candramasoḥ tulya-kāla-grahaṇam pratipadyate kāla-bheda-agrahaṇāt mithyā-pratyayaḥ eṣaḥ
037.13	utpala-dala-śata-vyatibheda-vat iti/ katham punar avagamyate kāla-bheda-agrahaṇa-
037.14	nimittaḥ eṣaḥ yugapad-pratyayaḥ na punar eka-kālaḥ eva/ idam anumānam āvaraṇa-
037.15	anupapatti^aḥ   iti/ yadi aprāpya-kārin cakṣus bhavati na kuḍya-kaṭa-ādi^aḥ   āvaraṇasya
037.16	sāmarthyam asti iti āvaraṇa-anupapttiḥ syāt na ca vyavahita-artha-upalabdhiḥ asti
037.17	tasmāt na aprāpya-kārin iti/ dūra-antika-anuvidhānam ca anupalabdhi-upalabdhi^oḥ na
037.18	syāt/ aprāpya cakṣus artham gṛhṇāti iti/ yad idam asya dūre agrahaṇam
037.19	antike ca grahaṇam ubhayam etad na syāt/ dṛṣṭam tu tasmāt na aprāpya-kārin iti
037.20	viṣaya-bhāvāt iti cet/ na/ sambandham antareṇa viṣaya-bhāva-anabhyupagamāt/
037.21	atha api idam syāt/ yaḥ cakṣuṣoḥ viṣaya-bhavati arthaḥ saḥ upalabhyate/
037.22	yaḥ tu na bhavati na asau upalabhyate iti/ na ca vyavahitānām
038.01	dūra-avasthitānām vā arthānām cakṣuṣaḥ viṣaya-bhāvah asti tasmāt na te
038.02	gṛhyante iti/ tad ca na evam sambandham antareṇa viṣaya-bhāva-anabhyupagamāt
038.03	kaḥ sambandha-vyatirekena   viṣaya-bhāvaḥ kevalam bhavataḥ saṃjñā-mātram
038.04	bhidyate na arthaḥ iti/ mayā ucyate sambandhaḥ iti bhavatā abhidhīyate viṣaya-
038.05	bhāvaḥ iti na kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ iti/ atha prāpya-kāri-tve cakṣuṣaḥ kim
038.06	pramāṇam/ indriya-tvam eva/ prāpya-kārin cakṣus indriya-tvāt/ ghrāṇa-ādi-vat/
038.07	ghrāṇa-ādi-indriyam prāpta-kārin dṛṣṭam tathā ca cakṣus tasmāt prāpya-kārin iti/
038.08	atha punar na kim cit indriyam prāpya-kārin pratipadyate tadā sarvāṇi
038.09	pakṣa^kṛtya karaṇa-tvāt iti vācyam/ karaṇam vāśī-ādi prāpya-kārin
038.10	dṛṣṭam tathā ca indriyāṇi tasmāt prāpya-kārīṇi iti/ atha punar vaiyātyāt
038.11	sarvān eva arthān aprāpya-kāriṇaḥ pratipadyate tadā katham saḥ api dṛṣṭa-sāmarthyānām
038.12	kāraṇānām sarvatra kārya-anutpattyā pratyavastheyaḥ yadi khalu etāni kāraṇāni
038.13	paraspara-prāpti-anapekṣāṇi svayam anupajāta-śaktikān kāryam ārabhante
038.14	iti kasmāt kāryam sarvatra na bhavati iti vaktavyam ataḥ na kāraṇam
038.15	aprāpya-kārin asti iti/ aneka-daṇḍa-cakra-ādi-udāharaṇam loke iti/ tasmāt vyavasthitam
038.16	etad indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam iti//
038.17	atha jñāna-grahaṇam kim artham sukha-ādi-vyavaccheda-artham/ indriya-artha-
038.18	sannikarṣāt sukha-duḥkhe api bhavataḥ tad-vyudāsa-artham āha jñānam iti/
038.19	tad ca indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam viṣaya-nāma-dheyena   abhidhīyate iti
038.20	bhāṣyam/ tad-pratiṣedha-artham āha avyapadeśyam iti/ yad idam anupayukta-
038.21	śabda-artha-sambandhasya viṣaya-bheda-anuvidhāyin vijñānam tad pratyakṣam kṛta-śabda-artha-
038.22	sambandhasya api tad-utpatti-kāle tādṛś eva/ apare tu avyapadeśyam iti anena anumānam
039.01	nirākurvanti/ tad ca na evam/ kasmāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam
039.02	iti vacanāt na hi anumeyasya indriyena   sannikarṣāt anumānam bhavati/ ataḥ
039.03	na prasaṅgaḥ anumāne iti/ grīṣme marīcyaḥ  iti bhāṣyam/ tad-pratiṣedha-
039.04	artham idam ucyate avyabhicārin iti/ kim idam vyabhicārin iti/ yad atasmin tad
039.05	iti bhavati/ kim punar atra vyabhicārin kim arthaḥ āho jñānam iti/
039.06	eke tāvat varṇayanti/ arthasya vyabhicāraḥ arthaḥ tu tathā na bhavati iti
039.07	tad-vyabhicārāt tad-viṣayam jñānam api vyabhicārin iti ucyate iti/ tad ca
039.08	na evam/ kasmāt arthasya tathā-bhāvāt/ yad tad udaka-ādi jñānam upajāyate
039.09	marīciṣu spandamāna^su na tatra arthaḥ vyabhicarati iti/ na hi tena
039.10	marīcyaḥ  na vā spandante kim tu jñānam vyabhicarati/ atasmin tad iti
039.11	bhāvāt iti/ na hi tatra udakam asti/ tān ca marīcīn indriya-upaghāta-
039.12	doṣāt indriyena   ālocya viparyeti iti jñānasya vyabhicāraḥ na arthasya iti/
039.13	dūrāt cakṣuṣā artham paścan na avadhārayati iti bhāṣyam tad-vyudāsa-artham āha vyavasāya-
039.14	ātmakam iti/ na/ saṃśayasya anindriya-artha-sannikarṣa-pūrvaka-tvāt/ na hi indriya-
039.15	artha-sannikarṣāt saṃśaya-jñānam bhavati kim tu mānasaḥ pratyayaḥ/ saṃśītiḥ
039.16	saṃśayaḥ iti/ tad ca na evam/ saṃśayasya ubhaya-nimitta-tvāt/ ubhayam tu
039.17	saṃśayasya nimittam ātma-antaḥkaraṇa-saṃyogaḥ indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ ca iti/
039.18	tatra yad indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-anavadhāraṇa-pūrvakam manasā anavadhāraṇam tad iha
039.19	sambadhyate/ tasya hi indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ kāraṇam/ pūrvam tu na sambadhyate/
039.20	yad ātma-antaḥkaraṇa-saṃyogāt bhavati iti/ tasmāt asti viṣayaḥ viśeṣaṇasya iti
039.21	yuktam vyavasāya-ātmakam iti/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti evam ādi-lakṣaṇam
039.22	ātma-ādiṣu sukha-ādiṣu ca na asti/ manasaḥ anindriya-tvāt avyāpakam etad
040.01	pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam iti/ katham punar indriyam manas na bhavati/ indriya-sūtre
040.02	apaṭhita-tvāt/ paripaṭhitāni ghrāṇa-ādīni indriyāṇi/ na ca teṣu manas
040.03	paṭhitam/ tasmāt manas na indriyam/ pṛthak ca anabhidhānāt na asti manasaḥ
040.04	indriya-tve pramāṇam tataḥ ca na indriyam manas na ca evam pratyakṣāḥ sukha-ādayaḥ
040.05	bhaviṣyanti iti/ pratyakṣāh ca ete na ānumānikāḥ liṅga-abhāvāt/ na hi
040.06	liṅgam antareṇa anumeya-arthaḥ gamyate/ na api anyat pramāṇam pratipādakam asti
040.07	sukha-ādīnām/ na ca teṣām anumeya-tvam/ na ca anyā gatiḥ asti/
040.08	tasmāt kartavyam sukha-ādīnām pratyakṣena   grahaṇa-upasaṃkhyānam iti/ kaḥ ca evam āha
040.09	na pratyakṣāḥ sukha-ādyaḥ  iti/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-janya-pratyakṣa-vādin^ḥ āha/
040.10	na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/ manasaḥ indriya-tvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam sukha-ādi-jñānam
040.11	iti/ yad tu sūtre anabhidhānam tad vaidharmyāt/ kim tad vaidharmyam sarva-
040.12	viṣayatva-asarva-viṣayatve/ sarva-viṣayam manas asarva-viṣayāṇi itarāṇi/ sarva-
040.13	viṣayam tu manas smṛti-kāraṇa-saṃyoga-ādhāra-tvāt ātmavat sukha-grāhaka-
040.14	saṃyoga-adhikaraṇa-tvāt samasta-indriya-adhiṣṭhātṛ-tvāt ca ātmavat/ bhautika-
040.15	abhautika-tvam tu na virodhāt/ na hi bhautikam manas na api abhautikam
040.16	iti/ kārya-dharmau etau bhautikatvam abhautikatvam ca/ na ca kāryam manas
040.17	tasmāt na bhautikam na api abhautikam iti/ śrotre ca asambhavaḥ yadi bhautikatva-
040.18	abhautikatva-lakṣaṇāt vaidharmyāt aparipāṭhaḥ sūtre manasaḥ śrotram api sūtre
040.19	na paṭhitavyam/ tarhi na hi śrotram bhautikam na api abhautikam iti/
040.20	svārthe pratyaya-vidhānam iti cet/ na/ pratyaya-vaiyarthyāt/ syāt eṣā buddhiḥ
040.21	svārthikaḥ eṣaḥ pratyayaḥ bhūtam eva bhautikam iti/ tad ca na/ pratyaya-vaiyarthyāt
040.22	na hi bhautikam iti anena kaḥ cit tad-hita-arthaḥ labhyate/ tasmāt
041.01	vyartham etad svārthe pratyaya-vidhānam iti/ yad punar etad pṛthak-abhidhānam
041.02	na asti iti/ na na asti yugapad-jñāna-anupapatti^aḥ   iti/ yugapad-jñāna-anutpattiḥ
041.03	manasaḥ liṅgam iti ucyate tena ca pratipāditam etad manasaḥ karaṇa-tvam
041.04	iti/ saguṇānām indriya-bhāvaḥ vaidharmyam iti etad api ayuktam/ śrotra-anabhidhāna-
041.05	prasaṅgāt eva/ tasmāt sarva-viṣayatva-asarva-viṣayatvam eva vaidharmyam iti etad
041.06	eva jyāyaḥ/ tantra-antara-samācārāt ca/ tantra-antare manas indriyam iti
041.07	paṭhyate/ tad ca iha na pratiṣidhyate/ apratiṣedhāt upāttam tad iti/ na
041.08	śeṣa-abhidhāna-vaiyarthyāt/ śeṣāṇi api indriyāṇi taiḥ paripaṭhitāni tasmāt
041.09	tāni api na vaktavyāni yadi apratiṣedhāt upādānam syāt iti/ na/ tantra-
041.10	yukti-anavabodhāt/ na bhavatā tantra-yuktiḥ parijñāyate/ para-matam apratiṣiddham
041.11	anumatam iti hi tantra-yuktiḥ na ca yasya sva-mata-parigrahaḥ
041.12	na asti tasya sva-matam para-matam vā bhidyate/ bhavatā ca para-mata-anurodhena
041.13	sarvam sva-matam nivāryate iti/ tad-nivāraṇāt sva-matam para-matam iti etad eva
041.14	na syāt/ tasmāt asti manas indriyam ca iti/ tad upapannam indriya-artha-
041.15	sannikarṣa-utpannam sukha-ādi-jñānam iti vyāpakam lakṣaṇam/ samastam asamastam
041.16	ca iti sandehaḥ/ kim idam pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam samastam āho asamastam iti
041.17	sandehaḥ kutaḥ ubhayathā darśanāt samastam lakṣaṇam bhavati vyastam ca/
041.18	iccha-prayatna-dveṣa-sukha-duḥkha-jñānāni ātmanaḥ liṅgam iti vyasta-lakṣaṇam ekaśaḥ
041.19	pratipādaka-tvāt tathāÊsamāna-aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ   iti evam ādi vyastam/ samastam
041.20	punar vacana-vighātaḥ artha-vikalpa-upapattyā chalam iti/ samastena   anena chalam
041.21	lakṣyate/ prasiddha-sādharmyāt sādhya-sādhanam upamānam iti ca/ lakṣaṇam ca idam
041.22	ataḥ sandehaḥ kim samastam uta asamastam iti/ samastam iti āha/ yasmāt
042.01	ekaśaḥ anumāna-sukha-śabda-viparyaya-saṃśaya-jñānāni nivartyante iti/ yadi
042.02	indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti ādi ekaśaḥ padam abhidhīyate/ yaḥ ayam ekena
042.03	padena   arthaḥ saṅgṛhītaḥ saḥ pratyakṣaḥ syāt iti tena pūrva-uktānām anumāna-
042.04	ādīnām pratyakṣatva-prasaṅgaḥ syāt iti/ evam dvayoḥ padayoḥ upādāne pada-
042.05	dvaya-upāttasya pratyakṣatva-prasaṅgaḥ/ evam trayāṇām padānām upādāne taiḥ
042.06	upāttasya pratyakṣatva-prasaṅgaḥ/ evam caturṇām padānām upādāne taiḥ upāttasya
042.07	pratyakṣatva-prasaṅgaḥ/ tasmāt eka-dvi-tri-catur-pada-paryudāsāt pañca-pada-parigrahena
042.08	triṃśat-kotīḥ vyudasya samastam lakṣaṇam iti ucyate/ tatra eka-pada-parigrahena   tāvat
042.09	pañcan koṭyaḥ  dvi-pada-parigrahena   daśan tri-pada-parigrahena   api daśan eva catur-pada-
042.10	parigrahena   pañcan/ ekatriṃśattamī punar samasta-parigrahena   upāttā iti/
042.11	kasmāt viśeṣa-vidhi-pratiṣedhayoḥ śeṣa-pratiṣedha-vidhi-abhyanujñā-viṣaya-tvāt/
042.12	ayam khalu viśeṣa-vidhiḥ pravartamānaḥ śeṣam pratiṣedhati yathā dakṣiṇena
042.13	akṣi^ṇā paśyati iti/ viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ ca śeṣa-abhyanujñā-viṣayaḥ bhavati/
042.14	yatha vāmena   akṣi^ṇā na paśyati iti/ tathā iha api ekadeśa-abhyupagame śeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ
042.15	śeṣa-vidhānam vā gamyate iti vyavasthitam pratyakṣam iti/ apare punar varṇayanti/
042.16	tataḥ arthāt vijñānam pratyakṣam iti/ tad na/ tataḥ arthāt iti yasya
042.17	arthasya yad vijñānam vyapadiśyate yadi tataḥ eva tad bhavati na artha-antarāt bhavati
042.18	tataḥ pratyakṣam/ etena anumāna-ādi-jñānam apakṣiptam bhavati/ na hi tataḥ
042.19	eva tad bhavati kim tarhi tataḥ ca anyataḥ ca tad bhavati/ tatra tāvat artha-grahaṇam na
042.20	kartavyam iti tataḥ vijñānam iti ucyamāne gamyate eva tad arthāt iti/
042.21	avadhāraṇa-arthaḥ artha-śabdaḥ iti cet/ syāt matiḥ eṣā avadhāraṇa-arthaḥ artha-śabdaḥ
042.22	bhaviṣyati iti/ yad uktam bhavati tataḥ eva iti tad uktam bhavati tataḥ arthāt
043.01	iti/ tad na yuktam eka-padasya avadhāraṇa-arthasya dṛṣṭa-tvāt/ ap-bhakṣaḥ iti
043.02	yathā/ etena anumāna-ādi-vyudāsaḥ api pratyuktaḥ/ yad punar tad saṃvṛti-
043.03	jñānam anena apakṣiptam iti tad etad na budhyāmahe katham tad apakṣiptam
043.04	iti/ yadi brūṣe rūpa-ādibhyaḥ utpannam jñānam ghaṭasya vyapadiśyate na tataḥ
043.05	bhaviṣyati iti apakṣiptam tad na yuktam/ na hi rūpa-ādibhyaḥ utpannam jñānam
043.06	ghaṭasya vyapadiśyate rūpa-ādibhyaḥ utpannam rūpa-ādīnām ghaṭa-ādibhyaḥ utpannam
043.07	jñānam ghaṭasya iti na prasaṅgaḥ/ atha api evam manorathaḥ na rūpa-ādibhyaḥ
043.08	vyatiriktāḥ paṭa-ādyaḥ  iti/ manas-modaka-upabhoga-mātram etad/ yathā
043.09	tad-vyatiriktāḥ ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  tathā upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ/ sarvam ca sva-viṣayāt
043.10	vijñānam bhavati iti tataḥ-grahaṇam anarthakam iti/ nanu ca mithyā-jñānam
043.11	atasmāt api bhavati/ na hi atasmāt tad bhavati iti kim tarhi atasmin tad bhavati
043.12	iti/ na bhavatā mithyā-jñānam api vyajñāyi/ tataḥ-grahaṇam artha-grahaṇam
043.13	ca antareṇa vijñāna-mātram avaśiṣyate/ tathā ca na lakṣaṇam uktam syāt/
043.14	sarvam ca jñānam pratyakṣam syāt/ yadi api etad sūtram yathāśruti bhavati tathā
043.15	api grāhya-grāhaka-jñānayoḥ ayugapad-bhāvāt jñānam apratyakṣam syāt/ nāśa-utpādau
043.16	samam iti cet/ tad ca na udāharaṇa-abhāvāt/ na hi samam nāśa-
043.17	utpāde kim cit udāharaṇam asti vinaṣṭaḥ ca arthaḥ pratyakṣaḥ iti syāt/ tulyam
043.18	iti cet syāt matiḥ bhavatām yugapad-avasthāne kim udāharaṇam iti/ tad ca
043.19	na evam ukta-uttara-tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etad/ sphaṭika-ādi-sūtre iti/ apare
044.01	tu manyante pratyakṣam kalpanā-apoḍham iti/ atha kā iyam kalpanā nāma
044.02	jāti-yojanā iti/ yad kila na nāmnā abhidhīyate na ca jāti-ādibhiḥ
044.03	vyapadiśyate viṣaya-svarūpa-anuvidhāyi-paricchedakam ātma-saṃvedyam tad partyakṣam
044.04	iti/ te idam praṣṭavyāḥ/ atha pratyakṣa-śabdena   kaḥ arthaḥ abhidhīyate iti
044.05	yadi pratyakṣam katham avācyam/ atha na pratyakṣam avācakaḥ tarhi pratyakṣa-śabdaḥ
044.06	atha pratyakṣa-śabdena sāmānyam ucyate iti/ etad api sāmānyam kim pratyakṣa-
044.07	vyatirekin āhosvit avyatirekin iti/ yadi pratyakṣa-vyatirekin na partyakṣam
044.08	uktam atha avyatirekin katham na uktam/ kalpanā-apoḍha-śabdena   api yadi
044.09	pratyakṣam ucyate tada vyāghātaḥ atha na ucyate tathā api kalpanā-apoḍha-vacanam
044.10	vyartham pratyakṣam kalopanā-apoḍham iti na vākyam/ atha asya vākyasya kaḥ arthaḥ
044.11	yadi pratyakṣam vyāghātaḥ katham pratyakṣam kalpanā-apoḍham iti ca anena vākyena
044.12	abhidhīyate na ca abhidheyam iti kaḥ anyaḥ bhadantāt vaktum arhati/ atha na
044.13	pratyakṣam asya arthaḥ varṇa-uccāraṇa-mātram tarhi etad vākyam pratyakṣam kalpanā-apoḍham iti/
044.14	anitya-ādi-śabda-viṣaya-tvāt ca na sarvatha avācyam/ anityam pratyakṣam duḥkha-śūnyam
044.15	anātmakam ca pratyakṣam iti eṣām cet śabdānām viṣayatām upayāti katham
044.16	avācyam atha na upaiti sarvam saṃskṛtam anityam iti etad tathāgatena   na ākhyātam/
044.17	atha svarūpataḥ na vyapadeśyam iti eṣaḥ kalpanā-apoḍha-śabda-arthaḥ sarve arthāḥ
044.18	tarhi pratyakṣāḥ prāpnuvanti kim kāraṇam na hi kaḥ cit suśikṣitaḥ api
044.19	padārthānām svarūpam nirdeṣṭum śaknoti/ asamāyika-tvāt/ sarvasya ca
044.20	vastunaḥ dvau ākārau sāmānya-ākāraḥ viśeṣa-ākāraḥ ca/ tatra vastu sāmānyena
044.21	eva ākārena   abhidhīyate na viśeṣa-ākārena  / viśeṣa-anabhidhānāt na uktam
044.22	bhavati/ na hi manuṣya-śabdasya brāhmaṇaḥ na vācyaḥ/ ye tu tasya asādhāraṇa-
045.01	dharmāḥ puruṣa-antara-vyāvṛtta-pratyaya-hetu^āḥ na ca taiḥ saha anabhidhānāt
045.02	na uktaḥ bhavati/ evam jñānam api sāmānya-viśeṣa-ākāravat tasya
045.03	viśeṣa-ākārena   na abhidhānam sāmānya-ākārena   tu abhidhānam eva/ yadi ca
045.04	viśeṣa-ākārena   anabhidhānam yad tad lakṣaṇam pratyakṣasya na kevalam pratyakṣasya
045.05	trailokyasya etad lakṣaṇam iti/ evam pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam na uktam syāt/ atha kalpanā-
045.06	apoḍha-śabdena   pratyakṣasya svarūpam abhidhīyte/ evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ
045.07	svarūpam ca anabhidheyam iti anena śabdena   abhidhīyate iti/ atha anena
045.08	śabdena   na eva kim cid abhidhīyate/ kim asya śabdasya uccāraṇa-sāmarthyam pratyakṣam
045.09	kalpanā-apoḍham iti/ apratipādaka-tvāt mūka-svapna-sadṛśam etad/ evam
045.10	yathā yathā idam lakṣaṇam vicāryate tathā tathā nyāyam na sahate iti/ sat-samprayoge
045.11	puruṣasya indriyāṇām buddhi-janman tad pratyakṣam iti/ etad api
045.12	saṃśaya-ādi utpatti-nimitta-tvāt alakṣaṇam iti/ tathā ca uktam pratyakṣa-sūtram varṇayadbhiḥ
045.13	iti/ etena pratyakṣam indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-manas-prakāśa-viśeṣa^su satsu iti
045.14	pratyuktam/ tathā śrotra-ādi-vṛttiḥ iti/ kim kāraṇam pañca-pada-parigrahena
045.15	pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam uktam yatra anyatara-pada-parigrahaḥ na asti tad pratyakṣa-ābhāsam iti/
045.16	evam anena nyāyena   yāni api anuktāni lakṣaṇāni tāni etasmāt lakṣaṇāt na
045.17	bhidyamānāni na lakṣaṇāni iti vyākhyātam pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam iti//
045.18	[1-1-5]atha tad-pūrvakam trividham anumānam pūrvavat śeṣavat sāmānyatodṛṣṭam
045.19	ca
046.01	atha tapūrvakam trividham anumānam iti/ athety ānantarye/ anumāna-
046.02	viśeṣaṇa-artham sūtram tad-pūrvakam iti anena samāna-asamāna-jātīya^bhyaḥ anumānam
046.03	vyavacchidyate iti/ tāni te tad pūrvam yasya tad idam tad-pūrvakam/
046.04	yadā tāni iti vigrahaḥ tadā samasta-pramāṇa-abhisambandhāt sarva-pramāṇa-pūrvaka-
046.05	tvam anumānasya varṇitam bhavati/ pāramparyena   punar tad pratyakṣaḥ eva vyavatiṣṭhate
046.06	iti tad-pūrvaka-tvam uktam bhavati/ yadā api vivekāt te pūrve yasya iti
046.07	te dve pratyakṣe pūrve yasya pratyakṣasya tad idam tad-pūrvakam pratyakṣam iti/ te ca
046.08	dve pratyakṣe/ liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-darśanam ādyam pratyakṣam liṅga-darśanam dvitīyam/
046.09	bubhutsāvataḥ dvitīyāt liṅga-darśanāt saṃskāra-abhivyakti-uttara-kālam
046.10	smṛtiḥ smṛti-anantaram ca punar liṅga-darśanam ayam dhūmaḥ iti/ tad idam antimam
046.11	pratyakṣam pūrvābhyām pratyakṣābhyām smṛti^ā ca anugṛhyamāṇam parāmarśa-rūpam anumānam
046.12	bhavati/ kaḥ punar anumāna-arthaḥ/ anumīyate anena iti karaṇa-arthaḥ/ kim
046.13	punar asya phalam agni-viṣayā pratipattiḥ/ katham punar anya-viṣayam karaṇam anya-
046.14	viṣayām kriyām karoti/ śāli-ādi-viṣayasya muśala-ādi^aḥ   karaṇasya
046.15	śyāmāke abhihatiḥ bhavati/ na aniyamāt iti yad-viṣayam karaṇam tad-viṣayā
046.16	kriyā iti na niyamaḥ asti/ dṛṣṭā hi vṛkṣa-ādi-viṣayasya chedanasya avayava-
046.17	kriyā iti/ vṛkṣaḥ chidyate avayave kriyā iti/ kva cit punar yad-viṣayam karaṇam
046.18	tad-viṣayā eva kriyā/ tad yathā taṇḍulāḥ pacyante pākaḥ teṣu eva/ kva cit
046.19	punar ātman^ḥ eva bhavati kartṛ^ḥ karaṇam kriyā tasya eva/ tad yathā vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati iti/
046.20	vṛkṣaḥ ātmanā tiṣṭhati/ kim uktam bhavati ātmanā eva iti/ yasmāt ayam sva-sthitau
047.01	karaṇa-antaram na prayuṅkte/ evam anya-viṣayasya karaṇasya anya-viṣayā kriyā iti/
047.02	kva cit punar yad-viṣayam pramāṇam tad-viṣayā kriyā/ yadā sa eva arthaḥ
047.03	pramīyate iti/ kim punar tasya pramīyate pramita-tvāt/ pramīyate
047.04	heyatvena   upādeyatvena   apekṣaṇīyatvena   vā avagamyate iti/ tad na/ pramāṇa-
047.05	phalayoḥ viṣaya-bheda-anabhyupagamāt/ yadā punar tad-pūrvam yasya tad idam tad-pūrvakam
