047.06	iti tadā bhedasya avivakṣita-tvāt/ liṅga-liṅgisambandha-darśana-anantaram
047.07	liṅga-darśana-sambandha-smṛtibhiḥ liṅga-parāmarśaḥ viśiṣyate tasya tad-pūrvaka-
047.08	tvāt kim punar taiḥ anumīyate/ śeṣa-arthaḥ iti/ anumānam iti atra kim
047.09	kārakam bhāvaḥ karaṇam vā/ yadā bhāvaḥ tadā hāna-ādi-buddhyaḥ  phalam/
047.10	yadā karaṇam tadā śeṣa-vastu-paricchedaḥ phalam iti/ liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-
047.11	smṛtiḥ liṅga-darśanam vā iti sandehaḥ/ eke tāvat varṇayanti liṅga-liṅgi-
047.12	sambandha-smṛtiḥ anumānam iti/ itaraiḥ liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-darśana-ādhibhiḥ anugṛhyamānā/
047.13	apare tu manyante liṅga-parāmarśaḥ anumānam iti/ vayam
047.14	paśyāmaḥ/ sarvam anumānam anumiti^aḥ   tad-nāntarīyaka-tvāt/ pradhāna-upasarjana-
047.15	tā-vivakṣāyām liṅga-parāmarśaḥ iti nyāyam/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ/
047.16	ānantarya-pratipattiḥ yasmāt liṅga-parāmarśāt anantaram śeṣa-artha-pratipattiḥ iti
047.17	tasmāt liṅga-parāmarśaḥ nyāyaḥ iti/ smṛtiḥ na pradhānam kim kāraṇam smṛti-anantaram
047.18	apratipatti^aḥ  / na hi bhavati yatra dhūmam adrākṣam tatra agnim adrākṣam iti/
047.19	etasyāḥ ca smṛti^aḥ   anantaram tasmāt agniḥ iti śeṣa-artha-grahaḥ iti yuktam vaktum/
047.20	tasmāt smṛti-anugṛhītaḥ liṅga-parāmarśaḥ abhīṣṭa-artha-pratipādakaḥ bhavati iti/
047.21	evam ca upanayasya arthavat-tā yadi ca ayam smṛti-anugṛhītaḥ liṅga-parāmarśaḥ anumānam
048.01	bhavati/ evam sati upanayaḥ arthavān iti/ evam ca sati vākya-aṅga-tvam
048.02	upanayasya uktam bhavati/ tasmāt vyavasthitam etad/ tad-pūrvakam anumānam
048.03	iti/ yadi pratyakṣa-pūrvakam anumānam saṃskāre nirṇaye ca prasaṅgaḥ iti/
048.04	pratyakṣa-pūrvakaḥ bhāvanā-ākhyaḥ smṛti-hetuḥ saṃskāraḥ nirṇayaḥ ca pratyakṣa-pūrvaka-tvāt
048.05	anumānam prasajyate iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ vijñānasya adhikṛta-tvāt/ indriya-artha-
048.06	sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam iti jñāna-adhikāraḥ vartate iti tena na saṃskāre
048.07	atiprasaṅgaḥ/ nirṇaye tu ubhayathā kadā cit pramāṇam kadā cit phalam/
048.08	sva-viṣaya-paricchedaka-tvāt phalam/ śeṣa-paricchedaka-tvāt pramāṇam iti/
048.09	trividham iti/ anvayin^ḥ vyatirekin^ḥ anvayavyatirekin^ḥ ca iti/ tatra
048.10	anvyavyatirekin^ḥ vivakṣita-tad-jātīya-upapatti^i  vipakṣa-avṛttiḥ yatha anityaḥ
048.11	śabdaḥ sāmānya-viśeṣavat-tve sati asmad-ādi-bāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ghaṭa-vat
048.12	iti/ anvayin^ḥ vivakṣita-tad-jātīya-vṛtti-tve sati vipakṣa-hīnaḥ yathā
048.13	sarva-anitya-tva-vādinām anityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtaka-tvāt iti asya hi vipakṣaḥ
048.14	na asti/ vuatorelom vivakṣita-vyāpaka-tve sati sapakṣa-abhāve sati vipakṣa-
048.15	avṛttiḥ/ yathā na idam jīvat-śarīram nirātmakam aprāṇa-ādimat-tva-prasaṅgāt
048.16	iti/ atha vā trividham iti pūrvavat śeṣavat sāmānyatodṛṣṭam ca iti/
048.17	pūrvam sādhyam tad vyāpti^ā yasya asti iti tad pūrvavat/ sādhya-tad-jātīyaḥ śeṣaḥ
048.18	tad yasya asti iti tad śeṣavat/ pūrvavat nāma sādhya-vyāpakam śeṣavat iti samāne
048.19	asti/ sāmānyataḥ ca adṛṣṭam/ ca-śabdāt pratyakṣa-āgama-viruddham ca iti evam
048.20	catur-lakṣaṇam pañca-lakṣaṇam anumānam iti/ atha vā trividham iti/ pūrvavat
048.21	śeṣavat sāmānyatodṛṣṭam ca iti/ tatra pūrvavat nāma yatra kāraṇena
048.22	kāryam anumīyate iti bhāṣyam/ kim punar idam uktam bhavati kāraṇena
049.01	kāryam anumīyate iti/ yadi tāvat ayam arthaḥ kāraṇa-darśanāt kārya-asti-
049.02	tvam pratipadyate iti tad na asti na hi kāraṇam gṛhītvā svastha-ātman^ḥ
049.03	kaḥ cit kāryam pratipadyate iti tathā ca vyāhatam bhavati iti/ atha
049.04	punar evam anumīyate yatra kāraṇam tatra kāryam iti/ etad api na asti/
049.05	kārya-kāraṇayoḥ bhinna-deśa-tvāt/ tantu^āḥ sva-avayava^su aṃśuṣu paṭaḥ tantuṣu
049.06	iti kāraṇa-darśanāt ca kāryam anumīyate iti bruvāṇaḥ anumāna-mudrām
049.07	bhinatti/ kā punar iyam anumāna-mudrā na anupalabdhe na nirṇīte nyāyaḥ
049.08	pravartate iti/ yadi kāraṇa-darśanāt kāryam anumīyate anupalabdhe arthe
049.09	nyāyaḥ pravartate iti syāt/ tathā ca vyāhatam syāt/ na anabhyupagamāt
049.10	kaḥ evam āha kāraṇa-darśanāt kārya-asti-tvam pratipadyate iti/
049.11	kaḥ vā bravīti yatra kāraṇam tatra kāryam iti/ kāryam tu kāraṇa-
049.12	viśeṣaṇa-tvena   upayuktam guṇa-bhūtam anumīyate iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ tathā ca na
049.13	mudrā-bhedah iti/ evam śeṣavat-ādiṣu api draṣṭavyam/ atra api kāraṇam
049.14	kāryasya aṅga-bhūtam anumīyate iti/ katham punar śeṣa-śabdena   kāryam ucyate/
049.15	dvayoḥ anumāna-bhāvena   upakṣiptayoḥ kāraṇasya upayogāt anupayuktam kāryam
049.16	iti/ kāryam śeṣa-śabda-vācyam/ udāharaṇam megha-unnatti^ā bhaviṣyati
049.17	vṛṣṭiḥ iti kāraṇena   kārya-anumānam/ katham punar asya prayogaḥ/ vṛṣṭimantaḥ
049.18	ete meghāḥ gambhīra-dhvānavat-tve sati bahula-balākāvat-tve sati
049.19	acira-prabhāva-tve sati unnatimat-tvāt vṛṣṭimat-megha-vat iti/ nadīāḥ
049.20	pūrṇatvam śeṣavat udāharaṇam katham punar etad nadī-pūraḥ nadīām vartamānaḥ upari
049.21	vṛṣṭimat-deśam anumāpayati vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt/ na eva upari vṛṣṭimat-deśa-anumānam
049.22	nadī-pūraḥ kim tarhi nadīāḥ eva upari vṛṣṭimat-deśa-sambandhi-tvam
050.01	anumīyate nadī-dharmena  / upari vṛṣṭimat-deśa-sambandhinī nadī-srotas
050.02	śīghra-tve sati pūrṇa-phala-kāṣṭhā-ādi-vahanavat-tve sati pūrṇa-tvāt pūrṇa-vṛṣṭimat-
050.03	nadī-vat iti/ bhaviṣyati bhūtā vā iti kālasya avivakṣita-tvāt/ yaḥ
050.04	kaḥ cit kālaḥ upādeyaḥ iti/ sāmānyatodṛṣṭam nāma akārya-kāraṇa-
050.05	bhūtena   yatra avinābhāvinā viśeṣaṇena   viśeṣyamāṇaḥ dharmin^ḥ gamyate tad
050.06	sāmānyatodṛṣṭam yathā balākayā salila-anumānam/ katham punar balākayā
050.07	salila-anumānam yāvanasya deśaḥ balākayā ajahat-vṛtti-tvena   prasiddhaḥ
050.08	bhavati tāvantam antarbhāvya vṛkṣa-ādikam artham pakṣa^kṛtya balākāvat-tvena
050.09	sadhayati/ apare tu manyante sāmānyatodṛṣṭam ādityasya gati-anumānam
050.10	iti/ tad na buddhyāmahe/ katham anumīyate yadi tāvat gatimān ādityaḥ
050.11	iti asya gatiḥ anumīyate tad kena pratipadyate/ na hi ādityasya gati^aḥ   ca
050.12	kim cit sambandhi-liṅgam asti na ca asambaddhaḥ arthaḥ anumātum śakyate iti
050.13	sarvam sarveṇa anumīyeta iti/ atha deśa-antara-prāptiḥ liṅgam iti tad na
050.14	adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ na hi savitṛ^aḥ  kaḥ cit deśa-antara-prāptim paśyati/ deśa-antaram
050.15	khalu ākāśa-ādi-diś-deśaḥ vā/ ubhayam ca apratyakṣam/ na ca anyā gatiḥ
050.16	asti/ tasmat deśa-antara-prāpti-darśanam ayuktam/ sarvatra khalu ayam ādityasya
050.17	maṇḍalam eva kevalam upalabhate na ca vastu-mātra-darśanāt anumānam yuktam/
050.18	api ca kena cit prakārena   ādityasya gatiḥ śakyā draṣṭam upalabdhi-
050.19	lakṣaṇa-prāpta-tvāt/ na punar deśa-antara-prāptiḥ nityā atīndriya-tvāt/ na hi
050.20	kadā cit pratyakṣa-apratyakṣa-vṛttiḥ saṃyogaḥ bhavati pratyakṣaḥ/ atha devadattasya
050.21	deśa-antara-prāpti-darśanena   ādityasya gati-anumānam sāmānyatodṛṣṭam iti
050.22	manyase na kevalam ādityasya sarva-arthānām gati-anumāne hi bhavān api
051.01	gacchati iti evam kim iti na anumīyate/ na eva idam āditya-gati-anumānam sākṣāt
051.02	kim tarhi deśa-antara-prāptim anumāya tayā gati-anumānam iti adoṣaḥ/
051.03	deśa-antara-prāptimān ādityaḥ dravyatve sati kṣaya-vṛddhi-pratyaya-aviṣaya-tve ca
051.04	prāc-mukha-upalabhya-tve ca tad-abhimukha-deśa-sambandhāt anutpanna-pāda-vihārasya parivṛtya
051.05	tad-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt maṇi-ādivat iti maṇi-ādi^i  etad sarvam asti saḥ
051.06	deśa-antara-prāptimān evam ca ādityaḥ tasmāt deśa-antara-prāptimān iti/
051.07	anayā deśa-antara-prāpti^ā anumitayā gatiḥ anumīyate iti/ deśa-antara-
051.08	prāptimat-tve vā anumānam deśa-antara-prāptimān ādityaḥ acala-cakṣuṣaḥ vyavadhāna-
051.09	anupapatti^i  dṛṣṭasya punar darśana-aviṣaya-tvāt devadatta-vat iti/ eke
051.10	tāvat diś-pratyakṣa-tve anumānam bruvate/ pratyakṣā diś aṅguli^ā vyapadeśāt
051.11	candravat iti/ tad ca na/ arūpi-tvāt/ arūpā diś katham bāhya-
051.12	karaṇa-pratyakṣā bhaviṣyati katham tarhi aṅguli^ā vyapadeśaḥ diś-deśa-sambandhiṣu
051.13	vṛkṣa-ādiṣu diś-vyapadeśāt/ ye diś-deśa-sambandhinaḥ vṛkṣa-ādyaḥ  tān diś
051.14	iti upacaranti/ ādityena   saha upalabdhi^aḥ   prācī iti ayam śabdaḥ āditya-sambandha-
051.15	viśiṣṭa-diś-deśe vartate/ tena ādyena   āditya-darśanena   saha vṛkṣa-ādīn upalabhate
051.16	tān prācī iti upacaranti upacarya ca aṅguli^ā vyapadiśanti/ atha vā trividham
051.17	iti liṅgasya prasiddha-sat-asandigdha-tām āha porasiddham iti pakṣe vyāpakam
051.18	sat iti sajātīye asti asandigdham iti sajātīya-avinābhāvin/
051.19	atha vā trividham iti niyama-artham anekadhā bhinnasya anumānasya trividhena
051.20	pūrvavat-ādinā saṃgrahaḥ iti niyamam darśayati/ katham anekadhā bhinnam iti/
051.21	anvayavitirekin dvidhā sajātīye sat eva sat-asat ca/ anvayin api dvidhā eva/
051.22	evam vyatirekin eka-rūpam sapakṣa-abhāvāt/ tad idam pañca-vidhā-bhinnam anumānam
052.01	kāla-bhedena   pañcadaśa-dhā bhavati/ tad api punar puruṣa-bheda-anuvidhānāt
052.02	ṣaṣṭi-bhedam bhavati tasya tu āntargaṇikaḥ anantabhedaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam anumāna-
052.03	bhedaḥ evam bhinnāḥ trividham iti anena saṃgṛhītaḥ iti ataḥ niyama-artham
052.04	trividha-grahaṇam iti/ pūrvavat iti uktam kim punar atra pūrvavat/ kim kāryam uta
052.05	kāraṇam yadi pūrvam asya asti iti pūrvavat kāryam pūrvavat prāpnoti/
052.06	tataḥ ca kāraṇena   kārya-anumānam iti vyāghātaḥ/ pūrvam asya asti iti pūrvavat
052.07	iti brūmaḥ na punar kāryam kim tarhi jñānam jñānasya pūrvam viṣayaḥ/
052.08	tad idam pūrvavat iti tena kārya-anumānam iti/ evam śeṣavat-ādiṣu api
052.09	tad-viṣayāṇi jñānāni iti uktam bhavati/ atha vā pūrvavat iti vati-pratyayaḥ eṣaḥ
052.10	yathā pūrvam pratyakṣena   dṛṣṭah arthaḥ tathā anumānena   api tam eva artham poratipadyate
052.11	iti pūrvavat dṛṣṭaḥ bhavati/ anye punar anyathā varṇayanti/ yathā tena eva
052.12	dhūmena   agnim pratipadyate iti/ kim punar anena dhūmena   pratipadyate iti
052.13	kim agnim uta deśam uta sattā uta agnimantam deśam/ tatra na tāvat agnim
052.14	pratipadyate/ dharma-dharmi-bhāva-anupapatti^aḥ  / na agniḥ dhūmasya dharmaḥ na vā agni-dharmaḥ
052.15	dhūmaḥ pratīta-tvāt ca agni^aḥ   na anumeya-tvam asti/ etena sattā deśaḥ ca vyākhyātaḥ/
052.16	agni-sattāyāḥ pratīta-tvāt deśasya ca iti/ agnimān deśaḥ iti cet
052.17	na dhūmasya atad-dharma-tvāt atha api idam syāt agnimān deśaḥ dhūmena   anumīyate/
052.18	tad ca na evam/ kasmāt atad-dharma-tvāt na hi dhūmaḥ agnimataḥ
052.19	deśasya dharmaḥ/ na vā agni^aḥ   deśa-mātra-sambandhaḥ na pratītaḥ/ ayam agnimān iti
052.20	cet/ na/ tasya adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ deśa-viśeṣaḥ agnimat-tayā anumīyate na deśa-
052.21	mātram na tasya adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ na hi ayam deśa-viśeṣam paśyati kevalam tu
052.22	śūnyam abhidhānam uccārayati/ ayam deśa-agnimān iti na ca dhūma-mātram
053.01	agni-pratipādakam dhūma-mātre vā ayam paśyati/ ataḥ deśa-viśeṣaḥ na anumeyaḥ
053.02	avinābhāvena   pratipādyati iti cet/ atha api idam syāt avinābhāvaḥ agni-
053.03	dhūmayoḥ ataḥ dhūma-darśanāt agnim pratipadyate iti/ tad na/ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ  /
053.04	agni-dhūmayoḥ avinābhāvaḥ iti kaḥ arthaḥ kim kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ uta eka-artha-
053.05	samavāyaḥ/ tad-sambandha-mātram vā astu tāvat pūrvaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ
053.06	iti/ tad na/ atad-vṛtti-tvāt/ na hi dhūmaḥ agni^i  vartate na api agniḥ dhūme/
053.07	sva-kāraṇa-vṛtti-tvāt/ ataḥ na kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ/ na eka-artha-samavāyaḥ
053.08	api/ tābhyām anyasya anārambhāt/ na hi bhinna-jātīyābhyām dravyam ārabhyate/
053.09	na ca tau anyatra vartete sva-kāraṇa-vṛtti-tvāt iti uktam/ sambandha-mātram
053.10	tatra vartate iti/ tad api anumātum na śakyate iti/ katham yadi tāvat ayam
053.11	kurute asti sambandhaḥ agni-dhūmayoḥ iti/ tad na/ apratīta-tvāt/
053.12	anagnikasya api dhūmasya darśanāt na sambandha-anumānam/ rūpa-sparśavat sāhacaryam
053.13	bhaviṣyati/ na/ ubhayoḥ vyabhicāri-tvāt/ anagniḥ dhūmaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
053.14	adhūmaḥ ca agniḥ iti ubhayam vyabhicārin tasmāt na sāhacaryam iti/ yatra
053.15	dhūmaÆḥ tatra vahniḥ iti anena eva pratyuktam/ na ca anyā gatiḥ iti/ tasmāt na
053.16	dhūmena  vahniḥ anumīyate/ loka-virodhah iti cet yadi dhūmena  agni^aḥ
053.17	anumānam va bhavet/ nanu lokaḥ viruddhyate iti cet/ na asti virodhaḥ
053.18	dhūma-viśeṣena  agni-viśeṣaṇasya dhūmasya pratipādya-tvāt/ katham punar ayam agniḥ
053.19	dhūma-viśeṣaṇam bhavati/ yadā guṇa-bhūtaḥ bhavati/ anumeyaḥ agnimān ayam
053.20	dhūmaḥ iti dhūma-viśeṣena  asādhāraṇena  anumīyate iti/ ubhayam hi tadā
053.21	pratyakṣam dhūmaḥ ca tad-gatāḥ ca sātatya-saṃhati-ūrdhva-gati-svabhāva-ādyaḥ  dharmāḥ iti
053.22	te ca ete dhūma-dharmāḥ dhūma-vṛttyaḥ  aprasiddham dhūma-dharmam anumāpayanti/ sarvasya
054.01	anumeyasya vastunaḥ dharmī pratipādakaḥ dharmaḥ prasiddhaḥ bhavati yathā
054.02	śabdasya ātma-sattā prasiddhā kṛtakatvam ca dharmaḥ tu anityatva-lakṣaṇaḥ aprasiddhaḥ
054.03	iti/ tad-viśeṣaṇaḥ ayam anumīyate iti/ seṣavat nāma pariśeṣaḥ iti
054.04	bhāṣyam/ na karma-śabdaḥ śabda-antara-hetu-tvāt iti ubhaya-vyāvṛtti^aḥ   asādhāraṇam na
054.05	hi śabda-antara-hetu-tvam karmaṇi na akarmaṇi iti/ na anya-artha-tvāt/ śabda-
054.06	antara-hetu-tvam iti asya samāna-jātīya-ārambhaka-tvāt iti arthaḥ/ tathā ca na doṣaḥ/
054.07	sāmānyatodṛṣṭam nāma yatra apratyakṣe liṅga-liṅginoḥ iti bhāṣyam/
054.08	liṅga-liṅginoḥ sambandhaḥ apratyakṣaḥ iti kim yadā anumīyate tadā apratyakṣaḥ
054.09	āho na kadā cit pratyakṣaḥ yadi yadā anumīyate tadā apratyakṣaḥ sarvam anumānam
054.10	evam iti viśeṣaṇam anarthakam/ atha na kadā cit pratyakṣaḥ katham
054.11	tarhi anumānam atra pravartate/ vyāhatam ca bhavati na anupalabdhe na nirṇītaḥ iti/
054.12	na viśeṣaṇa-bhūtasya apratyakṣasya sarvadā anumeya-tvāt/ yad itara-dharma-darśanāt
054.13	dharminaḥ adhigati-sādhanam tad sāmānyatodṛṣṭam yathā icchā-ādibhiḥ ātman^ḥ
054.14	icchā-ādyaḥ  khalu dharmiṇah bhavanti/ ātman^ḥ ca viśeṣaṇam guṇabhūtaḥ
054.15	iti icchā-ādīnām guṇatvam pratītaḥ dharmaḥ/ tena dharmena  ātma-guṇa-viśiṣṭān eva
054.16	icchā-ādīm anumimīte/ paratantrāḥ icchā-ādyaḥ  guṇa-tvāt rūpavat pāratantrya-
054.17	pratipattiḥ asmāt anumānāt ātma-tantra-tā tu kutaḥ ātma-tantra-tā pariśeṣāt
054.18	ātma-saṃvedya-tvāt bāhyakaraṇa-apratyakṣa-tvāt ca pṛthivī-ādiṣu
054.19	pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ ātma-para-ātma-pratyakṣāḥ bāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣāḥ ca ātmā
054.20	abāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣāḥ ca icchā-ādyaḥ  tasmāt na pṛthivī-ādiṣu ākāśa-anta^su na
054.21	diś-kāla-manassu tad-guṇānām tadvat-atīndriya-tvāt/ na ca dravya-antaram
054.22	śiṣyate/ ātman^ḥ śeṣaḥ/ tasmāt tad-tantraḥ iti/ sat-viṣayam ca pratyakṣam sat-
055.01	asat-viṣayam ca numānam iti bhāṣyam/ kim tāvat dharmiṇam abhipretya ayam bhedaḥ
055.02	uta dharmam iti/ yadi tāvat dharmiṇam abhipretya na kadā cit asat-viṣayam
055.03	anumānam/ na hi anupalabdha-sāmānye arthe anumānam pravartate iti uktam
055.04	na ca asataḥ sāmānya-darśanam asti/ atha dharmam abhipretya brūṣe dharmāḥ api
055.05	trividhā bhavanti/ vidhīyamānāḥ pratiṣidhyamānāḥ svatantrāḥ ca iti
055.06	tatra vidhīyamānaḥ dhramaḥ pṛthivīām gandhavattvam pratiṣidhyamānaḥ apṛthivīām
055.07	gandhavattvam svatantraḥ samavāyinām samavāyaḥ iti/ katham punar svatantraḥ
055.08	samavāyaḥ samavāya-antara-abhāvāt/ yad hi yatra vartate tad vṛtti^ā vartate na ca
055.09	samavāya-antaram samavāyasya vṛttiḥ asti atha samavāya-antaram syāt samavāyasya
055.10	tasya api samavāya-antara-kalpanāyām anavasthā syāt vyavasthāyām
055.11	ādye eva vyavasthā kim idam śraddadhānena  pratipattavyam anāśritaḥ samavāyaḥ
055.12	iti āhosvit nyāyaḥ vā api asti/ na sandehaḥ asti eva nyāyaḥ pañca-
055.13	padārtha-vṛtti-śabda-viṣaya-tvāt/ paramāṇuvat anāśritaḥ samavāyaḥ iti
055.14	nyāyaḥ vyāpakatve sati iha-buddhi-nimitta-tvāt/ ātmavat/ yadi punar
055.15	āśritaḥ samavāyaḥ syāt tataḥ kim syāt/ kāryam anādhāram syāt/
055.16	katham iti/ pūrvam tāvat kāryam ātmānam labhate paścāt kāraṇa^su samavāyena
055.17	vartate iti prāk vṛtti^aḥ   anāśritam prāpnoti/ samavāyaḥ ca kārya-kāraṇayoḥ
055.18	vartate iti/ vṛttiḥ asya vācyā na hi vartamānam kim cit avṛttimat dṛṣṭam
055.19	prāpti-tvāt saṃyogavat vartate iti cet/ na/ vyāghātāt/ prāptiḥ saṃyogaḥ
055.20	vartate iti kim ayam prāpti-dhramaḥ āhosvit kārya-dhramaḥ iti/ vayam ca
055.21	brūmaḥ kārya-dhramaḥ na prāpti-dhramaḥ iti/ tathā kārya-antarāṇi api prāptimanti
055.22	santi iti yadi punar iyam vṛttiḥ prāpti-dhramaḥ abhaviṣyat prāpti^aḥ   api prāpti-
056.01	antaram abhaviṣyat iti anavasthā-doṣaḥ/ prāpti^aḥ   vṛtti-sat-tvāt/  na ca etām anavasthām
056.02	kaḥ cit śaktaḥ pratipādayitum pramāṇa-abhāvāt iti/ samavāyaḥ ca samavāya-
056.03	antarena  vartate it bruvāṇaḥ śāstram bādhate tattvam bhāvena  vyākhyātam
056.04	iti/ sambandhi-nivṛtti^i  sambandhaḥ avatiṣṭhate iti na pramāṇam asti/
056.05	na na asti khyāti-nimittānām nivṛtti^aḥ   khyāti-nimittāni asya nivartate/
056.06	na samavāyaḥ akṛtaka-tvāt/ akṛtakaḥ samavāyaḥ iti ca kāryasya ādhāravat-
056.07	tvena  anumīyate/ yadi ayam kṛtakaḥ syāt kāryena  saha upādānāt anāśritam
056.08	kāryam syāt/ atha kāryāt pūrvam bhavati iti/ tathā api kasya iti
056.09	vācyam/ atha paścāt bhavati iti kārya-anādhāra-tā-doṣaḥ tad-avasthaḥ
056.10	tasmāt svatantraḥ samavāyaḥ iti siddham/ tatra pratiṣidhyamāna-dharma-
056.11	viṣayam anumānam asat-viṣayam na punar vidhīyamāna-svatantra-viṣayam bhavati
056.12	iti asat-viṣayam iti cet bhavati jāyate iti ekaḥ arthaḥ/ na jāyamāna-
056.13	artha-anabhyupagamāt bhavati vidhyate iti/ evaṃ tāvat vyavasthitam
056.14	etad tad-pūrvakam anumānam iti/ apare tu bruvate nāntarīyaka-artha-
056.15	darśanam tad-vidaḥ anumānam iti/ asya arthaḥ/ yaḥ arthaḥ yam artham antareṇa na
056.16	bhavati saḥ nāntarīyakaḥ nāntarīyakaḥ ca asau arthaḥ ca iti nāntarīyaka-arthaḥ/
056.17	tasya darśanam tad-vidaḥ anumānam yaḥ tam veda tad-nāntarīyakaḥ ayam iti/ atra artha-
056.18	grahaṇam atiricyate/ na hi nāntarīyakaḥ syāt na arthaḥ iti nāntarīyaka-
056.19	arthaḥ iti ca samāsa-padam etad/ tatra yadi ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ nāntarīyakasya
056.20	arthaḥ iti nāntarīyakam tāvat kṛtakatvam tasya arthaḥ dharmaḥ/ prayojanam
056.21	vā yadi dharmaḥ kṛtakatvasya arthaḥ/ sattva-meyatva-abhidheyatva-ādi-anumānam
056.22	prāptam/ atha prayojanam anityatva-pratipatti-hetuḥ prāptaḥ/ atha bahuvrīhiḥ
057.01	nāntarīyakaḥ arthaḥ yasya iti tatra api kṛtakatvam nāntarīyakam tad yasya saḥ hetuḥ
057.02	prāptaḥ tad ca kṛtakatvam ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ   śabdasya anityatvasya vā yadi ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ
057.03	ghaṭa-ādiḥ hetuḥ prāptaḥ anityaḥ śabdaḥ ghaṭāt iti/ atha śabdasya śabdaḥ hetuḥ
057.04	prāptaḥ anityaḥ śabdaḥ śabdāt atha anityatvasya sādhana-bhāvena  kṛtakatvam
057.05	dharmaḥ tathā api anityaḥ śabdaḥ anityatvāt iti prāpnoti anityatvasya arthaḥ
057.06	kṛtakatvam iti/ sarvathā kṛtakatvam anityatvena  hetuḥ/ atha sāmānādhikaraṇyam
057.07	nāntarīyakaḥ ca asau arthaḥ ca iti tathā api asamarthaḥ samāsaḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-
057.08	niyama-asambhavāt/ ubhaya-pada-vyabhicāre sati samānādhikaraṇaḥ
057.09	bhavati nīla-utpala-vat/ nīla-śabdasya api aneka-artha-vṛtti-tvāt utpala-śabdasya
057.10	api tathā-bhāvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyam bhavati na punar iha nāntarīyakaḥ
057.11	iti eke asti vyabhicāraḥ arthaḥ anarthaḥ iti yataḥ artha-grahaṇam samartham syāt iti/
057.12	eka-pada-vyabhicāre api dṛṣṭam sāmānādhikaraṇyam yathā pṛthivī dravyam
057.13	atra api ubhaya-pada-vyabhicārah pradhāna-aṅga-bhāva-bhedena  dravya-śabdena  dravyam
057.14	ucyate dravyatvam ca pṛthivī-śabdena  api pradhāna-aṅga-vivakṣāyām dravyam ucyate
057.15	pṛthivī pṛthivītvam ca ataḥ ubhaya-pada-vyabhicārāt pṛthivī dravyam iti
057.16	yuktam uktam/ idam punar na yuktam nāntarīyaka-artha-darśanam iti/ kasmāt artha-pratyāyana-
057.17	artha-tvāt ca prayogasya artha-pratyāyana-artham hi śabda-prayogam icchanti
057.18	nāntarīyakam iti eke arthaḥ gamyate/ ataḥ na yuktaḥ artha-śabdaḥ iti
057.19	tad-vidaḥ iti ca na yuktam na eva anyathā nāntarīyakaḥ iti na hi nārikela-
057.20	dvīpa-vāsinaḥ dhūma-darśane nāntarīyakam iti jñānam asti ataḥ tad-vidaḥ
057.21	iti api na vaktavyam/ etena tādṛś-avinābhāvin dharma-upadarśanam hetuḥ iti
057.22	pratyuktam/ kaḥ atideśa-arthaḥ/ yathā nāntarīyaka-artha-darśane artha-grahaṇam ayuktam
058.01	tathā dharma-grahaṇam api iti/ udāharaṇam tu yathā dhūmaḥ agni^aḥ   iti/ etad ca
058.02	na samabhavati iti anekadhā varṇitam/ apare tu manyante/ anumeye atha
058.03	tad-tulye sat-bhāvaḥ nāstitā asati iti anumānam/ etena anumeya-ekadeśa-vṛtti^aḥ
058.04	api saṅgṛhīta-tvāt alakṣaṇam/ yathā anityāh paramāṇu^āḥ gandhavat-tvāt
058.05	ghaṭavat anumeye sat-bhāvaḥ iti abhidhānāt alprasaṅgaḥ/ na aprasaṅgaḥ ekadeśa-
058.06	vṛtti^aḥ   tad-dharma-tvāt vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛtti-vat/ yathā vipakṣa-ekadeśe vartamānaḥ
058.07	na vipakṣe na asti evam pakṣa-ekadeśe vartamānaḥ na pakṣe na asti iti etad-vyudāse
058.08	yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ/ na kartavyaḥ avadhāraṇāt nivṛtti^aḥ  / anumeye sat-bhāvaḥ
058.09	iti avadhāraṇe dve kim anumeye eva sat-bhāvaḥ atha anumeye sat-bhāvaḥ eva kim
058.10	punar anena pūrvena  avadharaṇena  kriyate kim asambhavaḥ nivartyate atha sambhavaḥ
058.11	jñāpyate ubhayathā api na avadhāraṇa-arthaḥ vinā api tad-adhigati^aḥ   vinā api
058.12	avadhāraṇena  ayam arthaḥ gamyate anumeye sat-bhāvaḥ asat-bhāvaḥ na iti/ na ca ekadeśa-
058.13	vṛttiḥ nirākṛtaḥ iti avadhāraṇam vyartham uttara-pada-bādhā ca atha uttaram
058.14	avadhāraṇam avagamyate tasya vyāptiḥ arthaḥ tathā api anumeyam avadhāritam vyāpti^ā
058.15	na dharmaḥ yataḥ eva karaṇam tataḥ anyatra avadhāraṇam iti/ sambhava-vyāpti^ā
058.16	ca anumeyam niyatam vyāpti-ativyāptibhyām ca sambhavaḥ prasṛtaḥ tasya dvau rāśī
058.17	śiṣyamāṇau tad-tulyaḥ viparītaḥ ca tatra nāstitā asati iti anena viparītāt
058.18	nirvartyatām tad-tulye sat-bhāvaḥ iti kim artham ārabhyate na hi anena aprāptam
058.19	kim cit prāpyate tad-tulye ca sambhava-mātram vivakṣitam iti/ sambhava-mātram
058.20	ca vyāpti-ativyāptibhyām aniyatena sambhavena labhyate/ atha tad-tulye sat-bhāvaḥ
058.21	iti etad api avadhāraṇa-artham ārabhyate/ kim punar atra avadhāryate kim tad-tulye eva
058.22	sat-bhāvaḥ tad-tulye sat-bhāvaḥ eva iti/ yadi tad-tulye eva sat-bhāvaḥ iti
059.01	sat-bhāvaḥ avadhāritaḥ/ tadā pūrva-uttara-pade bādhite bhavataḥ na hi bhavati
059.02	devadattam eva bhojaya yajñadattam ca tathā iha api tad-tulye eva sat-bhāvaḥ anumeye
059.03	ca iti unmatta-vākyam/ atha tad-tulye sat-bhāvaḥ eva tad-tulya-ekadeśa-vṛtti^aḥ
059.04	ahetu-tvam yaḥ tad-jātīyasya ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ prayatna-nāntarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ tena saḥ hetuḥ
059.05	iti prāptam asati nāstitā iti ca asampradhārya proktam yad asat svarūpam eva
059.06	tad na asti na hi asat-ādhāraḥ bhavati yataḥ tad-pratiṣedhaḥ syāt etad api avadhāraṇa-
059.07	artham ārabhyate/ kim avadhāryate nāstitā eva asati eva vā iti/ yadi
059.08	tāvat nāstitā eva asati iti vyartham anukte api tad-adhigati^aḥ/ anukte api etad gamyate
059.09	asati na asti iti/ atha punar asati eva nāstitā go^ḥ viṣāṇi-tvāt iti ayam
059.10	api hetuḥ prāpnoti iti/ evam hi asati eva na asti na punar na asti eva yad api eka-
059.11	dvi-pada-paryudāsena saptikā-sambhave ṣaṭ-pratiṣedham uktvā tri-lakṣaṇaḥ hetuḥ
059.12	abhidhīyate etad api dvi-pada-yuktayoḥ anvayinoḥ hetu-tvāt/ dvi-pada-yuktasya
059.13	ca vyatirekiṇaḥ ekasya hetu-tvāt ayuktam anabhyupagata-nitya-pakṣasya kṛtakatva-
059.14	prayatna-nāntarīyakatve dvi-pada-yukte eva vipakṣa-abhāvāt hetū bhavataḥ vyatirekin^ḥ
059.15	api tad-tulya-asambhavāt dvi-pada-yuktaḥ ca bhavati hetuḥ ca iti/ yathā na idam
059.16	nirātmakam jīvat-śarīram aprāṇitva-prasaṅgāt/ ataḥ avyāpaka-tvāt alakṣaṇam
059.17	etad iti/ etena sambandhāt ekasmāt pratyakṣāt śeṣa-siddhih anumānam iti
059.18	lakṣaṇam pratyuktam/ katham iti/ na hi ekasmāt pratyakṣāt anumānam bhavati/
059.19	atha api sambandhāt ekasmāt pratyakṣāt iti/ etad api na eva na hi ayam tadā
059.20	sambandham paśyati yadā anumimīte iti/ atha pūrvam dṛṣṭam iti tathā api
059.21	anupalabdha-liṅgasya anumānam prasajyeta na ca anyā gatiḥ asti/ rūpena ca
059.22	sparśa-anumāne sva-dṛṣṭi-vyākopaḥ/ na hi ayam rūpa-sparśayoḥ sambandham upalabhate/
060.01	atha eka-artha-samavāyaḥ sambandhaḥ iti/ saḥ api sva-darśana-vyāghātāt ayuktaḥ iti/
060.02	na hi rūpa-sparśau ekasmin arthe vartete yatra rūpam tatra sparśaḥ iti/ etad api
060.03	na asti na kva cit rūpam sparśaḥ vā paraspara-ādhāra-ādheya-tvena syāt iti cet/
060.04	paraspara-ādhāra-ādheya-bhāve api na rūpam sparśe na sparśaḥ rūpe iti/ etena
060.05	sapti-vidhaḥ sambandhaḥ iti pratyuktam/ na hi kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-ādīnām
060.06	sambandhānām rūpa-sparśayoḥ anyataraḥ sambandhaḥ iti rūpena sparśa-anumānam
060.07	yuktam iti ataḥ avyāpaka-tvāt alakṣaṇam iti niścitam anumānam//
060.08	atha upamānam/ prasiddha-sādharmyāt sādhya-sādhanam upamānam/
060.09	[1-1-6]prasiddha-sādharmyāt sādhya-sādhanam upamānam
060.10	sūtra-arthaḥ pūrvavat/ prasiddha-sādharmyāt iti prasiddham sādharmyam yasya
060.11	prasiddhena vā sādharmyam yasya saḥ ayam prasiddha-sādharmyaḥ gavayaḥ tasmāt
060.12	sādhya-sādhanam iti samākhyā-sambandha-pratipattiḥ upamāna-arthaḥ/ kim uktam
060.13	bhavati āgama-āhita-saṃskāra-smṛti-apekṣam sārūpya-jñānam upamānam/ yadā
060.14	hi anena śrutam bhavati yathā go^ḥ evam gavayaḥ iti prasiddhe go-gavaya-sādharmye
060.15	punar gau^ā sādharmyam paśyataḥ asya bhavati ayam gavayaḥ iti samākhyā-sambandha-
060.16	pratipattiḥ/ pratyakṣa-āgamābhyām na upamānam bhidyate/ katham iti/ yadā
060.17	tau ubhau go-gavayau pratyakṣena paśyati tadā hi ayam anena sarūpaḥ iti pratyakṣataḥ
060.18	pratipadyate/ yadā api śṛṇoti yathā go^ḥ evam gavayaḥ iti tadā asya
060.19	śṛṇvate eva buddhiḥ upajāyate ke cit go^aḥ dharmāḥ gavaye anvayinaḥ upalabhyante
060.20	ke cit vyatirekiṇaḥ iti anyathā hi yathā tathā iti eva na syāt
060.21	bhūyaḥ tu sārūpyam gau^ā gavayasya iti evam pratipadyate tasmāt na upamānam
060.22	pratyakṣa-āgamābhyām bhidyate iti/ gau^ā gavaya-sādṛśyam pratipadyate gavaya-
061.01	sattām vā iti/ aho pramāṇa-abhijñatā bhadantasya gau^ā gavaya-sārūpya-
061.02	pratipatti^aḥ tu saṃjñā-saṃjñi-sambandham pratipadyate iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ tasmāt
061.03	aparijñāya sūtra-artham yad kim cit ucyate//
061.04	atha śabdaḥ/ āpta-upadeśaḥ śabdaḥ iti//
061.05	[1-1-6]āpta-upadeśaḥ śabdaḥ
061.06	na śabda-mātram iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ āptaḥ khalu sākṣāt-kṛta-dhramaḥ/
061.07	sākṣāt-karaṇam arthasya āptiḥ tayā saha vartate iti āptaḥ/ svarga-apūrva-devatā-ādiṣu
061.08	upadeśaḥ na prāpnoti atīndriya-tvāt/ yadi sākṣāt-karaṇam arthasya āptiḥ
061.09	svarga-apūrva-devatā-ādīn na kaḥ cit paśyati iti tad-pratipādakaḥ vyavahāraḥ na syāt/
061.10	tasmāt āptaḥ ca asau upadeśaḥ ca iti yuktam na āptasya upadeśaḥ iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/
061.11	na brūmaḥ asmad-ādīnām pratyakṣāî svarga-ādyaḥ  iti api tu yasya pratyakṣāḥ
061.12	tasya upadeśaḥ iti/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ svarga-ādyaḥ  kasya cit pratyakṣāḥ
061.13	iti brūmaḥ sāmānya-viśeṣavat-tvāt āśrita-tvāt kasya cit pratyakṣāḥ
061.14	iti/ yadā āśritam tad kasya cit pratyakṣam iti/ para-artha-tvāt/ yad para-artham
061.15	tad api kasya cit pratyakṣam iti/ vastu-tvāt āgama-viṣaya-tvāt/ yad vastu yad ca
061.16	kathyate tad kasya cit pratyakṣam bhavati yathā ghaṭa-ādi/ anitya-tvāt kasya
061.17	cit pratyakṣāḥ iti/ asiddham apūrvasya anityatvam/ na/ prāyaṇa-anupapatti^aḥ/
061.18	yadi dharma-adharmau nityau bhavataḥ kasya prakṣayāt porāyaṇam iti/ yadi
061.19	punar anityaḥ vipacyamānayoḥ upabhogāt prakṣaye sati vipacyamāna-
061.20	karma-āśaya-antara-abhāvāt pūrva-śarīrāt pracyavanam manasaḥ uttara-śarīre samprāptiḥ
061.21	ca iti janma-maraṇe staḥ tasmāt na nityau iti/ nitye vā apūrve parikalpate
061.22	tasya sādhāraṇatā asādhāraṇatā vā vaktavyā/ yadi sādhāraṇatā
062.01	sarva-prāṇi-sādhāraṇaḥ abhyudayaḥ syāt na vā anyat niyāmakam asti kriyā-lopaḥ
062.02	ca iti/ nityatve vyañjaka-bhedāt na ubhaya-doṣaḥ nityatve sādhāraṇatve vā adṛṣṭasya
062.03	na sādhāraṇaḥ abhyudayaḥ na api kriyā-vilopaḥ iti/ kasmāt vyañjaka-
062.04	bhedāt nityam api apūrvam yaḥ abhivyanakti tasya phalam abhivyakti-arthā kriyā iti/
062.05	ataḥ na kriyā-lopaḥ iti/ yena yad abhivyajyate tasya eva tad phala-dātṛ^ḥ
062.06	bhavati iti dṛṣṭam/ na dṛṣṭam/ na hi devadatta-prakāśitam ghaṭam yajñadattah
062.07	na paśyati/ nityasya vā apūrvasya kā abhivyaktiḥ kim upalabdhiḥ atha
062.08	phala-pradhāna-sāmarthyam āhosvit āvaraṇa-apagamaḥ/ yadi upalabdhiḥ sā na asti/
062.09	na hi kadā cit api ayam apūrvam poaśyati atīndriya-tvāt/ atha phala-pradhāna-
062.10	sāmarthyam abhivyaktiḥ phala-pradhāna-sāmarthyam kim apūrvam atha apūrva-dhramaḥ iti/
062.11	yadi apūrvam na hi kim cit kṛtam atha apūrva-dharmaḥ tad-vyatirekena api anyat apūrvam iti atra
062.12	kim pramāṇam/ vayam tu paśyāmaḥ yataḥ phalam tad apūrvam iti/ atha āvaraṇa-ādi-
062.13	apagamaḥ abhivyaktiḥ kim asya āvaraṇam iti vaktavyam na hi atīndriyāṇām arthānām
062.14	āvaraṇa-sambhava-asambhavau cintyete/ etena pratipuruṣam nityatvam pratiṣiddham
062.15	veditavyam/ katham iti/ tatra api kriyā-vilopa-doṣaḥ vyañjakasya
062.16	ca asāmarthyam iti/ utkarṣa-arthā ca pravṛttiḥ ayuktā nityasya abhedya-tvāt iti/
062.17	vyañjaka-bheda-anuvidhānāt bhedaḥ iti cet/ na/ ekasmin adṛṣṭa-tvāt atha
062.18	api idam syāt ekam api apūrvam vyañjaka-bheda-anuvidhānāt bhinnam iva bhavati tad-bhedāt
062.19	utkarṣa-apakarṣau bhavataḥ iti/ etad api na asti/ ekasmin adṛṣṭa-tvāt/
062.20	na hi ekam kim cit vyañjaka-bheda-anuvidhāyin dṛṣṭam yataḥ evam syāt/ nanu ca
062.21	khaṅga-ādi-bhedāt mukha-bhedaḥ tad-anuvidhānāt dṛṣṭaḥ na dṛṣṭaḥ it brūmaḥ/
062.22	kim punar tad yadi na dṛṣṭam mithyā pratyayaḥ saḥ iti brūmaḥ/ bhinnam iva
063.01	tad na punar bhinnam eva kasya aneka-rūpasya asambhavāt/ na hi ekam aneka-rūpam
063.02	sambhavati/ tasmāt apūrvasya ekatvam vā hātavyam utkarṣa-apakarṣa-hetu-tvam vā iti
063.03	prati puruṣam anekam nityam vā iti/ atha ayam pakṣaḥ āśrīyate pratipuruṣam
063.04	anekatvam nityatvam vā apūrvasya/ anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ prāyaṇa-anupapatti-
063.05	prasaṅga-doṣaḥ kriyā-vilopaḥ na vyāvartate/ abhivyaktiḥ tu phalam dadāti iti
063.06	kim yadā abhivyaktiḥ tadā phalam dadāti uta kriyā-nivṛtti-uttara-kālam iti/
063.07	yadā abhivyaktiḥ tadā phalam dadāti tad na dṛṣṭam/ na hi aśvamegha-kriyā-
063.08	anantaram eva svarga-prāptiḥ bhavati/ atha uttara-kālam asatī kriyā vyañjikā
063.09	tayā ca anugṛhyamāṇam dadāti iti citram na hi kim cit apūrvam na abhivyaktam
063.10	iti sarvam sarvadā phala-dātṛ^ḥ syāt iti/ atha api sva-pakṣa-rirakṣayiṣayā
063.11	kriyā-nityavam api pratipadyethāḥ evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ kriyā-
063.12	vilopa-ādikaḥ iti tad evam yathā yathā apūrvasya nityatvam pratipadyate
063.13	tathā upapattim na sahate iti/ āpta-upadeśaḥ iti kim āptānām
063.14	avisaṃvāditvam vā pratipādyate āhosvit arthasya tathā-bhāvaḥ iti
063.15	yadi āptānām avisaṃvāditvam pratipādyate tad-anumānāt/ atha arthasya
063.16	tathā-bhāvaḥ saḥ api pratyakṣena/ yadā hi ayam artham pratyakṣena upalabhate tadā
063.17	tathā-bhāvam arthasya pratipadyate iti/ tad na/ sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt/
063.18	na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ/ āpta-upadeśaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ api tu indriya-sambandha-asambandha^su
063.19	artha^su yā śabda-ullekhena pratipattiḥ sā āgama-arthaḥ/ tasmāt asambaddhaḥ ayam
063.20	vikalpaḥ//
063.21	[1-1-8]saḥ dvividhaḥ dṛṣṭa-adṛṣṭa-tvāt
063.22	saḥ dvividhaḥ iti niyama-artham/ anekadhā-bhinnaḥ āgamaḥ pratyakṣa-anumāna-
064.01	viṣaya-tāyām eva vyavatiṣṭhate iti/ vaktṛ-bhedena vā dvaividhyam dṛṣṭa-
064.02	adṛṣṭa-artha-pravaktṛka-tvāt/ pratyakṣataḥ upalabdha-arthaḥ dṛṣṭa-arthaḥ/ anumāna-upalabdha-
064.03	arthaḥ adṛṣṭa-arthaḥ/ ṛṣi-ārya-mlecchānām etad samānam lakṣaṇam iti sarveṣām
064.04	āgamena vyavahāra-darśanāt aniyamaḥ/ evam catvāri etāni pramāṇāni
064.05	yaiḥ deva-manuṣi atiraścām vyavahāraḥ kalpyate iti//  iti pramāṇa-
064.06	vārttikam//
064.07	atha prameyam//
064.08	[1-1-9]ātma-śarīra-indriya-artha-buddhi-manas-pravṛtti-pretyabhāva-
064.09	phala-duḥkha-apavargāḥ tu prameyam
064.10	 kim punar anena pramāṇena artha-jātam pramātavyam iti bhāṣyam/ anupapannaḥ ayam praśnaḥ sāmarthyataḥ pramāṇa-viṣaya-adhigati^aḥ/ na hi imāni pramāṇāni nirviṣayāni carcitāni, yataḥ ayam praśnaḥ syāt kim punar anena pramāṇena artha-jātam pramātavyam/ na na yuktaḥviśeṣa-viṣaya-tvāt na ayam prameya-viśeāaḥ prameya-mātrasya  pramāṇa-lakṣaṇa-
064.11	uddeśena bodhita-tvāt/  prameya-viśeṣa-avadhāraṇa- arthaḥ tu bhavet/ katamam tad prameyam/ yad anena
064.12	yathāvat parijñāyamānam apavargāya aparijñāyamānaḥ ca asau ātman^ḥ iti/ etad-
064.13	artha-prakāśanāya ātma-ādi-sūtram/ ayam eva ca sūtra-arthaḥ iti/ atra api
064.14	samānaḥ  ca arthe dvandvaḥ yathā-vacanam vigrahaḥ/ ubhaya-avadhāraṇa-prāpti^i anyatara-avadhāraṇe
064.15	doṣaḥ/ kim punar ātman^ḥ avadhāryate/ kim ātma-ādi eva prameyam atha prameyam
064.16	eva ātma-ādi iti/ yadi ātma-ādi eva prameyam  atyalpam idam ucyate diś-ādīnām sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāyānām
064.17	sūtre anabhidhānāt avyāpakam atha punar prameyam eva ātma-ādi tathā
064.18	ca prameyā ca tulā-prāmāṇyavat iti vyāghātaḥ/ tatra suvarṇa-ādi-
065.01	dravya-pariccheda-sādhana-tvāt paricchedya-tvāt ca pramāṇa-prameya-śabda-abhilāpyam/
065.02	loka-virodhaḥ ca ekasya aneka-kāraka-vācya-tvāt vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati iti/ yathā
065.03	tatra ekam vṛkṣa-ākhyam vastu anekena kāraka-śabdena ucyate tasya tasya nimittasya
065.04	sannipātāt/ evam prameyam pramāṇam iti na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt/
065.05	ātma-ādi eva prameyam yathāvat paricchidyamānam apavṛkti^e bhavati iti ayam sūtra-arthaḥ
065.06	na prameya-antara-nirākaraṇa-arthaḥ sūtra-ārambhaḥ/ prameyam eva ātma-ādi iti na
065.07	doṣaḥ kim uktam bhavati iti mumukṣuṇā prameyam eva ātma-ādi boddhavyam eva/
065.08	na asya prameyatvam vidhīyate na anyasya ca prameyatvam nirākriyate api tu
065.09	mumukṣuṇā boddhavyam eva etad iti anūdyate tasmāt ubhayam avadhāryate/ na ca yathā-
065.10	ukta-doṣaḥ/ upetya brūmaḥ diś-ādi api atra eva labhyate upapatti-sāmarthyāt kā
065.11	punar upapattiḥ pravṛtti^aḥ viṃśati-bhedāt tad-saṃskāraka-tve api na diś-ādyaḥ  api
065.12	labhyante sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāyāḥ ca viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvena ātma-ādyaḥ
065.13	vijñeyatvena upadiṣṭāḥ teṣām ca itaretara-vyavacchedaka-bhāvena sāmānya-viśeṣa-
065.14	samavāyāḥ viśeṣaṇam kṛtam sūtre iti/ na punar idam prameya-avadhāraṇa-artham
065.15	sūtram na tu prameya-viśeṣa-avadhāraṇa-artham prameya-mātra-avadhāraṇa-artham ca pravṛtti^i
065.16	vihita-vidhānāt akuśalaḥ sūtra-kāraḥ syāt ādyena vā sūtrena prameyam
065.17	vihitam iti punar vidhānāt unmatta-vākyam idam syāt enam ca artham  jijñāpayiṣuḥ
065.18	kam sūtra-kāraḥ tu-śabdam āha/ anyat api prameyam asti yasya tu tattva-jñānāt niḥśreyasam
065.19	tad idam prameyam iti tu-śabdena jñāpayati/ sukham punar kasmāt atra
065.20	na uktam/ kim abhāvāt anyataḥ upalabdhi^aḥ vā na tāvat abhāvāt pratyātma-vedanīya-
065.21	tvāt pratyātmam eva vedanīyam sukham upalabhyamānam na śakyam na asti iti
066.01	vaktum/ na anyataḥ upalabdhiḥ asmāt eva/ anabhidhānam tu vairāgya-jñāpana-
066.02	artham katham na ayam mokṣyamāṇaḥ sarvam duḥkham bhāvayet iti tasya duḥkha-bhāvanā-
066.03	arthaḥ sukhasya anpadeśaḥ sarvam duḥkham bhāvayataḥ trailokyasya anabhirati-saṃjñā
066.04	bhavati tām ca upāsīnasya trailokya-viṣayā tṛṣṇā vicchidyate tṛṣṇā-
066.05	nāma-punarbhava-prārthanā sā asya sarvam duḥkham iti bhāvayataḥ na bhavati na
066.06	hi kaḥ cit duḥkham porārthayate tṛṣṇā-prahāṇāt yāni punarbhava-sādhanāni tāni
066.07	na upādatte tad-anupādānāt anāgatau dharma-adharmau na bhavataḥ yau api utpannau
066.08	tau api upabhogāt kṣayam gacchataḥ/ saḥ ayam dharma-adharma-lakṣaṇasya saṃsāra-bījasya
066.09	ucchedāt appavṛjyate na punar jāyate iti asya arthasya poradarśana-artham sukha-anabhidhānam
066.10	iti//
066.11	ātmanaḥ samāna-asamāna-jātīya-viśeṣaṇa-artham sūtram/ icchā iti
066.12	[1-1-10]icchā-dveṣa-prayatna-sukha-duḥkha-jñānāni ātmanaḥ liṅgam
066.13	iti
066.14	āgamasya anumānena pratisandhāna-artham vā āgamena pratipannaḥ ātman^ḥ
066.15	tasya anumānena pratisandhāna-artham pramāṇa-saṃplavasya udāharaṇa-artham vā yad tad
066.16	avocāma pramāṇāni saṃplavante iti tasya ayam viṣayaḥ/ yad-jātīyasya
066.17	arthasya sannikarṣāt iti bhāṣyam/ tatra icchā-ādīnām pratisandhānam ātma-
066.18	astitva-pratipādakam/ tatra idam vicāryate/ katham icchā-ādi-anupalabhyamānam
066.19	ātmānam gamayati smṛti^ā saha eka-viṣaya-bhāvāt yasmāt eva icchā-ādyaḥ  smṛti^ā
066.20	saha eka-viṣayāḥ bhavanti tasmāt eka-kartṛka-tvam pratipādayanti/ na hi
067.01	nānā-kartṛkāṇām nānā-viṣayāṇām nānā-nimittānām ca pratisandhānam
067.02	asti na hi rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-pratyayāḥ pratisandhīynate na hi bhavati
067.03	yad rūpam adrākṣam saḥ ayam sparśaḥ iti/ na api bhavati yad sparśam aspārkṣam tad rūpam
067.04	paśyāmi iti na api devadatta-dṛṣṭe yajñadatta-pratisandhānam dṛṣṭam/ na hi
067.05	bhavati devadattaḥ yam adrākṣīt yajñadattaḥ tam adrākṣam iti/ kim kāraṇam
067.06	buddhi-bhedānām pratiniyata-viṣaya-tvāt iti pratiniyata-viṣayāḥ itaretara-vyāvṛtti-
067.07	rūpāḥ nairātmya-vādinaḥ na bhavanti iti na yuktam pratisandhānam tasmāt yaḥ
067.08	pratisandhātṛ^ḥ saḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānam iti
067.09	cet atha api manyethāḥ na eka-kartṛka-tvāt pratisandhānam kim tarhi kārya-
067.10	kāraṇa-bhāvāt pūrva-pūrva-buddhi-viśeṣāt uttaram uttaram buddhi-antaram pūrva-buddhi-śakti-anuvidhānāt
067.11	sakala-āhita-śakti-kalāpam utpadyate ataḥ nānātve api buddhīnām
067.12	kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānam bīja-ādivat iti tad yathā śāli-guṭikā-
067.13	ādi-anantaram aṅkuraḥ prādurbhavati tasya śāli-śakti-anuvidhānam tad-pūrvaka-tayā
067.14	vyavatiṣṭhate anantaram mahābhūtaiḥ anugṛhyamāṇam uttara-śāli-bījam janayati na
067.15	yava-bījam atad-pūrvaka-tvāt tathā iha api eka-sāntānikīnām buddhīnām kārya-kāraṇa-
067.16	vyavasthānāt pratisandhānam atad-pūrvaka-tvāt na santāna-antara-buddhīnām
067.17	na punar eka-kartṛka-tvāt pratisandhānam adarśanāt tad idam pratisandhānam
067.18	anyathā bhavan ātmanaḥ sattām pratipādayitum śaknoti na nānātvasya abādhanāt
067.19	kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānam bruvan na nānātvam bādhate kim
067.20	kāraṇam kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvasya nānātva-adhiṣṭhānāt kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ khalu
067.21	nānātva-adhiṣṭhānaḥ ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipanne ca nānātve na pratisandhānam dṛṣṭam
068.01	kasmāt anyena anubhūtasya anyena asmaraṇāt na ca asmṛtam pratisandhātum śaknoti
068.02	akārya-kāraṇa-bhāve anupapatti^aḥ na brūmaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāve dṛṣṭam pratisandhānam
068.03	api tu akārya-kāraṇa-bhāve na dṛṣṭam iti atad-pūrvaka-tvāt iti uktam/ tasmāt
068.04	ayathā-arthaḥ ayam upālambhaḥ iti/ tad ayuktam/ vivāda-kāraṇa-avyatirekāt iti/
068.05	evam bruvāṇaḥ bhavān na kāraṇam atirecayati mayā uktam nānātve na dṛṣṭam
068.06	iti/ tvam na brūṣe akārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt nānātve na dṛṣṭam iti/ anaikāntika-
068.07	tvāt na vivāda-kāraṇam atiricyate/ tulyam bhavataḥ api nānātve
068.08	adarśanāt iti bruvatā na vivāda-kāraṇam iti recitam bhavati/ abhyupagamāt
068.09	anuttaram tulyam iti bruvatā abhyupagatam bhavati na asādhānāt na mayā
068.10	kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānam sādhyate api tu anyathā eva tad bhavati iti
068.11	hetu^aḥ asiddha-artha-tā doṣaḥ abhidhīyate iti/ na/ hetu-artha-aparijñānāt/
068.12	na bhavatā hetu-rūpam viśeṣataḥ vyajñāyi kevalam hetu-sāmānyāt anyathā eva
068.13	bhavati iti hetu-doṣaḥ abhidhāyin/ viśeṣitam ca etad pratisandhānam smṛti^ā saha
068.14	pūrva-apara-pratyaya-eka-viṣaya-tvena poratisandhānam sā ca smṛtiḥ bhavat-pakṣe anupapannā
068.15	kasmāt anyena anubhūtasya anyena asmaraṇāt na hi anyena anubhūtasya anyena smaraṇam
068.16	na hi anyena anubhūtam anyaḥ smarati asti ca smṛtiḥ tasmāt asmin pakṣe smṛtiḥ
068.17	sambhavati tatra pratisandhānam iti nyāyam/ bhavān asmān smṛti-anupapatti^ā
068.18	bhāvayati na hi naḥ pakṣe smṛtiḥ na sambhavati katham iti kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt
068.19	eva yad kāya-citta-santāne smṛtiḥ anubhavaḥ vā utpadyete saḥ kāya-citta-
068.20	santānaḥ smartṛ^ḥ ca anubhāvitṛ^ḥ ca bhavati/ na asthiratvāt buddhīnām sthiram hi
068.21	vāsakena vāsyamānam dṛṣṭam na ca buddhīnām sthiratvam asti asambandhāt ca asambanddham
069.01	ca vāsakena vāsyamānam dṛṣṭam na ca vāsakena sambandhaḥ buddhīnām asti
069.02	śakti-viśiṣta-citta-utpādaḥ vāsanā iti cet atha api evam kalpeta pūrva-citta-
069.03	sahajāt cetanā-viśeṣāt pūrva-śakti-viśiṣtam cittam utpadyate saḥ asya śakti-viśiṣṭa-
069.04	citta-utpādaḥ vāsanā iti ucyate/ atra uktam asthiratvāt vāsakena asambandhāt ca iti
069.05	yaḥ ca asau cetanā-viśeṣaḥ pūrva-citta-sahabhāvin^ḥ saḥ na api vartamāne cetasi
069.06	upakāram karoti na api anāgate katham iti vartamānam tāvat avikāryam yathā-
069.07	bhūtam jāyate tathā-bhūtam eva naśyati anāgate saḥ api na sambadhyate na
069.08	ca asambaddham vāsayati iti uktam tasmāt kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt smṛtiḥ iti
069.09	aśikṣitaḥ vāsanam iti/ itaḥ ca na bhavat-pakṣe smṛtiḥ bhāvasya bhavitṛ-
069.10	apekṣa-tvāt sarvaḥ hi bhāvaḥ bhavitṛ^am apekṣate kiryā-tvāt utpatti-vat iti
069.11	smṛti^aḥ ca bhavitṛ^ḥ karman vā bhavet kartṛ^ḥ vā taṇḍulānām pācakaḥ iti
069.12	karmaṇi devadattasya gatiḥ kartari tad na tāvat karmaṇi asataḥ api sambhavāt
069.13	yadā vidyamānam smarati tadā anādhārā smṛtih prāpnoti atha
069.14	kartṛ-ādhārā sā tadā asmad-pakṣe prayuktā na bhavat-pakṣe kasmāt kartṛ^aḥ
069.15	abhyupagamāt na hi bhavantaḥ kartṛ^am smṛti-arthakam pratipadyante iti/ anādhārā
069.16	eva smṛtiḥ iti yadā ayam pakṣaḥ bhavati tadā anumānam na asti kāryasya
069.17	anādhārasya adarśanāt iti kāryam sarvam ādhāravat dṛṣṭam rūpa-ādi tad ca smṛtiḥ
069.18	tasmāt ādhāravatī kārya-kāraṇayoḥ bhāva-bhavitṛ-vyavahāraḥ kārya-kṣaṇah bhāvaḥ
069.19	kāraṇa-kṣaṇaḥ bhavitṛ^ḥ iti/ evam ca kim anyena bhavitṛā kalpitena iti
069.20	tad ca na kāla-bhedāt kārya-kāraṇayoḥ bhinna-kāla-tvāt āśraya-āśrita-bhāva-anupapatti^aḥ
069.21	kuṇda-vadara-ādi-vat iti/ atha api idam syāt utpattiḥ bhāvah utpattā
070.01	bhavitṛ^ḥ iti smṛtiḥ api utpadyate yasmāt tasyāḥ api utpattiḥ bhāvaḥ bhaviṣyati
070.02	smṛtiḥ api utpattrī bhavitṛ^ḥ iti/ tad ca na virodhāt anabhyupagamāt ca/ yadi
070.03	smṛti-vyatirikta-utpattiḥ vyāhatam bhavati svarūpam ca vaktavyam na hi bhavantaḥ
070.04	utpattimat-vyatirekena utpattim manyante/ vyatiriktām ca utpattim manyamānena
070.05	bhavatā tasyāḥ svarūpam vaktavyam/ svarūpam ca nirūpyamāṇam tantram
070.06	bādhate katham iti utpattiḥ sva-karaṇa-sattā-sambandhaḥ sattā vā sva-kāraṇa-
070.07	sambandha-viśiṣṭā ubhayam ca tantrena abhyupagatam iti virodhaḥ/ atha smṛti-
070.08	avyatirikta-utpatti-pūrvakam kim kva vartate iti śūnyam abhidhānam/ utpattiḥ
070.09	bhāvah utpattrī smṛtiḥ iti yadā ca utpattiḥ smṛti^aḥ bhāva-tvena vivakṣitā
070.10	bhavati tadā evam vaktum nyāyam yadā tu smṛtiḥ bhāvaḥ tadā bhavitṛā bhavitavyam
070.11	kasmāt bhāvasya bhavitṛ-apekṣa-tvāt iti uktam tasmāt katham cana bhavat-pakṣe
070.12	smṛtiḥ sambhavati iti smṛtim antareṇa na pratisandhānam asti iti asti ca idam
070.13	pratisandhānam tasmāt yad kartṛkam etad saḥ arthaḥ anyaḥ ekaḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ atha vā anvayena
070.14	anyaḥ sūtra-arthaḥ abhidhīyate iti devadattasya rūpa-rasa-sparśa-pratyayāḥ eka-
070.15	aneka-nimittakāḥ smṛti^ā saha mayā iti pratisandhānāt kṛta-saṃketānām
070.16	vṛddhānām ekasmin nartakī-bhrūkṣepe yugapad-aneka-pratyaya-vat yathā nānā-
070.17	kartṛkāṇām kṛta-saṃketānām nānā-bhūtāḥ pratyayāḥ nimittasya bhrūkṣepasya
070.18	ekatvāt pratisandhīyante tathā iha api nānā-viṣayāḥ nimittasya ekatvāt
070.19	pratisandhāsyante yad tad nimittam saḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ atha vā icchā-dveṣa-prayatna-sukha-
070.20	duḥkha-jñānāni ātmanaḥ liṅgam iti anyathā varṇayanti/ guṇāḥ icchā-ādyaḥ
070.21	guṇāḥ paratantrāḥ bhavanti iti nyāyaḥ guṇatvam pāriśeṣyāt na sāmānya-
070.22	viśeṣa-samavāyāḥ anitya-tvāt/ na dravyam karman vā vyāpaka-dravya-samavāyāt
071.01	śabda-vat iti ādi sāmānyatodṛṣṭam iti etasmin anumānam vyavasthitam/ etena
071.02	anitya-tvāt pāratantryam icchā-ādīnām kārya-tvāt ca rūpa-ādi-vat iti uktam/
071.03	ayāvat-dravya-bhāvi-tvena śarīre guṇatva-pratiṣedhaḥ tad-pratiṣedhāt ca ātman-guṇa-tvam
071.04	iti pariśeṣāt siddhaḥ ātman^ḥ iti//
071.05	[1-1-11]ceṣṭā-indriya-artha-āśrayaḥ śarīram
071.06	ceṣṭā-indriya-artha-āśrayaḥ śarīram iti/ kā punar iyam ceṣṭā hita-ahita
071.07	prāpti-parihāra-arthaḥ spandaḥ/ sukha-sādhana-upalabdhi^i tad-īpsā-prayuktasya yaḥ
071.08	parispandaḥ sā ceṣṭā iti gamyate/ evam duḥkha-sādhana-upalabdhi^i tad-jihāsā-
071.09	anuṣṭhāna-lakṣaṇaḥ parispandaḥ iti/ katham indriyāṇām śarīram āśrayaḥ
071.10	sva-kāraṇa-vṛtti-tvāt avṛtti-tvāt ca yāni tāvat kāryāṇi indriyāni tāni
071.11	sva-kāraṇa-vṛttīni yad akāryam tad avṛtti ghrāṇa-ādīni tvac-paryantāni
071.12	kāryāṇi śrotra-manasī tu akārye na ca ghrāṇa-ādi-manas-paryantam indriyam
071.13	śarīra-vṛtti tasmāt ayuktam indriya-āśrayaḥ śarīram iti/ śarīra-anuvidhānam
071.14	indriya-āśraya-tvam brūmaḥ na punar ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvena indriyāṇi śarīre vartante
071.15	iti/ api tu śarīrasya upaghāta-anuvidhānam indriya-āśraya-tvasya arthaḥ śarīra-
071.16	anugrahena anugṛhyante tad-upaghāte ca upahanyante iti ayam āśraya-arthaḥ tasmāt
071.17	ayathā-arthaḥ āpkṣepaḥ iti/ etena arthānām āśraya-arthaḥ uktaḥ veditavyaḥ kim
071.18	kāraṇam na hi gandha-ādyaḥ  arthāḥ śarīra-vṛttyaḥ  yad tu teṣām kāryam sukha-
071.19	duḥkha-upalabdhi-nimitta-tvam tad na asati śarīre bhavati iti śarīra-āśrayāḥ iti
071.20	ucyante/ yathā grāmīṇāḥ grāmaṇyam āśritāḥ iti/ katham punar ete ceṣṭā-
071.21	ādyaḥ  sāmānya-śabdāḥ santaḥ viśeṣa-vṛttyaḥ  bhavanti iti sāmānya-śabdānām
071.22	aspi viśeṣa-vṛtti-tvam sāmārthyāt sāmarthyam prakaraṇa-āḍi brāhmaṇān bhojaya
072.01	iti yathā aśeṣa-brāhmaṇa-bhojanasya aśakya-tvāt viśeṣena sannidhīyamānam
072.02	prakaraṇa-āpannam brāhmaṇa-viśeṣam āha/ evam ceṣṭā-adi-śabdānām api sāmarthyāt viśeṣe
072.03	avasthānam/ sāmarthyam ca lokataḥ tad-adhigatiḥ pramāṇa-asambhavah vā
072.04	lokataḥ tāvat cetṣā-śabdam na kriyā-mātre prayuṅkte kim tu viśeṣaḥ iti
072.05	yathā spandate sarpati dhāvati iti yathā ete spanda-ādi-śabdāḥ kriyā-vācakāḥ
072.06	santaḥ kriyā-viśeṣa^su avatiṣṭhante tathā ca ayam ceṣṭā-śabdaḥ sāmānya-vācin^ḥ api
072.07	pramāṇa-asambhavāt kriyā-viśeṣe vartate na kriyā-mātra-ādhārasya śarīra-
072.08	vyakti-hetu-tvam sambhavati iti/ evam ca ghaṭa-ādiṣu aprasaṅgaḥ yaiḥ api atra ādya-ādi-pada-
072.09	prayoge ghaṭa-ādiṣu aprasaṅgaḥ iti deśyam kriyate tad api anena vyākhyānena
072.10	pratyuktam/ sati tu prasaṅge samasta-pada-prayoge api na paramāṇuṣu prasaṅgaḥ
072.11	vāryate iti/ yathā tu asmābhiḥ varṇitam tathā na kva cit api śarīrāt anyatra
072.12	vartate iti//
072.13	[1-1-12]ghrāṇa-rasana-cakṣus-tvac-śrotarāṇi indriyāṇi bhūta^bhyaḥ
072.14	ghrāṇa-rasana-cakṣus-tvac-śrotrāṇi indriyāṇi bheta^bhyaḥ iti sūtram/ lakṣaṇa-
072.15	sūtrāṇi samāna-asamāna-jātīya-viśeṣaṇa-arthāni sarvāṇi iti sūtra-arthaḥ
072.16	draṣṭavyaḥ/ uddeśa-sāmānyāt lakṣaṇa-abhidhānam nāma-dheyena padārtha-abhidhāna-
072.17	mātram uddeṣaḥ iti uddeśa-lakṣaṇam uktam idam ca tathā-bhūtam tasmāt alakṣaṇam iti/
072.18	na idam tathā karaṇa-bhāvāt sva-viṣaya-grahaṇa-lakṣaṇa-tvam indriyāṇām karaṇa-
072.19	svabhāvakāṇi indriyāṇi iti teṣām atīndriyāṇām indriyāṇām yad sva-
072.20	viṣaya-grahaṇam tena lakṣyante iti sva-viṣaya-grahaṇa-lakṣaṇa-tvam veditavyam/
072.21	asādhāraṇa-tvāt iti/ bhūta^bhyaḥ iti pṛthivī-ādi-kāraṇa-upadeśaḥ niyama-arthaḥ/
072.22	kaḥ punar ayam niyamaḥ bhūta-guṇa-viśeṣa-grahaṇa-sādhana-tvam na sarvam indriyam
073.01	sarva-bhūta-guṇa-viśeṣam gṛhṇāti iti api tu yad jātīyam indriyam tasya
073.02	yaḥ guṇa-viśeṣaḥ itaretara-bhūta-vyavaccheda-hetuḥ gandha-ādiḥ saḥ tena eva indriyena
073.03	gṛhyate iti ayam niyamaḥ aikātmye punaru ayam niyamaḥ na syāt/ yadi
073.04	punar indriyāṇi eka-ātmakāni eka-kāraṇakāni syuḥ kāraṇa-svabhāva-anuvidhānāt
073.05	aikātmyāt viṣaya-vyavasthā na syāt sarvam sarva-artham ekam vā sarva-artham
073.06	iti syāt eka-kāraṇānām api svabhāva-bhedaḥ rūpa-ādivat iti cet/
073.07	atha manyethāḥ dṛṣṭaḥ eka-kāraṇānām pākaja-rūpa-ādīnām svabhāva-bhedaḥ
073.08	katamat punar ekam kāraṇam rūpa-ādīnām agni-saṃyogaḥ iti na siddhānta-
073.09	aparijñānāt na brūmaḥ agni-saṃyogāt ekasmāt rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti api tu pūrva-
073.10	rūpa-ādi-viśeṣa-apekṣāt yad dravyam pacyate agni-saṃyogena tasya ye pūrva-rūpa-
073.11	ādyaḥ  teṣām yaḥ svagataḥ viśeṣaḥ tam apekṣamāṇaḥ agni-saṃyogaḥ uttarān
073.12	rūpa-ādīn viśiṣṭān ārabhate/ evam ca kṛtvā paramāṇu^aḥ pakvatara-tama-
073.13	ādi-bhedaḥ tasmāt na eka-kāraṇa-pūrvakāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti na kim cit api eka-kāraṇakam
073.14	kāryam dṛṣṭam/ sarvam hi kāryam prādurbhavat samavāyi-asamavāyi-
073.15	nimittakāraṇa^bhyaḥ bhavati iti/ ekam karman saṃyoga-vibhāga-kāraṇam dṛṣṭam
073.16	iti cet na anabhyupagamāt syāt matiḥ nanu ca dṛṣṭam karman saṃyoga-vibhāga-
073.17	kāraṇakam iti na anabhyupagamāt na etad abhyupagacchāmaḥ nirapekṣam karman
073.18	saṃyoga-vibhāgayoḥ kāraṇam iti/ yadi tarhi karman api sāpekṣam saṃyoga-
073.19	vibhāgau karoti karma-lakṣaṇa-hāniḥ yad nirapekṣam saṃyoga-vibhāga-kāraṇam
073.20	tad karman iti uktam tad dhīyeta lakṣaṇa-hāni^aḥ ca na karman tataḥ ca saṃyoga-vibhāgau na
073.21	karma-pūrvakau iti/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ na brūmaḥ nirapekṣam karman kāraṇam iti na
073.22	kim cit apekṣate iti api tu carama-bhāvi-nimitta-antaram na apekṣate
074.01	iti ayam nirapekṣa-arthaḥ/ yathā dravya-jātam samāna-jātīya-dravya-saṃyogam ca carama-
074.02	bhāvi-nimittam apekṣamāṇam dravyam ārabhate na evam karma-jātīyam paścāt-bhāvi-nimittam
074.03	apekṣate api tu karman sat saṃyoga-vibhāgau ārabhate tasmāt na karma-lakṣaṇa-
074.04	hāniḥ na ca eka-pūrvaka-tvam saṃyoga-vibhāgayoḥ iti//
074.05	[1-1-13]pṛthivī āpaḥ tejas vāyuḥ ākāśam iti bhūtāni
074.06	[1-1-14]gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdāḥ pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ tad-arthāḥ
074.07	gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdāḥ pṛthivī-adi-guṇāḥ tad-arthāḥ iti etad sūtram/
074.08	pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ iti etasmin pade anekaḥ samāsaḥ sambhavati/ katham iti/
074.09	pṛthivī-ādīnām guṇāḥ iti ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ/ pṛthivā-ādīni ca guṇāḥ ca iti
074.10	dvandvaḥ/ pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  guṇāḥ yeṣām iti bahuvrīhiḥ/ evam aneka-samāsa-upapatti^aḥ
074.11	saṃśayaḥ/ kim atra tattvam iti/ dvandvaḥ samāsaḥ iti tattvam/ ṣaṣṭhī-
074.12	samāsaḥ tāvat na pṛthivī-ādīnām anindriya-artha-tva-prasaṅgāt/ yadi pṛthivī-
074.13	ādīnām guṇāḥ iti ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ syāt pṛthivī-ādīnām gandha-ādi-guṇa-
074.14	viśeṣaṇa-tvena upayuktānām indriya-artha-tvam na syāt viśeṣaṇa-vaiyarthyam ca
074.15	syāt iti/ gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdāḥ iti ukte pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ gamyante
074.16	tataḥ ca pṛthivī-ādi-grahaṇam vyartham/ na hi gandha-ādīnām dvairāśyam asti
074.17	pṛthivī-ādi-guṇa-tvam ca anya-guṇa-tvam ca yataḥ viśeṣaṇam arthavat syāt tasmāt na
074.18	ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ/ guṇinām asiddhi^aḥ na bahuvrīhiḥ pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  guṇāḥ yeṣām ke
074.19	te guṇinaḥ yeṣām pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  guṇāḥ na ca pṛthivī-ādīnām guṇatvam śakyam
074.20	pratipādayitum na ca anyayā bahuvrīhiḥ prāyena ayam samānādhikaraṇa-pūrvaḥ
074.21	bhavati yathā citra-guḥ iti gomat-tāyām prasiddhāyām citra-guṇa-sambandhe
075.01	suprasiddhe gavām citra-guḥ iti bahuvrīhiḥ na punar iha guṇin^ḥ prasiddhaḥ
075.02	na api pṛthivī-ādīnām guṇatvam prasiddham/ viśeṣaṇa-tvāt guṇāḥ pṛthivī-ādyaḥ
075.03	iti syāt/ tad na sarvaḥ guṇaḥ eva syāt etasyām kalpanāyām na hi
075.04	kasya cit kaḥ cit viśeṣaṇam viśeṣyaḥ vā bhavati iti sarva-guṇa-tva-prasaṅgaḥ
075.05	tasmāt bahuvrīhiḥ api na/ ubhaya-samāsa-pratiṣedhāt anya-samāsa-abhāvāt ca dvandvaḥ/
075.06	yasmāt ṣaṣṭhī-tatpuruṣa-bahuvrīhī pratiṣiddhau samāsa-antaram ca na sambhavati
075.07	iti śiṣyamāṇaḥ ca dvandvaḥ tasmāt dvandvaḥ samāsaḥ iti/ na dvandvaḥ samāsaḥ
075.08	śāstra-yukti^yoḥ abhāvāt yadi ayam dvandvaḥ bhavati śāstram yuktiḥ vā pṛthivī-
075.09	ādīnām indriya-artha-tve vaktavye iti/ na ubhayasya api upapatti^aḥ iti/
075.10	ubhayam pṛthivī-ādīnām indriya-artha-tve śāstram yuktiḥ ca sambhavati/ śāstram
075.11	tāvat darśna-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt iti/ yuktiḥ api darśana-sparśanayoḥ
075.12	eka-viṣaya-tvena pratisandhānan yam aham adrākṣam tam spṛśāmi iti dṛṣṭi-sparśana-
075.13	viṣayā yuktiḥ/ śāstram api asmin pakṣe sambhavati iti aindriyaka-tva-abhidhānāt
075.14	ca sāmānyasya iti tasmāt siddham pṛthivī-ādīni ca guṇāḥ ca iti
075.15	dvandvaḥ samāsaḥ iti/ pṛthivī-ādi-grahaṇena pṛthivī-āp-tejas^i bāhyakaraṇa-
075.16	grāhyāṇi apadiśyante guṇa-grahaṇena ca sarvaḥ āśritaḥ guṇaḥ iti saṃkhyā-
075.17	parimāṇa-pṛthaktva-saṃyoga-vibhāga-paratva-aparatva-sneha-vega-karma-sāmānya-viśeṣāḥ anāśritaḥ
075.18	ca samavāyaḥ tad-dharma-tvāt guṇaḥ iti/ gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdāḥ tarhi
075.19	pṛthak na vaktavyāḥ guṇa-grahaṇena grahaṇāt/ pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ tad-arthāḥ iti
075.20	sūtre vaktavyam/ laghu ca evam bhavati iti saḥ eva arthaḥ setsyati iti na
075.21	kartavyam/ gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdānām pṛthak-abhidhānam indriya-viśeṣa-niyama-
075.22	jñāpana-artham/ indriyāṇi gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabda^su tad-sāmānya^su
076.01	niyatāni anyatra aniyatāni/ tatra pṛthivī-āp-tejas^i dvi-indriya-grāhyāṇi
076.02	śeṣaḥ ca guṇa-rāśiḥ sattā-guṇa-tve ca sarva-indriya-grāhye samavāyaḥ abhāvaḥ ca/
076.03	tathā darśana-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt iti etad amṛṣyamāṇaḥ paraḥ āha/
076.04	sparśa-grahaṇam eva sparśanena rūpa-grahaṇam eva cakṣuṣā iti/ sa paryanuyojyaḥ/
076.05	atha rūpa-sparśau cakṣus-sparśanābhyām gṛhete iti katham avagamyate/ yasmāt cakṣus-
076.06	sparśanābhyām tad-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyayaḥ bhavati/ anāspandaḥ tarhi pratiṣedhaḥ
076.07	ghaṭa-ādi^i api tad-viśiṣṭa-pratyaya-darśanāt ghaṭa-ādikam api artham darśana-sparśanābhyām
076.08	upalabhamānaḥ ca tad-viśiṣṭam ghaṭa-ādikam artham pratipadyate/ tasmāt asampradhāritaḥ
076.09	pratiṣedhaḥ iti/ rūpa-ādibhyaḥ tathā-sanniviṣṭa^bhyaḥ ghaṭa-ādi-pratyayaḥ
076.10	iti cet yad idam bhavatā apadiśyate darśana-sparśanābhyām ayam ghaṭam pratipadyate
076.11	iti/ mithyā etad rūpa-ādyaḥ  eva tena tena ākārena sanniviśante
076.12	tān ayam upalabdhā tathā-ākārān pratipadyamānaḥ ghaṭaḥ iti prapadyate na
076.13	punar rūpa-ādi-vyatiriktāḥ ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  iti/ na ākāra-artha-anabhidhānāt
076.14	atathā-bhūtasya tathā-bhūtena sāmānyam ākāra-śabdasya abhidheyam/ tad yathā
076.15	sthāṇu-ākāraḥ pumān iti apuruṣasya sthāṇu^aḥ ākāraḥ puruṣena yad
076.16	sāmānyam saḥ ākāra-śabdasya arthaḥ sthāṇu-puruṣa-prasiddhi^i satyām bhavati iti/
076.17	na punar bhavatām ghaṭa-ākārāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti rūpa-ādiṣu ghaṭa-ākāra-bījam
076.18	kim cit asti/ tasmāt ghaṭa-ākārāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti yad kim cit etad iti/
076.19	rūpa-ādi-mātre ca ghaṭa-ādi-buddhi-abhāvaḥ yasya rūpa-ādi-mātram ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  tasya
076.20	teṣām samāna-tvāt yātāḥ mā yāḥ tāḥ imāḥ buddhyaḥ  viviktāḥ go^ḥ aśvaḥ ghaṭaḥ iti
076.21	tāḥ na prāpnuvanti rūpa-ādi-gatasya buddhi-niyamakasya viśeṣasya hetu^aḥ
076.22	abhāvāt/ saṃsthāna-bhedāt bheda-pratyayaḥ iti cet kena cit saṃsthāna-viśeṣena
077.01	avatiṣṭhamānāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  saṃsthāna-bhedena anuvidhānāt ghaṭa-ādi-buddhi-hetu^āḥ
077.02	bhavanti iti na etad asti saṃsthānasya ananyatve saṃjñā-bheda-mātra-tvāt vyartha-abhidhāna-
077.03	prasaṅgāt ca yadi saṃsthānam rūpa-ādibhyaḥ anya-dravyam tad iti saṃjñā-bheda-
077.04	mātram atha ananyat tathā api rūpa-ādīnām saṃsthāna-bheda-anuvidhānāt ghaṭa-
077.05	ādi-pratyayāḥ iti vyartham abhidhānam/ ghaṭa-ādi-pratyayāḥ mithyā-pratyayāḥ
077.06	iti cet saḥ na manyeta ghaṭa-ādi-pratyayāḥ samyac-pratyayāḥ iti/ kim tu
077.07	śabda-vāsanā-vaśāt mithyā-pratyayāḥ bhavanti iti/ tad ayuktam/ mithyā-pratyayānām
077.08	samyac-pratyaya-anukāri-tvāt yatra te mithyā-pratyayāḥ loke bhavanti sarvatra
077.09	te samyac-pratyaya-anukāriṇaḥ bhavanti na punar ghaṭa-ādi-pratyayānām mithyā-
077.10	pratyaya-tvena kalpitānām bījam asti na ca ete nirbījāḥ prādurbhavanti
077.11	tasmāt na mithyā-pratyayāḥ iti/ mithyā-pratyayāḥ ca ete iti avyatireke pramāṇa-
077.12	upapatti^i satyām sidhyati na ca avyatireka-pratipādakam pramāṇam asti/
077.13	na na asti tad-agrahe tad-buddhi-abhāvāt yad yasmāt anartha-antaram bhavati tad-agrahe
077.14	tasya agrahaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tathā tad yathā yūṣasya paṅkti^aḥ ca/ yad ca yataḥ artha-antaram bhavati tad-agrahe
077.15	tasya grahaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tad yathā rūpa-ādi-agrahe bāla-grahaḥ iti/ na ayam hetuḥ
077.16	anaikāntika-tvāt ye ghaṭa-ādi-bhāvam āpannāḥ pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  teṣām pṛthivīām
077.17	saṃgṛhyamāṇāyām artha-antara-bhūtānām ap-ādīnām agrahaṇam/ atha ca artha-antara-
077.18	bhūtāḥ ap-ādyaḥ  na darśana-viṣaya-bhājaḥ bhavanti tasmāt anekāntaḥ atha na
077.19	catvāri mahābhūtāni tathā sanniviṣṭāni iti pakṣaḥ tathā api rūpam catvāri
077.20	mahabhūtāni iti vacanam vyāhanyate atha brūṣe na eva naḥ rūpa-ādi-vyatiriktāḥ
077.21	pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  iti tathā api asya vākyasya ayam arthaḥ bhavati rūpa-ādyaḥ
077.22	rūpam iti tataḥ kāme aṣṭa-dravyakaḥ aṇuḥ iti vyāhanyeta atha pṛthivī-ādīnām
078.01	saṃghāta-mātre rūpa-ādi-vyavahāraḥ tathā api na rūpam pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  vastutaḥ
078.02	santi/ katham iti/ pṛthivī-ādīnām saṃghātaḥ rūpam pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  ca
078.03	rūpa-ādi-saṃghātaḥ iti saṃhanyamānasya abhāvāt na saṃghātaḥ asti sarvaḥ saṃghātaḥ
078.04	saṃhanyamāna-tantraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti sarvathā na vyāghātāt mucyate tad-agrahe
078.05	tad-buddhi-abhāvāt iti tad-śabda-artha-anusṛti^i asambaddha-artham/ yadā tad-śabdasya
078.06	arthaḥ anusriyate tadā tad-agrahe tasya eva agrahaḥ iti prāptam/ tad ca asambaddha-artham
078.07	yadā pṛthivī-ādi rūpa-ādi-viśeṣaṇam sādhyate tadā tad-agrahe tad-buddhi-abhāvāt
078.08	iti asya hetu^aḥ pṛthivī-ādi-agrahe pṛthivī-ādi-buddhi-abhāvāt iti hetu-arthaḥ prāpnoti
078.09	kim kāraṇam pṛthivī-ādi-viśeṣaṇānām rūpa-ādīnām tad-śabdena anabhisambandhāt/
078.10	etena pṛthivī-ādi-viśeṣaṇāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  pratyuktāḥ/ atra api rūpa-ādi-agrahe
078.11	rūpa-ādi-buddhi-abhāvāt iti hetu-arthaḥ prāpnoti/ tad-śabdasya pradhāna-sambandhi-
078.12	tvāt/ evam rūpa-ādi-mātram pṛthivī pṛthivī-mātram rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti
078.13	pṛthivī eva rūpa-ādyaḥ  rūpa-ādyaḥ  eva pṛthivī iti pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ/ sarvatra
078.14	pratijñā-padayoḥ vyāghātaḥ vacana-bhedāt pṛthivī iti eka-vacanam rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti
078.15	bahuvacanam vacana-bheda-anuvidhānam ca yatra tatra artha-bhedaḥ/ tad yathā nakṣatrāṇi
078.16	śaśin^ḥ iti/ asti ca atra api vacana-bhedaḥ tasmāt atra api artha-bhedena bhavitavyam
078.17	nanu ekasmin arthe vacana-bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tad yathā catvāraḥ āśramāḥ cāturāśramyam
078.18	iti/ tad na anabhyupagamāt/ na ayam vacana-bhedaḥ kva cit ekatve dṛṣṭaḥ
078.19	cāturāśramyam iti caturṇām āśramāṇām samānam dharm-sādhana-tvam abhidhīyate/
078.20	etena ṣaḍ eva guṇāḥ ṣāḍguṇyam viśeṣaḥ eva viśeṣikam iti pratyuktam/
078.21	tad-agrahe tad-buddhi-abhāvāt iti ca asiddhaḥ api ayam hetuḥ katham asiddhaḥ rūpa-ādi-
078.22	agrahe dravya-grahāt yasmāt nīla-ādi-upadhāna-bheda-anuvidhāyinaḥ sphaṭika-maṇi^aḥ
079.01	rūpa-anupalabdhi^i sphaṭikaḥ iti pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yaḥ api ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ yūṣa-vat
079.02	paṅkti-vat ca iti tad na bhavatā yūṣaḥ vyajñāyi na paṅktiḥ/ yūṣaḥ hi nāma
079.03	utpanna-pākajānām dravyāṇām kāla-viśeṣa-anugrahe sati dravya-antara-saṃpṛktānām
079.04	pākaja-utpatti^i yaḥ saṃyogaḥ saḥ yūṣaḥ iti saḥ ca artha-antaravat
079.05	iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ etena pāka-kāñcika-viveka-avakṣāra-ādyaḥ  pratyuktāḥ/
079.06	paṅktiḥ api eka-diś-deśa-ambandhiṣu paraspara-pratyāsatti-anugṛhīta^su avadhārita-anavadhārita-
079.07	iyatteṣu bhinna-abhinna-jātīya^su ādhāra^su vartamānā bahutva-saṃkhyā
079.08	eva ucyate/ evam aniyata-diś-deśa-sambandhiṣu gaja-turaga-syandana^su para-pratyāsatti-
079.09	anugṛhīta^su avadhārita-anavadhārita-iyatteṣu vartamānā bahutva-saṃkhyā eva
079.10	senā iti ucyate/ evam jāti-viśiṣṭa-puruṣa-upagrahena vartamānā pariṣat pravrajita-
079.11	viśeṣa-upagrahena vartamānā saṅghaḥ/ evam yathā-sambhavam pūga-vana-yūtha-
079.12	brāhmaṇa-gaṇa-śabdāḥ api draṣṭavyāḥ/ evam aniyata-diś-deśa-sambandhiṣu anādi-
079.13	anta^su madhya-śūnya^su vartamānā kuṇḍalakam bahutva-saṃkhyā eva iti/ tasmāt paṅktiḥ
079.14	anartha-antaram iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ atha artha-antare kim pramāṇam iti
079.15	upalabhyasya samastaiḥ upalabhyaiḥ vyapadeśaḥ yad idam candanam upalabhyate tasya śvetam
079.16	rūpam tiktaḥ rasaḥ paṭuḥ gandhaḥ śītaḥ sparśaḥ iti/ yaḥ ca ayam pratyakṣasya
079.17	pratyakṣena vyapadeśaḥ saḥ artha-antare dṛṣṭaḥ yathā brāhmaṇasya kamaṇḍaluḥ iti/
079.18	na khalu evam pratipadyate yad idam śvetam rūpam tad anumīyamāna-rasa-gandha-sparśānām
079.19	iti/ senā-vana-ādibhiḥ anekāntaḥ iti cet atha api syāt buddhiḥ
079.20	dṛṣṭah anartha-antare api vyapadeśaḥ senāyāḥ hastī kānanasya vṛkṣaḥ iti na
079.21	asiddha-tvāt asiddham etad anartha-antara-tvam senāyāḥ kānanasya ca yathā ca
079.22	tad-artha-antaram senā vanam vā tad uktam iti tad asattvam iti cet atha api evam
080.01	kalpyet saṃkhyā eva tāvat na asti kutaḥ artha-antara-bhāvaḥ iti/ na eva saṃkhyā
080.02	na asti iti bruvatā eka-aneka-pratyayānām vidhīyamānam pratiṣidhyamānam vā
080.03	nimittam upādeyam/ kasmāt eka-aneka-pratyayānām pratyākhyeya-tvāt
080.04	viśiṣṭāḥ ca pratyayāḥ bhavantaḥ na nimitta-pratyākhyānena bhavitum arhanti
080.05	kim kāraṇam kumbha-pratyaya-anya-nimittakaḥ eka-aneka-pratyayaḥ kumbha-pratyaya-vilakṣaṇa-
080.06	tvāt nīla-pratyayavat tasmāt yad tad-pratyaya-nimittam sā saṃkhyā iti/
080.07	tad pratyaya-viṣaya-nimitta-pratyaya-vyatirekena tataḥ anyasya tad-viśiṣṭa-
080.08	pratyayasya utpatti^i nimitta-antara-ākāṅkṣi-tvāt yaḥ pratyaya-viṣaya-nimitta-
080.09	pratyaya-vyatirekena pratyayaḥ bhavati tasya nimitta-antara-ākāṅkṣi-tvam dṛṣṭam/
080.10	tad aytha vastra-carma-kambala-pratyayāḥ te eva bhavanti teṣu
080.11	tu yaḥ anyaḥ nīlam iti pratyayaḥ saḥ nimitta-antarāt bhavan dṛṣṭaḥ tathā ca
080.12	ghaṭa-ādiṣu api ghaṭa-aviṣaya-nimitta-vyatireka-bhāj tatra eka-ādi-pratyayāḥ
080.13	tasmāt taiḥ api nimitta-antarāt bhavitavyam/ yad nimittam te saṃkhyā-ādyaḥ  iti/
080.14	mahat-puṣpita-śabdābhyām sāmānādhikaraṇyāt na saṃkhyā senā vanam vā
080.15	bhavitum arhati/ yadi saṃkhyā senā vanam vā mahatī senā puṣpitam vanam
080.16	prāpnoti na saṃkhyāyāḥ mahatvam asti na puṣpa-yogaḥ iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/
080.17	mahatī senā iti kim ucyate tān eva gaja-ādīn saṃkhyā-anavadhārita-avadhārita-
080.18	iyattān sthāni-aṅga-antara-upacaye sati mahatī senā iti apadiśanti/ tatra iyam
080.19	saṃsthāniṣu mahat-śabda-vācya^su upacaya-upakaraṇāt bahutva-saṃkhyā mahatī
080.20	iti ucyate/ puṣpitam iti atra api puṣpa-sambandhiṣu vṛkṣa^su puṣpa-sambandhena eka-
081.01	artha-samavāyam anubhavantī bahutva-saṃkhyā eva abhidhīyate/ yathā madhuraḥ rasaḥ/
081.02	tiktaḥ guḍaḥ ca iti/ ataḥ eva vyapadeśāt saṃkhyā-ādīnām api artha-antara-bhāvaḥ/
081.03	asati darśanāt na hetuḥ yathā gartaḥ chidram iti na aparijñānāt gartaḥ
081.04	hi nāma rūpa-ādimat^bhiḥ sāvayava-ārabdha-kāryaiḥ madhya-śūnyaiḥ saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-
081.05	upahitam ākāśam iti/ etena chidram vyākhyātam/ ekasmin api darśanāt
081.06	vyapadeśāt artha-antara-abhāvaḥ na yuktaḥ/ tad yathā khadirasya sthāṇuḥ iti/
081.07	bahuṣu vā asaṃyukta^su darśanāt tad yathā prāsādānām mālā iti/ saṃyukta^su
081.08	vā asajātīya^su darśanāt trivṛt-pṛthivī-udaka-tejasām vivekaḥ iti
081.09	yad tāvat ekasmin api darśanāt iti/ tad ayuktam/ khadira-śabdasya jāti-
081.10	viśiṣṭa-dravya-vāci-tvāt khadira-śabdena jāti-viśiṣṭam dravyam ucyate sthāṇu-
081.11	śabdena api saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ abhidhīyate saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ ca pracaya-ākhyaḥ saṃyogaḥ
081.12	saḥ ca artha-antaram iti/ etena sarpa-kuṇḍalakam pratimāyāḥ śarīram suvarṇasya
081.13	aṅgurīyakam śilā-putrakasya śarīram iti ādikam bheda-vācakam iti uktam bhavati/
081.14	prāsādānām mālā paṅktivat draṣṭavyā saṃyoge vivekaḥ iti uktam etad guṇa-samudāya-
081.15	vyatirekena agrahaṇāt iti cet/ atha anumanyethāḥ yadi rūpa-ādi
081.16	vyatiriktam dravyam syāt tad rūpa-ādīn antareṇa pṛthak upalabhyeta na tu upalabhyate
081.17	tasmāt na asti agrahaṇasya dvi-nimitta-tvāt anekāntaḥ kim idam agrahaṇam grāhyasya
081.18	abhāvāt āho grahe hetu-abhāvaḥ iti sandihyate/ grāhya-abhāvāt iti cet
081.19	pratijñā-arthena hetu-arthasya ākṣepāt ayuktaḥ hetuḥ grahaṇa-abhāvāt iti cet
081.20	na vyatireka-pratiṣedhaḥ yaḥ ca yad-agrahe tad-buddhi-abhāvāt iti hetu^aḥ doṣaḥ
081.21	saḥ asya api draṣṭavyaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam ananya-tvam vādah yathā yathā vicāryate
081.22	tadā tadā spaṣtam anya-tvam pratipādayati pramāṇānām ekatra upapatti^aḥ ekatra
082.01	ca anupapatti^aḥ iti/ tasmāt vyavasthitam etad pṛthivī-ādīni guṇāḥ ca iti
082.02	dvandvaḥ samāsah//
082.03	atha buddhi^aḥ avasara-prāptāyāḥ lakṣaṇa-apadeśa-dvāreṇa sūtram//
082.04	[1-1-15]buddhiḥ upalabdhiḥ jñānam iti anartha-antaram
082.05	buddhiḥ upalabdhiḥ iti ādi/ etaiḥ paryāya-śabdaiḥ yaḥ abhidhīyate padārthaḥ
082.06	sā buddhiḥ iti/ paryāya-śabdāḥ katham lakṣaṇam vyavaccheda-hetu-tvāt sarvam hi
082.07	lakṣaṇam itaretara-padārtha-vyavacchedakam etaiḥ ca paryāya-śabdaiḥ na anyaḥ padārthaḥ abhidhīyate
082.08	iti asādhāraṇa-tvāt lakṣaṇam/ upapatti-sāmarthyāt paryāya-śabda-abhidhānāt
082.09	buddhi-vṛtti^aḥ nirākaraṇam/ ye āhuḥ buddhi^aḥ vṛttiḥ ātmanaḥ upalabdhiḥ
082.10	vṛttiḥ iti/ tad-nirākaraṇa-artham samāna-arthāḥ ete/ śabdāḥ iti
082.11	buddhi-vṛttiḥ pṛthak-nirākṛtā bhavati/ kā punar atra upapattiḥ bhavati
082.12	ātman^ḥ upalabhate buddhiḥ jānīte iti bruvāṇaḥ tayoḥ caitanyam pratipadyate
082.13	ubhaya-caitanye ca pratyaya-vyavasthā-anumānam syāt/ yadi buddhiḥ cetanā ātman^ḥ
082.14	cetanaḥ na buddhi-avasita-anartha-anātman^ḥ upalabhate kim kāraṇam cetana-upalabdhānām
082.15	cetana-antarasya apratipatti^aḥ asti ca pratipattiḥ tasmāt ekaḥ cetanaḥ iti
082.16	buddhi-vṛtti-aviśiṣṭāyām jñātṛ-vṛtti^i kalpitāyām ātmanaḥ svarūpam vaktavyam/
082.17	buddhiḥ adhyavasyati ātman^ḥ cetayate iti cet atha api idam syāt yathā buddhiḥ
082.18	arthān adhyavasyati tathā ātman^ḥ adhideśam dhikālam ca upalabhate iti/ adhyavasyati
082.19	upalabhate iti paryāya-śabdau na ca paryāya-śabdaiḥ padārtha-nānātvam
082.20	yuktam yathā dhvaniḥ nādaḥ iti anyathā hi dhvaniḥ nādaḥ iti atra api
082.21	nānātvam syāt atha ātman^ḥ cetayate buddhiḥ jñāpayati iti addhā buddhi^ā
082.22	jānīte puruṣaḥ na buddhiḥ buddhiḥ jñāpayati iti buddhiḥ jñāna-sādhanam bhavati iti//
083.01	smṛti-anumāna-āgama-saṃśaya-pratibhā-svapna-jñāna-ūhāḥ iti bhāṣyam/ ete
083.02	smṛti-ādyaḥ  manasaḥ liṅgam bhavanti/ kim ete eva kim tarhi iyam ca
083.03	yugapad-jñāna-anutpattiḥ manasaḥ liṅgam//
083.04	[1-1-16]yugapad-jñāna-anutpattiḥ manasaḥ liṅgam
083.05	yasmāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣe sati api yugapad-jñānāni sambhavanti/ ataḥ
083.06	gamyate asti tad-indriya-saṃyogi-sahakāri-nimitta-antaram avyāpin yasya
083.07	sannidhāna-asannidhāna-anuvidhānāt jñānasya utpatti-anutpattī bhavataḥ iti/
083.08	kutaḥ kāraṇa-vaikalye kārya-pratibandhāt iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ katham punar smṛti-
083.09	ādyaḥ  manasaḥ liṅgam bhavanti atra vartamānāḥ iti/ na brūmaḥ smṛti-ādyaḥ
083.10	manas-vṛtti-tvāt manasaḥ liṅgam bhavanti api tu gandha-ādi-kriyā-anya-tve sati
083.11	kriyā-tvāt karaṇa-antara-prayojaka-tvam yā gandha-ādibhyaḥ anyā kriyā sā
083.12	karaṇa-antara-prayojyā dṛṣṭā/ yathā ratha-ādi-kriyā/ kriyā ca iyam
083.13	smṛti-ādikā tasmāt iyam api karaṇa-antaram prayojayati/ sukha-ādyaḥ
083.14	rāga-anya-tve sati viṣaya-tvāt cakṣus-ādi-vyatirikta-karaṇa-antara-prayojikāḥ ratha-
083.15	ādi-vat iti/ tad yathā ratha-ādyaḥ  viṣayāḥ cakṣus-ādi-vyatiriktam karaṇam
083.16	vāsi^ā syāt iti prayojayanti viṣayāḥ ca sukha-ādyaḥ  tasmāt taiḥ api karaṇa-
083.17	antaram prayojayitavyam/ atha ayugapad-jñāna-utpattiḥ jñānadharmaḥ sā katham
083.18	manas-liṅgam bhavati/ atha asambaddham api liṅgam iṣyate sarvam sarvasya liṅgam
083.19	syāt asti ātman^ḥ kākasya kārṣṇyāt iti api syāt/ na tu evam bhavati/
083.20	tasmat na asambaddham liṅgam iti/ asambaddham api liṅgam bhavati iti eke yathā
083.21	pratyagra-śarāva-darśanam kulāla-avṛtti kulāla-sadbhāvam gamyati bhrāntimat cakra-
083.22	darśanam ca iti/ tad na na eva atra kulāla-sadbhāvaḥ boddhavyaḥ kim tu deśaḥ
084.01	eva adūra-kulāla-tvena sādhyaḥ tasya pratyagra-śarāvatvam dharmaḥ bhrāntimat-cakra-
084.02	tvena api deśaḥ avidūra-kulālavat-tvena sādhyate tasmāt na asambaddham anumānam
084.03	kva cit iti/ yugapad-jñāna-anutpattiḥ tarhi katham manasā asambaddhā manas-
084.04	liṅgam bhavati/ yugapad-jñāna-anutpatti^aḥ eva pakṣa^karaṇāt na asambaddham katham iti
084.05	rūpa-ādi-grahaṇāni cakṣus-ādi-vyatirekena adhiṣṭhāyaka-antara-apekṣāṇi ayugapad-
084.06	utpatti^aḥ/ tad yathā aneka-śilpa-paryavadātasya anekam vāsi-ādi yugapad
084.07	sannidhānena upasthitam hasta-ādi-adhiṣṭhāyaka-apekṣam na yugapad aneka-ratha-ādi-kriyām
084.08	nivartayati yathā cakṣus-ādi na yugapad anekam jñānam karoti tasmāt tad
084.09	api adhiṣṭhāyaka-antaram apekṣate iti indriyāṇi vā pakṣa^kṛtya vaktavyam/ katham
084.10	iti cakṣus-ādi-indriyam ātma-prayukta-avaṣṭhāyaka-antara-apekṣam ayugapad-pravṛtti^aḥ
084.11	vāsi-ādi-vat iti/ yadi tarhi cakṣus-ādi-indriyam manas-sanndihāna-apekṣam
084.12	arthānām ayugapad-grāhin ye eka-indriya-grāhyāḥ teṣu katham ayugapad-grahaṇam na hi
084.13	na tatra asannihitam manas iti/ sambandhi-bhedāt iti cet yugapad-sambandhiṣu
084.14	katham bhavatu tāvat nīla-ādi-bheda^su sambandhi-bhedāt ayugapad-grahaṇam atha ye
084.15	yugapad sambadhyante katham śuklaḥ go^ḥ gacchati iti pratyayāḥ yugapad prāpnuvanti
084.16	bubhutsā-bhedāt ayugapad-grahaṇam iti eke eke tāvat bruvate sambaddhaḥ api arthaḥ abubhutsita-
084.17	tvāt na gṛhyate iti/ tad na/ manas-vṛtti-prasaṅgāt evam tarhi manas
084.18	parityājyam yadi bubhutsā-bhedāt ayugapad-grahaṇāni bhavanti/ anyatra
084.19	abubhutsā-bhedaḥ na daṇḍa-vāritaḥ iti manas idānīm anarthakam parityājyam/
084.20	satyām ca bubhutsāyām ayugapad-grahaṇe nyāyaḥ anyaḥ avadhāraṇīyaḥ tasmāt anya-
084.21	nimittam ayugapad-grahaṇam iti kim punar nimittam karaṇatvam yathā eva ayam
084.22	karaṇa-dharmaḥ nānā-adhiṣṭhitam karaṇam pravartate iti/ tathā sambaddhasya api
085.01	aneka-kriyā-kāritvam na asti na hi karaṇam sambaddham api sat ekadā anekām
085.02	kriyām bhinnām śaknoti kartum/ yadi punar ātman^ḥ eva adhiṣṭhātṛ^ḥ bhavet
085.03	tathā api ātmanaḥ yugapad-aneka-indriya-sambaddhasya aniṣedhāt ayugapad-jñāna-utpatti^i
085.04	nyāyaḥ anyaḥ avadhāryaḥ/ saḥ ca na antareṇa manas iti siddham manas//
085.05	[1-1-17]pravṛttiḥ vāc-buddhi-śarīra-ārambhaḥ iti
085.06	pravṛttīiḥ vāc-buddhi-śarīra-ārambhaḥ iti/ śarīrena manasā vācā ca/
085.07	sā iyam pravṛttiḥ prayekam daśa-vidhā puṇyā ca pāpā ca/ puṇyā kāyena
085.08	paritrāṇam paricaraṇam dānam iti/ vācā satyam hitam priyam sva-adhyāyaḥ
085.09	iti/ manasā dayā spṛhā śraddhā ca iti/ viparyayena pāpā daśa-vidhā eva/
085.10	kṣaṇika-tvāt pravṛtti^aḥ janma-kāraṇa-tva-anupapattiḥ sā iyam pravṛttiḥ kṣaṇikā ca
085.11	satī na janma-kāraṇam iti yuktam/ tataḥ ca dvitīya-sūtra-vyāghātaḥ na
085.12	pravṛtti-phale pravṛtti-upacārāt/ pravṛtti-phalam dharma-adharmau asmin sūtre
085.13	pravṛttiḥ iti ucyate kasmāt tad-sādhana-tvāt annam vai prāṇinaḥ prāṇāḥ iti//
085.14	[1-1-18]pravartanā-lakṣaṇāḥ doṣāḥ
085.15	pravartanā-lakṣaṇāḥ doṣāḥ/ kā punar iyam pravartanā yayā avaśaḥ pravartate
085.16	tam pravartamānam rāga-ādyaḥ  pravartayanti iti/ sā iyam pravṛtti-hetu-tvāt
085.17	pravartanā iti ucyate yathā kāraṇā hāraṇā iti/ sā punar iyam pravartanā katham
085.18	gamyate pratyātmam pratyakṣā para-śarīre tu ānumānikī ātman-vat iti/ yathā
085.19	ātman-ahaṅkāra-viṣaya-tvena avatiṣṭhamānaḥ pratyātmam pratyakṣaḥ bhavati/ na hi iyam
085.20	ahaṅkāra-buddhiḥ ānumānikī/ na āgamikī/ katham na ānumānikī liṅga-
086.01	abhāvat/ katham na aupadeśikī anupadiṣṭa-artha-pratipatti^aḥ tasmāt rūpa-ādi-vat
086.02	pratyātmam ātman^ḥ pratyakṣaḥ iti/ pravṛtti-nivṛtti-anumeyaḥ tu para-śarīre iti//
086.03	[1-1-19]punar-utpattiḥ pretyabhāvaḥ
086.04	punar-utpattiḥ pretyabhāvaḥ/ pūrva-upātta-śarīra-ādi-parityāgāt anya-śarīra-
086.05	upasaṃkrāntiḥ saḥ pretyabhāvaḥ iti/ punar grahaṇam saṃsāra-anādi-tva-jñāpana-
086.06	artham/ punar punar janma-maraṇe bhavataḥ iti anādi-tvam saṃsārasya darśayati/ kaḥ
086.07	punar ayam saṃsāraḥ duḥkha-ādīnām mithyā-jñāna-paryantānām avicchedena kārya-
086.08	kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ saṃsāraḥ iti/ saḥ ca anādiḥ/ pūrva-apara-kāla-niyama-abhāvāt/
086.09	yadi pūrvam duḥkham tad-janmanā vinā na yuktam/ atha pūrvam janman tadā
086.10	dharma-adharmau antareṇa na yuktam/ atha pūrvam dharma-adharmau tau api vinā
086.11	rāga-dveṣābhyām na yuktau/ atha pūrvam rāga-dveṣau na mithyā-jñānāt ṛte tayoḥ
086.12	prādurbhāvaḥ iti/ mithyā-jñānam tarhi ādiḥ tad api śarīra-ādīn antareṇa na
086.13	yuktam/ saḥ ayam duḥkha-ādīnām mithyā-jñāna-paryantānām kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ
086.14	avicchinnaḥ saṃsāraḥ iti/   ājaraṃjarībhāvaḥ  ājavaṃjavībhāvaḥ iti codyate/ saḥ ayam
086.15	saṃsāraḥ kim ātmanaḥ āho manasaḥ iti/ yadi kriyām adhikṛtya ucyate
086.16	tadā manasaḥ tad hi saṃsarati/ atha upabhogam adhikṛtya ucyate tadā ātmanaḥ saḥ
086.17	hi sukha-duḥkhe upabhuṅkte iti//
086.18	[1-1-20]pravṛtti-doṣa-janitaḥ arthaḥ phalam
086.19	pravṛtti-doṣa-janitaḥ arthaḥ phalam/ śrīra-ādi-janyam sarvam phalam dharma-
086.20	adharma-pravartaka-tvāt mukha-kalpanāyām sukha-duḥkha-upabhogaḥ eva tad-avasānāt
086.21	yasmāt dharma-adharmayoḥ sukha-duḥkha-upabhogaḥ āvasānikam phalam tasya tu na antareṇa
087.01	śarīra-ādīn saṃbhavaḥ iti kṛtvā śarīra-ādīn dharma-adharmau ārabhya
087.02	sukha-duḥkha-upabhogam kurutaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam phala-śabdaḥ sukha-duḥkha-upabhoge
087.03	mukhyaḥ śarīre gauṇaḥ iti//
087.04	[1-1-21]bādhanā-lakṣaṇam duḥkham iti
087.05	bādhanā-lakṣaṇam duḥkham/ etad eva śarīra-ādi bādhanā-anuṣaṅgāt duḥkham
087.06	iti ucyate/ svabhāvataḥ tu duḥkham eva duḥkham lakṣaṇa-śabdaḥ anuṣaṅga-arthaḥ/ sarvam idam
087.07	śarīra-ādi duḥkha-anuṣaṅktam iti/ atra śarīram duḥkhasya nimittam nimitta-
087.08	arthaḥ anuṣaṅgaḥ/ indriyāṇi viṣayāḥ buddhyaḥ  iti sādhanam sādhana-arthaḥ
087.09	anuṣaṅgaḥ/ sukham duḥkha-avinābhāvin atra api avinābhāvaḥ anuṣaṅgaḥ/ svarūpataḥ tu
087.10	duḥkham duḥkham iti sarvam svarūpataḥ duḥkham iti ke cit/ na pratyakṣa-
087.11	virodhāt/ na hi pratyakṣam sukham śakyam pratyākhyātum/ duḥkha-vikalpaḥ
087.12	iti cet atha api idam syāt dukha-vikalpaḥ eva sukham iti/ na punar
087.13	svarūpataḥ asti iti na vikalpena nañ-prayoga-abhāvāt/ na vikalpaḥ na
087.14	nañ-vartamānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ na hi brāhmaṇa-viśeṣe bhavati abrāhmaṇaḥ iti/ duḥkham
087.15	duḥkha-viśeṣe aduḥkham iti na syāt/ yadi ca svam na syāt dharma-vaiyarthyam/
087.16	kim kāraṇam sukha-sādhanam dhramaḥ iti sukhaṃ ca na asti iti vyarthaḥ dharmaḥ
087.17	duḥkha-pratiṣedhaḥ phalam asya iti na yuktam/ dharmasya abhāva-phala-tva-prasaṅgāt
087.18	abhāva-phalaḥ dhramaḥ iti pravṛtti-dvaitam ca loke dṛṣṭam tad na syāt/
087.19	hitam āpsyāmi iti ekaḥ pravartate/ aniṣṭam hāsyāmi iti aparaḥ pravartate hitasya
087.20	abhāvāt pravṛtti-dvaitam ca na syāt duḥkha-bhāvanā-upadeśaḥ na syāt pratipakṣa-
087.21	abhāvāt saḥ hi abhāvaḥ na hi kaḥ cit duḥkhe sajyate iti/ tasmāt na mukhyaḥ
087.22	sarvam duḥkham duḥkha-bhāvanā-upadeśena ca duḥkham iti ucyate//
088.01	[1-1-22]tad-atyanta-vimokṣaḥ apavargaḥ
088.02	tad-atyanta-vimokṣaḥ apavargaḥ iti/ tena śarīra-ādinā duḥkhena ātyantikaḥ
088.03	viyogaḥ iti/ ātyantikī sukha-abhivyaktiḥ iti eke eke tu ātyantikīm
088.04	sukha-abhivyaktim muktim bruvate/ tad na/ pramāṇa-asambhavāt/ muktasya ātmanaḥ nityam
088.05	sukham iti na pramāṇam asti/ na na asti niyāmaka-tvāt iti cet aneka-
088.06	kāraṇa-niṣpanna-sukhasya ātmani samavāyaḥ niyāmakam antareṇa na yuktaḥ
088.07	tasmāt ātmani nityam sukham asti/ yena idam sukham niyamyamānam ātmānam
088.08	āśrayate iti cet na sarva-utpattimat-prasaṅgāt sukhavat-ātmani duḥkham api
088.09	nityam kalpayitavyam icchā-ādyaḥ  ca iti/ anaikāntikatā vā abhivyakti-
088.10	artha-anabhidhānāt ca/ nityam sukham abhivyajyate iti kaḥ abhivyakti-arthaḥ jñānam
088.11	iti cet nityam anityam vā iti kalpanā-anupapattiḥ/ anityam iti
088.12	cet kāraṇam vaktavyam/ ātman-manas-saṃyogaḥ vā kāraṇam tasya apekṣā-
088.13	kāraṇam vaktavyam/ yadi manyase ātman-manas-saṃyogaḥ jñānasya kāraṇam
088.14	tasya tarhi apekṣā-kāraṇam vaktavyam/ na hi dravya-guṇa-karmasu ārabdha^su saṃyogaḥ
088.15	nirapekṣam kāraṇam iti/ ātman-manas-saṃyogaḥ sukha-apekṣaḥ iti cet na
088.16	kaivalya-virodhāt/ atha manyase ātman-manas-saṃyogaḥ nityam ātmani
088.17	vyavasthitam sukham apekṣamāṇaḥ jñāna-kāraṇam bhavati iti tad na yuktam kaivalya-
088.18	virodhāt/ yathā ayam ātman-manas-saṃyogaḥ viṣaya-mātram apekṣamāṇaḥ nimitta-
088.19	antara-nirapekṣaḥ sukha-jñānam karoti/ evam rūpa-ādīnām api viṣayān
088.20	apekṣamāṇam tad-viṣayāṇi jñānāni kuryāt/ tataḥ ca kaivalyam nivartate/
089.01	sarvān arthān ayam ātman^ḥ labhate iti nitya-upalabdhi-prasaṅgāt ca iti/ yogaja-
089.02	dharma-anugṛhīte nitye sukhe jñānam kariṣyati iti cet na yuktam/ tad-kṣaye
089.03	tad-abhāvāt yadā yogajaḥ dharmaḥÊkṣīnaḥ bhavati tadā asya kim anugrāhakam
089.04	iti vācyam yogaja-dharmaḥ na kṣīyate iti cet na yuktam/
089.05	utpatti-dharmakasya sarvasya anitya-bhāvāt sarvam utpatti-dharmakam anityam dṛṣṭam
089.06	iti/ nityaḥ asau yoga-samādhi-haḥ dhramaḥ iti cet yogaja-samādhijaḥ ca
089.07	nityaḥ ca iti vyāhatam nityam jñānam iti cet/ na/ sukhavat pramāṇa-
089.08	abhāvāt mukta-saṃsāra-sthayoḥ aviśeṣa-prasaṅgāt ca/ sukha-duḥkha-vedana-paryāyaḥ ca
089.09	na syāt nityam ayam sukham upalabheta tataḥ ca mokṣa-arthaḥ prayāsaḥ vyarthaḥ
089.10	syāt na ca ayam sukham jihāsati viveka-hānasya aśakya-tvāt duḥkham
089.11	jihāsamānaḥ sukham api jihāsati/ na ca ayam kadā cit duḥkham upalabhate
089.12	iti kasya hāna-artham pravartate śarīra-ādi-prabandhāt aprasaṅgaḥ iti cet nityam
089.13	sukham upalabhate iti na asti prasaṅgaḥ yasmāt śarīra-ādyaḥ  nitya-sukhasya upalabdhi^aḥ
089.14	pratibandhakāḥ bhavanti iti na śarīra-ādīnām upabhoga-artha-tvāt nitya-
089.15	śarīra-ādi-prasaṅgāt ca/ yathā muktasya nityam sukham kalpyate evam
089.16	śarīra-ādyaḥ  api nityāḥ kalpayitavyāḥ/ evam asya vaikalyam kaivalyam sādhīyaḥ
089.17	bhavati śarīra-ādīnām nitya-tvam dṛṣṭa-viruddham aśakyam kalpayitum iti cet
089.18	na sukhasya api tarhi asmad-ādi^aḥ adṛṣṭa-viruddham anitya-tvam śakyam kalpayitum/
089.19	pramāṇa-abhāvāt iti uktam tad ca na asti kasmāt iṣṭa-artha-adhigama-artha-tvāt
089.20	iha ayam lokaḥ pravartamānaḥ iṣṭa-adhigama-artham pravartate pravartante ca mokṣamāṇāḥ
090.01	teṣām api iṣṭa-adhigama-arthayā pravṛtti^ā bhavitavyam sā iyam pravṛttiḥ
090.02	nitya-sukhe arthavatī na anyathā iti/ na/ pravṛtti-dvaita-darśanāt/ dve pravṛttī
090.03	loke dṛṣṭe iṣṭa-adhigama-arthā aniṣṭa-hāna-arthā ca/ tatra idam pārivrājyam kim
090.04	iṣṭa-adhigama-artham āho aniṣṭa-hāna-artham iti sandihyate āgamāt iti cet
090.05	āgamāt etad gamyate muktasya ātmanaḥ nityam sukham iti muktaḥ sukhin^ḥ bhavati iti
090.06	śrūyate/ āgamaḥÊapi evam vicāraṇīyaḥ kim ayam nityena sukhena yogam āha
090.07	uta duḥkhena ātyantika-viyogam āha iti/ dṛṣṭaḥ ca duḥkha-abhāve api sukha-śabda-
090.08	prayogaḥ bahudhā loke iti/ evam ca jvara-ādi-viyoge laukikāḥ vyācakṣāṇāḥ
090.09	bhavanti sukhinaḥ saṃvṛttāḥ smaḥ iti/ yadi punar ayam saṃcakṣāṇakaḥ
090.10	mokṣe nityam sukham iti sukha-rāgena pravartate na mucyeta kasmāt rāgasya
090.11	bandhana-samājñānāt bandhana-samājñātaḥ rāgaḥ iti yadi api dveṣāt
090.12	pravartate duḥkham hāsyāmi iti tathā api na mucyeta dveṣasya bandhana-samājñānāt
090.13	iti rāga-dveṣau hi bandhanam iti na pratikūla-tvāt/ apratikūlam
090.14	duḥkha-hānam bhavati na punar ayam duḥkham dveṣṭi adviṣan ca ayam pravartamānaḥ
090.15	pratikūlam duḥkha-hānam adhigacchati/ cittam vimucyate iti anye rāga-ādīnām
090.16	tatra sāmarthyāt/ yad rāga-ādi-vaśam cittam ālambana-antara-gati-antara^su utpadyate
090.17	na punar ātmani rāga-ādīnām sāmarthyam asti/ na/ ayane  ayatnataḥ tad-siddhi^aḥ/ ye
090.18	cittasya anutpādanam virodham vā apavargam icchanti teṣām ayane  ayatnataḥ siddhaḥ
090.19	mokṣaḥ kim kāraṇam janmanaḥ vināśa-artha-tvāt jātam nivaśyad ayane siddham ayatna-siddham
090.20	santati^aḥ anutpādaḥ apavargaḥ iti cet/ na/ tasya aśakya-tvāt/ santati^aḥ
091.01	utpādaḥ na śakyate kartum/ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-pravāhasya santati-bhāvāt
091.02	anāgata-anutpādaḥ kriyate iti cet anāgata-anutpādasya vidyamāna-
091.03	tvāt kim kriyate iti sarvathā na cittasya apavargaḥ siddhyati iti kasya
091.04	tarhi apavargaḥ yaḥ apavṛjyate kaḥ pavṛjyate ātman^ḥ/ atha kā apavṛktiḥ
091.05	duḥkha-ādibhiḥ viyogaḥ iti//
091.06	prameya-anantaram prāptiḥ saṃsayasya sthānavataḥ lakṣaṇa-vacanam iti yā
091.07	prameya-anantaram krama-prāptiḥ sā abhidhīyate//
091.08	[1-1-23]samāna-aneka-dharma-utpatti^aḥ vipratipatti^aḥ upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-
091.09	avyavasthātaḥ ca viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ
091.10	samāna-aneka-dharma-utpatti^aḥ iti sūtram/ tatra samāna-dharma-utpatti^aḥ aneka-
091.11	dharma-utpatti^aḥ vipratipatti^aḥ ca saṃśayaḥ/ trividhaḥ eva saṃśayaḥ itara-pada-viśeṣaṇāt
091.12	bhavati iti sūtra-arthaḥ/  tatra viṣaya-svarūpa-anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ saṃśayaḥ samāna-dharma-ādibhyaḥ utpannaḥ viṣayasya viṣayasya
091.13	viśeṣam na avadhārayati yaḥ saḥÊsaṃśayaḥ iti ucyate/ pratyayaḥ na avadhāraṇa-
091.14	ātmakaḥ ca iti vyāhatam pratyayasya etad yadvat viṣaya-avadhāraṇa-ātmaka-tvam na ca idam
091.15	viṣaya-svarūpam avadhārayati pratyayatvam vyāhatam bhavati na svarūpa-pratyāyanāt
091.16	svarūpasya pratīyate na punar ayam viṣaya-svarūpam avadhārayati ataḥ ca
091.17	saṃśayaḥ pratīyate iti pratyayaḥ ucyate/ samāna-śabdaḥ sādhāraṇa-arthaḥ
091.18	samānasya dharma-upapatti^aḥ sādhāraṇasya iti yāvat/ kim punar atra sādhāraṇam
091.19	kim guṇaḥ āho sāmānyam iti/ yadi guṇaḥ saḥ na sādhāraṇaḥ
091.20	kasmāt ekad-dravya-vṛtti-tvāt pramāṇasya eka-dravya-vṛtti parimāṇam tad
091.21	katham sādhāraṇam bhaviṣyati iti sāmānyam api na yuktam dravya-avṛtti-tvāt
092.01	na hi ūrdhvatvam dravye vartate kim tu guṇe/ na hi ūrdhvatvam parimāṇe pravartamānam
092.02	sāmānyam dravye saṃśayam kartum arhati kasmāt guṇasya avadhārita-tvāt yad-vṛtti
092.03	sāmānyaḥ saḥ avadhāritaḥ iti na sādhāraṇa-arthasya anyathā-vyākhyānāt/ na
092.04	brūmaḥ guṇaḥ sādhāraṇaḥ iti/ na api sāmānyam api tu sādṛśya-arthaḥ/
092.05	yāvat aham arthau pūrvam adrākṣam tayoḥ arthaḥ dharmaḥ ūrdhvatva-lakṣaṇaḥ vartate tena dharmena
