092.06	sadṛśaḥ ayam dharmaḥ upalabhate iti tasya upapattiḥ adhyavasāyaḥ yad uktam bhavati
092.07	sadṛśasya dharmasya upalabdhi^aḥ tad uktam bhavati samānasya dharmasya upapatti^aḥ iti
092.08	yasmāt punar evam na ucyate samāna-dharma-upalabdhi^aḥ iti ayuktam api yasmāt
092.09	gamyate gamyamānasya abhidhānam vyartham kena punar etad gamyate iti
092.10	viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti vacanena katham viśeṣasya apekṣā ākāṅkṣā sā ca anupalabdhyamāna-
092.11	viśeṣe yuktā/ yadi ca ayam viśeṣam na paśyati viśeṣa-anupalabdhi^aḥ
092.12	gamyate evam sāmānyam paśyati atha punar na ayam sāmānyam viśeṣam
092.13	paśyet tadā viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti vyartham vacanam syāt anena sāmarthyena gamyate
092.14	sāmānyam upalabhate iti/ atha yā upalabdhi-paryāyaḥ eva upapatti-śabdaḥ iti
092.15	upapattiḥ pramāṇa-gamyatā sā ca upalabdhiḥ yaḥ punar anupalabhyamāna-sadbhāvaḥ
092.16	dharmaḥ saḥ avidyamānavat bhavati iti/ kā punar vidyamānasya aidyamānena samānatā
092.17	pramāṇa-anālambana-tvāt/ avidyamānam api pramāṇasya ālambanam svatantram
092.18	na bhavati tad api anupalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptam iti viṣaya-śabdena vā viṣayiṇam
092.19	pratyāha  prati ayam āha samāna-dharma-upapatti-śabdena vā viṣayin^ḥ pratyayaḥ vidhīyate laukikam
092.20	vā nyāyam anena vākyena avaruṇaddhi iti yathā loke vaktṛ^āḥ bhavanti/
093.01	dhūmena agniḥ anumīyate iti na ca vākye darśana-śabdaḥ śrūyate/ artha-
093.02	pratyāyaka-tvāt na vākye darśana-śabdam anujānāti dhūmam dṛṣṭvā artha agniḥ anumīyate
093.03	iti/ avyavaccheda-hetu^aḥ iti vaktavyam/ yad idam samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
093.04	iti padam etad na vyavaccheda-hetu^aḥ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti vaktavyam/
093.05	na hi kevalā samāna-dharma-upapattiḥ saṃśaya-kāraṇam anyathā kṛtakatva-ādinā
093.06	api saṃśayaḥ syāt samānam kṛtakatvam sarva-anityānām iti vyavaccheda-
093.07	hetu-tvāt na bhavati/ na/ samāna-artha-aparijñānāt/ vyavaccheda-hetuḥ ca bhavati
093.08	samānaḥ ca dharmaḥ iti na yujyate vyavaccheda-hetuḥ nāma vivakṣita-tad-jātīya-
093.09	vṛtti-tve sati yaḥ vijātīya-vṛttiḥ saḥ vyavaccheda-hetuḥ tasya ca samāna-
093.10	artha-tā na asti/ samānaḥ hi dharmaḥ yaḥ vivakṣita-tad-jātīya-vṛtti-tve
093.11	sati anya-jātīya-vṛttiḥ tasmāt avyavaccheda-hetu^aḥ iti na vaktavyam/
093.12	saḥ ayam sādhāraṇaḥ dharmaḥ upalabhyamānaḥ saṃśaya-hetuḥ kim kevalaḥ iti na
093.13	kevalaḥ kim tarhi upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthiti^aḥ ca/ yadi ca upalabdhi-anupalabdhī
093.14	na vyavasthite bhavataḥ iti kim etāvat-mātram sādhanam iti
093.15	na iti ucyate/ yadi viśeṣa-ākāṅkṣā bhavati samāna-dharmam upalabhate/ upalabdhi-
093.16	anupalabdhī na vyavatiṣṭhete/ idam tu yāvān idantayā vā viśeṣa-
093.17	ākāṅkṣāyām ca satyām artha-sandehaḥ bhavati iti/ kim idam samastam kāraṇam
093.18	asamastam vā samastam iti brūmaḥ/ yadi samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti kevalam
093.19	ucyate/ upalabdha-viṣayasya api sāmānyataḥ upalabdhiḥ asti iti tasya
093.20	api saṃśayaḥ syāt/ yadi punar upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthitiḥ ca saṃśayaḥ
093.21	iti etāvat ucyeta anupalabdha-sāmānyasya api kva cit upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthā
093.22	asti iti saṃśayaḥ syāt/ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti etāvati ca ucyamāne anupalabdha-
094.01	sāmānyasya api kva cit viśeṣa-smṛtiḥ asti iti saṃśayaḥ syāt/ evam samāna-
094.02	dharma-upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthitiḥ ca iti pada-dvaye api nau-yāna-preṅkha-ādi-gatasya
094.03	na bhavati saṃśayaḥ/ evam upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātaḥ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti
094.04	pada-dvaye vidhīyamāne anyatra api anupalabdhi^aḥ arthe saṃśayaḥ syāt/ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
094.05	viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti ca ucyamāne samāna-darśane sati api viśeṣa-ākāṅkṣāyām
094.06	satyām upalabdhi-anupalabdhi^oḥ vyavasthānāt bhavati saṃśayaḥ/ yadā ayam draṣṭṛ pūrvam
094.07	sāmānya-viśeṣavantam upalabhate tatra ca upalabdhi-anupalabdhī vyavatiṣṭhete saḥ ayam
094.08	draṣṭṛ tasmāt sthānāt yadā apaiti tataḥ asya apagamāt viprakarṣāt nimittāt
094.09	alpa-viṣaya-viśeṣāḥ na ābhāsante mahā-viṣayam sāmānyam ābhāsate upalabdhi-
094.10	anupalabdhī punar vyavatiṣṭhete viśeṣa-anusmṛtiḥ ca asti na ca sandihyate
094.11	tasmāt eka-dvi-pada-paryudāsena samastam lakṣaṇam ucyate iti/ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
094.12	upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātaḥ ca viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti ca eka-pada-paryudāsaḥ
094.13	kṛtaḥ/ samāna-dharma-upalabdhi^aḥ upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātaḥ ca samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
094.14	viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti ca upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātaḥ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ
094.15	iti ca dvi-pada-paryudāsaḥ kṛtaḥ/ samasta-pada-parigrahena yasmāt samāna-
094.16	dharma-upalabdhi^aḥ upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātaḥ ca viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ
094.17	iti āha/ tena jñāpayati samastam etad lakṣaṇam iti/ vimarśaḥ iti
094.18	nānā-artha-avamarśaṇam vimarśaḥ/ ubhau arthau mṛśati iva saṃśītiḥ saṃśayaḥ
094.19	bhāva-sādhanam/ karaṇa-sādhanam vā/ saṃśayyate anena ātman^ḥ suptaḥ iva
094.20	bhavati kaḥ upamā-arthaḥ artha-anavadhāraṇam upamā-arthaḥ/ etena aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
094.21	vipratipatti^aḥ ca iti vyākhyātam/ kaḥ atideśa-arthaḥ yathā ādya-pade tri-pada-
095.01	parigrahena eka-pade dvi-pada-parityāgaḥ kṛtaḥ/ tathā ādya-anantarayoḥ api
095.02	draṣṭavyam iti/ aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ anekasya anekaḥ ca dharmaḥ
095.03	iti cet anekasya dharmaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ/ anekasya dravya-guṇa-karma-lakṣaṇasya
095.04	saṃyogajatvam dharmaḥ anekaḥ ca dharmaḥ saṃyogajatva-nirguṇatva-niṣkriyatva-kṣaṇikatvāni
095.05	śabde tad evam aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti cet tad ayuktam
095.06	samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti atra carita-artha-tvāt/ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti anena eva
095.07	yaḥ ca ekoneka-vṛttiḥ ca aneka-vṛttiḥ saḥ labhyate iti carita-artha-tvāt/ na
095.08	punar aneka-dharma-abhidhānena prayojanam asti iti/ atha aneka-dharma-śabdasya kaḥ arthaḥ
095.09	asādharaṇaḥ dharmaḥ katham punar asādhāraṇaḥ dharmaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ iti anena
095.10	samāna-padena abhidhīyate samāna-asamāna-jātīya-viśeṣaka-tvāt samāna-
095.11	asamāna-jātīyam ca anekam tasmāt viśeṣakaḥ dharmaḥ anekasmāt viśeṣaḥ aneka-
095.12	dharmaḥ iti tasya vā anekasya dharmaḥ yathā svam saḥ ayam aneka-dharmaḥ iti/
095.13	eka-aneka-pratyaya-hetuḥ vā dharmaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ yataḥ eva pratyayaḥ bhavati idam ekam
095.14	anekam iti/ tatra eka-pratyaya-hetuḥ abhedaḥ/ aneka-pratyaya-hetuḥ dharmaḥ viśeṣaḥ
095.15	yathā śabdasya vibhāgajatvam/ sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāya^bhyaḥ śabdasya
095.16	sat-ādinā viśeṣena nirbhaktasya tasmin tu dravyam guṇaḥ karman vā iti vibhāgajatvāt
095.17	saṃśayaḥ/ na hi dravya-guṇa-karmaṇām anyatamam vibhāgāt jāyamānam
095.18	dṛṣṭam sarvatra asambhavāt vibhāgajatvam saṃśayam karoti sarvataḥ vyāvṛtti^aḥ
095.19	iti/ nanu ca vibhāgajaḥ vibhāgaḥ vidyate guṇaḥ/ anabhyupagata-
095.20	vibhāgaja-vibhāgasya etad evam bhavati/ yaḥ punar anabhyūpagata-vibhāgaja-vibhāgaḥ
095.21	tasya ayam nirṇaya-hetuḥ guṇaḥ śabdaḥ vibhāgajatvāt vibhāgaja-vibhāga-vat
095.22	iti/ astu vā tasya api vibhāgaja-vibhāga-asamavāyi-kāraṇa-tvāt
096.01	vibhāgaja-vibhāga-asamavāyi-kāraṇaḥ śabdaḥ na anyaḥ padārthaḥ iti/ tad evam
096.02	vibhagajatvam vibhāgaja-vibhāga-asamavāyi-kāraṇa-tvam na te  na ṛteśabdāt bhavati iti
096.03	sarvataḥ vyāvṛttaḥ saṃśaya-hetuḥ tulya-jātīya^su artha-antara-bhūta^su viśeṣasya
096.04	ubhayathā dṛṣṭa-tvāt iti/ samāna-dharma-upayogāt vā asamāna-aneka-dharmaḥ bhavati/
096.05	yad vā dvayasya api saṃśaya-kāraṇa-tvena upanītasya samāna-dharmasya asamānasya ca
096.06	samānasya dharmasya upayogāt anupayuktaḥ asamānaḥ saḥ aneka-śabdasya viṣaya-
096.07	viśeṣe iti/ kasmāt punar ekam eva na ucyate samāna-asamāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ
096.08	iti na evam śakyam abhidhātum anekasmāt vyāvṛttaḥ yaḥ dharmaḥ iti ayam
096.09	vigrahaḥ na labhyate lāghavam vā prayojanam eka-akṣara-apacayāt lāghavam
096.10	prayojanam āśritya abhidhānam vā/ yadi aneka-dharma-arthaḥ asādhāraṇa-arthaḥ asādhāraṇaḥ
096.11	ca dharmaḥ saṃśaya-kāraṇam iti/ na idam nirātmakam jīvat-śarīram aprāṇa-
096.12	ādimat-tva-prasaṅgāt iti ayam api asādhāraṇa-tvāt saṃśaya-hetuḥ prāptaḥ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ
096.13	yathā eva sādhāraṇaḥ dharmaḥ saṃśaya-hetuḥ iti na anvayinaḥ sādhāraṇa-tve sati
096.14	saṃśaya-hetu-tvam bhavati/ anvaya-vyabhicārāt evam vyatirekiṇaḥ api sati asādhāraṇa-
096.15	tve sati vyabhicāra-avyabhicārau saṃśaya-nirṇaya-hetū/ yaḥ vyabhicārī saḥ
096.16	saṃśaya-hetuḥ yaḥ avyabhicārī saḥ nirṇaya-hetuḥ iti yadi tarhi ubhayoḥ vyabhicāri-
096.17	tvāt saṃśaya-hetu-tvam nanu asamāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti anena eva gatam etad gata-artha-tvāt
096.18	na saṃśaya-hetu-tvena ayam pṛthak  upādheyam/ satyam/ vyabhicāritām antareṇa na anyena
096.19	na saṃśaya-kāraṇam api tu vyabhicāritāyām satyām vidhīyamāne vyabhicāraḥ
096.20	pratiṣidhyamāna-vyabhicāraḥ ca iti bhedaḥ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti anena vidhīyamāna-
096.21	vyabhicāraḥ upadiśyate vibhāgaja-tvāt iti anena pratiṣidhyamāna-vyabhicāraḥ
096.22	iti etāvatā pṛthak-abhidhānam/ nañaḥ paryudāsa-viṣaya-tvāt
097.01	avyabhicāri-dharma-dvaya-upanipātaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ iti ke cit/ eke tu aneka-dharmaḥ
097.02	iti anyathā vyācakṣate ekasmāt anyaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ iti/ evam ca viruddha-avyabhicāri-
097.03	dharma-dvaya-upanipātaḥ labhyate yam prati tarkam āhuḥ saḥ ca saṃśaya-
097.04	hetuḥ yathā śrāvaṇatva-kṛtakatve śabdasya iti/ tad ayuktam/ asambhavāt/
097.05	na hi avyabhicāriṇau viruddhau ekasmin arthe dharmau sambhavataḥ vastunaḥ
097.06	dvairūpya-asambhavāt yadi ubhau avyabhicāriṇau syātām ekatamam vastu dvi-ātmakam
097.07	bhavet na ca etad asti tasmāt na ubhau avyabhicāriṇau iti/ na ca ayam prayogaḥ
097.08	yuktaḥ/ katham iti/ yadi tāvat ayam prayuṅkte yathā prayukte naḥ hetuḥ
097.09	tathā ayam api iti/ prasiddhena vyapadeśāḥ bhavanti iti siddham sarvasya ahetu-tvam
097.10	viruddha-artha-pratipatti-bādhita-tvāt uttarasya anabhidhānam/ atha evam kriyate
097.11	yathā eva dvitīyam anumānam na bhavati tathā prāktanam api iti evam pratipannam
097.12	dvitīya-anumānasya asādhaka-tvam atra viruddha-dharmau ekatra sambhavataḥ iti
097.13	sambhavāt na viruddhau/ kaḥ ca viruddha-arthaḥ kim saha-asambhavaḥ āho viruddha-artha-
097.14	sādhaka-tvam atha svarūpam iti/ yadi saha-asambhavaḥ saḥ na asti darśanāt/
097.15	atha viruddha-artha-sādhaka-tvam virodhaḥ tad api na yuktam vastunaḥ dvi-ātmaka
097.16	tva-asambhavāt yadi viruddha-artha-sādhaka-tvam anayoḥ dharmayoḥ bhavet ekam vastu
097.17	dvi-ātmakam āpadyeta/ atha svarūpam anayoḥ viruddham iti na kim cit bādhitam
097.18	bhavati yasmāt kṛtakatvam na śrāvaṇatvam śrāvaṇatvam vā na kṛtakatvam tasmāt na
097.19	saṃśayaḥ iti/ upetya vā virodham yadi viruddha-arthābhyām kṛtakatva-śrāvaṇatvābhyām
097.20	śabde saṃśayaḥ kriyate viśeṣa-darśanāt upajātaḥ kasya darśanāt nirvartate
097.21	na hi viśeṣa-darśane sati viśeṣa-ākāṅkṣā bhavati yathā upalabdha-
097.22	sāmānyasya viśeṣa-ākāṅkṣā bhavati/ pratyakṣatah nivartiṣyate iti cet/
098.01	na/ yaḥ anumānābhyām sannipātābhyām arthe saṃśayaḥ saḥ pratyakṣāt nivartiṣyate
098.02	iti/ tad na yuktam/ pratyakṣa-aviṣaya-tvāt/ na ayam arthaḥ pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ
098.03	iti/ kim śabdaḥ anityaḥ vā iti/ āgamāt nivartiṣyate iti cet/ na/
098.04	āgamasya ca vicāryamāṇa-tvāt āgamaḥ eva ayam vicāryate kim śabdaḥ
098.05	nityaḥ atha anityaḥ iti/ na ca ayam pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ na āgama-viṣayaḥ anumānābhyām
098.06	ca ana ayam paricchettum śakyate saḥ ayam anivartyaḥ saṃśayaḥ prāptaḥ/ bhavatu
098.07	kim naḥ bādhyate katham na bādhyate/ yadā vastu-antara-paricchedakam anumānam
098.08	na bhavati yathā anityā buddhiḥ kṛtakatvāt iti idam khalu kṛtakatvam
098.09	śabde dṛṣṭam saḥ ca anavadhārita-svabhāvaḥ nityaḥ anityaḥ iti/ tatra ca upalabhyamānam
098.10	kṛtakatvam anyatra buddhi-ādi^i śaṃśaya-hetuḥ iti sarvam  anumānam
098.11	anityatva-sādhanam na syāt sarvam ca anumānam ayam nyāyaḥ bādhate iti yaḥ ca
098.12	nyāyaḥ anumāna-mūlam ucchinatti na ayam nyāyaḥ bādhitum yuktaḥ nitya-asambhavāt
098.13	na ayam asmāt nyāyaḥ bādhate yasya nityam kim cit asti tasya viruddha-
098.14	avyabhicārin^ḥ bhavati/ asmākam tu pakṣe nityam kim cit na asti iti/ na/
098.15	bādha-aparijñānāt na bhavatā bādha-viṣayaḥ avalokitaḥ kim tu nityam
098.16	asmākam na asti iti etāvat-mātrena parapakṣe doṣaḥ abhidhīyate bhavatām api
098.17	pakṣe viruddha-avyabhicārin^ḥ kṛta-avakāśaḥ katham sapratigham vijñānam sattvāt
098.18	rūpa-vat iti ekaḥ apratigham vijñānam svarūpa-tvāt vedanā-vat iti aparaḥ/ tad evam
098.19	avyabhicāri-dharma-dvaya-upanipātāt na sapratigham na apratigham siddhyet tasmāt
098.20	apanyāyaḥ ayam viruddhayoḥ ca dharmayoḥ sannipātāt śabde saṃśayaḥ iti/
098.21	dharmayoḥ sātatyāt abhilāpa-anupapattiḥ na ayam atad-dharmā kadā cit api
098.22	śabdaḥ upalabhyate satatam tu tad-dharmā bhavati śrāvaṇaḥ kṛtakaḥ ca iti
099.01	abhilāpaḥ na syāt nityaḥ anityaḥ iti/ na hi kadā cit ayam draṣṭṛ
099.02	ekasmin vastuni sthāṇu-puruṣa-dharmau upalabhamānaḥ śaknuyāt vaktum sthāṇuḥ vā
099.03	puruṣaḥ vā/ na evam bhaviṣyati iti vyāhatam ucyate/ nanu ca bhavatā abhidhīyate
099.04	nityaḥ iti mayā api abhidhīyate anityaḥ iti katham na abhilapyate
099.05	sah ayam viruddha-avyabhicāri-nyāyaḥ vicaryamāṇaḥ sarvathā anumānam bādhate
099.06	iti alam anena/ atha kṛtakatva-śrāvaṇatve sahite hetū bhavataḥ iti
099.07	tathā api kṛtakatva-śrāvaṇatve sahite na vaibhāgajatvāt bhidyete yathā eva
099.08	vibhāgajatvam anya-aviṛtti-tvāt asādhāraṇam śabde saṃśayam karoti tathā kṛtakatva-
099.09	śrāvaṇatve api śabda-vṛtti-tvāt anyatra asambhavāt saṃśaya-hetū bhavataḥ
099.10	saḥ ayam asādhāraṇaḥ eva na dharma-antaram tad-grahaṇena eva saṅgṛhītaḥ iti tasmāt
099.11	ekasmāt anyaḥ dharmaḥ aneka-dharmaḥ iti vaktavyam iti/ yadā punar evam-bhūtau
099.12	dharmau ekasya vastunaḥ bhavataḥ tadā tena upalabdhvā tasya vastunaḥ kim
099.13	pratipattavyam tada tayoḥ eva dharmayoḥ sāmarthya-adhigati^i yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ
099.14	na ubhau etau sādhane kataraḥ atra sādhanam kataraḥ ca asadhanam iti sāmarthya-
099.15	adhigame yatnaḥ karaṇīyaḥ iti/ yatnaḥ ca kriyamāṇaḥ anitya-sādhana^su eva avatiṣṭhate
099.16	kutaḥ nityatvasya pramāṇa-bādhita-tvāt tad ca upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ/
099.17	tad evam vyavasthitam etad aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ asādhāraṇāt dharmāt saṃśayāḥ
099.18	iti vipratipatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti vyāhata-artha-pravādaḥ vipratipatti-
099.19	śabdasya arthaḥ vyāhata-artha-pravāda-viṣaya-tvam upalabhamānasya upalabdhi anupalabdhi^oḥ ca
099.20	avyavasthāne sati tad-gata-viśeṣa-artha-smṛti^i satyām saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti/
099.21	tatra samānaḥ anekaḥ ca dharmaḥ jñeya-sthaḥ upalabdhi-anupalabdhY punar jñātṛ-sthe iti
099.22	bhāṣyam/ tatra upalabdhi-anupalabdhi^oḥ tāvat pṛthak saṃśaya-kāraṇa-tvam na bhavati
100.01	iti carccitam etad/ samānaḥ anekaḥ ca dharmaḥ jñeyasya iti etad api na
100.02	buddhyāmahe/ kim atra dharmaḥ saṃśaya-kāraṇam atha jñānam iti/ na dharmaḥ
100.03	saṃśaya-kāraṇam iti anekadhā samarthitam/ samāna-aneka-dharma-jñānam tu saṃśaya-
100.04	kāraṇam tad ca jñātṛ^i vartate iti na asti bhedaḥ/ samāna-aneka-dharmayoḥ tu
100.05	pṛthak-abhidhānam pṛthak-abhidhāne uktam prayojanam vidhīyamāna-pratiṣidhyamāna-dharma-bhedāt
100.06	iti/ vipratipatti^aḥ iti ayam vaktṛ-gataḥ saṃśaya-hetuḥ/ ke atra samyac-pratiptannāḥ
100.07	ke mithyā iti śrotṛ^aḥ sandehaḥ bhavati etāvatā bhedena pṛthak-abhidhānam/
100.08	samāna-dharmaḥ saḥ eva ayam iti cet samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ
100.09	vimarśaḥ ca pratipatti^aḥ ca iti sarvaḥ eva ayam samāna-dharmaḥ abhidhīyate tasmāt vaiyarthyāt
100.10	pṛthak-aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ vipratipatti^aḥ ca iti na prayojanam asti/
100.11	ataḥ evam vaktavyam/ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti
100.12	na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt sūtra-artham aparijñāya etad deśyate/ yathā ca bhedaḥ
100.13	samāna-aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ vipratipatti^aḥ ca iti tathā varṇitam/ apare punar
100.14	samāna-dharma-upapatti-ādibhiḥ padaiḥ pṛthak pañca-vidham saṃśayam varṇayanti/
100.15	samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti evam śeṣa^su pada^su
100.16	tad na yuktam upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthāyāḥ pūrva-pada-viśeṣaṇa-tvāt samāna-
100.17	dharma-upapatti^i satyām upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthāyām ca satyām viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ
100.18	vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ evam aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ vipratipatti^ah ca iti
100.19	vācyam/ tasmāt na upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthā pṛthak saṃśaya-kāraṇam iti/
100.20	upalabdhi-anupalabdhi^oḥ dvaividhyāt saṃśayaḥ na yuktaḥ kutaḥ loka-virodhāt
101.01	upalabdhi-anupalabdhi^oḥ dvaividhyāt saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti bruvāṇaḥ lokam bādhate/
101.02	katham iti yad kim cit ayam upalabhate sarvatra asya saṃśayaḥ na saṃśayena bhavitavyam/
101.03	kim kāraṇam upalabhyamānam dvedhā bhavati/ upalabdhi^oḥ dvaividhyāt tu yaḥ
101.04	saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti tasya kutaḥ nivṛttiḥ viśeṣa-darśanāt nivartate iti
101.05	cet viśeṣa^su api evam ye ete viśeṣāḥ upalabhyante kim ete santaḥ āho asantaḥ
101.06	iti yāvat upalabdhi-dvaividhyāt saṃśayaḥ iti anivartyaḥ ssṃśayaḥ/ evam anupalabdhi-
101.07	dvaividhye api vaktavyam na ca asya kva cit samāśvāsaḥ syāt/ yadā ayam
101.08	apavaraka-ādi^i sarpa-ādīm upalabhate tadā sarpa-vat tad-veśman uta asarpa-vat iti
101.09	saṃśayaḥ ayam api saṃśayaḥ anivartyaḥ eva iti sarvatra āśvāsaḥ na syāt/ yeṣām ca
101.10	pañca-vidhaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ taiḥ asya kāraṇa-kṛtaḥ bhedaḥ vaktavyaḥ
101.11	svabhāva-kṛtaḥ vā/ tad yadi kāraṇa-kṛtaḥ na pañca-vidhaḥ aneka-rūpaḥ saṃśayaḥ
101.12	iti prāptam/ atha svabhāva-bhedāt svabhāva-bhedasya asambhavāt eka-rūpaḥ
101.13	saṃśītiḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ tasmāt pañca-vidhaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti na sūtra-arthaḥ
101.14	samāna-dharma-grahaṇāt na saṃśayaḥ dharmi-viṣaya-tvāt yad idam ucyate samāna-dharma-
101.15	upapatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ tad ayuktam/ dharmi-viṣaya-tvāt/ na hi samāne
101.16	dharme upalabdhe dharmiṇi saṃśayaḥ (na) yuktaḥ dhrama-dharmiṇoḥ bhedāt/ na hi
101.17	gavi dṛṣṭe aśve saṃśayaḥ (dharmiṇaḥ ca adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ na anupalabdhe na nirṇīte
101.18	saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti ca vyāghātaḥ/ dharma-dharmi-bhāvāt saṃśayaḥ iti eke/
101.19	eke tāvat parihāram bruvate dharme dṛṣṭe dharmiṇi saṃśayaḥ) dharma-
101.20	dharmi-bhāva-anupapatti^aḥ na hi dharma-dharmi-bhāvena nānātvam nivartate/ atha
102.01	dharma-dharmi-bhāve niyāmakaḥ icchā-ādi-sūtram tarhi vyāhanyate katham iti
102.02	icchā-ādyaḥ  pratisanndhānāt ātmānam gamayanti iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ tatra paraḥ
102.03	pratyavasthitaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānāt iti na nānātvasya
102.04	abadhānāt abādhanāt iti anena nivartyate/ iha punar bhavatā dharma-dharmi-bhāvāt iti
102.05	punar bruvatām nānātvam eva dharma-dharmi-bhāva-viśiṣṭam niyāmakam ucyate iti
102.06	śaknuyāt paraḥ api vaktum kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ niyāmakaḥ iti tasmāt apavyākhyānam
102.07	etad dharma-dharmi-bhāvāt saṃśayaḥ iti/ atha samāna-dharma-upalabdhi^aḥ
102.08	anyasmin upalabdhe dharmiṇi saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti kaḥ parihāraḥ/
102.09	ayam parihāraḥ bahuvrīhiḥ samāsaḥ ataḥ dharmiṇaḥ abhidhānam samāna-
102.10	dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti anena padena bahuvrīhi-samāse dharmin^ḥ eva vyapadiśyate katham
102.11	samāsaḥ dharmaḥ yasya saḥ bhavati samāna-dharman samāna-dharmaṇaḥ upapattiḥ
102.12	samāna-dharma-upapattiḥ kim uktam bhavati samāna-dharmavataḥ dharmiṇaḥ darśanāt
102.13	iti/ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ viśeṣa-smṛti-
102.14	apekṣaḥ iti/ atha sā viśeṣa-smṛtiḥ kim viśeṣa-viṣayā uta anya-viṣayā iti
102.15	yaḥ asau saṃśayasya viṣayaḥ tam samāna-dharmāṇam upalabhate tasya ayam viśeṣān
102.16	anusmarati uta anyasya iti/ yadi tāvat tasya tad na yuktam tad-viśeṣāṇām
102.17	kva cit ananubhavāt saḥ arthaḥ pūrvam upalabdha-viśeṣaḥ tad-gatān viśeṣān anusmarati
102.18	iti yuktam tad-viśeṣāṇām anubhūta-tvāt yadā punar ayam anupalabdha-viśeṣe
102.19	sāmānye upalabhyamānaḥ sandihyate tatra viśeṣa-anusmṛtiḥ katham tad-viśeṣāṇām
102.20	anubhūta-tvāt iti/ na/ sāmānya-vacana-tvāt/ viśeṣa-apekṣaḥ iti
103.01	sāmānya-vacanam punar anena avadhāryate tasya vā anyasya vā iti yadā tad-gatāḥ
103.02	viśeṣāḥ susumūrṣitāḥ bhavanti tadā anubhūta^su viśeṣa-smṛtiḥ yadā tu anupalabdha-
103.03	pūrve saṃśayaḥ tadā sādṛśyāt anya-gatān viśeṣān susmūrṣate evam
103.04	tāvat vyavasthite tri-vidhaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ anena ca saṃśaya-lakṣaṇena yāni
103.05	anyāni viruddhāni tāni api saṃgṛhītāni/ yathā kāśyapīyam/
103.06	sāmānya-pratyakṣāt viśeṣa-apratyakṣāt viśeṣa-smṛti^aḥ ca saṃśayaḥ iti/ asya arthaḥ
103.07	sāmānya-pratyakṣāt iti sāmānyavataḥ pratyakṣāt iti viśeṣa-apratyakṣa-tvam
103.08	avyavasthita-viśeṣa-tvam na asya viśeṣāḥ vyavasthitāḥ iti viśeṣa-smṛti^aḥ ca saṃśayaḥ
103.09	iti samānam/ katham punar anena sūtrena asādhāraṇaḥ labhyate iti
103.10	labhyate ca vyabhicāri-tvāt vyabhicārin^ḥ sādhāraṇaḥ tad ca asādhāraṇe
103.11	api vyabhicāri-tvam asti iti labhyate/ aneka-dharma-upapattiḥ tarhi pṛthak na
103.12	paṭhitavyā anvaya-vyatireka-bheda-vivakṣāyām pṛthak-abhidhānam punar saṃśaya-
103.13	kāraṇa-bheda-vivakṣayā iti/ ye tu atra viśeṣa-apratyakṣāt viśeṣa-asmṛti^aḥ viśeṣa-smṛti^aḥ ca iti padayoḥ
103.14	paunaruktyam varṇayanti/ sāmānya-pratyakṣāt viśeṣa-smṛti^aḥa ca saṃśayaḥ iti abhidhīyamāne
103.15	kila gamyate etad viśeṣa-apratyakṣāt iti na hi kaḥ cit viśeṣān
103.16	pratyakṣān smarati/ tad na/ sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt/ na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ
103.17	viśeṣāṇām apratyakṣa-tvam iti/ api tu avyavasthita-viśeṣavat-tvam viśeṣa-apratyakṣa-
103.18	tvam iti ataḥ na ayam doṣaḥ iti/ anye tu saṃśaya-lakṣaṇam anyathā vyācakṣate/
103.19	sādharmya-darśanāt viśeṣa-upalipsu^aḥ vimarśaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ taiḥ api sādharmyasya
103.20	avadhārita-tvāt saṃśayasya viṣayah vaktavyaḥ/ dharmin^ḥ iti cet nanu uktaḥ saṃśayasya
104.01	viṣayaḥ dharmin^ḥ satyam uktaḥ na punar yuktaḥ katham na yuktaḥ yadi tāvat dharma-
104.02	vyatiriktam dharmiṇam abhyupaiti vyāhatam bhavati/ dharma-dharmiṇoḥ ca nānātva0
104.03	abhyupagame dharmasya avadhṛta-tvāt dharmiṇaḥ ca adṛṣṭa-tvāt na hi anyasmin dṛṣṭe
104.04	anyatra saṃśayaḥ bhavati/ atha api asmad-diśā sādharmya-abhidhānena dharmin^ḥ eva ucyeta
104.05	tad na yuktam sadharmaṇaḥ bhāvaḥ sādharmyam sādharmyam iti ca bhāva-abhidhānena
104.06	dharmaḥ abhidhīyate na dharmin^ḥ na ca yuktaḥ anyasya darśanāt anayatra saṃśayaḥ iti/
104.07	atha artha-antara-bhāvam na abhyupaiti tathā api sādharmyasya avadhṛta-tvāt kva saṃsayaḥ
104.08	iti/ yadi ca ayam dharmiṇam na upalabhate kasya ayam viśeṣam upalipseta/
104.09	na hi sādharmyasya viśeṣāḥ sambhavanti iti/ atha sāmānyavataḥ viśeṣāḥ
104.10	iti tad na tathā asya adṛṣṭa-tvāt iti uktam/ na sāmānya-viśeṣāḥ santi iti
104.11	sāmānyavataḥ viśeṣāḥ bhavanti iti evam bruvatā sāmānya-viśeṣa-tadvatām bhedaḥ
104.12	anujñātaḥ bhavati vyartham ca abhidhānam na sāmānya-viśeṣāḥ tadvataḥ viśeṣāḥ
104.13	iti/ upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-vyavasthāne ca sādharmya-darśane sati api viśeṣa-
104.14	upalipsāyām ca na saṃśayaḥ bhavati iti upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthātam iti
104.15	vaktavyam aneka-dharma-darśanāt iti ca samāna-aneka-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti evam
104.16	ādi-vākyam saṃśaya-lakṣaṇam tad vicāryate kim tāvat ayam kāraṇa-upadeśaḥ
104.17	āho saṃśaya-svarūpa-avadhāraṇam iti/ yadi kāraṇa-nirdeśaḥ atyalpam idam
104.18	ucyate samāna-aneka-dharma-ādibhyaḥ iti anyāni api saṃśaya-kāraṇāni
104.19	tāni upasaṃkhyeyāni/ yathā ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ āntarasya ātman-manas-sannikarṣaḥ
104.20	indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ bāhyasya iti/ atha svarūpa-avadhāraṇam kāraṇam nirdeśaḥ
104.21	anarthakaḥ saṃśītiḥ saṃśayaḥ iti vaktavyam/ atha tāvat kāraṇa-
104.22	avadhāraṇam nanu uktam kāraṇa-antaram api upasaṃkhyeyam iti na na idam kāraṇa-
105.01	avadhāraṇa-artham api tu asādhāraṇa-kāraṇa-nirdeśaḥ ayam tasmāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-
105.02	ādīnām aprasaṅgaḥ pratyakṣa-sādhāraṇa-tvāt svarūpa-nirdeśaḥ vā bhavatu
105.03	idam eva asya svarūpam samāna-dharma-ādibhyaḥ janman iti saḥ ayam evam-bhūtaḥ saṃśayaḥ
105.04	vicāra-aṅga-tayā upādīyate/ saṃśaya-vat viparyayaḥ api vicāra-aṅgam saḥ api
105.05	pada-artha-tvena vaktavyaḥ satyam asau padārthaḥ na tu vicāra-aṅgam iti ataḥ
105.06	na abhidheyaḥ katham na vicāra-aṅgam yathā sandigdhaḥ tad-viśeṣa-pratipatti^e
105.07	yatate na evam viparyastaḥ iti nyāya-vidyā-anaṅga-tvāt ataḥ na abhidhīyate//
105.08	[1-1-24]yam arhtam adhikṛtya pravartate tad prayojanam
105.09	yam artham adhikṛtya pravartate tad prayojanam/ yam artham adhikṛtya iti/
105.10	vyavasāyaḥ adhikāraḥ/ kasya vyavasāyaḥ sukha-duḥkha-sādhanānām/ idam
105.11	sukha-sādhanam iti jñātvā sukha-āpti^e prayatate idam suhkha-sādhanm iti
105.12	ca adhigamasya duḥkha-hānāya iti/ sukha-duḥkhayoḥ avāpti-hānābhyām ayam lokaḥ
105.13	prasajyate iti/ sukha-duḥkha-āpti-hānī prayojanam iti/ anena ca prayojanena
105.14	sarve arthāḥ saṃgṛhītāḥ bhavanti iti/ ekasmin ca sūtra-arthe parasya
105.15	doṣa-vivakṣayā codyamānasya vaktum aśakyasya vyavahāraḥ laukikaḥ ayam arthaḥ
105.16	prasajyate anena iti prayojanam/ na ca anena nyāyasya kim cit
105.17	kriyate iti nyāya-aṅga-bhāvaḥ na asti iti yaḥ tāvat laukikaḥ ayam arthaḥ iti na
105.18	ṛte prakṛṣṭa-tārkikāt anyaḥ vaktum arhati/ ayam eva ca padārthaḥ laukikaḥ
105.19	na pramāṇa-ādyaḥ  iti kaḥ ca asau laukikaḥ iti/ yadi pramāṇa-upapannam
106.01	iti ayam arthaḥ atidoṣaḥ ayam pramāṇa-upapannam na vaktavyam iti/ atha
106.02	alaukika-śabda-arthaḥ tad na budhyāmahe katham anyaḥ iti tad api prayojanam
106.03	nyāyasya aṅgam na bhavati iti/ tad ayuktam/ yā khalu niḥprayojanā cintā
106.04	sā na nyāya-aṅgam iti parīkṣā-vidhi^aḥ tu pradhāna-aṅgam prayojanam eva tad-mūla
106.05	tvāt parīkṣā-vidhi^aḥ
106.06	[1-1-25]laukika-parīkṣakāṇām yasmin arthe buddhi-sāmyam saḥ
106.07	dṛṣṭāntaḥ
106.08	laukika-parīkṣakāṇām iti sūtram/ buddhi-sāmya-viṣayaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
106.09	iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ evam ca ākāśa-ādi-avabodhaḥ iti yadi punar evam avadhāryate
106.10	laukikānām parīkṣakāṇām ca yaḥ viṣayaḥ saḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti alaukika-
106.11	arthaḥ na dṛṣṭāntaḥ syāt ākāśa-ādiḥ iti udāharaṇa-tvena tu laukika-
106.12	parīkṣaka-viṣayasya abhidhānāt na tu laukika-parīkṣakāṇām eva iti/ saḥ ayam
106.13	dṛṣṭāntaḥ sārūpya-vyutpatti-arthaḥ vā syāt asiddha-sādhana-arthaḥ vā yadi tāvat
106.14	sārūpya-vyutpatti-arthaḥ tadā na upamānāt bhidyate atha asiddham-sādhana-arthaḥ na udāharaṇāt
106.15	bhidyate yadi upamānam pramāṇe antarbhāvaḥ atha udāharaṇam avayavam iti
106.16	na pṛthak dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti iti/ idam tu uttaram apratisamādheyam iti paśyāmaḥ/
106.17	kasmāt trayasya api avijñānāt/ evam bruvatā na dṛṣṭāntaḥ na udāharaṇam
106.18	na upamānam vijñātam iti/ sārūpya-vyutpatti-artham tāvat upamānam na bhavati iti
106.19	varṇitam/ dṛṣṭāntaḥ sārūpya-vyutpatti-arthaḥ asiddha-sādhana-arthaḥ vā iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ
106.20	na bhavati udāharaṇa-artham udāharaṇam varṇayantaḥ vakṣyāmaḥ iti//
106.21	[1-1-26]tantra-adhikaraṇa-abhyupagama-saṃsthitiḥ siddhāntaḥ
107.01	[1-1-27]sarva-tantra-pratitantra-adhikaraṇa-abhyupagama-saṃsthiti-artha-antara-
107.02	bhāvāt
107.03	idam ittham-bhūtam ca iti bhāṣyam/ idam iti sāmānyataḥ avagamam āha/
107.04	ittham-bhūtam ca iti viśeṣataḥ/ etad uktam bhavati/ sāmānya-viśeṣavat-artha-abhyanujñā
107.05	siddhāntaḥ iti/ asya arthasya pradarśana-artham sūtram/ tantra-adhikaraṇa-
107.06	abhyupagama-saṃsthitiḥ siddhāntaḥ iti/ kim punar idam sūtram lakṣaṇa-artham
107.07	āho vibhāga-artham iti/ kim ca ataḥ yadi lakṣaṇa-artham tantra-adhikaraṇa-
107.08	grahaṇam na kartavyam etāvat bhavatu abhyupagama-vyavasthā siddhāntaḥ iti/ atha
107.09	vibhāga-artham sarva-tantra-grahaṇam kartavyam pratijñā-ādi-vat yathā pratijñā-hetu-udāharaṇa-
107.10	upanaya-nigamanāni avayavāḥ iti samasta-abhidhānam/ evam sarva-tantra-
107.11	pratitantra-adhikaraṇa-abhyupagama-saṃsthitiḥ iti vaktavyam/ lakṣaṇa-artham ca sūtra-
107.12	antaram vaktavyam/ sarva-tantra-pratitantra-abhyupagama-saṃsthiti-artha-antara-bhāvāt iti asya
107.13	sūtra-arthaḥ anyaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ atha idam vibhāga-artham tathā api pūrva-sūtram parityājyatām
107.14	atha vibhaktānām punar vibhāgaḥ trividhā ca asya śāstrasya
107.15	pravṛttiḥ iti vyāhatam/ vibhāgaḥ ca niyama-arthaḥ/ vibhaktānām vibhāgaḥ
107.16	kim-arthaḥ/ yadi niyama-arthaḥ ādyena sūtrena kṛta-tvāt na prayojanam asti iti
107.17	tasmāt pūrvam uttaram vā sūtram anārṣam iti/ na anārṣam/ ādyasya lakṣaṇa-
107.18	artha-tvāt uttarasya vibhāga-artha-tvāt iti/ vibhāgaḥ ca niyama-arthaḥ iti uktam/
107.19	etena anekadhā bhinnasya siddhāntasya caturdhā asaṃgrahaḥ iti niyamam
107.20	darśayati/ atha ādyam sūtram katham lakṣaṇa-artham iti/ tantra-adhikaraṇānām
108.01	arthānām abhyupagamaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ tantram adhikaraṇam yeṣām arthānām
108.02	bhavati te tantra-adhikaraṇāḥ teṣām abhyupagama-saṃsthitiḥ ittham-bhāva-vyavasthā-
108.03	dharma-niyamaḥ siddhāntaḥ bhavati iti kim uktam bhavati yaḥ arthaḥ na śāstritaḥ
108.04	tasya abhyupagamaḥ na siddhāntaḥ iti//
108.05	[1-1-28]sarva-tantra-aviruddhaḥ tantre adhikṛtaḥ arthaḥ sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ
108.06	sarveṣām sampratipatti-viṣayaḥ sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ iti/ yathā
108.07	pramāṇāni prameya-sādhanāni na dṛṣṭāntāt sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ bhidyate
108.08	tatra api avipratipattiḥ iha api iti bhidyate iti āha/ dṛṣṭāntaḥ hi vādi-
108.09	prativādibhyām eva niścitaḥ na punar evam sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ iti/
108.10	anumāna-āgamayoḥ āśrayaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ va evam sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ iti/
108.11	yadi anumāna-āgamayoḥ āśrayaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti pratyakṣe api prasaṅgaḥ pratyakṣam api
108.12	anumāna-āgamayoḥ āśrayaḥ tad-kāraṇa-tvāt yaḥ pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ saḥ arthaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
108.13	adhigata-arthaḥ kathyate iti anumāna-āgamayoḥ āśrayaḥ/ adhigama-sādhanam tu
108.14	pratyakṣam tasmāt na pratyakṣe prasaṅgaḥ//
108.15	[1-1-29]samāna-tantra-siddhiḥ para-tantra-siddhiḥ pratitantra-siddhāntaḥ
108.16	sāmānya-viśeṣa-tadvatām niyamena abhyupagamaḥ pratitantra-siddhāntaḥ
108.17	iti/ yathā bhautikāni indriyāṇi iti yogānām abhautikāni iti
108.18	sāṃkhyānām iti//
108.19	[1-1-30]yad-siddhi^i anya-prakaraṇa-siddhiḥ saḥ adhikaraṇa-siddhāntaḥ
109.01	vākya-artha-siddhi^i tad-anuṣaṅgin^ḥ yaḥ arthaḥ saḥ adhikaraṇa-siddhāntaḥ iti
109.02	tasya udaharaṇam bhāṣye/ yathā indriya-vyatiriktaḥ jñātṛ^ḥ darśana-sparsanābhyām
109.03	eka-artha-grahaṇāt iti//
109.04	[1-1-31]aparīkṣita-abhyupagamāt tad-viśeṣa-parīkṣaṇam abhyupagama-
109.05	siddhāntaḥ
109.06	aparīkṣita-abhyupagamāt iti sūtram/ aparīkṣitaḥ asūtritah iti
109.07	yaḥ arthaḥ sūtra^su na upanibaddhaḥ śāstre ca abhyupagataḥ saḥ abhyupagama-siddhāntaḥ
109.08	iti/ yathā naiyāyikānām manas indriyam iti/ vaiśeṣikāṇām
109.09	naiyāyikānām ca śrotram ākāśam iti/ aparīkṣitaḥ śāstra-anabhyupagataḥ
109.10	sva-buddhi-atiśaya-cikhyāpayiṣayā para-buddhi-avajñānāt ca pravartate iti na
109.11	yuktam/ kutaḥ para-avajñānasya ayukta-tvāt/ yadi tāvat parasya asāmarthyam
109.12	buddhvā na ayam samarthaḥ iti pravartate tadā anena na paraḥ pratipāditaḥ
109.13	bhavati kim tu vipratipāditaḥ yaḥ ca ajñaḥ kim tasya pratipādayitavyam/
109.14	atha parijñāta-sāmarthyam puruṣam avajānāti tadā api na atiśaya-buddhimataḥ
109.15	avajñānasya ayukta-tvāt tasmāt na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ aśāstrita-abhyupagamaḥ siddhāntaḥ
109.16	iti/ sarvaḥ eva ayam pakṣaḥ svamukhena artha-āpatti^ā vā vidhīyate iti kim
109.17	anena aitivisṛtena iti ke cit/ pakṣa-paryāyaḥ eva siddhāntaḥ iti apare/
109.18	pakṣasya kila ayam paryāyaḥ siddhāntaḥ iti siddhāntaḥ prayāyaḥ ayam iti
109.19	na buddhyāmahe siddhāntaḥ iti upapanna-pramāṇakaḥ ayam arthaḥ tad-pratyayāt abhyupagamḥ
109.20	pakṣaḥ iti anyatara-vādi-vipratipannasya anyataram prati karmatayā vastunaḥ yad
109.21	upādānam saḥ pakṣaḥ yaḥ ca kāraka-śabdaḥ yaḥ ca vastu-śabdaḥ na etau paryāyau
109.22	anyathā paraśuḥ chedanam iti paraśuḥ iti kadā cit abhidhīyate yadā
110.01	puruṣa-vyāpāra-nirapekṣam vastu sannidhīyamanatayā avatiṣṭhate chedanam iti
110.02	ca yadā udyamya udyamya dāruṇi nipātyate tau etau chedana-paraśu-śabdau
110.03	vastunaḥ kriyā-sambandha-asambandha-apekṣau pravartete iti tathā sarve eva
110.04	kāraka-śabdāḥ na dravya-mātre na vā kriyā-mātre vartante/ kim tarhi kriyā-
110.05	sādhane kriyā-viśeṣa-yuktaḥ iti kāraka-śabdaḥ ca pakṣaḥ na siddhāntaḥ/
110.06	saḥ katham paryāyaḥ bhavati iti sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ ca pakṣa-paryāyaḥ iti/
110.07	āho paryāya-śabda^su kauśalam bhadantasya pakṣaḥ vicāraṇāyām iṣṭaḥ arthaḥ
110.08	iti ca abhidhīyate avicāraṇīyaḥ ca arthaḥ pakṣe antarbhavati iti citram/
110.09	atha punar sarva-tantra-siddhāntam na eva pratipadyate tena api sarva-tantra-siddhāntam
110.10	nihnuvānena tad-asattva-pratipādakaḥ nyāyaḥ abhidheyaḥ yaḥ ca asau nyāya-abhyupagamaḥ
110.11	saḥ sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ iti vyāhatam/ sarva-tantra-siddhānte na asti iti/
110.12	atha nyāyam api na pratipadyate anivrttaḥ vyāghātaḥ vacane avacane ca
110.13	yadi tāvat nyāyaḥ asti iti bravīti pratipādayati na asti ca iti vyāhatam/
110.14	atha bravīti avacane asya arthaḥ na siddhaḥ tasmāt sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ
110.15	na asti iti saṃbhrama-vyākṛtam/ idam tāvat bhavān praṣṭavyaḥ jāyate kim
110.16	pakṣaḥ siddhānta-sāmānyam bhavatu atha saṃgrahaḥ pakṣa-śabdena siddhāntasya
110.17	atha siddhāntasya pakṣa-śabdaḥ paryāyaḥ iti tad yadi tāvat siddhānta-sāmānyam
110.18	tad na asti avyāpaka-tvāt na hi pakṣaḥ sarva-tantra-siddhāntam vyāpnoti asādhya-
110.19	tvāt yathā sattā dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi vyāpnoti vyāpaka-tvāt sāmānyam
110.20	bhavati na evam pakṣaḥ iti/ paryāya-śabdaḥ api na bhavati iti uktam/ yadi ca
110.21	pakṣaḥ siddhānta-paryāyaḥ sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ sadhyaḥ jāyate sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ
111.01	sadhyaḥ ca iti vyāghātah arthāpattitaḥ adhikaraṇa-siddhāntasya abhedaḥ
111.02	iti cet atha api na manyethāḥ arthāpattitaḥ adhikaraṇa-siddhāntaḥ na
111.03	bhidyate bhidyate vākya-artha-pratipatti^i tad-viparīta-vākya-artha-pratipattiḥ arthāpattiḥ
111.04	vākya-anuṣaṅgin^ḥ yaḥ arthaḥ saḥ adhikaraṇa-siddhāntaḥ iti//
111.05	[1-1-32]pratijñā-hetu-udāharaṇa-upanaya-nigamanāni avayavāḥ
111.06	pratijñā-hetu-udāharaṇa-upanaya-nigamanāni avayavāḥ/ avayavānām vibhāga-
111.07	uddeśa-artham sūtram/ vibhāga-uddeśaḥ ca niyama-arthaḥ/ kim niyantavyam daśa-
111.08	avayavam tri-avayavam ca vākyam eke tāvat bruvate daśa-avayavam vākyam/
111.09	apare tri-avayavam iti/ ubhaya-niyama-jñāpana-arthaḥ pratijñā-ādīnām vibhāga-
111.10	uddeśaḥ iti/ katham punar jijñāsā-ādyaḥ  avayavāḥ na bhavanti para-apratipādaka-
111.11	tvāt/ para-pratipādakāḥ ye vākya-aṅgam-bhūtāḥ itaretara-apratyāyitena
111.12	arthena arthavantaḥ vākya-aṅga-tām upayānti te avayavāḥ/ vākya-aṅga-tvam avayava-arthaḥ
111.13	kim punar vākyam yasya pratijñā-ādibhiḥ upahṛtasya viśeṣa-avasthāpanam arthaḥ
111.14	tad vākyam vākya-artham saṃhatya ete pañcan niṣpādayanti iti avayavāḥ iti ucyante na
111.15	punar jijñāsā-ādyaḥ  para-pratipādakāḥ iti ataḥ na vākyasya avayavāḥ iti
111.16	niścita-tvāt/ niścitasya sādhayitṛ^ḥ bhavati/ na tasya jijñāsā-
111.17	saṃśayau staḥ prayojanam api sādhanāt eva gamyate śakya-prāptiḥ ca iti na
111.18	hi aśakyam arayojakam vā kaḥ cit sādhayati iti tasmāt prayojana-śakya-prāptī
111.19	api na vākya-avayavau/ prakaraṇe tu jijñāsā-ādyaḥ  samarthāḥ iti
111.20	bhāṣyam/ prakaraṇam ete utthāpayanti iti/ na hi jijñāsā-ādīn antareṇa
112.01	prakaraṇasya utthānam asti iti prakaraṇa-utthāpakāḥ avayavāḥ jijñāsā-ādyaḥ  iti/
112.02	para-pratipādaka-tvāt tu pratijñā-ādīnām ayam adhikāraḥ iti te upadisyante/
112.03	tri-avayavam api vākyam tathā na bhavati tathā upanaya-nigamanayoḥ artha-antara-
112.04	bhāvam varṇayantaḥ vakṣyāmaḥ//
112.05	[1-1-33]sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā
112.06	teṣām tu avayava-tvena sāmānyena saṃgṛhītānām itaretara-viśeṣakam
112.07	lakṣaṇam ucyate iti/ sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti tatra prajñāpanīya-dharma-
112.08	viśiṣṭaḥ dharmin^ḥ sādhyaḥ tasya nirdeśaḥ pratijñā parigraha-vacanam udāharaṇam
112.09	anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti siddha-tvāt dharmiṇaḥ na sādhya-tā iti ke cit/ dharmin^ḥ
112.10	kila dharma-lakṣaṇaḥ siddhaḥ eva siddhaḥ ca sādhyaḥ na bhavati na eṣaḥ
112.11	doṣaḥ prajñāpanīya-dharma-viśiṣṭasya iti vacanāt/ na brūmaḥ dharmi-mātram
112.12	sādhyam api tu prajñāpanīya-dharma-viśiṣṭaḥ dharmin^ḥ iti/ yadi prajñāpanīyaḥ
112.13	na viśeṣaṇam atha viśeṣaṇam na prajñāpanīyaḥ na asiddham viśeṣaṇam
112.14	bhavati iti siddhena ayam viśiṣyate na sādhyena iti satyam aprajñātam viśeṣaṇam
112.15	na bhavati iti prajñātam tu idam anityatvam ghaṭe śabdasya sādhyam iti/ evam
112.16	tarhi śabdasya anityatvam sādhyam na śabde iti śabdyasya iti viśeṣaṇāt na
112.17	doṣaḥ śabdasya iti bruvatā na anityatva-mātram sādhya-tvena abhijñāyate na
112.18	dharmi-mātram kim tarhi dharmiṇaḥ śabdasya prajñātasya nityatvasya adharma-tvena
112.19	yaḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvaḥ itaretara-niyāmaka-tvena aniyamaḥ saḥ sādhyaḥ
112.20	saḥ ca ubhaya-āśritaḥ bhavati/ ubhaya-āśraya-tve sati kim uktam bhavati dharmin^ḥ
112.21	viśeṣaṇam dharmaḥ vā viśeṣaṇam iti śabdasya anityatvam anityatvasya vā
112.22	śabdaḥ iti dharmin^ḥ viśiṣyate iti yuktam/ sāmānyataḥ adhigata-tvāt
113.01	viśeṣataḥ anadhigata-tvāt ca viśeṣa-pratipādaka-tvāt ca anumānasya na ca punar
113.02	dharmasya sāmānya-adhigamaḥ asti na na asti kim ayam dharmaḥ śabdasya uta
113.03	anyasya iti/ evam tarhi śabde anityatvam iti ayam arthaḥ sādhyaḥ tasya ca na
113.04	kṛtakatva-ādi-yogaḥ iti yuktam/ prajñāpanīya-dharma-viśiṣṭaḥ dharmin^ḥ sādhyaḥ
113.05	tasya nirdeśaḥ pratijñā ubhaya-avadhāraṇa-prāpti^i anyatara-avadhāraṇe ca doṣaḥ/
113.06	yadi sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti pratijñā-lakṣaṇam vyatireki-tvāt iti
113.07	atra sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā eva sādhya-nirdeśaḥ avadhṛtaḥ na ca pratijñā sādhya-
113.08	nirdeśaḥ na anyathā asti iti evam api sādhya-nirdeśaḥ na pratijñā-lakṣaṇam
113.09	avyāpaka-tvāt/ yad api vyatirekin tad api alakṣaṇam yathā viṣāṇitvam go^aḥ
113.10	yad api avyāpakam tad api alakṣaṇam yathā gandhavattvam dravyasya/ atha pūrva-uttare
113.11	asādhāraṇe na kriyete tathā api kila vākyam anarthakam bhavati na ca anyā
113.12	gatiḥ asti tasmāt sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti na yuktam/ sarvasmin vākye
113.13	asādhāraṇam iti tu na buddhyāmahe tad yathā go-pālakena mārge apadiṣṭe
113.14	eṣaḥ panthāḥ śrughnam gacchati iti na avadhāraṇasya viṣayam paśyāmaḥ avadhāraṇasya
113.15	tu viṣayaḥ sāmānya-śruti^i niyamaḥ yena vākyena samāna-
113.16	śruti^ā aneka-arthaḥ gamyate tatra atiprasakti^i atiprasaṅga-nirākaraṇa-artham avadhāraṇam
113.17	iti na punar sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti ukte kva cit prasaṅgaḥ asti yad-nirākaraṇāya
113.18	avadhāraṇam kriyate sarvatra ca sādhāraṇam kurvāṇaḥ lokam bādhate
113.19	iti/ yatra ca viśeṣaṇasya avakāśaḥ tatra avadhāraṇasya api iti na ayam doṣaḥ
113.20	pūrva-uttare avadhāraṇe na kalpyete iti/ nanu sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti ukte
113.21	sādhyayoḥ hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ api prasaṅgaḥ yathā anityaḥ śabdaḥ cākṣaṣa-tvāt
114.01	nityaḥ śabdaḥ asparśa-tvāt buddhi-vat ca iti na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt sādhya-nirdeśaḥ
114.02	pratijñā iti prajñāpanīya-dharma-viśiṣṭasya dharmiṇaḥ parigraha-vacanam/ na ca
114.03	cākṣuṣa-tvam prajñāpanīya-dharma-viśiṣṭasya dharmiṇaḥ parigraha-vacanam api tu
114.04	dharma-nirdeśaḥ ayam cākṣuṣa-tvāt iti/ tathā buddhi^aḥ nityatvam iti/ atha
114.05	punar sādhya-nirdeśa-grahaṇāt asādhya-nirdeśa-nivṛttiḥ sādhya-nirdeśa-grahaṇāt
114.06	kila asādhya-nirdeśaḥ nivartyate asādhyam ca dvedhā siddham anupapadyamāna-
114.07	sādhanam ca/ tatra sādhya-nirdeśaḥ iti anena vacanena ubhayam nivartyate siddham
114.08	anupapadyamāna-sādhanam ca tatra anupalabhyamāna-sādhanam cākṣuṣa-tvam buddhi-
114.09	nityatvam ca tasmāt na tatra prasaṅgaḥ na punar kṛtakatva-ādi-anyatara-pakṣa-siddham
114.10	sādhya-tayā upanīyate tadā kṛtakatva-ādi^aḥ sādhya-nirdeśasya pratijñā-tvam prāptam
114.11	iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ ca yadi tāram bravīti kṛtakatvam
114.12	sādhyam iti tadā sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt iti parihāraḥ/ atha kṛtakaḥ
114.13	śabdaḥ iti etad sādhyam iti evam pratyavatiṣṭhate tadā abhyupagamaḥ eva doṣa-tvena
114.14	darśitaḥ iti na kim cit bādhyate/ yad ca idam ucyate/ asādhya-nirdeśa-nivṛtti-
114.15	dvāreṇa sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti ucyate iti tad-vidhīyamāna-pratiṣidhyamāna-
114.16	śabda-artha-abhyupagamāt niyamaḥ na yuktaḥ kva cit vākye vidhīyamānaḥ
114.17	arthaḥ abhidhīyate kva cit pratiṣidhyamānaḥ iti ekānta-vādinaḥ tu
114.18	doṣaḥ tathā ca na pratiṣidhyamānaḥ eva padārthaḥ bhavati tad ca upariṣṭāt
114.19	vakṣyāmaḥ iti/ sādhya-nirdeśaḥ iti ca pratijñāyām sādhyayoḥ hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ
114.20	prasaṅgaḥ iti na prasaṅgaḥ siddhānta-viśeṣaṇa-tvāt sādhya-śabdasya,
114.21	siddhānta-viśeṣaṇaḥ ayam sādhya-śabdaḥ na sādhya-mātram tathā ca kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ
115.01	hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ siddhāntaḥ ca anantara-ukti^ā sādhya-śabdasya viśeṣaṇam bhavati
115.02	siddhāntaḥ anantaram avadhārita-tvāt yadi anantara-ukti^ā siddhāntaḥ viśeṣaṇam
115.03	tad-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti kāryam na sarva-tantra-siddhānta-nirākaraṇa-artha-tvāt/
115.04	yadi tad-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti ucyate sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ api prakṛta-tvāt
115.05	tad-śabdena anukṛṣyeta iti tad-nirdeśah api pratijñā iti syāt ataḥ sādhya-
115.06	grahaṇena sādhyaḥ siddhāntaḥ sambaddhyate na sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ iti sāmarthyāt
115.07	sarva-tantra-siddhānta-nirākaraṇam iti/ yadi api ayam sāmānya-śabdaḥ
115.08	tad-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti tathā api sāmarthyāt sarva-tantra-siddhāntaḥ nirākriyate
115.09	sarva-tantra-siddhāntasya asādhya-tvāt itarasya ca pratitantra-ādi^aḥ avasthāyām
115.10	sādhya-tvāt iti/ udāharaṇam brāhmaṇān bhojaya iti aśeṣa-brāhmaṇa-
115.11	bhojanasya aśakya-tvāt sāmarthyāt niyamaḥ iti/ yadi sāmarthyam āsthīyate
115.12	samastasya anabhidhānam tad-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti etad api na kartavyam pratijñā iti
115.13	vaktavyam/ sāmarthyataḥ niyamaḥ gamyate iti na avocaḥ kim naḥ bādhyate iti
115.14	katham na bādhyate yad abhyupagatam tad nivartate tad-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti jijñāsā-
115.15	ādi-viśeṣaṇāt vā na prasaṅgaḥ sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti jijñāsā-ādi-
115.16	viśeṣaṇāt vā na prasaṅgaḥ sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti jijñāsā-ādibhiḥ
115.17	prakaraṇa-utthāpana-avayavaiḥ viśeṣitam idam vākyam sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti
115.18	yasmin arthe jijñāsā-ādyaḥ  saḥ arthaḥ sādhyaḥ iti tasya nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti/
115.19	tathā ca hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ arhati arthe vā kṛtya-abhidhānam
115.20	sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti arhati arthe kṛtyaḥ sādhanam arhati iti sādhyaḥ tasya
115.21	nirdeśaḥ pratijñā na ca hetu-dṛṣṭāntau sādhana-arhau ataḥÊna prasaṅgaḥ
115.22	karma-karaṇayoḥ dharma-bhedāt vā anyaḥ karma-dharmaḥ anyaḥ karaṇa-dharmaḥ iti kartṛ^aḥ
116.01	īpsitatamam karman iti karma-dharmaḥ na ca itarasya dharmaḥ itara-dharmaḥ bhavitum arthati
116.02	iti karma-nirdeśaḥ ca ayam sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti karaṇa-nirdeśaḥ cākṣuṣa-
116.03	tvāt iti ataḥ na prasaṅgaḥ/ sādhya-asiddhā-siddha-bhedāt vā anyat sādhyam
116.04	anyat siddham anyat ca asiddhām iti sādhyam anyatara-pakṣa-siddham anyataram prati
116.05	karma-tayā yad upādīyate ubhaya-pakṣa-asampratipannam asiddham ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam
116.06	siddham iti tena yathā siddham iti uktena sādhyena asiddhe ca prasaṅgaḥ
116.07	tathā sādhyam iti uktena asiddhe siddha^ ca prasaṅgaḥ atha aprasaktam api deśyate
116.08	sarve arthāḥ siddhāḥ api pratijñā-tvena deśanīyāḥ bhavanti/ atha vā
116.09	sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti pratijñā-ādi-avayava-viṣayaḥ yaḥ arthaḥ saḥÊsādhyaḥ yaḥ
116.10	pratijñā-ādīnām avayavānām viṣayaḥ dharmin^ḥ prasiddha-dharma-viśiṣṭaḥ saḥ dharma-
116.11	antara-adhikaraṇa-tvena sādhyate na tathā hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ prasaṅgaḥ cet ayam
116.12	prasaṅgaḥ asti tasmāt iṣṭa-grahaṇam anarthakam sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti kila
116.13	sādhyasya iṣṭa-grahaṇena aviśeṣita-tvāt sādhyayoḥ hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ prasaṅgaḥ iti
116.14	manyamānaiḥ kaiḥ cit anyathā pakṣa-lakṣaṇāni kriyante tad yathā pakṣaḥ yaḥ
116.15	sādhayitum iṣṭaḥ iti/ atra iṣṭa-grahaṇam tāvat anarthakam sādhya-padena hetu-
116.16	dṛṣṭānta-ābhāsa-ukti^aḥ nirākṛta-tvāt tad-nirākaraṇa-artham iṣṭa-grahaṇam iti ayuktam
116.17	karma-grahaṇāt ca avāptam etad iṣṭam īpsitam iti ca anartha-antaram atha api aniṣṭa-
116.18	pakṣa-vyudāsa-artham iṣṭa-grahaṇam kriyate arthataḥ kila asya na iṣṭam bhavati yad
116.19	svavacana-ādinā virudhyate iti yathā ca vācakāḥ śabdāḥ iti avācakatvam
116.20	ca śabdānām pratijñāyate śabdāḥ eva artha-pratyāyanāya uccāryante
116.21	iti vyāhatam anuṣṇaḥ agniḥ iti pratyakṣa-virodhaḥ aśrāvaṇaḥ śabdaḥ iti
116.22	pratyakṣa-virodham ke cit varṇayanti tad ayuktam indriya-vṛttīnām atīndriya-tvāt
117.01	indriya-vṛttyaḥ  atīndriyāḥ idam anena indriyena gṛhyate na idam anena iti na
117.02	kasya cit pratyakṣam asti kim tu tad-bhāva-abhāva-anuvidhānāt/ rūpa-ādi-
117.03	jñānāt indriya-vṛttyaḥ  anumīyante tasmāt na idam udāharaṇam udāharaṇam tu
117.04	anuṣṇaḥ agniḥ iti yuktam/ āgama-viruddham iti vaiśeṣikasya nityaḥ śabdaḥ
117.05	iti yathā idam api na āgama-viruddham iti paśyāmaḥ/ na hi vaiśeṣikena
117.06	śabda-anitya-tvam āgamataḥ pratipannam api tu anumānāt kāraṇataḥ adhikārāt
117.07	iti evam ādi^aḥ tad api anumāna-viruddham eva/ atha sūtra-kāra-vacanāt
117.08	pratipannaḥ iti āgama-virodhaḥ nanu ca nityaḥ ghaṭaḥ iti ayam api āgama-virodhaḥ
117.09	prāptaḥ tasmāt brāhmaṇena surā peyā iti āgama-virodhaḥ prasiddhi-viruddham tu
117.10	na buddhyāmahe kaḥ ayam prasiddhi-virodhaḥ iti prasiddhiḥ pratyakṣa-ādīnām
117.11	pramāṇānām anyatamena artha-pratipattiḥ yathā candraḥ śaśin^ḥ iti/ tasmāt
117.12	pūrva-pramāṇa-virodhaḥ eva antarbhavati iti na prasiddhi-virodha-abhidhāne pṛthak
117.13	prayojanam paśyāmaḥ śakyam tu pratipattum sarvaḥ eva ayam prasiddhi-virodhaḥ
117.14	iti tad-artha-nirākaraṇa-artham iṣṭa-grahaṇam paṭhanti/ etad ca sarvam na yuktam
117.15	iti paśyāmaḥ/ katham sva-artha-apavāda-ādi-doṣāṇām pratijñā-doṣa-tvena
117.16	abhyupagamāt sarve ete sva-artha-apavāda-ādi-doṣāḥ pratijñā-doṣa-tvena sambhavanti
117.17	iti na pakṣe doṣa-tvena katham iti arthasya tādavasthyāt yathā-bhūtaḥ
117.18	arthaḥ abhidhīyamānaḥ tathā-bhūtaḥ eva anabhidhīyamānaḥ iti vacanam api
117.19	tarhi evam eva iti vacane api ete doṣāḥ mā bhūvan satyam amī na artha-doṣāḥ na
117.20	vacana-doṣāḥ kim tu puruṣa-doṣāḥ ye hi kartṛ-doṣāḥ te kriyā-dvāreṇa
117.21	udbhāvyante iti kriyāyām kartṛ-doṣam upacarya duṣṭā kriyā iti ucyate
118.01	evam vaktṛ-doṣān vacane upacarya duṣṭam vacanam iti ucyate/ vastutaḥ tu
118.02	pakṣa-doṣaḥ na arthe na vacane iti arthasya kriyā-sāmarthyāt sarvaḥ arthaḥ svasyām
118.03	svasyām kriyāyām samarthaḥ saḥ tu sva-kriyā-vyatirekena kriyā-antare viniyujyamānaḥ
118.04	kartṛ^aḥ eva akauśalam prakāśayati/ evam vacanam api svasmin arthe
118.05	samartham viṣaya-antare tu prayujyamānam vaktṛ^aḥ saṃmīham prakaṭayati/ pratijñāyāḥ
118.06	pakṣa-viṣaya-tvāt pratijñā-doṣāḥ pakṣa-doṣāḥ iti ucyante na etad yuktam
118.07	hetu-ādi-doṣāṇām api pakṣa-doṣa-tva-prasaṅgāt/ evam sati hetu-ādyaḥ  api
118.08	pakṣa-viśeṣāḥ iti kṛtvā hetu-ādi-doṣāḥ api pakṣa-doṣāḥ bhavanti tataḥ ca
118.09	dūṣaṇam pakṣa-doṣaḥ iti vaktavyam na punar dūṣaṇāni nyūnatā-avayava-uttara-doṣa-
118.10	ākṣepa-tayā udbhāvanāni iti/ atha vācya-vacaka-bhāvena niyamaḥ bhaviṣyati iti
118.11	na sādhya-sādhaka-bhāvena iti na yuktam pramāṇa-abhāvāt iti sādhana-
118.12	viṣaya-tvena avatiṣṭhate pakṣaḥ vacana-viṣaya-tvena ca tatra vācya-vācaka-bhāva-
118.13	niyamāt vacana-doṣāḥ pakṣe bhavantu, mā bhūvan sādhya-sādhaka-bhāvāt/ sādhana-
118.14	doṣāḥ pakṣa-doṣāḥ iti na pramāṇam asti vacana-doṣāḥ ca pakṣe upacaryante
118.15	na hi sambhave sati upacāraḥ labhyate tad yathā mañcāḥ krośanti iti
118.16	kroṣana-kriyāyāḥ mañce asambhavāt laukika-vākya-ākhyānam kurvatā mañca-sthāḥ
118.17	iti sthāniṣu puruṣa^su na mañca-śabdam āropya pramāṇa-asambhavena upacāraḥ
118.18	kriyate mañcāḥ krośanti iti/ na punar pratijñā-ādi-doṣāḥ pratijñāyām
118.19	na sambhavanti yataḥ tatra asambhavantaḥ avaśyam anvākhyeya-tayā vyavasthitāḥ pakṣe
118.20	upacaryeran iti ākasmikam ca mukhya-artha-vyatikramam kurvāṇaḥ yad uktam naiyāyike
118.21	hetu-pratiṣedhe na ākasmikaḥ mukhya-artha-vyatikramaḥ labhyate iti
119.01	tad vyāhanyeta/ atha iṣṭa-grahaṇena laukikaḥ abhidhīyate iṣṭam abhipretam iti/
119.02	ayam api arthaḥ sādhanāt eva gamyate na hi kaḥ cit aniṣṭam sādhayati
119.03	yaḥ api aniṣṭam bhayāt tasya api aniṣṭa-nivṛttiḥ iṣṭā iti na aniṣṭam
119.04	sādhayati tasmāt iṣṭa-grahaṇam anarthakam/ evam vicāraṇāyām iṣṭaḥ arthaḥ
119.05	pakṣaḥ iti atra api iṣṭa-grahaṇam anarthakam kā ca iyam vicāraṇā yadi sādhana-
119.06	dūṣaṇa-prayogaḥ tatra aneka-artha-sannipāte na jñāyate katham iṣṭam iti kim
119.07	sādhya-tvena sādhan-tvena dūṣaṇa-tvena vā iti/ atha saṃśayaḥ vicāraṇā
119.08	tad yathā ātmanaḥ astitva-nāstitva-vicāraṇāyām iti ātmanaḥ astitva-nāstitva-
119.09	saṃśayaḥ iti yāvat uktam bhavati tāvat vaktavyam bhavati ātman'no ātmanaḥ astitva-
119.10	nāstitva-vicāraṇāyām iti saṃśayaḥ vicāraṇā-padasya na ayam arthaḥ na
119.11	vicāraṇā saṃśaya-padasya vicāraṇā hi nāma saṃśaya-uttara-kāla-bhāvinī
119.12	sādhana-dūṣaṇa-prayoga-rūpā anyatara-adhikaraṇa-nirṇaya-avasānā sā na saṃśayaḥ
119.13	iti saṃśayaḥ na punar avadharaṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ iti na ca vāde saṃśayaḥ
119.14	bhavati tayor niśicita-tvāt niścitau hi vivādam kurutaḥ iti
119.15	tena sadhya-tvena īpsitaḥ pakṣaḥ iti pratyuktam/ īpsita-grahaṇasya aniṣṭa-
119.16	nirākaraṇa-artha-tvāt viruddha-artha-anirākṛtaḥ iti na vaktavyam/ pakṣaḥ
119.17	yaḥ sadhayitum iṣṭaḥ iti atra iṣṭa-grahaṇena aniṣṭa-pakṣa-nirākaraṇam iha api īpsita-
119.18	grahaṇāt aniṣṭa-pakṣa-nirāsaḥ iti uttara-kārikā-arddhaḥ na vaktavyaḥ viruddha-
119.19	artha-anirākṛtaḥ iti/ atha viruddha-artha-anirākṛtaḥ iti anena aniṣṭāḥ
119.20	pakṣa-doṣāḥ nirākriyante īpsita-grahaṇam tarhi vyartham/ atha ubhayam
119.21	īpsita-grahaṇam viruddha-artha-anirākṛtaḥ iti ca pakṣa-doṣāṇām nirākartṛ
119.22	tam vā pakṣaḥ yaḥ sādhayitum iṣṭaḥ iti atra api viruddha-artha-anirākṛtaḥ
120.01	iti kartavyam/ sarvathā ete pakṣa-lakṣaṇe vicāryamāṇe ekasya nyūnam ekasya
120.02	adhikyam khyāpayate iti/ svayam sādhya-tvena īpsitaḥ iti ca svayam-grahaṇam
120.03	na kartavyam/ kim kāraṇam/ karmaṇaḥ kartṛ-apekṣa-tvāt yathā kartṛ^ḥ vṛkṣam
120.04	chinatti iti ukte svayam it gamyate na hi anyasya chedyam anyaḥ chinatti/ evam
120.05	yasya sādhyam saḥ sādhayitṛ^ḥ iti svayam iti gamyate/ saḥ aym parasya akṣara-
120.06	adhikya-doṣān avivicya sva-vacana-yoga^su ssṃmūḍhaḥ iti/ yad api vādavidhāna-
120.07	ṭīkāyām sādhayati iti śabdasya svayam parena ca tulya-tvāt
120.08	svayam iti viśeṣaṇam sādhayati iti kila ayam śabdaḥ prayojye prayoktṛ^i
120.09	ca tulya-rūpaḥ bhavati iti/ satyam/ tulya-rūpaḥ eṣaḥ śabdaḥ na punar
120.10	sādhāraṇa-prayoge prayojanam paśyāmaḥ/ ataḥ evam vaktavyam/ pakṣaḥ yaḥ
120.11	sādhayitum iṣṭaḥ iti/ tattva-bhāktayoḥ ca tattva-sampratipatti^aḥ prayoktṛ^i
120.12	asampratipatti^aḥ yadi api ayam tulya-rūpaḥ śabdaḥ tathā api prayojyaḥ eva sambhavati
120.13	sādhayati iti na prayoktṛ^i bhākta-tvāt prayojye ca añjasa-tvāt tumunaḥ ca
120.14	samāna-kartṛke niyamāt sādhayitum iti tumunaḥ prayogaḥ ayam saḥ ca ayam
120.15	samāna-kartṛka^su eva dṛṣṭaḥ/ tad yathā snātum gacchāmi iti na punar anunmattaḥ
120.16	evam brūyāt svayam snātum yāmi iti tathā iha api/ yadi api sādhayati-śabdaḥ
120.17	tulya-rūpaḥ tathā api tumunā viśeṣita-tvāt prayoktṛ^i anatiprasaṅgaḥ yad api
120.18	svayam-śabdena śāstra-anapekṣam abhyupagamam darśayati iti eva uktam/ kim uktam/
120.19	para-avajñānasya ayukta-tvāt iti evam ādi kim punar śāstram yad-apekṣam anapekṣam abhyupagamam
120.20	darśayati nanu śāstram pratyakṣa-āgamābhyām aviruddhaḥ āgamaḥ tad-anapekṣam abhyupagamam
120.21	darśayati iti bruvatā apramāṇa-ekam apramāṇakam artham abhyupaiti iti uktam/ yathā apramāṇakaḥ
121.01	abhupagamaḥ na asau abhyupagantum svastha-ātmanā yuktaḥ na api pratipādayitum
121.02	yuktaḥ iti/ yad api vādavidhi^i sādhya-abhidhānam pratijñā iti
121.03	pratijñā-lakṣaṇam uktam tad api ubhayathā doṣāt na yuktam/ katham iti/ yadi
121.04	tāvat pūrva-prakṛta-pakṣam apekṣamāṇena idam ucyate sādhya-abhidhānam pratijñā iti tadā
121.05	sādhya-grahaṇa-anarthakyam/ prakṛtaḥ pakṣaḥ tad-śabdena abhisambhantsyate iti tad-abhidhānam
121.06	pratijñā iti vaktavyam/ atha pakṣa-anapekṣam svatantram etad lakṣaṇam tathā api
121.07	yaḥ naiyāyika-pratijñāyām doṣaḥ uktaḥ saḥ iha prasaktaḥ yaḥ tu tatra bhavatā
121.08	parīhāraḥ kriyate mama api saḥ eva parīhāraḥ bhaviṣyati iti na vaktavyam/
121.09	mayā anabhyupagamāt bhavatā ca abhyupagata-tvāt yathā nāma kaḥ cit param
121.10	brūyāt mātṛ^ḥ tava bandhakī strī-tvāt iti saḥ tasya uttaram brūyāt/ na
121.11	bandhakītve strītvam hetuḥ kim tu sva-puruṣa-vyatirekena puruṣa-antara-sambandhaḥ
121.12	iti strītvāt bandhakītvam bruvāṇasya mātṛ^aḥ bandhakītvam aparihāryam/ atha
121.13	para-puruṣa-sambandham hetum abhyupeyāt strītvāt bandhakītvam vyāhatam iti tathā
121.14	bhavataḥ api vyāghātaḥ sādhyayoḥ hetu-dṛṣṭāntayoḥ nirdeśaḥ pratijñā prāpnoti
121.15	iti tasmāt apeta-sarva-doṣam idam vākyam sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti//
121.16	[1-1-35]udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt sādhya-sādhanam hetuḥ
121.17	hetu^aḥ avasara-prāptasya lakṣaṇa-apadeśa-dvāreṇa udāharaṇa-sādharmyam iti sūtram
121.18	udāharaṇena sādharmyam udāharaṇa-sādharmyam/ kim punar udāharaṇam
121.19	vakṣayamāṇakam tena sādharmyam samāna-dharmatā yaḥ dharmaḥ sādhye bhavati
121.20	tathā-bhūtaḥ eva udāharaṇe api iti na punar saḥ eva anya-dharmasya anyatra avṛtti^aḥ
122.01	na hi anyasya dharmaḥ anyatra vartate kim tu tad-tulyaḥ saḥ eva iti ucyate iti/ yadi
122.02	punar sādharmyam hetuḥ iti etāvan mātram ucyeta bhavati evam vivakṣita-artha-siddhiḥ
122.03	aniṣṭa-pratiṣedhaḥ tu na syāt sādharmya-mātram hetuḥ syāt iti ataḥ aniṣṭa-pratiṣedha-
122.04	artham udāharaṇa-grahaṇam udāharaṇa-sādharmya-grahaṇāt anudāharaṇa-sādharmyam
122.05	arthāt nirākṛtam bhavati avadhāraṇena vā nirākriyate udāharaṇena eva
122.06	sādharmyam na punar anudāharaṇena api sādharmyam eva punar vaidharmyam api iti/ kasya
122.07	punar udāharaṇena sādharmyam kasya anyasya punar syāt sādhyasya prakṛta-tvāt
122.08	pratyāsatti^aḥ ca prakṛtam pratyāsannam ca sādhyam tasmāt udāharaṇa-sādharmyam sādhyasya/
122.09	api ca sādhyena sādharmyam iti sādharmyam eva iti avadhāryate kim punar anena
122.10	avadhāraṇena labhyate sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ ahetu iti labhyate/ saḥ ayam hetuḥ
122.11	sādhya-udāharaṇābhyām pratisaṃhitaḥ kim punar asya pratisandhānam sādhye
122.12	vyāpakatvam udāharaṇe ca asambhavaḥ evam dvi-lakṣaṇaḥ tri-lakṣaṇaḥ ca hetuḥ labhyate
122.13	udāharaṇena eva sādharmyam iti evam bruvatā anabhyupagata-vipakṣasya api udāharaṇena eva
122.14	sādharmyam iti dvi-lakṣaṇaḥ api hetuḥ bhavati iti uktam/ yadā punar vipakṣam abhyupaiti
122.15	tadā api udāharaṇena eva sādharmyāt na anudāharaṇena iti tad-lakṣaṇaḥ hetuḥ
122.16	iti uktam bhavati/ tad evam dvau api anvaya-vyatirekiṇau yathā vaiśeṣikasya anityaḥ
122.17	śabdaḥ kṛtaka-tvāt sāmānyataḥ asmad-ādi-bāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt vā iti dvau
122.18	anvayinau yathā sarva-anitya-vādinaḥ anitayḥ śabdaḥ prameya-tvāt amūrta-tvāt ca
122.19	tad evam anvaya-vyatireki-bhedavān anvayavān catur-prakāraḥ hetuḥ iti uktam bhavati/
122.20	tad evam hetu-svarūpa-avadhāraṇāt hetvābhāsāḥ nirākṛtāḥ bhavanti/ tatra udāharaṇena
122.21	eva sādharmyam iti anena viparyaya-hetu^aḥ anaikāntikasya ca nirākaraṇam/
123.01	sādharmyam eva udāharaṇena iti anena avadhāraṇena sādhya-avṛttiḥ sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ ca
123.02	nirākṛtaḥ iti/ tatra tāvat viparyaya-hetuḥ aśavaḥ viṣāṇi-tvāt gau viṣāṇi-
123.03	tvāt iti anaikāntikaḥ sādhya-avṛttiḥ cākṣuṣa-tvāt anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ sādhya-
123.04	ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ anityāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ gandhavat-tvāt iti svarūpataḥ tarhi anaikāntika-
123.05	ādi-grahaṇam na kartavyam/ yadi anena hetu-lakṣaṇena evam bhāvyamānena anaikāntika-
123.06	hetu-tva-ādyaḥ  nirākriyante/ nanu anaikāntikaḥ svavyabhicāraḥ savyabhicaraḥ iti evam
123.07	ādi-sūtra-anārambhaḥ na atiprasaktasya niyama-artha-tvāt/ anekadhā hetvābhāsāḥ
123.08	bhinnāḥ iti teṣām niyama-jñāpana-artham anaikāntika-ādi-grahaṇam iti/ udāharaṇa-
123.09	sādharmyāt ca kim anyat sādhya-sādhanam iti eke/ na kila udāharaṇa-sādharmya-
123.10	vyatirekena sādhya-sādhanam asti iti ataḥ evam kartavyam/ svataḥ udāharaṇa-sādharmyam
123.11	hetuḥ iti/ atha punar sādhya-sādhana-śabda-upādānam udāharaṇa-sādharmya-viśeṣaṇa-
123.12	artham evam api pañcamī-apadeśaḥ anarthakaḥ iti/ na hi bhavati nīlāt
123.13	utpalam iti/ anye tu pañcamī-apadeśāt anarthakam anyathā varṇayanti/ artha-
123.14	antare dṛṣṭa-tvāt iti anarthakaḥ iti/ artha-antare kila pañcamī dṛṣṭā yathā
123.15	grāmāt āgacchati/ na punar udāharaṇa-sādharmya-vyatirekena sādhyasya sādhanam
123.16	asti iti ataḥ pañcamī-apadeśaḥ anarthakaḥ iti/ sādhya-sādhana-viśiṣṭam ca udāharaṇa-
123.17	sādharmyam hetu-tvena bruvāṇena abhidheyaḥ hetuḥ iti uktam bhavati bhavati/ tathā
123.18	ca sādhya-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā iti vyāhatam bhavati na ca abhidhāna-abhidheya-
123.19	ātmakaḥ samudāyaḥ dṛṣṭah iti na eva ubhayeṣām avayava-tvam/ tad na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ na
123.20	parīhāra-antaram prayojayati tena eva apākṛta-tvāt udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt
123.21	sādhya-sādhana-vacanam hetuḥ iti eva vyācakṣāṇena samastaḥ eva doṣaḥ apākṛtaḥ
123.22	bhavati iti/ ataḥ na parīhāra-antaram prayojayati sādharmyasya vyabhicāri-
124.01	avyabhicāri-tvat viśeṣaṇa-yogaḥ na tad-vacanasya aprakāraka-tvāt yad tad-
124.02	prakāravat tad viśeṣyate sādharmye ca etad asti na ca vacasi iti vacasaḥ kārakatvam
124.03	na bhavati iti na buddhyāmahe yathā eva arthaḥ prakāravān tathā vacanam
124.04	api iti/ arthaḥ tāvat katham prakāravān ubhayathā-bhāvāt yasmāt ayam arthaḥ
124.05	ubhayathā bhavati nityaḥ anityaḥ mūrtaḥ amūrtaḥ ca vacanam api tarhi ubhayathā
124.06	bhavati iti tulyam/ nitya-anitya-mūrta-amūrta-śabda-abhidheyam iti/ dṛṣṭaḥ ca
124.07	śabde viśeṣaṇa-yogaḥ yathā kim ayam āha go^ḥ iti ayam āha iti-paratayā
124.08	śabdaḥ śabda-antarāt vyavacchidyate dvayam vacana-viśeṣakāṇi bahūni vākyāni
124.09	prayuktāni/ tad yathā sva-para-pakṣayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti/ atha
124.10	vacanam na evam-vidham iti siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti evam ādi vyāhatam/
124.11	saḥ ayam sūkṣmaḥ akṣikayā parasya doṣa-anabhidhānah lokāt api apabhraṣṭaḥ
124.12	iti/ yadi api artha-antare dṛṣṭa-tvāt iti anarthakaḥ pañcamī-apadeśaḥ iti evam bruvāṇaḥ
124.13	sva-siddhāntam bādhate na hi bhavantaḥ senā-vana-ādīni artha-antara-bhūtāni pratipadyante
124.14	atha ca pañcamī-apadeśaḥ bhavati vanāt ayam vṛkṣaḥ ānītaḥ senātaḥ aśvaḥ
124.15	iti/ dṛṣṭaḥ ca tantra-antare pañcamī-apadeśaḥ anartha-antare sandhi-vigrahābhyām
124.16	ṣāḍguṇyam sampadyate/ anabhyupagata-artha-antara-viśeṣasya ca hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ
124.17	iti/ pañcamīā artha-antara-vācaka-tvam kṛtaḥ yad api udāharaṇa-sādharmyasya iti
124.18	vaktavyam na udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt iti tad api na vivakṣātaḥ kāraka-śabda-
124.19	prayogāt yadā sadharmyam abhidhīyamāna-tayā vivakṣitam bhavati udāharaṇa-
124.20	sādharmyasya iti yuktam/ yada tu udāharaṇa-sādharmyasya nimitta-bhāvaḥ
124.21	vacane vivakṣitaḥ tadā udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt iti nimitta-pañcamī yuktā katham
125.01	punar udāharaṇa-sadharmyasya nimitta-bhāvaḥ sati bhāvāt yasmāt udāharaṇa-
125.02	sādharmyam buddhvā vivakṣā-prayatna-vāyu-udīraṇa-tālu-ādi-abhighāta-ādyaḥ  śabdasya
125.03	nimittam bhavanti ataḥ pāramparyena udāharaṇa-sādharmyam api nimittam iti
125.04	tasmāt pañcamī-abhidhānam eva jyāyaḥ udāharaṇam anityaḥ śabdaḥ utpatti-
125.05	dharmaska-tvāt utpatti-dharmakam anityam sthālī-ādi dṛṣṭam iti/ kim punar
125.06	anityam nāma yasya anityatvam atha anityatvam kim ubhaya-
125.07	anta-avacchinna-vastu-sattā-sambandhaḥ sattā vā tad-viśeṣaṇā atha utpatti-dharmakam
125.08	kim utpatti-dharmaḥ yasya saḥ utpatti-dharman^ḥ utpatti-dharman^ḥ eva utpatti-dharmakaḥ
125.09	kā punar iyam utpattiḥ asat-viśeṣaṇasya sataḥ atyantam abhāva-bhāva-pratiṣedhaḥ
125.10	sat-asat-viśeṣaṇam sat bhavati tad-pūrvam na āsīt paścāt bhavati iti gamyate tasya
125.11	atyantam abhāvaḥ atyantam ca bhāvaḥ iti pratiṣedhaḥ yaḥ saḥ utpatti-śabda-arthaḥ
125.12	sattā vā evam-viśeṣaṇa vākya-artha-abhyanujñānāt ca padārtha-abhyanujñātaḥ yaḥ vākyasya
125.13	arthaḥ abhūtvā bhavati iti saḥ padasya arthaḥ utpadyate iti/ sā utpattiḥ dharmaḥ
125.14	yasya saḥ bhavati utpatti-dharmakaḥ iti//
125.15	[1-1-35]tathā vaidharmyāt
125.16	kim etāvat hetu-lakṣaṇam iti na iti ucyate kim tarhi tathā vaidharmyāt/
125.17	atra api udāharaṇa-vaidharmyāt iti udāharaṇena eva vaidharmyam na anudāharaṇena iti vaidharmyam
125.18	eva udāharaṇena sādharmyam api iti udāharaṇam anityaḥ śabdaḥ utpatti-
125.19	dharmaka-tvāt anutpatti-dharmakam nityam dṛṣṭam ātma-ādi-dravyam iti bhāṣyam/
125.20	etad tu na samañjasam iti paśyāmaḥ prayoga-mātra-bhedāt, prayoga-mātram hi
125.21	bhidyate na arthaḥ iti/ na ca prayoga-mātra-bhedāt vastu-antaram bhavitum arhati iti
126.01	udāharaṇa-mātra-bhedāt ca udāharaṇa-mātram kevalam bhidyate ātman^ḥ ghaṭaḥ
126.02	iti/ yadi ca udāharaṇa-bhedāt bhedaḥ bhavati tathā vaidharmyāt iti na
126.03	paṭhitavyam/ kim kāraṇm/ udāharaṇa-bhedāt eva bhedaḥ gamyate udāharaṇa-
126.04	sūtram ca bhedakam asti tad-viparyayāt vā viparītam iti tasmāt na idam
126.05	udāharaṇam nyāyam iti/ udāharaṇam tu na idam nirātmakam jīvat-śarīram
126.06	aprāṇa-ādimat-tva-prasaṅgāt iti/ yad ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam aprāṇa-ādimat
126.07	tad sarvam nirātmakam dṛṣṭam na ca idam prāṇa-ādimat bhavati tasmāt na idam nirātmakam
126.08	iti/ saḥ ayam avītaḥ para-pakṣa-pratiṣedha-arthaḥ eva bhavati tau etau
126.09	vīta-avīta-hetū lakṣaṇābhyām pṛthak-abhihitau iti/ tatra svarūpena artha-paricchedakatvam
126.10	vīta-dharmaḥ avītaḥ punar para-pakṣa-pratiṣedhena eva pravartate iti/
126.11	ekasya vidhīyamānaḥ arthaḥ itarasya pratiṣidhyamānaḥ katham punar ayam avītaḥ
126.12	artha-paricchedakaḥ iti/ atha yaḥ abhyanujñātaḥ vītaḥ artha-paricchedakaḥ
126.13	saḥ katham anvayāt iti cet vītaḥ hetuḥ anvayena pratipādayati/ atha
126.14	prameyatvam kasmāt na hetuḥ yadi anvayasya etad sāmarthyam vyabhicārāt prameyatvam
126.15	na hetuḥ na tarhi anvayaḥ pratipādakaḥ api tu avyabhicāraḥ yadi ca avyabhicārāt
126.16	hetuḥ artham pratipādayati, tadā adeśyam etad katham avītaḥ pratipādakaḥ
126.17	iti avītasya api vyatireka-avyabhicāri-tvāt pratipādaka-tvam
126.18	iti/ katham yāvat apramāṇa-ādimat aprāṇa-ādimat tad sarvam nirātmakam dṛṣṭam iti
126.19	aprāṇa-ādimattvam ca jīvat-śarīrāt nivartate tasmāt tad-avyabhicāri-nirātmakatvam
126.20	api nivartsyati iti/ atha punar aprāṇa-ādimattva-nivṛttiḥ eva pratipādyate
126.21	na punar nirātmakatvasya na yuktam evam bhavitum kim kāraṇam pakṣe
127.01	evam sati vyabhicāraḥ darśitaḥ syāt pakṣa-vyabhicāra-upadarśane sati
127.02	śakyam vaktum anvayini tu hetu^i śabde nityatvam astu sati kṛtakatvaḥ
127.03	iti tasmāt yathā anvayinaḥ anvaya-sambandha-avyabhicāraḥ pratipādakaḥ tathā
127.04	vyatirekiṇaḥ api vyatireka-avyabhicāraḥ iti/ etena yathā avīta-hetu^aḥ
127.05	avyabhicāriṇaḥ ekasya dharmasya darśanāt itara-dharma-anumānam evam vīta-hetu^i
127.06	api eka-dharma-nivṛtti-darśanāt itara-dharma-nivṛtti-anumānam iti/ sarva-ātmaka-
127.07	tva-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ yadi eka-dharma-nivṛtti-darśanāt
127.08	itara-dharma-nivṛttiḥ anumīyate tad-nivṛtti^ā ca sātmaka-tvam siddhyati iti manyase
127.09	evam ca sarva-ātmakam śarīram prāpnoti kim kāraṇam aprāṇa-ādi-nivṛtti^aḥ
127.10	aḍittha-ādi-nivṛtti-pratipādaka-tvāt na idam aḍittha-vat-śarīram prāṇa-ādimat-tva-
127.11	prasaṅgāt evam aḍittha-ādi-pratiṣedhaḥ api vaktavyaḥ iti/ tad ca na evam vikalpa-
127.12	anupapatti^aḥ edam tāvat bhavān pṛṣṭaḥ vyācaṣṭām kim avadhārita-kārya-svabhāvaḥ
127.13	padārthaḥ ḍittha-śabda-vācyaḥ āho na iti/ yadi avadhārita-kārya-svabhāvaḥ
127.14	saṃjñā-bheda-mātram ātman^ḥ saḥ iti/ atha anavadhārita-kārya-svabhāvaḥ tasya
127.15	vyāvṛttiḥ aśakyā pratipādayitum vyāvṛtti-pradhānam ca idam vākyam tasmāt
127.16	avīta-hetu^aḥ artham abuddhvā sarva-ātmaka-tā deśyate iti/ yadi tarhi asādhāraṇaḥ
127.17	dharmaḥ hetuḥ nityā pṛthivī gandhavat-tvāt iti ādyaḥ  hetu^āḥ prāpnuvanti na
127.18	hetu-artha-aparijñānāt satyam asādhāraṇaḥ vaidharmya-hetuḥ na punar vaidharmyam
127.19	vyabhicārin gandhavattvam ca nitya-anitya-vyabhicārin tasmāt anvayinaḥ vyatirekiṇaḥ
127.20	ca na anvaya-vyatirekau hetu-bhāve nimittam kim tu anvaya-vyatirekayoḥ
127.21	avyabhicāraḥ iti/ yaḥ punar asādhāraṇaḥ dharmaḥ pakṣaḥ eva kevalam
127.22	yasya tad-tulya-vipakṣau na staḥ saḥ kasmāt na hetuḥ yathā sarvam nityam sattvāt
128.01	satyam ayam asādhāraṇaḥ na vyāvṛttaḥ avyāvṛtti^aḥ hetuḥ iti etena yaḥ
128.02	pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sapakṣa-vipakṣaḥ saḥ na hetu iti uktam bhavati/
128.03	yathā sarvam nityam amūrta-tvāt iti yaḥ pakṣa-ekadeśe vartate avidyamāna-sapakṣaḥ
128.04	vipakṣāt ca nivartate yathā anabhyupagata-nitya-pakṣasya nitye vāc-manase srāvaṇa-
128.05	tvāt iti saḥ kasmāt na hetuḥ ayam api na hetuḥ sūtra-arthena apodita-tvāt
128.06	sūtra-arthaḥ tathā vaidharmyāt iti atra ca vaidharmyam udāharaṇena eva vaidharmyam eva
128.07	ca udāharaṇena na punar idam vaidharmyam eva tasmāt na prasaṅgaḥ/ evam anayoḥ vīta-
128.08	avīta-hetu^oḥ pṛthak-artha-pratipādaka-tvam āmumāram prasiddhi^aḥ yad uktam vīta-avītau
128.09	na pṛthak pratipatti-hetū iti tad saṃmūḍhena uktam hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ iti anye/
128.10	anye tu anyathā lakṣaṇam vyācakṣate hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ iti yadi punar
128.11	etāvat-mātram ucyeta hetuḥ viśeṣaḥ iti kim syāt viśeṣa-mātram hetuḥ syāt
128.12	sādharmya-mātra-nirākaraṇam ca syāt sādharmya-mātra-nirākaraṇe viśeṣa-mātra-
128.13	parigrahe ca iṣṭa-aniṣṭa-saṃgrahaḥ iti/ ataḥ aniṣṭa-hetu-nirākriyāyai vipakṣa-
128.14	grahaṇam vipakṣāt yaḥ viśeṣaḥ saḥ hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ hetuḥ iti etāvati
128.15	codyamāne vipakṣāt viśeṣa-mātrasya hetu-tvam saḥ ca aneka-bhedaḥ tad-nivṛtti-artham
128.16	vipakṣāt eva avadhāryate avadhāraṇe ca etasmin vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ ekaḥ
128.17	anuṣajyate go^ḥ viṣāṇi-tvāt saḥ hi vipakṣāt eva viśeṣaḥ iti na ayam
128.18	anena avadhāraṇena nirākriyate iti tad-nivṛtti^i dvitīyam asādhāraṇam
128.19	viśeṣaḥ eva ca vipakṣāt na tu ayam viśeṣaḥ eva vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛtti-tvāt
128.20	sāmānyam api tad evam ābhyām avadhāraṇābhyām anvaya-vyatirekin^ḥ tri-lakṣaṇaḥ
128.21	hetuh labhyate iti/ satyam ekaḥ tri-lakṣaṇaḥ hetuḥ labhyate samāna-pakṣa-ekadeśa-
129.01	vṛttiḥ ekaḥ tri-lakṣaṇaḥ eva nirākriyate iti/ katham iti/ ādyena
129.02	avadhāraṇena vipakṣāt eva yaḥ viśeṣaḥ saḥ hetuḥ iti bruvatā yaḥ tulya-ekadeśa-
129.03	vṛttiḥ saḥ nivartitaḥ bhavati na hi ayam vipakṣāt eva viśeṣaḥ kim tu
129.04	sapakṣa-ekadeśāt api iti vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛtti-pratiṣedhāt samāna-pakṣāt yaḥ viśeṣaḥ
129.05	eva saḥ hetuḥ iti uktam bhavati yathā aśvaḥ ayam viṣāṇi-tvāt na punar ayam prayatna-
129.06	anantarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ vipakṣād eva viśeṣaḥ kim tu tad-tulya-ekadeśāt api yathā
129.07	śrūyamāṇakena arthena avadhāraṇe arthavatī/ evam ca sati tulya-ekadeśa-vṛtti-vat
129.08	sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛtti^aḥ api hetutva-prasaṅgaḥ yena eva sāmarthyena tad-tulya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ
129.09	labhyate tena eva sāmarthyena anumeya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ api hetuḥ prāptaḥ/ yathā
129.10	nityāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ gandhavat-tvāt iti/ te ete avadhāraṇe yadi tāvat
129.11	yathā-śrute bhavataḥ tataḥ dṛṣṭaḥ prayatna-anantarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ na hetuḥ/ atha anyathā
129.12	śrūyate anyathā prakalpyate abhipreta-artha-siddhi-artham tathā api abhipreta-prāpti-vat
129.13	aniṣṭaḥ api gandhavat-tva-ādiḥ labhyate iti ubhayam ca lakṣaṇa-doṣaḥ abhipreta-
129.14	hāniḥ aniṣṭa-prasaṅgāt ca/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ vṛtti^i viśeṣita-tvāt aniṣṭasya gandhavat-
129.15	tva-ādi^aḥ prāptiḥ iti ataḥ doṣaḥ/ yasmāt vṛtti^i āha dharmaḥ pakṣasya iti/ anena
129.16	cākṣuṣa-tva-ādiḥ pakṣa-asambhavin^ḥ nirākriyate pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ tu katham na
129.17	hi asau na pakṣa-dharmaḥ na bhavati tathā vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ na pakṣasya dharmaḥ iti
129.18	tasmāt anyathā nirākaraṇe yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ iti na kartavyaḥ avadhāraṇāt nivṛtti^aḥ/
129.19	avadhāraṇāt ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ nivartiṣyate kim punar avadhāryate kim dharmaḥ eva
129.20	pakṣasya uta pakṣasya eva dharmaḥ iti vastu tāvat dharmaḥ eva pakṣasya iti kim punar
129.21	asya avadhāraṇasya sāmarthyam kim anena sambhavaḥ jñāpyate uta asambhavaḥ
129.22	vyāvartyate ubhayathā api na avadhāraṇasya sāmarthyam asti sambhava-jñapti^aḥ asambhava-
130.01	nivṛtti^aḥ ca avadhāraṇam antareṇa api avagati^aḥ na ca ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ nirākriyate iti
130.02	vyartham avadhāraṇam/ atha punar vyāptiḥ asya avadhāraṇasya arthaḥ dharmaḥ eva pakṣasya iti
130.03	na punar adharmaḥ api yaḥ punar pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ asau adharmaḥ api satyam anena ekadeśa-
130.04	vṛttiḥ nirākriyate iti etasmin api ekam sandhitsate idam anyat pracyavate/
130.05	bhavatu ekadeśa-vṛtti^aḥ nirākaraṇam sapakṣe siddhaḥ etad-padam kim artham ārabhyate iti
130.06	katham punar idam anārabhyam yasmāt vyāpti-arthena avadhāraṇena carita-artha-tvāt/
130.07	dharmaḥ eva pakṣasya iti pakṣaḥ niyataḥ dhārmaḥ aniyataḥ tasya anityatasya vyāpti-
130.08	avyāptibhyām tad-tulya-pakṣa-gatiḥ tatra vipakṣe na asti iti sapakṣe siddhaḥ iti etad-padam
130.09	mā bhūt/ atha punar idam avadhāraṇam anyathā kriyate pakṣasya eva iti/ evam
130.10	anantare dvi^i api pakṣe siddhaḥ vipakṣe na asti iti ca bādhite bhavataḥ katham
130.11	sapakṣe siddhaḥ iti etad-avadhāraṇa-artha-bādhitam pakṣasya eva dharmaḥ sapakṣe siddhaḥ
130.12	iti/ na hi loke evam-prakārāṇi vākyāni pravartante devadattam eva
130.13	bhojaya yajñadattam ca uttaram tu padam vipakṣe na asti iti gamyamāna-tvāt akāryam
130.14	kena gamyamāna-tvāt iti avadhāraṇena pakṣasya eva dharmaḥ sāmānyasya iti gamyate
130.15	iti vipakṣe na asti iti etad na kartavyam iti na ca pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ nirākriyate
130.16	iti yad-artham avadhāraṇam saḥ eva arthaḥ hīyate iti/ atha punar
130.17	sapakṣe siddhaḥ iti etad api padam avadhāraṇa-artham eva ārabhyate sapakṣe eva siddhaḥ
130.18	siddhaḥ eva sapakṣe iti kim punar asmin padena avadhāraṇena labhyate yadi
130.19	sambhavaḥ saḥ anavadhāritaḥ api gamyate sapakṣe siddhaḥ/ atha vyāptiḥ tad-jātīyasya
130.20	ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ ahetuḥ iti prāptam/ atha sapakṣe eva siddhaḥ iti tathā api
130.21	pūrva-uttare pade vyāhate bhavataḥ pakṣasya dharmaḥ vipakṣe na asti iti avadhāraṇa-
131.01	artha-bādhita-tvāt pūrvam anārabhyam gamyamāna-tvāt uttaram api/ atha vipakṣe
131.02	na asti it etad api avadhāraṇa-artham eva iti/ atra api kim avadhāryate vipakṣe
131.03	eva na asti iti na asti eva vipakṣe iti/ yadi vipakṣe eva na asti tadā
131.04	go^ḥ viṣāṇi-tvāt iti hetuḥ prāptaḥ saḥ hi vipakṣe na asti iti/
131.05	atha vipakṣe na asti eva iti na punar madhyasya padasya anārambhaḥ tatra pūrva-padam
131.06	vyāpti-artham uttara-padam vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛtti-pratiṣedha-artham atha sapakṣe siddhaḥ
131.07	iti etad padam kim-artham ārabhyate iti yadi sambhava-artham vyāpti-arthena ādya-avadhāraṇena
131.08	sambhavaḥ labhyate iti vyartham/ tad etasmin lakṣaṇe saha-vṛtti^ā
131.09	vicāryamāṇe aniṣṭa-hetu-upanipātaḥ vā iṣṭa-hetu-parityāgaḥ vā abhipreta-
131.10	grasta-hāniḥ vā iti doṣaḥ hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ iti/ yadā sautrāntika-pakṣam
131.11	āśritya lakṣaṇam vicāryate tadā vipakṣa-asambhavāt hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ
131.12	iti vipakṣa-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ na hi asati vipakṣe vipakṣa-artham paśyāmaḥ iti/
131.13	na ca asati pañcamīāḥ prayogaḥ sambhavati vipakṣāt iti pañcamī-viṣayaḥ
131.14	vācyaḥ asati vipakṣe na adhikaraṇa-arthaḥ sambhavati iti vipakṣe na asti iti
131.15	saptamī-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ vipakṣa-asambhave sati pakṣa-tad-jātīya-vyāpaka-tvāt kṛtaka-
131.16	tva-ādyaḥ  viśeṣaḥ arthaḥ cintyaḥ/ kṛtaka-tvāt iti tadā asādhāraṇatvam asāmānyam
131.17	viśeṣaḥ iti/ tad etad-lakṣaṇam sva-siddhāntena asaṅgata-tvāt alakṣaṇam iti
131.18	tad api hetuvārttikam kurvāṇena uktam/ saptikā-sambhave ṣaṭ-pratiṣedhāt eka-
131.19	dvi-pada-paryudāsena tri-lakṣaṇaḥ hetuḥ iti tad api ayuktam dvi-pada-lakṣaṇa-yuktayoḥ
131.20	hetu-tvāt tatra kila saptikā sambhavati pakṣasya eva dharmaḥ samāne eva
132.01	siddhaḥ/ vipakṣe eva na asti pakṣasya dharmaḥ samāne ca siddhaḥ
132.02	pakṣasya dharmaḥ vipakṣe na asti samāne eva ca siddhaḥ, vipakṣe na asti
132.03	pakṣasya dharmaḥ, samāne ca siddhaḥ vipakṣe ca na asti iti/ tad evam saptikā-
132.04	sambhave ti sati ṣaṭ-pratiṣedham uktvā yad ayam tri-pada-parigrahena tri-lakṣaṇasya
132.05	hetu-tām śāsti tena eka-dvi-pada-paryudāsam jñāpayati iti tad ca na evam/ dvi-
132.06	lakṣaṇa-yuktayoḥ hetu-tvāt iti anabhyupagata-nitya-pakṣasya kṛtaka-tvāt iti ayam
132.07	dvābhyām lakṣaṇābhyām yujyate prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvāt iti ca anayoḥ hetu-bhāvaḥ
132.08	na syāt yaḥ ca ayam vyatirekin^ḥ hetuḥ na idam nirātmakam jīvat-śarīram
132.09	aprāṇa-ādimat-tva-prasaṅgāt iti evam ādiḥ saḥ na hetuḥ syāt dvi-pada-yogāt
132.10	iti tasmāt hetuḥ vipakṣāt viśeṣaḥ iti etad-lakṣaṇam vicāryamāṇam abhimatam vā
132.11	hetum āpādayati iṣṭam bādhate grastam nirākaroti śāstram bādhate
132.12	iti/ tatra yaḥ samāna-jātīye dvedhā ca asan tad-atyaye iti anye san sajātīye
132.13	dvedhā ca asan tad-atyaye iti asapakṣe na asti/ etāvatā ca kila
132.14	lakṣaṇena tri-lakṣaṇaḥ hetuḥ labhyate iti yathā-śruti na labhyate/ yadi
132.15	tāvat etad-lakṣaṇam yathā-śruti bhavati vipakṣe vidyate iti kena etad labhyate
132.16	iti/ nanu ca uktam hetuḥ tad-ābhāśaḥ va prāyaḥ pakṣa-dharmaḥ eva bhavati/
132.17	uktam etad na punar anena pakṣa-dharmaḥ hetuḥ iti labhyate kim tu apakṣa-dharmaḥ na hetuḥ
132.18	na hetvābhāsaḥ iti/ atha api arthataḥ labhyeta apakṣa-dharmaḥ hetuḥ na hetvābhāsaḥ
132.19	iti ayam yadi vākya-arthaḥ bhavati arthāt gamyate hetuḥ hetvābhāsaḥ ca pakṣa-dharmaḥ
132.20	iti satyam arthāt pakṣa-dharmaḥ gamyate na tu vyāpake niyataḥ labhyate iti/
132.21	apakṣa-dharma-nivṛtti-mātra-tvena sambhava-mātram labhyate iti/ tad ca dvedhā
132.22	vyāpakam avyāpakam ca/ tatra avyāpakaḥ aniṣṭaḥ hetuḥ prāptaḥ na prāptaḥ pakṣa-dharmaḥ
133.01	eva iti abhidhānāt yaḥ pakṣasya dharmaḥ eva saḥ hetuḥ prāptaḥ na tu ayam pakṣa-dharmaḥ
133.02	eva ekadeśa-vṛtti-tvāt iti na avadhāraṇasya artha-tvāt anya-artham avadhāraṇam
133.03	bhavantaḥ kalpayanti kim artham hetu-hetvābhāsa-niyama-jñāpana-artham tad
133.04	avadhāraṇam anya-artham sat katham avyāpakam nivartayiṣyati/ atha upakārin
133.05	bhavati avyāpaka-nivṛtti-artham hetu-hetvābhāsa-niyama-jñāpana-artham ca tathā api
133.06	san sajātīye dvedhā ca iti akaraṇīyam/ katham, vyāpti-arthena avadhāraṇena
133.07	pakṣaḥ api niyataḥ dharmaḥ ca niyataḥ iti/ aniyatasya vyāpti-avyāptibhyām
133.08	dharmasya dvau rāśī śiṣyamāṇau sajātīyaḥ tad-atyayaḥ tatra atiprasakti^aḥ asan
133.09	tad-atyaye iti san sajātīye iti kim artham/ yadi sambhava-artham
133.10	tad na aniyamāt vyāpti-avyāpti^oḥ sambhavaḥ gamyate iti kim anena san
133.11	sajātīye iti dvedhā ca iti sarvathā na vaktavyam/ idam tāvat
133.12	gamyamāna-tvāt san sajātīye iti na kartavyam/ sat-śabdena vyāpti-
133.13	avyāpti^oḥ sāmānyena abhidhānāt dvedhā ca iti pramatta-vākyam/ atha manyeta
133.14	san sajātīye iti avadhāraṇa-artham ārabhyate iti avadhāraṇe arthaḥ yuktaḥ
133.15	ārambhaḥ avadhāraṇam na yuktam/ katham iti yadi tāvat evam avadhāryate
133.16	san eva sajātīye iti prayatna-anantarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ tena hetuḥ yasmāt
133.17	prayatna-anantarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ san asan api dvidhā ca iti tad avdhāraṇa-artha-
133.18	bādhitam/ na hi bhavati brāhmaṇam eva bhojaya kṣatriyam ca iti/
133.19	atha sajātīye eva san iti avadhāryate tathā api pūrva-uttare bādhite bhavataḥ
133.20	katham/ ekam avadhāraṇa-artham abādhitam ekam gamyamāna-tvāt/ atha punar
133.21	dvidhā ca iti avadhāraṇa-artham/ atra api kim avadhāryate kim sajātīye
133.22	eva dvidhā dvidhā eva sajātīye iti/ yadi tāvat sajātīye eva dvidhā
134.01	na anyatra pakṣe na tad-atyaye tad dvidhā iti uktam bhavati yadi evam san sajātīyam api
134.02	na kartavyam/ prāpti-arthena avadhāraṇena anya-pade sajātīye sambhavasya ukta-tvāt
134.03	dvidhā ca iti anya-pada-dvi-dhā-nivṛtti-para-tvāt dvidhā ca iti abhidhānāt sambhavaḥ
134.04	gamyate iti san sajātīye iti sarvathā na vaktavyam sajātīye eva
134.05	dvidhā ca iti anena eva avadhāraṇena pakṣa-vyāptiḥ labhyate iti dharmaḥ eva pakṣasya iti
134.06	vyāpti-artham avadhāraṇam uktam/ na abhūt sarva-pada-avadhāraṇa-vyāghātaḥ iti
134.07	na anavadhāraṇe tathā api madhyame dvi^i pade api na kartavye/ saḥ ayam gaḍu-
134.08	praveśa-akṣitārakā-nirgama-nyāyaḥ iti/ atha punar dvidhā eva sajātīye iti
134.09	tathā api kṛtaka-tva-ādiḥ na hetuḥ na hi asau sajātīye dvidhā eva tri-lakṣaṇam ca
134.10	hetum bruvāṇena sva-siddhānta-upāttayoḥ dvi-pada-yuktayoḥ ahetu-tvam iti prāptam/
134.11	vyatirekin^ḥ ca na hetuḥ iti prāptam/ na eva hetuḥ iti cet kṛtaḥ tasya
134.12	hetu-tve yatnaḥ iti anuttaram/ atha punar avadhāraṇāni na kriyante
134.13	tathā api pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ anaikāntikaḥ ca anuṣaktaḥ iti/ etena grāhya-dharmaḥ
134.14	tad-aṃśena vyāpti^oḥ hetuḥ iti pratyuktam/ anena avyāpaka-ādiḥ labhyate iti
134.15	ukta-uttaram etad api iti/ asan tad-atyaye iti etad api vipakṣe na asti iti anena
134.16	samānam iti/ tādṛś-avinābhāvi-dharma-upadarśanam hetuḥ iti apare/ tādṛśā
134.17	vinā na bhavati iti anena kila dvayam labhyate vipakṣa-asattvam tādṛś-asattvam ca
134.18	upadarśana-grahaṇena kila pakṣe sattvam labhyate iti evam tri-lakṣaṇa-pravibhāvitṛ^ḥ
134.19	ātman^ḥ pakṣaḥ labhyate iti/ yad tāvat tādṛś-avinābhāvi-grahaṇāt vipakṣe
134.20	asattvam tādṛś-sattvam ca labhyate iti na yuktam/ tādṛśā vinā na
134.21	bhavati iti etasmāt vipakṣe asattvam gamyate tādṛśe tu sambhavaḥ iti etad eva
134.22	kutaḥ na hi ayam niyamaḥ yaḥ vipakṣe na asti saḥ avaśyam tad-jātīye asti iti/
135.01	evam ca śrāvaṇa-tva-ādi api hetuḥ prāptaḥ śrāvaṇa-tva-ādi api tādṛśā vinā na
135.02	bhavati iti/ bhavatu tāvat tādṛś-avinābhāvi-grahaṇe tad-tad-jātīye sambhavataḥ
135.03	saḥ punar pakṣe sambhavati iti etad uktaḥ/ na nu upadarśana-grahaṇāt etad labhyate
135.04	na labhyate upadarśana-grahaṇasya abhidhāna-artha-tvāt upadarśana-grahaṇam abhidhāna-
135.05	artham upadarśyate anena iti na punar asya etad sāmarthyam asti pakṣe anyatra ca iti
135.06	kva punar anyatra syāt yatra sambhavah kva ca asya sambhavaḥ tad-jātīye/ tasmāt
135.07	etad uktam bhavati tad-jātīya-avinābhāvinaḥ tad-jātīye eva abhidhāna-
135.08	hetuḥ iti/ evam ca te cākṣuṣatvam hetuḥ bhavati na hi cākṣuṣatvam anityatvena
135.09	vinābhāvin upety vā bhavatu tādṛś-avinābhāvinaḥ dharmasya pakṣe
135.10	upadarsanam iti/ tathā api pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttim ken nirākaroṣi upadarśana-
135.11	grahaṇena iti cet na tasya sambhava-mātra-jñāpana-artha-tvāt/ atha manyeta
135.12	upadarśana-grahaṇena vyāptiḥ labhyate iti na yuktam upadarśana-grahaṇasya
135.13	sambhava-mātra-jñāpana-artha-tvāt upadarśana-grahaṇena sambhava-mātram jñāpyate pakṣe saḥ
135.14	punar vyāpakaḥ iti na yuktam/ atha atra api kena cit nyāyena avadhāraṇāni
135.15	kriyeran tāni avyāpaka-hetu-nirākaraṇena samarthāni iti pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ
135.16	yad tu idam udāharaṇam etasmin prayatna-anantarīyaka-tva-ādiḥ anityatvasya agni^aḥ dhūmaḥ
135.17	iti/ tatra dhūmaḥ tāvat agni^aḥ apratipādakaḥ iti uktam/ prayatna-anantarīyakam api
135.18	ahetuḥ kutaḥ prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvasya pakṣa-avṛtti-tvāt na hi prayatna-anantarīyaka-
135.19	tvam śabda-dharmaḥ na hi kaḥ cit śabdaḥ prayatna-anantarīyakaḥ asti
135.20	saṃyoga-vibhāga-yoni-tvāt śabdasya/ atha pāramparya-kramena prayatnaḥ śabda-
135.21	kāraṇam pāramparyena sarve arthāḥ śabda-kāraṇam iti viśeṣaṇam ayuktam
135.22	prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvāt iti anityaḥ śabdaḥ yataḥ tad-utpatti^aḥ iti vaktavyam
136.01	yad ca idam prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvam śabdasya anityatve kāraṇam bhāva-abhidhānena
136.02	ucyate kim punar tad kim janman uta upalabdhiḥ iti/ yadi tāvat janman
136.03	tad na eva samartham iti prayatna-anantarīyaka-tva-grahaṇam vyartham/ na hi bhavati
136.04	anityaḥ śabdaḥ devadatta-kṛtaka-tvāt iti/ ataḥ evam vaktavyam janmavat-tvāt iti
136.05	yadi ca idam janman vyabhicārin bhavet tataḥ prayatna-anantrīyaka-tva-grahaṇam
136.06	arthavat kim kāraṇam vyabhicāriṇi viśeṣaṇāni arthavanti bhavanti/
136.07	atha upalabdhiḥ prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvāt iti anena bhāva-abhidhānena ucyate
136.08	evam api upalabdhi^aḥ eva sāmarthyam asti iti viśeṣaṇam/ na hi upalabhyamānam
136.09	dvedhā bhavati nityam anityam prayatna-anantarīkam anyathā ca iti/ atha brūṣe
136.10	prayatna-anantaram upalabhyamānam ghaṭatvam nityam iti tvayā eva etad vyabhicārin tad iti
136.11	śabda-anitya-tva-anumitai^i prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvam hetuḥ avyāpakam ca prayatna-
136.12	anantarīka-tvam śabda-rāśi^aḥ pakṣa^kṛta-tvāt/ atha śabda-viśeṣam pakṣa-
136.13	kṛtya ucyate tatra api ādye eva prayatna-anantarīyaka-tvam sambhavati na dvitīya-
136.14	ādiṣu iti avyāpakatvam anivṛttam/ atha yaḥ prayatna-anantarīyakaḥ
136.15	sah anityaḥ iti tatha api anyaḥ hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ hetu^aḥ pakṣa-viśeṣaṇa-tvena upātta-
136.16	tvāt yad api uktam aprayatna-anantarīyakasya trayī gatiḥ iti kim cit nityam
136.17	ākāśa-ādi anyeṣām kim cit anityam vidyut-ādi kim cit eva ākāśa-kusuma-ādi
136.18	idam mahā-naiyāyika-tvam mahā-vādi-tvam ca yad tāvat asat aprayatna-anantarīyakam
136.19	ca kim cit ca iti citram/ nityam tu kim cit bhavati na punar aprayatna-
136.20	anantarīyaka-tvam na hi ākāśam prayatna-anantarīyakam iti śakyam vaktum
136.21	aprayatna-anantarīyakasya janma-viśeṣita-tvāt na ca ākāśasya janman asti
136.22	tasmāt na prayatna-anantarīyakam ākāśam iti/ etena abhāvaḥ vyākhyātaḥ
137.01	abhāve api evam eva iti/ tad evam etāni na hetu-lakṣaṇāni sambhavanti idam eva ārṣam
137.02	hetu-lakṣaṇam nyāyam iti//
137.03	[1-1-36]sādhya-sadharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ udāharaṇam
137.04	sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ udāharaṇam iti sūtram/
137.05	asya udāharaṇa-upalakṣaṇam arthaḥ/ sādhyasya sādharmyam sādhyena samāna-dharma-tā/
137.06	atra api sādhyena eva sādharmyam na punar asādhyena api sādharmyam eva sādhyena
137.07	punar vaidharmyam api yasmāt sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ bhavati/ kim
137.08	kutaḥ cit sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ na bhavati na na bhavati api yathā
137.09	amūrta-tva-ādiḥ^aḥ karmaṇaḥ tasmāt sādhya-sādharmyāt yatra tad-dharmaḥ bhavati saḥ
