137.10	bhavati tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ yaḥ ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvi-tvena
137.11	viśeṣaṇena yujyamāne udāharaṇam bhavati udāhriyate anena dharmayoḥ
137.12	sādhya-sādhana-bhāvaḥ iti udāharaṇam/ nanu ca karaṇa-kāraka-parigrahāt vacanam
137.13	udāharaṇam dṛṣṭāntaḥ ca arthah na ca anayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam yujyate na
137.14	hi viṣāṇa-ādimat iti abhidhānam gavā samānādhikaraṇam bhavati/ na eṣaḥ
137.15	doṣaḥ vacana-viśeṣaṇa-tvena dṛṣṭāntasya upādānāt na svatantraḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ udāharaṇam/
137.16	kim tu sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvi-tve sati abhidhīyamānaḥ
137.17	iti/ tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ tasya dharmaḥ tad-dharmaḥ tasya iti sādhyasya/ sādhyam ca
137.18	dvedhā bhavati dharmaḥ dharmin^ḥ ca/ ekadā dharmaḥ viseṣaṇa-tvena anyadā dharmin^ḥ
137.19	viśeṣaṇa-tvena iti tasya iti dharmi-grahaṇam yuktam/ na pṛthak dharmasya grahaṇāt
137.20	yasmāt dharmiṇaḥ dharmaḥ utpatti-dharmaka-tva-ādyaḥ  na dharmasya dharme ca sādhye
137.21	udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt iti vyāghātaḥ/ na hi dharmasya udāharaṇena kim cit
137.22	sādharmyam kim tu sādhya-sādharmyam ca udāharaṇasya dharmiṇaḥ eva sādhye ca upasaṃhāraḥ
138.01	dharmiṇi eva tasmāt dharmin^ḥ sādhyaḥ iti tasya dharmiṇaḥ sādhyasya
138.02	yaḥ dharmaḥ sādhyaḥ saḥ yasmin dṛṣṭānte bhavati vidyate saḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
138.03	udāharaṇam/ udāharaṇam sthālī-ādi-dravyam iti tad utpatti-dharmakam
138.04	ca anityam dṛṣṭam iti//
138.05	[1-1-37]tad-viparyayāt vā viparītam
138.06	tad-viparyayāt vā viparītam/ sādhya-vaidharmyāt atad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
138.07	udāharaṇam iti/ udāharaṇam avīta-hetu^i draṣṭavyam iti/ anye tu
138.08	sādhya-sādharmyāt tad-dharma-bhāvi-tvam dṛṣṭāntasya iti sūtram paṭhanti/ teṣām tu ayam
138.09	abhiprāyaḥ dṛṣṭānta-udāharaṇayoḥ mā bhūt aikyam iti/ kim punar tad-dharma-
138.10	bhāvi-tvam sādhya-sādhanābhyām yogaḥ sādhya-sādhana-yoge ca tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ
138.11	bhavati ataḥ bhāva-abhidhānena sādhya-sādhanābhyām yogaḥ abhidhīyate iti/
138.12	etad ca sūtram apare dūṣayanti/ asmākam tu na ayam sūtra-pāṭhaḥ tasmāt na ayam
138.13	doṣaḥ iti tasmin api pāṭhe kila bhāvi-grahaṇam anarthakam/ kasmāt
138.14	sarva-avayavānām sādhya-dharma-bhāvi-tvāt/ yasmāt kila sarva-avayavāḥ
138.15	sādhya-dharma-bhāvinaḥ bhavanti bhāvayanti gamayanti iti na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ
138.16	iti/ anye tu tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ iti etad padam anyathā nirākurvanti tad-dharma-
138.17	bhāvin^ḥ bhavan uṣṇa-bhoji-nyāyena vā bhavet daṇḍi-nyāyena vā bhavet
138.18	tad yadi tāvat uṣṇa-bhoji-nyāyena uṣṇam bhoktum śīlam asya iti uṣṇa-bhojin^ḥ tad-dharmam
138.19	vā bhāvayitum śīlam asya iti tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ/ atra api bhāvayitum gamayitum
138.20	yāvat uktam syāt iti/ na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ iti na kim cit etad daṇḍi-
138.21	nyāyaḥ tu daṇḍaḥ yasya asti iti saḥ daṇḍin^ḥ tad-dharma-bhāvaḥ yasya asti saḥ
139.01	bhavati tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ/ atra bhāvi-grahaṇam anarthakam tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ iti katham
139.02	iti tad-dharmaḥ ca asau bhāvaḥ ca iti tad-dharma-bhavaḥ saḥ yasya asti saḥ bhavati
139.03	tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ/ evam sati viśeṣya-viśeṣaṇa-bhāva-anupapatti^ḥ tad-dharma-bhāvin^ḥ iti
139.04	yuktam na hi tad-dharmaḥ ca syāt abhāvaḥ ca syāt yataḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvaḥ
139.05	samarthitaḥ syāt iti/ ataḥ tad-dharmin^ḥ iti kila vaktavyam/ na vaktavyam/
139.06	tad-dharmāṇām ubhayathā abhāvāt ubhayathā bhavanti dharmāḥ vidhīyamānāḥ
139.07	pratiṣidhyamānāḥ ca/ tatra anvayini vidhīyamāna-dharmavān dṛṣṭānta-grahaṇam
139.08	yathā sthālīām utpatti-dharmaka-tvam anityatvam ca vidyamānau dharmau iti
139.09	tasmāt tad-dharmaḥ ca asau bhāvaḥ ca iti yuktam sāmānādhikaraṇyam yad punar etad
139.10	dharmin^ḥ iti vaktavyam etad api na parijñātam/ na hi bahuvrīhi^i punar
139.11	matu-arthīyaḥ labhyate iti/ atha avaśyam vayam śikṣayitavyāḥ/ tatra evam
139.12	tad-dharman^ḥ iti vaktavyam iti/ santi ca evam-vādinaḥ yeṣām kṛtakatva-anityatve
139.13	abhāvaḥ iti/ tad-nirākaraṇa-artham ca idam vaktavyam tad-dharmaḥ ca asau bhāvaḥ ca iti/
139.14	tad etasmin avayava-traye evam lakṣaṇena upapādite teṣām trayaḥ durvibhāvāḥ
139.15	iti/ anena vākyena mahā-naiyāyika-tvam ātmanaḥ khyāpitam bhavati/
139.16	tathā siddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti anye/ katham punar asya arthaḥ yathā sādhyaḥ sādhayitum
139.17	iṣṭaḥ tathā ayam asiddhaḥ anityatvena yathā ca viśiṣṭaḥ pratyaya-bheda-
139.18	bhedi-tvena tathā yaḥ siddhaḥ saḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhya-sādhanavān iti/ atra
139.19	visphūrjitā api sva-siddhāntam atyajatā kṛtakatva-anityatvayoḥ na śakyaḥ
139.20	sambandhaḥ darśayitum kṛtakatva-anityatvayoḥ kāla-bhedāt vastu-bādhanāt ca/
139.21	anyadā kṛtakatvam anyadā ca anityatvam iti yadā ca vastu na tadā prāc-
139.22	pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau staḥ iti/ etena tayoḥ sambandhi-nidarśanam dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti
140.01	pratyuktam/ sādhyena anugamaḥ hetu^ḥ sādhya-abhāve ca nāstitā iti ca//
140.02	[1-1-38]udāharaṇa-apekṣaḥ tathā iti upasaṃhāraḥ na tathā iti vā sādhyasya
140.03	upanayaḥ
140.04	udāharaṇa-apekṣaḥ tathā iti upasaṃhāraḥ na tathā iti vā sādhyasya upanayaḥ/
140.05	evam ca ayam kṛtakaḥ iti yathā tathā iti pratibimbana-artham kim punar atra
140.06	partibimbanam dṛṣṭānta-gatasya dharmasya avyabhicāri-tve siddhe tena sādhya-
140.07	gatasya tulya-dharma-tā evam ca ayam kṛtakaḥ iti sādhye vā sambhavaḥ upanaya-
140.08	arthaḥ/ nanu ca kṛtaka-tvāt iti anena sambhavaḥ uktaḥ sādhya-sādhana-bhāva-
140.09	nirdeśāt sādhya-sādhana-bhāva-mātra-nirdeśaḥ kṛtakaḥ kṛtaka-tvāt anityaḥ
140.10	śabdaḥ bhavati/ tad punar śabde kṛtakatvam asti na asti iti upanayena sambhavaḥ
140.11	gamyate asti ca śabde kṛtakatvam iti saḥ yadā dharmena udāharaṇam tadā
140.12	tathā iti yadā vaidharmyena tadā na tathā iti//
140.13	[1-1-39]hetu-apadeśāt pratijñāyāḥ punar-vacanam nigamanam
140.14	hetu-apadeśāt pratijñāyāḥ punar-vacanam nigamanam/ pratijñāyāḥ punar-
140.15	vacanam iti/ pratijñā-viṣayasya arthasya aśeṣa-pramāṇa-upapatti^i sādhya-viparīta-
140.16	prasaṅga-pratiṣedha-artham yad punar-abhidhānam nigamanam iti/ etasmin sūtra-arthe
140.17	parasya avakāśaḥ na asti iti abhyupetya tad-doṣa-nirācikīrṣayā vākyam
140.18	upakṣipyate/ upanaya-nigamane na avayava-antare artha-aviśeṣa-ādi-tad-vākyam
140.19	yathā-śruti na kena cit arthena sambadhyate atha hetu-upanayau ekena pakṣa-
140.20	kṛtya artha-aviśeṣāt iti hetuḥ saḥ vipakṣa-vṛtti-tvāt viparyaya-hetuḥ artha-aviśeṣasya
140.21	ekatve adarśanāt iti nānātve ca darśanāt iti/ yathā bahūnām ghaṭānām
141.01	ekaḥ artha-uddhāraṇa-ādiḥ na ca ekatvam iti/ atha eka-prayojanaka-tvena pakṣa^kṛtya
141.02	brūyāt ekatvam eka-prayojana-tvam iti/ tathā api hetuḥ na asti pratijñā-arthena
141.03	ākṣipta-tvāt hetu-upanayayoḥ eka-artha-tvam iti pratijñā-arthaḥ/ artha-aviśeṣāt iti
141.04	hetu-arthaḥ api saḥ eva anyaḥ ca hetu-arthaḥ anyaḥ ca upanaya-arthaḥ iti asiddhaḥ api ayam
141.05	hetuḥ tad-varṇitam pakṣa-dharma-tva-sambandha-sādhya-ukti^aḥ anya-varjanam iti etad anena eva pratyuktam/
141.06	yad api yathā tathā iti upasaṃhāre kṛte tathā iti anena śabdena sarva-sāmānyam vā
141.07	kṛtakatva-sāmānyam vā abhidhīyate kṛtakatva-viśeṣaḥ vā sarva-sāmānyam tāvat na
141.08	yuktam tathā iti-vyapadeśa-aśakya-tvāt iti/ kṛtakatva-viśeṣaḥ api na yuktaḥ anyathā
141.09	śabdasya kṛtakatva-apariśeṣāt kṛtakatva-sāmānyam tad ca hetunā eva uktam iti/
141.10	tad api ayuktam/ upanayasya upamāna-artha-tvāt upamāna-arthaḥ upanayaḥ iti tad ca
141.11	upamānam na sarvathā sādhya-sādhana-bhāvau āśritya pravartate iti yad kim cit
141.12	etad/ kṛtakatva-sāmānyam tu śabda-sannidhi^i abhidhīyate iti citram
141.13	idam kṛtakatvam khalu aśabda-sannidhi^i abhidhīyate iti śabdena ca viśeṣyamāṇam
141.14	katham sāmānyam bhaviṣyati tasmāt sāmānya-viśeṣavān hetuḥ iti
141.15	gata-artha-tvāt na upanayaḥ iti anena pratyuktam/ yad api uktam yathā tathā iti upamānam etad
141.16	upamānam ca upanayaḥ na dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti etena nigamanam pratyuktam/ te
141.17	ete vīta-avīta-vākye svatantre pañca-avayavena punar ekam anvaya-vyatirekin ca iti//
141.18	[1-1-40]avijñāta-tattve arthe kāraṇa-upapattitaḥ tattva-jñāna-artham ūhaḥ
141.19	tarkaḥ
141.20	avijñāta-tattve arthe kāraṇa-upapattitaḥ tattva-jñāna-artham ūhaḥ tarkaḥ iti
141.21	sūtram/ asya arthaḥ pūrvavat avijñāta-tattve arthe iti/ yathā saḥ arthaḥ bhavati
141.22	saḥ asya tathā bhāvaḥ tattvam etasmin avijñāte sāmānyataḥ avagamyate kutaḥ
142.01	punar etad sāmānyataḥ avagatam iti/ avijñāta-tattve pratyabhijñānāt
142.02	yasmāt ayam āha avijñāta-tattve iti tena jñāpayati sāmānyam avagatam iti/
142.03	yadi punar sāmānyam api na adhigatam syāt na brūyāt avijñāta-tattve iti
142.04	avijñāte iti evam brūyāt iti avijñāta-tattve iti samāsaḥ ayam na ca arthaḥ
142.05	gamyate kim avijñātam tattvam yena uta avijñātam tattvam yasya kaḥ ca atra viśeṣaḥ/
142.06	yadi avijñātam tattvam yena saḥ ayam avijñāta-tattvaḥ paricchettṛ^ḥ tasmin avijñāta-
142.07	tattve paricchettṛ^i tad-avagata-artham ūhaḥ tarkaḥ iti prāpnoti/ atha avijñātam
142.08	tattvam yasya tad avijñāta-tattvam yasmin avijñāta-tattve iti ṣaṣṭhī-vigrahena
142.09	yuktam etad/ viśeṣa-hetu-abhāvāt na artha-adhigamaḥ na atra viśeṣa-hetuḥ iti kim ayam
142.10	tṛtīyā-vigrahena samāsaḥ uta ṣaṣṭhī-vigrahena iti yuktam artha-grahaṇa-sāmarthyāt
142.11	yasmāt avijñāta-tattve iti arthaḥ ca āryamāṇa-tvāt karman tena jñāpayati
142.12	ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ ayam/ tṛtīyā-vigrahaḥ iti artha-grahaṇam antareṇa api sāmānyataḥ
142.13	avagati^aḥ avijñātam tattvam yasya iti api anukte api arthe gamyate eva etad arthaḥ iti
142.14	evam api samāse sandeha-tādavasthyam/ mā bhūt samāsaḥ vākyam eva astu
142.15	avijñātam tattvam asya iti bhavati eva abhīṣṭa-artha-prāptiḥ lāghava-artham tu samāsa-
142.16	kāraṇam anukte api arthe sāmarthyāt gamyate avijñātam tattvam asya iti atra uktam
142.17	sandehaḥ evam syāt iti samasta-anabhidhāna-prasaṅgāt ca/ yadi ca sāmarthyam
142.18	āśritya artha-grahaṇam pratiṣidhyate tadā evam yuktam ūhaḥ tarkaḥ iti sāmarthyataḥ
142.19	śeṣam gamyate iti tasmāt artha-grahaṇam jñātṛ-nirākaraṇa-artham iti/ mā bhūt
142.20	samasta-abhidhānam kim no bādhyate na buddhi-dharma-nirākaraṇa-artha-tvāt ubhayathā
142.21	khalu ayam ūhaḥ bhavati vijñāta-tattve avijñāta-tattve ca tatra tāvat vijñāta-tattve
142.22	ūhaḥ śuśrūṣā-śravaṇa-dhāraṇa-vijñāna-ūha-apoha-tattva-abhiniveśāḥ buddhi-dharmāḥ
143.01	iti tattvam avadhṛtam vijānāti vijñātam ūhaḥ iti kim uktam bhavati/
143.02	yaḥ asau arthaḥ pūrvam anena vijñātaḥ tam eva ūhaḥ iti pūrva-anubhūta-paricchedaḥ
143.03	ūhaḥ iti ucyate na tu ayam tarkaḥ yaḥ punar avijñāta-tattve arthe ūhaḥ saḥ tarkaḥ/
143.04	ṣaṣṭhī-abhidhānam adhikaraṇa-artha-asambhavāt avijñāta-tattvasya arthasya iti/ vaktavyam
143.05	avijñāta-tattvaḥ arthaḥ jñāyamānaḥ karman bhavati karmaṇi ca ṣaṣṭhī eva jyāyasī
143.06	yathā ghaṭasya vijñānam iti/ na/ vibhakti-vyatyayāt/ ṣaṣṭhī-sthale eva eṣā
143.07	saptamī draṣṭavyā yathā anyatra iṣu^i ayugapad saṃyoga-viśeṣāḥ karma-anya-tve
143.08	hetu^āḥ iti vyatyayaḥ kasmāt yadi vyatyayena kaḥ cit arthaḥ labhyate yuktaḥ
143.09	vyatyayaḥ yathā yasmin eva ayugapad saṃyoga-viśeṣāḥ iti adhikaraṇa-arthaḥ
143.10	iṣu^aḥ api ye avayavāḥ teṣām api saṃyoga-viśeṣāḥ te api karma-anya-tve hetu^āḥ
143.11	iti/ na punar iha vyatyayena phalam paśyāmaḥ tasmāt ayuktaḥ vibhakti-
143.12	vyatyayaḥ iti/ na na yuktaḥ/ sāmānya-adhigatasya viśeṣa-jñāpana-artham
143.13	yasya eva sāmānyam adhigatam tad-vartinaḥ viśeṣāḥ api avagantavyāḥ iti te ca
143.14	viśeṣāḥ saṃyoginaḥ samavāyinaḥ ca bhavanti iti kāraṇa-upapattitaḥ pramāṇa-
143.15	upapattitaḥ ca/ upapattiḥ sambhavaḥ sambhavati asmin arthe pramāṇam iti
143.16	bhavet ayam arthaḥ iti/ udāharaṇam yaḥ ayam jñātṛ^ḥ jñātavyam artham jānāti tam
143.17	tattvataḥ jānīya iyam iti kim utpatti-dharmakaḥ atha anutpatti-dharmaḥ iti
143.18	yāvat/ ekaḥ ubhayam jānāti iti bhāṣyam/ anutpatti-dharmakena anena
143.19	bhavitavyam iti yaḥ ūhaḥ saḥ tarkaḥ iti/ nanu etad tattva-jñānam eva kim
143.20	ucyate tattva-jñāna-arthām iti na idam tattva-jñānam anavadhāraṇāt/ na hi ayam avadhārayati
143.21	evam eva iti kim tu anutpatti-dharmakena jñātṛā bhavitavyam iti
144.01	pramāṇa-viṣayam anujānāti katham punar ayam tattva-jñāna-artham bhavati pramāṇa-
144.02	viṣaya-avivecanāt pramāṇa-viṣayam anena vivinakti ayam arthaḥ yuktaḥ iti
144.03	pramāṇāni pravartamānāni tarka-viviktam artham tathā-bhūtam avagacchanti iti
144.04	ūhaḥ saṃśaya-nirṇayābhyām na bhidyate iti ke cit/ ayam kila ūhaḥ
144.05	saṃśayaḥ vā yadi saṃgrahaḥ ten saṃgṛhīta-tvāt pṛthak na paṭhitavyaḥ atha
144.06	nirṇayaḥ tena saṃgṛhītaḥ iti/ apare tu anumānam tarkaḥ iti āhuḥ/ hetuḥ
144.07	tarkaḥ nyāyaḥ anvīkṣā iti anumānam ākhyāyate iti/ apare tu anumānam eva
144.08	tarkam yukti-apekṣam varṇayanti yad tāvat nirṇaya-saṃśayābhyām na ūhaḥ bhidyate iti/
144.09	tad na pratyaya-svarūpa-anabodhāt/ avijñāta-tattve arthe bhavati iti sāmānyena
144.10	ākṣiptaḥ bhavān satyam avijñāta-tattve bhavati iti na punar pratyaya-svarūpam
144.11	vyajñāyi bhavatā anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ saṃśayaḥ kim-svit iti
144.12	avadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ evam idam iti nirṇayaḥ/ ayam tu saṃśayāt
144.13	pracyutaḥ kāraṇa-upapattiḥ asti nirṇayam ca aprāptaḥ viśeṣa-adarśanāt viśeṣa-
144.14	darśanāt nirṇayaḥ bhavati na ca asmin viśeṣa-darśanam asti/ etena anumānam
144.15	tarkaḥ iti etad api pratyuktam/ viśeṣa-adarśanāt iti kim punar asya svarūpam
144.16	bhavet iti eva pratyayaḥ iti etasya svarūpam iti yaiḥ api anumānam yukti-apekṣam tarkaḥ
144.17	iti ucyate taiḥ api anumānāt tarka-bhedaḥ svayam vaktavyaḥ yuktiḥ pramāṇa-upapattiḥ
144.18	pramāṇa-upapattim apekṣamāṇaḥ yaḥ pratyayaḥ saḥ tarkaḥ iti kevalam
144.19	saṃjñā-bheda-mātram/ atha na pramāṇa-upapattiḥ yuktiḥ anumānam yukti-apekṣam
144.20	iti apekṣā-sāmarthyam vaktavyam/ sva-viṣaya-adhigame ca anumānam kim apekṣate/
144.21	atha itara-pramāṇa-apekṣā yuktiḥ iti ucyate evam api artha-graha-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ katham
144.22	pratyakṣa-āgamābhyām anumānam anugṛhyate iti/ atha anumānasya pratyakṣa-āgama-
145.01	eka-viṣaya-tvam tatra api tarkaḥ trīṇi etāni pramāṇa-viṣaya-tayā pratisaṃhitāni
145.02	iti bhavet/ avadhāraṇa-pratyayau bhinna-nimittau viśeṣavat-tvāt pratyakṣa-
145.03	anumāna-pratyaya-vat anumānam tarkaḥ liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-apekṣa-tvāt
145.04	anumāna-vat na tarka-svarūpa-anabodhāt na eva liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-apekṣaḥ
145.05	tarkaḥ katham iti dvayasya anadhigati^aḥ yatra dvayam adhigamyate dharmin^ḥ sādhana-
145.06	dharmaḥ ca tatra anumānam pravartate yatra punar dharmi-mātra-adhigatiḥ na liṅga-adhigatiḥ
145.07	asti sat-karma-viśeṣe iti/ tasmāt liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-apekṣa-tvam
145.08	anupapannam anumānam ca dharmi-gata-dharma-upalabdhi^i pravartate na punar tarkaḥ tarkaḥ
145.09	tu anya-gata-dharma-pradarśanena api pravartate yathā bhavitavyam anena puruṣena iti yathā
145.10	asmin deśe asvāḥ vāhyante iti aśva-vāhanam puruṣa-dharmaḥ saḥ ayam anya-gata-dharma-
145.11	upapatti^ā sthāṇu-vyavaccheda-mātram ūhaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam tarkaḥ pramāṇāni saṃdhattaḥ iti
145.12	pramāṇa-sahitaḥ vāde apadiṣṭaḥ na punar ayam pramāṇa-paricchedaka-tvāt iti//
145.13	[1-1-41]vimṛṣya vimṛśya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām artha-avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ
145.14	etasmin tarka-viṣaye vimṛṣya vimṛśya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām artha-avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ
145.15	iti sūtram/ sambandhaḥ arthaḥ ca pūrvavat iti/ vimṛṣya vimṛśya iti/ vimarṣam vimarśam kṛtvā
145.16	pakṣa-anya-pratipakṣāt pakṣāt ca pratipakṣāt ca pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām pakṣa-śabdena pakṣa-viṣayam sādhanam
145.17	ucyate pratipakṣa-śabdena pratipakṣa-viṣayasya sādhanasya upālambhaḥ tābhyām
145.18	sādhana-upālambhābhyām yad artha-avadhāraṇam saḥ nirṇayaḥ iti/ kva punar ayam
145.19	niyamaḥ kim tāvat nirṇaye āhosvit pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām atha vimṛṣya vimṛśya
145.20	eva iti yadā vimṛṣya vimṛśya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām eva nirṇayaḥ iti nirṇaye
145.21	niyamaḥ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam jñānam iti asya vyāghātaḥ/ etena
146.01	vimṛṣya vimṛśya eva iti pratyuktam/ atha pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām nirṇayaḥ eva ubhaya-
146.02	āśrayaḥ nirṇayaḥ prāpnoti tarka-viṣaye niyamaḥ na anyatra yaḥ ayam tarkasya
146.03	viṣayaḥ etasmin tarka-viṣaye vimṛṣya vimṛśya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām eva nirṇayaḥ
146.04	bhavati pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām eva nirṇayaḥ vimṛṣya vimṛśya eva iti sāmānya-nirṇaya-
146.05	lakṣaṇam etad kutaḥ ayam niyamaḥ tarka-viṣayaḥ iti tarkasya anantara-uddeśāt
146.06	tarka-viṣayaḥ iti niyamaḥ anyathā api nirṇaya-bhāvāt na nirṇayaḥ iti/
146.07	atha nirṇayasya kim lakṣaṇam artha-paricchedaḥ avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ iti
146.08	saḥ ayam nirṇayaḥ pramāṇa-viṣaye bhavan ekaśaḥ pramāṇaiḥ kriyate saṃhatya ca
146.09	tarka-viṣaye saṃhatya anyatra ekaḥ iti vāde śāstre ca vimarṣa vimarśa-varjam vāde pakṣa-
146.10	pratipakṣābhyām eva na śāstre vāde vā vimarṣaḥ vimarśaḥ asti ubhayoḥ niścita-tvāt
146.11	śāstra-abhyupagata-padārthasya niścita-tvāt ca/ pakṣa-pratipakṣa-śabdena sādhana-upālambhayoḥ
146.12	grahaṇam iti ayuktam pramāṇa-abhāvāt pakṣa-pratipakṣa-śabdena sādhana-upālambhau
146.13	ucyete iti/ kim pramāṇam kasmāt punar yathā-śruti na bhavati
146.14	pakṣa-pratipakṣayoḥ karma-tvāt na ca karma-artha-paricchedakam paricchedya-tvāt
146.15	tasmāt pakṣa-pratipakṣa-śabdena paricchedakayoḥ sādhana-upālambhayoḥ grahaṇam
146.16	tayoḥ akarmaka-tvāt tayoḥ ekasmāt na grahaṇam evam vaktavyam vimṛṣya vimṛśya sādhana-
146.17	upālambhābhyām artha-avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ iti/ evam sati sva-viśiṣṭaḥ arthaḥ
146.18	bhavati/ na ca lāghava-kṛtaḥ kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ iti na eka-viṣaya-jñāpana-artha-tvāt
146.19	yad-viṣayau sādhana-upālambhau tad-viṣayaḥ nirṇayaḥ/ anyathā hi sādhana-
146.20	upālambhābhyām nirṇayaḥ iti ukte na jñāyate kva punar nirṇayaḥ iti/ pakṣa-pratipakṣa-
146.21	śabdābhyām ca sādhana-upālambhayoḥ grahaṇam tad-viṣayasya ca iti/ artha-grahaṇam
146.22	tarhi na kartavyam gamyamāna-tvāt kena gamyamāna-tvāt vimṛṣya vimṛśya pakṣa-
147.01	pratipakṣābhyām eva avadhāraṇam iti ukte gamyate arthasya iti tasmāt artha-grahaṇam na
147.02	kartavyam/ śruti-sāmarthyāt anyatara-nirṇaya-jñāpana-artham gamyamānam artha-grahaṇam
147.03	yad karoti tena jñāpayati anyatarasmin pakṣe nirṇayaḥ iti/ pakṣa-
147.04	pratipakṣābhyām artha-avadhāraṇam iti ayuktam kasmāt ekasmāt adhigataḥ na idam
147.05	pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām saha tābhyām avadhāraṇam kim tarhi ekasmāt yaḥ avitiṣṭhate iti
147.06	ubhābhyām eva iti āha/ katham iti/ ubhay-nivṛtti^i anivṛttaḥ hi vimarṣaḥ vimarśaḥ
147.07	ubhaya-avasthāne ca anivṛttaḥ iti yadā tu ekaḥ avatiṣṭhate ekaḥ nivartate
147.08	iti tadā nirṇayaḥ iti ataḥ saṃhatya pakṣa-pratipakṣau nirṇayahetū
147.09	iti/ etasmin viṣaye kadā nirṇayaḥ bhavati kim prathame uta
147.10	dvitīye uta tṛtīye iti/ sarvatra bhavati arthasya tathā-bhāvāt atha vā
147.11	tṛtīye iti yuktam/ katham iti/ ekaḥ tāvat sādhanena pakṣam avasthāpayati
147.12	dvitīyaḥ sādhanam dṛṣṭvā nivartate sādhana-ābhāsam vā buddhvā sādhana-
147.13	doṣa-udbibhāvayiṣayā pravartate tasmin nivartya sādhanam brūte prathame idānīm
147.14	niścitam sādhanam iti/ yadi manyate na pravartate atha dūṣaṇa-ābhāsam
147.15	iti buddhyate tad-udbhāvanā-artham pravartate tad-nivṛtti^i dvitīya-pakṣa-sādhanam
147.16	api nivartate/ kim kāraṇam prathama-pakṣa-sādhanasya nirdoṣa-tvāt
147.17	nirdoṣe ca prathama-pakṣa-sādhane na dvitīyasya avakāśaḥ iti nivartate na
147.18	dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ iti yatra avatiṣṭhate tasmin nirṇayaḥ saḥ nirṇayaḥ sambhava-
147.19	asambhavābhyām pakṣa-pratipakṣayoḥ saṃhatya kriyate/ nirṇayaḥ anumānam eva iti
147.20	ke cit/ ke cit varṇayanti nirṇayaḥ anumānam eva na padārtha-antaram iti/ na/
147.21	liṅga-liṅgi-sambandhi-apekṣa-tvāt liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-apekṣa-tvāt anumānasya nirṇaye ca tad-abhāvaḥ pramāṇa-
148.01	phala-tvāt pramāṇam anumānam pramāṇa-phalam tu nirṇayaḥ nirṇayaḥ sva-viṣayaḥ
148.02	eva anumānam tu sva-viṣaye anyatra ca/ yathā dhūma-darśanam dhūma-paricchedena anumānam
148.03	api nirṇayaḥ iti/ yadā ca paricchinatti dhūmena tadā anumīyate
148.04	anena iti anumānam agni-paricchedaḥ phalam/ yadā ca bhāva-sādhanam āśritya
148.05	ucyate anumānam nirṇayaḥ iti tadā abhyanujñayā vartitavyam/ yadā ca
148.06	karaṇa-sādhana-tām āśritya ucyate anumānam nirṇayaḥ iti tadā pratiṣedhaḥ
148.07	vacanīyaḥ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ bhedāt kāraṇam anumānam kāryam nirṇayaḥ
148.08	tasmāt na anumānam nirṇayaḥ iti//
148.09	iti - audyotakare nyāya-vārttike prathamasya adhyāyasya
148.10	ādyam āhnikam//148.11	atha dvitīyam āhnikam
148.12	tisraḥ kathāḥ bhavanti vādaḥ jalpaḥ vitaṇḍā iti/ na ayam kathā-
148.13	niyamaḥ kim tarhi vicāra-vastu-niyamaḥ yad vastu vicāryate tad tredhā
148.14	vicāryate/ tatra vicāraḥ vādaḥ jalpaḥ vitaṇḍā iti/ tatra guru-ādibhiḥ
148.15	saha vādaḥ/ vijigīṣuṇā saha jalpa-vitaṇḍā^i/ tatra yathā-uddeśam lakṣaṇa-
148.16	apadeśaḥ vādasya lakṣaṇam ucyate tatra pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ
148.17	iti sūtram
149.01	[1-2-1(42)]pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ pañca-
149.02	avayava-upapannaḥ pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigrahaḥ vādaḥ
149.03	tatra vādaḥ pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigrahaḥ kau punar etau pakṣa-pratipakṣau
149.04	vastu-dharmau eka-adhikaraṇau viruddhau eka-kālau anavasitau vastu-dharmau iti
149.05	vastu-viśeṣau vastunaḥ sāmānyena adhigata-tvāt viśeṣataḥ anadhigata-tvāt ca/
149.06	viśeṣa-avagama-nimittaḥ vicāraḥ tasmāt vastu-dharmau aviśeṣau eka-adhikaraṇau
149.07	nānā-adhikaraṇau vicāram aprayojayataḥ ubhayoḥ pramāṇa-upapatti^aḥ tad yathā
149.08	anityā buddhiḥ nityaḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ aviruddhau api evam yau viruddhau tau
149.09	vicāram prayojayataḥ na aviruddhau iti tad yathā kriyāvat-dravyam guṇavat ca iti/
149.10	tatra kālau iti nirṇaya-uttara-kālam vicāra-abhāvāt tau etau viruddhau
149.11	evam viśeṣaṇau dharmau pakṣa-pratipakṣau tayoḥ parigrahaḥ ittham-bhāva-niyamaḥ/
149.12	evam-dharman^ḥ ayam dharmin^ḥ na evam-dharman^ḥ iti saḥ ayam pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigrahaḥ vādaḥ/
149.13	asya kathā mākutaye mārga-traye api samāna-tvāt viśeṣaṇa-artham pramāṇa-tarka-
149.14	sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti pramāṇaiḥ tarkena ca sādhanam upālambhaḥ ca asmin kriyate
149.15	asmin bhavati iti asmin iti saptamī-nirdeśaḥ viṣaya-jñāpana-arthaḥ/
149.16	asmin vicāra6i katham punar tarkena sādham upālambhaḥ ca apramāṇa-tvāt
149.17	pramāṇa-anugrāhaka-tvāt na pramāṇa-saṃgṛhītaḥ tarkaḥ na pramāṇa-antaram iti
149.18	uktam/ saḥ ayam apramāṇa-ātmakaḥ san katham siddhi-upālambhayoḥ karaṇam bhaviṣyati
149.19	na brūmaḥ tarkaḥ siddhi-upālabdhi^oḥ karaṇam api tu pramā-viṣaya-vivecanāt
149.20	tarkaḥ pramāṇāni anugṛhṇāti iti tarka-viviktam viṣayam pramāṇāni pravartamānāni
149.21	paricchindanti/ saḥ ayam pramāṇānām anugrāhaka-tvāt pramāṇa-
150.01	sahitaḥ vāde apadiṣṭaḥ iti/ sādhanam upālambhaḥ ca asmin bhavati iti kim
150.02	tāvat siddhiḥ sādhanam upālambhanam upālambhaḥ/ atha sādhyate anena iti sādhanam
150.03	upālabhate anena iti upālambhaḥ/ yadi tāvat bhāva-sādhanau sādhana-upālambha-
150.04	śabdau tadā pramāṇaiḥ tarkena ca na yuktau pramāṇānām tarkasya ca
150.05	para-apratipādaka-tvāt pramāṇāni ātma-pratipatti-sādhanāni para-pratipādana-
150.06	arthaḥ ca ayam vicāraḥ tasmāt na poramānaiḥ tarkena ca siddhi-upālabdhī bhavataḥ atha
150.07	karaṇa-sādhanau tathā api pañca-avayava-upapannaḥ iti pṛthak na paṭhitavyam/
150.08	kim kāraṇam pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti karaṇa-sādhanena padena
150.09	avayava-arthasya ukta-tvāt na anya-artha-tvāt pañca-avayava-upapannaḥ iti asya anyaḥ arthaḥ tathā
150.10	ca vakṣyāmaḥ/ atha etau sādhana-upālambha-śabdau bhāva-sādhanau kim-viṣayau
150.11	pramāṇaiḥ tarkena ca kriyete/ nanu uktam pakṣa-viṣayau iti pakṣa-viṣayam sādhanam
150.12	pratipakṣa-viṣayaḥ upālambhaḥ satyam uktam na punar yuktam uktam/ kā punar
150.13	yukti-hāniḥ atra upālambhasya pratipakṣa-aviṣaya-tvāt pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-
150.14	asiddhiḥ pakṣasya bhavatu na punar upālambhasya pratipakṣaḥ viṣayaḥ na brūmaḥ
150.15	pratipakṣa-viṣayaḥ upālambhaḥ iti kim tu sādhana-viṣayaḥ pratipakṣasya yad
150.16	sādhanam tad-viṣayaḥ upālambhaḥ iti/ katham punar pratipakṣa-sannidhi^i upadiśyamānaḥ
150.17	upālambha-śabdaḥ pratipakṣa-vyatirekena sādhanena sambaddhyate sāmarthyāt
150.18	asāmarthyāt ca yad upālambha-yogyam tad upālabhyate na ca pratipakṣaḥ upālambha-
150.19	yogyaḥ katham iti upālabhyamānasya ca anupālabhyamānasya ca vastunaḥ tathā-
150.20	bhavāt tathā-bhūtam eva vastu-upālabhyamānam tathā-bhūtam ..hasthānāni labhyante/
150a.01	.....tathā-bhūtam eva anupālabhyamānam sādhane api tarhi tulyam sādhanam api
150a.02	karaṇam tad api svarūpam na kadā cit jahāti/ tasmāt sādhanasya
150a.03	api upālambhaḥ na yuktaḥ iti/ evam anupālambhaḥ karaṇe na karmaṇi karmaṇaḥ
150a.04	sva-viṣaye sāmarthyāt sarvam karman sva-viṣaye samarthaḥ karaṇam api evam tasya tu
150a.05	viṣaya-antare na sāmarthyam asti/ yad ca viṣaya-antare asamartham na tena
150a.06	karmaṇaḥ karaṇasya vā vaiguṇyam/ kasya tarhi ayam upālambhaḥ yaḥ nigṛhyate/
150a.07	kaḥ ca nigṛhyate puruṣaḥ/ apratipatti-vipratipattī puruṣasya na karmaṇaḥ
150a.08	na karaṇasya saḥ ayam pratipādayitṛ^ḥ asamarthayoḥ karma-karaṇayoḥ upādānāt nigṛhyate/
150a.09	pratipattṛ^ḥ api yathā-abhihita-pada-artha-pratipatti^ā/ saḥ ayam puruṣa-
150a.10	dharmaḥ vacana-dvāreṇa udbhāvyate iti vacanam upacarya puruṣa-dharma-vacanam upālabhyate
150a.11	iti/ katham punar ayam arthaḥ sūtra-padena labhyate labhyate iti āha/ katham
150a.12	pramāṇa-tarka-sādhanaḥ pramāṇaiḥ tarkena ca sādhanasya upālambhaḥ pramāṇa-tarka-
150a.13	sādhanaḥ ca pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ ca pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ ekasya
150a.14	sādhana-śabdasya gamyamāna-artha-tvāt lopaḥ yathā uṣṭra-mukhin^ḥ iti/ atha pramāṇa-
150a.15	tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti anena padena jalpa-vitaṇḍābhyām katham vādaḥ
150a.16	viśiṣyate/ nanu idam pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigraha-vat trayāṇām api samānam
150a.17	bhavati/ na samānam vāde pramāṇam vāde pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhasya
150a.18	niyata-tvāt pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ eva vādaḥ/ chala-ādi-sādhana-upālambhe
150a.19	jalpa-vitaṇḍā eva/ yathā viṣāṇitvam gavi gavaye ca samānam api saṃvṛtta-
150a.20	kaṇṭha-tayā sāsna-ādimat-tayā ca viśiṣyamāṇam ubhau go-gavayau viśinaṣṭi
150b.01	tathā pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti/ chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-sādhana-
150b.02	upālambhaḥ jalpaḥ iti abhidhānāt vāde nigrahasthāna-pratiṣedhaḥ viśeṣa-vidhānāt
150b.03	tataḥ ca anupālambhaḥ vādah iti/ pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti abhidhānāt
150b.04	vāde api nigrahasthānāni iti adoṣaḥ/ upālambha-grahaṇāt samasta-
150b.05	nigrahasthāna-prasakti^i saṃjñā-bheda-mātram/ yadi upālambha-grahaṇāt vāde nigrahasthānāni
150b.06	vidyante iti manayase nanu saṃjñā-bheda-mātram jalpāt vādasya na
150b.07	viśeṣaḥ/ tatra api nigrahasthānāni iha api iti na uttarayoḥ niyama-artha-tvāt/
150b.08	yasmāt eva upālambha-śabdena samastāni nigrahasthānāni....
151.01	ataḥ eva aniyama-arthe uttare pade ārabhyate siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ pañca-
151.02	avayava-upapannaḥ ca iti/ siddhānta-viruddhaḥ iti anena kila padena viruddhaḥ
151.03	hetvābhāsaḥ labhyate tad na anyataḥ adhigati^aḥ anyaḥ eva ayam labhyate pañca-avayava-
151.04	upapannaḥ iti pañca-grahaṇāt nyūna-adhike labhyete avayava-grahaṇāt tad-
151.05	ābhāvaḥ ābhāsāḥ labhyante iti/ labhyamānasya arthasya punar-abhidhāne piṣṭam piṣṭam
151.06	syāt tasmāt na ayam arthaḥ/ atha kim idam padam siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ iti
151.07	anarthakam eva (tasmāt na ayam arthaḥ) na anarthakam apasiddhānta-avirodhāt siddhāntam
151.08	abhyupetya aniyamāt kathā-prasaṅga-upasiddhāntaḥ iti saḥ anena avaruddhyate
151.09	tasmāt na anarthakam kasmāt punar ayam niyamaḥ vāde avayava-abhāsā-apasiddhāntaḥ ca
151.10	nigrahasthānam iti/ guru-ādibhiḥ saha vāda-upadeśāt niyamaḥ yasmāt ayam
151.11	tattva-bubhutsuḥ guru-ādibhiḥ saha tri-vidham phalam ākāṅkṣan vādam karoti
151.12	tataḥ asya bubhutsāvataḥ tāvat sādhanam vaktavyam yāvat anena jñātavyam apratidvandvi-
151.13	tvāt pratidvandvinā saha na vādaḥ kim tu jalpaḥ tatra samasta-nigrahashtāna-
151.14	prayogaḥ iti yuktam/ pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ iti abhidhānāt
151.15	ubhayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-prasaṅgaḥ yadi pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ vādaḥ
151.16	ubhayoḥ vādi-prativādinoḥ pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambha-abhidhāne na adhikāraḥ
151.17	iti ubhaya-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ apartijñānāt na idam pratijñāyate
151.18	ubhayoḥ vādinoḥ pramāṇāni santi iti api tu ubhābhyām sādhanam
151.19	vaktavyam etad niyamyate na punar sādhanam eva etad bhavati iti dūṣaṇam api evam/
151.20	apare tu sva-para-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti vāda-lakṣaṇam varṇaynti/
151.21	atra ca pakṣa-viśeṣaṇa-artham upāttayoḥ sva-para-pakṣayoḥ anupapattiḥ vikalpa-
152.01	anupapatti^aḥ samāsa-asambhavāt ca/ etau khalu sva-para-śabdau parigraha-
152.02	varjana-arthau vā syātām sādhanīya-dūṣaṇīya-arthau vā pūrva-uttara-pakṣa-artha-vyavasthāpakau
152.03	va pakṣa-pratipakṣa-avadyotakau vā prathama-dvitīya-pakṣa-abhiprāyakau
152.04	vā ekasya vā aneka-viśeṣaṇa-abhisandhinaḥ aneka-śabda-abhidhānam iti/ tatra
152.05	parigraha-parivarjana-artha-tā tāvat anupapannā kimaḥ artha-asambhavāt/ na parigraha-
152.06	artha-tāyām kimaḥ arthaḥ sambhavati kasya ayam svaḥ bhaviṣyati kaḥ ca sva-arthaḥ
152.07	iti sva^karaṇam parigraha-arthaḥ yasya sva-kāraḥ bhavati tasya svaḥ iti/
152.08	tad eva cintyate kim tad sva^karaṇam iti/ mama iti pratyaya-utpatti-
152.09	kāraṇa-bhāva-abhāvau sva-para-śabda-arthaḥ nirdiṣṭaḥ sva-arthaḥ narasya svam na hi
152.10	śāstra-kārasya svaḥ paraḥ vā sambhavati yad artham idam syāt iti/ yad ca
152.11	svasya svam bhavati tad tasya asiddhi-artham kim cit upakaroti na punar svāminaḥ
152.12	pakṣena kaḥ cit upakāraḥ kriyate tasmāt parigraha-varjana-arthau tāvat
152.13	sva-para-śabdau na bhavataḥ/ sādhanīya-dūṣaṇīya-arthau sva-para-śabdau iti
152.14	cet atha api idam syāt sādhanīyaḥ sva-pakṣaḥ dūṣaṇīyaḥ para-pakṣaḥ iti
152.15	aho śāstra-artha-kauśalam sādhanīyaḥ sādhyaḥ syāt sādhanīyaḥ sva-pakṣaḥ
152.16	iti kutaḥ etad lakṣaṇam ca etad pakṣasya sādhaitum iṣṭaḥ pakṣaḥ iti sādhanīya-
152.17	śabdena api tad eva ucyate vyavaccheda-artham ca idam ucyate sva-pakṣaḥ para-pakṣaḥ
152.18	iti para-pakṣāt ca ayam sva-śabdena vyavacchedyamānena samānam artham śaknoti
152.19	vaktum vyartham vā viśeṣaṇam sāmānya-śabdāḥ api viśeṣe vartante
152.20	iti adṛṣṭam etad na te sāmānya-śabdāḥ ye viśeṣa^su vartante iti kim tu
152.21	pramāṇa-ādi-bheda-anuvidhāyinaḥ viśeṣa-śabdāḥ eva api ca sādhanīyaḥ
152.22	pakṣaḥ sva-pakṣaḥ dūṣaṇīyaḥ pakṣaḥ para-pakṣaḥ/ etad api na buddhyāmahe sādhanīya-
153.01	śabdasya pakṣa-viṣaya-tvāt sādhanīyaḥ pakṣaḥ bhavatu dūṣaṇam tu na kadā
153.02	cit para-pakṣena sambadhyate para-pakṣasya tādavasthyāt/ atha dūṣaṇasya kaḥ
153.03	viṣayaḥ kasya cit sthāpanā-viṣayaḥ iti kim cit dūṣaṇam sva-para-pakṣābhyām
153.04	asambaddham eva/ katamat dūṣaṇam sthāpanayā sambaddhyate kā ca iyam sthāpanā ca iti
153.05	sthāpanā pañca-avayavam pakṣa-sādhakam vācyam vākya-doṣaḥ dūṣaṇam tad-vākyam
153.06	nyūnam adhikam ca bhavati avayava-ābhāsa-upapannam ca tad-udbhāvanam dūṣaṇam etad
153.07	sthāpanayā sambadhyate tad-viṣaya-tvāt/ śeṣam tu dūṣaṇam na sthāpanayā na
153.08	sva-para-pakṣābhyām sambaddham uttara-doṣaḥ ākṣepa-bhāvaḥ ca iti/ satyam na sākṣāt
153.09	pakṣasya dūṣaṇena abhisambandhaḥ sthāpnā-nivṛtti-dvāreṇa tu pravartamānam
153.10	dūṣaṇam na pakṣam nivartayati sthāpanā-nivṛtti^aḥ para-pakṣam dūṣayati iti ucyate
153.11	upacarita-śabda-prayogaḥ lakṣaṇe anupapannaḥ lakṣaṇasya tattva-viṣaya-tvāt
153.12	tattva-vyavacchedakam lakṣaṇam na punar upacarita-śabda-prayogena vyavasthā asti/
153.13	yadi ca pāramparyena pravṛtti^ā pravartamānam dūṣaṇam para-pakṣam nivartayati
153.14	tadā evam vaktum kartum para-pakṣāt vyartham vacanam vādaḥ iti/ evam abhidhīyamāne
153.15	kim bhaviṣyati iti laghu ca sūtram bhavati iti ubhayoḥ ca sādhana-dūṣaṇayoḥ
153.16	pāramparyena para-pakṣa-asiddhi-artha-tā iti/ atha mā bhūt ubhayatra pāramparyam iti
153.17	svarūpataḥ evam vaktavyam/ pakṣa-sthāpanā-siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti/
153.18	evam hi spaṣṭa-tā lakṣaṇasya yathā-viṣayam ca abhisambandhaḥ iti sthāpanā api
153.19	na pūrvam dūṣaṇam sambaddhyate iti doṣa-tādavasthyam kasya cit sambhavāt/
153.20	kim ca dūṣaṇam sthāpanayā sambaddhyate iti sāmānya-abhidhāne api sāmarthyāt
153.21	niyamaḥ tasmāt idam anupapannam/ sādhanīya-dūṣaṇīya-arthau sva-para-
154.01	śabdau iti sva-para-śabdau pūrva-uttara-pakṣa-arthau bhaviṣyataḥ iti yad uktam bhavati
154.02	pūrva-uttara-pakṣau iti na yuktam etad api iti sādhana-dūṣaṇayoḥ niyama-abhyupagamāt
154.03	tena sādhana-dūṣaṇaiḥ iti etasmin sūtre sādhan-dūṣaṇayoḥ niyama-
154.04	abhidhānam sva-pakṣa-sādhanam para-pakṣe dūṣaṇam iti/ etasmin ca niyamataḥ uttara-
154.05	viṣayam prāpnoti tena uttara-pakṣa-vādin^ḥ svam eva pakṣam dūṣayati iti prāptam/
154.06	sva-pakṣaḥ ca uttara-vādinaḥ pūrva-pakṣāt anyaḥ iti sva-pare śabdābhyām pūrva-uttara-
154.07	pakṣayoḥ abhidhānam iti na padārthaḥ na paribhāvane rūḍhiḥ/ etena pakṣa-
154.08	pratipakṣa-artha-tā prathama-dvitīya-pakṣa-artha-tā ca vyākhyātā/ ekaḥ eva ayam pakṣaḥ
154.09	iti viśeṣaṇa-dvaya-yogāt sādhya-tayā dūṣya-tayā ca viśeṣyamāṇaḥ sva-para-
154.10	śabda-vācyaḥ iti yuktam etad dvi-vacanam tu na yuktam/ ekasya aneka-viśeṣaṇa-
154.11	yogaḥ na pratiṣidhyate dvi-vacanam tu na bhavati iti brūmaḥ na hi ekasya
154.12	aneka-viśeṣaṇa-abhisambandhe sati dva-vacana-bahu-vacane bhavataḥ na hi devadattam
154.13	chatra-daṇḍābhyām samupalabhya vaktṛ^āḥ bhavanti chatri-daṇḍinau āgatau iti
154.14	tad evam vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ sva-para-śabda-anupapattiḥ samāsa-asambhavāt ca/ sva-para-
154.15	pakṣaḥ iti samāsa-padam etad tatra tāvat viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-svarūpa-anavagati^aḥ na
154.16	ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ yatra punar ṣaṣṭhī samasyate tatra viśeṣaṇam viśeṣyāt
154.17	pṛthak avadhāryate yathā rāja-puruṣaḥ iti bhedāt ca yatra ca viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-
154.18	bhāvaḥ iti tatra bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ rāja-puruṣa-vat eva asati api bhede dṛṣṭaḥ iti
154.19	cet asati api bhede dṛṣṭaḥ ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ tad yathā senā-patiḥ pānaka-
154.20	aṅgam iti tad ca na anabhyupagamāt kaḥ hi sustha-ātman^ḥ pānaka-śabda-artham
154.21	aṅga^bhyaḥ na artha-antaram pratipadyate senām ca sainika^bhyaḥ iti/ yathā ca
155.01	senā-ādīni artha-antara-bhūtāni tathā uktam guṇa-guṇi-vādaḥ iti/ atha manyase
155.02	na arthaḥ śabdasya arthaḥ pakṣa-śabdasya iti tad na apodita-tvāt/ yathā sva-artha-vācakaḥ
155.03	śabdaḥ na bhavati tathā uktam, tasmāt na ṣaṣṭhī-samāsaḥ/ etena karmadhārayaḥ
155.04	pratyuktaḥ/ svaḥ ca asau pakṣaḥ ca iti viśeṣaṇa-svarūpa-asiddhi^aḥ bheda-abhāvāt ca/ etena
155.05	bahuvrīhiḥ api pratyuktaḥ/ sādhayitṛ-viṣayam ca sādhanam prāpnoti ataḥ
155.06	na bahuvrīhiḥ/ nanu ca sādhanīya-tā sva-arthaḥ pakṣa-viśeṣaṇa-tvena sambhavati
155.07	na sambhavati paryāya-tvāt sādhanīyaḥ pakṣaḥ iti paryāyaḥ eva na ca
155.08	paryāya-śabdaiḥ samānādhikaraṇaḥ bahuvrīhiḥ sambhavati na hi bhavati
155.09	vṛkṣaḥ tarum chinatti iti virodhāt ca/ yadi tāvat ayam pakṣa-śabdaḥ sva-pakṣāt
155.10	bhidyate para-pakṣa-vacanam pratijñā iti vyāghātaḥ/ kim kāraṇam, pratijñāyāḥ
155.11	sva-pakṣa-viṣaya-tvāt/ atha abhinnaḥ tathā api idam na prāpnoti sva-para-
155.12	pakṣayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti na ca anyaḥ samāsaḥ sambhavati
155.13	na ca idam vākyam, tasmāt sva-para-pakṣayoḥ iti asāram/ āstām tāvat
155.14	sva-para-śabda-arthaḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvaḥ ca sva-para-śabdayoḥ/ atha samāsaḥ
155.15	kasmāt na kriyate sva-para-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti kim evam
155.16	kṛte bhavati saḥ eva arthaḥ lāghavam ca/ atra ke cit parihāram bruvate/
155.17	yadi kila samāsaḥ kriyate ubhaya-āśrite siddhi-asiddhī bhavataḥ na
155.18	doṣa-tādavasthyāt na ayam doṣaḥ samāsa-karaṇena parihriyate katham iti
155.19	sva-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-apara-pakṣa-siddhi-artham sva-para-pakṣayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-artham iti
155.20	na asāmarthyāt na hi ayam samāsaḥ samarthaḥ sva-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-artham iti
155.21	kasmāt vyāhata-tvāt yadi siddhi-artham yatate asiddhi-artham iti vyāghātaḥ
155.22	yadi asiddhi-artham yatate siddhi-artham iti vyāghātaḥ yadi asāmarthyāt na samāsaḥ
156.01	samāsaḥ api tarhi asāmarthyāt na ubhaya-pakṣa-siddhi-asiddhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ eka-
156.02	vacana-bahu-vacana-prasaṅgaḥ iti anye/ yadi kila samāsaḥ kriyeta samāsaḥ
156.03	anabhivyakta-vacana-bhedaḥ tasmāt vacana-bheda-vyakta-nimitta-asamāsaḥ/ katham nāma
156.04	sva-para-pakṣayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ syāt na sva-para-pakṣāṇām na
156.05	sva-para-pakṣasya iti na asambhavāt ekasya siddhi-artham vacanam na eva asti sva-para-
156.06	pakṣāṇām sādhanam anumānam ekam iti tathā api eṣā na asti ataḥ asthāne āśaṅkā/
156.07	anityaḥ samāsa-vidhiḥ iti apare/ apare samāsa-vidhi^aḥ anitya-tām varṇayanti/
156.08	evam āhuḥ/ na ayam avaśyam vidhiḥ sarvatra samāsaḥ iti/ kva cit
156.09	samāsaḥ kva cit ca vākyam yathā ca rājñaḥ puruṣaḥ rāja-puruṣaḥ iti
156.10	samāsaḥ/ na hi ubhaya-prāptiḥ asamāsaḥ kasmāt anityaḥ samāsa-vidhiḥ iti
156.11	evam bruvatā uktam bhavati prāptam ubhayam atha ubhaya-prāpti^i kasmāt asamāsaḥ
156.12	iti samāsaḥ kasmāt anivṛttaḥ anuyogaḥ na anivṛttaḥ lāghava-artha-tvāt
156.13	samāse lāghavā lāghavam samāse go^ḥ evam anugati-artham ca śabdānām upādānam
156.14	iti guru-abhidhāne prāyoganam vācyam/ varṇe guru-lāghava-anabhyupagamaḥ
156.15	iti cet na ayam parihāraḥ anuyogasya anivṛtti^aḥ varṇe guru-lāghava-
156.16	anuyogam na pratipadyāmahe iti cet na nivartyate anuyogaḥ kasmāt iti
156.17	śāstra-tvena ca vāda-abhidhānam abhyupagamyate na ca varṇe guru-lāghavam āśrīyate
156.18	iti na yuktam siddhi-asiddhi-artham iti etad api na yuktam kasmāt dvedhā
156.19	anupapatti^aḥ ayam khalu asiddhi-śabdaḥ kalpyamānaḥ dvedhā kalpyeta vyakti^i
156.20	utpatti^i vā tad na tāvat utpatti^i yujyate pratijñā-ādikasya sādhanasya
156.21	sādhana-hāni-prasaṅgāt na hi pratijñā-ādi-sādhanena vastu utpādyate kim tu
157.01	vidyamāna-prakāśaka-tvam sādhana-arthaḥ saḥ hīyate siddhi^i utpatti^i kalpitāyām
157.02	iti/ vyakti^i iti cet na adhikaraṇa-ādhāra-tvāt/ atha manyase siddhi-
157.03	asiddhiḥ vyaktiḥ na utpattiḥ yuktam etad uttaram tu vyāhatam bhavati adhikaraṇa-
157.04	pratyāyanam siddhi-asiddhī iti ye ca ete adhikaraṇa-pratyāyana-lakṣaṇe siddhi-asiddhī
157.05	te adhikaraṇe vartamāne pakṣasya katham sapakṣasya bhavataḥ iti ṣaṣṭhī-arthaḥ
157.06	vācyaḥ saḥ ca ayam durvacaḥ tad-viṣaya-tvāt tayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi^oḥ pakṣaḥ
157.07	viṣayaḥ ataḥ tad-viṣaya-tvāt tasya iti/ na yuktam etad/ ye ete siddhi-asiddhī
157.08	pakṣa-viṣaye kim tāvat pakṣe siddhe uta asiddhe yadi tāvat siddhe na adhikaraṇa-
157.09	pratyāyanam siddhiḥ kim tarhi yataḥ tad-pratyāyanam saḥ siddhiḥ kutaḥ ca
157.10	tad bhavati arthasya tathā-tvāt yasmāt saḥ arthaḥ tathā bhavati katham tathā bhavati
157.11	yathā pramāṇaiḥ abhisambaddhaḥ iti yad pramāṇaiḥ avisaṃvāditvam saḥ tathā-
157.12	bhābhāvaḥ bhāvaḥ/ atha asiddhe pakṣe adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam bhavati sādhana-prayogāt
157.13	prāc-adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam asti iti vāda-vaiyarthyam syāt pakṣasya siddhiḥ adhikaraṇa-
157.14	pratyāyanam asiddhiḥ tu katham na hi para-pakṣa-siddhi-dūṣaṇena
157.15	kriyate iti uktam sthāpanā-nivṛtti^aḥ upacāraḥ iti cet syāt matiḥ dūṣaṇam
157.16	prayujyamānam sākṣāt sthāpanām nivartayati sthāpanā-nivṛtti^aḥ tu para-pakṣa-
157.17	nivṛttiḥ upacaryate iti na prayojana-abhāvāt/ na hi prayojanam antareṇa
157.18	mukhya-artha-vyatikramaḥ labhyate api tu pramāṇa-asambhavena upacāraḥ mañcāḥ
157.19	kroṣanti iti yathā loka-prayukta-śabda-viṣayaḥ ca upacāraḥ loka-gati^ā
157.20	śabda-arthaḥ na ca idam laukikam vākyam sva-para-pakṣayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam
157.21	vādaḥ/ apare tu pratijñāta-artha-ajñāpanam pakṣasya asiddhim varṇayanti tad vyadhikaraṇa-
158.01	viṣayam na nivartate prasaṅgaḥ katham pakṣasya asiddhiḥ iti siddhivat-
158.02	prasaṅgaḥ tasmāt siddhi-asiddhī pakṣasya dharmau eva yataḥ etad bhavati siddhaḥ
158.03	pakṣaḥ asiddhaḥ ca iti evam sati draṣṭṛ-sthe vyāghātaḥ/ yadi siddhi-asiddhī
158.04	pakṣa-dharmau draṣṭṛ-sthe siddhi-asiddhī iti vyāhatam bhavati/ atha mā bhūt
158.05	vyāghātaḥ iti draṣṭṛ-sthe eva te tathā api pakṣasya vyakti-avyaktī siddhi-asiddhī
158.06	iti vyāhatam/ atha tathā tathā te padārthasya siddhi-asiddhī tathā
158.07	api adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam siddhi-asiddhī iti vyāhatam bhavati/ ke punar ete
158.08	vyakti-avyaktī vastu-dharmau abhidhāna-pratyaya-hetū ye etau abhidhāna-pratyayau
158.09	vyaktaḥ vyaktaḥ ca iti bhavataḥ te vyakti-avyaktī iti/ artha-śabda-anupapattiḥ
158.10	vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ artha-śabdaḥ khalu pravartamānaḥ tredhā pravartate uddeśa-
158.11	prayojana-abhidheya-viṣaya-tvena uddeśe tāvat artha-śabdaḥ yathā brāhmaṇa-artham
158.12	bhakte iti/ evam catur-vidha-vākyam saṅgrahaḥ prayojane artha-śabdaḥ kim artham
158.13	āgataḥ asi iti abhidheye artha-śabdaḥ yathā go^ḥ iti abhidhānasya kaḥ arthaḥ iti/
158.14	tad na tāvat uddeśe sambhavati yukta-ayukta-tvena iti viśeṣita-tvāt sva-pakṣasya
158.15	yukta-tvena para-pakṣasya ayukta-tvena iti evam viśeṣite uddeśa-śabda-arthaḥ na hi ayam
158.16	yukta-ayukta-śabdaḥ dūṣaṇa-ābhāse vā sādhana-ābhāse vā sambhavati sādhana-
158.17	yogaḥ yuktatā dūṣaṇa-yogaḥ ayuktatā iti/ atha punar śabda-arthasya tathātva-
158.18	atathātve yukta-ayukta-tve yuktatvam tathā-bhāvaḥ ayuktatvam atathā-bhāvaḥ iti tathā api
158.19	tayoḥ karma-tayā upādānāt yuktatvena adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam ayuktatvena vā iti
158.20	vyāghātaḥ na hi pratyāyam pratyāyanam bhavati iti/ atha prayojane artha-śabdaḥ
158.21	tathā api sādhana-dūṣaṇa-ābhāsa-vādinaḥ avāditā syāt/ kim kāraṇam, sādhana-
158.22	dūṣaṇa-ābhāsayoḥ siddhi-asiddhi-aprayojana-tvāt na hi sādhana-dūṣaṇa-ābhāsayoḥ
159.01	siddhi-asiddhī prayojanam/ evam ca tad-vādin^ḥ avādin^ḥ syāt tathā ca avādin^ḥ
159.02	nigṛhyate iti doṣaḥ/ atha abhidheye artha-śabda-prayogaḥ abhidhāna-svabhāva-
159.03	anavadhṛti^aḥ iti ayuktam etad api na hi grantha-kāra-bhāṣya-kārābhyām viśeṣitam
159.04	evam prakāram abhidhānam siddhi-asiddhi^oḥ vācakam iti/ nanu viśeṣitam yena
159.05	siddhi-asiddhī abhidhīyete tad tayoḥ vācakatā iti satyam uktam, evam tu
159.06	siddhi-asiddhī iti ete abhidhāne vādaḥ prāptaḥ na ca anyaḥ artha-śabdasya arthaḥ iti/
159.07	tasmāt artha-śabdaḥ api ayuktaḥ iti/ siddhi-asiddhi-artham vacanam vādaḥ iti bruvāṇena
159.08	pūrvam uttaram vā na ālocitam naiyāyika-hetu-pratiṣedhena viśeṣyam vacanam
159.09	iti abhyupagama-siddham punar sūtram iti/ samasta-vacana-viśeṣaṇa-artham iti
159.10	vyāghātaḥ/ tad evam etāni sūtra-padāni vicāryamāṇāni svatantra-
159.11	vyāghātakāni iti lokam ca bādhante iti/ evam tāvat sautrāṇi
159.12	padāni svena nyāyena asamarthāni yathā ca vṛtti-padāni asambaddhāni
159.13	tathā idam ucyate svasya pakṣasya iti sva-artha-asiddhi^aḥ ayuktam, sva-arthaḥ ca pratiṣiddhaḥ
159.14	ataḥ artham svasya pakṣasya iti yad api ṣaṣṭhī-saptamī-vacanayoḥ tulya-rūpa-tvāt
159.15	ṣaṣṭhītva-jñāpana-artham svasya pakṣasya iti tad api ayuktam/ adhikaraṇa-artha-asambhavāt
159.16	na hi pakṣa-vyatirekena pakṣe kim cit sādhyam sambhavati tasmāt aprasaktaḥ
159.17	saptamī-arthaḥ tad-aprasakti^i viśeṣaṇa-anarthakyam iti yukta-ayukta-tve anupapattiḥ ca
159.18	vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ yukta-ayukta-tve sādhana-dūṣaṇe vā syātām sādhana-
159.19	dūṣaṇābhyām vā yogaḥ padārthasya vā tathā tathā ca iti/ tad na tāvat
159.20	sādhana-dūṣaṇe yukta-ayukta-tve kasmāt sādhana-dūṣaṇayoḥ pṛthak-abhidhānāt
159.21	te sādhana-dūṣaṇaiḥ iti pṛthak ukte sādhana-dūṣaṇe yukta-ayukta-tva-śabdābhyām ca
159.22	sādhana-dūṣaṇayoḥ abhidhānam iti te sādhana-dūṣaṇaiḥ iti sūtram uccāryam
160.01	carita-artha-tvāt/ atha manyase tayoḥ eva yukta-ayuktayoḥ viśeṣaṇa-artham
160.02	te sādhana-dūṣaṇaiḥ iti na prathama-abhidhāna-prasaṅgāt/ yadi yukta-ayukta-tva-
160.03	viśeṣaṇa-artham te sādhana-dūṣaṇaiḥ iti sūtram prathamā eva nyāyā te sādhana-
160.04	dūṣaṇāni iti na hi viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāve bhavati nīlam utpalaiḥ iti/
160.05	yogaḥ iti cet/ atha sādhana-dūṣaṇābhyām yogaḥ yuktatva-ayuktatve iti evam
160.06	cet manyase tattva-anirdeśāt sādhana-dūṣaṇābhyām yogaḥ nirdiṣṭaḥ evam-vidhaḥ
160.07	iti anirdeśāt ayuktam etad sādhana-yogaḥ iti ca kadā bhavati
160.08	yadā asau nirṇītaḥ padārthaḥ tadā ca na sādhana-arthaḥ kaḥ cit asti iti na
160.09	yukta-ayukta-tvena adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam iti vyāghātāt ataḥ na yogaḥ/ apadārthasya
160.10	tathātā-atathātā^i yukta-ayukta-tve tad api an karaṇa-arthasya asambhavāt
160.11	ye tathātva-atathātva-lakṣaṇe yukta-ayukta-tve te vyavacchedye na tu vyavacchedasya
160.12	sādhane tasmāt karaṇa-arthaḥ na asti ataḥ yukta-ayukta-tvasya iti yuktam
160.13	vaktum/ adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam iti tu na yuktam śāstra-sambandha-
160.14	asaṅgata-tvāt śāstram sambandham kurvāṇena uktam sandeha-viparyaya-pratiṣedha-arthaḥ
160.15	śāstrasya ārambhaḥ iti etad nirapekṣena idam ucyate adhikaraṇa-pratyāyanam siddhi-
160.16	asiddhī iti na ca prāśnika^su saṃśaya-viparyayau staḥ paricchedaka-tvāt
160.17	tasmāt na prāśnikāḥ pratyāyāḥ iti yad ca uktam prāśnika-pratyāyanāt eva prativādi-
160.18	pratyāyanam kṛtam bhaviṣyati iti/ kim kāraṇam, prativādin^ḥ kila
160.19	pakṣa-rāgāt santam api artham na pratipadyate santam api artham na pratipadyate iti
160.20	vyāhatam ucyate katham ca anyathā pratipannaḥ san iti abhidhīyate/ atha pratipadyamānaḥ
160.21	api parasya na kāma-kāram dadāti tathā api na pratipadyate iti
160.22	vyāhatam ucyate yad ca prāśnika-pratyāyanāt tad pratyāyanam iti tad api na
161.01	pratīti^aḥ karaṇa-bhedāt prāśnika-pratīti^aḥ anyāni karaṇāni anyāni
161.02	prativādi-pratīti^aḥ iti na ca anya-viṣayasya karaṇasya kriyā iti niyama-
161.03	abhāvāt ca/ na ayam niyamaḥ prāśnika-pratyāyana-arthaḥ eva vādaḥ prāśnikān antareṇa
161.04	api guru-ādibhiḥ saha vādaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti/ yadā tu ayam na tattva-bubhutsuḥ
161.05	lābha-pūja-ākhyāti-kāmaḥ vādam ārabhate tadā prāśnikaiḥ prayojanam asti iti
161.06	satyam asti prayojanam vādaḥ tu na bhavati iti na traividhya-anabhyupagamāt
161.07	ekaḥ eva ayam kathā-mārgaḥ tasya prayojanam tattva-avabodhaḥ lābha-ādyaḥ  ca
161.08	na pratiṣedhāt prasiddhaḥ stuti-māna-lābha-lipsu^aḥ vādaḥ yad api idam ucyate na
161.09	traividhya-anabhyupagamāt iti na anabhyupagama-āyattaḥ padārtha-sadbhāvah api tu
161.10	sadbhāve sati abhyupagamaḥ iti yathā ca traividhyam kathā-mārgasya tathā lakṣaṇa-
161.11	bhedāt vā jalpa-vitaṇḍā^yoḥ bhedam upapādayiṣyāmaḥ/ tasmāt ajñātvā viśeṣam
161.12	ucyate traividhya-anabhyupagamāt iti yad ca uktam pakṣa-siddhi-viṣayam pratyāyanam
161.13	pakṣa-siddhi-śabdena upacaritam yathā śūnyatā-viṣayaḥ samādhiḥ śūnyatā iti na
161.14	kāmataḥ upacāraḥ labhyate api tu pramāṇa-sambhavena loka-prayukta-śabda-
161.15	ākhyānam upacāraḥ uktam ca na upacarita-śabda-prayogaḥ lakṣaṇe upapannaḥ iti/
161.16	tad-artham vacanam iti etad api kila catur-vidha-vākya-jñāpana-artham uktam sādhanam
161.17	sādhana-ābhāsaḥ dūṣaṇam dūṣaṇa-ābhāsaḥ ca sambhantsyate sampatsyate iti/ tad ayuktam yukta-
161.18	ayukta-tvena iti viśeṣita-tvāt evam viśeṣite tasmin kutaḥ catur-vidha-vākya-
161.19	saṃgrahaḥ iti adhikaraṇam punar yaḥ vādi-prativādibhyām adhikṛtaḥ iti
161.20	adhikaraṇa-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ na hi vāde prāśnika-artham kim cit asti iti pratyāyana-
161.21	ādhāra-tvāt adhikaraṇam iti cet na ukta-uttara-tvāt pakṣa-viṣayam pratyāyanam
161.22	na prāśnika-viṣayam iti tasmin sati bhāvāt iti cet na anyathā api
162.01	darśanāt anyathā api guru-ādibhiḥ saha dṛṣṭaḥ vādaḥ iti tad-vāde parīkṣā
162.02	kā kasya kva iti vaktavyam/ sādhana-dūṣaṇa-prayogaḥ sthāpanā-viṣayaḥ vādaḥ
162.03	iti tasya parīkṣā sādhana-dūṣaṇa-tad-ābhāsa-parijñānam tad-artham adhikāraḥ
162.04	tasmin iti saptamī-arthaḥ na asti yad api prāśnika-prativādinoḥ
162.05	priya-apriyavacasi vāda-prasaṅgaḥ iti codyam kṛtvā pratisādhanam uktam tad
162.06	siddhi-asiddhibhyām sākṣāt pāramparyena asambandhāt yukta-ayukta-viśeṣaṇāt ca asambaddham
162.07	uttarena tu asaṅgata-padārtha-abhidhāyinaḥ dattaḥ sva-hastaḥ pūrva-aparayoḥ
162.08	asambandhāt iti/ yadi tu asaṅgatasya api uttaram vākyam tataḥ evam vaktavyam
162.09	na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt iti tad etad sūtram vicāryamāṇam saha-vārttikam na
162.10	yukti^ā saṅgacchate iti yathā-nyāyam eva astu//
162.11	[1-2-1(43)]yathā-ukta-upapannaḥ chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-sādhanena upālambhaḥ
162.12	jalpaḥ
162.13	yathā-ukta-upapannaḥ chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-sādhanena upālambhaḥ jalpaḥ/ samastam
162.14	vāda-lakṣaṇam chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-sādhanena upālambhaḥ jalpaḥ iti/ yathā-
162.15	ukta-upapannaḥ iti na yuktam jalpa-nigrahasthāna-niyamasya anabhyupagamāt
162.16	yadi evam ucyeta saḥ na tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ pañca-avayava-
162.17	upapannaḥ pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigrahaḥ chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-upālambhaḥ jalpaḥ
162.18	iti na yuktam evam bhavitum/ siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ pañca-avayava-upapannaḥ iti
162.19	ca padayoḥ upālambha-śravaṇāt samasta-nigrahasthāna-pasakti^i niyama-artha-tvāt
162.20	niyama-arthe ete pade siddhānta-aviruddhaḥ pañca-avayava-upapannaḥ iti jalpe
162.21	ca niyantavyam na kim cit asti tasmāt niyama-arthe ete pade na atideṣṭavye
163.01	iti ataḥ na vaktavyam yathā-ukta-upapannaḥ iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ sambhavataḥ atideśāt
163.02	yatra sambadhyate tad atideśyate kim ca sambadhyate pramāṇa-tarka-sādhana-upālambhaḥ
163.03	iti etad tasmāt yathā-ukta-upapannaḥ iti lakṣaṇa-mātrasya atideśaḥ yathā
163.04	anyatra aneka-dravyena samavāyāt rūpa-viśeṣāt ca rūpa-upalabdhiḥ iti anena sambandha-
163.05	sparśa^su jñānam vyākhyātam iti na svarūpa-viśeṣaḥ atidiśyate iti api
163.06	tu aneka-dravya-dravya-samavāya-mātram sva-gataḥ viśeṣaḥ sāmarthyāt tathā iha api yad
163.07	samartham tad atidiśyate samartham ca lakṣaṇam tasmāt tad atidisyate na
163.08	niyama-arthe pade niyantavyasya bādhāt/ atha vā yathā-ukta-upapannena upapannaḥ
163.09	yathā-ukta-upapanna-upapannaḥ iti prāpte gamyamāna-tvāt ekasya upapanna-śabdasya lopaḥ
163.10	yathā go^aḥ atha iti kena punar ayam gamyate iti uktam sāmarthyena iti/
163.11	na hi niyama-arthayoḥ padayoḥ jalpa-sambhavaḥ tasmāt na te apadiśyete iti
163.12	bhāvyam idānīm katham na sūtra-pāṭha-anukrama-jñāpana-artha-tvāt bhāvyasya etasmin
163.13	sūtre yad upapannam lakṣaṇa-tvena tad atidiśyate iti/ chala-jāti-nigrahasthānaiḥ
163.14	na ekasya kasya cit sādhanam upālambhaḥ vā yuktaḥ ayukta-uttara-tvāt/ chalam
163.15	tāvat na sādhana-upālambhāya alam kasmāt ayukta-uttara-tvāt ayuktam
163.16	uttaram chalam tad ana sādhana-upālambhāya iti evam jātiḥ/ aṅga-bhāvaḥ iti
163.17	cet atha api idam syāt yad tad pramāṇa-tarkaiḥ sādhana-upālambhaḥ tasya etāni
163.18	prayujyamānāni chala-jāti-nigrahasthānāni rakṣaṇa-artha-tvāt aṅgam bhavanti iti
163.19	tad api ayukta-uttara-tvāt anupapannam/ kim artham tarhi prayogaḥ yadi etāni chala-
163.20	jāti-nigrahasthānāni sādhana-upālambhayoḥ na sādhanam aṅgam vā kim artham
163.21	eteṣām upapādanam iti sādhana-vighāta-artham sādhanam vihaniṣyāmi iti
163.22	anayā dhiyā apakṛtaḥ pravartate yatra ca etāni chala-jāti-nigrahasthānāni
164.01	prayujyante na saḥ vādaḥ na punar etad abhidhīyate chala-jāti-nigrahasthānāni
164.02	sādhanam aṅgāni vā iti sādhu sādhana-upādāne parena ākulita-buddhiḥ chala-
164.03	vādin^ḥ niyuṅkte kadā cit chala-ādibhiḥ ākula-kṛtasya para-ājayaḥ api syāt
164.04	na punar etāni tattva-bubhutsunā vaktavyāni kim tu vijigīṣuṇā iti/
164.05	saḥ ayam pakṣa-pratipakṣa-parigrahah chala-jāti-nigrahasthāna-prayoga-aprayogābhyām
164.06	bhidyamānaḥ vādaḥ jalpaḥ vitaṇḍā ca bhavati/ atra api viruddhau
164.07	dharmau eka-adhikaraṇa-sthau iti samānam viruddhayoḥ dharmayoḥ eka-adhikaraṇa-
164.08	tva-anupapattiḥ ubhaya-svabhāva-asambhavāt yadi viruddhau eka-adhikaraṇau
164.09	dharmau bhavataḥ tad ekam vastu ubhaya-svabhāvam na ca etad asti tasmāt na viruddhau
164.10	dharmau eka-adhikaraṇau iti/ na adhyāropita-svarūpa-dharma-pratipatti^ā tad-upapatti^aḥ
164.11	ekah tatra dharmaḥ svarūpataḥ ekaḥ tatra adhyāropita-svarūpa-pravṛttiḥ tasmāt ubhaya-
164.12	dharman^ḥ dharmin^ḥ kim uktam eka-dharmi-sthau viruddha-arthau iti etad api na buddhyāmahe
164.13	kaḥ viruddha-arthaḥ iti na svarūpa-viparyaya-upapatti^aḥ na svarūpa-mātra-viparyayaḥ
164.14	virodhaḥ punar bādhā vadhya-ghātaka-bhāvaḥ vā iti//
164.15	[1-2-3(44)]sa pratipakṣa-sthāpanā-hīnaḥ vitaṇḍā
164.16	sa pratipakṣa-sthāpanā-hīnaḥ vitaṇḍā/ pratipakṣa-sthāpanā-hīnaḥ iti

164.18	sthāpayati/ apare tu bruvate dūṣaṇa-mātram vitaṇḍā iti/ dūṣaṇa-mātram iti

165.05	[1-2-4(45)]savyabhicāra-viruddha-prakaraṇasama-sādhyasama-atītakālāḥ

166.01	viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-asiddhi-bhedāt catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ/ evam samartha-asamartha-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-

167.18	ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ anitye vāc-manase amūrta-tvāt  9sādhya-tad-jātīya-ekadeśa-

167.20	pratyakṣa-tvāt  10sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vyāpakaḥ avidhyamāna-vipakṣaḥ
167.21	siddhānta-bheda-āśrayena anitye rūpa-vijñāne amūrta-tvāt vedanā-ādi-vat
167.22	 11sādhya-tad-jātīya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣaḥ anitye cakṣus-vijñāne
168.01	amūrta-tvāt rūpa-ādi-vat iti  12sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-
168.02	vipakṣaḥ anitye śabda-manasī śrāvaṇa-tvāt  13sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ
168.03	avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣa-vyāpakaḥ anitye vāc-manase utpatti-dharmaka-tvāt
168.04	 14sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ ca nitye cakṣus-
168.05	vijñāne mūrta-tvāt  15sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣa-avṛttiḥ
168.06	na idam nirātmakam śarīram anindriya-adhiṣṭhāna-tva-prasaṅgāt  16sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ
168.07	avidyamāna-sapakṣa-vipakṣaḥ sarvam anityam mūrta-tvāt iti ete ca sādhya-ekadeśa-
168.08	vṛttyaḥ  ṣoḍaśa yathā-uktāḥ sarve hetvābhāsāḥ//
168.09	atha sādhya-avṛttyaḥ /  1sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vipakṣa-vyāpakaḥ anityā
168.10	pṛthivī gandhavat-tvāt agandhavat-tvāt num2sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vyāpakaḥ vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ
168.11	anityaḥ śabdaḥ aśrāvaṇa-tvāt  3sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vyāpakaḥ vipakṣa-
168.12	avṛttiḥ arthaḥ śabdaḥ aśrotra-grāhya-sāmānyavat-tvāt  4sādhya-jātīya-avṛttiḥ
168.13	vipakṣa-vyāpakaḥ arthaḥ śabdaḥ asāmānyavat-tvāt  5sādhya-tad-jātīya-avṛttiḥ
168.14	vipakṣa-avṛttiḥ ca kāraṇavān śabdaḥ artha-tvāt anartha-tvāt  6sādhya-tad-jātīya-vipakṣa-
168.15	avṛttiḥ nityaḥ śabdaḥ asat-tvāt  7sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ vipakṣa-
168.16	vyāpakaḥ asparśaḥ śabdaḥ dravya-tvāt  8sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vipakṣa-ekadeśa-
168.17	vṛttiḥ kāraṇavān śabdaḥ mūrta-tvāt  9sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-ekadeśa-
168.18	vṛttiḥ vipakṣa-avṛttiḥ sva-āśrayavān śabdaḥ cākṣuṣa-tvāt aulūkya-pakṣe  10sādhya-
168.19	avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-vyāpakaḥ avidyamāna-vipakṣaḥ siddhānta-bheda-āśrayena anityaḥ
168.20	śabdaḥ aśrāvaṇa-tvāt  11sādhya-avṛttiḥ tad-jātīya-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ avidyamāna-vipakṣaḥ
168.21	anityaḥ śabdaḥ mūrta-tvāt  12sādhya-tad-jātīya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-
168.22	vipakṣaḥ anityaḥ śabdaḥ asat-tvāt  13sādhya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣa-
169.01	vyāpakaḥ nityaḥ śabdaḥ aśrāvaṇa-tvāt  14sādhya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ
169.02	vipakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttiḥ nityaḥ śabdaḥ   mūrta-tvāt  15sādhya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-sajātiḥ vipakṣa-avṛttiḥ nityaḥ śabdaḥ asat-tvāt  15sādhya-avṛttiḥ avidyamāna-
169.03	samāna-vipakṣaḥ anityam sarvam asat-tvāt te ete ṣoḍaśa sādhya-avṛttyaḥ
169.04	hetvābhāsāḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-asiddha-bheda-avṛttyaḥ  ṣaḍbhiḥ bhavanti iti uktam/
169.05	tad katham vyāpaka-avyāpaka-viśeṣaṇa-yogāt ye eva sādhya-vyāpakāḥ ṣoḍaśa
169.06	tayeva ye ca pakṣa-ekadeśa-vṛttyaḥ  te viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-asiddha-bhedena catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ
169.07	bhavanti iti/ tatra vyāpakānām tāvat udāharaṇāni/ anityaḥ
169.08	śabdaḥ anabhidheya-tve sati prameya-tvāt viśeṣaṇa-asiddhaḥ prameya-tve sati abhidheya-
169.09	tvāt iti viśeṣya-asiddhaḥ/ evam sarveṣu sādhya-vyāpaka^su viśeṣaṇa-
169.10	viśeṣya-bhedaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ nāni tāni eva ca udāharaṇāni sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttīnām
169.11	udāharaṇam/ pṛthivī-ap-tejas-vāyu-ākāśāni anityāni anabhidheya-tve sati
169.12	agandhavat-tvāt viśeṣaṇa-asiddhi^aḥ agandhavat-tve sati anabhidheya-tvāt iti
169.13	viśeṣya-asiddhi^aḥ/ evam sarveṣu sādhya-ekadeśa-vṛttiṣu/ evam asamartha-viśeṣaṇa-asamartha-
169.14	viśeṣyāḥ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ eva draṣṭavyāḥ/ yathā anityaḥ śabdaḥ kṛtaka-tve
169.15	sati prameya-tvāt prameya-tve sati kṛtaka-tvāt iti/ evam viśeṣa^su udāharaṇa^su/
169.16	evam sandigdha-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhedāḥ api draṣṭavyāḥ/ yathā
169.17	mayūra-śabdaḥ ayam ṣaḍja-ādimat-tve sati avarṇa-ātmaka-tvāt avarṇa-ātmaka-tve
169.18	sati ṣaḍja-ādimat-tvāt te ete ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannāḥ vidyamāna-sādhya-
169.19	dharmāḥ udāhṛtāḥ iti/ evam anyatara-asiddhāḥ vyāpaka-avyāpaka-bhedena
169.20	dvātriṃśakam śatam/ te ca punar vyadhikaraṇa-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-sandigdha-
169.21	asamartha-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhedena dvānavatam śatam/ yathā agnimān deśaḥ dhūmavat-
170.01	tvāt asti ca ātman^ḥ icchā-ādi-guṇa-tvāt asamartha-avyāpaka-asiddha-viśeṣaṇānām
170.02	udāharaṇam anityaḥ śabdaḥ prayatna-anantarīyaka-tve sati kṛtaka-
170.03	tvāt/ avyāpaka-asamartha-viśeṣyāṇām udāharaṇam anityaḥ śabdaḥ prameya-
170.04	bheda-bhedi-tve sati upalabhyamāna-tvāt/ vyadhikaraṇa-viśeṣyāṇām udāharaṇam
170.05	asti pradhānam bhedānām anvaya-darśanāt/ vyadhikaraṇa-viśeṣaṇānām
170.06	udāharaṇam rūpa-ādi-śabdāḥ candana-śabdāt atyantabhinna-arthāḥ samudāya-samudāyi-
170.07	asambhave sati tena vyapadeśāt/ evam eva vyadhikaraṇasya viśeṣyasya
170.08	udāharaṇam viparyayena anyathā viruddha-udāharaṇam anyeṣām abhyāsāt
170.09	nityaḥ śabdaḥ sandigdha-aprasiddha-viśeṣaṇānām udāharaṇam yathā sandihyamāna-
170.10	dhūma-ādi-bhāvaḥ agnimān deśaḥ dhūmavat-tve sati prakāśaka-tvāt
170.11	viparyayena sandigdha-viśeṣyāḥ vaktavyāḥ/ evam sandigdha-aprasiddha-aprasiddhāḥ
170.12	ubhaya-anyatara-siddhāḥ vyāpaka-avyāpaka-bhedena catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ bhavanti iti/ te ca
170.13	punar pūrvavat-bhedena śata-trayam caturaśītam/ evam anyathā-siddha-bhedaḥ draṣṭavyaḥ/
170.14	evam viruddha-viśeṣaṇa-viruddha-viśeṣyāḥ ca sarve eva draṣṭavyāḥ iti/ eṣām
170.15	tu udāharaṇāni hetvābhāsa-vārttike draṣṭavyāni svayam ca api ūhyāni abhyūhyāni/ te
170.16	ete ahetu^āḥ hetvābhāsāḥ ca asaṅkīrṇa-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhedena dve sahasre
170.17	dvātriṃśike asiddha-ādi-samuccayena anantaḥ bhedaḥ iti/ sādhya-avṛttyaḥ  tu
170.18	asat-tvāt na viśeṣaṇa-yoginaḥ iti ataḥ na abhidhīyante/ ete vidyamāna-sādhya-
171.01	vyāpaka-avyāpaka-dharmāḥ/ prapañcaḥ uktaḥ/ tatra mukhya-asiddha-bhedāḥ ṣoḍaśa
171.02	te ete pakṣa-avṛttyaḥ / anaikāntikāḥ tu saṅkīrṇāḥ mukhyataḥ ṣaḍ eva sādhya-
171.03	vyāpaka-varge viruddhāḥ catvārah eva tasmin antimaḥ tu pakṣa-mātra-dharma-tvāt
171.04	anupasaṅhārin^ḥ eva śeṣāḥ hetvābhāsāḥ te asiddha-anaikāntikāḥ viruddha-avyāpaka-
171.05	prasiddha-avyāpaka-bhedena saṅkīrṇāḥ udāharaṇa^su svayam ūhyāḥ iti/ ke cit tu
171.06	viruddha-avyabhicāriṇam anaikāntikam varṇayanti/ tad na asat-tvāt/ na ayam asti
171.07	iti uktam/ upetya tasya pañcaviṃśati-dhā bhedaḥ pañcānām hetūnām sarūpa-
171.08	asarūpa-pratisandhānāt pañcapañcakāḥ bhavanti iti/ teṣām tu sarveṣām na udāharaṇāni
171.09	santi iti na udāhriyante/ te ete hetvābhāsāḥ saṃkṣepena etāvatā
171.10	bhedena bhedavantaḥ teṣām saṃgraha-artham hetvābhāsānām vibhāga-uddeśa-artham
171.11	sūtram/ lakṣaṇataḥ eva pañcatvam gamyate iti cet yadi etāni pañca-hetvābhāsānām
171.12	lakṣaṇa-sūtrāṇi taiḥ eva pañcatvam gamyate iti niyama-artham sūtram
171.13	anarthakam/ na anarthakam lakṣaṇasya itaretara-vyavacchedaka-tvāt vyavacchedakam
171.14	lakṣaṇam na punar niyama-artham na hi niyamaḥ lakṣaṇena gamyate iti//
171.15	[1-2-5(46)]anaikāntikaḥ savyabhicāraḥ
171.16	teṣām anaikāntikaḥ savyabhicāraḥ/ etasmin ante niyataḥ aikāntikaḥ/
171.17	kaḥ punar ayam vyabhicāraḥ sādhya-tad-jātīya-anya-vṛtti-tvam/ yad
171.18	khalu sādhya-tad-jātīya-vṛtti-tve sati anyatra vartate tad-vyabhicārin
171.19	tad-vṛtti-tvam vyabhicāraḥd sarvaḥ ayam padārtha-bhedaḥ anta-dvaye avatiṣṭhate anyatra
171.20	prameyāt nityaḥ ca anityaḥ vyāpakaḥ ca avyāpakaḥ ca iti evam ādi/ tatra yaḥ
171.21	hetuḥ upāttaḥ ubhau antau āśritya pravartate saḥ anaikāntikaḥ(/) anaikāntikaḥ
172.01	iti/ kim punar ayam paryudāsaḥ uta prasajya-pratiṣedhaḥ iti kim ca ataḥ
172.02	yadi paryudāsaḥ sarvā anaikāntikatā aikāntikāt anyaḥ anaikāntikaḥ iti
172.03	bruvataḥ sarvaḥ anaikāntikaḥ prāptaḥ/ evam ca ekaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ iti prāptam/
172.04	atha prasajya-pratiṣedhaḥ tathā api aikāntika-abhāvaḥ anaikāntikaḥ iti prāptam/
172.05	tasya vyabhicāraḥ arthaḥ na asti iti ayuktaḥ anaikāntikaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ na ayam
172.06	paryudāsa-pakṣaḥ kim tu prasajya-pratiṣedhaḥ prasajya-pratiṣedhe abhāvaḥ iti cet
172.07	na abhāvaḥ dharma-viśeṣaka-tvāt dharmaḥ ayam viśiṣyate ye ete sanniyataḥ dharmaḥ
172.08	iti na bhavati na punar abhāvaḥ abhāve viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-asambhavāt na
172.09	abhāvaḥ ekāntaḥ sanniyataḥ na anaikāntikaḥ sanniyataḥ katham abrāhmaṇa-
172.10	vat iti/ yathā abrāhmaṇa-śabdaḥ prasajya-pratiṣedha-viṣaya-tvena pravartamānaḥ
172.11	yad uttare uttara-padam tad pratiṣedhati iti na punar abhāvam pratipādayati tathā iha api iti
172.12	adoṣaḥ/ udāharaṇam nityaḥ śabdaḥ asparśavat-tvāt śabdasya asparśatvam iti
172.13	ṣaṣṭhīā bhedaḥ pradarśyate kim punar tad sparśa-anāśrayasya sva-ātma-sattā-anubhavaḥ
172.14	asparśatvam sparśa-anādhārasya sattā-sambandhaḥ vā ubhayam ca śabda-vyatiriktam
172.15	iti arthavatī ṣaṣṭhī/ kutaḥ punar etad tad-bhāve bhāvāt tad-abhāve
172.16	abhāvāt iti yad khalu sparśavat sattām anubhavati na tatra asparśa-śabdaḥ
172.17	prasajyate iti yad ca asat tatra api na prasajyate yad asparśam sattā-anubhāvin
172.18	tatra prasajyate iti/ vākya-artha-anujñānāt ca pada-artha-abhyanujñā sparśa-anādhārasya
172.19	vastunaḥ sattā-sambandhaḥ iti yaḥ vākya-arthaḥ saḥ eva asparśavattva-
172.20	śabdasya arthaḥ/ atideśa-anupapattiḥ ayukta-tvāt iti cet anaikāntikaḥ
172.21	savyabhicāraḥ iti na kva cit anaikāntikaḥ uktaḥ iha anaikāntika-grahaṇāt
172.22	savyabhicāraḥ gamyate ataḥ na yuktam anaikāntikaḥ savyabhicāraḥ
173.01	iti na idam ayuktam lokataḥ tad-adhigati^aḥ/ yad punar lokataḥ na gamyate
173.02	tatra śāstram arthavat yad punar lokataḥ eva gamyate tatra upadeśaḥ anarthakaḥ
173.03	lokataḥ ca etad gamyate ubhaya-anta-sanniyataḥ anaikāntikaḥ iti/ yadi ca
173.04	yad lokataḥ gamyate tad ayuktam it manyase duḥkha-ādīni api deśayitavyāni
173.05	kim idam duḥkham iti tasmāt paripelavam etad iti avyāpaka-
173.06	tvāt alakṣaṇam etad iti atha api idam syāt anaikāntika-lakṣaṇena na sarvaḥ
173.07	anaikāntikaḥ gamyate yathā asādhāraṇena iti na anena eva saṃgrahāt katham
173.08	iti vyāvṛtti-dvāreṇa abhidhīyamānaḥ ayam ubhaya-anta-vyāvṛtti^aḥ anaikāntikaḥ
173.09	iti/ apare punar prakaraṇasamam anaikāntike eva antarbhāvayanti katham
173.10	iti anityaḥ ātman^ḥ śarīrāt anya-tvāt iti śarīrāt api anyat nityam
173.11	anityam ca dṛṣṭamataḥ anaikāntikaḥ iti/ taiḥ tu na lakṣaṇa-vyabhicāraḥ avagataḥ
173.12	na udāharaṇa-vyabhicāraḥ iti anaikāntikaḥ savyabhicāraḥ iti
173.13	etad lakṣaṇam vyabhicāriṇaḥ yadi ca anena prakaraṇasama-saṃgrahaḥ bhavati
173.14	prāptaḥ tarhi lakṣaṇasya vyabhicāraḥ na punar udāharaṇa-vyabhicāraḥ anaikāntika-
173.15	lakṣaṇa-vyabhicāraḥ iti na lakṣaṇasya tu ubhayam doṣaḥ lakṣya-avyāptiḥ
173.16	alakṣya-vyāptiḥ ca iti vakṣyamāṇakam prakaraṇasamasya lakṣaṇam īkṣitavyam//
173.17	[1-2-6(47)]siddhāntam abhyupetya tad-virodhin^ḥ viruddhaḥ
173.18	siddhāntam abhyupetya tad-virodhin^ḥ viruddhaḥ iti/ kaḥ asya sūtrasya arthaḥ/
173.19	abhyupagata-artha-virodhin^ḥ viruddhaḥ/ evam ca sarva-anukta-viruddha-saṃgrahaḥ/
173.20	yāvān kaḥ cana viruddha-bhedaḥ sarvaḥ asau anena saṃgṛhītaḥ iti tasmāt
173.21	abhyupagatam bādhate abhyupagatena bādhyate/ nanu evam ekaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ viruddhaḥ
174.01	prāpnoti satyam ekaḥ eva hetvābhāsaḥ viruddhaḥ tasya tu sāmānyena
174.02	viruddha-tvena saṃgṛhītasya pañcadhā apadeśaḥ yathā prameyam iti anena ṣoḍaśa-
174.03	padārthāḥ iti/ pṛthak-abhidhānam tarhi viruddhasya na kartavyam sāmānyataḥ
174.04	adhigatasya viśeṣa-jñāpana-artham yathā prameyasya eva tatra anaikāntika-ādi-
174.05	hetvābhāsaḥ dvi-rūpaḥ anaikāntika-ādiḥ viruddhaḥ etasmin tu viruddhatā eva iti
174.06	asādhāraṇatayā tṛṇa-ulapa-ādi-nyāyena niṣkṛṣya-abhidhānam/ tasmāt anaikāntika-
174.07	ādiṣu nimitta-samāveśāt saṃjñā-dvaya-samāveśaḥ vivakṣita-
174.08	tad-jātīya-anya-vṛtti-tvena anaikāntikatvam hetu-viṣaya-svarūpa-bādhā-rūpena tu
174.09	viruddhaḥ/ evam sarvatra udāharaṇam tad etad trailokyam vyakti^aḥ apaiti nityatva-
174.10	pratiṣedhāt apetam api asti vināśa-pratiṣedhāt vināśa-pratiṣedhāt iti
174.11	anena na nityam abhyanujñātam nityatva-pratiṣedhāt iti anena pratiṣiddham/ ete
174.12	te vākye paraspara-artha-bādhite tayoḥ ca paraspara-artha-bādhā-virodhaḥ pratijñā-
174.13	hetu^oḥ vā virodhaḥ yaḥ vā pratijñā-hetu^oḥ virodhaḥ saḥ viruddhaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ/
174.14	nanu pratijñā-virodhaḥ pṛthak-upadiṣṭaḥ nigrahasthāne pṛthak kva
174.15	hetvābhāsaḥ tasmāt na pratijñā-hetu^oḥ virodhaḥ vaktavyaḥ iti/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ
174.16	ubhaya-āśrita-tvāt virodhasya/ vivakṣātaḥ anyatara-nirdeśaḥ pratijñā-hetu^oḥ
174.17	virodhaḥ iti pratijñā-hetū āśritya ubhaya-āśritaḥ bhavati tatra yadi
174.18	pratijñā^ā virodhaḥ vivakṣitaḥ tadā pratijñā-virodhaḥ iti ucyate yadi
174.19	pratijñā^ā hetu-virodhaḥ hetu^aḥ vā pratijñā-virodhaka-tvam tadā viruddhaḥ
174.20	hetuḥ iti ataḥ pratijñā-virodhaḥ hetu-virodhaḥ vā iti adoṣaḥ/ hetu-virodha-
174.21	udāharaṇam nityaḥ śabdaḥ utpatti-dharmaka-tvāt pratijñā-virodhasya
174.22	udāharaṇam na asti ātman^ḥ iti pratijñā-hetu-virodha-udāharaṇam guṇa-vyatiriktam
175.01	dravyam artha-antarasya anupalabdhi^aḥ hetu-virodha-udāharaṇam na asti ekaḥ bhāvaḥ
175.02	samūhe bhāva-śabda-prayogāt iti samūhaḥ iti bruvāṇena aikāntikaḥ abhyupagataḥ
175.03	bhavati ekaḥ samuccayaḥ hi samūhaḥ iti//
175.04	[1-2-7(48)]yasmāt prakaraṇa-cintā saḥ nirṇaya-artham apadiṣṭaḥ prakaraṇasamaḥ
175.06	yasmāt prakaraṇa-cintā saḥ nirṇaya-artham apadiṣṭaḥ prakaraṇasamaḥ/
175.07	kasmāt prakaraṇa-cintā tattva-anupalabdhi^aḥ yasmāt upalabdha-tattve nivartate
175.08	cintā tasmāt sāmānyena adhigatasya yā viśeṣataḥ anupalabdhiḥ sā
175.09	prakaraṇa-cintām prayojayati/ udāharaṇam nityaḥ śabdaḥ anitya-dharma-anupalabdhi^aḥ
175.10	ataḥ eva prakaraṇam iti ataḥ na hetuḥ/ nanu ayam sādhya-aviśiṣṭaḥ eva
175.11	yathā śabdasya nityatvam sādhyam tathā anitya-dharma-anupalabdhiḥ api iti na aviśiṣṭaḥ
175.12	tasya eva prakaraṇa-pravṛtti-hetu^aḥ dharmasya hetu-tvena upādānāt yatra
175.13	sādhyena samānaḥ dharmaḥ hetu-tvena upādīyate saḥ sādhya-aviśiṣṭaḥ/ yatra punar
175.14	prakaraṇa-pravṛtti-hetuḥ eva prakaraṇasamaḥ ye tu saṃśaya-hetu-tvāt prakaraṇasama-
175.15	anaikāntikam varṇayanti teṣām pratyakṣa-anaikāntika-prakaraṇasamānām
175.16	abhedaḥ prāpnoti pratyakṣam api saṃśaya-kāraṇam iti samūhaḥ saṃśayasya kāraṇam
175.17	iti ataḥ na pratyakṣa-prasaṅgaḥ/ sāmānya-darśanam viśeṣa-avyavasthā viśeṣa-smṛtiḥ ca
175.18	samūhaḥ iti saṃśayasya kāraṇam na ekam pratyakṣam ataḥ pratyakṣa-prasaṅgaḥ
175.19	samūhaḥ kāraṇam iti abhidhānāt apratiṣedhaḥ samūhaḥ kāraṇam iti evam bruvatā
175.20	na pratyakṣasya saṃśaya-kāraṇa-tvam pratiṣiddham bhavati tasmāt prasaṅgataḥ tad-avasthaḥ
175.21	eva iti ubhaya-dharma-anupalabdhi^i anyatara-dharma-upādānam prakaraṇasama-arthaḥ/ yatra
176.01	khalu ubhaya-viśeṣa-anupalabdhiḥ tatra anyatara-viśeṣasya upādānam yad prakaraṇa-apanodāya
176.02	saḥ prakaraṇasamaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ ubhaya-viśeṣaṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ abhidhāna-
176.03	aśakya-tvāt na hi ubhaya-viśeṣaḥ anupalabdhaḥ śakyaḥ darśayitum śarīrāt
176.04	anyatvam tu na sūtra-arthaḥ n hi ayam śarīrāt anyatvam upalabhamānaḥ api yadā
176.05	tattvam upalabhate tadā pravartate iti tadā tattvam na upalakṣyate śarīrāt
176.06	anyatvam upalabhamānaḥ api tadā pravartate iti/ śarīrāt anyatvam ca na aikāntikam anaikāntikam
176.07	ataḥ ca na sūtra-arthaḥ//
176.08	[1-2-8(49)]sādhya-aviśiṣṭaḥ sādhya-tvāt sādhyasamaḥ
176.09	sādhya-aviśiṣṭaḥ sādhya-tvāt sādhyasamaḥ/ sādhyena aviśiṣṭaḥ yaḥ
176.10	sādhana-dharmaḥ sādhyavat prajñāpayitavyaḥ saḥ sādhyasamaḥ hetvābhāsaḥ/
176.11	tasya udāharaṇam dravyam chāyā gatimattvāt iti/ yathā eva dravyam chāyāyāḥ
176.12	sādhyam tathā gatimattvam api iti/ gatimattvam deśa-antara-darśanāt iti cet
176.13	tathā api idam syāt gatimat-chāyā deśa-antare darśanāt iti/ yad khalu
176.14	deśa-antara^su upalabhyate tad-gatimat yathā kumbhaḥ tathā chāyā tasmāt
176.15	gatimatī na āśraya-asiddha-tvāt/ evam api āśraya-asiddhaḥ hetuḥ sati dravya-
176.16	bhāve chāyāyāḥ deśa-antare darśanam samartham syāt dravya-bhāvaḥ tu asiddhaḥ
176.17	tasmāt āśraya-asiddhaḥ hetuḥ upetya deśa-antare darśanam tasya anyathā-siddhi^aḥ
176.18	asiddhi^aḥ yad tu deśa-antare darśanam chāyāyāḥ tad-anyathā bhavati anyathā-bhāvan
176.19	na artham bādhati katham anyathā āvaraṇa-santānāt asannidhi-santāna-tejasi
176.20	yāvake āvarake dravye apasarpati tejasaḥ asannidhi-viśiṣṭam dravyam yad upalabhyate tad tu
177.01	chāyā iti ucyate/ saḥ ayam asiddhaḥ tredhā bhavati prajñāpanīya-dharma-samānaḥ
177.02	āśraya-asiddhaḥ anyathā-siddhah ca iti/ nityaḥ śabdaḥ asparsavat-tvāt iti eva
177.03	na udāharaṇam na hi asparśatvam śabdasya jñāpanīyam na āśraya-asiddhaḥ na anyathā-
177.04	siddham iti yathā ca asmābhiḥ sūtram varṇitam tathā ca udāhṛtam/ tathā
177.05	ayam asambaddhaḥ doṣaḥ na sūtra-arthaḥ vijñātaḥ na akṣara-arthaḥ na hetvābhāsaḥ
177.06	na dṛṣṭāntābhāsaḥ iti//
177.07	[1-2-9(50)]kāla-atyaya-apadiṣṭaḥ kālātītaḥ
177.08	kāla-atyaya-apadiṣṭaḥ kālātītaḥ/ yasya apadiśyamānasya kāla-
177.09	atyayena ekadeśaḥ yujyate saḥ ekadeśa-atyayāt kāla-atyaya-apadiṣṭaḥ kālātītaḥ
177.10	iti ucyate/ udāharaṇam nityaḥ śabdaḥ saṃyoga-vyaṅgya-tvāt iti upalabdhi-
177.11	kāle saṃyogaḥ na asti saḥ ayam saṃyogaḥ hetu-viśeṣaṇa-tvena upāttaḥ
177.12	upalabdhi-kālam atyeti yadā upalabhyate tadā saṃyogaḥ na asti dāru-vraścane
177.13	dāru-paraśu-saṃyoga-nivṛtti^i śabda-upalabdhiḥ iti/ nanu ayam anaikāntikaḥ eva
177.14	saṃyoga-vyaṅgya-tvāt iti anityam api saṃyogena vyajyamānam dṛṣṭam yathā
177.15	ghaṭaḥ iti saṃyoga-vyaṅgya-tvena anavasthānasya sādhya-tvāt na brūmaḥ nityaḥ
177.16	śabdaḥ iti api tu avatiṣṭhate śabdaḥ iti pratijñā-arthaḥ tathā ca saṃyoga-
177.17	vyaṅgya-tvāt iti ayam hetuḥ na anaikāntikaḥ/ na hi anavasthitam kim cit saṃyogena
177.18	vyajyamānam dṛṣṭam iti avayava-viparyāsa-vacanam api na sūtra-arthaḥ sāmarthyāt
177.19	na hi paścāt abhidhīyamānaḥ hetuḥ udāharaṇa-sādharmyāt iti etad hetu-lakṣaṇam
177.20	jahāti ajahatve tu lakṣaṇam na hetvābhāsaḥ bhavitum arhati iti avayava-
177.21	viparyāsa-vacanam aprāpta-kālam iti nigrahasthānam uktam/ tad eva punar ucyate
178.01	iti samīkṛte abhidhānāt nigrahasthānam iti cet atha manyase yasmāt
178.02	samīkṛte pakṣe paścāt abhidhīyate tataḥ nigrahasthānam iti kena samīkṛtaḥ
178.03	iti vaktavyam hetu-anabhidhānena samīkṛtaḥ/ yadi hetu-anabhidhānena
178.04	samīkṛtaḥ kim atra hetu^aḥ asāmarthyam etad asya asāmarthyam yad ayam paścāt abhidhīyate
178.05	iti na etad hetu^aḥ asāmarthyam yad ayam paścāt abhidhīyate iti api tu
178.06	vaktṛ^aḥ iti na hi svatantraḥ hetuḥ sādhanam api tu sādhayitṛ-tantra-tvāt na
178.07	hetu-doṣaḥ sāmarthyāt ca iti uktam tasmāt avayava-viparyāsa-vacanam na sūtra-arthaḥ na
178.08	samīkṛta-abhidhānam iti//
178.09	[1-2-10(51)]vacana-vighātaḥ artha-vikalpa-upapatti^ā chalam
178.10	[1-2-11(52)]tad trividham vāk-chalam sāmānya-chalam upacāra-chalam
178.11	ca iti
178.12	[1-2-12(53)]aviśeṣa-abhihite arthe vaktṛ^aḥ abhiprāyāt artha-antara-kalpanā
178.13	vāk-chalam
178.14	vacana-vighātaḥ artha-vikalpa-upapatti^ā chalam/ vacana-vighātah yaḥ
178.15	kriyate sāmānyasya śabdasya viśeṣa-aneka-sambandhi-tve sati avivakṣita-
178.16	āropena chalam tad veditavyam/ tad trividham iti niyama-artham pūrvavat anekadhā-
178.17	bhinnam chalam saṃgṛhyate iti/ aviśeṣa-abhihite arthe iti sūtram aviśeṣa-
178.18	abhihitam sāmānya-śruti vākyam padam vā aviśeṣa-abhihitam iti ucyate
178.19	vākyam sāmānya-śruti yathā nava-kambalaḥ ayam iti padam sāmānya-śruti yathā
178.20	aśvaḥ aśvaḥ iti aviśeṣa-śabda-anabhidhānam prayoga-abhāvāt na hi aviśeṣa-
178.21	śabdena kasya cit abhidhānam sambhavati na ca asya prayogaḥ yuktaḥ iti vākyam
179.01	ca sarvam uccarat-viśeṣam eva pratyāyayati iti sāmānya-pratyāyanam ayuktam na
179.02	prakaraṇa-ādīnām antareṇa śruti-mātrāt tad-upapatti^aḥ yadā prakaraṇa-ādi-nirapekṣam
179.03	vākyam uccāryate śvetaḥ dhāvati iti tadā vākya-śruti-sāmānyāt śrotṛ^aḥ
179.04	sammohaḥ sammohe sati avivakṣitam artham adhyāropya pratiṣedhati/ evam pade
179.05	api iti/ artha-grahaṇam śabda-nirākaraṇa-artha-viṣayam chalam na śabda-viṣayam
179.06	iti na hi ayam pratiṣeddhum śaknoti na ayam nava-kambala-śabdaḥ iti tasya
179.07	pratyavasthānam jñātvā ajñātvā vā prayogāt pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ/ yadi
179.08	tāvat nava-kambalasya artham buddhvā na ayam nava-kambalaḥ iti pratyavatiṣṭhate tadā
179.09	artha-antaram āha anyat buddhyate anyat pratiṣidhyate iti/ atha punar na eva nava-
179.10	kambalasya artham buddhyate tathā api ajñānam iti//
179.11	[1-2-13(54)]sambhavataḥ arthasya atisāmānya-yogāt asambhūta-artha-kalpanā
179.12	sāmānya-chalam
179.13	sambhavataḥ arthasya atisāmānya-yogāt asambhūta-artha-kalpanā sāmānya-chalam/
179.14	sāmānyasya vivakṣita-artha-atikramaḥ atisāmānyam udāharaṇam vidyā-caraṇa-
179.15	sampannaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ iti param tu brāhmaṇyam vidyā-caraṇa-sampanna-tve hetuḥ iti
179.16	kṛtvā pratyavatiṣṭhate stuti-arthe vākye anyathā-kāram pratyavasthānam ubhayathā
179.17	doṣaḥ buddhvā abuddhvā vā iti//
179.18	[1-2-14(55)]dharma-vikalpa-nirdeśe artha-sadbhāva-pratiṣedhaḥ upacāra-chalam
179.20	dharma-vikalpa-nirdeśaḥ iti sūtram/ dharma-vikalpa-nirdeśa-śabdena
180.01	abhidhāna-dharmaḥ dvedhā abhidhīyate pradhānam bhāktaḥ ca mañcāḥ iti kāṣṭha-
180.02	saṃghāta^su pradhānam mañca-śabdaḥ krośana-kriyāyāḥ asambhavam īkṣitvā
180.03	sthāniṣu puruṣa^su bhāktaḥ saḥ ayam abhidhāna-abhidheya-prakāraḥ evam vyavatiṣṭhate
180.04	yaḥ etasmin anyathā-kāra-pratiṣedhaḥ tad upacāra-chalam iti/ asya api
180.05	pūrvavt pratiṣedhaḥ jñātvā ajñātvā vā iti//
180.06	[1-2-15(56)]vāc-chalam eva upacāra-chalam tad-aviśeṣāt
180.07	[1-2-16(57)]na tad-artha-antara-bhāvāt
180.08	vāc-chalam eva upacāra-chalam iti sūtram/ ekatvam aviśeṣāt kaḥ
180.09	aviśeṣaḥ iha api artha-antara-kalpanā tatra api iti na tad-artha-antara-bhāvāt aviśeṣāt
180.10	iti asya hetu^aḥ anena sūtrena asiddhatām udbhāvayati/ katham asiddhiḥ
180.11	ekatra vastu-sadbhāvaḥ pratiṣidhyate na eva kroṣṭṛ^āḥ mañca mañcāḥ iti ekatra vastu-
180.12	abhyanujñāya kambala-yogam abhyupagamya kambalasya anekatā dharmaḥ pratiṣidhyate
180.13	iti/ yatra dharmaḥ pratiṣidhyate yatra dharmin^ḥ saḥ ayam atimahān viśeṣaḥ
180.14	katham manyase kim te anena viśeṣeṇa iha api artha-antara-kalpanā tatra api iti//
180.15	[1-2-17(58)]aviśeṣe vā kim cit sādharmyāt eka-chala-prasaṅgaḥ
180.16	aviśeṣe vā kim cit sādharmyāt eka-chala-prasaṅgḥ/ kaḥ asya sūtrasya arthaḥ
180.17	virodhaḥ dvitvam abhyanujñāyamānam nivartayati katamena punar nyāyena dvitvam
180.18	abhyanujñāyamānam vāc-chalam eva upacāra-chalam tayoḥ ekatvam bruvatā sāmānya-
180.19	chalam anyat abhyanujñātam bhavati viśeṣaṇa-anarthakyam vā yadi sarvam chalam
180.20	eva iti abhyupagamaḥ vāc-chalam eva upacāra-chalam tad-aviśeṣāt iti vyartham viśeṣaṇam
180.21	katham ekatva-prasaṅgaḥ kim cit sādharmyāt yadi anartha-antara-kalpanā aviśeṣāt
181.01	ekatvam sarva-chalam ekam prāpnoti kim cit sādharmyāt iti kim punar tad
181.02	vacana-vighātaḥ artha-vikalpa-upapattiḥ ca tasmin chale iti//
181.03	[1-2-18(59)]sādharmya-vaidharmyābhyām pratyavasthānam jātiḥ
181.04	sādharmya-vaidharmyābhyām pratyavasthānam jātiḥ/ sādharmyena pratyavasthānam vaidharmyena
181.05	pratyavasthānam jātiḥ iti/ prathama-pakṣa-anuyogam darśayati sthāpanāyām
181.06	satyām pratīpam avasthānam pratyavasthānam pratipakṣavat iti/ sūtra-arthaḥ tu yathā-śruti
181.07	na punar udāharaṇa-sādharmyena udāharaṇa-vaidharmyena vā iti kim artham idam ucyate
181.08	vyāpaka-artham yadi yathā-śruti sūtra-arthaḥ bhavati tadā sarva-jātyaḥ
181.09	vyāpyante yena kena cit sādharmyam yena kena cit vaidharmyam iti lakṣaṇe jātīnām
181.10	avyāpaka-tvam tu doṣaḥ/ bhāṣye udāharaṇa-sādharmyam udāharaṇa-vaidharmyam
181.11	ca udāharaṇa-artham iti yathā ca udāharaṇena evam anudāharaṇena api iti//
181.12	[1-2-19(60)]vipratipattiḥ apratipattiḥ ca nigrahasthānam
181.13	vipratipattiḥ apratipattiḥ ca nigrahasthānam/ anyathā-sthitasya anyathā-
181.14	abhihitasya apratipattiḥ vipratipattiḥ ca sva-para-artha-uttarā sampratipattiḥ
181.15	apratipattiḥ samarthe sādhane nigrahasthānam aprāpti^i katham apratipattiḥ vipratipattiḥ/
181.16	yadā ayam sādhayitṛ^ḥ samarthena sādhanena upāttena parena jāti-
181.17	ādibhiḥ ākulīkṛtaḥ jñānam pratipadyate tadā katham apratipattiḥ katham vā
181.18	vipratipattiḥ iti tadā sādhanasya eva sāmarthya-aparijñānāt asamartham etad
181.19	sādhanam iti apratipattiḥ vipratipattiḥ iti//
181.20	[1-2-20(61)]tad-vikalpāt jāti-nigrahasthāna-bahutvam
182.01	tad-vikalpāt jāti-nigrahasthāna-bahutvam/ tāḥ etāḥ jātyaḥ  kiyatyaḥ
182.02	bhavanti kiyanti vā nigrahasthānāni iti avadhāraṇa-artham āha/ tad-vikalpāt
182.03	jāti-nigrahasthāna-bahutvam iti/ sādharmya-vaidharmyābhyām pratyavasthānasya
182.04	vikalpāt tayoḥ ca vipratipatti-apratipatti^oḥ vikalpāt bahvīaḥ jātyaḥ  bahūni
182.05	nigrahasthānāni bhavanti/ katamā jātyaḥ  sādharmya-pratyavasthānāt katamāḥ
182.06	ca vaidharmya-pratyavasthānāt katamāni nigrahasthānāni vipratipatti-
182.07	vikalpāt katamāni vā apratipatti-vikalpāt bhavanti iti pratiyogam
182.08	viśeṣa-lakṣaṇa^su utprekṣaṇīyāni iti/ ete pramāṇa-ādyaḥ  padārthāḥ uddiṣṭāḥ
182.09	lakṣitāḥ ca yathā-uddeśam ca yathā-lakṣaṇam ca parīkṣā vartiṣyate iti//
182.10	tantra-pratijñā-saṃsāraḥ tad-nivṛttiḥ ca saṃvidā/
182.11	uddeśaḥ lakṣaṇam ca eva tattvānām iha kīrtitam//
182.12	iti auddyotakare nyāyasūtravārttike prathama-adhyāye
182.13	dvitīyam āhnikam//
182.14	samāptaḥ ca ayam prathamaḥ adhyāyaḥ//183.01	nyāyavārttike
183.02	dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ/
183.03	namaḥ parama-ātmane
183.04	trividhā ca asya śāstrasya pravṛttiḥ uddeśaḥ lakṣaṇam parīkṣa parīkṣā
183.05	ca iti uktam/ tatra uddeśa-lakṣaṇe pūrva-adhyāy^i eva vyākhyāte parīkṣā vartiṣyate
183.06	iti/ tatra yathā uddeśaḥ tathā lakṣaṇam tathā parīkṣā iti sthānavatām parīkṣā-
183.07	vacanam iti pūrvam pramāṇam uddiṣṭam lakṣitam ca pūrvam eva tad parīkṣyate iti
183.08	avasara-prāptāni tāni vilaṅghya saṃśayaḥ parīkṣyate/ kim artham krama-bhedaḥ
183.09	parīkṣā-aṅga-tvāt pūrvam saṃśayaḥ parīkṣyate tathā ca uktam/ vimṛśya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām
183.10	artha-avadhāraṇam nirṇayaḥ/ nanu ca uktam na ayam nirṇaye niyamaḥ
183.11	vimṛṣya pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām sarvatra nirṇayaḥ iti vāde pakṣa-pratipakṣābhyām
183.12	eva śāstre ca vimarṣa-varjam iti śāstre ca ayam vicāraḥ tasmāt na saṃśaya-
183.13	pūrvakaḥ iti saṃśaya-pūrvakaḥ vicāraḥ tarka-viṣayaḥ eva/ na ayam tarka-viṣayaḥ
183.14	iti/ satyam/ na nirṇaye niyamaḥ vicāre hi niyamaḥ nirṇayaḥ
183.15	evam ca anyathā ca vicāraḥ saṃśaya-pūrvakaḥ eva yad api uktam vāde śāstre ca
183.16	vimarṣa-varjam iti vādi-prativādinau adhikṛtya tad uktam śāstra-abhyupagame
183.17	ca vādi-prativādinoḥ na saṃśyaḥ ubhayoḥ niścita-tvāt/ śāstre ca na
183.18	saṃśayaḥ/ niścitasya vastunaḥ śāstra-abhyupagamena abhyupagamāt/ tatra vādi-
183.19	prativādinau tāvat vicārayataḥ kim nitya-sādhanam balīyaḥ āho
183.20	anitya-sādhanam iti/ śāstre api ca vicāraḥ sādhana-sāmarthya-asāmarthya-
184.01	parijñāna-arthaḥ tatra saṃśayaḥ iti/ tasmāt saṃśaya-pūrvakaḥ eva vicāraḥ tad-
184.02	mūlaka-tvāt parīkṣā-vidhi^aḥ saṃśayaḥ pūrvam parīkṣyeta iti/ tatra saṃśayaḥ
184.03	vastu-svarūpa-anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ/ nirukta-vyāghātaḥ iti cet/
184.04	anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ ca pratyayaḥ ca iti vyāhatam/ na pratyayasya svarūpa-anavadhāraṇāt
184.05	avyavaccheda-ātmaka-tvam svarūpam asya paricchidyate/ na punar anena
184.06	viṣaya-svarūpam avadhāryate iti saḥ ayam saṃśayaḥ samāna-dharma-upapatti^aḥ iti
184.07	evam ādiyaḥ ādibhyaḥ tasya ākṣepaḥ//
184.08	[2-1-1]samāna-aneka-dharma-adhyavasāyāt anyatara-dharma-adhyavasāyāt vā
184.09	na saṃśayaḥ
184.10	samāna-aneka-dharma-adhyavasāyāt iti evam ādi sūtram/ tasya yathā-
184.11	śruti-utthānam iti samānasya dharmasya adhyavasāyāt saṃśayaḥ na samāna-dharma-
184.12	mātrāt upalabhyamānaḥ dharmaḥ saṃśaya-hetuḥ iti dharma-dharmi-grahaṇena vā
184.13	saṃśaya-abhāvaḥ iti samānam anayoḥ dharmam upalabhate iti dharma-dharmi-grahaṇe
184.14	kva saṃśayaḥ iti dharmiṇaḥ artha-antara-bhāva-tvāt na saṃśayaḥ na hi anyasmin upalabdhe
184.15	anyatra saṃśayaḥ iti/ atha vā na samāna-dharma-adhyavasāyaḥ saṃśayasya
184.16	kāraṇam vyavaccheda-ātmaka-tvāt saṃśaye ca tad-abhāvāt iti/ etena aneka-
184.17	dharma-adhyavasāyāt iti vyākhyātam/ adhigata-anadhigatayoḥ na saṃśayaḥ
184.18	adhigate vastuni saṃśayaḥ na yuktaḥ nirṇīta-tvāt na anadhigate
184.19	adṛṣṭa-tvāt na samāna-dharma-adhyavasāyāt saṃśayaḥ sati api abhāvāt sati api
184.21	dharma-anadhyavasāyāt eva saṃśayaḥ anyathā api bhāvāt anyathā api dṛṣṭaḥ saṃśayaḥ
185.01	yathā viruddha-avyabhicāri-dharma-dvaya-sannipāte tattva-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca saṃśayaḥ/
185.02	yathā prakaraṇasame eka-vṛtti-tvāt ca na samāne eka-vṛttiḥ ayam dharmaḥ na
185.03	ca eka-vṛttiḥ samānaḥ bhavitum arhati iti//
185.04	[2-1-2]vipratipatti-vyavasthā-adhyavasāyāt ca
185.05	[2-1-3]vipratipatti^i ca sampratipatti^aḥ
185.06	etena vipratipatti-avyavasthā-adhyavasāyāt iti pratyuktam/ vipratipanne
185.07	vyavasthā-adhyavasāyāt saṃśayaḥ na vipratipatti-mātrāt avyavasthā-mātrāt vā
185.08	vipratipatti^i ca sampratipatti^aḥ na vipratipattiḥ asti iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
185.09	[2-1-4]avyavasthā-ātmani vyavasthita-tvāt ca avyavasthāyāḥ
185.10	avyavasthā-ātmani vyavasthita-tvāt vā vyavasthāyāḥ na avyavasthā vedyate
185.11	iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
185.12	[2-1-5]tathā atyanta-saṃśayaḥ tad-dharma-sātatya-upapatti^aḥ
185.13	tathā atyanta-saṃśayaḥ tad-dharma-sātatya-upapatti^aḥ samāna-dharma-ādīnām sātatyāt
185.14	nityaḥ saṃśayaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
185.15	asya uttaram/
185.16	[2-1-6]yathā-ukta-adhyavasāyāt eva tad-viśeṣa-apekṣāt saṃśaye na asaṃśayaḥ
185.17	na atyanta-saṃśayaḥ vā
