185.18	yathā-ukta-adhyavasāyāt eva tad-viśeṣa-apekṣāt saṃśaye na asaṃśayaḥ na atyanta-
185.19	saṃśayaḥ vā na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ yad punar etad-dharma-
185.20	dharmi-grahaṇe saṃśaya-abhāvaḥ iti tad na aparijñānāt na brūmaḥ yaḥ asau dharmin^ḥ upalabhyate
186.01	tasya dharmam upalabhaḥ iti api tu yau mayā pūrvam sthāṇu-puruṣau
186.02	upalabdhau tayoḥ ayam samānaḥ dharmaḥ upalabhyate iti/ etena eka-vṛtti-tvāt
186.03	iti parihatam/ yad punar etad na artha-antara-adhyavasāyāt anyatra saṃśayaḥ iti
186.04	anabhyupagamāt/ apare artha-antara-bhāva-mātram mayā saṃśaya-hetu-tvena
186.05	na upādīyate kim tu artha-antara-viśeṣaḥ kaḥ punar asau viśeṣaḥ dharma-dharmi-
186.06	bhāvaḥ dharmaḥ upalabdhe dharmiṇi saṃśayaḥ iti parihāram manyante na punar
186.07	rūpa-sparśayoḥ dharma-dharmi-bhāvaḥ iti paśyāmaḥ/ tad ca na kim kāraṇam/
186.08	artha-antara-bhāvasya anivṛtti^aḥ yathā eva rūpa-sparśayoḥ artha-antara-bhāvaḥ tathā dharma-
186.09	dharmiṇoḥ iti na yuktaḥ artha-antara-darśanāt artha-antare saṃśayaḥ atra api
186.10	tad eva uttaram na anabhyupagamāt iti/ na brūmaḥ artha-antara-darśanāt artha-antare
186.11	dharmiṇi sandehaḥ api tu samāna-dharma-ādi-viśiṣṭa-dharmi-darśanāt aviśeṣavati
186.12	dharmiṇi eva saṃśayaḥ iti/ yadi api etad sārūpya-abhāvāt iti viśeṣa-
186.13	anavadhāraṇam ubhayaḥ sārūpyam yathā eva samāna-dharma-darśanena viśeṣaḥ na avadhāryate
186.14	iti tathā saṃśayena api iti/ kāraṇa-sadbhāvāt kārya-sadbhāvaḥ iti
186.15	na sārūpya-nirdeśaḥ ayam api tu kārya-kāraṇa-dharma-nirdeśaḥ tathā ca
186.16	kārya-antara^su api etad asti iti/ na hi kim cit kāryam kāraṇa-sadbhāvam
186.17	antareṇa sambhavati iti/ etena aneka-dharma-adhyavasāyāt iti pratiṣedhaḥ parihataḥ/
186.18	yaḥ eva samāna-dharma-adhyavasāye parihāraḥ saḥ eva aneka-dharma-adhyavasāye
186.19	api iti/ yad punar etad adhigata-anadhigatayoḥ abhāvāt iti tatra
186.20	aparijñānāt iti na brūmaḥ anyasmin adhigate anyatra saṃśayaḥ iti api ca
186.21	sāmānyataḥ adhigate dharmiṇi viśeṣa-anavadhāraṇa-ātmakaḥ pratyayaḥ saṃśayaḥ
186.22	iti// yad punar etad sati api abhāvāt iti na samāna-artha-aparijñānāt/
187.01	na hi kṛtakatvam samānaḥ dharmaḥ anyathā samāna-dharmasya abhidhānāt
187.02	samānaḥ dharmaḥ iti vivakṣita-tad-jātīya-anyavṛttiḥ yaḥ samānaḥ na
187.03	punar kṛtakatvam evam yad punar etad na samāna-dharma-adhyavasāyāt eva anyathā api
187.04	darśanāt iti tad na pratiṣedhāt anavadhāraṇāt ca/ viruddha-avyabhicāri-
187.05	dharma-adhyavasāyāt saṃśayaḥ iti pratiṣiddham etad anavadhāraṇena eva tattva-
187.06	anupalabdhi^aḥ iti pratyuktam/ na ca etad avadhāryate samāna-dharama-darśanāt eva
187.07	na anyasmāt iti anyasmāt api saṃśayaḥ aneka-dharma-adhyavasāyāt iti uktam vipratipatti-
187.08	avyavasthā-adhyavasāyāt eva iti anena eva pratyuktam/ na evam brūmaḥ vipratipatti-
187.09	mātrāt avyavasthā-mātrāt saṃśayaḥ iti na ca avyavasthātaḥ saṃśayaḥ upalabdhi-
187.10	anupalabdhi-avyavasthāyāḥ pūrva-pada-viśeṣaṇa-tvāt pūrva-pada-viśeṣaṇam upalabdhi-
187.11	anupalabdhī na svatantre saṃśaya-kāraṇam tad ca sūtre eva uktam iti/
187.12	yad punar etad vipratipatti^i sampratipatti^aḥ iti na saṃjñā-anyathā-tvāt arthasya anyathā-
187.13	tvam yasmāt vipratipatti-śabda-arthasya nimitta-antara-sannipāte sampratipattiḥ
187.14	iti nāma kṛtam na tāvatā vipratipattiḥ na asti vipratipatti^aḥ
187.15	tathā-bhāvāt saṃśaya-hetu-tvam/ yad punar etad avyavasthā-ātmani vyavasthita-
187.16	tvāt ca avyavasthāyāḥ na avyavasthā asti iti na tad api vyāghātāt avyavasthā
187.17	na asti avyavasthā-ātmani ca vyavasthita-tvāt ca vyāghātaḥ na ca nimitta-
187.18	antara-sannipātena arthasya saṃjñā-antara-karaṇam artha-tathā-bhāvam bādhate yathā-bhūtaḥ
187.19	eva arthaḥ saṃjñā-antara-karaṇe tathā-bhūtaḥ eva akaraṇe api iti/ yad punar etad
187.20	tathā-atyanta-saṃśayaḥ tad-dharma-sātatya-upapatti^aḥ iti tad api na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt
187.21	iti uktam/ na ayam samāna-dharma-ādibhyaḥ eva saṃśayaḥ kim tu tad-viṣaya-
187.22	adhyavasāyāt upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-avyavasthāne ca sati viśeṣa-smṛti-sahitāt
188.01	evam ca sati na asaṃśayaḥ na atyanta-saṃśayaḥ anyatara-dharma-adhyavasāyāt
188.02	iti na yuktam/ na etasmāt saṃśayaḥ iti na hi kadā cit anyatara-
188.03	dharma-adhyavasāye sati saṃśayaḥ anyatara-dharma-adhyavasāyasya nirṇaya-
188.04	hetu-tvāt iti//
188.05	[2-1-7]yatra saṃśayaḥ tatra evam uttara-uttara-prasaṅgaḥ
188.06	yatra saṃśayaḥ tatra evam uttara-uttara-prasaṅgaḥ/ kaḥ asya sūtrasya arthaḥ svayam na
188.07	saṃśayaḥ pratiṣeddhavyaḥ parena tu saṃśaye pratiṣiddhe evam uttaram vācyam iti
188.08	śiṣyam śikṣyati//
188.09	atha idānīm avasara-prāptāni pramāṇāni parīkṣyante iti kim punar
188.10	eṣām parīkṣyam/ ādi^i tāvat sambhavaḥ kim pramāṇāni santi uta na
188.11	santi iti sat-asatoḥ sāmānyāt prameya-tvāt viśeṣa-adarśanāt ca saṃśayaḥ
188.12	vyudasya saṃśayam paraḥ pakṣa-parigrahena pratyavatiṣṭhate
188.13	[2-1-8]pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam ca traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ
188.14	pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam ca traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti/ asya arthaḥ triṣu api
188.15	kāla^su pramāṇāni na artham sādhayanti iti//
188.16	[2-1-9]pūrvam hi pramāṇa-siddhi^i na indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt pratyakṣa-
188.17	utpattiḥ
188.18	pūrvam hi pramāṇa-siddhi^i iti sūtram/ na indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt
188.19	pratyakṣam jñānam utpadyate iti sūtra-arthe vyāghātaḥ doṣaḥ yadi pūrvam jñānam
188.20	na indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ tasya kāraṇam iti/ yad uktam indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-
188.21	utpannam iti tad vyāhanyate//
189.01	[2-1-10]paścāt siddhi^i na pramāṇa^bhyaḥ prameya-siddhiḥ
189.02	paścāt siddhi^i iti sūtram/ na pramāṇa-nimittā prameya-saṃjñā iti
189.03	vyāghātaḥ na pramāṇam antareṇa prameyam ātmānam labhate na pramāṇa-
189.04	āyattā prameya-siddhiḥ//
189.05	[2-1-11]yugapad-siddhi^i pratyartha-niyata-tvāt krama-vṛtti-tva-abhāvaḥ
189.06	buddhīnām
189.07	[2-1-12]traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ
189.08	yugapad-siddhi^i iti sūtram/ indriya-arthānām buddhīnām ca sannipāte
189.09	sati yugapad-bhāvaḥ iti krama-vṛtti-tvam dṛṣṭam na syāt iti vyāghātaḥ
189.10	ca doṣaḥ yugapad jñāna-anutpattiḥ iti vyāhanyate/ atra samādhiḥ/
189.11	upalabdhi-hetu^aḥ upalabdhi-viṣayasya ca arthasya pūrva-asthira-sahabhāva-aniyamāt
189.12	iti vākyasya asiddhatā-udbhāvanam arthaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam iti ca
189.13	pratijñā-padayoḥ vyāghātaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīni na artham sādhayanti iti ayam arthaḥ/
189.14	ayam ca vyāhataḥ katham iti pratyakṣa-ādīni ca na artham sādhayanti ca
189.15	yathā idam vastu na upalabhyate iti idam ca na upalabhyate iti vyāghātaḥ/ evam
189.16	pratyakṣa-ādīni ca na artham sādhayanti iti ca/ katham vā anyathā pratyakṣa-ādīni
189.17	na pratyakṣa-ādi-śabdāḥ artha-sādhakam antareṇa kṛta-arthāḥ bhavanti iti dharma-pratiṣedhāt
189.18	ca pratyakṣa-ādi-abhyupagamaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam iti evam bruvatā na
189.19	pratyakṣa-ādīni pratiṣiddhāni api tu pramāṇatvam dharmaḥ iti/ atha
189.20	bhāvasya asvatantra-tvāt tāni api pratiṣiddhāni pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam
189.21	iti bhāva-abhidhānam etad asvatantraḥ ca bhāvaḥ iti bhāva-pratiṣedhāt
189.22	tadvatām pratiṣedhaḥ iti evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ pratyakṣa-ādīnām abhāvaḥ
190.01	pratipādyaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīni ca na santi iti kena abhāvam pratipādayasi na
190.02	pratyakṣa-ādi-abhāve anyaḥ pratipādana-upādhiḥ kaḥ cit asti viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt ca
190.03	pramāṇa-antara-abhyanujñā pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam iti evam bruvatā pratyakṣa-ādi-
190.04	vyatireki-pramāṇam abhyanujñātam bhavati vyartham vā viśeṣaṇam pratyakṣa-ādīnām
190.05	aprāmāṇyam iti/ atha na pramāṇa-antaram abhyanujñāyate kim asya
190.06	viśeṣaṇasya sāmarthyam pratyakṣa-ādīni na pramāṇāni iti na eva pramāṇāni
190.07	santi iti evam vaktavyam iti etad api vyāhatam pramāṇāni na eva santi pratipādayasi
190.08	ca iti/ yad api tad-pramāṇa-antaram abhyanujñāyate tatra api traikālya-
190.09	asiddhi^aḥ iti ayam anupayuktaḥ hetuḥ yad ca triṣu api kāla^su na pratipādayati
190.10	katham tasya pramāṇatvam iti anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ atha dharma-pratiṣedhaḥ eva ayam
190.11	tathā api yāni prāmāṇya-vyatiriktāni pratyakṣa-ādīni abhyanujñāyante rāja-
190.12	puruṣa-pratiṣedha-nyāyena kaḥ teṣām svabhāvaḥ iti anuvṛtti^i anuyogaḥ vyāghātaḥ ca/
190.13	artha-antara-anabhyupagame ṣaṣṭhīāḥ tad-hitasya ca arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ yadi pratyakṣa-ādi-
190.14	vyatiriktam na prāmāṇyam asti pratyakṣa-ādīnām iti ṣaṣṭhī-arthaḥ kaḥ tataḥ
190.15	avaśyam vidhīyamānaḥ pratiṣidhyamānaḥ vā dharmaḥ abhyupeyaḥ tad-abhyupagame
190.16	ca pratyakṣa-ādīnām pṛthak svarūpam vaktavyam iti anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ etena
190.17	tad-hita-arthaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ yaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti ayam api vyadhikaraṇaḥ
190.18	traikālya-viṣaya-tvāt tri-kālasya bhāvaḥ traikālyam tasya asiddhiḥ iti
190.19	na pramāṇa-dharmaḥ atha ayam arthaḥ triṣu api kāla^su na pramāṇāni artham sādhayanti
190.20	iti tathā api hetu-arthaḥ na asti pratijñā-arthena ākṣipta-tvāt yaḥ eva
190.21	pratijñā-arthaḥ na pramāṇāni artham sādhayanti iti saḥ hetu-arthaḥ triṣu kāla^su
191.01	na artham sādhayanti iti/ yad punar etad pramāṇena pramīyamānaḥ arthaḥ prameyam iti
191.02	bhavati iti samākhyāyāḥ traikālya-yogāt apratiṣedhaḥ pramāṇa-prameyam iti
191.03	ca asyāḥ samākhyāyāḥ nimittam tri-kāla-yogin pramāsyate pramīyate
191.04	prāmāyi vā anena pramāṇam pramāsyate pramīyate prāmāyi vā iti prameyam/ evam
191.05	ca sati pramāsyate ayam arthaḥ bhaviṣyati asmin hetubhiḥ upalabdhiḥ iti prameyam
191.06	idam iti sidhyati anabhyanujñāne vyavahāra-artha-anupapattiḥ iti virodhaḥ
191.07	doṣaḥ/ yaḥ tu traikālikam pramāṇa-prameya-vyavahāram na pratipadyate tasya
191.08	pācakam ānaya iti ādi-vyavahāraḥ virudhyate iti/ pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam
191.09	traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti evam ādi-vākyam pramāṇa-pratiṣedhaḥ/ atha anena pratyakṣa-
191.10	ādīnām kim kriyate kim sambhavaḥ nivartyate atha asambhavaḥ jñāpyate
191.11	kim ca ataḥ yadi tāvat sambhavaḥ nivartyate tad na yuktam/ vyāghātāt
191.12	pratiṣedha-artha-aparijñānāt ca/ sambhavaḥ nivartyate iti evam bruvāṇena pramāṇāni
191.13	abhyanupagatāni bhavanti na hi asataḥ vṛtti-nivṛttiḥ bhavati iti/ na ca
191.14	pratiṣedhasya etad sāmarthyam yad vidyamānam padārtham anyathā kuryāt jñāpaka-tvāt ca
191.15	na sambhava-nivṛttiḥ jñāpakaḥ ayam pratiṣedhaḥ na sambhava-nivartakaḥ iti/
191.16	atha asambhavaḥ jñāpyate iti pramāṇa-lakṣaṇa-prāptaḥ tarhi pratiṣedhaḥ evam
191.17	traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ iti/ ayam api pratiṣedhah triṣu api
191.18	kāla^su na upapannaḥ iti ayam sūtra-arthaḥ vyāghātaḥ iti pramāṇāni na artham
191.19	sādhayanti iti pratiṣedhasya pramāṇa-lakṣaṇa-abhyanujñānāt iti traikālya-
191.20	asiddhi^aḥ pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ iti evam abhidadhānena abhyupagatam bhavati pratyakṣa-
191.21	ādīnām aprāmāṇyam traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ tasya sva-vacanasya vyāghāta-
191.22	ādeśanāt deśanāt sva-vacana-vyāghātaḥ mayā deśyate na abhyupagamyate katham
192.01	iti/ yad khalu triṣu api kāla^su na sādhakam tad asādhanam iti
192.02	bruvatā pratiṣedhasya asādhaka-tvam sva-vācā eva abhyupatam bhavati/ yathā kaḥ cit
192.03	sva-aṅguli-jvālayā param didhakṣuḥ saḥ ca param dahet vā na vā sva-aṅguli-
192.04	dāham anubhavati/ yaḥ ca ayam pratiṣedhaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīni na ayam sādhayanti iti
192.05	kim ayam pratyakṣa-ādīnām sāmarthya-pratiṣedhaḥ uta sattā-pratiṣedhaḥ iti/ yadi
192.06	sāmarthya-pratiṣedhaḥ na pratyakṣa-ādīni pratiṣiddhāni teṣām ca sāmarthya-
192.07	pratiṣedhāt pratyakṣa-ādīnām svarūpam vyaktavyam vaktavyam iti pūrvavat-prasaṅgaḥ/ atha
192.08	sattā-pratiṣedhaḥ tatra api katham na santi iti kim sāmānyataḥ atha viśeṣataḥ
192.09	iti/ yadi sāmānyataḥ pratyakṣa-ādi-grahaṇam vyartham evam vaktavyam na pramāṇāni
192.10	santi iti/ na ca dharmiṇām pratiṣedhe kaḥ cit nyāyaḥ iti/ atha
192.11	viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ tathā api pramāṇa-antara-abhyupagamaḥ na hi anabhyupagata-
192.12	sāmānyasya viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ avakāśam labhate iti/ pratyakṣa-ādīnām
192.13	aprāmāṇyam traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ pratyakṣa-ādīnām prāmāṇyam traikālya-siddhi^aḥ iti
192.14	vākyayoḥ eka-artha-tā vā syāt bhinna-artha-tā vā/ yadi eka-artha-tā pratyakṣa-
192.15	ādīnām prāmāṇyam traikālya-siddhi^aḥ iti evam eva vaktavyam abhinna-arthe ca ete
192.16	vākye kutaḥ pratipattiḥ iti yadi pramāṇataḥ anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ
192.17	atha anyataḥ tad pramāṇam iti saṃjñā-bheda-mātram/ atha bhinna-arthe tad kena
192.18	avagataḥ traikālya-siddhi^aḥ iti ca anayoḥ vākyayoḥ bhinnaḥ arthaḥ iti pratyakṣa-
192.19	ādīni na santi iti na asti na asamānādhikaraṇaḥ pratyakṣa-ādi-śabdaḥ ca na
192.20	pratyakṣa-ādīnām abhāvam śaknoti vaktum/ na hi ayam na asti na asamānādhikaraṇaḥ
192.21	ghaṭa-ādi-śabdaḥ ghaṭa-abhāvam pratipādayati api tu geha-ghaṭa-
192.22	saṃyogam vā kāla-viśeṣam vā ghaṭasya sāmarthyam vā pratiṣedhati/ evam
193.01	anena api pramāṇa-samānādhikaraṇena nāstinā pramāṇānām na abhāvaḥ
193.02	śakyaḥ pratipādayitum/ api tu anena api tantra-antara-abhyupagatānām pratiṣedhaḥ
193.03	syāt sāmarthya-pratiṣedhaḥ vā kāla-viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ vā iti sarvathā
193.04	pramāṇāni abhyupagatāni bhavanti tāni ca abhyupagatāni anena pratiṣedhena
193.05	pratiṣidhyante iti vyāghātaḥ/ pramāṇānām vā asattvam pratipādyamānam katham
193.06	kasya pratipādyate kaḥ ca pratipādayitṛ^ḥ/ apratipannaḥ pratipādyate iti/
193.07	pratipannaḥ ca pratipādayitṛ^ḥ kena pratipannaḥ yadi pramāṇena vyāghātaḥ
193.08	pramāṇa-anabhyupagamāt atha anyathā na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti pratipādya-pratipādakayoḥ
193.09	ca bheda-abhyupagame vyāghātaḥ yadi pratipādyam pratipādayitṛ^am ca
193.10	bhedena pratipadyase bheda-pratipādaka-pramāṇa-abhyupagamāt vyāghātaḥ/ atha bhedam
193.11	na pratipadyase kva kim pratipādyate iti anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ pramāṇāni
193.12	na santi iti pratijānānaḥ paryanuyojyaḥ katham na santi iti yadi pramāṇam
193.13	bravīti vyāhatam/ atha na bravīti arthaḥ na asya sidhyati tad evam
193.14	etad vākyam yāvat yāvat vicāryate tāvat tāvat pralīyate iti//
193.15	[2-1-13]sarva-pramāṇa-pratiṣedhāt ca pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ
193.16	sarva-pramāṇa-pratiṣedhāt ca pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ/ traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti asya
193.17	hetu^aḥ udāharaṇam upādīyate na vā yadi upādīyate vyāghātaḥ bhavati
193.18	pratyakṣa-ādīnām ca aprāmāṇyasya abhyupagamāt upādīyamānam api na artham sādhayati
193.19	iti vyartham/ atha sādhayati iti vyāghātaḥ atha na upādīyate
193.20	apradarśitam hetu-arthasya sādhaka-tvam iti apradarśita-dṛṣṭāntaḥ hetuḥ na sādhakaḥ
193.21	iti saḥ ayam hetuḥ sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ vipratiṣiddhaḥ bhavati siddhāntam
194.01	abhyupetya tad-virodhin^ḥ viruddhaḥ iti vākya-arthaḥ hi asya siddhāntaḥ saḥ ca
194.02	vākya-arthaḥ pramāṇāni na artham sādhayanti iti/ idam ca pramāṇānām upādānam
194.03	artha-sādhanāya pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti buddhi-
194.04	vivakṣā-prayatna-vāyu-udīraṇa-tālu-abhighāta-oṣṭhya-praspanda-ādīni avarjayan buddhi^ā
194.05	vākyam uccārayasi sthāṇu-kaṇṭaka-sarpa-ādīn pariharan pāda-viharaṇa-ādīni
194.06	karoṣi te ete vākya-pravṛttī pramāṇa-pratiṣedhāt na sambhavataḥ iti//
194.07	[2-1-14]tad-prāmāṇye vā na sarva-pramāṇa-vipratiṣedhaḥ
194.08	tad-prāmāṇye vā na sarva-pramāṇa-vipratiṣedhaḥ/ atha sva-vākya-āśrita-
194.09	pramāṇa-vyatirekena pramāṇāni pratiṣidhyante evam sati na sarvam pramāṇam
194.10	pratiṣiddham bhavati yaḥ ca nyāyaḥ sva-vākya-āśrita-pramāṇa-abhyupagame saḥ
194.11	eva anya-āśrita-pramāṇa-abhyupagame api iti vi iti ayam upasargaḥ viśeṣa-pratiṣedhe na
194.12	vyāghātaḥ iti kim uktam bhavati kāni cit pramāṇāni pratiṣidhyante
194.13	kāni cit abhyanujñāyante iti yāni sva-vākya-āśritāni tāni abhyanujñāyante
194.14	yāni para-vākya-āśritāni tāni pratiṣidhyante iti/ yad ca idam
194.15	vākyam pratyakṣa-ādīnām aprāmāṇyam traikālya-asiddhi^aḥ iti kim idam pramāṇa-
194.16	svarūpam atha na iti yadi pramāṇa-svarūpam tadā vyāghātaḥ atha na
194.17	tathā api na anena kaḥ cit arthaḥ pratipādyate iti vyartham//
194.18	[2-1-15]traikālya-apratiṣedhaḥ ca śabdāt ātodya-siddhi-vat tad-
194.19	siddhi^aḥ
194.20	traikālya-apratiṣedhaḥ ca śabdāt ātodya-siddhi-vat iti sūtram/ yad
194.21	tad-pūrvam uktam upalabdhi-hetu^aḥ upalabdhi-viṣayasya ca arthasya pūrva-apara-sahabhāva-
195.01	aniyamāt yathā-darśanam vibhāga-vacanam iti tad-utthāna-jñāpana-arthamataḥ sūtra-
195.02	antarasya utthānam iti/ kasmāt punar tatra eva idam sūtram na uktam/
195.03	aviśeṣāt tatra ca abhidhīyate iha cā na kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ iti sarvathā
195.04	tāvat ayam arthaḥ prakāśayitavyaḥ iti/ ekām ca vidhām udāharati śabdāt
195.05	ātodya-siddhivat iti śeṣayoḥ tu vidhayoḥ yathā-ukte udāharaṇe veditavye/
195.06	yathā pūrva-siddham ātodyam paścāt siddhena śabdena anumīyate vīṇā
195.07	vādyate veṇuḥ pūryyate iti yaḥ asau vīṇā-veṇu-śabdayoḥ asādhāraṇaḥ dharmaḥ
195.08	vīṇa-ādi-nimitta-tvam tad-upalabhamānaḥ śabdasya vīṇā-śabdaḥ veṇu-śabdaḥ
195.09	iti pratipadyate pūrva-siddham ātodyam paścāt siddhaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ sahabhāvasya
195.10	udāharaṇam dhūma-darśanāt agnim dhūma-viśeṣaṇam pratipadyate pūrva-siddhaḥ
195.11	upalabdhi-hetuḥ ādityaḥ paścāt siddhānām prakāśakaḥ iti  pramāṇam prameyam
195.12	iti ca iyam samākhyā samāveśena bhavati samākhyā-nimitta-vaśāt
195.13	samāveśaḥ niyamaḥ samākhyā-nimitta-vaśāt samākhyā-nimitta-tantra-tvāt
195.14	iti pramāṇa-prameya-saṃjñayoḥ nimittam upalabdhi-hetu-tvam upalabdhi-viṣaya-tvam ca/
195.15	yadā punar ekam aneka-nimitta-sambandhin bhavati tadā katham tadā
195.16	nimitta-tantra-tvāt samākhyāyāḥ tad vastu (anekena nimittena sambandhena
195.17	sati) aneka-śabda-abhilāpyam asya ca arthasya jñāpana-artham sūtram//
195.18	[2-1-16]prameyatā ca tulā-prāmāṇya-vat
195.19	prameyatā ca tulā-prāmāṇya-vat iti/ kim uktam bhavati yathā gurutva-
195.20	parijñāna-sādhanam tulā-dravyam samāhāra-gurutvasya iyattā-pariccheda-nimitta-
196.01	tvāt pramāṇam suvarṇa-ādinā ca paricchidyamāna-iyattā eṣā tulā iti pariccheda-
196.02	viṣaya-tvena vyavatiṣṭhamānā prameyam udāharaṇam ca etad evam anavayavena
196.03	tantra-arthaḥ veditavyaḥ yathā ca pramāṇa-prameya-samākhye tathā kāraka-
196.04	śabdāḥ sarve nimitta-samāveśa-niyamena vartante tathā ca laukikam
196.05	udāharaṇam/ ekam anekena kāraka-śabdena abhidhīyate vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati iti
196.06	sva-sthiti^i svātantryāt kartṛ^ḥ/ kim punar vṛkṣasya sva-sthiti^i svātantryam
196.07	kāraka-antara-anapekṣa-tvam n hi vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhan sva-sthiti^i kāraka-antaram
196.08	prayuṅkte iti yadā ayam sthiti-śabdaḥ gati-niṣedha-vacanaḥ bhavati tadā evam
196.09	yadā api asti^aḥ abhidhāyakaḥ tadā api asti^aḥ vyakti-nimitta-tvena avatiṣṭhamānaḥ asti vyanakti
196.10	iti kartṛ^ḥ iti ucyate/ yadā tu geha-sambandha-ādi-abhidhāyakaḥ bhavati asti-
196.11	śabdaḥ yathā gehe devadattaḥ tiṣṭhati iti pratinirdiṣṭaḥ asti iti gamyate
196.12	tadā na sattām āha gati-pratiṣedham/ api tu geha-sambandham tadā api
196.13	devadatta-geha-sambandha-anubhava-kriyā/ tasyām api na kāraka-antarāṇi prayuṅkte
196.14	iti svatantraḥ bhavati/ yadā tu ayam abhagna-vacanaḥ sthiti-śabdaḥ tiṣṭhati vṛkṣaḥ
196.15	na bhagnaḥ iti tadā api sva-sthiti^i na kāraka-antarāṇi prayuṅkte iti
196.16	svatantraḥ bhavati samudāya-ekadeśam vā sva-sthiti^i prayuṅkte vṛkṣaḥ mūlaiḥ tiṣṭhati
196.17	iti prāsādaḥ stambhaiḥ ghriyate iti pūrva-apara-pratyaya-eka-viṣaya-tā vā sthitiḥ
196.18	yad vastu prāc siddham punar nimitta-antara-sannidhāne sati upalabhyate yad
196.19	tasya aneka-ayugapad-kāla-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam sā sthitiḥ tasyām sthiti^i asya
196.20	yad sahakāri-sādhanam cakṣus-adi tad karaṇam iti cakra-bhrānti-pradīpa-pratyayavat
197.01	mithyā-pratyayaḥ iti cet/ na, pramāṇa-abhāvāt atha manyase na ekam kim cit vastu ayugapad-kāla-aneka-
197.02	pratyaya-viṣayaḥ bhavati yadi na bhavati katham tarhi saḥ eva ayam vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati
197.03	iti pratyayaḥ/ na hi kadā cit ādyena ca darśanena saḥ eva ayam vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati
197.04	iti pratyayaḥ bhavati dṛṣṭaḥ anyatve api sthiti-pratyayaḥ cakram bhramat tiṣṭhati
197.05	iti pradīpaḥ jvalat tiṣṭhati iti anavasthita^su nānā-bhūta^su cakra-bhrānti-
197.06	pradīpa-ādiṣu ye ete tad-bhāva-pratyayāḥ atasmin tad iti mithyā-pratyayāḥ
197.07	ete yathā ca ete mithyā-pratyayāḥ evam vṛkṣa-ādi-sthitiṣu api iti na pramāṇa-
197.08	abhāvāt na hi bhavatā pramāṇam kim cit vṛkṣa-ādīnām nānātva-pratipādakam
197.09	abhidhīyate mithyā-pratyayāḥ ca samyac-pratyaya-anusārena bhavanti iti kva amī
197.10	samyac-pratyayāḥ bhavanti iti vaktavyam/ atha bhavataḥ kāla-antara-avasthāne kim
197.11	pramāṇam uktam pramāṇam rūpa-rasa-sparśa-pratyayānām eka-aneka-pratyaya-nimitta-
197.12	tva-pratisandhānāt iti kāryasya ādhāra-tvena vā kāraṇasya kārya-kāla-anubhavanam
197.13	iti/ yadi asya ādhāraḥ bhavati tad tasya sadbhāva-kālam anubhavati yathā
197.14	kṣīrasya kuṇḍam/ kāraṇam ca kārya-adhikaraṇam ataḥ tena api kārya-kālaḥ anubhavanīyaḥ
197.15	iti/ anādhāram eva iti cet - atha manyase apratītya kāraṇam
197.16	kāryam utpadyate iti kva kim vartate iti tatra dṛṣṭānta-abhāvāt na hi
197.17	kaḥ cit ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ kāryam anādhāram asti iti kāryam
197.18	ādhāravat iti atra api na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti iti samānam/ samānam iti anuttaram
197.19	abhyupagamāt abhyupagatam tāvat bhavatā na asmad-pakṣe dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti kim tu
197.20	bhavat-pakṣe api na asti iti/ virodhaḥ iti cet atha manyase  na mayā
198.01	abhyupagamyate dṛṣṭānta-abhāvaḥ kim tu bhavat-pakṣe virodhaḥ deśyate katham iti
198.02	yaḥ aupapadyamāna-dṛṣṭāntaḥ saḥ pakṣaḥ asādhīyān iti evam bruvāṇaḥ sva-siddhāntam
198.03	bādhate tad na aparijñānāt na bhavatā asmad-pakṣaḥ vyajñāyi na sva-pakṣaḥ/
198.04	asmad-pakṣe tāvat kāryam ādhāravat iti etad prasiddham bhavat-pakṣe api
198.05	rūpa-ādyaḥ  kāryāḥ ca ādhāravantaḥ ca iti abhyupagama-virodhaḥ bhūta-bhautika-samudāyaḥ
198.06	dravyam iti yadi rūpa-ādīn anāśritān manyase kaḥ asya tad-hitasya arthaḥ
198.07	bhautikāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti yad ca uktam maṇiḥ prabhāyāḥ āśrayaḥ tad-gati-sthiti-
198.08	vikāra-anuvidhānāt kuṇḍam badarāṇām āśrayaḥ tad-vaśena āpātāt tailasya
198.09	tilaḥ āśrayaḥ tad-vaśena asyandanāt aspandanāt iti evam ādi tad vyāhanyate anāśritāḥ ca
198.10	rūpa-ādyaḥ  sva-tantrāḥ ekam upanibandhanam vastu-antarena tila-mudga-māṣa-ādivat vibhajyeran
198.11	na tu vibhajyante tasmāt para-tantrāḥ iti tasmāt vyavasthitam etad
198.12	smṛti^ā saha pūrva-apara-pratyaya-eka-viṣaya-tā vā sthitiḥ iti darśanena āptum
198.13	iṣyamāṇa-tvāt vṛkṣaḥ karman karmaṇi kaḥ kāraka-arthaḥ kriyā-viṣaya-tvam yad
198.14	khalu kriyā-viṣaya-bhāvena vyavatiṣṭhate iti etad karman etena karma-lakṣaṇena
198.15	tathā yuktam vā anīpsitam iti saṃgṛhītam ānantarya-pratipattiḥ karaṇasya
198.16	sādhakatama-tva-arthaḥ vṛkṣena candramasam jñāpayati iti vṛkṣam gṛhītvā anantaram
198.17	candramasam pratipadyate iti vṛkṣaḥ karaṇa-kāraka-śabda-vācyaḥ karmaṇā āptum abhīṣṭam
198.18	sampradānam yad vastu karmaṇā prāptum abhipretam bhavati tad sampradāna-saṃjñakam/
198.19	vṛkṣāya udakam āsiñcati iti āsicyamānam udakam karman tena karmaṇā udakena
198.20	vṛkṣam prāpnoti iti vṛkṣaḥ sampradānam bhavati iti apagacchati avatiṣṭhate yad
199.01	tad apādānam yathā vṛkṣāt patati iti vṛkṣa-upādāna-saṃjñakaḥ/ yad patati
199.02	tad apagacchati tasmin apagacchati avatiṣṭhate vṛkṣaḥ iti/ ghṛti-hetuḥ
199.03	adhikaraṇam yasmin yad ādheyam tad ādheyasya yad ghṛti-hetuḥ tad adhikaraṇam
199.04	vṛkṣe vayāṃsi iti vṛkṣa-sambandhaḥ vayasām gurutva-pratibandhe kāraṇam iti
199.05	vṛkṣaḥ adhikaraṇa-śabda-vācyaḥ/ evam ca sati na dravya-mātram kārakam na
199.06	kriyā-mātram kim uktam bhavati na ayam kāraka-śabdaḥ kriyā-mātre vartate
199.07	na dravya-mātre na hi dravyam śuddham upalabhamānaḥ kāraka-śabdam prayuṅkte na api
199.08	kriyām kevalām api tu yadā kriyā dravya-sambandham upalabhate tadā
199.09	sāmānyena kāraka-śabdena abhidhatte kārakam iti/ sāmānyam ca kārakāṇām
199.10	kriyā-nimitta-tvam avivakṣite viśeṣe kriyā-nimitta-tve kevale
199.11	vivakṣite kārakam iti śabdaḥ pravartate yadā tu viśeṣaḥ vivakṣitaḥ
199.12	tadā tu asādhāraṇena kāraka-dharmena itaretara-vyāvṛttena svātantrya-ādinā
199.13	viśeṣena viśiṣyamāṇam vastu kartṛ^ḥ karman karaṇam iti vyapadiśyate sarvasya
199.14	ca kārakasya sva-kriyā-nimittaḥ kartṛ-vyapadeśaḥ pradhāna-kriyā-savyapekṣaḥ tu
199.15	kāraka-śabda-prayogaḥ/ evam ca śāstram kārake janake nirvartake kasyāḥ
199.16	kriyāyāḥ iti tathā vyapadeśaḥ tasmāt na idam sva-kriyā-mātram apekṣya uktam
199.17	kim tu pradhāna-kriyām tasyām eva pradhāna-kriyāyām kim cit ārāt upakārakam
199.18	kim cit āsanna-upakārakam iti kim cit katham cit upakaroti tad-
199.19	apekṣāḥ ca kartṛ-ādi-śabdāḥ iti/ tad uktam purastāt/ yadi kriyā-sambandha-
199.20	nimittakaḥ kāraka-śabda-prayogaḥ pacati eva pācaka-śabdaḥ pravarteta tena na
199.21	apākṣīt pācakaḥ pakṣyati iti pācakaḥ na śakti^aḥ tri-kāla-avasthānāt
200.01	śaktiḥ triṣu api kāla^su avatiṣṭhate śakti-apekṣaḥ ca atīta-anāgatayoḥ kāraka-
200.02	śabda-vyapadeśaḥ pacati apākṣīt pakṣyati iti śakti^aḥ tri-kāla-avasthānāt tri-
200.03	kāla-vyaktiḥ ayuktā yathā eva dravyam tri-kāla-yogi tathā śaktiḥ api iti yathā
200.04	dravye traikālyam na vyajyate tathā śakti^i api iti apacati api pacati iti syāt
200.05	pakṣyati pācakaḥ apākṣīt pācakaḥ iti eva na syāt na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ kriyā-
200.06	sambandhasya śakti-vyakti-hetu-tvāt kriyā iti anena śabdena dhātu-arthaḥ abhidhīyate
200.07	tasya yad pradhāna-sādhanam saḥ kartṛ^ḥ saḥ ayam kartṛ^ḥ yadā kriyayā sambaddhyate
200.08	tadā śaktim vyanakti kā punar iyam śaktiḥ kriyā-kartṛ-vyatiriktaḥ kartṛ-dharmaḥ
200.09	sāmarthyam upāya-parijñānam ca kriyā-sambandhena vyavatiṣṭhate yadā upāya-vijñāna-
200.10	sāmarthya-kriyā-sambandhāḥ tadā mukhyataḥ kāraka-śabdaḥ pravartate pacati
200.11	pācakaḥ iti yadā tu kriyā-sambandham antareṇa upāya-parijñāna-sāmarthya-mātra-
200.12	upādāne anyatama-vaikalyāt pācaka-śabdaḥ pravartate tadā upacārataḥ apākṣīt
200.13	pācakaḥ pakṣyati pācakaḥ iti/ kāraka-śabdaḥ ca ayam pramāṇam prameyam iti
200.14	kutaḥ kriyā-viśeṣa-yogāt yathā eva amī pācaka-ādi-śabdāḥ kriyā-viśeṣa-yoge
200.15	sati pravartante tathā pramāṇa-prameya-śabdau api iti/ atha bhoḥ kāraka-
200.16	śabdānām nimitta-samāveśāt niyamaḥ pratyakṣa-ādīni pramāṇāni saṃvedyamānāni
200.17	prameyam upalabdhi-viṣaya-tvāt tatra idam cintyate/ kim etāni
200.18	pratyakṣa-ādīni sva-upalabdhi^i pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatiriktam pramāṇa-antaram (prasiddhi^aḥ
200.19	yadi pramāṇam sādhanam pramāṇa-antaram prāpnoti) prayojayanti atha
200.20	niḥsādhanā eva pramāṇa^su upalabdhiḥ iti//
201.01	[2-1-17]pramāṇataḥ siddhi^aḥ pramāṇānām pramāṇa-antara-siddhi-
201.02	prasaṅgaḥ
201.03	kim ca ataḥ pramāṇataḥ siddhi^aḥ pramāṇānām pramāṇa-antara-siddhi-prasaṅgaḥ
201.04	pramāṇa-viṣayāyāḥ siddhi^aḥ yadi pramāṇam sādhanam pramāṇa-antaram prāpnoti
201.05	pratyakṣa-ādīnām sādhya-tvāt na sādhyam sādhanam bhavati iti/ atha pramāṇa-antaram
201.06	antareṇa niḥsādhanā eva pramāṇa^su upalabdhiḥ//
201.07	[2-1-18]tad-vinivṛtti^aḥ vā pramāṇa-antara-siddhi-vat prameya-siddhiḥ
201.08	tad-vinivṛtti^aḥ vā pramāṇa-antara-siddhi-vat prameya-siddhiḥ/ yathā pramāṇāni
201.09	sva-upalabdhi^i nivartante evam prameya-siddhi^i api//
201.10	asya uttaram/
201.11	[2-1-19]na pradīpa-prakāśa-vat tad-siddhi^aḥ
201.12	na pradīpa-prakāśa-vat tad-siddhi^aḥ iti/ pradīpa-prakāśaḥ ghaṭa-
201.13	ādi-upalabdhi-sādhanasya aṅga-tvāt pramāṇam saḥ ca pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ eva upalabhyate
201.14	na asau ātma-upalabdhi^i pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatireki-pramāṇam prayojayati taiḥ eva
201.15	upalabhyate tathā pramāṇāni api pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ eva upalabhyante iti dṛṣṭānta-
201.16	mātram etad kaḥ atra nyāyaḥ iti/ ayam nyāyaḥ ucyate/ pratyakṣa-ādīni
201.17	sva-upalabdhi^i pramāṇa-antara-aprayojakāni pariccheda-sādhana-tvāt pradīpa-vat
201.18	yathā pradīpaḥ pariccheda-sādhanam sva-upalabdhi^i na pramāṇa-antaram prayojayati
201.19	iti tathā pramāṇāni tasmāt tāni api pramāṇa-antara-aprayojakāni iti
201.20	siddham/ sāmānya-viśeṣavat-tvāt ca yad sāmānya-viśeṣavat tad sva-upalabdhi^i
202.01	na pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatireki-pramāṇam prayojayati yathā pradīpaḥ iti saṃvedya-
202.02	tvāt yad saṃvedyam tad pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatireki-pramāṇa-antara-aprayojakam yathā
202.03	pradīpaḥ iti/ āśrita-tvāt karaṇa-tvāt vā iti evam ādi pradīpavat-indriya-ādyaḥ
202.04	api pratyakṣa-aṅga-tvāt pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatirikta-pramāṇa-antara-aprayojakāḥ iti
202.05	samānam/ tena eva tasya agrahaṇam iti cet/ atha manyase pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ
202.06	pratyakṣa-ādīnām upalabdhi^i tena eva tad eva gṛhyate iti prāptam na ca etad asti
202.07	na hi sādhyam sādhanam bhavati iti na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/ artha-bhedasya lakṣaṇa-sāmānyāt
202.08	pramāṇa-lakṣaṇena anekaḥ arthaḥ saṃgṛhītaḥ tatra kena cit kasya cit grahaṇam iti adoṣaḥ/
202.09	yathā uddhṛtena udakena āśaya-sthasya yathā uddhṛtam udakam gṛhītvā āśaya-stham api evam
202.10	bhūtam iti pratipadyate iti na punar evam abhidhīyate tena eva tasya grahaṇam
202.11	api tu tathā-bhūtena tathā-bhūtasya na ca ayam ekāntaḥ na tena eva tasya grahaṇam
202.12	bhavati iti bhavati api kadā cit yathā-ātmani saḥ eva jñātṛ^ḥ jñeyaḥ bhavati
202.13	yadā ātmānam jānīte iti/ nimitta-bhedaḥ atra iti cet atha manyase yadā
202.14	ayam ātman^ḥ jñātṛ^ḥ tadā anya-nimittam yadā jñeyaḥ tad anyad iti samānam
202.15	yathā eva jñātṛ^ḥ sukha-ādi-sambandha-apekṣaḥ tad viśeṣaṇam ātmānam pratipadyate aham
202.16	sukhin^ḥ aham duḥkhin^ḥ ca iti tathā pramāṇam api pramāṇa-viṣaya-tvena avatiṣṭhamānam
202.17	prameyam iti pratyakṣa-ādīnām ca aviṣayasya anupapatti^aḥ/ yadi kaḥ cit viṣayaḥ
202.18	pratyakṣa-ādi-viṣayaḥ syāt pratipadyemahi pratyakṣa-ādi-vyatiriktam pramāṇam
202.19	saḥ ca na śakyaḥ kena cit pratipādayitum sarvam idam sat ca asat ca pratyakṣa-
202.20	ādīnām eva viṣayaḥ tasmāt pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ eva upalabhyate/  apare tu
202.21	hetu-viśeṣa-parigraham antareṇa dṛṣṭānta-mātram pradīpa-prakāśa-sūtrena upādadate
202.22	yathā kila pradīpa-prakāśaḥ pradīpa-antara-prakāśam antareṇa gṛhyate tathā
203.01	pramāṇāni api pramāṇa-antaram antareṇa grahīṣyante iti tān prati idam ucyate//
203.02	[2-1-20(*)]kva cit nivṛtti-darśanāt anivṛtti-darśanāt ca kva cit anaikāntaḥ/ yathā pradīpa-
203.04	iti kaḥ atra niyama-hetuḥ/ sthāli-ādi-rūpa-prakāśane ca pradīpa-prakāśaḥ
203.05	na nivartate kasmāt evam na pramāṇāni iti ayam ca pradīpa-prakāśa-dṛṣṭāntaḥ
203.06	pramāṇa-pakṣe bhavatu mā bhūt prameya-pakṣe iti tatra api niyama-hetuḥ
203.07	vaktavyaḥ pradīpa-prakāśaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ bhavatu mā bhūt sthāli-adi-dṛṣṭāntaḥ
203.08	iti atra api niyama-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ saḥ ayam ubhayathā anekāntaḥ viśeṣa-hetum
203.09	antareṇa dṛṣṭāntaḥ prasaktaḥ viśeṣa-hetu-parigrahe sati upasaṃhāra-abhyanujñānāt
203.10	apratiṣedhaḥ yadi punar ayam pradīpa-prakāśaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ viśeṣa-hetunā prakāśatva-
203.11	ādinā saṃgṛhītaḥ/ tataḥ ekasmin pakṣe abhyanujñāyamānaḥ na śakyaḥ
203.12	pratiṣeddhum iti anekāntaḥ iti ayam doṣaḥ na bhavati pratyakṣa-ādīnām pratyakṣa-
203.13	ādibhiḥ upalabdhi^i anavasthā syāt iti cet yadi pratyakṣa-ādīni pratyakṣa-
203.14	ādibhiḥ upalabhyante tāni api anyaiḥ tāni api anyaiḥ iti anavasthā anavasthāyām vā ādya-
203.15	pramāṇa-adhigamaḥ na syāt/ atha vyavasthā asti ādya-pramāṇa-adhigatiḥ apramāṇam
203.16	prasajyet na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/ saṃvitti-viṣaya-nimittānām upalabdhyāḥ
203.17	vyavahāra-siddhi^aḥ saṃvitti-viṣayam saṃvitti-nimittam ca upalabhamānasya saṃvitti^aḥ
203.18	viṣayam prameyam saṃvitti-nimittam pramāṇam pramāṇa-prameyayoḥ upalabdhi^i sarvaḥ
203.19	vyavahāraḥ sidhyati dharma-artha-sukha-apavarga-prayojanaḥ tad-pratyanīka-parivarjana-prayojanaḥ
203.20	ca na tu anavasthā-sādhanīyam kim cit prayojanam asti yena ayam prayuktaḥ
204.01	anavasthām upādadīta/ tasmāt pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ pratyakṣa-ādīnām upalabdhiḥ na
204.02	ca anavasthā iti//
204.03	evam tāvat sāmānyataḥ samadhigatam etad asnti pramāṇāni prameya-
204.04	sādhanāni/ atha idānīm sāmānyataḥ adhigatānām viśeṣa-parīkṣā/ tatra
204.05	pratyakṣam ādi^i uktam tad parīkṣyate iti//
204.06	[2-1-21(20)]pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇa-anupapattiḥ asamagra-vacanāt
204.07	pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇa-anupapattiḥ asamagra-vacanāt/ yad idam pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam
204.08	ucyate indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti tad asamagram kasmāt anyāni
204.09	api pratyakṣasya kāraṇāni santi tāni na uktāni yathā ātma-manas-
204.10	saṃyogaḥ iti evam ādīni/ atha indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-utpannam iti etad lakṣaṇam iti
204.11	lakṣaṇam api etad na bhavati kasmāt utpatti-kāraṇa-apadeśāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-
204.12	utpannam iti etad kāraṇam apadiśyate na ca kāraṇa-apadeśaḥ vastunaḥ
204.13	lakṣaṇam/ na hi ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ kāryasya mṛd-piṇḍa-ādi-krama-utpattiḥ tad-lakṣaṇam iti
204.14	na ubhayathā api adoṣaḥ yadi kāraṇam tathā api na doṣaḥ atha lakṣaṇam tathā api
204.15	na doṣaḥ katham kāraṇa-pakṣe tāvat uktam bhavati na idam kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham
204.16	sūtram/ api tu pratyakṣasya pramāṇasya yad asādhāraṇam anumāna-ādi-vyavacchedakam
204.17	tad uktam iti/ na ātma-manas-saṃyoga-ādiṣu prasaṅgaḥ indriya-manas-
204.18	saṃyogaḥ tarhi upasaṃkhyeyaḥ yadi asādhāraṇa-tvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣasya grahaṇam/
204.19	na tu etasmāt eva indriya-manas-sannikarṣaḥ api upasaṃkhyeyaḥ iti uktam atra api na yāvat
204.20	asādhāraṇam tad sarvam vaktavyam/ api tu anyatra-grahaṇena api viśeṣitam
205.01	etad/ lakṣaṇa-pakṣe api na doṣaḥ asādhāraṇa-tvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣasya
205.02	indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ pratyakṣa-utpādaka-tvāt kāraṇam samāna-asamāna-jātīya^bhyaḥ
205.03	viśeṣaka-tvāt ca lakṣaṇam ca iti/  arthataḥ vā abhidhānam/ ātma-
205.04	manas-saṃyogasya indriya-manas-saṃyogasya ca katham iti jñāna-utpatti-darśanāt
205.05	na asaṃyukte dravye saṃyoga-jasya guṇasya utpattiḥ iti manas-saṃyoga-anapekṣam
205.06	ca indriyam pravartate iti yugapad-aneka-upalabdhiḥ syāt na bhavati ataḥ
205.07	manas-sannikarṣaḥ api kāraṇam iti/ tad idam sūtram ukta-bhāṣyam//
205.08	[2-1-22(21)]na ātama-manasoḥ sannikarṣa-abhāve pratyakṣa-utpattiḥ
205.09	na ātama-manasoḥ sannikarṣa-abhāve pratyakṣa-utpattiḥ iti/ ātma-manasoḥ
205.10	sannikarṣa-abhāve na utpadyate buddhiḥ indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-abhāva-vat iti/ na
205.11	ukta-uttara-tvāt ukta-uttaram etad iti na idam kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham sūtram api tu
205.12	viśiṣṭa-kāraṇa-vacanam iti/ ye ca sati bhāvāt kāraṇa-bhāvam varṇayanti
205.13	yasmāt kila indriya-artha-sannikarṣe sati jñānam bhavati asati na bhavati
205.14	tasmāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ kāraṇam iti//
205.15	[2-1-23(22)diś-deśa-kāla-ākāśa^su api evam-prasaṅgaḥ
205.16	teṣam diś-deśa-kāla-ākāśa^su api evam-prasaṅgaḥ/ diś-deśa-kāla-ākāśa^su
205.17	satsu api jñānam sambhavati sati bhāvāt tāni api kāraṇam iti prāptam/
205.18	katamena punar nyāyena diś-ādi-akāraṇam sāmarthya-anupalabdhi^aḥ na hi jñāna-utpatti^i
205.19	diś-ādi^aḥ sāmarthyam upalabhyate tasmāt akāraṇam sannidhi-mātrāt iti cet
205.20	atha manyase diś-ādi sannidhi-mātrena jñāna-utpatti^aḥ kāraṇam iti
206.01	tad ayuktam/ sannidhi^aḥ avarjanīya-tvāt/ nityam diś-ādi vyāpakam ca iti/
206.02	nitya-tvāt vyāpaka-tvāt ca na śakyaḥ sannidhiḥ parivarjayitum iti/ tatra
206.03	kāraṇa-bhāve hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ na hi sannidhi-mātram hetu-tve kāraṇam/ katham
206.04	rūpa-upalabdhi^aḥ tejas-sparśa-sadbhāva-vat iti/ yathā rūpa-upalabdhi^i tejasaḥ rūpa-
206.05	viśeṣaḥ hetuḥ na punar tejas-sparśaḥ sannidhi-mātrena sāmarthya-adarśanāt rūpasya
206.06	punar dṛṣṭam sāmarthyam tad-bhāva-abhāva-anuvidhānāt upalabdhi^aḥ yasmāt yatra rūpam bhavati
206.07	tatra upalabdhiḥ yatra na bhavati na tatra upalabhyate tasmāt rūpam eva upalabdhi^aḥ
206.08	kāraṇam na sparśaḥ iti/ yathā cāndramasam rūpam vinā tejas-sparśa-uṣṇena
206.09	prakāśayati/ apavaraka-ādi-sthitam ca auṣṇam vinā rūpena prakāśayati/
206.10	evam ca ātma-indriya-manas-artha-sannikarṣa^bhyaḥ jñānam bhavat na diś-ādibhyaḥ bhavati
206.11	sannidhi-mātra-tvāt/ itareṣām sāmartha-adarśanāt iti/  yadi evam ātma-manas-
206.12	saṃyogaḥ tarhi upasaṃkhyeyaḥ na upasaṃkhyeyaḥ gamyamāna-tvāt/ kena gamyamāna-tvāt//
206.13	[2-1-24(23)]jñāna-liṅga-tvāt ātmanaḥ na anavarodhaḥ
206.14	jñāna-liṅga-tvāt ātmanaḥ na anavarodhaḥ/ jñānam ātma-liṅgam iti uktam/
206.15	tena ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ anuktaḥ api anumeyaḥ katham punar jñānam ātma-liṅgam tad-guṇa-
206.16	tvāt/ guṇa-bhāva-siddhiḥ iti cet/ atha manyase guṇa-bhāvaḥ eva
206.17	jñānasya asiddhaḥ kutaḥ ātma-guṇa-tvam setsyati iti na ātma-samavāyāt
206.18	sukha-ādi-vat iti/ na hi dravyam karman vā ātmani samavaiti tasmāt na dravyam na
206.19	karman ātmani tu samavāyaḥ jñānasya sukha-ādi-vat tasmāt ātma-guṇaḥ na
206.20	pṛthivī-ādi-guṇaḥ sva-saṃvedya-artha-tvāt/ yad pratyakṣam sva-saṃvedyam na tad pṛthivī-
206.21	ādi-guṇaḥ yathā sukha-ādi yad ca pratyakṣam sva-para-saṃvedyam tad pṛthivī-ādi-guṇaḥ
206.22	yathā rūpa-ādi na ca evam jñānam tasmāt ātma-guṇaḥ iti/ uktam ca atra kim uktam
207.01	na yāvat pratyakṣasya kāraṇam sarvam tad vaktavyam iti/  atha indriya-manas-
207.02	sannikarṣaḥ kasmāt na abhidhīyate//
207.03	[2-1-25(24)]tad-ayaugapadya-liṅga-tvāt ca na manasaḥ
207.04	tad-ayaugapadya-liṅga-tvāt ca na manasaḥ/ yugapad-jñāna-anutpattiḥ manasaḥ
207.05	liṅgam iti uktam/ tena manasaḥ api avarodhaḥ iti na yugapad-utpatti-sūtrasya
207.06	anya-artha-tvāt yad idam ucyate yugapad-jñāna-anupapattiḥ manasaḥ liṅgam iti anena
207.07	jñāna-kāraṇam manas iti tad ayuktam anya-artha-tvāt/ anya-artham sūtram na punar tena
207.08	jñāna-kāraṇa-tvam manasaḥ abhidhīyate gamyate/ na hi jñānam svatantram anya-arthasya
207.09	api tad-artha-prakāśaka-tvam upapatti-sāmarthyāt yadi api tasmin sūtre manasaḥ
207.10	jñāna-kāraṇa-tvam na sākṣāt-uktam/ tathā api upapatti-sāmarthyāt jñāna-kāraṇam
207.11	manas iti gamyate/ na hi jñānam svatantram na cakṣus-ādi svatantram
207.12	iti/  atha kasmāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ abhidhīyate katham ca na ātma-
207.13	manas-sannikarṣaḥ abhidhīyate indriya-manas-sannikarṣaḥ vā iti//
207.14	[2-1-26(*)]pratyakṣa-nimitta-tvāt ca indriya-arthayoḥ sannikarṣasya sva-śabdena vacanam
207.15	/ indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ pratyakṣasya eva nimittam ātma-manas-sannikarṣaḥ
207.16	pratyakṣasya ca anyasya ca iti indriya-artha-sannikarṣena samāna-tvāt indriya-manas-
207.17	saṃyogaḥ vaktavyaḥ yadi sādhāraṇa-tvāt ātma-manas-sannikarṣaḥ na abhidhīyate/
207.18	indriya-manas-saṃyogaḥ asādhāraṇa-tvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-vat vaktavyaḥ
207.19	iti/ na idam kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham iti uktam/ na yāvat asādhāraṇam sarvam
207.20	tad vaktavyam api tu na sādhāraṇam vaktavyam iti ayam sūtra-arthaḥ//
208.01	[2-1-27(*)]supta-vyāsakta-manasām ca indriya-arthayoḥ sannikarṣa-nimitta-tvāt/ indriya-
208.02	artha-sannikarṣasya prādhānya-param idam sūtram/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
208.03	[2-1-28(25)]taiḥ ca apadeśaḥ jñāna-viśeṣāṇām
208.04	prādhānye ca hetu-antaram/ taiḥ ca apadeśaḥ jñāna-viśeṣāṇām/ yasmāt idam
208.05	pratyakṣa-jñānam utpadyamānam viṣayena kāraṇena vā vyapadiśyate yad ca asādhāraṇam
208.06	tena vyapadeśaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yathā śāli-aṅkuraḥ iti//
208.07	[2-1-29(26)]vyāhata-tvāt ahetuḥ
208.08	vyāhata-tvāt ahetuḥ/ vyāhata-tvāt iti anena tri-sūtrīm pratyācakṣe pratyācaṣṭe
208.09	pratyakṣa-nimitta-tvāt indriya-arthayoḥ sannikarṣasya, supta-vyāsakta-manasām ca
208.10	indriya-arthayoḥ sannikarṣa-nimitta-tvāt, taiḥ ca apadeśaḥ jñāna-viśeṣāṇām
208.11	iti iyam kila tri-sūtrī ātma-manas-sannikarṣasya jñāna-kāraṇa-tvam
208.12	pratiṣedhati iti vyāhatam idam bhavati/ jñāna-liṅga-tvāt ātmanaḥ na anavabodhaḥ anavarodhaḥ
208.13	tad-ayaugapadya-liṅga-tvāt ca na manasaḥ iti/ manas-saṃyoga-anapekṣāyām
208.14	yugapad-jñāna-utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ iti dṛṣṭa-vyāghātaḥ/ atha mā bhūt vyāghātaḥ iti
208.15	jñānam ātma-manas-sannikarṣāt utpadyate/ deśya-avastham eva etad kasmāt ātma-
208.16	manas-saṃyogaḥ na abhidhīyate atra uktam kim uktam na idam kāraṇa-avadhāraṇa-artham
208.17	sūtram api tu viśiṣṭa-kāraṇa-vacanam iti/  yā api iyam tri-sūtrī
208.18	pratyakṣa-nimitta-tvāt ca indriya-arthayoḥ sannikarṣasya iti evam ādi iyam api//
209.01	[2-1-30(27)]na artha-viśeṣa-prābalyāt
209.02	na artha-viśeṣa-prābalyāt/ na anayā tri-sūtrīā ātma-manas-sannikarṣasya
209.03	kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ pratiṣidyate api tu prādhānyam indriya-artha-sannikarṣasya
209.04	upapādyate/ prādhānyam ca upapādayatīā upapādayantīā na ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ
209.05	bhavati manasi kriyā-kāraṇa-abhidhānam supta-vyāsakta-manasām indriya-arthayoḥ
209.06	sannikarṣa-nimitta-tva-ādi-abhidhānena manas-saṃyogaḥ api kāraṇam iti uktam/
209.07	tatra manasi kriyā-kāraṇam vācyam iti/ atha sukha-duḥkha-upabhogasya
209.08	adṛṣṭa-nimitta-tvāt adṛṣṭam kāraṇam yathā eva ātmani guṇa-antaram icchā-janitam
209.09	prayatna-lakṣaṇam kriyā-hetuḥ tathā ātmani saṃskāraḥ dharma-adharma-samākhyātaḥ vartate
209.10	tena preritam manas saṃyujyate vibhajyate vā tena hi apreryamāṇe manasi
209.11	tataḥ anyasya kriyā-hetu^aḥ abhāvāt sarva-artha-tā tasya nivartate/ na hi
209.12	dharma-adharmau antareṇa manasi ca kaḥ cit anyaḥ kriyā-hetuḥ ādi^i sambhavati
209.13	iti ataḥ adṛṣṭam kāraṇam iti//
209.14	evam tāvat pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇam samāhitam ataḥ param idānīm pratyakṣasya
209.15	anumāne antarbhāvam kurvāṇaḥ idam āha//
209.16	[2-1-31(28)]pratyakṣam anumānam ekadeśa-grahaṇāt upalabdhi^aḥ
209.17	pratyakṣam anumānam ekadeśa-grahaṇāt upalabdhi^aḥ/ yad idam indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt
209.18	utpadyate jñānam ayam vṛkṣaḥ iti etad kila pratyakṣam tad anumānam
209.19	eva kasmāt ekadeśa-grahaṇāt vṛkṣa-upalabdhi^aḥ ekadeśam ayam gṛhītvā vṛkṣam
210.01	upalabhate na ca ekadeśaḥ vṛkṣaḥ tatra yathā anyasya darśanāt anya-pratipattiḥ anumānam
210.02	evam ekadeśa-grahaṇāt vṛkṣa-pratipattiḥ anumānam iti/ yathā dhūmam
210.03	gṛhītvā agni-pratipattiḥ evam ekadeśam gṛhītvā vṛkṣa-pratipattiḥ iti na vikalpa-
210.04	anupapatti^aḥ/ ekadeśa-grahaṇāt artha-antaram pratipadyate iti/ kim tad-
210.05	artha-antaram yad ekadeśa-grahaṇāt pratipadyate iti/ avayava-samūha-pakṣe tāvat
210.06	avayava-antarāṇi dravya-antara-utpatti-pakṣe tāni ca avayavin iti cet na yadi
210.07	tāvat avayava-samūha-mātram vṛkṣam adhikṛtya ucyate etasmin pakṣe gṛhyamāṇa-
210.08	avayava-vyatirekena yāni agṛhyamāṇa-anya-avayava-antarāṇi saḥ vṛkṣaḥ/ atha
210.09	dravya-antara-utpatti-pakṣe āśrīyate tathā api arvāc-bhāga-vyavahitāni avayava-
210.10	antarāṇi artha-antaram ca saḥ vṛkṣaḥ iti/ kim punar atra ekadeśa-grahaṇāt ayam
210.11	pratipadyate kim avayava-antarāṇi uta dravya-antaram iti/ avayava-samūha-pakṣe
210.12	tāvat vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ abhāvaḥ para-bhāgasya avṛkṣa-tvāt arvāc-bhāga-vat/ yathā eva ayam
210.13	arvāc-bhāgaḥ na vṛkṣaḥ evam madhya-para-bhāgau api na vṛkṣaḥ sā iyam avayava-samūha-
210.14	pakṣe avṛkṣe vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ utpadyamānā nirviṣayā arvāc-bhāgaḥ para-bhāgaḥ
210.15	madhya-bhāgaḥ iti etad ca na asti/ katham bhāgaḥ iti etad avayavasya etad nāma yaḥ
210.16	punar artha-antaram avayavinam na pratipadyate tasya avayavaḥ iti nirabhidheyam
210.17	vākyam avayavaḥ ca para-bhāgaḥ arvāc-bhāgaḥ saḥ ca avayavi-pratyākhyāne na
210.18	yuktaḥ iti/ atha ekadeśaḥ bhāgaḥ iti ucyate kaḥ ayam ekadeśaḥ ekadeśaḥ
210.19	hi avayavinaḥ ādhāraḥ avayavinam ca pratyācakṣāṇena ekadeśaḥ api pratyākhyātaḥ/
210.20	atha akārya-kāraṇa-vyāvṛttātām -bhūtānām paraspara-pratyāsatti-upagrahena ekadeśa-
210.21	avasthānam avayava-arthaḥ/ aho śabda-artha-kauśalam akārya-kāraṇa-bhūtāḥ ca
210.22	paraspara-pratyāsatti-upagrahena vartante iti/ na ca paraspara-upakāram kurvanti
211.01	avayavāḥ ca abhidhīyante iti citram/ apare tu arvāc-bhāga-darśanena para-
211.02	bhāga-anumānam kṛtvā pratisandhāna-pratyaya-jām avṛkṣe vṛkṣa-buddhim adhyavasyanti/
211.03	etasmin pakṣe vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ abhāvaḥ eva katham arvāc-bhāgasya para-bhāgasya
211.04	ca avṛkṣa-tvāt/ na ca avṛkṣa-pratisandhāna-jā vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ utpattum arhati/ na
211.05	ca etasmin pakṣe anumānam bhavati arvāc-bhāgam ayam gṛhītvā para-bhāgam
211.06	anumimīte iti na anumānasya viṣayaḥ jñātaḥ katham na jñātaḥ yadi tāvat
211.07	evam anumīyate vṛkṣaḥ ayam arvāc-bhāgavat-tvāt katamaḥ asau vṛkṣaḥ yasya arvāc-
211.08	bhāgavat-tvam dharmaḥ anabhyupagata-artha-antara-avayavinaḥ arvāc-bhāga-mātram pratyakṣam
211.09	na ca dharmiṇi anupalabdhe anumānam pravartate/ na ca ayam jātu api dharmiṇam
211.10	upalabhate avidhyamāna-tvena abhyupagamāt na ca anumānam anumāne evam
211.11	vidhasya pratisandhānasya abhāvāt na hi anumāne evam-vidham pratisandhanam asti
211.12	na ayam anumātṛ^ḥ pūrvam anupalabhya agnim dhūma-viśeṣaṇam pratipadya pratisandhatte
211.13	ayam dhūmaḥ ayam ca agniḥ hiti na ca pratisandhāya anumīyate agninā atra
211.14	bhavitavyam iti/ kim kāraṇam vaiyarthyāt/ vyartham anumānam etasyām avasthāyām
211.15	agni^aḥ viśeṣaṇa-bhūtasya pratīta-tvāt tasmāt na anumāne pratisandhānam asti
211.16	pratisandhāya api ca na samudāye pratisandhānam na ca vṛkṣe na hi ayam ekadeśa-
211.17	darśanāt samudāyam pratipadyate na vṛkṣam kim tu śeṣam samudāyinam eva/
211.18	kim kāraṇam samudāyi-vyatirekena samudāyasya anabhyupagamāt na
211.19	hi bhavantaḥ samudāyi-vyatiriktam samudāyam pratipadyante kiti na ca asat-
211.20	samudāyaḥ anumātum śakyaḥ/ kim ca arvāc-bhāgam ayam gṛhītvā kim anyat
211.21	anumimīte ayam arvāc-bhāgaḥ para-bhāgavān na yuktam anumātum arvāc-
212.01	bhāgasya para-bhāgavat-tva-asambhavāt na hi arvāc-bhāgaḥ para-bhāgavān bhāgasya
212.02	bhāgavat-tva asambhavāt na hi bhāgasya apara-bhāgaḥ vidyate iti/ atha
212.03	bhāgam bhāgavantam pratipadyase na saḥ bhāgaḥ bhāgavān bhāgin^ḥ eva asau
212.04	bhāgi-viṣayam ca anumānam iti ayuktam ubhayasya pratyakṣa-tvāt/ ubhayam hi atra
212.05	pratyakṣam arvāc-bhāgaḥ bhāgin^ḥ ca atra arvāc-bhāgavat-tvena bhāginaḥ para-
212.06	bhāga-anumānam asmad-pakṣe yuktam bhavat-pakṣe tu bhāga-mātra-darśanāt na yuktam
212.07	iti/ yad ca tad-arvāc-bhāga-darśanam na tasya liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-anugrahaḥ
212.08	asti sambaddhayoḥ ca arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgayoḥ adṛṣṭa-tvāt na hi ayam draṣṭṛ^ḥ
212.09	arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgayoḥ sambandham paśyati/ na arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgau sambaddhau
212.10	na ca anubhavam antareṇa smṛtiḥ na ca smṛtim antareṇa liṅga-darśanam kevalam
212.11	anumānam saḥ katham punar ayam arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgau sambaddhau na paśyati/
212.12	nityam arvāc-bhāgena para-bhāgasya ca vyavadhānāt nityam arvāc-bhāgam eva kevalam
212.13	upalabhate para-bhāgam tu na katham cana arvāc-bhāga-vyavahita-tvāt/ satyam/
212.14	na ubhayoḥ upalabdhiḥ asti ubhau ca sambaddhau satyam sambaddhau anumānam tu aśakyam
212.15	ubhayoḥ adarśanāt/ arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgayoḥ ca dharma-dharmi-bhāva-anupapatti^aḥ na anumānam
212.16	yatra khalu anumānam bhavati tatra dharmiṇaḥ dharma-prasiddhi^i dharma-antara-viṣayam
212.17	anumānam na punar arvāc-bhāgasya para-bhāgaḥ dharmaḥ para-bhāgasya na arvāc-
212.18	bhāgaḥ tataḥ ca na anumānam/ yad ca idam ucyate pratisandhāna-pratyaya-jā vṛkṣa-
212.19	buddhiḥ iti tad ayuktam vṛkṣasya asiddha-tvena abhyupagamāt na pratisandhānam
212.20	pratisandhānam hi nāma pūrva-pratyaya-anurañjitaḥ pratyayaḥ piṇḍa-antare bhavati/
212.21	yathā rūpam mayā upalabdham ramaḥ ca iti bhavat-pakṣe punar arvāc-bhāgam gṛhītvā para-
213.01	bhāgam anumāya arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāgau iti etāvān pratisandhāna-pratyayaḥ yuktaḥ
213.02	vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ tu kutaḥ na tāvat arvāc-bhāgaḥ vṛkṣaḥ na para-bhāgaḥ iti/ arvāc-bhāga-
213.03	para-bhāgayoḥ ca avṛkṣa-bhūtayoḥ yā vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ sā atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ na
213.04	anumānāt bhavitum arhati iti pramāṇasya yathā-bhūta-arthasya paricchedaka-tvāt/
213.05	yaḥ ca ayam atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ saḥ pradhāna-anukārena bhavati iti vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ
213.06	pradhānam vaktavyam na hi agṛhīta-sāmānyasya anadhyāropita-tad-viparīta-
213.07	dharmasya vā atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ bhavati pradhānam ca na asti vṛkṣasya
213.08	anabhyupagamāt/ tasmāt na anumāna-pratisandhānāt vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ/ etena
213.09	arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāga-pratyayāḥ api pratyuktāḥ/ anabhyupagatau anabhyupagata-antya-avayavinaḥ
213.10	ca arvāc-bhāgaḥ para-bhāgaḥ iti etad api na asti kasmāt mahataḥ dravyasya upalabdhi-
213.11	lakṣaṇa-prāptasya para-bhāga-vyavadhāyakasya etad nāma arvāc-bhāgaḥ iti tathā-bhūtasya
213.12	ca vyavahitasya para-bhāgaḥ iti/ na ca bhavatā kim cit dravyam avayavi-
213.13	bhūtam pratipadyate yad arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāga-śabda-vācyam syāt iti/ paramāṇuṣu
213.14	tathā-sanniviṣṭa^su arvāc-bhāga-para-bhāga-vṛkṣa-ādi-pratyayāḥ iti cet atha manyase
213.15	na asmābhiḥ avayavi-dravyāṇi kāni cit pratipadyante kim tu teṣu eva paramāṇuṣu
213.16	paraspara-pratyāsatti-upasaṃgrahena saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-avasthita^su te śabdāḥ
213.17	pravartante arvāc-bhāgaḥ para-bhāgaḥ iti vṛkṣaḥ iti ca na tathā-śabdasya artha-antara-
213.18	anabhidhānāt tathā-sanniviṣṭa^su tathā-abhidhānam pravartate pūrva-prasiddhi^i
213.19	satyām na ca paramāṇuṣu pūrva-apara-ādi-śabda-pravṛtti^aḥ bījam asti yathā prasiddhe
213.20	gavi avayaḥ tathā-rūpaḥ iti/ evam tāvat avayava-samūha-pakṣe vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ abhāvaḥ
213.21	iti uktam dravya-antara-utpatti-pakṣe tu na avayavi-anumeyaḥ pratyakṣa-tvāt arvāc-bhāga-
214.01	vat iti yathā eva ayam arvāc-bhāgam indriya-sambaddham pratyakṣataḥ upalabhate tathā
214.02	vṛkṣam api iti na anumeyaḥ vṛkṣaḥ/ atha indriya-sambaddham api vṛkṣam anumeyam eva
214.03	pratipadyate vṛkṣa-vat arvāc-bhāgaḥ api anumeyaḥ syāt tathā ca vyāhatam bhavati
214.04	arvāc-bhāgam ayam gṛhītvā vṛkṣam pratipadyate iti sarvam eva anumānam iti
214.05	tasmin pakṣe anumāna-abhāvaḥ pratyakṣataḥ na adhigata-tvāt dharmiṇaḥ na dharmiṇi
214.06	pratyakṣataḥ anadhigate anumānam pravartate/ yad api atīndriya^su artha^su anumānam uktam
214.07	tad na asti na hi atīndriya-arthaḥ anumātum śakyaḥ katham iti dharmiṇaḥ anadhigata-
214.08	tvāt iti uktam/ kaḥ punar sāmānyataḥ dṛṣṭasya pratyakṣa-pūrvaka-anumānāt viśeṣaḥ
214.09	yadi sarvam pratyakṣa-pūrvakam eva anumānam iti ayam viśeṣaḥ pratyakṣa-pūrvakam
214.10	trika-prasiddhi^i pravartate sādhya-sādhana-dharmi-upalabdhi^i yatra ayam sādhyam sādhanam ca
214.11	dharmiṇam ca pūrvam gṛhītvā punar dharma-dharmi-darśanena dharma-antaram anumimīte tad
214.12	pratyakṣa-pūrvakam yad punar sādhana-dharmam dharmiṇam ca upalabhy atyanta-parokṣam
214.13	dharma-antaram anumimīte tad sāmānyataḥ dṛṣṭam iti/ bhavat-pakṣe tu na
214.14	sāmānyataḥ dṛṣṭāt anumānāt vṛkṣa-buddhiḥ na pratyakṣa-pūrvakāt dharmiṇaḥ anabhyupagamāt
214.15	pratyakṣa-pratiṣedhāt ca iti/  viruddhaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ ekadeśa-grahaṇāt vṛkṣa-upalabdhi^aḥ
214.16	iti/ katham iti//
214.17	[2-1-32(29)]na pratyakṣena yāvat tāvat api upalambhāt
214.18	na pratyakṣena yāvat tāvat api upalambhāt/ pratyakṣam anumānam iti evam
214.19	bruvatā pratyakṣam apahnūyate pratyakṣa-apahnavāt ca yad ekadeśa-grahaṇam pratyakṣam
214.20	tad vyāhatam bhavati/ katham iti/ yad tad ekadeśa-grahaṇam bhavatā āśrīyate
214.21	pratyakṣam tad tāvatā api ca pratyakṣasya lakṣaṇam sidhyati/ na ca evam pratijñāyate
214.22	sarvam pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ kim tu yāvat indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt upalabhyate
215.01	tāvat pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ iti anyathā api na pratyakṣam anumānam
215.02	aprakāravat-tvāt prakāravat anumānam aprakāravat pratyakṣam uktam anumāna-
215.03	sūtram varṇayadbhiḥ iti/ tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvāt ca anumānam na pratyakṣam tri-kāla-
215.04	viṣayam anumānam na tu evam pratyakṣam iti liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-anugraha-
215.05	apekṣa-tvāt ca na anumānam pratyakṣam liṅga-parāmarṣa-jñānasya liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-
215.06	smṛti-anugrahaḥ asti na ca evam pratyakṣasya tasmāt na pratyakṣam anumānam/ itaḥ ca
215.07	pratyakṣasya na anumāna-tva-prasaṅgaḥ tad-pūrvaka-tvāt pratyakṣa-pūrvakam anumānam na
215.08	tu pratyakṣam ayam ca anayoḥ viśeṣaḥ iti indriya-sambandhāt ca/ na indriyasya
215.09	anumeyena arthena sambandhāt utpadyate jñānam yad tad anumānam iti ucyate/
215.10	saḥ ayam pramāṇa-dṛṣṭaḥ pratyakṣa-anumānayoḥ eka-tvam āsañjayatā bhedaḥ pratyākhyāyate
215.11	iti//
215.12	[(2-1-30)]na ca ekadeśa-upalabdhiḥ avayavi-sadbhāvāt
215.13	na ca ekadeśa-upalabdhiḥ avayavi-sadbhāvāt/ yad idam bhavatā abhidhīyate
215.14	ekadeśa-grahaṇāt ayam vṛkṣaḥ pratipadyate iti tad anupapannam yasmāt ekadeśa-
215.15	upalabdhiḥ ca tad-sahacarita-avayavi-upalabdhiḥ ca na hi indriyena sannikṛṣyamāṇe
215.16	ekadeśe tad-sahacaritaḥ avayavin^ḥ na sannikṛṣṭaḥ tena yathā ekadeśaḥ sannikarṣāt
215.17	upalabhyate evam avayavin^ḥ api sannikarṣāt upalabhyate iti/ kasmāt avayavi-
215.18	sadbhāvāt asti hi ayam ekadeśa-vyatiriktaḥ avayavin^ḥ tasya avayava-sthānasya
215.19	upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptasya ekadeśa-upalabdhi^i anupalabdhiḥ anupapannā kim kāraṇam
215.20	mahat-aneka-dravya-rūpa-ādi-upalabdhi-kāraṇāni tāni ekadeśavat-ekadeśini api
216.01	vidyante tasmāt ekadeśaḥ upalabhyate na ekadeśin iti ayuktam/ na avayavinaḥ
216.02	upalabdhiḥ yuktā kasmāt vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ ayam khalu avayava^su vartamānaḥ
216.03	ekasmin kārtsnyena vā vartate ekadeśena vā/ yadi ekasmin kārtsnyena
216.04	vartate śeṣa-avayava-vaiyarthyam na hi śeṣaiḥ avayavaiḥ kim cana avayavinaḥ kriyante
216.05	ekadravyaḥ ca asau avayavin^ḥ tāvat na upalabhyate kim kāraṇam mahat-aneka-dravyavat-
216.06	tvasya upalabdhi-kāraṇa-tvāt na ca asya vināśaḥ prāpnoti kāraṇa-
216.07	dravya-vibhāga-asambhavāt na hi ekasya vibhāgaḥ sambhavati/ na ca ekena ārabhyamāṇasya
216.08	mahattvam yuktam kāraṇa-bahutva-mahattva-pracaya-asambhavāt/ tasmāt na
216.09	ekasmin kārtsnena vartate iti/ na api ekadeśena na hi asya kāraṇa-vyatirekena
216.10	anye ekadeśāḥ sambhavanti saḥ ayam ekadeśa-upalabdhi^i avayavi-upalabhyamānaḥ
216.11	na kṛtsnaḥ upalabhyate sā iyam ekadeśa-upalabdhiḥ anivṛttā eva iti/ atha
216.12	upalabhyamānaḥ avayavin^ḥ katham upalabhyeta yadi gṛhyamāṇa^su avayava^su kṛtsnaḥ
216.13	parisamāptaḥ syāt na ca ayam gṛhyamāṇa^su parisamāptaḥ śeṣa-avayava-vaiyarthya-
216.14	prasaṅgāt iti uktam/ aśeṣa-avayava-upalabdhiḥ api na asti madhya-bhāga-para-
216.15	bhāgayoḥ arvāc-bhāgena vyavadhānāt tena avayavinaḥ na grahaṇa-vikalpaḥ
216.16	sambhavati na api vṛtti-vikalpaḥ ekasmin kṛtsna-ekadeśa-śabda-asambhavāt
216.17	apraśnaḥ kim avayavin^ḥ ekadeśena vartate atha kṛtsnena eva vartate iti na
216.18	yuktaḥ praśnaḥ na avayavin^ḥ kṛtsnaḥ na ekadeśaḥ/ kṛtsnam iti khalu ekasya aśeṣasya
216.19	abhidhānam/ ekadeśaḥ iti ca aneka-tve sati kasya cit abhidhānam/
216.20	tau imau kṛtsna-ekadeśa-śabdau ekasmin anupapannau katham tarhi avayava^su avayavin^ḥ
216.21	vartate iti/ āśraya-āśrayi-bhāvena āśritaḥ avayavin^ḥ āśrayāḥ avayavāḥ
217.01	iti/ kā punar iyam vācaḥ yuktiḥ na ekadeśena vartate iti ca na kṛtsnaḥ
217.02	vartate atha ca vartate iti/ yad yathā-bhūtam tad tathā-bhūtam nirdiśyate iti eṣā
217.03	eva vācaḥ yuktiḥ/ kṛtsna-ekadeśa-śabdau na avayavini staḥ kṛtsnasya eka-tvāt
217.04	iti uktam/ tasmāt āśraya-āśrita-dharmi-nirdeśa-mātram avayavin^ḥ avayava^su vartate
217.05	iti/ kā punar iyam vṛttiḥ ekasya anekatra āśraya-āśrita-bhāva-lakṣaṇā
217.06	prāptiḥ api ca ekaḥ anekatra vartate iti pratijānānaḥ na anuyoktavyaḥ
217.07	kasmāt ubhayena vyāghātāt/ ekam anekatra vartate iti bruvāṇaḥ kim
217.08	ekadeśena vartate uta sarva-ātmanā iti anuyoktavyaḥ kasmāt ubhayena
217.09	vyāghātāt/ yadi ekam anekatra vartamānam pratyavayavam sarva-ātmanā vartate na ekam
217.10	anekatra vartate anekam anekatra vartate iti āpannam evam ca anuyoge adhikaraṇa-
217.11	vyāghātaḥ/ atha ekam anekatra vartamānam pratyavayavam ekadeśena vartate
217.12	tathā api na ekam anekatra vartate iti prāptam anekam anekatra vartate iti
217.13	āpannam ye ca tatra ekadeśāḥ pratyekam avayava^su vartante te avayavinaḥ iti
217.14	prāptam/ ekasmin pakṣe na ekam anekatra vartate kim tarhi anekam anekatra iti/
217.15	atha pratyekam parisamāpti^ā vartate iti ayam arthaḥ na ekatra vartate iti
217.16	tathā api anuyoga-adhikaraṇam nivartate iti/ yathā khalu ayam arthaḥ anekam anekatra
217.17	vartate iti saḥ eva arthaḥ pratyekam parisamāpti^ā vartate iti sarvathā anuyoga-
217.18	adhikaraṇam nivartate iti/ anuyogasya adhikaraṇa-bādhaka-tvāt ayam
217.19	khalu anuyogaḥ kriyamāṇaḥ adhikaraṇam bādhate/ ataḥ dharma-vikalpa-bhāvena
217.20	na vartate/ na ayam vikalpaḥ kva cit āśrayavān iti ataḥ na anuyoktavyaḥ iti/
218.01	evam sarva^su aneka-dravya-vṛttiṣu saṃkhyā-pṛthaktva-saṃyoga-vibhāga-sāmānya-ādiṣu
218.02	anuyoga-adhikaraṇa-vyāvṛtti-doṣaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ grahaṇa-agrahaṇa-bhedāt bhedaḥ avayavavat
218.03	iti cet yathā gṛhyamāṇānām agṛhyamāṇānām ca avayavānām bhedaḥ
218.04	tathā avayavinaḥ api gṛhyamāṇa-agṛhyamāṇa-avayava-āśritasya bhedaḥ prāptaḥ tataḥ ca
218.05	na ekaḥ avayavin^ḥ na bhedaḥ ekasmin api darśanāt ekasmin api gṛhyamāṇe
218.06	grahaṇam agrahaṇam ca dṛṣṭam devadatta-ādi-vat iti/ yathā devadatta-ādiḥ
218.07	kena cit saha gṛhyate kena cit ca saha na gṛhyate na tāvatā tasya bhedaḥ/
218.08	tathā avayavinaḥ api iti adoṣaḥ gṛhīta-tvāt ca yad idam bhavatā abhidhīyate ekadeśa-
218.09	grahaṇāt etasya grahaṇam ekadeśa-agrahaṇāt ca agrahaṇam iti ayam anāspadaḥ vikalpaḥ
218.10	avayavinaḥ gṛhīta-tvāt/ ekadeśa-sahacaritaḥ avayavin^ḥ gṛhītaḥ
218.11	kim asya agṛhītam yena asya grahaṇa-agrahaṇa-vikalpaḥ āspadavān bhavet/
218.12	madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgau na gṛhītau iti cet na tayoḥ avayavinaḥ anya-tvāt
218.13	anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ anyau madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgau iti/ avayavinaḥ anya-tvāt avayavinaḥ
218.14	madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgau na gṛhyete iti/ kim kāraṇam/ grahaṇa-
218.15	hetu-abhāvāt/ yathā arvāc-bhāgena saha avayavinaḥ indriya-prāptiḥ upalabdhi-
218.16	nimittam asti na evam madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgābhyām saha tasmāt arvāc-bhāgena
218.17	saha upalabhyate na madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgābhyām iti avayava-samūha-pakṣe tu
218.18	sarvathā vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ abhāvaḥ katham mūla-skandha-śākhā-palāśānām samūhaḥ vā
218.19	vṛkṣaḥ syāt prāptiḥ vā samudāyinām ubhayathā ca vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ abhāvaḥ/
218.20	avayavaiḥ avayava-antara-vyavadhānāt aśeṣa-grahaṇam na asti/ prāpti-grahaṇam ca ayuktam/
219.01	prāptimatām agrahaṇāt/ na hi prāptimati agṛhyamāṇe prāpti-grahaṇam asti
219.02	bhavati hi idam anena saṃyuktam iti/ sā iyam ekadeśa-grahaṇa-sahacaritā vṛkṣa-
219.03	buddhiḥ dravya-antara-utpatti-pakṣe kalpate na samudāya-mātre iti//
219.04	[2-1-33(32)]sādhya-tvāt avayavinai sandehaḥ
219.05	sādhya-tvāt avayavinai sandehaḥ/ kāraṇa^bhyaḥ dravya-antaram utpadyate iti
219.06	sādhyam etat/ kim punar atra sādhyam/ kim avyatirekaḥ atha avayavin^ḥ iti/
219.07	yadin avayavin^ḥ na liṅgam asti dharmiṇi vipratipatti^aḥ/ na hi dharmiṇi
219.08	vipratipadyamānaḥ kutaḥ cit abhyanujñām labdhum arhati/ na ca dharmiṇi vipratipatti^aḥ
219.09	bījam asti sādṛśya-abhāvāt/ na hi sādṛśyam antareṇa vipratipatti^aḥ
219.10	bījam asti/ kim kāraṇam/ vipratipattiḥ nāma viparītā pratipattiḥ
219.11	tasyāḥ ca kāraṇam samāna-dharma-upalabdhi^i anavagata-viśeṣasya tad-viparīta-
219.12	dharma-adhyāropaḥ na ca anabhyupagata-avayavinaḥ avayavini sādṛśyam kva cit asti
219.13	na api tad-viparīta-dharma-adhyāropaḥ tasmāt na vipratipattiḥ/ atha avyatirekaḥ
219.14	sādhyaḥ tatra avyatireka-pratipādakam pramāṇam vaktavyam/ na hi aprāmāṇikī
219.15	kā cit pratipattiḥ asti idam pramāṇam artha-antara-anavayava-tvam artha-
219.16	antaram artha-antarasya avayavaḥ na bhavati yathā an go^ḥ aśvasya avayavaḥ avayavāḥ ca
219.17	tantu^āḥ avayavin^ḥ ca paṭaḥ tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram iti/ idam tāvat
219.18	yathā-śruti vākyam hetu-hīnam na hi atra hetuḥ asti iti/ nanu ca upanayena
219.19	vyajyamānaḥ hetuḥ avayavatvam asti na artha-antaram paṭāt tantu^āḥ tad-avayava-tvāt iti
219.20	na vyāghātāt/ artha-antara-bhāve na avayavatvam siddhyati tantūnām iti/
220.01	kim kāraṇam/ avayavi-apekṣa-tvāt/ avayavi-apekṣaḥ avayavaḥ na ca avayavi-
220.02	pratyākhyāne kim cit bījam asti artha-antare ca darśanāt viruddhaḥ
220.03	syāt dṛṣṭaḥ avayavaḥ artha-antara-bhāve na anartha-antara-bhāvaḥ iti/ yathā tantu^āḥ
220.04	ghaṭāt artha-antaram iti/ ṣaṣṭhī-viśeṣaṇāt aprasaṅgaḥ iti cet paṭasya avayavāḥ
220.05	iti ukte ghaṭa-ādiṣu kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ/ nanu atra uktam kim vyāghātāt iti/ na
220.06	hi anartha-antara-bhāve avayavaḥ asti na ca ṣaṣṭhī na ca tantuḥ tantu^aḥ avayavaḥ/
220.07	na api ṣaṣṭhī-arthaḥ tantu^aḥ tantuḥ avayavaḥ iti utpatti^ā avayava-tvam tantūnām tasya
220.08	na anvayaḥ na vyatirekaḥ iti asādhāraṇa-tvāt ahetuḥ anartha-antaram paṭāt
220.09	tantu^āḥ iti ca tantūn pakṣa^kṛtya tad-avayava-tvāt iti bruvāṇaḥ tantūnām
220.10	tantu-avayava-tvam bravīti/ etat ca viruddham na tantu^āḥ tantūnām avayavāḥ iti/
220.11	atha paṭaḥ pakṣa-kriyate tathā api vyadhikaraṇaḥ hetuḥ avayavatvasya
220.12	tantu-dharma-tvāt tantu-dharmaḥ avayavatvam na paṭa-dharmaḥ iti/ avayava-śabdasya
220.13	ca kāraṇa-viśeṣa-vācaka-tvāt/ asati avayavini vyāghātaḥ/ yadi avayavinam
220.14	artha-antaram na pratipadyase kasya tantu^āḥ kāraṇam na hi kāraṇam
220.15	ātmānam nirvartayati iti na hi naḥ kim cit nirvartyam asti te eva tantu^āḥ
220.16	saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-avasthitāḥ paṭa-ākhyām labhante iti/ apaṭa-ākhyāḥ tantu^āḥ
220.17	paṭa-śabdena abhidhīyante iti uktam bhavati/ etat ca viruddham mukhya-asambhavāt
220.18	na hi tantūnām apaṭānām paṭena kim cit sāmānyam asti/ na ca asati
220.19	sāmānye mithyā-pratyayasya bījam asti iti uktam/ yad ca idam ucyate saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-
220.20	avasthānam it kim tad-artha-antaram āho na iti/ yadi artha-antara-bhūtam kim
220.21	tad iti vaktavyam atha na cyate śūnyam tarhi idam vākyam saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-
221.01	avasthānam iti/ asmākam tu saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ saṃyogaḥ sa ca artha-antaram
221.02	na anabhyupagamāt na mayā artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ pratipādyate anya-asaṃyogāt iti/
221.03	anyat anyasya saṃyogaḥ na bhavati/ yathā go^ḥ aśvasya na saṃyogaḥ/ aṅguli^oḥ
221.04	astu saṃyogaḥ tasmāt na artha-antaram iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt ukta-uttaram etat kim
221.05	iti artha-antara-anartha-antarayoḥ vyāvṛtti^aḥ saṃyogaḥ asādhāraṇaḥ pratijñā-ekadeśaḥ ca
221.06	na artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ saṃyoga-tvāt iti/ viśeṣaṇa-bhāvāt ca artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ
221.07	saṃyukte dravye āhara iti ukte yayoḥ dravyayoḥ saṃyogam paśyati te itarebhyaḥ viśiṣya
221.08	saṃyukte āharati na viśeṣaṇasya anyathā-siddha-tvāt/ yad idam bhavatā
221.09	abhidhīyate saṃyukte dravye āhara iti viśeṣaṇa-bhāvāt artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ
221.10	iti tad-anyathā sambhavati katham pratyāsatti^i pratīghāta-avasānāyām
221.11	saṃyoga-vyavahāraḥ tāvat dravyāṇi pratyāsīdanti yāvat pratihatāni bhavanti
221.12	tasmin pratighāte saṃyoga-vyavahāraḥ na artha-antaraḥ iti anabhyupagata-artha-
221.13	antara-saṃyogena pratyāsatti-pratīghātau vaktavyau tatra saṃyukta-saṃyoga-alpīyas-tvam
221.14	pratyāsattiḥ mūrta-sparśavat-dravya-saṃyogaḥ pratīghātaḥ yaḥ punar saṃyogam na pratipadyate
221.15	tena pratyāsatti^aḥ pratighātasya ca arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ iti/ yadi ca artha-
221.16	antaram saṃyogaḥ na syāt kṣetra-udaka-bīja-agni-indhana-ādīni sarvatra avasthitāni
221.17	sarvatra aṅkura-pāka-ādi-kāryam kuryuḥ tāni eva tāni iti/ tasmāt yaḥ taiḥ
221.18	apekṣyate saḥ arthaḥ anyaḥ tasmin saṃyogaḥ iti saṃjñā iti/ kṣetra-udaka-bīja-agni-
221.19	indhanāni aṅkura-pāka-ādi-kārya-utpatti^i sāpkṣāṇi sarvadā tad-kārya-
221.20	anārambhāt daṇḍa-ādi-vat yathā daṇḍa-ādi-anekam kāraṇam saṃyoga-ādi-nimitta-
221.21	antara-apekṣam sarvadā na ghaṭa-paṭa-ādi-kāryam karoti tathā bīja-ādi api
221.22	tasmāt tad api sāpekṣam iti/ yad tu atra parena ucyate kārya-utpattiḥ vinā
222.01	tena śabda-ayaskānta-karma-vat iti/ asya arthaḥ yathā kila śabdaḥ śabda-
222.02	antaram ārambhamāṇaḥ vinā saṃyogam ārabhate yathā ayaskāntaḥ ayaḥ samākarṣati
222.03	vinā saṃyogena yathā karman saṃyoga-vibhāgau karoti vinā
222.04	saṃyogena tathā anyathā api kārya-utpattiḥ vinā saṃyogena bhaviṣyati iti/ na
222.05	hetu-artha-aparijñānāt na brūmaḥ asti saṃyogaḥ kārya-utpatti^aḥ api tu kṣetra-
222.06	udaka-bīja-agni-indanāni sāpekṣāṇi iti/ yadi ca nirapekṣam kim cit kāraṇam
222.07	sarvadā ārambhakam syāt bhavet anaikāntikaḥ hetuḥ tad tu na kena cit śakyam
222.08	pratipādayitum, śabdaḥ śabda-antaram ārabhamāṇaḥ sva-kāraṇa-paricchinna-
222.09	ākāśa-deśa-pratyāsatti-apekṣaḥ ārabhate śabdasya avyāpaka-tvāt/ ayaskāntaḥ
222.10	api ayaḥ samākarṣati sva-āśraya-pratyāsatti-apekṣaḥ eva yadi ca nirapekṣaḥ
222.11	ākarṣeta na kva cit ayaṃsi avatiṣṭheran tasmāt na nirpakṣaḥ iti/ karman api
222.12	sva-āśraya-pratyāsatti^ā abhimukhya-apekṣam saṃyogam karoti na nirapekṣam karman na
222.13	pratilomam iti/ yadi anapekṣam saṃyogam kuryāt pāṇḍya-mathura-asambandhini
222.14	devadatte utpannam karman takṣaśilā-asambandhini yajñadatte saṃyogam kuryāt
222.15	na tu idam asti na ca idam pratijñāyate sarvasya kāraṇam saṃyogaḥ api tu
222.16	sāpekṣam ārabhate iti/ sūtra-vyāghātaḥ iti cet/ atha manyaṣe yadi
222.17	sāpekṣam karman karoti nanu pāramarṣam sūtram vyāhanyeta eka-dravyam
222.18	aguṇam saṃyoga-vibhāgayoḥ anapekṣa-kāraṇam karman iti/ na vyāghātaḥ sūtra-
222.19	artha-aparijñānāt na bhavatā sūtra-arthaḥ vyajñāyi na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ nirapekṣam
222.20	karman saṃyoga-vibhāga-kāraṇam iti āśraya-ādi api na apekṣate iti/ api tu
222.21	carama-bhāvi-nimitta-antaram na apekṣate iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ yathā dravyam utpannam
223.01	carama-bhāvinam saṃyogam apekṣyamāṇam paścāt ārabhate yathā vā saṃyogaḥ
223.02	guṇa-karma-sva-ārabdhavya^su paścāt-bhāvi-saṃskāra-prayatna-adṛṣṭa-ākhyam nimittam apekṣate
223.03	yathā vā kāraṇa^su vartamānāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  kārya-rūpa-ādiṣu ārabdhavya^su dravya-utpattim
223.04	apekṣante utpādyasya ādhāra-bhāvāt yad utpādyam dravyam tad teṣām ādhāraḥ iti/
223.05	na tu karman paścāt-bhāvi-nimittam apekṣate iti anapekṣa-tvam karmaṇaḥ śāstre
223.06	uktam/ na ppunar karma-āśraya-ādi api na apekṣate iti/ saṃyoge sāpekṣa-
223.07	tva-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet yadi paścāt-bhāvi-nimitta-antara-anapekṣa-tvam karmaṇaḥ
223.08	nirapekṣa-tva-arthaḥ api tarhi saṃyoge kartavye karman sāpekṣam prāpnoti
223.09	vibhāga-apekṣi-tvāt na hi vibhāgam antareṇa karman saṃyogam karoti
223.10	tasmāt vibhāga-apekṣi-tvam karmaṇaḥ prasaktam/ tataḥ ca tad-avasthaḥ sūtra-vyāghāta-
223.11	doṣaḥ na sandehāt kim vibhāga-apekṣena karmaṇā saṃyogaḥ janyate uta
223.12	pratibandhakam vibhāgena nivartyate iti sandihyate/ vayam tu brūmaḥ vibhāgaḥ
223.13	utpannaḥ pūrva-saṃyoga-nivṛttim karoti nivṛtte saṃyoge karman pratibandhaka-
223.14	apagamāt saṃyogam karoti/ yathā gurutvam nirapekṣam karma-kāraṇam
223.15	iti uddiṣṭam śāstre/ atha ca vṛnta-phala-vibhāgāt nivṛtte vṛnta-phala-saṃyoge
223.16	gurutvam labdha-vṛtti tad ca patana-karman karoti tathā karman api iti adoṣaḥ/
223.17	yathā vā saṃyogaḥ sādhāraṇa-kārya-dravya-saṃyoga-vināśa-uttara-kālam dravya-
223.18	antara-ārambhe nirapekṣaḥ kāraṇam iti na vibhāga-apekṣaḥ dravyam ārabhate
223.19	tathā karman saṃyogam ārabhamāṇam na vibhāgam apekṣate iti/ ataḥ api
223.20	na sūtra-vyāghātaḥ/ yad api uktam karman vinā saṃyogena saṃyogam karoti
223.21	tad api na vyāghātāt/ na saṃyogaḥ asti karman ca saṃyogam karoti iti
224.01	vyāhatam/ na hi asati saṃyoge vākyam etat arthavat-tāyām vyavatiṣṭhate
224.02	iti/ atha vā anena vyāghātaḥ gati-vibhāga-saṃyogāḥ pratiṣidhyante/ atha
224.03	ca karman saṃyogam karoti iti vyāhatam/ atha manuṣe para-mata-vyapekṣayā
224.04	uktam, saṃyoga-vibhāga-karmaṇām pratiṣedhaḥ na asmābhiḥ saṃyoga-
224.05	vibhāga-karmāṇi abhyupagamyante iti/ evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ yāni
224.06	tāni para-mata-abhyupagatāni saṃyoga-vibhāga-karmāṇi tāni bhavatā
224.07	katham pratipadyante/ yadi pramāṇataḥ katham para-abhyupagatāni/ atha
224.08	pramāṇam antareṇa katham pratipadyase na hi pramāṇam antareṇa kā cit
224.09	pratipattiḥ yuktā iti/ atha na evam pratipadyase vyāhatam tarhi etat bhavati para-pakṣa-
224.10	prasiddhāni saṃyoga-vibhāga-karmāṇi iti/ atha manuṣe yāni bhavatā
224.11	abhyupagamyante saṃyoga-vibhāga-karmāṇi tāni na santi na punar saṃyoga-
224.12	vibhāga-karmaṇām pratiṣedhaḥ katham deśa-antare virala-dravya-utpāde gati-vyavahāraḥ/
224.13	yaḥ santānaḥ viralaḥ deśa-antara^su utpadyate tatra gati-vyavahāraḥ
224.14	nirantara-utpāde saṃyoga-vyavahāraḥ ye vastunī nirantaram utpadyete tayoḥ
224.15	yaḥ nirantara-utpādaḥ saḥ saṃyogaḥ saṃyoga-viparyayāt vibhāgaḥ/ atra
224.16	tāvat uktam vṛkṣa-sthitam bhāvayadbhiḥ na pramāṇa-abhāvāt iti/ na hi kṣaṇika-
224.17	tve pramāṇam asti iti sāntara-nirantara-buddhi^oḥ ca nimittam vaktavyam/ yadi
224.18	saṃyogam vibhāgam nimitta-antaram na pratipadyase sāntaram iti buddhi^aḥ nirantaram
224.19	iti buddhi^aḥ bheda-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ na hi nimitta-bhedam antareṇa buddhīnām
224.20	bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ rūpa-ādi-vat iti/ yā ca iyam acalati calati iti buddhiḥ yā
224.21	asaṃyukte saṃyuktam iti avibhakte vibhaktam iti ca sārva-pradhānam antareṇa
224.22	na bhavati sarvāḥ etāḥ mithyā-buddhyaḥ  pradhāna-anukārena bhavanti iti
225.01	pradhānam vaktavyam na hi niṣpradhānam bhāktam dṛṣṭam sthāṇu-puruṣa-vat iti/
225.02	yathā sthāṇu^i sati puruṣe sthāṇuḥ iti buddhiḥ puruṣe vā sati sthāṇu^i
225.03	puruṣa-buddhiḥ iti/ yadi ca artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ na syāt/ kuṇḍalin^ḥ iti asyāḥ
225.04	buddhi^aḥ kim cit nimittam avaśyam vidhīyamānam pratiṣidhyamānam vā nimitta-
225.05	antaram upādeyam/ na tāvat kuṇḍala-nimittā na api devadatta-nimittā
225.06	na bhavatā nimitta-antaram pratipādyate nimitta-antaram ca antareṇa yathā
225.07	katham cit vyavasthitābhyām devadatta-kuṇḍalābhyām kuṇḍalin^ḥ iti buddhi^ā bhavitavyam/
225.08	tasmāt avaśyam vidhīyamānam pratiṣidhyamānam nimitta-antaram abhyupagantavyam/
225.09	yadi pratiṣidhyamānam yad api atra bhavati tad anyatra pratiṣidhyate iti pratiṣidhyamānasya
225.10	viṣayaḥ vaktavyaḥ tasmāt na katham cana saṃyogaḥ pratiṣeddhum śakyate
225.11	iha-buddhi-nimitta-tvāt ca iyam iha-buddhiḥ pravartamānā na ṛte sambandhāt
225.12	pravartate yathā iha kuṇḍe badarāṇi iti/ na iyam badara-mātra-nimittā na kuṇḍa-
225.13	mātra-nimittā iti yad asyāḥ nimittam saḥ saṃyogaḥ iti/ iha iti buddhiḥ yadi
225.14	saṃyogāt aniyamaḥ na prāpnoti yadi saṃyogāt iha-buddhiḥ bhavati asati
225.15	saṃyoge iha-buddhiḥ na syāt yathā santi iha vane tilakāḥ santi iha vane
225.16	kiṃśukāḥ iti/ dūrāt ca antaram apaśyatām kim-kṛtā iha-buddhiḥ na hi tatra asti
225.17	saṃyogaḥ iti na iha-buddhi^aḥ ubhaya-nimitta-tvāt ubhayam iha-buddhi^aḥ nimittam
225.18	saṃyogaḥ vibhāga-adarśanam ca/ tatra ekā mukhyā ekā bhāktī yā saṃyogāt
225.19	sā tattva-buddhiḥ iti yā vibhāga-adarśanāt sā bhāktī iti yā
225.20	ca iyam bhāktī sā pradhāna-sāmānya-darśanāt upajāyate na pradhānam pratyācaṣte
225.21	na hi pradhāna-pratyākhyānena bhāktī buddhiḥ bhavitum arhati iti/ bhāktīaḥ eva
225.22	sarvāḥ bhaviṣyanti iti na yuktam pradhāna-apekṣi-tvāt iti uktam/ yad api idam
226.01	ucyate vinā saṃyogena iha-buddhiḥ dṛṣṭā yathā santi iha vane tilakāḥ
226.02	santi iha vane kiṃśukāḥ iti/ tatra sampradhāryam kim sambandhaḥ na asti
226.03	uta saṃyogaḥ na asti/ vayam tu brūmaḥ saṃyogaḥ na asti na tu sambandhaḥ/
226.04	sambandhatvam sambaddham hi vanam tilaka-ādibhiḥ iti kim punar idam vanam nāma
226.05	na idam durvacam/ uktam etat bahutva-saṃkhyā saviśeṣaṇā iti ūrdhava-avasthite ca
226.06	dravye saṃyukta-buddhi^i satyām iha-buddhiḥ kasmāt na bhavati tayoḥ eva dravyayoḥ
226.07	tasmin saṃyoge sati na bhavati iti kim-kṛtaḥ niyamaḥ na aparijñānāt
226.08	sambandham antareṇa nirnimittā iha-buddhiḥ na bhavati iti brūmaḥ na punar bhavati
226.09	eva iha-buddhiḥ sati saṃyoge iti/ na ca uttara-ādhāra-anapekṣena saṃyogena iha-
226.10	buddhiḥ kriyate iti tad-pūrvikā na bhavati/ yad punar etat asati sambandhe
226.11	asya dravyasya iha samavāyaḥ iti iha-buddhiḥ na prāpnoti/ yathā iha tantuṣu
226.12	paṭaḥ iti buddhi^aḥ samavāyaḥ nimittam, na punar samavāyasya samavāyaḥ
226.13	asti iha samavāyaḥ iti na syāt/ atra uktam, kim uktam, mithyā-
226.14	pratyayaḥ eva eṣaḥ iti kim punar samavāyavadbhiḥ dravyaiḥ samavāyasya sāmānyam
226.15	yad-apekṣayā saḥ mithyā-pratyayaḥ pravartate bheda-adarśanam, yāni samavāyavanti
226.16	na teṣām bhedaḥ dṛśyate samavāye ca etat asti iti yuktaḥ mithyā-pratyayaḥ
226.17	vākya-dharma-bhedāt ca aniyamaḥ/ na ca ekasmin vākye yaḥ dharmaḥ saḥ vākya-
226.18	antare api bhavati iti yathā rājñaḥ puruṣaḥ gacchati iti trīṇi padāni
226.19	trīṇi api svena svena arthena arthavanti bhavanti iti/ udakasya bhāvaḥ bhavati iti
226.20	na etat vākya-sāmānyāt ekasmin api vākye trīṇi api padāni pṛthak-arthavanti
227.01	bhavitum arhanti/ kaḥ punar asya vākyasya arthaḥ udakasya bhāvaḥ bhavati iti
227.02	yāvat uktam bhavati udakam bhavati tāvat uktam bhavati udakasya bhāvaḥ
227.03	bhavati iti/ evam anyāni api vākyāni duṇḍe badarāṇām vṛttiḥ iti
227.04	yathā vṛtti^i darśitāyām vṛttimatsu badara^su sampratyayaḥ etat uktam bhavati
227.05	kuṇḍe badarāṇi vartante iti tathā iha ca dravye dravyasya samavāyaḥ iti
227.06	bruvāṇena uktam bhavati atra dravye dravyam vartate iti/ tasmāt ajñātvā
227.07	vākya-artham vibhāgam ucyate asya dravyasya iha samavāyaḥ asati nimitte
227.08	iti/ tasmāt apapannam artha-antaram saṃyogaḥ iti/ upapatti^i ca na saṃsthāna-
227.09	viśeṣena avasthitāḥ tantu^āh paṭa-buddhi^aḥ kāraṇam bhavanti iti/ apare punar
227.10	anyathā artha-antara-anavayava-tvāt iti etat vākyam varṇayanti avayava-avayavi-bhāvāt
227.11	iti/ etat tu taiḥ kutaḥ labhyate na hi ayam arthaḥ artha-antara-anavayava-tvāt
227.12	iti etasmin vākye śrūyate upanayena labhyate iti āha/ yasmāt ayam upanayaḥ
227.13	avayavāḥ tantu^āḥ avayavin^ḥ paṭaḥ iti upanayena avayava-avayavi-tvāt iti
227.14	hetuḥ labhyate satyam labhyate viruddhaḥ tu bhavati na hi tantu^āḥ ātmanaḥ avayavāḥ
227.15	ca avayavin^ḥ ca na paṭaḥ ātmanaḥ avayavaḥ avayavin^ḥ ca iti/ kim kāraṇam/
227.16	avayava-avayavi-śabdayoḥ sambandhi-śabda-tvāt sambandhi-śabdau etau
227.17	avayavaḥ avayavin^ḥ iti/ na ca anyatara-pratyākhyānena sambandhi-śabda-pravṛttiḥ asti
227.18	tasmāt ayam api na sūtra-arthaḥ/ dṛṣṭaḥ anartha-antare api pradesa-pradeśi-vyavahāraḥ
227.19	yathā ākāśasya pradeśaḥ iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etat/ anyaḥ
227.20	avayava-arthaḥ anyaḥ ca pradeśa-arthaḥ iti/ kaḥ avayava-arthaḥ kāraṇa-viseṣa-abhidhānam
227.21	avayavaḥ iti ādhāra-abhidhānam pradesaḥ pradiśyate asmin iti na
228.01	ākāśam kva cit pradiśyate na ca ākāśasya kim cit kāraṇam asti tasmāt na
228.02	kāraṇa-arthaḥ na ādhāra-arthaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam ākāśe pradeśa-śabdaḥ pradeśavat-dravya-
228.03	sāmānyāt draṣṭavyaḥ kim punar pradeśavatā dravyena sāmānyam saṃyogasya avyāpya-
228.04	vṛtti-tvam/ enam ca artham upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ iti/ tad-pradeśa-ātmaka-tvāt iti
228.05	cet/ atha manyase ayam anyaḥ hetuḥ avayavinaḥ anartha-antara-tvam asādhakaḥ
228.06	tad-pradeśa-ātmaka-tvam kaḥ asya arthaḥ anartha-antaram paṭāt tantu^āḥ tad-pradeśa-vyapadeśa-
228.07	viṣaya-tvāt iti ye yasya pradeśa-tvena vyapadiśyante te tasya ātmānaḥ bhavanti
228.08	yathā ākāśasya pradeśāḥ paṭasya ca pradeśa-tvena vyapadiśyante tantu^āḥ tasmāt
228.09	paṭa-ātmānaḥ tantu^āḥ iti/ idam tāvat yathā-śruti vākyam pūrvavat-hetu-hīnam
228.10	tad-pradeśa-ātmaka-tvasya artha-antara-anartha-antarayoḥ adarśanāt asādhāraṇaḥ pradeśa-śabdasya
228.11	kāraṇa-paryāya-tvāt viruddhaḥ tad-pradeśa-vyapadeśa-viṣaya-tvasya tantubhiḥ
228.12	asambandhāt asiddhaḥ/ yaḥ api ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ ākāśasya pradeśaḥ iti saḥ api asiddhaḥ
228.13	katham iti ākāśasya pradeśaḥ iti saṃyogasya avyāpya-vṛtti-tvam ucyate na ca
228.14	saṃyogasya avyāpya-vṛtti-tva-mātram ākāśam tasmāt asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ dravya-antara^bhyaḥ
228.15	dravya-antara-aniṣpatti^aḥ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ/ na kila dravya-antara^bhyaḥ
228.16	dravya-antaram utpadyamānam dṛṣṭam na ca go-hasti-aśva^bhyaḥ puruṣaḥ utpadyate tantubhyaḥ
228.17	tu utpadyate paṭaḥ tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram iti asya api pūrvavat-hetu-nyūna-tā
228.18	upanayena vyajyamānaḥ tebhyaḥ tu utpatti^aḥ iti hetuḥ/ ayam api artha-antara-anartha-antarayoḥ
228.19	vyāvṛtti^aḥ asādhāraṇaḥ artha-antarāt ca turī-ādi^aḥ artha-antarasya paṭasya utpatti-
228.20	darśanāt viruddhaḥ paṭa-pakṣa^karaṇe asambaddha-arthaḥ na paṭaḥ paṭāt utpadyate iti/
228.21	ataḥ tad-śabdena tantu^āḥ sambaddhyante tantubhyaḥ paṭaḥ utpadyate iti/ tathā api
229.01	viruddhaḥ na tantuḥ tantubhyaḥ bhavati na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ avayavi-antara-avayavānām
229.02	avayavi-antara-artha-antara-bhāvāt iha ye avayavinaḥ artha-antaram bhavanti te avayavi-
229.03	antara-avayavāh bhavanti yathā cakra-ādi^āh ratha-avayavāḥ iti te artha-antaram
229.04	paṭāt/ yadi ca tantu^āḥ api paṭāt artha-antaram syuḥ te api paṭa-vyatirekena avayavi-
229.05	antara-avayavāḥ syuḥ na ca te tad-vyatirekena avayavi-antara-avayavāḥ tasmāt na
229.06	artha-antaram iti vākya-mātre hetu-nyūna-tā asādhāraṇa-tā ca doṣaḥ/ hetuḥ
229.07	tad-vyatirekena anya-anavayava-tvāt na tantu^āḥ tantūnām avayavāḥ iti viruddhaḥ/
229.08	artha-antara-pratyākhyānāt ca avayavaḥ avayavin^ḥ iti etat na syāt/ yad api idam ucyate ye
229.09	avayavāḥ avayavinaḥ artha-antaram bhavanti te tad-vyatirekena anya-avayavāḥ bhavanti
229.10	yathā cakra-ādyaḥ  iti/ na ubhayena vyāghātāt/ cakra-ādyaḥ  avayavāḥ iti
229.11	bruvatā abhyupagatam artha-antaram bhavati/ anabhyupagame vā kaḥ asya vākyasya arthaḥ
229.12	cakra-ādyaḥ  ratha-avayavāḥ iti/ ye ca cakra-ādyaḥ  ratha-avayava-tvena abhyupagamyante
229.13	te api artha-antara-pratiṣedhāt na rathasya na api anyasya avayavāḥ iti ucchinnaḥ
229.14	avayava-avayavi-śabdasya viṣayaḥ tasmāt ahetuḥ ayam, na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ
229.15	dravya-antara-utpatti-deśa-vyavacchedāt yad ubhaya-sampratipannam artha-antara-tvena tad
229.16	tasmāt bhinna-deśam utpadyamānam dṛṣṭam yathā go^ḥ aśvāt na tu tantubhyaḥ
229.17	bhinna-deśaḥ paṭaḥ utpadyate tasmāt na sau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram iti/ atra api
229.18	hetu-nyūna-tā ca asādhāraṇa-tā ca pūrvavat iti tad-deśa-utpatti^aḥ iti hetuḥ na
229.19	tantuḥ tantu-deśe utpadyate iti viruddhaḥ utpatti-pratyākhyānam vā paṭa-pratiṣedhāt
229.20	yadi tantubhyaḥ paṭaḥ na utpadyate kaḥ utpatti-śabdasya arthaḥ na
229.21	tantuḥ utpadyate na paṭaḥ iti utpatti-deśa-vyavacchedāt iti utpatti-śabdasya arthaḥ
230.01	vaktavyaḥ iti/ saḥ ca nirūpyamāṇah vyakti-ādi-dharma-bhedena apūrvam janma āha iti
230.02	viruddhaḥ/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ avayava-antara-avayavinaḥ avayava-antara-artha-antara-
230.03	bhāvāt/ iha yaḥ avayava-antarāṇām avayavin^ḥ bhavati saḥ avayava-antara^bhyaḥ
230.04	artha-antaram bhavati yathā cakra-ādibhyaḥ ghaṭaḥ cakra-ādibhyaḥ avayava^bhyaḥ artha-antaram
230.05	ghaṭa iti cakra-ādi-avayava-vyatirekena avayava-antarāṇām avayavin^ḥ bhavati na tu
230.06	paṭah tantu-vyatirekena avayava-antarāṇām avayavin^ḥ bhavati/ tasmāt na asau tantubhyaḥ
230.07	artha-antaram/ atra api asādhāraṇa-tā hetu-nyūna-tā ca pūrvavat/ avayava-
230.08	antarāṇām avayavin^ḥ iti bruvatā avayavin^ḥ artha-antara-bhūtaḥ abhyanujñātaḥ bhavati/
230.09	śūnyam vā vākyam/ atha na abhyanujñāyate kaḥ asya vākyasya arthaḥ/ avayava-
230.10	antarāṇām avayavin^ḥ iti na hi avayavi-pratyākhyānāt etat vākyam arthavat-tāyām
230.11	śakyam vyavasthāpayitum tad-vyatirekena anya-anavayavi-tvam hetuḥ asya api prasaṅgaḥ
230.12	pūrvavat/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ sati anya-kartṛ^aḥ artha-antara-bhāvāt iha ye
230.13	yasmin sati anyat kurvanti te tasmāt artha-antaram bhavanti yathā kaṭāt
230.14	tantu^āḥ sati kaṭe artha-antaram paṭam kurvanti iti kaṭāt artha-antaram bhavanti na
230.15	punar sati paṭe tantu^āḥ dravya-antaram kurvanti tasmāt te na artha-antaram
230.16	iti/ asya api hetu-arthaḥ sati anya-akaraṇāt/ asya api asādhāraṇa-tā
230.17	nyūna-tā ca pūrvavat/ sati kaṭe tantu^āḥ paṭam kurvanti iti brūṣe na
230.18	ca artha-antaram iti vyāhatam/ na hi tantu^āḥ ātmānam kurvanti paṭam
230.19	api artha-antara-abhāvāt na kurvanti iti cet kriyā-viṣaya-ucchedaḥ na tantu^āḥ
230.20	ātmānam kurvanti it na artha-antaram kurvanti it kriyā-viṣayaḥ ucchinnaḥ
230.21	bhavati iti/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ ātma-saṃyoga-vyatireka-bhāvi-avayavi-anya-
231.01	tvāt iha ye yasmāt artha-antaram bhavanti te ātma-saṃyoga-vyatirekena bhavanti
231.02	tad yathā tantu-saṃyoga-vyatirekena kaṭaḥ bhavati na ca tantu-saṃyoga-vyatirekena
231.03	paṭaḥ bhavati tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram/ asya api hetu-arthaḥ
231.04	tantu-saṃyoga^bhyaḥ tad-utpattiḥ iti asya api asādhāraṇa-tā hetu-nyūna-tā ca pūrvavat
231.05	eva/ tantu-saṃyoga^bhyaḥ tad-utpattiḥ iti bruvatā tantu-vyatiriktaḥ saṃyogaḥ ca
231.06	avayavin^ḥ ca abhyupagataḥ bhavati anabhyupagame tantu-saṃyoga^bhyaḥ tad-utpatti^aḥ iti
231.07	vākya-arthaḥ hīyate/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ dravya-antara-guṇāmām dravya-antara-
231.08	guṇa-akāraṇa-tve aguṇa-tva-prasaṅgāt/ na kila dravya-antara-guṇānām dravya-antara-
231.09	guṇa^bhyaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ utpādaḥ/ yathā na go-guṇa^bhyaḥ aśva-guṇānām/ tantu-guṇa^bhyaḥ tu
231.10	paṭa-guṇānām utpattiḥ tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram iti/ saḥ ayam anyāyaḥ
231.11	vartate kaḥ atra anyāyaḥ tantu-guṇa^bhyaḥ paṭa-guṇānām utpattim pratipadyase na
231.12	ca artha-antaram paṭaḥ iti/ na ca tantu-guṇa^bhyaḥ tantu-guṇānām utpattiḥ iti tantu-
231.13	guṇa^bhyaḥ tad-guṇa-utpattiḥ iti hetuḥ viruddhaḥ/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ apratyakṣa-
231.14	tva-prasaṅgāt pratyakṣa-apratyakṣa-vṛttiḥ avayavin^ḥ apratyakṣaḥ syāt yadi avayavin^ḥ artha-antaram
231.15	syāt yathā garbha-mātṛ-saṃyogaḥ pratyakṣa-apratyakṣa-vṛttiḥ na pratyakṣaḥ pratyakṣaḥ tu avayavin^ḥ
231.16	tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram/ pratyakṣa-tvāt eva na artha-antaram iti viruddhaḥ
231.17	hetuḥ/ garbha-mātṛ-saṃyogaḥ ca apratyakṣaḥ iti kim ayam pratyakṣa-apratyakṣābhyām
231.18	ārambhāt apratyakṣaḥ uta pratyakṣa-apratyakṣa-vṛtti-tvāt apratyakṣaḥ iti/ yadi ādyaḥ himavat-
231.19	paramāṇukam apratyakṣam prāpnoti tasya hi ekaḥ eva pratyakṣaḥ ekaḥ eva
231.20	apratyakṣah iti/ atha dvitīyaḥ bhāvaḥ apratyakṣaḥ prāpnoti saḥ hi pratyakṣa-
231.21	apratyakṣa-vṛttiḥ iti/ na ca ayam pakṣaḥ asti avayavānām apratyakṣa^tve avayavin^ḥ
232.01	na pratyakṣaḥ iti/ uktam ca atra kim uktam, yeṣām indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ asti
232.02	avayavānām taiḥ saha avayavin^ḥ gṛhyate yeṣām na asti taiḥ saha na gṛhyate na
232.03	ca etat kṛtaḥ asti bhedaḥ avayavānām apratyakṣa-tvāt avayavinaḥ apratyakṣa-tvam bruvataḥ
232.04	sarvam atīindriyam prāpnoti na hi kasya cit madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgau jñāyete
232.05	iti saḥ ayam pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ ucchinnaḥ bhavati/ pratyakṣa-tvāt iti ca bruvāṇena
