232.06	avayava-atiriktaḥ avayavina^ḥ abhyupagataḥ bhavati paramāṇūnām atīndriya-tvāt
232.07	na hi paramāṇu^āḥ kadā cit upalabhyante na pramāṇu^āḥ atīndriyāḥ rūpa-ādimatām
232.08	atīndriya-tva-nivṛtti^aḥ/ na hi rūpa-ādimat kim cit atīndriyam asti
232.09	dṛṣṭam ca rūpa-ādimat aindriyakam ghaṭa-ādi, aindriyakāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ iti
232.10	kaḥ arthaḥ kim tāvat indriya-karaṇa-pratyakṣāḥ uta indriya-sambaddhāḥ iti/ kim
232.11	ca ataḥ yadi indriya-karaṇa-pratyakṣāḥ aindriyakāḥ iti tad na yuktam, na
232.12	hi indriyam anyasya anyathā artham prakāśayati/ anyathā anyasya indriya-vṛttīnām
232.13	avikalpita-tvāt bhavatām cet paramāṇu^āḥ aindriyakāḥ asmad-ādīnām
232.14	api aindriyakāḥ syuḥ/ atha indriya-sambaddhāḥ aindriyakāḥ iti tasmāt na virodhaḥ
232.15	na hi paramāṇu^āḥ indriyena na sambaddhyante iti/ tasmāt indriya-sannikarṣe
232.16	sati api mahattva-abhāvāt na paramāṇu^āḥ upalabhyante iti/ katham punar evam
232.17	gamyate mahattvam api upalambhe rūpavat-kāraṇam iti bhāva-abhāvayoḥ tadvat-tvāt
232.18	yasmāt sati mahattve rūpe ca upalabdhiḥ bhavati asati ca na bhavati/
232.19	tasmāt ubhayam kāraṇam iti/ yad tu atra uktam tejas-sparśa-vat iti/ sati tejas-
232.20	sparśe upalabdhiḥ bhavati asti ca na bhavati na ca tejas-sparśaḥ api rūpavat-
232.21	dravya-upalabdhi^aḥ kāraṇam iti/ tad na vyabhicārāt/ vyabhicārāt sparśaḥ
233.01	upalabdhi^aḥ akāraṇam na punar rūpa-mahattve kva cit vyabhicarataḥ tasmāt rūpa-mahattve
233.02	eva upalabdhi^aḥ kāraṇam iti ayam ca arthaḥ sūtra-kārena sūtra-dvayena uktaḥ/ adravya-
233.03	dravya-tvāt paramāṇu^i anupalabdhiḥ, rūpa-saṃskāra-abhāvāt vāyu^aḥ anupalabdhiḥ
233.04	iti/ rūpa-saṃskāraḥ rūpa-samavāyaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ atha punar
233.05	sparśasya api rūpa-mahattva-vat avyabhicāraḥ kasmāt sparśaḥ na kāraṇam iti
233.06	vaktavyam/ samavadhārya ca etat bhavatā abhidhīyate sparśa-vat etat syāt iti yena
233.07	pramāṇena avadhṛtam etat sparśaḥ na kāraṇam iti/ mayā api tataḥ eva akāraṇa-
233.08	tvam/ atha akāraṇa-tva-pratipādakam na pramāṇam asti kaḥ hi asya vākyasya
233.09	arthah sparśa-vat etat syāt iti tad-bhāva-bhāvi-tvam ca vyabhicārayatā kārya-
233.10	kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ ucchinnaḥ bhavati/ idam asya kāraṇam idam asya kāryam iti
233.11	pareṇa paryanuyuktaḥ tad-bhāva-bhāvi-tva-vyatirekena anyena na pratipādayasi
233.12	ataḥ virodhāt anuttaram etat/ yad punar etat atīndriya-saṃyoga-atīndriya-tvāt
233.13	iti/ atīndriyayoḥ kila ātma-manasoḥ saṃyogaḥ hi atīndriyaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
233.14	iti/ yadi ca paramāṇu^āḥ atīndriyāḥ syuḥ tad-kāryam api atīndriyam syāt
233.15	aindriyakam ca dṛṣṭam, tasmāt aindriyakāḥ iti/ yaḥ ayam dṛṣṭānta-prayogaḥ
233.16	atīndriya-saṃyoga-atīndriya-tvāt iti dṛṣṭānta-prayoga-mātre ca hetu-nyūna-tā
233.17	yaḥ api atra katham cit hetuḥ labhyate na atīndriya-kāryam mahābhūta-ādi-vyaktam aindriyaka-
233.18	tvāt ghaṭa-vat iti ghaṭa-ādi aindriyakam aindriyaka-kāryam dṛṣṭam tathā ca
233.19	mahābhūta-ādi-vyaktam aindriyakam tasmāt tad api aindriyaka-kāryam iti/ ayam api
233.20	pratyakṣa-pramāṇa-virodhāt viruddhaḥ anuṣṇaḥ agniḥ kṛtaka-tvāt iti yathā/ eke
233.21	tu vātāyana-cchidra-dṛśyam truṭim pamāṇum varṇayanti/ tad na yuktam/ tasya
234.01	bhedya-tvāt/ abhedyaḥ paramāṇuḥ bhidhyate truṭiḥ iti/ katham avagamyate bhidyate
234.02	truṭiḥ iti/ dravya-tve sati asmad-ādi-bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ghaṭa-vat iti/
234.03	katham ca anyathā abhedyaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti ucyate anitya-tvāt ca na paramāṇuḥ
234.04	sāmānya-viśeṣavataḥ asmad-ādi-bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ca tasmāt na aindriyakāḥ
234.05	paramāṇu^āḥ iti teṣām atīndriya-tvāt yad pratyakṣam tad anyat iti viruddhaḥ
234.06	hetuḥ/ saṃhtānām utpatti-nirodhāt na asaṃhtaḥ paramāṇuḥ asti iti apare/
234.07	apare tu asaṃhtam paramāṇum varṇayanti saṃhatāḥ eva hi utpadyante niruddhyante
234.08	ca asaṃhatāḥ tu paramāṇu^āḥ na santi eva iti/ tad ayuktam/ saṃhata-arthasya
234.09	anukta-tvāt asaṃhatāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ na sati iti bruvatā saṃhata-śabda-arthaḥ
234.10	vaktavyaḥ saṃhataḥ hi nāma bahūnām akārya-kāraṇa-bhūtānām saṃyuktānām
234.11	avasthānam/ na ca asaṃhata-pratiṣedhe saṃhatasya nimittam asiti saṃhanyamāna-
234.12	tantra-tvāt nityam ayam saṃhataḥ saṃhanyamāna-tantraḥ bhavati/ na ca asaṃhata-
234.13	pratiṣedhe saṃhanyamānam asti tasmāt na asaṃhataḥ asti ti virodhāt na kim cit
234.14	etat/ eka-pratiṣedhāt aneka-abhyupagama-vat iti/ yathā nāma kaḥ cit mugdhaḥ
234.15	eka-pratiṣedhāt anekam abhyupeyāt tasya ca eka-pratiṣedhāt anekam nivarteta eka-
234.16	samudāya-tvāt anekasya tathā-vidham idam bhavati/ itaḥ ca na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ
234.17	saṃyoga-aprāpti-abhāvāt/ yad kahlu yasmāt artha-antaram bhavati tasya tena
234.18	saṃyogaḥ bhavati aprāptiḥ vā yathā gavā aśvasya na punar tantubhiḥ paṭasya saṃyogaḥ
234.19	na aprāptiḥ tasmāt na asau tebhyaḥ artha-antaram iti/ avayavinaḥ pakṣa^karaṇe
234.20	saṃyoga-vibhāga-abhāvasya saṃyoga-vibhāga-viṣaya-tvāt asambaddham/ atha asaṃyogi-
234.21	tva-avibhāgi-tve hetu-bhūte te asiddhe saṃyujyate ca ayam vibhajyate ca iti/
235.01	atha sva-avayavena asaṃyogi-tvāt saḥ api anupasaṃhāryaḥ na asya anvayaḥ na vyatirekaḥ
235.02	iti/ atha api sarpa-kuṇḍalaka-ādi udāharaṇam syāt sarpa-kuṇḍalaka-ādi
235.03	kila sva-kāraṇena asaṃyogi na ca artha-antaram iti/ tad asiddham/ yathā
235.04	ca artha-antaram sarpa-kuṇḍalaka-ādi tathā uktam punastāt/ yad api sva-kāraṇena
235.05	asaṃyogi iti iti cintyam, kim guṇa-tvāt sva-kāraṇena asaṃyogi atha anartha-
235.06	antara-bhāvāt iti/ vayam tu brūmaḥ guṇa-tvāt na anartha-antara-bhāvāt guṇaḥ ca
235.07	sarpa-kuṇḍalakaḥ saṃyogaḥ saḥ ca artha-antaram iti pratipāditam etat artha-
235.08	antara-bhāve ca darśanāt viruddhaḥ/ sattva-rajas-tamāṃsi bhavatām pakṣe mithas
235.09	artha-antara-bhūtāni teṣām ca na saṃyogaḥ na aprāptiḥ iti pradhāna-puruṣayoḥ na
235.10	saṃyogaḥ na aprāptiḥ ubhayoḥ vyāpaka-tvāt viruddhaḥ/ na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ gurutva-
235.11	antara-kārya-agrahaṇāt/ gurutva-antara-kārya-agrahaṇāt iti etat na katham cit api
235.12	pakṣena sambaddhyate kva na anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ kva ca gurutva-antara-kārya-agrahaṇam
235.13	gurutva-antara-kārya-agrahaṇāt gurutva-antara-kāryam na syāt na gurutvam na gurutva-
235.14	antaram na avayavin^ḥ na udaka-pratiṣedhāt kamaṇḍalu^aḥ kapālānām vā pratiṣedhaḥ
235.15	yuktaḥ/ eke tu gurutva-antara-kārya-agrahaṇāt iti anena guṇa-antara-ārambham pratiṣedhati
235.16	udāharaṇa-mātra-artha-tvāt gurutva-antara-ārambha-pratiṣedhasya/ katham iti/
235.17	na kāryam kāraṇa-rūpa-ādi-pūrvaka-rūpa-ādimat-gurutva-antaravat-tva-prasaṅgāt/ yadi
235.18	kāraṇa-gatāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  kārya-rūpa-ādīni ārabheran api tarhi kāraṇa-gatāni
235.19	api gurutvāni kārye gurutva-antaram ārabheran tataḥ ca gurutva-antaravat
235.20	kāryam upalabhyeta na ca upalabhyate tasmāt na kāraṇa-rūpa-ādi-pūrvaka-rūpa-ādimat
235.21	kāryam iti/ upapāditaḥ pakṣa-dharmaḥ saḥ tu pratyakṣa-virodhāt na kim cit/
235.22	katham iti/ abhyupetya kāryam rūpa-ādyaḥ  kārye pratiṣiddhyante kārya-abhyupagamāt
236.01	ca pratiṣeddhavyam abhyanujñātam bhavati kāraṇa-rūpa-ādyaḥ  ca kārya-rūpa-
236.02	ādīn na ārabhante iti bruvataḥ kāryam aguṇam syāt na tu idam aguṇam dṛśyate
236.03	tataḥ ca virodhāt ayam ahetuḥ/ atha kāryam na eva asti na kāryam kāraṇa-
236.04	rūpa-ādi-pūrvaka-rūpa-ādimat iti anarthakam, na hi kamaṇḍalu^aḥ abhāve anudakaḥ
236.05	kamaṇḍaluḥ iti yuktam uktam/ etena kārye rūpa-ādi-pratipattiḥ pratyuktā/
236.06	yaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ gurutva-antaravat-tva-prasaṅgāt iti/ ayam api abhyupagamāt na
236.07	kim cit na abhyupagamaḥ eva doṣa-tvena deśyate/ yadi tarhi gurutva-antaravat
236.08	kārya-dravyam rūpa-ādi-antaravat ca tataḥ gurutva-antara-kāryam upalabhyeta yathā
236.09	rūpa-ādi-antara-kāryam na tu upalabhyate na hi tulāyām vibhakta-piṇḍa-dvaya-upanyāsa-
236.10	vat sambaddhayoḥ api kārya-bhedam paśyāmaḥ tasmāt vibhaktayoḥ iva saṃyuktayoḥ
236.11	api na asti dravya-antara-ārambhaḥ iti/ ke cit tu na gurutva-antaravat
236.12	kārya-dravyavatī tulā iti pakṣayitvā avanamana-viśeṣa-abhāvāt iti hetum
236.13	bruvate saḥ ayam vyadhikaraṇaḥ avanamana-viśeṣa-abhāvasya atulā-dharma-tvāt/ na
236.14	hi avanamana-viśeṣa-abhāvaḥ tulā-dharmaḥ iti evam tu yuktam utpaśyāmaḥ/ avanamana-
236.15	viśeṣa-anādhāra-tvāt na gurutva-antaravat kārya-dravyavatī tulā iti/
236.16	asiddhaḥ tāvat ayam hetuḥ avanamana-viśeṣa-anādhāra-tvasya atulā-vṛtti-tvāt na
236.17	tadvatā anupalabdhi^aḥ ayam api anaikāntika-tvāt ahetuḥ gurutva-antaravat-dravya-sannipāte
236.18	sati avanamana-viśeṣa-anādhāra-tvasya dṛṣṭa-tvāt/ yathā gurutvavati dravye unmīyamāne
236.19	truṭi-bhūte rajasi gurutvavati sannipatitaḥ iti mahā-gurutve
236.20	ca unmīyamāne gurutva-mātra-upahitānām avanamana-viśeṣam na karoti/ sama-
236.21	hīna-adhika-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet yadi tāvat kāraṇa-gurutvaiḥ samam kārya-gurutvam
237.01	yāvat vipalābhyām asambaddhe dvis tāvat sambandhe sati syāt/ atha
237.02	kāraṇa-gurutva-adhikam kārya-gurutvam tathā api adhikam prasajyeta/ atha kāraṇa-
237.03	gurutvāt hīnam kārya-gurutvam tathā api viśeṣaḥ gṛhyeta na tu idam asti tasmāt na
237.04	kārya-gurutvam asti na kārya-kāraṇa-gurutva-iyattā-anavadhāraṇāt yadi etat avadhāritam
237.05	syāt etāvat kāraṇa-gurutvam etāvat kārya-gurutvam iti/ tad etat
237.06	yujyate vaktum, sama-adhika-hīna-kārya-prasaṅgaḥ iti tad tu anavadhāritam iyat
237.07	kāraṇe gurutvam iyat kārya-gurutvam iti yadi na kārya-kāraṇa-gurutvam avadhāritam
237.08	yaḥ ayam pratyayaḥ tulayā unmīyamāne dravye dvi-palam pañca-palam iti
237.09	na prāpnoti na na prāpnoti/ dravya-samāhāra-gurutva-avadhāraṇāt/ yad idam
237.10	bhavatā manyate dvi-palam pañca-palam iti na atra kārya-kāraṇa-gurutve avadhāryete
237.11	kim tu ā caramāt ā ca paramāṇu^aḥ dravya-samāhārah unmīyate tatra manuṣya-
237.12	dharmaṇaḥ na yuktam vaktum iyat kāraṇa-gurutvam iyat kārya-gurutvam iti/ na
237.13	ca samāhāraḥ kāraṇam api tu anārabdha-kāryam carama-dravyam kāraṇam iti/
237.14	ke cit tu ārabhya ārabhante iti pratipadyante tān prati idam ucyate na pūrva-
237.15	uttara-kārya-dravye samāna-deśe mūrta-tvāt ghaṭa-ādi-vat iti/ śeṣaḥ tu ārabhya-
237.16	ārambhaka-vicāraḥ na aprastuta-tvāt nirākriyate iti/ niranumānam tarhi
237.17	kārya-gurutvam yadi gurutva-antaravat dravya-upacaye sati kārya-bhedaḥ na gṛhyate
237.18	katham pratipattavyam iti/ kaḥ evam āha kārya-gurutvam na gṛhyate iti/
237.19	yadi gṛhyate kim tad patanam, na hi kārya-gurutvam antareṇa kārya-pāte
237.20	anyaḥ hetuḥ asti tasmāt gurutva-antaravat kāryam iti/ etena tulā-avanati-
238.01	viśeṣa-agrahāt na kārya-gauravam iti pratyuktam/ atha manyase kāraṇa-
238.02	gurutvaiḥ eva kāryam pātyate na kārya-gurutvam asti ataḥ kārya-pātasya anya-nimitta-
238.03	tvāt na sidhyati gurutva-antaravat kāryam iti/ na kārya-kāraṇayoḥ
238.04	asaṃyogāt yatra gurutvam āśraya-antare pāta-hetuḥ bhavati tatra saṃyogaḥ upakaraṇam,
238.05	yathā tulā-paṭalaka-gurutvavat-dravya-saṃyogaḥ iti/ na tu kārya-
238.06	kāraṇayoḥ saṃyoga^ḥ ayuta-siddha-tvāt yuta-siddhiḥ asambaddhasya vidyamāna-tā
238.07	na punar kāryam kāraṇa-asambaddham vidyate jātaḥ sambaddhaḥ ca iti ekaḥ kālaḥ
238.08	tasmāt na kārya-kāraṇe saṃyukte samavāya-upakaraṇam gurutvam pātayiṣyati iti
238.09	cet na asaṃkīrtanāt virodhāt ca/ atha manayse satyam na kārya-kāraṇayoḥ
238.10	saṃyogaḥ asti asti ca samavāyaḥ samavāyaḥ gurutva-apekṣaḥ kāryam pātayiṣyati
238.11	iti na sidhyati kārya-gurutvam na asaṃkīrtanāt na samavāyaḥ
238.12	kriyā-hetu-tvena kva cit api saṃkīrtitaḥ sūtra-kārena yathā saṃyogaḥ
238.13	nodanā-abhighāta-saṃyukta-saṃyogāt ca pṛthivīām karman iti na kevalam asaṃkīrtanāt eva
238.14	virodhāt ca yadi samavāyaḥ kriyā-hetuḥ syāt kāraṇa-rūpa-ādyaḥ  api
238.15	kāryena eka-artha-samavāyāt pateyuḥ patantu rūpa^ādyaḥ  api kim naḥ bādhyate iti
238.16	cet na gurutvasya api pāta-prasaṅgāt yathā rūpa-ādyaḥ  kārya-dravyam anupatanti
238.17	tathā gurutvam api anupatet/ gurutvam ca anupatat kutaḥ patati iti vaktavyam, na tu
238.18	gurutvasya gurutva-antaram pāta-hetuḥ asti/ tasmāt na gurutvam patati na rūpa-ādyaḥ
238.19	paṭanti iti/ yadi rūpa-ādyaḥ  na patanti dravye patati tiṣṭheyuḥ iti adravyāḥ
238.20	rūpa-ādyaḥ  syuḥ dravyam ca rūpa-hīnam upalabhyeta/ atha dravye patati patanti
238.21	kriyāvantaḥ rūpa^ādyaḥ  prāpnuvanti/ atha na tiṣṭhanti na patanti saḥ ayam
238.22	yukti-virodhaḥ avaśyam bhavatā abhyupagantavyaḥ/ paṭati patanti tiṣṭhanti
239.01	vā iti veda punar-bhavān yukta-ayuktayoḥ nānā-kāraṇam vedmi iti paraḥ/ yuktam
239.02	pramitam ayuktam apramitam/ ayuktam tarhi idam patati patanti tiṣṭḥanti iti
239.03	vā tad ca uktam gurutvasya gurutva-antara-prasaṅgāt rūpa-ādi-śūnyasya dravyasya adarśanāt
239.04	adravyāṇām ca rūpa-ādīnām adarśanāt iti/ yad ca patati gacchati
239.05	vā tad prāptam jahāti aprāptam ca āpnoti na punar rūpa-ādyaḥ  prāptam jahati
239.06	aprāptam vā prāpnuvanti rūpa-ādyaḥ  aprāptam na prāpnuvanti iti na yuktam/
239.07	bhavati idam dravyayoḥ prāptayoḥ rūpe api prāpte iti na sandehāt kim ayam
239.08	prāpti-pratyayaḥ prāpti-nimittaḥ āho aprāpti^aḥ adarśana-nimittaḥ iti sandihyate/
239.09	vayam tu brūmaḥ aprāpti^aḥ adarśana-nimittaḥ iti/ na hai dravyayoḥ
239.10	prāptayoḥ rūpayoḥ aprāptim paśyati dravyayoḥ api evam eva iti cet/ atha manyase
239.11	yathā rūpayoḥ prāpti-pratyayaḥ aprāpti^aḥ adarśana-nimittaḥ evam dravyayoḥ aprāpti-
239.12	adarśana-nimittaḥ bhaviṣyati na prāpti-nimittaḥ iti/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ
239.13	pratyaya-dvaitasya aneka-nimitta-tvāt na khalu pratyaya-dvaitam eka-nimittam dṛṣṭam/
239.14	tad yathā citra-gatāsu pratikṛtiṣu krodha-bhaya-parihāre api kruddha-bhīta-pratyayāḥ
239.15	bhavanti na tāvatā puruṣa^su api krodha-bhaya-abhāve kruddha-bhīta-pratyayāḥ
239.16	prādurbhavitum arhanti iti/ evam ayam prāpti-pratyayaḥ kva cit saṃyoga-nimittaḥ
239.17	kva cit aprāpti-adarśana-nimittaḥ iti tasmāt vyavasthitam na gurutvam patati na
239.18	rūpa-ādyaḥ  patanti iti/ apare tu kārya-gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutvam pratibaddham
239.19	bruvate/ kārya-gurutvaiḥ kila kāraṇa-gurutvāni pratibadddhāni ataḥ avanati-
239.20	viśeṣaḥ na bhavati etat tu na yuktam iti paśyāmaḥ/ kutaḥ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ
240.01	pāta-darśanāt yadi kārye patati kāraṇam avatiṣṭheta pratiṣadyemahi
240.02	kārya-gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutvam paratibaddhamiti na tu idam asti ataḥ
240.03	ayuktam tad anādhāra-tva-prasaṅgāt ca yadi kārya-gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutvam
240.04	pratibaddham na kāraṇe kriyām ārabhet kārye patati na kāraṇam patet iti
240.05	kāryam anādhāram syāt/ etena kārya-gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutva-vināśaḥ api
240.06	vyākhyātaḥ/ vibhaktānām ca pātaḥ na syāt iti adhikam/ yadi kārya-
240.07	gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutvam vināśyeta kārya-dravya-vināśāt kāraṇānām
240.08	vibhaktānām pātaḥ na syāt gurutvam eva ca na syāt yadi kārya-
240.09	gurutvena kāraṇa-gurutvam vinaśyeta api tarhi na kva cit gurutvam syāt na hi
240.10	kasya cit paramāṇu^aḥ atītam kāryam na asti ataḥ aguru^āḥ paramāṇu^āḥ syuḥ/
240.11	paramāṇuṣu ca gurutva-abhāvāt kārya-gurutvam kutaḥ utpadyeta tasmāt apratiṣedhaḥ
240.12	ayam kārya-gurutvaiḥ kāraṇa-gurutvānām vināśa-pratibandhau iti
240.13	ataḥ pūrvaḥ eva pratiṣedhaḥ na anaikāntāt iti kārya-kāraṇa-gurutva-anavadhāraṇāt
240.14	ca/ tad evam avayavinaḥ asattva-avyatireka-pratipādakam na pramāṇam
240.15	asti iti ataḥ sādhya-tvāt avayavini sandehaḥ iti ayuktam/ ataḥ ca sādhya-tvāt avayavini
240.16	sandehaḥ iti na yuktam asaṃkīrtanāt na hi sādhya-tvam saṃśaya-
240.17	hetuḥ iti kīrtitam kva cit api ataḥ na bhavati sādhya-tvāt saṃśayaḥ/ kaḥ
240.18	evam āha sādhya-tvāt saṃśayaḥ api tu sādhya-tvāt vipratipattiḥ vipratipatti^aḥ
240.19	saṃśayaḥ iti/ sādhya-tva-kāryāyām vipratipatti^i sādhya-tvam adhyāropya
240.20	uktam sādhya-tvāt avayavini sandehaḥ//
241.01	[2-1-34(32)]sarva-agrahaṇam avayavi-asiddhi^aḥ
241.02	sarva-agrahaṇam avayavi-asiddhi^aḥ/ saṃśaya-apākaraṇa-artham sūtram/ sarva-agrahaṇam
241.03	avayavi-asiddhi^aḥ iti/ sarveṣām arthānām agrahaṇam prasajyeta/ yadi avayavin^ḥ artha-
241.04	antara-bhūtaḥ avayava^bhyaḥ na asti/ katham iti pramāṇu^āḥ tāvat darśana-viṣaya-
241.05	bhāvam na āpadyante atīndriya-tvāt iti uktam/ avayavi-dravyāṇi ca bhavadbhiḥ na
241.06	pratipadyante tad-anabhyupagame ca yaḥ ayam dravya-pratyayaḥ kumbhaḥ iti guṇa-pratyayaḥ ca
241.07	śyāmaḥ iti kriyā-pratyayaḥ calati iti sāmānya-pratyayaḥ asti iti viśeṣa-
241.08	pratyayaḥ kalaśaḥ iti samavāya-pratyayaḥ iha ghaṭe rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti/
241.09	tathā anye api pratyayāḥ ekaḥ mahān saṃyuktaḥ vibhaktaḥ pṛthak paraḥ aparaḥ
241.10	iti ca na syuḥ/ asti ca sarvasya grahaṇam dravya-guṇa-karma-ādi-prapañcasya
241.11	vayam tena sarvasya grahaṇāt paśyāmaḥ avayava-atiriktaḥ asti avayavin^ḥ iti/
241.12	atha vā sarva-agrahaṇam iti sarvaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ agrahaṇam katham pratyakṣasya
241.13	vartamāna-mahat-viṣaya-tvāt/ yad vartamānam mahat ca tad bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣam ghaṭa-
241.14	ādi-avayavi-pratyākhyāne ca na bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ asti tasya abhāvāt
241.15	anumāna-ādi^aḥ api abhāvaḥ sā iyam sarva-pramāṇa-nivṛttiḥ upalabhyante ca arthāḥ
241.16	pratyakṣa-ādibhiḥ pramāṇaiḥ tai sarvaiḥ grahaṇāt paśyāmaḥ asti avayavin^ḥ iti/ tad idam
241.17	sūtram avayavi-anabhyupagame virodha-pradarśana-param saḥ ayam sarva-ākāraḥ virodhaḥ
241.18	loka-paridṛṣṭa-pratyaya-ucchedaḥ iti//
241.19	[2-1-35(33)]dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇa-upapatti^aḥ ca
241.20	dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇa-upapatti^aḥ ca/ avayavin^ḥ artha-antara-bhūtaḥ iti ca arthaḥ/ kim idam
242.01	dhāraṇam nāma ekadeśa-grahaṇa-sāhacrye sati avayavninaḥ deśa-antara-prāpti-
242.02	pratiṣedhaḥ dhāraṇam/ yadā ayam avayavinaḥ ekadeśam gṛhṇāti tadā ekadeśa-
242.03	grahaṇena saha avayavinam api gṛhṇāti tena ca grahaṇena yad avayavinaḥ
242.04	deśa-antara-prāpti-nivāraṇam tad dhāraṇam/ ākarṣaṇam nāma ekadeśa-grahaṇa-
242.05	sāhacarye sati avayavinaḥ deśa-antara-prāpaṇam pūrvavat kutaḥ etat lokataḥ
242.06	lokaḥ khalu dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe evam prayuṅkte iti/ te ete dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe
242.07	avayavinam sādhayataḥ katham iti niravayave ca avayave ca adarśanāt/ na
242.08	hi dāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe niravayave avayave ca dṛṣṭe dṛṣṭe ca dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe
242.09	tasmāt avayavi-dharmau iti/ yad atra uktam saṃgraha-kārite dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe na
242.10	avayavi-kārite pāṃśu-rāśi-prabhṛtiṣu abhāvāt tṛṇa-upala-kāṣṭḥa-ādiṣu jatu-
242.11	saṃgṛhīta^su ca darśanāt iti/ tad na anavadhāraṇāt/ na brūmaḥ sarvasmin
242.12	avayavini dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe bhavataḥ api ca anyatra na bhavataḥ/ na ca
242.13	virodhaḥ avayaviṣu eva darśanāt yāni tṛṇa-upala-kāṣṭhāni jatu-saṃgṛhītāni
242.14	ākṛṣyante dhāryante ca iti avayavinaḥ eva ete yadi ca niravayave
242.15	ca avayave ca dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe syātām syāt virodhaḥ yad idam ucyate saṃgraha-
242.16	kārite te iti na viśeṣa-hetu-abhāvāt saṃgraha-kārite dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe
242.17	na avayavi-kārite iti/ na ca bhavatā viśeṣa-hetuḥ apadiśyate iti
242.18	pāṃśu-rāśi-prabhṛtiṣu ca kasmāt saṃgrahaḥ na asti iti vācyam/ yaḥ eva atra
242.19	saṃgraha-abhāve bhavataḥ hetuḥ saḥ eva avayavinaḥ vidyamānasya dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇayoḥ
242.20	abhāvaḥ iti/ kaḥ punar asau uktaḥ asau ekadeśa-gṛhītasya tad-sahacaritasya
242.21	sambandha-viśeṣaḥ iti/ saḥ ca pāṃśu-rāśi-prabhṛtiṣu na asti
243.01	tasmāt na tatra dhāraṇa-ākarṣaṇe iti saḥ ayam vyatirekin^ḥ hetuḥ/ atha idānīm
243.02	anvayinaḥ hetu^aḥ prayogam upanyasiṣyan idam āha/ atha avayavinam pratyācakṣāṇakaḥ
243.03	mā bhūt pratyakṣa-lopaḥ iti/ aṇu-sañcayam darśana-viṣayam pratijānānaḥ
243.04	kim anuyoktavyaḥ iti/ eka-buddhi^aḥ viṣayam paryanuyojyaḥ yā iyam buddhiḥ ekam idam
243.05	dravyam idam iti kim iyam nānā-viṣayā uta abhinna-viṣayā iti/ yadi
243.06	nānā-viṣayā bahuṣu adarśanāt ayuktā/ na ca bahuṣu ekam idam iti yuktaḥ
243.07	pratyayaḥ/ atha abhinna-artha-viṣayā yaḥ asau eka-buddhi^aḥ viṣayaḥ saḥ avayavin^ḥ iti
243.08	eka-aneka-buddhī bhinna-viṣaye viśeṣavat-tvāt rūpa-ādi-viṣaya-buddhi-vat iti/
243.09	atha vā eka-aneka-buddhī bhinna-viṣaye samuccita-asamuccita-viṣaya-tvāt
243.10	idam iti yathā idam ca idam iti yathā//
243.11	[2-1-36(34)]senā-vana-vat grahaṇam iti cet na atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām
243.12	senā-vana-vat grahaṇam iti cet/ atha manyase asati artha-antara-bhāve asati
243.13	ca eka-aneka-vibhede ca dṛṣṭaḥ buddhi-viśeṣaḥ/ anyā senā-buddhiḥ anyāḥ gaja-
243.14	ādi-buddhyaḥ  iti tathā anyāḥ khadira-ādi-buddhyaḥ  anyā ca vana-buddhiḥ iti na
243.15	ca tāvatā senā vanam ca anartha-antaram/ tasmāt anaikāntika-tvāt ahetuḥ/ idam
243.16	tāvat sūtram sādhana-pakṣe dṛṣṭānta-mātra-artha-tvāt asambaddham, anaikāntika-
243.17	karaṇa-pakṣe tu na asiddha-tvāt na kena senā-vanayoḥ anartha-antara-bhāvaḥ
243.18	abhyupagamyate yathā tu senā-vanāni artha-antara-bhūtāni tathā uktam/ abheda-
243.19	pratyayasya ca aneka-nimitta-tvāt na yuktam, asti ayam abheda-pratyayaḥ abhinnaḥ eva
244.01	asti ca bhinne upalabhyamāne bhedasya adarśanāt abhinne tāvat eka-pratyayaḥ
244.02	yad ekam ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipatti^ā vyavasthitam, ekam na asti iti aneka-pratyayaḥ
244.03	avyāhataḥ eka-samuccayaḥ hi anekam iti anekasmin api upalabhyamāne bhedasya
244.04	adarśanāt abheda-pratyayaḥ bhedasya adarśna-mārāt nimittāt yathā senā-vana-
244.05	ādiṣu yāni tāni senā-vana-aṅgāni hasti-ādīni tāni ekaśas upalabhyamāna-
244.06	pṛthaktvāni ārāt nimittāt bhedam na upalambhayanti bheda-anupalambhe
244.07	bhinna-vastuni abhinna-pratyayaḥ na ca pramāṇu^āḥ kena cit upalabhyante kasmāt
244.08	atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām iti//
244.09	atīndriyāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ iti pratipāditam etat/ yaḥ ca aindriyakaḥ
244.10	padārthaḥ saḥ upalabhyamāna-pṛthaktvaḥ ārāt pṛthaktvasya agrahaṇe abheda-pratyaya-
244.11	nimittam bhavati/ yathā upalabhyamāna-jātīyānām ārāt na jāti-grahaṇam
244.12	upalabhyamāna-praspandānām ca na ārāt praspanda-grahaṇam iti/ gṛhyamāṇa-
244.13	agrahaṇasya anya-nimitta-tvāt/ yad ca idam gṛhyamāṇasya padārthasya agrahaṇam tad anya-
244.14	nimittam dṛṣṭam, yathā teṣām eva senā-vana-aṅgānām, na tu paramāṇu^āḥ
244.15	gṛhyante teṣām atīndriya-tvāt tasmāt na teṣām anya-nimittam agrahaṇam iti/
244.16	anupajāti-viśeṣāṇām ca buddhi-bheda-ahetu-tvāt virodhaḥ yasya paramāṇu^āḥ
244.17	ghaṭa-ādi-buddhi-hetu^āḥ bhavanti saḥ ca idam praṣṭavyaḥ kim paramāṇu^āḥ upajāta-
244.18	viśeṣāḥ buddhi-bhedam kurvanti uta anupajāta-viśeṣāḥ iti/ kim ca ataḥ yadi
244.19	upajāta-viśeṣāḥ yaḥ asau viśeṣaḥ saḥ avayavin^ḥ iti prāptam, atha anupajāta-
244.20	viśeṣāḥ te eva atīndriyāḥ aindriyakāḥ ca iti mahān virodhaḥ/ viśeṣaḥ
245.01	saṃyogaḥ paramāṇūnām te paraspara-pratyāsatti^i satyām saṃyoga-upakaraṇāḥ
245.02	upalapsyante iti na atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām saṃyogaḥ pratyakṣaḥ bhavitum
245.03	arhati/ bhavati hi idam anena saṃyuktam iti/ tasmāt na saṃyogaḥ viśeṣaḥ/
245.04	yaḥ ca ayam anekasmin eka-pratyayaḥ saḥ mithyā-pratyayaḥ mithyā-pratyaya-nimittam ca
245.05	sāmānya-darśanam viśeṣa-anupalabdhiḥ na ca atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām sāmānya-
245.06	darśanam asti tad-abhāve kutaḥ viśeṣa-adarśanam, sāmānya-darśane viśeṣa-
245.07	adarśane vā asati na tad-viparīta-dharma-adhyāropaḥ/ na ca nimittam antareṇa
245.08	naimittakasya mithyā-pratyayasya prādurbhāvaḥ yuktaḥ iti/ etena bhāktaḥ
245.09	aupamikaḥ ca pratyayaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ bhaktiḥ nāma atathā-bhūtasya tathā
245.10	bhāvibhiḥ sāmānyam, ubhayena bhajyate iti bhaktiḥ yathā vāhīkasya
245.11	mandāmantaḥ-saṃjñām upādāya vāhīkaḥ go^ḥ iti na tad-atīndriya-tvāt
245.12	paramāṇūnām asti/ upamāna-pratyayaḥ api na yuktaḥ/ atad-bhāvinaḥ tad-bhāvibhiḥ
245.13	asāmānyāt yasya atathā-bhūtasya tathā bhāvibhiḥ sāmānyam sāmānyam tatra
245.14	upamāna-pratyayaḥ yuktaḥ yathā siṃhaḥ māṇavakaḥ iti saṃhaḥ iva siṃhaḥ
245.15	kim punar atra upamīyate sauryam, na tu paramāṇūnām atīndriya-tvāt mithyā-
245.16	pratyayānām nimittam asti iti/ mithyā-pratyayāḥ api ete na bhavanti
245.17	pradhāna-abhāvāt/ indriya-antara-viṣaya^su abheda-pratyayaḥ pradhānam iti cet/
245.18	atha manyase śrotra-ādi-viṣaya^su eka-ādi-pratyayāḥ pradhānam bhavanti tad-abhāve
245.19	tu mithyā-pratyayāḥ iti viśeṣa-hetu-abhāvāt dṛṣṭānta-avyavasthā/ evam sati
245.20	dṛṣṭānta-upādānam na vyavatiṣṭḥate tasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ atasmin tad iti
246.01	ca pratyayaḥ/ asti ca ayam paramāṇuṣu sañcita^su tad iti pratyayaḥ saḥ ca
246.02	kim atasmin tad iti āhosvit na iti sandihyate śabda-ādiṣu eka-pratyayaḥ
246.03	pradhānam ghaṭa-ādiṣu eka-pratyayaḥ mithyā-pratyayaḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ ghaṭa-ādīn
246.04	abhyupaiti na hi anabhyupagata^su ghaṭa-ādiṣu śabda-ādi-sāmānyam eka-pratyaya-bījam
246.06	paramāṇūnām sañcitānām śabdena sāmānyāt eka-pratyaya-utpatti-nimitta-
246.07	tvam iti/ tatra uktam/ na atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām iti/ yān ca śabda-ādīn
246.08	pradhāna-pratyaya-nimitta-tvena bhavān pratipadyate te api sañcitāḥ eva
246.09	sañcita-tvāt teṣu api ayam eka-pratyayaḥ na yuktaḥ śabda-ādiṣu ca eka-pratyayaḥ
246.10	samuccaya-nivṛtti-nimittaḥ eka-tvāt dravya-sāmānyāt bhāktaḥ yāni ekatvavanti
246.11	dravyāṇi teṣām samuccaya-nivṛttiḥ dharmaḥ asti ca śabda-ādiṣu samuccaya-
246.12	nivṛttiḥ tena ekatvavat-dravya-sāmānyāt śabda-ādiṣu eka-pratyayaḥ bhāktaḥ/ sarvatra
246.13	samuccaya-nivṛtti-nimittaḥ eva iti cet na dve iti atra eka-anekamatau sandehaḥ
246.14	na syāt/ yadi sarvatra ayam samuccaya-nivṛtti-nimittaḥ eva eka-pratyayaḥ bhavati
246.15	na dve iti atra kim ekam anekam iti dvi-pratiṣedha-sāmānyāt sandehaḥ na
246.16	syāt sati ca sandehe ekam iti niyama-artham yataḥ niyamaḥ tad ekatvam,
246.17	yataḥ tri-ādi-pratyayāḥ te tritva-ādyaḥ  iti/ evam dvitva-ādi-pratyayāḥ api
246.18	śabda-ādiṣu samuccaya-nimittāḥ draṣṭavyāḥ kasmāt dvitva-ādimat-dravya-sāmānyāt
246.19	dvitva-ādimatā dravyena śabda-ādīnām samuccayaḥ samānaḥ/ sarvatra samuccyāt
246.20	eva iti cet na ekam iti atra sāmānyamatau sāmānya-gati^i na saṃśayaḥ syāt yadi
246.21	samuccaya-nimitta-tvāt dvi-ādi-pratyayāḥ bhavanti na ekam iti pratiṣedhe dve
247.01	trīṇi iti sāmānyamatau sāmānya-gati^i na saṃśayaḥ syāt tasmāt yataḥ vyavasthā te
247.02	dvitva-ādyaḥ  iti/ yaḥ punar ekatvam dvitva-ādīn ca na pratipadyate tasya na
247.03	samuccayaḥ na samuccaya-nivṛttiḥ anabhyupagata-ekatvasya samuccayaḥ kasya anabhyupagata-
247.04	dvitvasya ca kasya pratiṣedhaḥ eka-dvitva-ādīn ca artha-antara-bhūtān anabhyupagacchataḥ
247.05	ekam śatam ekam dvitvam iti eka-pratyayaḥ na syāt/ bheda-agrahāt ca
247.06	śabda-ādiṣu eka-pratyayaḥ/ yasmāt śabda-ādiṣu bhedaḥ na gṛhyate upalabhyamāna^su
247.07	ataḥ teṣu abheda-pratyayaḥ na punar paramāṇuṣu abheda-grahaṇasya nimittam
247.08	asti atīndriya-tvāt aṇūnām iti uktam, udāharaṇa-mātra-tvāt ca eka-pratyaya-paryanuyogasya
247.09	eka-pratyaya-vat aneka-pratyayam api anuyoktavyaḥ/ yaḥ avayavinam artha-antaram na
247.10	pratipadyate tasya yathā eka-pratyayaḥ anupapannaḥ tathā aneka-pratyayāḥ api na
247.11	prāpnuvanti/ yathā ca eka-aneka-pratyayān evam ghaṭa-ādi-pratyayān paryanuyojyaḥ
247.12	iti/ evam parimāṇa-saṃyoga-praspanda-jāti-viśeṣa-pratyayān api paryanuyojyaḥ
247.13	teṣu ca evam prasaṅgaḥ/ yadi avayavinam artha-antaram na pratipadyase mahat iti
247.14	saṃyuktaḥ iti vā aśvaḥ gacchati iti pratyayāḥ na prāpnuvanti na hi atīndriya^su
247.15	paramāṇuṣu ete pratyayāḥ yujyante iti/ kim ete eka-ādi-pratyayāḥ
247.16	aṇu-sañcaya-mātre āhosvit artha-antara-bhāve iti sandihyate sandehe sati
247.17	viśeṣa-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ iti/ ayam viśeṣa-hetuḥ mahat-pratyaya-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt
247.18	eka-pratyayaḥ mahati yaḥ khalu yad-samānādhikaraṇaḥ pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
247.19	saḥ tad-dharma-yuktaḥ bhavati yathā nīlam utpalam iti ubhaya-dharma-yogini
247.20	nīla-utpala-pratyayaḥ pravartate asti ca mahat-sāmānādhikaraṇyam eka-pratyayasya
247.21	tasmāt mahati eka-pratyayaḥ/ aṇu-samūha-atiśaya-grahaṇam iti cet/
248.01	atha manyase na asmābhiḥ artha-antara-bhūtaḥ avayavin^ḥ abhyupagamyate api tu aṇu-samūham
248.02	upalabhya-aṇu-samūha-antare atiśayam gṛhṇataḥ mahat-pratyayaḥ bhavati/ saḥ ayam
248.03	atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ iti pūrvavat-prasaṅgaḥ iti/ amahatsu aṇuṣu amahatsu
248.04	ca aṇu-samūha^su yaḥ ayam mahat-pratyayaḥ saḥ atasmin tad iti mithyā-pratyayaḥ
248.05	mithyā-pratyayaḥ ca pradhānam antareṇa na bhavati iti pūrvavat eva prasaṅgaḥ/ aṇuḥ
248.06	śabdaḥ mahān iti vyavasāyāt pradhāna-siddhiḥ iti cet/ atha manyase
248.07	mahān śabdaḥ aṇuḥ śabdaḥ iti śabde dṛṣṭaḥ mahat-aṇu-pratyayaḥ pradhānam anyatra
248.08	mahat-aṇu-pratyayasya iti na manda-tīvratā-grahaṇam iyattā-anavadhāraṇāt na śabde
248.09	mahattvam na aṇutvam guṇa-tvāt aṇu-mahat-pratyayaḥ tarhi śabde na prāpnoti/ na
248.10	na prāpnoti/ anya-nimitta-tvāt/ kim nimittam mandatā tīvratā
248.11	ca kasmāt iyattā-anavadhāraṇāt na hi ayam mahān śabdaḥ iti vyavasyan
248.12	iyān iti avadhārayati/ yathā kuvala-āmalaka-vilvāni avadhārayan kuvalāt
248.13	āmalakam mahat āmalakāt ca mahat vilvam tāni ca iyattayā paricchinatti
248.14	iyat-parimāṇam etad iti na punar śabda^su tad asti śabda^su api
248.15	pūrva-avadhṛtam śabdam upalabhya tasmāt śabdāt ayam mahān aṇuḥ vā iti avadhāryate
248.16	na avadhāraṇam pratiṣidhyate/ idam tu cintyate/ yaḥ ayam śabde aṇu-mahat-pratyayaḥ
248.17	kim ayam parimāṇa-yogāt uta anyasmāt nimittāt iti/ anyasmāt nimittāt
248.18	iti yuktam/ guṇasya guṇa-antara-sambandha-anupalabdhi^aḥ/ na hi guṇasya
248.19	guṇa-antara-sambandhaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yataḥ parimāṇa-sambandhaḥ api gamyeta mahat-pratyayāt eva
248.20	guṇavān śabdaḥ dravya-vat iti cet na/ atha ayam mahat-pratyayaḥ dravya^su
248.21	bhavat-mahat-parimāṇa-yogāt bhavati/ śabde api ca ayam mahat-pratyayaḥ bhavati/
249.01	ataḥ mahat-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt mahat-parimāṇa-yuktaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ na anaikāntāt/
249.02	mahat-pratyaya-viṣaya-tve sati api mahat-parimāṇa-yogaḥ na dṛṣṭaḥ/
249.03	yathā mahat-parimāṇaḥ iti/ dravye tarhi mahat-pratyayaḥ katham, yadi
249.04	tāvat mahat-pratyaya-viṣaya-tve sati api mahat-parimāṇayogam na pratipadyase
249.05	mahat-parimāṇa-yuktam dravyam iti etat katham, na mahat-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt
249.06	mahat-parimāṇa-yuktam dravyam/ api tu mahat-parimāṇa-yoge sati
249.07	mahat-pratyayaḥ dravyaḥ iti/ mahat-parimāṇa-yogaḥ tu dravyasya viśeṣa-pratyayānām
249.08	anākasmika-tvena/ yadi viśeṣa-pratyayānām anākasmika-tvena mahat-
249.09	parimāṇayogaḥ dravye śabde api evam, śabdasya mahat-pratyayaḥ asti tena api
249.10	na akasmāt bhavitavyam/ kaḥ ca evam āha śabde api ayam ākasmikaḥ iti/ kim-nimittaḥ
249.11	manda-tīvratā-nimittaḥ mandatā tīvratā ca śabda-dharmaḥ ataḥ
249.12	aṇu-mahat-pratyayaḥ śabde iti/ kā punar iyam mandatā tīvratā ca aṇu-
249.13	dravya-sāmānyāt sāmānya-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam mandatā mahat-dravya-sāmānyāt ca
249.14	sāmānya-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam tīvratā pāṭavam iti/ saṃyukte iti dvitva-
249.15	samāna-āśrayam prāpti-grahaṇam avayavinaḥ pratipādakam, katham iti saṃyukta-
249.16	mahat-pratyayau eka-viṣayau pratisandhānāt nīla-utpala-ādi-pratyaya-vat yathā ca
249.17	nīlam utpalam ca iti pratisandhīyamānau pratyayau eka-viṣayau bhavataḥ tathā
249.18	saṃyukte mahatī iti ca pratisandhānam asti tasmāt pratisandhānāt eka-
249.19	viṣayau iti/ etena ekam mahat calati khadiraḥ iti pratyayāḥ vyākhyātāḥ/
249.20	samudāya-dvaya-apekṣaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase dvau samudāyau āśritya
249.21	saṃyukta-mahat-pratyayau bhavataḥ/ tathā eka-ādi-mahat-pratyayāḥ api eka-samudāya-apekṣāḥ
250.01	iti cet na/ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ samudāya-apekṣāḥ saṃyukta-mahat-ādi-pratyayāḥ
250.02	kaḥ ayam samudāyaḥ kim anekaḥ samudāyin^ḥ uta anekasya prāptiḥ iti/
250.03	anekasya prāptiḥ samudāyaḥ iti cet prāptī prāpte iti pratyayaḥ syāt
250.04	kim kāraṇam, samudāyasya prāpti-tvena abhyupagamāt/ ime vastunī saṃyukte
250.05	iti na syāt samudāyaḥ samudāya-antara-prāpti^i eka^bhūtaḥ iti prāpti-
250.06	pratyayaḥ na syāt samudāyasya eka-tvāt/ atha anekaḥ samudāyin^ḥ samudāyaḥ
250.07	dvitvena sāmānādhikaraṇyam na syāt saṃyukte ime vastunī iti dvitva-
250.08	samānādhikaraṇaḥ pratyayaḥ na syāt tasmāt mahatī dvitva-āśraya-bhūte dravye
250.09	saṃyogasya sthānam iti/ saṃyogaḥ asti iti cet uktam atra yathā artha-antaram
250.10	saṃyogaḥ iti/ idam ca avayava-samudāya-vādin^ḥ praṣṭavyaḥ/ yaḥ ayam go^ḥ aśvaḥ puruṣaḥ
250.11	iti jāti-nimittaḥ pratyayaḥ saḥ kva bhavati iti na eva jātiḥ asti kutaḥ
250.12	jāti-nimittaḥ pratyayaḥ bhaviṣyati iti/ jāti-viśeṣasya pratyaya-anuvṛtti-
250.13	liṅgasya apratyākhyānam na jāti-viśeṣam antareṇa pratyaya-anuvṛtti^aḥ nimittam
250.14	asti na ca nirnimittaḥ pratyayaḥ nāmā sthātum nāma āsthātum arhati/ asti ca ayam
250.15	anuvṛttaḥ pratyayaḥ imāni kuṇḍalāni iti/ asti ca vyāvṛtti-pratyayaḥ
250.16	na imāni kuṇḍalāni iti tasmāt jāti-viśeṣaḥ na pratyaya-ākhyeyaḥ vyadhikaraṇasya
250.17	anabhivyakti^aḥ adhikaraṇa-vacanam na ca ayam jāti-viśeṣaḥ vyadhikaraṇaḥ
250.18	vyajyate iti adhikaraṇam avaśyam vaktavyam, anu-samavasthānam adhikaraṇam
250.19	iti/ atha manyase paramāṇu^āḥ eva kena cit samavasthānena avatiṣṭhamānāḥ
250.20	tām tām jātim vyañjayanti ataḥ na avayavin^ḥ sidhyati iti tatra tāvat
251.01	uktam samavasthānam saṃyogaḥ artha-antaram iti/ yad ca tad-samavasthānam jāti-
251.02	viśeṣam vyanakti iti manyase kim tad cakṣuṣā prāptam vyanakti āhosvit aprāptam
251.03	iti/ prāptam vyanakti iti cet madhya-bhāga-para-bhāgayoḥ aprāpta-tvāt anabhivyaktiḥ/
251.04	saḥ yadi prāpte samavasthāne jāti-viśeṣaḥ vyajyate madhya-bhāga-
251.05	para-bhāgayoḥ arvāc-bhāga-vyavadhānāt aprāpti^i anabhivyaktiḥ yāvat prāptam tāvati
251.06	vyaktiḥ iti cet na tāvataḥ adhikaraṇa-tvāt/ atha manyase yāvat cakṣuṣā
251.07	samavasthānam prāptam tāvati bhavati jāti-vyaktiḥ tāvat eva tarhi adhikaraṇam
251.08	jāti^aḥ prāptam, evam sati pratīyamāna-bhedaḥ yaḥ ayam vṛkṣaḥ iti
251.09	samudāyaḥ pratīyate tasya vṛkṣasya bhedaḥ prāpnoti/ kim kāraṇam/
251.10	yāvati yāvati jātiḥ vyajyate saḥ saḥ vṛkṣaḥ iti prāpnoti tataḥ ca ekaḥ vṛkṣaḥ
251.11	anekaḥ iti pratīyate iti eka-pratyayaḥ na syāt/ arvāc-bhāgasya ca avṛkṣa-
251.12	tvāt na kva cit vṛkṣatvam iti vṛkṣa-buddhi^aḥ viṣayaḥ vaktavyaḥ tasmāt samudita-
251.13	aṇu-sthāna-artha-antarasya jāti-viśeṣa-abhivyakti-hetu-tvāt avayavin^ḥ artha-antara-bhūtaḥ
251.14	iti/ samuditāḥ aṇu^āḥ sthānam yasya saḥ ayam samudhita-aṇu-sthānaḥ
251.15	samudita-aṇu-sthānaḥ ca asau artha-antaram ca tasya jāti-viśeṣa-vyakti-hetu-tvam
251.16	na aṇūnām iti sidhyati avayavin^ḥ artha-antara-bhūtaḥ anabhyupagata-artha-antara-avayavinā
251.17	paramāṇu-śabdasya arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ paramāṇu-śabdasya parama-apakarṣa-vāci-tvāt/
251.18	na hi asati mahati na ca asati aṇu^u paramāṇuḥ iti viśeṣaṇam yuktam/
251.19	parimāṇa-prakarṣa-yoge sati paramāṇuḥ iti viśeṣaṇam arthavat asati anarthakam
251.20	iti/ saḥ ca ayam parimāṇa-prakarṣa-yogaḥ sati avayavini siddhyati iti
252.01	sattva-rajas-tamasām sarva-apakṛṣṭaḥ saṃghātaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti kasya cit darśanam
252.02	tad na yuktam iti paśyāmaḥ vyāghātāt sarva-apakṛṣṭaḥ saṃghātaḥ ca iti vyāhataḥ
252.03	na hi saṃhanyamānam antareṇa saṃghātaḥ sambhavati artha-antaram paṭāt tantu^āḥ
252.04	tad-hetu-tvāt turī-ādi-vat iti/ turī-ādi-paṭa-kāraṇam artha-antaram iti dṛṣṭam/
252.05	tathā ca tantu^āḥ tasmāt artha-antaram iti/ sāmarthya-bhedāt viṣa-agada-vat
252.06	bhinna-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt rūpa-sparśa-vat/ tantu-paṭa-rūpe bhinna-kāraṇe viśeṣavat-
252.07	tvāt rūpa-sparśa-vat/ eke tu bhinna-rūpa-tvāt iti evam hetum kṛtvā paramāṇunā
252.08	pratyavatiṣṭhante/ ekasmin api paramāṇu^u rūpa-bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ raktaḥ śyāmaḥ
252.09	iti ca/ saḥ tu evam pratyavastheyaḥ na hetu-artha-aparijñānāt/ na brūmaḥ
252.10	bhinna-rūpa-tvāt anyaḥ avayavin^ḥ avayava^bhyaḥ api tu tantu-paṭa-rūpe bhinna-kāraṇe
252.11	iti pakṣayitvā viśeṣavat-tvāt iti hetum brūmaḥ/ tathā ca ayam apratiṣedhaḥ
252.12	bahu-grantha-vistāriṇī tu eṣā kathā iti uparamyate paramparayā parīkṣitam
252.13	pratyakṣam//
252.14	atha idānīm avasara-prāptam anumānam parīkṣyate/ kim idam anumānam/
252.15	uktam tad-pūrvakam anumānam iti/ atra ca pūrvavat-ādi^aḥ udāharaṇāni/ nadī-
252.16	pūraḥ pipīlikā-aṇḍa-sañcaram mayūra-vāsitam iti/ udāharaṇa-vyabhicāra-
252.17	dvārakam sūtram//
252.18	[2-1-37(35)]rodha-upaghāta-sādṛśya^bhyaḥ vyabhicārāt anumānam
252.19	apramāṇam
252.20	rodha-upaghāta-sādṛśya^bhyaḥ vyabhicārāt anumānam apramāṇam iti/ tatra
252.21	rodhaḥ nāma dravatva-pratibandha-hetuḥ upaghātaḥ pipīlikā-gṛha-upadravaḥ
253.01	sādṛśyam mayūra-śabda-puruṣa-śabdayoḥ samāna-pratyaya-kartṛ-tvam ebhyaḥ vyabhicārāt
253.02	anumānam apramāṇam iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ yad tāvat anumānam apramāṇam
253.03	iti uktam tad ayuktam/ pratijñā-padayoḥ vyāghātāt anumānam iti sambaddhasya
253.04	atīndriya-artha-sādhakasya etat nāma/ apramāṇam iti tasya pratiṣedhaḥ/ saḥ ayam
253.05	pratijñā-padayoḥ vyāghātaḥ anujñāta-pratiṣiddhayoḥ ekatva-anupalabdhiḥ pratijñā-
253.06	hetu^oḥ ca virodhaḥ anumānam apramāṇam iti pratijñā rodha-upaghāta-sādṛśya^bhyaḥ
253.07	vyabhicārāti iti hetuḥ/ yad vyabhicārin tad apramāṇam iti/
253.08	yathā go-siddhi^i viṣāṇitvam saḥ ayam hetuḥ pratijñām vyāhanti pratijñā
253.09	ca hetum iti/ yad ca idam ucyate anumānam apramāṇam iti tad kim anumāna-
253.10	mātrasya apramāṇatvam uta anumāna-viśeṣasya iti/ kim ca ataḥ yadi anumāna-mātram
253.11	apramāṇam iti pakṣaḥ hetu^aḥ ekadeśa-vṛtti-tvāt avyāpaka-tvam/ samastam
253.12	anumānam pakṣitam na sarvasmin anumāne vyabhicāraḥ asti/ na hi vyabhicārasya
253.13	apramāṇatva-sādhakasya avyabhicāraḥ śakyaḥ na hi vyabhicāre avyabhicārasya
253.14	kim cit pramāṇam asti iti avyāpaka-tvāt ahetuḥ/ atha yad vyabhicārin
253.15	tad apramāṇam iti brūṣe pratijñā-viśeṣaṇa-tvāt hetu^aḥ hetu-antaram racanīyam
253.16	siddha-sādhanam ca etat yad vyabhicārin tad pramāṇam na bhavati/  yāni api
253.17	etāni udāharaṇāni bhavatā vyabhicārīṇi iti ucyante teṣu api vyabhicāraḥ
253.18	na asti iti asiddhatā-udbhāvana-dvārakam sūtram//
253.19	[2-1-38(36)]na ekadeśa-trāsa-sādṛśya^bhyaḥ artha-antara-bhāvāt
253.20	na ekadeśa-trāsa-sādṛśya^bhyaḥ artha-antara-bhāvāt/ yad tāvat rodhāt api nadī-
253.21	pūraḥ bhavati iti na atra nadī-pūra-mātrena upari vṛṣṭimad-deśa-sambandhi-tvam
254.01	nadīāḥ sādhyate api tu pūra-viśeṣe vā tasya avyabhicārāt asiddhaḥ upaghātāt
254.02	pipīlikā-aṇḍa-sañcāra-mātrasya ca vyabhicāraḥ na sañcaraṇa-viśeṣasya
254.03	kaḥ punar asau viśeṣaḥ/ avitrastānām bhūyasīnām bhūyas^su sthāna^su
254.04	pipīlikā-piṇḍa-bhūyastvena ūrdhva-sañcāraṇam yad tad anāgata-vṛṣṭi-avinābhāvin
254.05	śabda-vyabhicāraḥ api na asti yasmāt na śabda-mātram anumānam mayūra-
254.06	asti-tve na ca atra mayūraḥ sādhyate api tu śabda-gatāt aviśeṣāt pakṣi-
254.07	anuṣaṅga-ādi^aḥ śabdaḥ mayūra-viśiṣṭaḥ anumīyate evam hi vyabhicāraḥ
254.08	na asti iti asiddhaḥ hetuḥ/  tri-kāla-viṣayam anumānam traikālya-grahaṇāt iti uktam
254.09	tatra tāvat kālaḥ eva na asti iti ke cit/ tad ca na pramāṇa-abhāvāt na
254.10	hi kāla-asat-tve pramāṇam asti iti/ nanu ca anupalabdhiḥ kāla-asat-tvam
254.11	pratipādayati na pratipādayati vyabhicārāt tri-kāraṇā iyam anupalabdhiḥ
254.12	tatra anupalabdhi^ā kāraṇa-vaikalyam gamyeta na tu upalabhyam eva na asti iti etat eva
254.13	kutaḥ/ atha upalabhyamānasya abhāvāt anupalabdhiḥ iti ayam pratijñā-arthaḥ tathā
254.14	ca hetuḥ na asti pratijñā-arthena ākṣipta-tvāt ākṣiptaḥ hetuḥ pratijñā-arthaḥ
254.15	na iti/ na ca asati dharmiṇi dharmaḥ sambhavati āśraya-abhāvāt/ atha
254.16	dharmaḥ api na asti sādhana-arthaḥ hīyate na hi asataḥ sādhana-artham paśyāmaḥ parāpara-
254.17	ādi-pratyayānām ca sanimitta-tvāt kāla-pratiṣedhaḥ na yuktaḥ yaḥ ca kālam
254.18	na pratipadyate tena parāpara-ādi-pratyayānām nimittam vaktavyam/ na
254.19	hi asati nimitte naimittikān kāla-viśeṣavataḥ pratyayān paśyāmaḥ
254.20	rūpa-ādi-pratyaya-vat iti/ kāla-eka-tvāt pratyaya-bheda-anupapattiḥ iti cet/
255.01	atha manyase kāla-eka-tve sati na yuktaḥ parāpara-ādi-pratyaya-bhedaḥ na abhinna-
255.02	nimittam bhinna-kāryam karoti iti na sāpekṣa-tvāt/ pitṛ-ādi-pratyaya-
255.03	bheda-vat iti/ yathā ekasmin puruṣe aneka-sambandha-bheda-anuvidhāyini abhinne
255.04	pitṛ^ḥ putraḥ bhrātṛ^ḥ iti pratyayāḥ bhavanti tadvat ekaḥ kālaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-viśeṣa-
255.05	apekṣaḥ parāpara-ādi-pratyaya-hetuḥ iti/ evam upapāditaḥ kālaḥ/
255.06	tasya upapannasya vartamāna-kāla-pratiṣedha-artham sūtram//
255.07	[2-1-39(37)]vartamāna-abhāvaḥ patataḥ patita-patitavya-kāla-upapatti^aḥ
255.08	vartamāna-abhāvaḥ patataḥ patita-patitavya-kāla-upapatti^aḥ/ vṛntāt pracyutasya
255.09	phalasya bhūmin pratyāsīdataḥ yad ūrdhvam phalam avadhim kṛtvā yāvat vṛntam
255.10	iti saḥ patitaḥ adhvan^ḥ yad adhastāt patatam avadhim kṛtvā yad bhūmiḥ iti
255.11	saḥ patitavyaḥ adhvan^ḥ na idānīm tṛtīyaḥ adhvan^ḥ vidyate yatra patati iti syāt/
255.12	na ca adhvānam antareṇa kāle vyakti-hetuḥ asti tasmāt vartamānaḥ kālaḥ
255.13	na asti iti vartamānaḥ kālaḥ na asti iti bruvan abhyupagatau kālau bādhate/
255.14	etat anujñāpana-artham sūtram//
255.15	[2-1-40(38)]tayoḥ api abhāvaḥ vartamāna-abhāve tad-apekṣa-tvāt
255.16	tayoḥ api abhāvaḥ vartamāna-abhāve tad-apekṣa-tvāt/ vartamāna-sāpekṣau
255.17	itarau kālau yasya vartamānaḥ na asti tasya vartamāna-anabhyupagame abhyupagatayoḥ
255.18	kālayoḥ abhāvaḥ iti/ na ca ayam kālaḥ adhva-vyaṅgyaḥ kim tarhi
255.19	kriyā-vyaṅgyaḥ patati iti yadā dravya-kriyā-sambandham upalabhate saḥ ayam sambandha-
255.20	viśiṣṭaḥ kālaḥ vartamānaḥ ucyate kriyā-sambandha-atikramāt atītaḥ yadā tu
255.21	kriyā-kāraṇāni abhimukhyena avasthitāni apratibaddha-śaktikāni tadā bhaviṣyati
256.01	kriyā iti anāgataḥ kālaḥ iti/ yadi ca ayam vartamānām kriyām
256.02	na pratipadyate kasmāt atītām anāgatām ca pratipadyate na kālaḥ
256.03	atītaḥ na phalam atītam/ kālasya sarvadā vartamāna-tvāt phalasya ca
256.04	apatat patati patiṣyati iti/ kriyā-viśeṣaṇa-tvena pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt kriyāyām
256.05	sambhavaḥ na phale iti kiryā kāla-abhivyakti-nimittam na adhvan^ḥ gantavyasya
256.06	tādavasthyāt yaḥ asau gantavyaḥ deśaḥ saḥ yathā-bhūtaḥ eva anutpanna-kriye
256.07	phale tathā tathā-bhūtaḥ eva utpanna-kriyaḥ iti abhedāt na adhvan^ḥ kāla-vyakti-hetuḥ/
256.08	atha manyase vartamāna-kāla-anabhyupagame api atīta-anāgatau itaretara-apekṣau
256.09	setsyete etat pratiṣedha-artham sūtram//
256.10	[2-1-41(39)]na atīta-anāgatayoḥ itaretara-apekṣā siddhiḥ
256.11	na atīta-anāgatayoḥ itaretara-apekṣā siddhiḥ iti/ na hi atīta-anāgatau
256.12	itaretara-apekṣau siddhyataḥ/ katham iti vartamāna-abhāve kim atītam katham
256.13	vā iti kim anāgatam katham ca iti na śakyate vaktum avyākaraṇīyam etat vartamāna-
256.14	lopaḥ iti/ viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt ca/ vartamānaḥ abhyupagataḥ yasmāt ayam
256.15	pratiṣedhaḥ patataḥ patita-patitavya-kāla-upapattiḥ iti/ na ca vartamāna-
256.16	kāla-anabhyupagame patataḥ iti padasya arthaḥ asti na ca etat pada-artha-anabhyupagame
256.17	patataḥ iti yuktam vaktum/ saḥ ayam vyāghātaḥ vartamānaḥ ca na asti
256.18	patataḥ iti ca prayogaḥ na ca itaretara-apekṣayā kasya cit siddhiḥ iti/
256.19	yasmāt eka-abhāve anyatara-abhāvāt dvaya-abhāvaḥ/ yadi itaretara-apekṣayā siddhiḥ
256.20	syāt yāvat itaram na asti tāvat itaram na asti iti ubhayoḥ abhāvaḥ  artha-
256.21	sattā-vyaṅgyaḥ ca vartamāna-kālaḥ vidyate guṇaḥ vidyate karman iti yasya ca ayam
256.22	na asti tasya//
257.01	[2-1-42(40)]vartamāna-abhāve sarva-agrahaṇam pratyakṣa-anupapatti^aḥ
257.02	vartamāna-abhāve sarva-agrahaṇam pratyakṣa-anupapatti^aḥ/ sūtra-arthaḥ avirodhaḥ katham
257.03	punar vartamāna-abhāve pratyakṣa-anupapattiḥ yasmāt idam pratyakṣam vartamāna-ādhāram
257.04	kārya-tvāt kṣīra-vat iti/ yad kāryam tad vartamāna-ādharam dṛṣṭam yathā kṣīram
257.05	kāryam ca pratyakṣam tasmāt vartamāna-ādhāram iti/ vartamāna-kāla-anabhyupagamāt
257.06	anādhāram pratyakṣam prāpnoti/ anādhāram ca kāryam na asti iti pratyakṣa-
257.07	anupapattiḥ pratyakṣa-anupapatti^aḥ ca sarva-pramāṇa-lopaḥ sarva-pramāṇa-lope ca
257.08	sarvasya grahaṇam na syāt/ asti ca sarvasya grahaṇam sarvasya grahaṇāt paśyāmaḥ
257.09	asti vartamānaḥ pratyakṣa-nimittam pratyakṣa-viṣayaḥ pratyakṣam ca iti saḥ ayam
257.10	vartamānaḥ kālaḥ iti  upapannaḥ ca san dvedhā vibhidyate kva cit kriyā-
257.11	mātra-vyaṅgyaḥ kva cit kriyā-santāna-vyaṅgyaḥ kriyā-mātra-vyaṅgyaḥ vidyate
257.12	dravyam iti kriyā-santāna-vyaṅgyaḥ pacati chinatti iti/ kriyā-santānaḥ
257.13	api dvedhā bhavati eka-ākāra-kriyaḥ nānā-ākāra-kriyaḥ ca iti/ eka-ākāra-
257.14	kriyaḥ chinatti iti/ tatra hi udyamya udyamya nipāta-kriyā eka-ākārā
257.15	nānā-ākāra-kriyaḥ ca santānaḥ pacati iti/ tatra hi anekā kriyā aneka-
257.16	ākārā tad yathā edha-upasarpaṇam agni-abhijvālanam sthāli-adhiśrayaṇam udaka-āsecanam
257.17	taṇḍula-pacanam darvī-ghaṭanam adha- maṇda-śrāvaṇam avatāraṇam iti/ saḥ hi evam-bhūtaḥ kriyā-
257.18	santānaḥ pacati iti anena eka-śabdena abhidhīyate kriyā-santānasya hi atra avicchedaḥ
257.19	vivakṣitaḥ nānā-ārambhaḥ na uparamaḥ iti santāna-anārambha-vivakṣāyām
257.20	pakṣyati iti uparama-vivakṣāyām apākṣīt iti/ yad ca idam chidyamānam pacyamānam
257.21	tad kriyamāṇam tasmin akriyamāṇe//
258.01	[2-1-43(41)]kṛtatā-kartavyatā-upapatti^aḥ tu ubhyathā grahaṇam
258.02	kṛtatā-kartavyatā-upapatti^aḥ tu ubhyathā grahaṇam/ kasya vartamānasya/ katham
258.03	ubhayathā/ kevalasya ca apavṛktasya atīta-anāgatābhyām sampṛktasya ca tābhyām
258.04	iti/ kava punar hi apavṛktasya vidyate dravyam iti atra hi kevalaḥ śuddhaḥ
258.05	vartamānaḥ abhidhīyate pacati chinatti iti atra sampṛktaḥ katham kāḥ cit atra
258.06	kriyāḥ vyatītāḥ kāḥ cit anāgatāḥ ekā ca vartamānā iti/ anyaḥ ca
258.07	loke pratyāsatti-prabhṛti^aḥ arthasya vivakṣāyām eka-prakāraḥ vartamānasya
258.08	prayogaḥ/ kadā cit ayam vartamānam atīte prayuṅkte āgataḥ san bravīti eṣaḥ
258.09	āgacchāmi iti kadā cit anāgate prayuṅkte yathā sthitaḥ san bravīti eṣaḥ
258.10	gacchāmi iti/ evam anya^su prayoga^su utprekṣitavyam iti//
258.11	atha idānīm upamānam avasara-prāptam parīkṣyate/ kim punar upamānam prasiddha-
258.12	sādharmyāt sādhya-sādhanam iti uktam tasya ākṣepaḥ//
258.13	[2-1-44(42)]atyanta-prāya-ekadeśa-sādharmyāt upamāna-asiddhiḥ
258.14	[2-1-45(43)]prasiddha-sādharmyāt upamāna-siddhi^aḥ yathā-ukta-doṣa-anupapattiḥ
258.15	atyanta-prāya-ekadeśa-sādharmyāt upamāna-asiddhiḥ iti/ na atyanta-sādharmyāt
258.16	upamānam bhavati yathā go^ḥ evam go^ḥ iti/ bhūyas sādharmyāt api
258.17	na upamānam siddhyati na hi bhavati yathā go^ḥ evam mahiṣaḥ iti/
258.18	ekadeśa-sādharmyāt api na bhavati yathā meruḥ tathā sarṣapaḥ iti/ na
258.19	ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt na upamānam siddhyati/ atyanta-ādi-sādharmyāt upamāna-
258.20	pratiṣedhāt na upamāna-pratiṣedhaḥ/ yad atyanta-prāya-ekadeśa-sādharmyāt upamānam
259.01	tad pratiṣiddhyate tena sūtrena punar upamāna-mātrasya pratiṣedhaḥ/
259.02	evam sati anyathā upapmānam tad-svarūpam vaktavyam/ atha na eva anyathā upamānasya
259.03	svarūpam asti iti manyase katham tarhi idam vākyam atyanta-prāya-ekadeśa-sādharmyāt
259.04	upamāna-asiddhiḥ iti/ anabhyupagacchatā hi upamānam evam vaktavyam,
259.05	upamānam na pramāṇam artha-apratipādaka-tvāt iti/  prasiddha-sādharmyasya vaidharmyasya
259.06	ca upamāna-hetu-tvāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ yaḥ ayam bhavatā pratiṣedhaḥ kriyate atyanta-
259.07	ādi-sādharmyāt upamānam na siddhyati iti ayam anupapannaḥ anabhyupagamāt/ kaḥ
259.08	khalu evam āha atyanta-ādi-sādharmyāt upamānam iti api tu prasiddha-sādharmyāt upamānam,
259.09	sādhya-sādhana-bhāvam āśritya tathā ca ayam apratiṣedhaḥ sarvathā abhāvāt
259.10	ca atyanta-sādharmyāt api upamānam siddhyati tad-kriyayā eva tad-kriyā upamīyate/
259.11	yathā rāma-rāvaṇayoḥ yuddham rāma-rāvaṇayoḥ iva iti/ bhūyas sādharmy^ina api
259.12	bhavati yadā go^aḥ bala-jijñāsā bhavati tadā bhūyas sādharmyena upamānam
259.13	yathā mahiṣaḥ tathā go^ḥ iti/ ekadeśa-sādharmyāt api bhavati yadā tu
259.14	sat-mātra-jijñāsā-nimittam bhavati meru^aḥ sattā kim rūpā iti yathā
259.15	sarṣapasya tathā meru^aḥ tasmāt apratiṣedhaḥ ayam iti/  astu tarhi upamānam
259.16	anumānam//
259.17	[2-1-46(44)]pratyakṣena apratyakṣa-siddhi^aḥ
259.18	[2-1-47(45)]na apratyakṣe gavaye pramāṇa-artham upamānasya paśyāmaḥ
259.19	iti
259.20	[2-1-48(46)]tathā iti upasaṃhārāt upamāna-siddhi^aḥ na aviśeṣaḥ
260.01	pratyakṣena pratyakṣa-siddhi^aḥ/ yathā pratyakṣena dhūma-dharmena ūrdhva-gati-ādinā
260.02	apratyakṣaḥ dhūma-dharmaḥ agniḥ anumīyate tathā pratyakṣena gavā apratyakṣasya gavayasya
260.03	pratipattiḥ iti apratyakṣa-artha-pratipādaka-tvāt upamānam anumānam iti/ na aparijñānāt/
260.04	na bhavatā upamāna-lakṣaṇam vyajñāyi//
260.05	na ca apratyakṣe gavaye upamāna-artham paśyāmaḥ/ atha pratyakṣe gavaye
260.06	kaḥ upamāna-arthaḥ yā asau saṃjñā-saṃjñi-sambandha-pratipattiḥ saḥ upamāna-arthaḥ/
260.07	evam na upamānam āgamaḥ na pratyakṣam na hi ayam āgamāt pratyakṣāt ca ayam gavayaḥ
260.08	iti saṃpratipadyate/  para-artha-tvāt ca para-artham ca upamānam bhavati na hi yathā
260.09	go^ḥ evam gavayaḥ iti etad āgamam antareṇa sārūpya-mātra-parijñānam upamānam/
260.10	na hi āgama-mātram sārūpya-parijñānam antareṇa upamānam bhavitum arhati/ na
260.11	ca evam anumānam/ tasmāt yathā liṅga-liṅgi-saṃbandha-smṛti-anugrahe sati liṅga-
260.12	parāmarśaḥ anumānam, tad ca pratyakṣam/ tathā āgama-āhita-saṃskāra-sambandha-
260.13	smṛti-apekṣam sārūpya-pratyakṣam upamānam iti//
260.14	pratipatti-bhedāt ca na anumānam evam bhavati/ yathā dhūmaḥ tathā agniḥ iti/
260.15	asti upamāne yathā go^ḥ evam gavayaḥ iti/ yatra pratipatti-bhedaḥ tad pramāṇa-
260.16	antaram/ yathā pratyakṣāt anumāna-pūrva-anadhigamāt upamānam na bhavati
260.17	iti anena pratyuktam//
260.18	atha avasara-prāptaḥ śabdaḥ parīkṣyate/ kaḥ punar ayam śabdaḥ/ āpta-
260.19	upadeśaḥ śabdaḥ iti uktam/ tasya ākṣepaḥ na śabdaḥ pramāṇam sati api aprasiddhi^aḥ/
260.20	yasmāt vidyamānaḥ api ayam śabdaḥ aśrūyamāṇaḥ na pratipatti-hetuḥ pratipatti-
260.21	hetuḥ ca pramāṇam iti/ viṣaya-abhāvāt ca na pratyakṣa-anumāna-vyatirekin^ḥ
260.22	viṣayaḥ śabdasya asti/ na ca aviṣayam pramāṇam iti avijñāna-ātmaka-
261.01	tvāt ca na pramāṇam, yad yad pramāṇam tad tad vijñāna-ātmakam yathā pratyakṣam,
261.02	avijñāna-ātmakam ca na pramāṇam ghaṭa-ādi-vat eva bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ca
261.03	ghaṭa-ādi-vat eva na pramāṇam, ātmani asamavāyāt/ na ghaṭa-ādi-vat eva
261.04	pramāṇam, ākāśa-guṇa-tvāt saṃkhyā-vat na pramāṇam yad tāvat sati api
261.05	apramiti^aḥ iti/ tad na asiddha-tvāt/ na hi kadā cit ca upalabhyamānaḥ apramāṇam
261.06	bhavati anupalabhyamānaḥ na bhavati iti na asau pramāṇam/ pramāṇam
261.07	hi nāma yena pramīyate kadā ca tena pramīyate yadā upalabhyate/ yad
261.08	punar etad viṣaya-abhāvāt iti kaḥ evam āha pratyakṣa-anumāna-vyatirekin^ḥ
261.09	viṣayaḥ na asti iti asiddhaḥ hetuḥ yathā pratyakṣa-ādibhyaḥ bhedaḥ tathā uktam/
261.10	yad punar etad avijñāna-ātmaka-tvāt bāhya-karaṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ātmani asamavāyāt
261.11	ca iti tad na anekāntāt pradīpa-vat pradīpa-vat sarvam asti pramāṇam
261.12	ca pradīpaḥ pratyakṣa-aṅga-tvāt iti uktam/ tasmāt anaikāntika-tvāt ahetuḥ ataḥ
261.13	yad punar etad ākāśa-guṇa-tvāt ayam api anaikāntikaḥ eva/ yathā ākāśe yad
261.14	parimāṇam tad mahat-pratyaya-kartṛ-tvāt pramāṇam bhavati tasmāt ākāśa-guṇa-tvāt
261.15	iti ayam ahetuḥ/ anumānam śabdaḥ smṛti-apekṣa-tvāt yasya smṛti-apekṣi-tvam
261.16	tad anumānam smṛti-apekṣaḥ ca śabdaḥ artha-pratyāyane tasmāt anumānam tri-kāla-
261.17	viṣaya-tvāt ca tri-kāla-viṣayam anumānam tathā ca śabdaḥ tasmāt anumānam/
261.18	anvaya-vyatireka-upapatti^aḥ yad anvaya-vyatirekin tad anumānam,
261.19	yathā dhūma-viśeṣa-darśanam agnimat-pratīti^i tathā ca śabdaḥ anvaya-vyatirekābhyām
261.20	eva artha pratipādayati tasmāt anumānam//
262.01	[2-1-49(47)]śabdaḥ anumānam arthasya anupalabdhi^aḥ anumeya-tvāt
262.02	etaḥ ca anumānam śabdaḥ arthasya anupalabdhi^aḥ/ pratyakṣena anupalabhyamāna-artha-
262.03	viṣaya-tvāt iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
262.04	[2-1-50(48)]upalabdhi^aḥ advi-pravṛtti-tvāt
262.05	itaḥ ca anumānam upalabdhi^aḥ advi-pravṛtti-tvāt/ prāmāṇa- pramāṇa-antara-bhāve upalabdhi-
262.06	hetu^i bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yathā pratyakṣe anumāne ca/ anyathā hi pratyakṣe
262.07	upalabdhiḥ anyathā anumāne na tu evam śabde iti/ tasmāt na pramāṇa-antaram//
262.08	[2-1-51(49)]sambandhāt ca
262.09	sambandhāt ca/ sambaddha-artha-pratipādaka-tvāt ca iti sūtra-arthaḥ sambaddha-artha-
262.10	pratipādakam anumānam tathā ca śabdaḥ iti/  yad tāvat smṛti-apekṣa-
262.11	tvāt anumānam śabdaḥ iti tad na anekāntāt/ anumāne api smṛti-apekṣitvam
262.12	asti yathā saṃśaye yathā tarke yathā upamāne iti/ etena
262.13	tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvam pratyuktam/ yad punar etad anvaya-vyatireka-upapatti^aḥ iti/
262.14	anvaya-vyatireka-upapattiḥ pratyakṣe api/ yathā yatra ghaṭaḥ tatra ghaṭa-jñānan/
262.15	yatra na asti tatra tad-abhāvaḥ iti/ yad punar etad anupalabhyamāna-
262.16	artha-viṣaya-tvāt iti satyam anupalabhyamāna-artha-viṣayam anumānam na
262.17	punar svarga-apūrva-devatā-ādi-apekṣa-laukikasya pratyakṣam anumānam vā api tu
262.18	āpta-upadeśa-sāmarthyāt śabdāt arthe saṃpratyayaḥ//
262.19	[2-1-52(50)]āpta-upadeśa-sāmarthyāt śabdāt arthe saṃpratyayaḥ
262.20	na hi ayam śabda-mātrāt svarga-ādīn pratipadyate/ kim tu puruṣa-
263.01	viśeṣa-abhihita-tvena pramāṇatvam pratipadya tathā-bhūtāt śabdāt svarga-ādi
263.02	pratipadyate na ca evam anumāne tasmāt na anumānam śabdaḥ iti/ evam
263.03	ca ullikhitā pratipattiḥ śabdāt na anumānāt iti/ etena advi-pravṛtti-tvam
263.04	vyākhyātam/ yad punar etad sambandhāt ca iti/ asti śabda-arthayoḥ
263.05	sambandhaḥ anujñātaḥ asti ca pratiṣiddhaḥ vācya-vācaka-bhāva-lakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ
263.06	anujñātaḥ/ asya śabdasya ayam arthaḥ vācyaḥ iti/ yaḥ punar ayam svābhāvikaḥ
263.07	śabda-arthayoḥ sambandhaḥ iti/ kaiḥ cit abhyupagamyate saḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ iti
263.08	na svābhāvikaḥ śabda-arthayoḥ sambandhaḥ asti katham//
263.09	[(2-1-51)]pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ
263.10	pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ/ tatra pratyakṣena tāvat śabda-arthasya anupalabdhiḥ
263.11	atīndriya-viṣaya-tvāt/ yasmāt śabdasya atīndriyaḥ api arthaḥ viṣayaḥ tasmāt na
263.12	pratyakṣena upalabdhiḥ iti/  anumānena upalapsyate iti cet na anumānena
263.13	vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ śabdaḥ vā artha-deśam upasamp0adyate arthaḥ vā śabda-deśam
263.14	ubhayam vā na tāvat arthaḥ śabda-deśam upasampadyate//
263.15	[2-1-53(52)]pūraṇa-pradāha-pāṭana-anupalabdhi^aḥ sambandha-abhāvaḥ
263.16	pūraṇa-pradāha-pāṭana-anupalabdhi^aḥ/ āsya ekadeśa-sthāna-karaṇa-uccāraṇīyaḥ
263.17	śabdaḥ iti/ anna-agni-asi-śabda-uccāraṇe pūraṇa-pradāha-pāṭanāni gṛhyeran/
263.18	mūrtimān padārthaḥ modaka-ādiḥ go-ādi-vat āgacchan upalabhyeta/ tataḥ ca
263.19	loka-vyavahāraḥ ucchidyeta/ atha śabdaḥ artha-antikam upasampadyate tasya
264.01	api anupapattiḥ vyāghātāt/ nityaḥ śabdaḥ santāna-vṛtti^ā ca āgacchati iti
264.02	vyāhatam/ atha na gacchati artha-deśe eva bhavati iti nityaḥ ca bhavati iti
264.03	cet na anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ sthāna-karaṇa-prayatna-asambhavāt ca artha-deśe na bhavati/
264.04	atha na gacchati na bhavati tatra vyajyate ekaḥ vitatya-avasthitaḥ iti
264.05	cet na sarva-artha-upalabdhi-prasaṅgāt/ yadi sarva-śabdāḥ eva vitatya-avasthitāḥ
264.06	ca sthāna-karaṇa-ādi-nimitta-sannidhāne vyajyante iti manyase sarva-arthānām
264.07	upalabdhiḥ prāptā yad ekasya śabdasya vyañjakam tad śabda-antarāṇām api iti/
264.08	vitatya-avasthitaḥ ca śabdaḥ vyaktaḥ iti sva-viṣayam vyāpya-avasthāne sati yatra
264.09	kva cana avasthitānām go-śabdaḥ abhivyakti^i kakuda-ādimat-artha-pratyāyanam syāt
264.10	yathā sāmānyam sva-viṣayam vyāpya-avasthitam saḥ ca sarvatra upalabhyate tathā
264.11	śabdaḥ api sva-viṣayam vyāpya-avasthitaḥ iti niyamaḥ bhaviṣyati/ na
264.12	yuktam evam bhavitum, sāmānyasya āśraya-upalabdhiḥ vyakti-hetuḥ na ca punar
264.13	śabdasya āśraya-upalabdhiḥ asti na ca vācye vṛttiḥ tasmāt asamaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
264.14	yadi ca vācya-artha-vṛttiḥ śabdaḥ syāt vācya-upalabdhi^i akṛta-samayena api
264.15	pratipādyeta yathā sāmānyam akṛta-samayaḥ api pratipadyate iti/ tasmāt
264.16	na anumānāt api pratipadyate/ itaḥ ca sva-ālayāt anyena api na sambandhin^ḥ
264.17	śabdaḥ guṇa-tvāt anyena go-ādinā śabdam prati samavāyaḥ api na asti
264.18	śabdasya ākāśa-vṛtti-tvāt/ na api śabda-arthayoḥ eka-artha-samavāyaḥ tābhyām
264.19	ekasya anārambhāt/ upamānāt pratipatsyate iti na upamānasya api
264.20	na ayam viṣayaḥ/ āgamāt pratipatsyate iti saḥ eva atra vicāryate kim
264.21	śabda-arthau sambaddhau āhosvit asambaddhau iti/ yad idam bhavatā abhidhīyate
265.01	smṛti-ādi-apekṣa-tvāt anumāna-sādharmyāt anumānam śabdaḥ iti/ tasmāt eva na anumānam
265.02	na hi tena eva tasya sādharmyam iti/ atha smṛti-ādi-sādharmyāt
265.03	śabda-anumānayoḥ eka-tvam evam tarhi ekam pramāṇam prāpnoti/ asti hi artha-
265.04	paricchedaka-tvam sarva-pramāṇānām sādharmyam iti/ bhavatu ekam pramāṇam kim naḥ
265.05	bādhyate iti/ katham na bādhyate iti/ yad abhīṣṭam tad nivartate/ kim
265.06	abhīṣṭam pratyakṣa-anumāne bhinne iti/ atha sāmānyam āśritya abhedaḥ deśyate
265.07	tadā upekṣayā vartitavyam, sāmānyam āśritya abhedaḥ viśeṣam āśritya bhedaḥ
265.08	iti na kim cit bādhitam/ na ca anumānam śabdaḥ sva-para-ātma-pratyāyaka-
265.09	tvāt/ yad sva-para-ātma-pratyāyakam tad anumānam na bhavati yathā
265.10	pradīpaḥ pradīpaḥ yena api kriyate tasya api upalabdhi-sādhanam bhavati anyasya
265.11	api śabdaḥ api evam tasmāt na anumānam iti//
265.12	[2-1-54(53)]śabda-artha-vyavasthānāt apratiṣedhaḥ
265.13	śabda-artha-vyavasthānāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ yadi śabda-arthau asambaddhau
265.14	syātām, śabda-artha-pratyaya-vyavasthā na syāt/ śabdaḥ sambaddhaḥ artham pratipādayati
265.15	pratyaya-niyama-hetu-tvāt pradīpa-vat/ yaḥ pratyaya-niyama-hetuḥ
265.16	saḥ sambaddhaḥ artha-pratyāyakaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yathā pradīpaḥ/ śabdaḥ ca evam-dharmin^ḥ tasmāt
265.17	śabdaḥ api sambaddhaḥ artha-pratyāyakaḥ iti/ yad vā pratyayaḥ pakṣa^kartavyaḥ
265.18	sambaddhābhyām śabda-arthābhyām vastu-pratyayaḥ bhavati iti niyamāt kuṇḍalin^ḥ iti
265.19	pratyaya-vat//
265.20	[2-1-55(54)]na sāmayika-tvāt śabda-artha-sampratyayasya
265.21	na sāmayika-tvāt śabda-artha-sampratyayasya/ na sambandha-kāritam śabda-artha-
266.01	vyavasthānam kim tarhi samaya-kāritam kaḥ punar ayam samayaḥ/ abhidhāna-
266.02	abhidheya-niyama-niyogaḥ samaya-yukte śabda-artha-vyavasthā bhavati iti kaḥ cit
266.03	sambandhaḥ anujñātaḥ iti yadi avocāma iti/ sā iyam śabda-artha-vyavasthā anyathā
266.04	api bhavati iti ahetuḥ/ ayam ca vācya-vācaka-bhāva-lakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ
266.05	svābhāvika-śabda-artha-sambandha-vādinā api anujñeyaḥ/ na hi svābhāvika-sambandha-
266.06	vādinaḥ anupayukta-śabda-artha-sambandhasya śabda-agrahaṇāt viśeṣa-pratyayaḥ bhavati/
266.07	saḥ punar samayaḥ kutaḥ pratyetavyaḥ pada-jñānāt lokataḥ ca iti tad idam śāstram
266.08	śabdasya avilopa-artham praṇītam/ lokataḥ ca samayaḥ boddhavyaḥ mātṛ-ādīn
266.09	teṣu teṣu artha^su tān tān śabdān prayujyamānān upalabhya saḥ api tathā eva
266.10	śikṣitaḥ tān eva śabdān teṣu teṣu artha^su prayuṅkte na punar enam lipim iva
266.11	kaḥ cit śikṣayati iti//
266.12	[2-1-56(55)]jāti-viśeṣe ca aniyamāt
266.13	jāti-viśeṣe ca aniyamāt/ na svābhāvikaḥ śabda-arthayoḥ
266.14	sambandhaḥ/ yadi svābhāvikaḥ śabda-artha-sambandhaḥ abhaviṣyat na jāti-viśeṣe
266.15	śabda-artha-vyavasthā abhaviṣyat/ asti tu yathā-kāmam prayogaḥ jāti-viśeṣe
266.16	yathā-kāmam prayogaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ na tu svābhāvikena sambaddhānām jāti-viśeṣe
266.17	vyabhicāraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ na hi pradīpaḥ asmākam anyathā prakāśayati anyathā
266.18	jāti-viśeṣaḥ iti/ jāti-viśeṣa-śabdena punar deśaḥ abhidhīyate iti//
266.19	[2-1-57(56)]tad-aprāmāṇyam anṛta-vyāghāta-punarukta-doṣa^bhyaḥ
266.20	tad-aprāmāṇyam anṛta-vyāghāta-punarukta-doṣa^bhyaḥ iti sūtram/ tad iti adhikṛta-
267.01	śabda-abhidhānāt śabda-viśeṣa-adhikāraḥ yasmāt prāptam api prakaraṇena
267.02	śabdam tad-śabdena punar abhidhatte tad jñāpayati śabda-viśeṣam prati iyam cintā
267.03	na tu śabda-mātram anyathā evam vaktavyam/ apramāṇam śabdaḥ anṛta-vyāghāta-punarukta-
267.04	doṣa^bhyaḥ iti/ aprāmāṇyam arthasya apratyāyaka-tvam, anṛtatvam anyathā
267.05	artha-abhidhānam, vyāghātaḥ padayoḥ vākyayoḥ vā saha-asambhavaḥ, punaruktam pūrva-
267.06	abhihita-tva-abhidhānam/ udāharaṇāni bhāṣye/ tatra anṛtatve hetuḥ
267.07	phala-adarśanam, agni-hotram juhnayāt iti vidhi-vākyam/ asya vyāghātaḥ
267.08	phala-asambhavena udita-anudite samaya-adhyuṣita-pratiṣedhaḥ na ca anyaḥ
267.09	kālaḥ vidyate yatra agni-hotram havanam syāt/ udita-anudita-samaya-
267.10	adhyuṣita-vākyānām vā paraspara-virodhaḥ prathama-uttamayoḥ tris abhidhāne punaruktam/
267.11	udāharaṇam bhāṣye/ dṛṣṭānta-tvena etāni vākyāni upanyasya eka-
267.12	kartṛka-tvena śeṣa-vākyānām apramāṇa-tvam iti/ agni-hotra-ādi-vākyānām vā
267.13	pramāṇa-tva-pratiṣedhaḥ vaidika-tvena tad-anya-vākya-vat punarukta-tāyām pūrvam uttaram
267.14	vā na vākyam ekena praṇīyamāna-tvāt//
267.15	[2-1-58(57)]na karma-kartṛ-sādhana-vaiguṇyāt
267.16	na karma-kartṛ-sādhana-vaiguṇyāt/ yad tāvat anṛta-tvāt apramāṇāni veda-
267.17	vākyāni phala-adarśanāt iti tad atra sampradhāryam/ kim phala-adarśanam veda-
267.18	vākyānām apramāṇa-tvāt āho karma-kartṛ-sādhana-vaiguṇyāt iti/ vayam tu brūmaḥ
267.19	na karma-kartṛ-sādhana-vaiguṇyāt iti phala-adarśanāt tu kāraṇānām asāmagrīam
267.20	gamyate iṣṭi^aḥ karaṇa-sādhana-tvāt vā virodhaḥ/ na ca iyam iṣṭiḥ mayā sākṣāt
268.01	putra-kāraṇa-tvena abhyupagamyate api tu pitṛ^au iṣṭi^ā saṃprayujyamānau puttram
268.02	janayataḥ iti/ tatra na jñāyate kim pitṛ^oḥ asādguṇyāt puttra-janman na
268.03	bhavati ohosvit āhosvit iṣṭi^aḥ asādguṇyāt iti/ mantrāh ca iṣṭa-sādhana-tvena prayujyamānāḥ
268.04	iṣṭi-aṅgam bhavanti tatra api na jñāyate kim iṣṭiḥ mantrāṇām
268.05	vaiguṇyāt na sampannā āho darbha-indana-ādi^aḥ kāraṇasya vaiguṇyāt iti/
268.06	yathā eva mantrāṇām nyūna-adhika-bhāve prayujyamānānām iṣṭiḥ na nirvartate tathā
268.07	darbha-idhma-ādi^aḥ api sādhanasya vaiguṇyāt iti/ na kevalam sādhana-vaiguṇyāt
268.08	eva iṣṭi^aḥ anabhinirvṛttiḥ tathā iṣṭi^aḥ sādhayitṛ^aḥ karmaṇaḥ ca asādguṇyāt iti utsṛjya
268.09	iṣṭi-sampadam na kevalā eva iṣṭiḥ kāraṇam iti uktam/ tatra puttra-
268.10	janma-adarśanena na yuktam manuṣya-dharmaṇaḥ vaktum mantrāṇām asāmarthyāt
268.11	puttra-anutpattiḥ iti/ ubhayathā dṛṣṭa-tvāt sandehaḥ iti cet na abhyupeta-
268.12	hāni^aḥ/ atha manyase tri-kāraṇam puttra-janman tatra anyatara-kāraṇa-vaikalyāt
268.13	putraḥ na bhavati iti kāraṇa-vaikalyam gamyate/ tatra yathā eva pitṛ^oḥ vaiguṇyāt
268.14	puttra-anutpattiḥ tathā mantrāṇām apramāṇa-tvāt iti sandihyate
268.15	na abhyupeta-hāni^aḥ/ pūrvam bhavatā abhyadhāyi vedaḥ apramāṇam iti adhunā abhidhīyate
268.16	sandigdham pramāṇa-tvam iti evam bruvatā pūrvaḥ vādaḥ tyaktaḥ bhavati/
268.17	samānam iti cet/ atha manyase samānam etad bhavataḥ api kim pitṛ^aḥ vaiguṇyāt
268.18	putra-ajanman veda-vākyānām aprāmāṇyāt ca iti sandihyate/ na asādhanāt/
268.19	na mayā etad sādhyate veda-vākyāni pramāṇam apramāṇam vā iti bhavatā
268.20	tu sādhyate/ tatra mayā bhavataḥ sādhana-anyatara-vaikalyāt puttra-janman na
269.01	bhavati iti sandigdhaḥ hetuḥ apadiśyate tad-sahakāri-tvāt na upadeśaḥ dāru-
269.02	manthana-vat iti/ na iyam iṣṭiḥ sākṣāt-kāraṇam puttra-janmanaḥ apadiśyate
269.03	api tu puttra-kāmaḥ iṣṭim kurvīta iti/ yathā agni-kāmaḥ dāruṇin^ḥ
269.04	mathnīyāt iti upadeśaḥ tatra na kartṛ-karmaṇoḥ vaiguṇyāt agniḥ bhavati iti na upadeśa-
269.05	vaiyarthyam tathā iha api iti adoṣaḥ/ sāmagrye phala-sampatti-darśanāt ca/ yatra
269.06	samastāni kāraṇāni tatra phala-abhinirvṛttiḥ dṛṣṭā iti adoṣaḥ/ yad ca idam
269.07	vākyam tad-aprāmāṇyam anṛta-vyāghāta-punarukta-doṣa^bhyaḥ iti/ tad api vedasya
269.08	pakṣa^kṛta-tvāt avyāpakam/ atha yāni evam-dharmakāṇi vākyāni tāni
269.09	pakṣa^kriyante teṣu pakṣa-dharmāṇām viśeṣaṇa-tvena upayukta-tvāt aprāmāṇya-pratipādakaḥ
269.10	hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ anṛta-tvāt aprāmāṇyam iti cet kim idam anṛtatvam
269.11	nāma yadi ayathā-atathā-bhūta-artha-abhidhānam na tu ayam eva pratijñā-arthaḥ apramāṇam
269.12	iti/ anṛtatvam vā asiddham iti ayuktam etad/  yad punar etad vyāghāta-
269.13	doṣāt iti//
269.14	[2-1-59(58)]abhyupetya kāla-bhede doṣa-vacanāt
269.15	abhyupetya kāla-bhede doṣa-vacanāt na vyāghātaḥ/ agni-ādhānaḥ eva ayam
269.16	niyama-parigrahaḥ/ udite hotavyam anudite hotavyam samaya-adhyuṣite hotavyam
269.17	iti/ saḥ ayam abhyupetya kālam yadā atikrāmati tadā abhyupeta-kālāt
269.18	nindyate nindā-vacanena vyāghātaḥ ubhaya-pratiṣedha-anabhidhānāt na idam anyathā
269.19	yadi punar etad ubhaya-pratiṣedha-abhidhānam syāt bhavet ayam prasaṅgaḥ vyāghātaḥ
269.20	na punar etāni pratiṣedha-vākyāni udite hotavyam iti evam ādīni
269.21	tasmāt aprasaṅgaḥ ayam iti/ atha punar etāni vākyāni homa-vidhi^aḥ pratiṣedhakāni
270.01	syuḥ tadā udita-ādi-grahaṇam vyartham evam vaktavyam/ agni-hotram
270.02	juhuyāt iti  yad vākyam tad na kartavyam iti/ evam ca saṃgata-artha-tā yathā etad vyākhyātam
270.03	evam pūrva-uttare vākye saṅgate bhavataḥ havanam anūdya kāla-viśeṣa-vidhānāt
270.04	iti/ evam etad na punar yathā asmābhiḥ vyāghātaḥ abhidhīyate iti niyama-
270.05	hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ idam eva udita-ādi-vākyam niyama-hetuḥ/ katham iti idam
270.06	punar vākyam kāmataḥ vā kalpyeta abhihitam vā anūdyate iti/ abhihita-
270.07	anuvādaḥ nyāyaḥ/ tatra hi pramāṇānām avisaṃvādāt iti na
270.08	vyāghātaḥ iti  yad punar etad punarukta-doṣāt na punarukta-doṣaḥ
270.09	[2-1-60(59)]anuvāda-upapatti^aḥ
270.10	[2-1-61(60)]vākya-vibhāgasya ca artha-grahaṇāt
270.11	anuvāda-upapatti^aḥ/ punaruktam nāma tasya eva arthasya anaṅga^kṛta-viśeṣasya
270.12	sataḥ punar-vacanam/ anuvādaḥ tu punar śruti-sāmānyāt aṅga^kṛta-viśeṣasya
270.13	anuvādaḥ/ evam sati yathā-uktaḥ na doṣaḥ/ udāharaṇam bhāṣye/ puttra-
270.14	kāma-iṣṭi-vākyāni pramā veda-ekadeśa-tvāt bhūmi^aḥ uvapana- bhūmiḥ āvapanam mahat iti vākya-vat itaḥ ca
270.15	pada-ādi-niyamāt dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ iti vākya-vat/ itaḥ ca
270.16	kartṛ-viśeṣa-abhihita-tvāt agniḥ himasya bheṣajam iti vākya-vat//
270.17	samastāni vā veda-vākyāni pakṣa^kṛtya bhidhīyante pramāṇam veda-
270.18	vākyāni artha-vibhāgavat-tvāt manu-ādi-vākya-vat/ yathā manu-ādi-
270.19	vākyāni artha-vibhāgavanti artha-vibhāgavat-tve sati prāmāṇyam tathā ca
270.20	veda-vākyāni artha-vibhāga-vanti tasmāt pramāṇam  vibhāgaḥ ca tri-vidhaḥ
270.21	bhavati//
271.01	[2-1-62(61)]vidhi-artha-vāda-anuvāda-vacana-viniyogāt
271.02	vidhi-artha-vāda-anuvāda-vacana-viniyogāt/ tridhā khalu brāhmaṇa-
271.03	vākyāni bhavanti vidhi-vacanāni anuvāda-vacanāni artha-vāda-vacanāni
271.04	ca//
271.05	[2-1-63(62)]vidhiḥ vidhāyakaḥ
271.06	tatra vidhiḥ vidhāyakaḥ/ yad vākyam vidhāyakam deśakam saḥ vidhiḥ/
271.07	vidhiḥ tu niyogaḥ anujñā vā yad vākyam vidhatte idam kuryāt iti saḥ
271.08	niyogaḥ/ anujñā tu yad kartṛ^am anujānāti tad anujñā-vākyam,
271.09	yathā agni-hotra-vākyam eva etad sādhana-avāpti-pūrvaka-tvam anujānāti//
271.10	[2-1-64(63)]stutiḥ nindā parakṛtiḥ purākalpaḥ iti artha-vādaḥ
271.11	stutiḥ nindā parakṛtiḥ purākalpaḥ iti artha-vādaḥ/ eṣām udāharaṇāni
271.12	bhāṣye//
271.13	[2-1-65(64)]vidhi-vihitasya anuvacanam anuvādaḥ
271.14	vidhi-vihitasya anuvacanam anuvādaḥ? vidhi-artha-vacanam tathā anuvādaḥ/
271.15	vidhi-vihita-anuvacanam ca pūrvaḥ śabda-anuvādaḥ paraḥ artha-anuvādaḥ yathā
271.16	punaruktam dvi-vidham śabda-punaruktam artha-punaruktam ca śabda-punaruktam anityaḥ
271.17	nityaḥ vā iti/ artha-punaruktam anityaḥ nirodha-dharmakaḥ iti//
271.18	[2-1-66(65)]na anuvāda-punaruktayoḥ viśeṣaḥ śabda-abhyāsa-upapatti^aḥ
271.19	na anuvāda-punaruktayoḥ viśeṣaḥ śabda-abhyāsa-upapatti^aḥ/ kaḥ asya sūtrasya
272.01	arthaḥ/ pratīta-artha-śabda-abhyāsaḥ anuvāda-punaruktayoḥ sāmyam, punaruktaḥ api
272.02	pratīta-arthaḥ śabdaḥ abhyasyate/ anuvāde api evam ataḥ pratīta-artha-śabda-abhyāsāt
272.03	ubhayam asādhu iti//
272.04	[2-1-67(66)]śīghratara-gamana-upadeśa-vat abhyāsāt na aviśeṣaḥ
272.05	śīghratara-gamana-upadeśa-vat abhyāsāt na aviśeṣaḥ iti/ yathā śīghram gamyatām,
272.06	śīghrataram gamyatām iti/ tvarayā tarapā kriyā-atiśayaḥ gamyate tathā
272.07	anuvāda-lakṣaṇena api abhyāsena kriyā-atiśayaḥ abhidhīyate iti/ asya prayogaḥ
272.08	arthavān anuvāda-lakṣaṇaḥ abhyāsaḥ pratyaya-viśeṣe hetu-tvāt śīghratara-gamana-upadeśa-
272.10	vat iti/ atha śīghra-śabdāt śīghratara-śabdaḥ prayujyamānaḥ pratyaya-
272.11	viśeṣa-hetu-tvāt na punarukta-doṣam na bhavati/ tathā anuvāda-lakṣaṇaḥ api abhyāsaḥ
272.12	pratyaya-viśeṣa=-hetu-tvāt na punarukta-doṣam lapsyate iti/ kaḥ punar asau viśeṣaḥ
272.13	bhavati iti vaktavyaḥ tad ucyate/ ādye pacatu-śabda-prayoge pākaḥ nirvartanīyaḥ
272.14	iti paktṛ^aḥ pratyayaḥ bhavati dvitīyāt tu pacatu-śabdāt avadhāraṇa-
272.15	pratyayaḥ bhavati mayā eva paktavyam iti sātatya-pratyayaḥ vā bhavati satatam
272.16	mayā paktavayam iti adhyeṣaṇa-pratyayaḥ bhavati āśu mayā paktavaym iti/
272.18	punarukte tu na kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ gamyate iti mahān viśeṣaḥ punarukta-
272.19	anuvādayoḥ/ evam anyaḥ api anuvādasya prayogaḥ vākye boddhavyaḥ iti/  kim
272.20	punar pratiṣedha-hetu-tva-uddhārāt eva pramāṇa-tā veda-vākyānām siddhā na/ kim
272.21	kāraṇam/ na sādhanam antareṇa siddhiḥ asti iti kutaḥ tarhi pramāṇataḥ
272.22	kim tad pramāṇam artha-vibhāgavat-tvam//
273.01	[2-1-68(67)]mantra-āyurveda-prāmāṇyāt ca tad-prāmāṇyam
273.02	āpta-prāmāṇyāt
273.03	mantra-āyurveda-prāmāṇya-vat ca tad-prāmāṇyam āpta-prāmāṇyāt/ ca-śabdaḥ
273.04	pūrva-hetu-anukarṣaṇa-arthaḥ/ yathā mantra-āyurveda-vākyāni puruṣa-viśeṣa-abhihita-
273.05	tvāt pramāṇam tathā veda-vākyāni iti puruṣa-viśeṣa-abhihita-tvam hetuḥ/
273.06	kim āyurvedasya prāmāṇyam yad tad āyurvedena upadiśyate/ idam kṛtvā iṣṭam
273.07	adhigacchati idam kṛtvā aniṣṭam jahāti tasya anuṣṭhīyamānasya tathā-bhāvaḥ
273.08	aviparyayaḥ etad prāmāṇyam kim-kṛtam etad āpta-prāmāṇya-kṛtam/ kim
273.09	punar āptānām pramāṇa-tvam sākṣāt-kṛta-dharma-tā yam te padārtham upadiśanti
273.10	saḥ taiḥ sākṣāt-kṛtaḥ bhavati iti bhūta-dayā ca yasmai ca upadiśanti tam
273.11	pratyanukampā bhavati yathā-artha-parijñāna-artham cikhyāpayiṣā vā asya bhavati/
273.12	etena tri-vidhena viśeṣaṇena viśiṣṭaḥ vaktṛ^ḥ āptaḥ tena yaḥ upadeśaḥ
273.13	kriyate saḥ pramāṇam iti dṛṣṭa-vākya-avisaṃvādena ca aśeṣa-anumānam śakyam
273.14	dṛṣṭa-artham yathā vā grāma-kāmaḥ yajeta iti evam ādi tena śeṣa-anumānam pramāṇam
273.15	iti/ laukikam api vākyam anena eva tri-prakārena viśeṣaṇena
273.16	viśiṣṭasya vaktṛ^aḥ pramāṇam bhavati asya prayogaḥ/ pramāṇam veda-vākyāni
273.17	vaktṛ-viśeṣa-abhihita-tvāt mantra-āyurveda-vākya-vat iti/ eka-kartṛka-tvena vā
273.18	mantra-āyurveda-vākyāni pakṣa^kṛtya alaukika-viṣaya-pratipādaka-tvena vaidharmya-
274.01	hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ pauruṣeya-tvam asiddham nitya-tvāt iti cet/ atha manyase
274.02	nityāni veda-vākyāni nitya-tvāt ca eṣām prāmāṇyam tasmāt pauruṣeya-tvam
274.03	asiddham/ na asiddha-tvāt/ siddhe nityatve etad yuktam tad na na siddham ataḥ na
274.04	yuktam etad/ yadi na nityāni katham pramāṇam prameya-pratipādaka-tvāt
274.05	pramāṇam na nitya-tvāt ke cit tu bruvate na kim cit nityam pramāṇam asti iti
274.06	pramāṇa-tvāt eva anityāni iti/ tad tu na samyac iti paśyāmaḥ nityam api
274.07	pramāṇam yathā manaḥ ātman^ḥ ca pramāṇa-śabdasya samudāya-vāci-tvāt/
274.08	ayam khalu pramāṇa-śabdaḥ samudāye vartate tatra nityam anityam ca anekam
274.09	samudāyin pramāṇasya aṅga-tvāt pramāṇam iti ucyate yathā pradīpaḥ evam ca
274.10	nityam api pramāṇam asti iti/ yadā ca paramāṇu-ādi nityam itara-vastu-
274.11	sādhaka-tvena upādīyate tadā paramāṇu-ādi^aḥ nityasya pramāṇa-bhāvāt pramāṇa-
274.12	tvāt anityaḥ iti anaikāntaḥ bhavati iti anuttaram etad/ tasmāt yuktam artha-vibhāgavat-
274.13	tvāt antiyatvam laukika-vākya-vat iti/ yathā-artha-vibhāgavanti laukika-
274.14	vākyāni tathā ca veda-vākyāni tasmāt na nityāni iti/ laukikāni
274.15	api nityāni iti cet/ atha manyase yāni api etāni laukika-vākyāni
274.16	artha-vibhāgavanti tāni api nityāni iti artha-vibhāgaḥ na syāt
274.17	yaḥ ayam artha-vibhāgaḥ laukika-vākye saḥ na syāt dṛṣṭam tu tāni anityāni
274.18	iti cet viśeṣa-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ tulye ca artha-vibhāgavat-tve tulye ca artha-
274.19	pratipādaka-tve laukikāni anityāni vaidikāni nityāni iti viśeṣa-
274.20	hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ tulyam bhavataḥ api anityatve viśeṣa-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ iti/ uktaḥ artha-
274.21	vibhāgavat-tvam hetuḥ itaḥ ca varṇavat-tvāt varṇavanti laukika-vākyāni anityāni
274.22	tathā ca veda-vākyāni tasmāt tāni api anityāni tataḥ ca sāmānya-
275.01	viśeṣavat-tve sati śrotra-grāhya-tvāt laukika-vākya-vat/ itaḥ ca padavat-
275.02	tvāt laukika-vat/ darśanasya para-artha-tvāt iti cet/ atha manyase darśanam
275.03	para-artham darśana-śabdaḥ uccāraṇa-arthaḥ uccāraṇam para-pratipādana-artham na hi
275.04	kaḥ cit ātma-pratipatti^e śabdam uccārayati/ yadi ca ayam nityaḥ bhavati
275.05	śabdāt artha-pratyayaḥ yuktaḥ/ atha anityaḥ utpannaḥ pradhvaṃsin^ḥ tataḥ anyaḥ śrotṛ^aḥ
275.06	nityam apūrvaḥ śrotra-viṣayaḥ iti/ na ca apūrva-śravaṇāt pratipattiḥ yuktā/
275.07	na hi nārikela-dvīpa-vāsinaḥ aprasiddha-go-śravaṇāt kakuda-ādimat-artha-
275.08	pratipattiḥ bhavati/ tathā ca anitya-vādinaḥ sarve śabdāḥ tathā tasmāt
275.09	asya api śabdāt pratyaya-niyamaḥ na prāptaḥ iti/ na anekāntāt/ kṣaṇa-
275.10	vidhvaṃsiṣu pradīpa-ādiṣu apūrvasya pratyāyanam dṛṣṭam iti/ na udāharaṇa-vaidharmyāt
275.11	pradīpaḥ anākhyātaḥ prakāśanīya-artha-sambandhāt prakāśanīya-artha-sampratipattim
275.12	karoti na punar anākhyātaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ kaḥ evam āha ākhyātaḥ
275.13	śabdaḥ pratipādayati sambandhasya adarśanāt ākhyānam anupapannam/ uktam etad
275.14	yathā na śabda-arthayoḥ prāpti-lakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ asti iti vācya-vācaka-lakṣaṇaḥ tu
275.15	pauruṣeyaḥ sambandhaḥ saḥ ca kṛtakaḥ iti/ saḥ ca saṃsāra-anādi-tvāt
275.16	lokataḥ avagamyate ye tu saṃsāram anādimantam varṇayanti te evam upālabhyāḥ/
275.17	atha yaḥ ayam abhilāpaḥ nityāḥ varṇāḥ iti saḥ katham sampradāyasya
275.18	avicchedāt tāni eva veda-vākyāni manu-antara-catur-varga^su sampradāya abhyāsa-
275.19	avicchedena pravartante tad-apekṣayā laukikāḥ śabdāḥ na prayujyante
275.20	nityāḥ vedāḥ iti/ yathā nityāḥ parvatāḥ saritaḥ iti tad-manu-ādi-
275.21	vākya^su samānam/ kutaḥ evam sampradāya-avicchedāt nityatvam na
275.22	punar nitya-tvāt eva anityatva-ukta-pramāṇa-avighātāt yāni mayā anityatve
276.01	pramāṇāni upanyastāni tāni na vihanyante teṣām avighātāt anitya-tve
276.02	sati sampradāya-avicchedaḥ nitya-upacāraḥ iti//
276.03	iti - auddyotakare nyāyavārttike dvitīyasya adhyāyasya
276.04	ādyam āhnikam//276.05	atha dvitīyam āhnikam/
276.06	[2-2-1]na catuṣṭvam aitihya-arthāpatti-sambhava-abhāva-prāmāṇyāt
276.07	pratyakṣa-anumāna-upamāna-śabdāḥ pramāṇāni iti sūtram/ tasya apekṣaḥ
276.08	na catuṣṭvam aitihya-arthāpatti-sambhava-abhāva-prāmāṇyāt/ tasya arthaḥ ayathā-arthaḥ
276.09	ayam pramāṇa-uddeśaḥ iti/ na catvāri eva pramāṇāni aitihya-arthāpatti-sambhava-
276.10	abhāvāḥ api pramāṇāni iti/ saṃśaya-vyudāsa-artham ca aitihya-ādyaḥ  upadiśya
276.11	pratiṣidhyante pratyakṣa-ādīni ca pramāṇāni aitihya-ādīni ca iti
276.12	manyante tatra śrotṛ^aḥ saṃśayaḥ syāt kim vidyamānāḥ aitihya-ādyaḥ
276.13	na uktāḥ atha avidyamānāḥ iti vidhi-nyūnatā-parihāra-artham vā prakaraṇam/
276.14	vidyamānāni aitihya-ādīni na uktāni iti vidhi-vākya-hīnam śabda-śāstram
276.15	syāt doṣavatām doṣa-anabhidhānāt doṣa-hīnam vā śāstram syāt iti ataḥ
276.16	uddiśya nivartsyante/ doṣavantaḥ aitihya-ādyaḥ  iti ataḥ na abhidhīyante
276.17	iti/ doṣaḥ tarhi vaktavyaḥ atha doṣaḥ na abhidhīyate doṣa-hīnam śāstram
276.18	iti tad-parihāra-artham prakaraṇam upakṣipyate pratyakṣa-ādiṣu antarbhāva-pradarśana-
276.19	artham vā aitihya-ādīni pratyakṣa-ādiṣu antarbhāvayatā ye yatra antarbhavanti
277.01	tatra teṣām pradarśana-artham prakaraṇam iti siddha-tvāt anārambhaḥ na tu ayam arthaḥ
277.02	catvāri eva pramāṇāni iti asmin eva vibhāga-uddeśa-sūtre siddhaḥ iti siddha-
277.03	tvāt punar anārambhaḥ iti/ na/ avadhāraṇa-prayojanasya itaḥ ca siddhi^aḥ yena
277.04	nyāyena catvāri eva pramāṇāni iti etad avasthāpyate saḥ nyāyaḥ iha prakaraṇe
277.05	varṇyate iti tasmāta ārabhyam eva iti/ kim idam aitihyam nāma anirdiṣṭa-
277.06	vaktṛkam pravāda-pāramparyam itihocuḥ vṛddhāḥ kathayanti iti/ arthāt āpattiḥ
277.07	arthāpattiḥ āpattiḥ prāptiḥ prasaṅgaḥ yasmin vākye vidhīyamānāt
277.08	arthāt anyaḥ arthaḥ āpadyate sārtha-āpattiḥ yathā asatsu megha^su vṛṣṭiḥ na bhavati
277.09	iti kim atra arthāt āpadayte asatsu bhavati iti/ sambhavaḥ nāma avinābhāva-
277.10	varttinaḥ arthasya grahaṇāt anya-sattā-vijñānam/ yathā droṇasya grahaṇāt
277.11	āḍhakasya sattā iti/ abhāvaḥ pratyanīkasya grahaṇāt tad-virodhinaḥ grahaṇam/
277.12	yathā vidhārake vāyu-abhra-saṃyoge sati gurutva-pratibandhāt apām pātaḥ
277.13	pratiṣiddhaḥ tad-apām apātena virodhinam vāyu-abhra-saṃyogam pratipadyate iti
277.14	tāni etāni aitihya-ādīni iti/ evam-bhūtāni iti lakṣaṇa-abhedāt eteṣu eva antarbhavanti
277.15	na pṛthak ucyante/ viṣaya-abhāvāt ca na pratyakṣa-ādi-pramāṇa-vyatirekena
277.16	viṣayāḥ aitihya-ādīnām sambhavanti ataḥ ca na pramāṇa-antarāṇi/ atha
277.17	prayojana-bhedāt bhidyante iti manyase evam tarhi aṣṭatvam nivartate yad api
277.18	abhyupagatam pramāṇa-aṣṭatvam tad na vartate prayojana-bhedāt pramāṇa-bhedam abhyupagacchate
277.19	iti/ eka-karaṇaḥ ca prayojana-bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti anekāntaḥ/ yathā ekam
277.20	cakṣus-aneka-nīla-ādi-bhinna-rūpa-grahaṇa-prayojanam iti indriya-anekatvam vā
277.21	atha prayojana-bhedāt bhedam manyase ekam indriyam anekam prāpnoti/ atha
277.22	viṣaya-sāmānya-vyatirekāt ekam indriyam ayuktaḥ tarhi pratiṣedhaḥ na catuṣṭvam
278.01	aitihya-arthāpatti-sambhava-abhāva-prāmāṇyāt iti/ yadi api etāni pramāṇāni
278.02	kim tarhi antarbhavanti iti vaktavyam antarbhāva-pradarśana-artham sūtram//
278.03	[2-2-2]śabde aitihya-anartha-antara-bhāvāt anumāne arthāpatti-sambhava-
278.04	abhāva-anartha-antara-bhāvāt ca apratiṣedhaḥ
278.05	śabde aitihya-anartha-antara-bhāvāt anumāne arthāpatti-sambhava-abhāva-anartha-antara-
278.06	bhāvāt ca apratiṣedhaḥ/ śabde aitihyam antarbhavati samāna-lakṣaṇa-tvāt na
278.07	śabda-lakṣaṇam aitihyāt nivartate saḥ ayam bhedaḥ sāmānyena saṃgṛhītaḥ/ anumāne
278.08	arthāpatti-sambhava-abhāvānā, antarabhāvaḥ samāna-lakṣaṇa-tvāt katham arthāpattiḥ
278.09	anumānena saṃgṛhyate/ dvayoḥ ekatara-pratiṣedhasya dvitīya-abhyanujñā-
278.10	viṣaya-tvāt/ yatra yatra dvayoḥ vastunoḥ ekatara-vastu pratiṣidhyate tatra tatra
278.11	dvitīya-abhyanujñā dṛṣṭā yathā divā na bhuṅkte iti abhidhānāt rātri^i bhuṅkte
278.12	iti gamyate/ eke tu bruvate na iyam arthāpattiḥ divā na bhuṅkte iti/ atra
278.13	kim tu avadhāraṇa-dvayam divā na bhuṅkte eva divā eva na bhuṅkte iti ca ubhaya-
278.14	prāpti^i na vaktavyam rātri^i bhuṅkte iti/ etad tu na samyac-sambhavāt
278.15	divā na bhuṅkte eva iti ayam arthaḥ asambhavena nirākṛtaḥ na kaḥ cit
278.16	divā bhuṅkte eva bhuñjānaḥ api tāvat na bhuṅkte eva kimpunar abhuñjānaḥ iti
278.17	tasmāt asambhavena arthasya nirākṛta-tvāt kāla-pratiṣedhaḥ anena divā na
278.18	bhuṅkte iti/ anyathā anarthakam syāt kriyā-antara-nivṛtti^aḥ asambhavena nirākṛta0
278.19	tvāt kāla-pratiṣedhasya anabhyupagamāt anarthakam vākyam bhavatu anarthakam
278.20	kim naḥ bādhyate yad abhyupagatam tad nivartate kim ca abhyupagatam kriyā-antara-
278.21	pratiṣedhaḥ yatra kāla-pratiṣedhaḥ iti na ca idam anarthakam tasmāt dvayoḥ ekatara-
279.01	pratiṣedhe dvitīya-abhyanujñānāt anumānam eva arthāpattiḥ katamat eva anumānam
279.02	sāmānyataḥ dṛṣṭam/ etena sambhava-abhāvau vyākhyātau/ sambhavaḥ anumānam
279.03	abhāvaḥ api anumānam eva/ tad ca varṇitam/ anayoḥ api sāmānyataḥ
279.04	dṛṣṭe eva antarbhāvaḥ iti/  tad evam antarbhāvāt pṛthak-anuddeśaḥ iti manyase
279.05	tāni pramāṇāni na tu pramāṇa-antarāṇi iti uktam tatra arthāpatti^aḥ pramāṇa-
279.06	bhāva-abhyanujñā na upapadyate//
279.07	[2-2-3]arthāpattiḥ apramāṇam anaikāntika-tvāt
279.08	tathā iha iyam arthāpattiḥ apramāṇam anaikāntika-tvāt/ yā iyam bhavatā arthāpattiḥ
279.09	pramāṇa-tvena pratipādyate sā na pramāṇam iti na arthasya ekadā api
279.10	pratipādikā/ yathā megha^su asatsu vṛṣṭiḥ na bhavati iti arthāt āpannam
279.11	satsu bhavati iti satsu api ekadā na bhavati iti anaikāntikī arthāpatti-
279.12	mātrasya pakṣa^karaṇe anaikāntika-tvam avyāpakam yadi tāvat arthāpatti-mātram
279.13	pakṣayasi tataḥ arthāpatti^aḥ anaikāntika-tvam aśakyam/ atha anaikāntikī yā
279.14	sā pakṣa^kriyate tadā abhyupagamaḥ vayam api brūmaḥ anaikāntikī apramāṇam
279.15	iti hetuḥ ca na asti hetu^aḥ pratijñā-viśeṣa-tvāt yad anaikāntikam tad
279.16	apramāṇam iti pratijñā tatra hetuḥ na asti na ca ayam arthaḥ sādhyaḥ iti
279.17	hetu-abhāvāt vyāghātaḥ ca abhyupagamāt/ yā anaikāntikī arthāpattiḥ sā
279.18	pramāṇam na bhavati iti bruvatā aikāntikī sā pramāṇam iti abhyupagatam
279.19	bhavati anarthakam ca idam abhidhānam yā anaikāntikī arthāpattiḥ sā na
279.20	pramāṇam iti  yām api bhavān arthāpattim manyate sā api na anaikāntikī//
279.21	[2-2-4]anarthāpatti^i arthāpatti-abhimānāt
280.01	anarthāpatti^i arthāpatti-abhidhānāt/ na iyam arthāpattiḥ anaikāntikī
280.02	arthāpattiḥ ca satsu megha^su vṛṣṭiḥ bhavati iti sā iyam anaikāntikī katham na
280.03	satsu bhavati eva iti api tu satsu bhavati iti/ yad tu satsu megha^su vṛṣṭiḥ na
280.04	bhavati iti kāraṇa-pratibandhaḥ asau na tad arthāpatti^aḥ prameyam kim tu asyāḥ
280.05	prameyam kāraṇe sati kāryam bhavati iti/ sā iyam anaikāntikī katham syāt
280.06	yadi asati api kāraṇe kāryam utpadyate tad tu na dṛṣṭam iti na kadā cit
280.07	anaikāntikatvam//
280.08	[2-2-5]pratiṣedha-aprāmāṇyam ca anaikāntika-tvāt
280.09	pratiṣedha-aprāmāṇyam ca anaikāntika-tvāt/ yaḥ ayam bhavatā pratiṣedhaḥ
280.10	kriyate arthāpattiḥ na pramāṇam anaikāntika-tvāt/ ayam api anaikāntikaḥ
280.11	sattā-apratiṣedhāt na anena pratiṣedhaḥ na arthāpatti^aḥ sattā pratiṣidhyate katham
280.12	na sattā-pratiṣedhaḥ yasmāt arthāpatti^aḥ viśeṣaḥ pratiṣidhyate na arthāpatti-mātram
280.13	tena sattā-apratiṣedhāt anaikāntikaḥ bhavati/  atha manyase na pratiṣedhasya
280.14	sattvam viṣayaḥ kim tu pramāṇam saḥ ayam pratiṣedhaḥ sva-viṣaye pravartamānaḥ
280.15	na anaikāntikaḥ bhavitum arhati iti/ anaikāntikaḥ hi nāma sa jñeyaḥ
280.16	yaḥ sva-viṣaya-tad-jātīya-anya-vṛttiḥ na ca anaikāntikam pramāṇam asti
280.17	tasmāt na anaikāntikaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ iti//
280.18	[2-2-6]tad-prāmāṇye vā na arthāpatti-aprāmāṇyam
