280.19	[2-2-7]na abhāva-prāmāṇyam prameya-asiddhi^aḥ
280.20	tad-prāmāṇye vā na arthāpatti-aprāmāṇyam/ yadi sva-viṣaya-tad-sajātīya-
280.21	anya-vṛttiḥ anaikāntikaḥ iti manyase arthāpattiḥ api tarhi anaikāntikī
281.01	na bhavati na hi asati kāraṇe kāryam utpadyamānam dṛṣṭam iti tad idam sūtram
281.02	vyāghāta-deśana-artham yad uktam bhavatā sva-viṣayāt anyatra vartamānam anaikāntikam
281.03	bhavati arthāpattiḥ ca anaikāntikī iti vyāhatam/ ajñānam vā atha
281.04	abuddhvā eva anaikāntika-vyavahāre api ucyate arthāpattiḥ apramāṇam anaikāntika-tvāt
281.05	iti/ tatra upekṣayā vartitavyam na unmatta-vākyāni pratisandheyāni iti/
281.06	na abhāvaḥ pramāṇam viṣaya-abhāvāt/ yad aviṣayam tad apramāṇam yathā
281.07	go-śabdaḥ aśva-pratipādane tathā ca abhāvaḥ tasmāt apramāṇam/ kaḥ ca evam āha abhāvaḥ
281.08	pramāṇam api tu abhāva-viṣayā pratipattiḥ pramāṇam tayā pratīyate
281.09	iti/ kaḥ punar asyāḥ viṣayaḥ abhāvaḥ iti uktam/  tatra anekadhā bhinna-prapañcasya
281.10	udāharaṇa-artham sūtram//
281.11	[2-2-8]lakṣita^su alakṣaṇa-lakṣita-tvāt alakṣitānām
281.12	tad-prameya-siddhi^aḥ
281.13	lakṣita^su alakṣaṇa-lakṣita-tvāt alakṣitānām tad-prameya-siddhi^aḥ/ alakṣitāni
281.14	vāsāṃsi ānaya iti prayuktaḥ ubhaya-sannidhi^i yeṣu vāsas^su
281.15	lakṣaṇāni na paśyati tāni lakṣaṇa-abhāvena eva pratipadyate preṣaṇa-sampratipatti^oḥ
281.16	eka-viṣaya-tvāt pratipadya ca ānayati pratipatti-hetuḥ ca pramāṇam
281.17	tasmāt sidhyati abhāvasya prameyam//
281.18	[2-2-9]asati arthe na abhāvaḥ iti cet na anya-lakṣaṇa-upapatti^aḥ
281.19	asati arthe na abhāvaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase yatra yad bhavati tatra
281.20	tasya abhāvaḥ na ca alakṣita^su vāsas^su lakṣaṇāni bhavanti tasmāt na tara
281.21	na bhavanti iti na aparijñānāt na bhavatā asmad-abhiprāyaḥ abodhi na brūmaḥ
282.01	yatra yad bhavati tatra tad na bhavati iti api tu lakṣitāni vāsāṃsi upalabhamānaḥ
282.02	itarāṇi lakṣaṇa-abhāva-viśiṣṭāni pratipadyate iti/ ayathā-
282.03	arthaḥ ayam upalāmbhaḥ iti ataḥ ukta-artha-sūtram na anya-lakṣaṇa-upapatti^aḥ iti//
282.04	[2-2-10]tad-siddhi^aḥ alakṣita^su ahetuḥ
282.05	tad-siddhi^aḥ alakṣita^su ahetuḥ/ yāni tāni lakṣita^su vāsas^su lakṣaṇāni
282.06	tāni kasya cit na santi iti yāni ca santi teṣām abhāvaḥ vyāhataḥ/
282.07	atra uktam na aparijñānāt iti/ asya ca arthasya jñāpana-artham sūtram//
282.08	[2-2-11]na lakṣaṇa-avasthita-apekṣa-siddhi^aḥ
282.09	na lakṣaṇa-avasthita-apekṣa-siddhi^aḥ/ na brūmaḥ yāni bhavanti teṣām
282.10	tatra eva abhāvaḥ iti/ api tu keṣu cit lakṣaṇāni avasthitāni apekṣya yeṣu
282.11	tāni na bahvanti tāni lakṣaṇa-abhāvena pratipadyeta iti//
282.12	[2-2-12]prāk-utpatti^aḥ abhāva-upapatti^aḥ ca
282.13	[(2-2-13)]vimarśa-hetu-anuyoge ca vipratipatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ
282.14	prāk-utpatti^aḥ abhāva-upapatti^aḥ ca/ abhāva-dvaitam khalu bhavati prāk-utpatti^aḥ
282.15	avedyamāna-tā ūrdhvam ca vināśāt avidyamāna-tā iti/ tatra yaḥ ayam vināśāt
282.16	abhāvaḥ lakṣaṇānām alakṣita^su vāsas^su saḥ anupapannaḥ na itaraḥ iti/ asati
282.17	arthe na abhāvaḥ tad-siddhi^aḥ alakṣita^su ahetuḥ iti ubhe api ete chala-sūtre chalasya
282.18	uktam uttaram jñātvā ajñātvā vā asambandham ajñātam vā iti tasmāt aprayojyam
282.19	chalam iti/  āpta-upadeśsaḥ śabdaḥ iti pramāṇa-bhāve viśeṣaṇam bruvatā
282.20	nānā-prakāraḥ śabdaḥ iti jñāpyate katham na asati bhede viśeṣaṇam arthavat
283.01	na ca anarthakam viśeṣaṇam yuktam iti/ yuktaḥ nānā-bhūtaḥ śabdaḥ viśeṣaṇa-
283.02	sāmarthyāt iti tasmin sāmānyena vicāraḥ nityaḥ anityaḥ vā
283.03	vicāraḥ parīkṣā//
283.04	vimarśa-hetu-anuyoge ca vipratipatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ/ vipartipatti-śabdasya
283.05	uktaḥ arthaḥ/ tatra ākāśa-guṇaḥ śabdaḥ mahattva-vat abhivyaktidharman^ḥ iti eke/ eke
283.06	tāvat bruvate nityaḥ śabdaḥ iti avinaśyat-ādhāra-eka-dravya-ākāśa-guṇa-tvāt
283.07	yad avinaśyat-ādhāra-eka-dravyam ākāśa-guṇaḥ ca tad nityam dṛṣṭam, yathā ākāśa-
283.08	mahattvam, tathā śabdaḥ tasmāt nityaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam nityaḥ san abhivyakti-
283.09	dharman^ḥ tasya abhivyañjakāḥ saṃyoga-vibhāga-nādāḥ iti/ gandha-ādi-sahavṛttiḥ
283.10	dravya^su sanniviṣṭaḥ gandha-ādi-vat-āsthitaḥ abhivyakti-dharman^ḥ iti apare/ apare
283.11	punar bruvate gandha-ādibhiḥ saha sanniviṣṭaḥ śabdaḥ gandha-ādi-vat eva abhivyajyate
283.12	tasya abhivyañjakāḥ bhūta-viśeṣa-abhighātāḥ iti/ ākāśa-guṇaḥ śabdaḥ
283.13	utpatti-nirodha-dharman^ḥ iti ca anye/ anye punar varṇayanti anityaḥ śabdaḥ
283.14	ākāśa-guṇaḥ utpatti-nirodha-dharmakaḥ iti/ mahābhūta-saṃkṣobha-jaḥ śabdaḥ
283.15	na āśritaḥ utpatti-nirodha-dharman^ḥ iti ca apare/ ataḥ saṃśayaḥ kim atra
283.16	tattvam iti/ anityaḥ iti etad tattvam, kim idam anityatvam nāma
283.17	yataḥ anityaḥ iti bhavati/ kim punar prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau iti eke/ eke
283.18	tāvat varṇayanti yasya prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau staḥ tad anityam iti/ na sambandhāt
283.19	na hi prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvābhyām bhāvaḥ sambaddhyate iti/ anutpanna-
283.20	viśiṣṭayoḥ ca prasaṅgāt/ yadi ca prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau anityatā iti manyase
283.21	anutpanne vastuni prāk-abhāvaḥ asti ūrdhvam ca vastunaḥ pradhvaṃsa-abhāvaḥ asti
283.22	iti asati prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāve animittataḥ anitya-pratyayaḥ syāt/ anityasya
284.01	bhāvaḥ anityatā iti prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvayoḥ abhāva-tvāt bhāva-śabdena anabhidhānam/
284.02	anityam nāma yasya na asti atyantam bhāvaḥ tasya anityasya yaḥ
284.03	bhāvaḥ sā anityatā prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau ca bhāva-pratiṣedhau na ca abhāvau
284.04	bhāva-abhidhānena vaktum yuktau tasmāt na prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau anityatā iti/
284.05	ṣaṣṭhī-arthah ca na asti tasya bhāvaḥ tva-talau iti tasya iti dharmin^ḥ upadiśyate bhāvaḥ
284.06	iti dharmiṇaḥ dharmaḥ na ca abhāvaḥ dharmaḥ na ca dharma-kāle tad-śabda-vācyaḥ
284.07	dharmin^ḥ vidyate na ca avidhyamānasya ṣaṣṭhīā yogaḥ iti/ atha yad bhavati
284.08	tad prāk na bhavati ūrdhvam ca na prabhavati iti manyase/ evam api na
284.09	kim cit pratiṣiddham, vayam api brūmaḥ yad prāk na bhavati tad ūrdhvam na
284.10	bhavati iti/ ṣaṣṭhī-arthaḥ tu pūrvavat na asti ghaṭasya prāk-abhāvaḥ ghaṭasya pradhvaṃsa-
284.11	abhāvaḥ iti/ etad katham, na atra ṣaṣṭhīāḥ sambandhaḥ abhidhīyate api
284.12	tu prāk-idam vastu na asīt paścāt jātam iti arthaḥ/ ūrdhvam ca vināśāt
284.13	idam vastu na bhaviṣyati iti anityam iti ucyate tasya ca bhāvaḥ anityatā
284.14	iti/ vartamāne ca asambhavāt/ yadi prāk-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau anityatā iti
284.15	manyase vartamāne vastuni na prāk-abhāvaḥ na pradhvaṃsa-abhāvaḥ iti anityaḥ
284.16	iti abhilāpaḥ na syāt/ pācaka-ādi-śabda-vat iti cet atha manyase
284.17	yathā amī pācaka-ādi-śabdāḥ tri-kāla-viṣayāḥ tathā ayam anitya-śabdaḥ tri-kāla-
284.18	viṣayaḥ bhaviṣyati iti/ na sthūlaḥ tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvāt sthūlaḥ prayogaḥ
284.19	tri-kāla-viṣayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ pācakaḥ bhavati pācakaḥ bhaviṣyati iti pācakaḥ
284.20	abhūt iti/ pratyayaḥ tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvam iti cet/ atha manyase pratyayaḥ
284.21	api tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvam iti tad ca na adarśanāt na hi asati anitye anityaḥ
284.22	iti prayogam paśyāmaḥ/ tasmāt pratyayaḥ tri-kāla-viṣaya-tvam ayuktam iti/
285.01	ataḥ na prāk-abhāva-pradhvaṃsa-abhāvau anityatā iti vināśa-hetu-bhāvaḥ iti anye/
285.02	anye punar bruvate vināśa-hetu^aḥ asti-tvam anityatā iti/ etad tu na yuktam asati
285.03	bhāvāt prāk-vināśa-hetu-bhāvāt anitya-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ/ anupajāta-
285.04	kapāla-vibhāga^su ghaṭa-ādiṣu yadi vināśa-hetu-bhāvaḥ anityatā syāt vartamāna^su
285.05	ghaṭa-ādiṣu na syāt na hi asati jvare jvarita-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
285.06	vināśa-hetu-bhāvāt ca vināśa-hetuḥ asti iti yuktaḥ pratyayaḥ na anityaḥ iti/
285.07	na hi gotvāt aśva-pratyayaḥ bhavati iti/ atha manyase yasya vināśa-hetuḥ
285.08	asti tad-vināśavat yad yad vināśavat tad anityam iti/ etad api na
285.09	buddhyāmahe yasya vināśe hetuḥ asti tad-vināśavat iti kim tu yasya
285.10	vināśa-hetuḥ asti tad-vināśa-hetumat iti yuktam vaktum na ca anyena yogāt
285.11	anya-nimittaḥ pratyayaḥ yuktaḥ na hi daṇḍa-yogāt kuṇḍalin^ḥ iti bhavati/
285.12	vināśavat-śarīram iti-prayoga-darśanāt iti uktam iti cet/ atha manyase
285.13	vināśavat-śarīram iti dṛṣṭaḥ prayogaḥ na ca asati sambandhe prayogaḥ
285.14	bhavitum arhati/ na matupaḥ asattve adarśanāt/ na ayam matup kva cit asati
285.15	dṛṣṭaḥ na hi asatā kuṇḍalena kuṇḍalin^ḥ iti bhavati na ca asatā vināśena
285.16	sambandhaḥ asti tad-vināśavat-śarīram iti ayuktaḥ prayogaḥ/ dṛṣṭaḥ iti cet/
285.17	atha manyase dṛṣṭaḥ ayam prayogaḥ vināśin etad śarīram adhruvā te viṣayāḥ iti
285.18	na upacārāt vināśin iti upacārena prayogaḥ vināśaḥ yasya avaśyatayā
285.19	bhaviṣyati adhruvā viṣayāḥ iti anityatā eva ucyate tasmāt na vināśa-hetu-
285.20	bhāvaḥ anityatā/ upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptasya atyanta-tirobhāvaḥ anityatā
285.21	iti anye/ anye punar anityatā-śabda-artham anyathā varṇayanti yad upalabdhi-
285.22	lakṣaṇa-prāptam vastu tena eva ātmanā atyantam na upalabhyate tirobhūtam tad anityam
286.01	iti ucyate/ etad tu śūnyam abhidhānam, upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptam atyanta-tirobhūtam
286.02	na upalabhyate iti bruvāṇaḥ siddhāntam bādhate/ na hi kim cit
286.03	vastu-utpannam na upalabhyate iti siddhāntaḥ/ anupajāta-viśeṣasya ca vastunaḥ
286.04	tirobhāvaḥ na yuktaḥ iti anekadhā varṇitam/ tasmāt iyam anityatā na
286.05	yuktā ye punar varṇayanti saḥ eva bhāvaḥ abhūtvā bhavan bhūtvā vā abhavan
286.06	anityaḥ iti ucyate sā ca avasthā bhāva-pratyayena anityatā iti abhidhīyate/ etad
286.07	tu na yuktam, sva-arthe bhāva-pratyayasya adarśanāt na hi kaḥ cit sva-arthe bhāva-
286.08	pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ/ vinaya-ādi iti ādi-vat iti cet na anabhyupagamāt/ nanu
286.09	ca vinaya-ādibhyaḥ ṭhak iti sva-arthe pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ vinayaḥ eva vainayikam
286.10	iti na anabhyupagamāt/ na ayam sva-arthe pratyayaḥ api tu vinayena yogaḥ
286.11	vainayikam iti ucyate evam sarvatra draṣṭavyam/ anupalabhyamāna-bhedānām
286.12	api anumānataḥ bhedaḥ anumeyaḥ/ yān ca api bhavān anupalabhyamāna-bhedān pratyayān
286.13	sva-arthe manyate teṣu api anumānataḥ bhedah anumeyaḥ kim anumānam ṣaṣṭhīā
286.14	bheda-viṣaya-tve sarvā ṣaṣṭhī bheda-viṣaye dṛṣṭā iti yathā ca anartha-antare ṣaṣṭhī
286.15	na asti tathā uktam guṇa-guṇi-bhāva-vāde iti/ tad kim idānīm anityatā iti ayam
286.16	śabdaḥ anabhidheyaḥ eva/ na anabhidheyaḥ avadhi-apekṣa-anapekṣa-abhedāt sattā eva
286.17	ubhayathā yā ubhaya-anta-paricchinna-vastu-sattā sā anityatā iti/ yā tu ubhaya-
286.18	anta-anavacchinna-vastu-sattā sā nityatā/ yathā samavāyaḥ ekaḥ san
286.19	kāryena viśiṣyamāṇaḥ kāraṇam abhidhīyate kāraṇena viśiṣyamāṇaḥ
286.20	kāryam iti ca katham iti kāraṇam iti ayam pratyayaḥ na animittakaḥ
286.21	kadā cit bhāvāt na ca vastu-nimittaḥ avastu-pratyayaḥ vyatireki-tvāt na
286.22	ca sattātaḥ vailakṣaṇyāt sattātaḥ sat iti syāt ayam tu sattā-pratyayāt
287.01	vilakṣaṇaḥ tasmāt na sattātaḥ iha-viśeṣaṇa-nimitta-tvāt ca sambandha-nimittaḥ
287.02	iha tantuṣu paṭaḥ iti ādhāra-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyayaḥ upajāyate na ca asati
287.03	sambandhe ihapratyayena śakyam bhavitum yataḥ ayam iha-pratyayaḥ saḥ samavāyaḥ
287.04	kārya-samavāye sati kāraṇam iti abhidhīyate yasya guṇasya yogāt yasmin arthe
287.05	śabda-niveśaḥ tad-abhidhāne tva-talau kārya-samavāyasya bhāvāt etad bhavati iti
287.06	kārya-samavāyaḥ kāraṇatvam/ etena kāryatvam vyākhyātam/ kārya-ātmalābha-
287.07	hetuḥ samavāyaḥ kāraṇa-viśiṣṭaḥ kāryatvam yathā samavāyaḥ evam
287.08	sattā api ekā satī ubhaya-anta-paricchinna-vastu-sambandhi-tvena anityatā iti ucyate
287.09	viparyayāt nityatā iti/ etayā antiyatayā yogāt anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti//
287.10	[2-2-13(14)]ādimat-tvāt aindriyaka-tvāt kṛtaka-vat upacārāt ca
287.11	ādimat-tvāt ādiḥ yoniḥ kāraṇam iti/ kāraṇavat-tvāt anityaḥ
287.12	iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ kim punar idam kāraṇavat-tvam nāma nimittakāraṇa-
287.13	bheda-anuvidhānam utpatti-dharmaka-tvam tad-abhivyaṅgya^su na asti/ na hi kim cit
287.14	abhivyajjyamānam nimittakāraṇa-bheda-anuvidhāyin dṛṣṭam iti/ saṃyoga-
287.15	vibhāga-anantaram upalabdhi^aḥ sandehaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase saṃyoga-vibhāga-
287.16	anantaram upalabhyate śabdaḥ sā ca tad-anantara-upalabdhiḥ vyajyamānasya api yuktā
287.17	ataḥ sandehaḥ kim ayam saṃyoga-vibhāgābhyām vyajyate atha kriyate iti/
287.18	na vyajyate aindriyaka-tvāt kim idam aindriyaka-tvam, indriya-pratyāsatti-
287.19	grāhyam aindriyakam iti tataḥ kim yadi indriya-pratyāsatti-grāhyaḥ aindriyakaḥ
287.20	tataḥ na vyajyate/ na hi vyajyamānasya indriyena pratyāsattiḥ yuktā katham
287.21	iti na śrotram tāvat śabda-deśam upagacchati amūrta-tvāt yad amūrtam tad niḥkriyam
288.01	yathā rūpa-ādi amūrtam ca ākāśam tasmāt niḥkriyam iti/ kriyā-
288.02	kāraṇa-guṇa-samavāyāt kriyāvat-tvam iti cet/ atha manyase yatra kriyā-
288.03	kāraṇam guṇaḥ vartate tad kriyāvat dṛṣṭam yathā loṣṭham tathā ca ākāśam tasmāt
288.04	etad api kriyāvat iti/ na anekāntāt/ kriyā-kāraṇam ca guṇaḥ
288.05	ātmani na kriyāvān ātman^ḥ iti anekāntaḥ vidyamānasya api na kriyā-
288.06	hetu-tvam mahat-parimāṇa-pratibandhāt vidyamānaḥ api ākāśe abhighātaḥ na
288.07	kriyā-hetuḥ mahat-parimāṇa-pratibandhāt/ yad ākāśe mahattvam tena eka-artha-
288.08	samavetena tad-vṛttiḥ abhighātaḥ pratibadhyate iti yathā loṣṭha-gurutvam tad-
288.09	eka-artha-vṛttinā saṃyogena saṃyoga-pratibandhāt gurutvasya sāpekṣa-tva-
288.10	prasaṅgaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase yadi saṃyogena pratibadhyamānam gurutvam na
288.11	kriyām ārabhate tad pratibandha-apagama-apekṣam ārabhate iti/ nanu evam gurutvam
288.12	sāpekṣam kriyā-kāraṇam iti prāptam/ evam ca śāstra-vyāghātaḥ nirapekṣa-
288.13	gurutvam karma-kāraṇam iti hi śāstram/ na nirapekṣasya anyathā vyākhyānāt
288.14	na ayam nirapekṣasya arthaḥ gurutvam na kim cit apekṣate iti api tu carama-
288.15	bhāvi-nimitta-antaram na apekṣate iti nirapekṣam gurutvam karma-kāraṇam iti
288.16	na asti vyāghātaḥ/ tathā ca śāstram saṃyoga-abhāve gurutvāt patanam
288.17	iti/ viṣaya-abhāvāt ca kriyā-kāraṇa-guṇa-samavadhāna-mātram kriyāvat-
288.18	tve hetuḥ api tu kriyā-kāraṇa-guṇa-samavadhāne sati mūrtaḥ yadi
288.19	kriyā-viṣayaḥ mūrtiḥ bhavati kriyā-kāraṇam guṇaḥ apratibaddhaḥ bhavati tadā
288.20	kriyā utpadyate iti/ tasmāt akriyāvat-tvāt ākāśam na gacchati śabdaḥ api
288.21	na āgacchati niḥkriya-tvāt eva na ca aprāptasya grahaṇam asti sarva-śabda-upalabdhi-
288.22	prasaṅgāt/ pariśeṣāt tu santāna-siddhiḥ/ tatra ādyaḥ śabdaḥ saṃyoga-
289.01	vibhāga-hetukaḥ tasmāt śabda-antarāṇi kadamba-golaka-nyāyena sarva-diś^kāni
289.02	tebhyaḥ pratyekam ekaikaḥ śabdaḥ mandatara-tama-ādi-nyāyena āśraya-apratibandham
289.03	anuvidhīyamānaḥ prādurasti tataḥ antyasya atimāndyāt śabda-antara-utpatti-
289.04	śakti-vighātaḥ yena kena cit pratibandhāt bhavati iti/ ataḥ śabda-santāna-
289.05	ucchedaḥ tatra yaḥ karṇa-śaṣkulīm antamn ākāśa-deśam prāpnoti saḥ upalabhyate
289.06	na itaraḥ iti tasmāt aindriyaka-tvam tad ca anyathā na yuktam iti ataḥ anityaḥ iti/
289.07	ekaḥ eva iti cet na sarvaiḥ upalabdhi-prasaṅgāt/ atha manyase ne eva śabda-
289.08	antarāṇi mayā abhyupagamyante api tu ekaḥ eva ayam śabdaḥ vitatya-ākāśam avatiṣṭhate
289.09	saḥ saṃyoga-vibhāgābhyām abhivyaktaḥ san upalabhyate yathā ghaṭa-ādi-apavaraka-
289.10	avasthitam pradīpa-saṃyogena iti/ tad ca na sarvaiḥ upalabdhi-prasaṅgāt yadi ekaḥ
289.11	śabdaḥ vitatya-avasthitaḥ saṃyogena vyaktaḥ śrotravataḥ arthe pratyayam karoti iti
289.12	manyase tadā yena kena cit yatra tatra avasthitena śabdaḥ vyaktaḥ iti sarvaiḥ
289.13	śrotravadbhiḥ upalabhyeta na saḥ ca upalabhyate tasmāt kalpanā iyam/ ekadeśa-
289.14	abhivyaktiḥ iti cet na kimaḥ tādātmya-atādātmya-anupapatti^aḥ/ atha manyase
289.15	satyam vitatya-avasthitaḥ tasya tu avasthitasya ekadeśaḥ nimittena vyajyate
289.16	iti/ na kimaḥ tādātmya-atādātmya-anupapatti^aḥ/ yān tān ekadeśān śabdasya
289.17	manyase te kim śabda-ātmakāḥ āho na iti/ kim ca ataḥ yadi śabda-ātmakāḥ
289.18	tataḥ aneke śabdāḥ iti vyāghātaḥ atha aśabda-ātmakāḥ na tadā
289.19	śabdāt artha-pratyayaḥ iti prāptam/ teṣām svabhāvaḥ ca vimoktavyaḥ/ śabdasya
289.20	ākāśa-vṛtti-tvāt ekadeśa-artha-anabhidhānāt ca/ ekadeśaḥ iti samāsa-
289.21	padam etad ekaḥ ca asau deśaḥ ca iti/ tatra deśa-śabdaḥ ādhāra-arthaḥ kāraṇa-
290.01	arthaḥ vā/ tatra ādhāra-arthaḥ tāvat na yuktaḥ śabdasya ākāśa-vṛtti-tvāt ākāśa-
290.02	vṛttiḥ śabdaḥ tad-guṇa-tvāt tasmāt ākāśa-vyatiriktaḥ na ādhāraḥ iti/
290.03	kāraṇa-arthaḥ api na asti akṛtakatva-abhyupagamāt akṛtakaḥ śabdaḥ iti svayam
290.04	pratipadyamānāḥ bhavantaḥ na arhanti vaktum kāraṇam ekadeśaḥ iti na ca anyaḥ
290.05	ekadeśa-arthaḥ sambhavati iti śūnyam abhidhānam ekadeśaḥ śabdasya vyajyate ekadeśa-
290.06	abhivyakti^i ca artha-pratyayaḥ na syāt varṇa-vat varṇāḥ sarve eva vyāpakāḥ
290.07	bhavanti iti etasmin pakṣe varṇa-ekadeśa-vyaktiḥ na ca varṇa-ekadeśaḥ śakyaḥ
290.08	nirūpayitum na ca nirūpitāt api tasmāt artha-pratyayaḥ bhavati varṇānām
290.09	artha-pratyāyana-anabhyupagamāt/ varṇāḥ api tāvat ekaikaśaḥ artha-pratyāyakāḥ
290.10	na bhavanti kutaḥ tad ekadeśāḥ iti/ etena bahutvam pratyuktam/ yathā
290.11	ekasya vyakti^i doṣaḥ evam aneka-śabdasya api vyāpi-tve doṣaḥ kolāhalaḥ ca
290.12	syāt/ yadi ca aneka-śabdāḥ ākāśe vartante yugapat iti pratipadyante
290.13	eka-śabda-abhivyañjaka-upādāne samāna-deśānām śabdānām vyaktiḥ iti
290.14	kolāhalaḥ ca syāt/ yathā malla-naṭa-samāja^su prayoga-ja-jhaṅkāra-atiśaya-
290.15	āvarjita^su nadatsu bhavati tasmāt na anekaḥ vyāpakaḥ iti niyamaḥ ca na
290.16	syāt/ yadi ca aneke śabdāḥ yugapat ākāśe vartante iti/ evam ca yad
290.17	kim cit vyañjakam upāttam samāna-deśān sarvān abhivyanakti iti/ yadā
290.18	vīṇāḥ vādyate tadā rāsabha-dhvaniḥ api śrūyate na hi samāna-indriya-
290.19	grāhyāṇām samāna-deśānām vyañjaka^su niyamaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ/ yadi asya vyañjakam
290.20	tena tasya vyaktiḥ iti cet tad na adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ atha manyase aneka-śabda-sannipāte
290.21	sati vyañjakāni bhidyante vyañjaka-bhedāt ca anuvidhāyinīaḥ vyaktyaḥ
291.01	pratiśabdam upajāyante iti tad na adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ na hi samāna-deśānām eka-
291.02	indriya-grāhyāṇām vyañjaka^su niyamaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ/ na hi pradīpaḥ eka-indriya-grāhyam
291.03	anekam artham yugapat sannipatitam na prakāśayati/ sāmānyavat etad
291.04	syāt iti cet na anabhyupagamāt/ atha manyase aneka-artha-sannipāte sati
291.05	sarvābhiḥ vyaktibhiḥ sāmānyāni sambaddhyante aneka-sambandhe sati sāmānyasya
291.06	kena cit arthena kim cit sāmānyam vyajyate iti/ evam aneka-artha-sannipāte sati
291.07	kim cit vyañjakam kim cit śabdam vyanakti iti tad na anabhyupagamāt/ sāmānyam
291.08	anekena bhinna-jātīyena arthena sambaddhyate iti na abhyupagamyate api tu
291.09	sva-viṣaya-sarva-gatam sāmānyam gotvam goṣu eva na aśva-ādiṣu aśvatvam aśva^su eva na
291.10	go-ādiṣu tasmāt asamaḥ ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ vyañjaka-abhāve ca upalabdhi^aḥ na vyajyate
291.11	śabdaḥ/ yadi ca saṃyoga-vibhāgābhyām vyaktaḥ śabdaḥ upalabhyate iti manyase
291.12	saḥ api saṃyoga-abhāve na upalabhyeta upalabhyate ca dāru-vraścane dāru-paraśu-saṃyoga-
291.13	nivṛtti^i iti/ abhighātāt vāyuḥ iti cet atra uktam atha manyase
291.14	dāru-paraśu^oḥ abhighātāt vāyuḥ jāyte saḥ vāyuḥ santāna-vṛtti^ā vartate santāna-
291.15	vṛtti^ā vartamānaḥ karṇa-śaṣkulīm antam ākāśam prāpnoti/ tad-prāpti^i tatra-sthaḥ
291.16	śabdaḥ gṛhyate iti atra uktam yathā ekaḥ śabdaḥ karṇa-śaṣkulīm atyākāśa-
291.17	deśe vartate tathā sarve eva śabdāḥ vartante iti yad kim cit vyañjakam upāttam
291.18	yasya kasya cit śabdasya vyañjakam syāt iti sarva-śabda-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ niyamaḥ ca
291.19	na syāt nimitta-sannidhāne ca sarvatra upalabdhi^aḥ na vyajyate yad khalu
291.20	vyajyate tad-nimitta-sannidhāne sati na sarvatra vyajyamānam dṛṣṭam yathā
291.21	ghaṭa-arthinaḥ vibhrāṇāḥ pradīpam ghaṭa-ādi-rahita-anapavaraka-ādīn praviśya na
291.22	ghaṭa-ādīn upalabhante iti abhivyakti^ā ghaṭa-ādīn na pratipadyante/ na punar
292.01	tālu-ādi-abhighāta-sannipāte sati kva cit api śabdāt na paśyāmaḥ tasmāt na
292.02	vyajyate iti/ sarvatra santi iti cet na ukta-uttara-tvāt/ atha manyase
292.03	yasya ekadeśa-vṛttyaḥ  śabdāḥ tam pratyayam upālambhaḥ asmākam tu sarve eva
292.04	vyāpakāḥ tasmāt na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt ukta-uttaram etad vākyam na
292.05	punar pratisamādhānam prayojayati iti tasmāt na vyajyate iti/ nāda-upalabdhi^i
292.06	ca viprakṛṣṭa-deśa-avasthitena nāda-samāna-deśa-śabda-anupalabdhi^aḥ na vyañjakaḥ
292.07	nādaḥ/ ete na vāyavīyāḥ saṃyoga-vibhāgāḥ vyañjakāḥ iti pratyuktam/
292.08	kṛtaka-vat upacārāt ca tīvram mandam it kṛtakam upacaryate tīvram sukham mandam
292.09	sukham iti upacaryate ca tīvraḥ śabdaḥ mandaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ atra ca
292.10	prayogaḥ/ anityaḥ śabdaḥ tīvra-manda-viṣaya-tvāt sukha-duḥkha-vat iti kṛtaka-
292.11	vat upacārāt iti anena sūtrena sarva-anityatva-sādhana-dharma-saṃgrahaḥ/ kṛtaka-vat grahaṇasya
292.12	udāharaṇa-artha-tvāt/ yathā sāmānya-viśeṣavataḥ asmad-ādi-bāhyakaraṇa-
292.13	pratyakṣa-tvāt uapabhyasya anupalabdhi-kāraṇa-abhāve sati anupalabdhi^aḥ guṇasya
292.14	sataḥ asmad-ādi-bāhyakaraṇa-tvāt iti evam ādi/ tīvra-śabdasya tīvra-
292.15	artha-viṣaya-tvāt na śabda-ādi-viṣaya-tvam iti cet/ atha manyase tīvra-śabdena
292.16	tīvra-arthaḥ abhidhīyate na śabdaḥ na ca anya-viṣayaḥ śabdaḥ anyasya arthasya pratipādakaḥ
292.17	iti na śabda-śabdena samānādhikaraṇasya tīvra-śabdasya abhidhānāt
292.18	satyam ayam tīvraḥ śabdaḥ kevalaḥ uccāritaḥ dravya-guṇa-karmaṇām samāna-dharmam
292.19	pratipādayati śabdena śabdau uccaratā śabda-śabdaḥ api uccaran aneka-dharma-yoginam śabdam abhidhatte/
292.20	yadā punar etau tīvra-śabdau viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāva-āpannau abhidhīyete tadā
292.21	tīvra-śabdena dharma-antarāṇi vyavacchidyante śabda-śabdena dharmi-antarāṇi iti
293.01	ubhaya-śabda-prayogāt śabdaḥ gamyate tasmāt upapannam tīvra-śabda-viṣaya-tvāt
293.02	anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ vyañjakasya tathā-bhāvāt grahaṇasya tīvra-mandatā-
293.03	rūpa-vat iti cet na abhibhava-upapatti^aḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ grahaṇam nimitta-
293.04	bheda-anuvidhāyin iti cet na grahaṇa-bhedasya ekatra adṛṣṭa-tvāt/ atha manyase
293.05	na śabdaḥ bhidyate śabde tu abhinne grahaṇam nimitta-bheda-anuvidhāyin bhavati/
293.06	grahaṇāt ca abhibhavaḥ iti na grahaṇa-bhedasya ekatra adṛṣṭa-tvāt yadi abhinnaḥ
293.07	śabdaḥ grahaṇa-bhedaḥ na prāpnoti na hi abhinne grahaṇa-bhedam viṣaye paśyāmaḥ/
293.08	atha abhinne api viṣaye grahaṇāni bhidyante iti manyase nityam grahaṇa-
293.09	bhedaḥ syāt tataḥ bhinnaḥ abhinnaḥ iti bhinna-abhinna-pratyayau na
293.10	syātām/ abhibhava-anupapattiḥ ca grahaṇayoḥ yūgapat asambhavāt na hi
293.11	grahaṇe yugapat bhavataḥ na ca ekam grahaṇam ātmanaḥ eva abhibhāvakam na ca ayam
293.12	abhibhavaḥ na asti tasmāt bhidyate śabdaḥ abhibhava-anupapattiḥ ca vyañjaka-samāna-
293.13	deśasya abhivyakti^i prāpti-abhāvāt vyañjakena samāna-deśaḥ abhivyajyate
293.14	iti etasmin pakṣe na upapadyate abhibhavaḥ kasmāt vyañjaka-samāna-deśa-tvāt na
293.15	hi vyañjaka-samāna-deśena aprāptaḥ śabdaḥ abhibhavitum śakyaḥ aprāpta-tvāt
293.16	na hi aprāptam abhibhāvakam bhavati/ atha aprāptam api abhibhāvakam bhavati iti
293.17	manyase vīṇā-śabdānām aśravaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ atha aprāptāḥ śaṅkha-rāsabha-ādi-śabdāḥ
293.18	abhibhāvakāḥ iti na kadā cit vīṇā-ādi-śabdāḥ śrūyeran/ sarvatra
293.19	santi iti cet na pūrva-doṣa-anuvṛtti^aḥ/ atha manyase yaḥ vyañjaka-samāna-deśam
293.20	śabdam abhyupaiti tasya eṣaḥ doṣaḥ asmākam tu sarve eva śabdāḥ samāna-deśāḥ
293.21	tasmāt adoṣaḥ ayam iti na pūrva-doṣa-anativṛtti^aḥ samāna-deśa-tve śabdānām
293.22	vyañjaka^su niyamaḥ na asti iti yad kim cit vyañjakam upāttam na sarva-
294.01	śabdasya vyakti-hetuḥ iti pūrva-doṣa-anativṛttiḥ iti/ samāna-deśa-tve ca
294.02	vīṇā-veṇu-śabdānām saṅkha-śabdena vīṇā-veṇu-śabdayoḥ abhibhavaḥ iti
294.03	vīṇā-ādi-śabda-aśravaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ tasmāt śabdāḥ eva bhidyante iti teṣām ca
294.04	bhinnānām samāna-deśa-tve sati ye karṇa-śaṣkulīm atyākāśa-deśe yugapat
294.05	samavayanti tatra yaḥ paṭuḥ śabdaḥ saḥ itarasya abhibhāvakaḥ iti
294.06	abhibhavaḥ ca nāma mandasya paṭīyasaḥ grahaṇāt agrahaṇam iti/ yadi ca
294.07	santāna-vṛttiḥ śabdaḥ karṇa-śaṣkulīmat ākāśa-deśam prāptaḥ ca gṛhyate
294.08	diś-deśa-pratyayaḥ na prāpnoti pūrvena śabdaḥ uttarena śabdaḥ iti na hi
294.09	viṣaya^su pratyāsīdatsu diś-deśa-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ gandha-ādi-vat iti/ na upādāna-
294.10	nimitta-tvāt diś-deśa-vyapadeśasya yaḥ ayam śabda^su diś-deśa-vyapadeśaḥ na ayam
294.11	śabda-nimittaḥ pūrva-apara-ādi-bhinna^bhyaḥ nimitta^bhyaḥ upajāyamānānām ekadeśa-
294.12	grahaṇāt tulyaḥ diś-deśa-vyapadeśaḥ syāt/ tasmāt na śabda-nimittaḥ
294.13	kutaḥ tarhi ayam bhavati iti vācyam nimitta-bhedāt yāni nimittāni
294.14	śabda-kāraṇasya saṃyogasya anugraha-kārīṇi diś-deśavanti teṣām nimittānām
294.15	bhedāt diś-deśa-pratyayāḥ iti/ ye upalabhyamāna-nimittāḥ śabdāḥ
294.16	teṣu ayam yuktaḥ yeṣām tu nimittāni na upalabhyante teṣu diś-deśa-pratyayaḥ
294.17	na yuktaḥ na na yuktaḥ śabda-santānasya nimitta-bheda-abhimukhyena ādi^i
294.18	prāpti^aḥ yaḥ śabda-santānaḥ yasmāt nimittāt upajāyate saḥ samantāt
294.19	śabda-antarāṇi santanoti tatra yaḥ śabdaḥ ādi^i karṇa-śaṣkulīmat-
294.20	ākāśa-deśasya tad-abhimukhena bhāgena sambaddhyate tad-nimittaḥ pūrva-apara-ādi-
294.21	śabda-vyapadeśaḥ yadā punar ādya-śabda-paricchedaḥ na asti tadā viparyayaḥ
294.22	iti/eke tu bruvate na eva śabda^su diś-deśa-pratyayāḥ santi
295.01	kutaḥ cakṣus-viṣaya-siddhi^i diś-deśa-adhyavasāyāt/ tathā hi jāti-andhānām
295.02	pūrva-apara-ādi-pratyayāḥ śabde na santi iti/ apare tu diś-deśa-vyapadeśena
295.03	cakṣus-śrotre aprāpya-kāriṇī iti bruvate/ teṣām uktam uttaram pratyakṣa-sūtram
295.04	varṇayadbhiḥ iti//
295.05	[2-2-14(15)]na ghaṭa-abhāva-sāmānya-nitya-tvāt nitya^su api anitya-vat upacārāt
295.06	ca
295.07	na ghaṭa-abhāva-sāmānya-nitya-tvāt nitya^su api anitya-vat upacārāt ca/ sūtrasya
295.08	pūrva-hetūnām anaikāntika-tva-jñāpanam arthaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye
295.09	[2-2-15(16)]tattva-bhāktayoḥ nānātvasya vibhāgāt avyabhicāraḥ
295.10	tattva-bhāktayoḥ nānātvasya vibhāgāt avyabhicāraḥ/ nityatvam iti atra
295.11	kim tāvat tattvam/ ubhaya-anta-aparicchinna-vastu-sattā-saṃsparśaḥ iti/
295.12	viparyayāt anityatvam etad ca abhāve na asti/ bhāktam tu tad bhavati nityaḥ
295.13	iva nityaḥ ghaṭa-abhāvaḥ iti na punar nityaḥ eva kāla-dvaya-ananubhavāt
295.14	pradhvaṃsa-abhāvaḥ na prāk asti na ghaṭa-kāle asti prāk-abhāvaḥ na ghaṭa-kāle na
295.15	pradhvaṃsa-kāle iti/ katham tarhi nityaḥ iva nityaḥ iti ucyate/ bhakti^ā kā
295.16	punar iyam bhaktiḥ prāk-abhāvasya kāraṇa-abhāvaḥ pradhvaṃsa-abhāvasya vināśa-
295.17	abhāvaḥ ubhayam ca etad nitya-viṣayaḥ iti etad sāmānyāt nityaḥ iva nityaḥ na
295.18	punar nityaḥ eva iti  indriya-pratyāsatti-grāhyaḥ aindriyakaḥ iti//
295.19	[2-2-16(15)]santāna-anumāna-viśeṣaṇāt
296.01	santāna-anumāna-viśeṣaṇāt avyabhicāraḥ/ na vayam aindriyaka-tvāt iti
296.02	anena anityatvam pratipādayāmaḥ api tu śabdasya abhivyaktim pratiṣedhāmaḥ/
296.03	na hi vyajyamānasya aindriyaka-tvam yuktam iti purastāt uktam//
296.04	[2-2-17(18)]kāraṇa-dravyasya pradeśa-śabdena abhidhānāt nitya^su api avyabhicāraḥ
296.05	iti
296.06	kāraṇa-dravyasya pradeśa-śabdena abhidhānāt nitya^su api avyabhicāraḥ/ na hi
296.07	nityānām dravyāṇām pradeśa-śabdena kāraṇam ucyate/ ākāśasya pradeśaḥ
296.08	ātmanaḥ pradeśaḥ iti na ākāśa-ātmanoḥ kāraṇa-dravyam abhidhīyate yathā
296.09	kṛtakasya kim iti na abhidhīyate avidyamāna-tvāt na hi vidyamānāḥ
296.10	ākāśa-ādi^aḥ pradeśāḥ iti/ katham na vidyante pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ
296.11	yadi ākāśa-ādi^aḥ pradeśāḥ syuḥ pramāṇataḥ upalabhyeran iti katham
296.12	pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhiḥ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ pradeśa-śabdena kāraṇam vā abhidhīyate
296.13	ādhāraḥ vā na kāraṇam akṛtaka-tvāt yasmāt akṛtakam ākāśam ataḥ na
296.14	kāraṇa-arthaḥ pradeśa-śabdena ucyate na ādhāraḥ anāśrita-tvāt anāśritam ākāśam
296.15	tasya ādhāra-arthaḥ aśakyaḥ vaktum iti/ loka-virodhaḥ iti cet na anyathā
296.16	tad-anupapatti^aḥ yadi ākāśam niḥpradeśam pratipadyase nanu lokaḥ viruddhyate
296.17	pūrva-bhāgena vyāptaḥ ādityaḥ iti/ na anyathā tad-anupapatti^aḥ/ saṃyogasya
296.18	avyāpya-vṛtti-tve pradeśa-śabdena abhidhīyamāne na loka-virodhaḥ yasmāt yad
296.19	saṃyogasya avyāpya-vṛtti-tvam tad pradeśa-śabdena abhidhīyate/ etad ca pradeśavatā
297.01	dravyena ākāśasya sāmānyam/ ye khalu paricchinne āmalaka-ādike
297.02	bhavataḥ tayoḥ yaḥ saṃyogaḥ vartate saḥ te na vyāpnoti pradeśavatī iva te
297.03	ākāśa-saṃyogaḥ api na ākāśam vyāpnoti iti pradeśavat-dravya-sāmānyāt pradeśavat-
297.04	ākāśam iti bhāktaḥ pratyayaḥ/ kā punar iyam bhaktiḥ atathā-bhūtasya
297.05	tathā-bhāvibhiḥ sāmānyam ubhayena bhajyate iti bhaktiḥ/ etasmāt
297.06	sāmānyāt adhyāropita-pradeśa-vṛttiḥ pradeśa-śabdaḥ yasmāt ākāśasya pradeśavadbhiḥ
297.07	dravyaiḥ sāmānyam saṃyogasya vyāpya-vṛtti-tvam asti ataḥ pradeśa-śabdaḥ api ākāśe
297.08	prayujyate iti/ evam etad na tattvataḥ kutaḥ iti cet na pramāṇataḥ
297.09	anupalabdhi^aḥ/ atha manyase ākāśa-pradeśāḥ pradeśavat-dravya-sāmānyāt ākāśe
297.10	upacaryante na punar tattvataḥ tad asti iti na pramāṇam asti iti na pradeśasya
297.11	pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ/ na hi ākāśa-ādi^aḥ pradeśa-pratipādakam pratyakṣa-ādi
297.12	pramāṇam sambhavati iti/ svarūpena anabhidhānāt ca/ yadi ākāśasya pradeśāḥ
297.13	syuḥ te svarūpena vyapadiśyeran yathā paṭasya pradeśāḥ ke tantu^āḥ na
297.14	punar ākāśasya pradeśāḥ ke iti ukte svarūpena vyapadeṣṭum śakyāḥ iti/
297.15	kimaḥ anupapattiḥ ca pūrvavat/ yān ca ākāśa-pradeśa-antarān manyate te kim
297.16	ākāśa-ātmakāḥ uta na iti kim ca ataḥ yadi ākāśa-ātmakāḥ anekam ākāśam
297.17	iti prāptam/ atha na teṣām svabhāvaḥ vaktavyaḥ katham ca te tasya pradeśāḥ
297.18	iti vaktavyam/ anākāśa-ātmakāḥ santaḥ ākāśasya pradeśāḥ iti
297.19	kena arthena abhidhīyate tad yathā tantu^āḥ paṭasya pradeśāḥ iti ukte kāraṇa-arthaḥ
297.20	ādhāra-arthaḥ ca gamyate na punar ākāśasya pradeśa^su etad asti/ pradeśa-
297.21	abhāvāt anādhāraḥ saṃyogaḥ iti cet na anādhāraḥ ākāśa-vṛtti-tvāt/ atha
298.01	manuṣe yadi ākāśa-deśāḥ na santi ākāśa-deśa-āśrayaḥ saṃyogaḥ anādhāraḥ
298.02	na anādhāraḥ ākāśa-vṛtti-tvāt/ yasmāt tvayā eva ucyate ākāśa-
298.03	āśrayaḥ iti tasmāt na anādhāraḥ iti samāna-deśāḥ iti cet na abādhanāt/
298.04	atha manyase mūrtimat-dravya-saṃyogāḥ ye ākāśa-deśe vartante te pradeśa-abhāvāt
298.05	sarve eva samāna-deśāḥ prāpnuvanti evam ca samāna-deśa-tve sati na
298.06	kim cit bādhyate iti tad evam pradeśavat-dravya-sāmānyāt ākāśa-pradeśaḥ iti
298.07	bhāktaḥ pratyayaḥ iti/ niṣpradeśa-tvāt sarvena sarva-śabda-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ iti
298.08	cet na ukta-uttara-tvāt/ atha manuṣe yadi niṣpradeśam ākāśam tena
298.09	tarhi sarva-śabdāḥ samāna-deśāḥ iti sarvaiḥ sarvaḥ śabdaḥ upalabhyeta na
298.10	tu upalabhyate tasmāt anya-anya-vṛttyaḥ  śabdāḥ sarvaiḥ agrahaṇāt aṅguli-rūpa-ādi-vat
298.11	iti/ etad ca na ukta-uttara-tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etad yathā saṃyogasya
298.13	tvāt hetu^aḥ śabda-saṃyoga-ādi aṅguli-rūpa-vat vyāpakam pratipadyante te evam praṣṭavyāḥ
298.14	vyāpakatvam iti kaḥ arthaḥ yadi āśraya-vṛtti-tvam vyāpakatvam na kim cit bādhyate
298.15	atha anyat tad na asti/ vayam tu vyāptim aṅguli-rūpasya āśraya-upalabdhi^i upalabdhim
298.16	brūmaḥ na punar śabda-ādyaḥ  āśraya-upalabdhi^i upalabhyante tasmāt aṅguli-rūpa-
298.17	vat na vyāpakāḥ iti samudita-sthānānām eka-samudāyi-upalabdhi^i upalabdhi-
298.18	anupalabdhī vyāpti-avyāptī eka-āśrayāṇām āśraya-upalabdhi^i iti/ atha ye
298.19	ākāśe mūrtimatām saṃyogāḥ teṣām kān cit ke cit kṛtakān pratipadyante
298.20	kān cit akṛtakān iti/ tatra kārya-dravya-saṃyogaḥ kṛtakaḥ saṃyoga-ja-tvāt
298.21	utpadyamānasya kāraṇa-sambandhibhiḥ kāryasya saṃyoga-jaḥ saṃyogaḥ iti
299.01	kriyā-janyam tu ke cit na icchanti yāvat-dravya-bhāvi-tvāt saṃyogasya avibhaktasya
299.02	saṃyogaḥ na asti iti na asti ākāśe kriyā-jaḥ saṃyogaḥ iti/ aṇu-
299.03	ākāśa-saṃyogam tu kṛtakam iti icchanti/ tatra aṇu-ākāśa-saṃyogasya tāvat
299.04	kṛtaka-tve anumānam ākāśena kṛtaka-sambandhena sambandhī paramāṇuḥ mūrtimat-
299.05	tva-ādibhyaḥ ghaṭa-ādi-vat iti/ kārya-ākāśa-saṃyogaḥ vipratipatti-viṣayaḥ na
299.06	yāvat-dravya-bhāvin^ḥ avinaśyat-ādhāravat-tve kriyāvat-dravya-vṛtti-tve sati aprāpti-pratidvandvi-
299.07	tvāt mūrtimat-itara-saṃyoga-vat tasmāt kārya-dravyena ākāśasya saṃyoga-
299.08	jaḥ saṃyogaḥ kriyā-jaḥ ca paramāṇu^aḥ tu kriyā-jaḥ iti/ vipratipatti-viṣaya-
299.09	avibhāginaḥ ākāśa-ādyaḥ  kriyāvat-dravya-saṃyogi-tvāt paramāṇu-vat/
299.10	yathā paramāṇu^aḥ kriyāvat-dravya-saṃyogāḥ ayāvat-dravya-bhāvinaḥ, saṃyogavat-
299.11	śabda-vat buddhi-ādyaḥ  ca ātmani avyāpakāḥ saṃyogasya ca avyāpya-vṛtti-tvam ātmanaḥ
299.12	pradeśa-arthaḥ iti vyākhyātam/ ekaḥ ca aneka-mūrtimat-pradeśavat-dravya-sambandhi-tvam
299.13	pradeśa-arthaḥ/ yad vā yad ākāśasya ekasya sataḥ pradeśavatā anekena sambandhi-tvam
299.14	tad pradeśavat-dravya-sāmānyam yathā ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ/ kasmāt punar sūtra-kārasya asmin
299.15	arthe sūtram na śrūyate iti/ katam asmin arthe niṣpradeśam ākāśam niṣpradeśaḥ
299.16	ātman^ḥ iti arthataḥ adhigati^aḥ na sūtram yasmāt ayam arthaḥ arthataḥ adhigamyate tataḥ na
299.17	sūtritaḥ iti/ śabda-santāna-pratipādane vā na sūtram arthataḥ adhigati^aḥ/
299.18	śāstra-siddhāntāt vā tad-adhigatiḥ śāstra-siddhāntaḥ tu nyāya-samākhyātam bahu-
299.19	śākham anumānam tena anuktam api gamyate iti na sūtryate/  atha api idam asti idam
299.20	na asti iti bhāṣyam/ yaḥ ayam paryanuyogaḥ tasya kaḥ viṣayaḥ iti/ anupalabhyamāna-
300.01	asti-tvam ye śabdasya nitya-tvam kalpayanti te idam-paryanuyojyāḥ
300.02	idam asti idam na asti iti etad bhavantaḥ katham pratipadyante iti/ evam anuyuktāḥ
300.03	santaḥ pratibruvate pramāṇataḥ upalabdhi^aḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ ca iti avidyamānaḥ tarhi
300.04	śabdaḥ//
300.05	[2-2-18(19)]prāk-uccāraṇāt anupalabdhi^aḥ āvaraṇa-ādi-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca
300.06	prāk-uccāraṇāt anupalabdhi^aḥ āvaraṇa-ādi-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca iti/ yad ca ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam
300.07	ghaṭa-ādi-anitya-tvena tena ca anuyojyāḥ yd idam anityam ghaṭa-ādi
300.08	bhavadbhiḥ praipadyate tad katham anityam iti/ evam anuyuktāḥ yadi ghaṭa-ādi-
300.09	anitya-tva-nyāyam pratipadyante saḥ śabde api iti sūtram/ sataḥ anupalabdhi-
300.10	kāraṇa-abhāve kadā cit anupalabdhi^aḥ anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ śeṣam
300.11	bhāṣye/ vyañjaka-abhāvāt agrahaṇam iti cet na ukta-uttara-tvāt śabdasya uccāraṇam
300.12	vyañjakam tasya abhāvāt na upalabhyate iti asiddham viśeṣaṇam anupalabdhi-
300.13	kāraṇa-abhāvaḥ iti/ kim idam uccāraṇam nānam vivakṣā-janitena prayatnena
300.14	kauṣṭhyasya vāyu^aḥ preritasya kaṇṭhā-ādi-sthāna-abhighātaḥ tad ana ukta-uttara-tvāt
300.15	vāyu-gata-vega-apekṣaḥ vāyunā tālu-ādi-saṃyogaḥ abhighātaḥ saṃyogasya
300.16	vyañjaka-tvam prāk pratiṣiddham iti/  evam ca tattvam pāṃśubhiḥ avākarin
300.17	idam āha/ kaḥ asya arthaḥ/ tattve ukte jāti^ā pratyavatiṣṭhate sā ca jātiḥ//
300.18	[2-2-19(20)]tad-anupalabdhi^aḥ anupalambhāt āvaraṇa-upapattiḥ
300.19	tad-anupalabdhi^aḥ anupalambhāt āvaraṇa-upapattiḥ/ kaḥ asya vākyasya arthaḥ
300.20	āvaraṇa-ādi-anupalabdhi^ā anekāntaḥ iti arthaḥ/ yathā āvaraṇa-ādi-anupalabdhiḥ anupalabhyamānā
300.21	api asti tathā āvaraṇam iti anekāntaḥ atha anupalabdhiḥ anupalabhyamānā na asti
301.01	tad-abhāvāt upalabdhiḥ asti/ na ca avidyamānasya upalabdhiḥ asti iti siddham
301.02	āvaraṇam/ katham punar bhavān jānīyate na āvaraṇa-anupalabdhiḥ upalabhyate
301.03	iti kim atra jñeyam pratyātma-vedanīya-tvāt pratyātmam eva ayam āvaraṇa-anupalabdhim
301.04	āvaraṇa-upalabdhim ca vedayate evam ca sati apahṛta-viṣayam uttara-vākyam
301.05	iti apahṛta-viṣayam iti na asya utthānam asti iti/  abhyanujñāna-vādena
301.06	ca ucyate//
301.07	[2-2-20(21)]anupalambhāt api anupalabdhi-sadbhāvavat na āvaraṇa-anupapattiḥ
301.08	anupalambhāt
301.09	anupalambhāt api anupalabdhi-sadbhāvavat na āvaraṇa-anupapattiḥ anupalambhāt
301.10	iti asya sūtrasya anupalabdhi^aḥ anaikāntika-tvam arthaḥ//
301.11	[2-2-21(22)]anupalambha-ātmaka-tvāt anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ
301.12	anupalambha-ātmaka-tvāt anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ/ atathā-jātīyena pratyavasthānāt
301.13	anuttaram iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ yathā-jātīyakaḥ śabdaḥ anityaḥ tathā-jātīyakam
301.14	kim cit nityam na dṛṣṭam iti/ uktam ca atra kim uktam/ ubhaya-
301.15	anta-aparicchinna-vastu-sattā-sambandhaḥ nityatā iti/ tasmāt ayathā-artha-pratyavasthānāt
301.16	yad kim cit etad/ anityaḥ śabdaḥ guṇa-tve sati asmad-ādi-indriya-
301.17	viṣaya-tvāt buddhi-vat/ avyāpakasya vyāpaka-dravya-samavāyi-tve sati
301.18	pratyakṣa-tvāt sukha-vat/ ākāśa-niṣpradeśa^tve hetuḥ niṣpradeśam ākāśam
301.19	vyāpaka-tvāt ātma-vat dravyasya sataḥ sadā amūrta-tvāt ātma-vat eva vyāpaka-
301.20	tvāt eva akṛtaka-tvam vyāpaka-tvam ca dravya-vyāpakasya sataḥ sadā amūrta-tvāt/
301.21	etena diś-kālau vyākhyātau/ etāvat svasādhana-sthiti^ā vyavasthitam
301.22	anityaḥ śabdaḥ iti//
302.01	atha śabdasya nitya-tvam pratijānānaḥ iti bhāṣyam//
302.02	vipratipatti^aḥ pramāṇa-mūla-tvāt hetu^aḥ paripraśnaḥ/ vipratipattiḥ nāma
302.03	dvayoḥ eka-viṣayā viruddha-dharma-sampratipattiḥ na ca vipratipattiḥ pramāṇa-
302.04	antaram antareṇa yuktā iti nitya-tve pramāṇam vaktavyam iti ataḥ āha/ nityaḥ
302.05	śabdaḥ asparśa-tvāt//
302.06	[2-2-22(23)]asparśa-tvāt
302.07	[2-2-23(24)]na karma-anitya-tvāt
302.08	anupalabdha-vipakṣa-ekadeśasya prayogaḥ ākāśa-vat iti  tasya vyabhicāra-
302.09	darśana-artham sūtram/ na karma-anitya-tvāt/ śeṣam asya bhāṣye//
302.10	[2-2-24(25)]na aṇu-nitya-tvāt
302.11	[2-2-25(26)]sampradānāt
302.12	ayam anyaḥ hetuḥ śabda-nitya-tve sampradānāt/ na hi kim cit nityam
302.13	sampradīyamānam iti anvayinaḥ dṛṣṭāntasya abhāvāt viruddhaḥ/  atha ayam pratijñā-
302.14	arthaḥ avatṣṭhate śabdaḥ sampradānāt ghaṭa-ādi-vat iti/ evam api nityatvam
302.15	na siddhyati//
302.16	[2-2-26(27)]tad-antarāla-anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ
302.17	[2-2-27(28)]adhyāpanāt apratiṣedhaḥ
302.18	tad-antarāla-anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ/ avatiṣṭhamānaḥ hi dātṛ-pratigrahītṛ^oḥ
302.19	antarāle upalabhyeta iti iti sūtra-artha-virodhaḥ/  na virodhaḥ anumānāt
302.20	upalabdhi^aḥ na hi pratyakṣataḥ anupalabdhasya anumānena upalabdhiḥ sattām bādhate/
303.01	kim anumānam adhyāpanam kim idam adhyāpanam nāma dīyamānasya sampradānam
303.02	prāptiḥ dātṛ-pratigrahītṛ^oḥ antarāle śabdaḥ asti adyāpanāt śara-ādi-vat iti/
303.03	ubhayoḥ pakṣayoḥ anyatarasya adhyāpanāt apratiṣedhaḥ//
303.04	[2-2-28(29)]ubhayoḥ pakṣayoḥ anyatarasya adhyāpanāt apratiṣedhaḥ
303.05	na śabdasya adhyāpanam pratiṣidhyate api tu nṛtya-upadeśa-vat anityasya
303.06	adhyāpanam iti/ yad punar etad dātṛ-pratigrahītṛ^oḥ antarāle śabdaḥ asti iti
303.07	siddha-sādhanam etad iti/ vayam api brūmaḥ dātṛ-uccāritaḥ śabdaḥ santāna-
303.08	vṛtti^ā sampradānam prāpnoti tasmāt sampradānam avasthāne na hetuḥ  ayam
303.09	tarhi hetuḥ//
303.10	[2-2-29(30)]abhyāsāt
303.11	abhyāsāt/ vīṇa-ādi-vat iti/ anitya^su eva laṅghana-pūraṇa-ādiṣu
303.12	abhyāsaḥ iti viruddhaḥ/ atha avasthānam abhyāsāt sādhyeta tathā api anavasthita^su
303.13	eva abhyāsaḥ iti anekāntaḥ abhyāsa-svarūpa-anavadhāraṇāt asiddhaḥ ca/
303.14	abhyāsaḥ hi eka-viṣayaḥ aneka-jñāna-utpādaḥ eka-ākāra-viṣayaḥ vā/ tatra ayam
303.15	abhyāsaḥ eka-viṣayaḥ eka-ākāra-viṣayaḥ vā iti sandigdha-asiddhaḥ  abhyāsa-
303.16	asiddhi^i abhyāsa-upacāra-viṣaya-tvena sādhanam tasya anaikāntika-tva-jñāpana-artham//
303.17	[2-2-30(31)]na anya-tve api abhyāsasya upacārāt
303.18	na anya-tve api abhyāsasya upacārāt/  etad sūtram anya-tva-pratiṣedha-artham//
303.19	[2-2-31(32)]anyat anyasmāt ananya-tvāt ananyat iti anyatā-abhāvaḥ
304.01	anyat anyasmāt ananya-tvāt ananyat iti anyatā-abhāvaḥ/ yad idam anyat iti
304.02	manyase idam anyasmāt ananyat vā syāt/ yadi anyat ananyat na bhavati yathā
304.03	brāhmaṇāt anyaḥ na brāhmaṇaḥ iti/ atha ananyat tathā api anyat na bhavati
304.04	ananya-tvāt/ yad ananyat tad katham anyat bhaviṣyati iti//
304.05	[2-2-32(33)]tad-abhāve na asti ananyatā tayoḥ itaretara-apekṣa-siddhi^aḥ
304.07	tad-abhāve na asti ananyatā tayoḥ itaretara-apekṣa-siddhi^aḥ/ sūtrasya arthaḥ
304.08	avirodhaḥ/ ananya-tvam abhyupagatam tad nivartate iti/ katham nivartate pratiṣedhasya
304.09	anya-śabda-viṣaya-tvāt/ anya-abhāve ananyat na asti iti na hi brāhmaṇa-
304.10	abhāve abrāhmaṇaḥ siddhyati/ ayam tarhi hetuḥ pratyabhijñānāt tad-abhāve
304.11	pratyabhijñānam na dṛṣṭam maṇi-ādiṣu asti ca pratyabhijñānam śabde tasmāt
304.12	pratyabhijñānāt avatiṣṭhate śabdaḥ iti/ kim idam pratyabhijñānam tad-pratyaya-
304.13	viṣaya-tvam tad-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam anya-tve api iti anekāntaḥ/ atha pratyabhijñānam
304.14	tad-pratyaya-viṣayasya avyabhicāraḥ na hi śabde kadā cit tad-pratyaya-viṣaya-
304.15	vyabhicāraḥ asti yaḥ punar sadṛśe tad-pratyayaḥ saḥ viśeṣa-darśanāt nivartate/ ataḥ
304.16	yā asu tad-pratyayasya śabde avyāvṛttiḥ tad pratyabhijñānam iti na tad-pratyayasya
304.17	avyāvṛttiḥ asiddhā/ ayam tāvat tad-pratyayaḥ puruṣa-antare nivartamānaḥ
304.18	dṛṣṭaḥ/ yad punar etad viśeṣa-darśanāt sadṛśe tad-bhāva-pratyayaḥ nivartate
304.19	iti manyase evam etad śabde tu kim viśeṣasya adarśanāt sadṛśe tad-pratyayaḥ
304.20	uta tad-abhāvāt eva tad-pratyayaḥ iti sandihyate/ atha pratyabhijñānam tad-bhāvāt
304.21	bhavati iti pakṣaḥ tathā api anyaḥ hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/ kim kāraṇam pratyabhijñānasya
305.01	apekṣa-viśeṣaṇa-tve na upayukta-tvāt/ yad punar go-śabdaḥ go-śabdaḥ iti
305.02	pratyayasya-avyāvṛttiḥ asti iti/ na ca go-śabdasya go-pratyaya-kartṛ-tva-vyabhicāraḥ
305.03	iti etad api sandihyate kim artham kim kāraṇa-sāmānyāt go-
305.04	śabde go-pratyayasya anivṛttiḥ āho tad-bhāvāt iti/ etena tad-pratyaya-kartṛ-
305.05	tvam vyākhyātam/ yuktam tu kāraṇa-sāmānyāt go-śabde go-śabda-pratyayasya
305.06	avyāvṛttiḥ/ katham iti go-buddhi^i go-buddhi-pratyayaḥ na vyāvartate na ca asyāḥ
305.07	go-śabda-buddhi^aḥ tad-pratyaya-kartṛ-tvam nivartate tasmāt tad-pratyaya-hetu-tve pratyabhijñānam
305.08	ahetuḥ yaḥ ca anūpalabdha-viśeṣaḥ eva nivartate tam prati tasya vastunaḥ
305.09	kim viśeṣaḥ asti na asti iti bhavantaḥ eva praṣṭavyāḥ/ yadi asti viśeṣaḥ
305.10	pratyaya-avyāvṛttiḥ ahetuḥ anekāntāt/ atha na asti ekam vastu dvi-ātmakam
305.11	iti prāptam/ virodhāt ahetuḥ pratyaya-avyāvṛttiḥ ca aśeṣa-puruṣa-viṣaya-tayā
305.12	asiddhā/ puruṣa-mātra-viṣaya-tayā anaikāntikin^ḥ iti  ayam tarhi hetuḥ//
305.13	[2-2-33(34)]vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ
305.14	vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ/ yad anityam tasya vināśa-kāraṇam upalabhyate
305.15	yathā loṣṭhasya kāraṇa-dravya-vibhāgaḥ na tu śabdasya tasmāt nityaḥ
305.16	śabdaḥ iti//
305.17	[2-2-34(35)]aśravaṇa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ satata-śravaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ
305.18	aśravaṇa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ satata-śravaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
305.19	[2-2-35(36)]upalabhyamāne ca anupalabdhi^aḥ asattvāt anapadeśaḥ
305.20	upalabhyamāne ca anupalabdhi^aḥ asattvāt anapadeśaḥ/ anumānena śabdasya
306.01	vināśa-kāraṇam upalabhyate/ yaḥ ca anumānena ūpalbhyate na tad na asti/ kim
306.02	anumānam santāna-upapattiḥ iti/ tasmāt anapadeśaḥ ayam vināśa-kāraṇa-
306.03	anupalabdhi^aḥ/ yasmāt viṣāṇin^ḥ tasmāt aśvaḥ iti/ kim punar atra asat
306.04	viṣāṇam āhosvit viṣāṇa-asambandhaḥ iti/ ubhayam iti āha yadā viṣāṇinam
306.05	artham aśva-tvena sādhayati tadā viṣāṇam asti eva na viṣāṇam pratiṣidhyate
306.06	aśva-viṣāṇa-sambandha-abhāvāt anumānam asat iti yuktam uktam asat-tvāt anapedeśaḥ
306.07	iti/ karma-tvāt iti cet na āśraya-anitya-tvāt/ atha manuṣe
306.08	nityasya api upalabhyamānasya atyantam agrahaṇam dṛṣṭam/ yathā karmatvasya iti/
306.09	tad ca na āśraya-anitya-tvāt āśrayasya nitya-tvāt aśravaṇa-kāraṇa-anupapattiḥ
306.10	iti brūmaḥ/ karmatvasya punar agrahaṇa-kāraṇam āśraya-anitya-tvam asti/
306.11	tasmāt aprasaṅgaḥ iti/ ghaṇṭāyām abhihanyamānāyām tāraḥ tārataraḥ mandaḥ
306.12	mandataraḥ iti śruti-bhedāt nānā-śabda-santānaḥ hi avicchedena śrūyate tatra idam
306.13	cintyate/ śabdasya vyajyamānasya yad tad-vyakti-kāraṇam tad ghaṇṭā-stham
306.14	āhosvit anya-vṛtti iti/ yadi ghaṇṭā-stham kim avasthitam uta santāna-vṛtti
306.15	iti anya-vṛtti-tve api kim avasthitam uta santāna-vṛtti iti/ yadi ghaṇṭā-
306.16	stham avasthitam ca tadā śruti-bhedaḥ na prāpnoti/ atha ghaṇṭā-stham santāna-
306.17	vṛtti yugapat aneka-śabda-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ yāvān ghaṇṭā-sthena avasthitena
306.18	santāna-vṛttinā vā abhivyaktaḥ tāvān eva śabdaḥ ekasmin kāle upalabhyeta
306.19	ghaṇṭā-stham ca abhivyakti-kāraṇam katham anyatra vartamānam śabdam vyanakti iti vācyam
306.20	atha anya-āgatam avasthitam santāna-vṛtti vā abhivyakti-kāraṇam manuṣe
306.21	tadā ekasyām ghaṇṭāyām abhihatāyā śabdān vyanakti na pratyāsanna-deśa-vṛttiṣu
306.22	ghaṇṭā-antara^su iti niyama-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ asati śabda-bhede śrutīnām
307.01	bhedaḥ upapādayitavyaḥ tāraḥ mandaḥ iti nādaḥ abhidhīyate iti cet na
307.02	śabda-sāmānādhikaraṇya-grahaṇāt śabda-samānādhikaraṇaḥ ayam tāra-manda-
307.03	śabdaḥ pravartate na nāda-samānādhikaraṇaḥ tasmāt śabdaḥ abhidhīyate iti/
307.04	na ca asati śabda-viṣaya-tve śabda-sāmānādhikaraṇyam yuktam nīla-utpala-vat
307.05	iti/ vyāmoha-pratyayaḥ saḥ iti cet na nimitta-anabhidhānāt/ atha
307.06	manyase tāraḥ mandaḥ iti śabda-samānādhikaraṇaḥ vyāmoha-pratyayaḥ
307.07	bhavati/ yathā dīrghaḥ śabdaḥ mahān śabdaḥ iti/ na viśeṣa-hetu-abhāvāt
307.08	vyāmoha-pratyayaḥ iti na hetuḥ asti yadi ca ayam vyāmoha-pratyayaḥ
307.09	bhavati vyāmoha-pratyaya-nimittam vācyam/ yathā dīrgha-śabdaḥ iti
307.10	avirala-santāna-śruti^i śabde dīrgha-pratyayaḥ bhavati yāni khalu dīrghāṇi
307.11	dravyāṇi teṣām avayava-upacye sati grahaṇa-santati-viṣaya-tvam tad-sāmānyāt
307.12	śabde dīrgha-pratyayaḥ na tu nitya-śabda-vādinaḥ vyāmoha-pratyaya-bījam asti
307.13	iti ayuktam etad/ tulyam etad iti cet na tad-nimittasya kadā cit bhāvāt/
307.14	atha manyase yasya nityaḥ santānaḥ tena api ghaṇṭā-stham avasthitam santāna-
307.15	vṛtti ca anya-stham avasthitam santāna-vṛtti ca utpatti-kāraṇam vācyam
307.16	ghaṇṭā-stham utpatti-kāraṇam kadā cit śabdān utpādayate kadā cit na iti nitya-
307.17	vat-prasaṅgaḥ na prasaṅgaḥ kadā cit bhāvāt śabdānām utpattk-kāraṇam
307.18	ghaṇṭā-vṛtti tad kadā cit bhavati kadā cit bhāve sati santāna-vṛtti
307.19	tasmāt nimitta-bheda-anuvidhāyinaḥ śabdāḥ kadā cit bhavanti santāna-vṛtti-
307.20	tvāt mandatara-mandatama-ādi-bhinna-rūpa-anuvidhāyinam śabdam utpādayanti iti/
307.21	tad ca kāraṇam saṃskāraḥ iti/ kutaḥ tad-utpattiḥ pāṇi-saṃśleṣam apekṣamāṇāt
307.22	karmaṇaḥ pāṇi-ghaṇṭā-saṃśleṣāt pāṇi-gata-vega-apekṣāt ghaṇṭāyām karman
308.01	tad-karman pāṇi-abhighātam apekṣamāṇam vibhāga-sama-kālam saṃskāram karoti/
308.02	sā ca anantya-adhyātmikam vāyum upagṛhṇāti sā ca vāyunā abhihatā punar
308.03	karman karoti tataḥ karmaṇaḥ saṃskāraḥ saṃskārena punar karman punar vāyu-
308.04	upagrahaḥ iti evam ādi-nyāyena saṃskāraḥ utpadyate iti tatra antyasya atimāndyāt
308.05	ghaṇṭāyām mahābhūta-kṣobha-śakti^aḥ abhāvaḥ tataḥ vāyu-upagraha-ucchedaḥ tataḥ
308.06	saṃskāra-kṣayaḥ iti/  na saṃskāraḥ asti anupalabdhi^aḥ iti cet atha manyase
308.07	na eva saṃskāraḥ asti anupalabdhi^aḥ tad ca asat//
308.08	[2-2-36(37)]pāṇi-nimitta-praśeṣāt śabda-abhāve na anupalabdhiḥ
308.09	pāṇi-nimitta-praśeṣāt śabda-abhāve na anupalabdhiḥ/ ukta-artham sūtram/
308.10	yadi saṃskāram śabda-utpatti-nimittam na pratipadyase pāṇi-saṃśleṣāt śabde
308.11	santāna-ucchedaḥ na prāpnoti/ atha ghaṇṭā-sthaḥ saṃskāraḥ pāṇi-saṃśleṣāt nivartate
308.12	tathā api katham śabda-santāna-ucchedaḥ iti/ na brūmaḥ ghaṇṭā-saṃśleṣaḥ
308.13	śabdān ucchinatti api tu pāṇi-ghaṇṭā-saṃśleṣāt tad-vṛtti-saṃskāraḥ sparśavat-
308.14	dravya-saṃyoga-virodhi-tvāt nivartate nivṛtte kāraṇa-abhāvāt kārya-abhāvaḥ
308.15	iti santāna-ucchedaḥ//
308.16	[2-2-37(38)]vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca avasthāne tad-nitya-tva-prasaṅgaḥ
308.18	vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca avasthāne tad-nitya-tva-prasaṅgaḥ/ yad idam
308.19	ucyate vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ nityaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ yadi yasya vināśa-
308.20	kāraṇam na upalabhyate tad nityam yāni imāni śabda-grahaṇāni teṣām na
309.01	bhavatā vināśa-kāraṇam upapādyate anupapādanāt avasthanāt nityatvam iti/
309.02	atha nupalabdha-vināśa-kāraṇāni api śabda-grahaṇāni anityāni śabde
309.03	api evam iti anekāntaḥ/ atha anumānataḥ śabda-grahaṇa-vināśa-kāraṇāni
309.04	gamyante tad-pramāṇam śabdaḥ iti na kim cit bādhitam bhavati/
309.05	vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt ayuktam iti cet atha manyase vyadhikaraṇaḥ śabdaḥ katham
309.06	anya-vṛttinā saṃśleṣena nivartyate/ atha vyadhikaraṇaḥ api nivartyate
309.07	sarva-ghaṇṭā-śabda-uccheda-prasaṅgaḥ tasmāt pāṇi-ghaṇṭā-saṃśleṣa-samānādhikaraṇaḥ
309.08	śabdaḥ//
309.09	[2-2-38(39)]asparśa-tvāt apratiṣedhaḥ
309.10	asparśa-tvāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ asparśa-dravya-āśrayaḥ śabdaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/
309.11	katham anyatara vartamānasya pāṇi-ghaṇṭā-saṃśleṣaḥ nivartakaḥ iti/ na ukta-uttara-
309.12	tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etad kāraṇa-abhāvāt anya-śabda-anutpādaḥ/ yaḥ tu utpannaḥ
309.13	śabdaḥ saḥ mandatara-tama-ādi-nyāyena antyasya atimāndyāt yena kena cit virodhi-
309.14	tvāt kṣīyate yaḥ ayam saṃskāraḥ śabda-kāraṇa-tvena samadhigataḥ saḥ kim
309.15	ekaḥ anekaḥ vā iti sandihyate/ anekaḥ saṃskāraḥ iti tattvam śabda-bhedāt
309.16	kāraṇa-bhede sati kārya-bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ yasya ekaḥ saṃskāraḥ tasya iṣu^aḥ pātaḥ
309.17	prāpnoti agatvā eva yāvat gantavyam iti/ atha aprativadhyamāne saṃskāre
309.18	dravyam gacchati na kadā cit pātaḥ prāpnoti tasmāt anekaḥ saṃskāraḥ//
309.19	[2-2-39(40)]vibhakti-antara-upapatti^aḥ ca samāse
309.20	vibhakti-antara-upapatti^aḥ ca samāse/ na ayam śabdaḥ gandha-ādibhiḥ sanniviṣṭaḥ
310.01	vyajyante vibhakti-antara-upapatti^aḥ vibhāgaḥ ca vibhāga-anataram ca iti vibhakti-
310.02	antaram pratidravya-vidharmāṇaḥ ca śabdāḥ sarūpāḥ ca eka-dravyāḥ śrūyanate/
310.03	samāsaḥ samudāyaḥ etad ca sparśavat-dravya-vṛttiṣu na prāpnoti gandha-ādi-vat
310.04	yathā pratidravyam ekaḥ gandhaḥ evam pratidravyam ekaḥ śabdaḥ iti/ santāna-
310.05	utpatti^aḥ ca iti ca arthaḥ/ yadi ayam gandha-ādibhiḥ sanniviṣṭaḥ syāt na anyatra
310.06	vartamānaḥ śrotraḥ upalabhyeta tasmāt santāna-vṛtti-tvāt ākāśa-āśrayaḥ
310.07	śabdaḥ iti//
310.08	[2-2-40(41)]vikāra-ādeśa-upadeśāt saṃśayaḥ
310.09	vikāra-ādeśa-upadeśāt saṃśayaḥ iti/ saṃsaya-kāraṇa-jñāpana-artham
310.10	sūtram//
310.11	[2-2-41(42)]prakṛti-vivṛddhi^aḥ
310.12	prakṛti-vivṛddhi^i vikāra-vivṛddhi^aḥ iti sūtram/ prakṛti-anuvidhānāt iti
310.13	sūtra-arthaḥ/ vikāre prakṛti-anuvidhānam dṛṣṭam iti//
310.14	[2-2-42(43)]nyūna-sama-adhika-upalabdhi^aḥ vikārāṇām ahetuḥ
310.15	nyūna-sama-adhika-upalabdhi^aḥ vikārāṇām ahetuḥ iti sādhana-pakṣe dṛṣṭānta-
310.16	mātrāt ahetuḥ//   [2-2-43]dvi-vidhasya api hetu^aḥ abhāvāt asādhanam dṛṣṭāntaḥ
310.17	[2-2-44]na atulya-prakṛtīnām vikāra-vikalpāt
310.18	dūṣaṇa-pakṣe na atulya-prakṛtīnām vikāra-vikalpāt iti asambaddham/
311.01	atulyāyāḥ prakṛti^aḥ vikalpāt te vikārāḥ hrasva-dīrgha-anuvidhānam
311.02	tu e-kāre na asti iti//
311.03	[2-2-45]dravya-vikāra-vaiṣamya-vat varṇa-vikāra-vikalpaḥ
311.04	dravya-vikāra-vaiṣamya-vat varṇa-vikāra-vikalpaḥ/ yathā dravya-bhāvena tulyāyāḥ
311.05	prakṛti^aḥ vikāraḥ vikalpyate/ evam varṇa-bhāvena tulyāyāḥ prakṛti^aḥ vikāra-
311.06	vikalpaḥ syāt iti//
311.07	[2-2-46]na vikāra-dharma-anupapatti^aḥ
311.08	[2-2-47]vikāra-prāptānām apunarāpatti^aḥ
311.09	vikāra-prāptānām apunarāpatti^aḥ iti/ yadi vikriyante varṇāḥ punarāpattiḥ
311.10	na prāpnoti//
311.11	[2-2-48]suvarṇa-ādīnām punarāpatti^aḥ ahetuḥ
311.12	suvarṇa-ādīnām punarāpatti^aḥ ahetuḥ/ vikāra-prāptānām punarāpattiḥ
311.13	darśanāt anekāntaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
311.14	[2-2-49]na tad-vikārāṇām suvarṇa-bhāva-avyatirekāt
311.15	na tad-vikārāṇām suvarṇa-bhāva-avyatirekāt/ sarva-avastham suvarṇa-darśanam
311.16	na punar i-kāra-ya-kārayoḥ dharmayoḥ kaḥ cit varṇa-ātman^ḥ vyavasthitaḥ bhavati yaḥ
311.17	itvam hitvā yatvam āpadyate iti//
312.01	[(2-2-50)]varṇatva-avyatirekāt varṇa-vikārāṇām apratiṣedhaḥ
312.02	varṇatva-avyatirekāt varṇa-vikārāṇām apratiṣedhaḥ/ varṇa-vikārāḥ api
312.03	na varṇatvam vyabhicaranti iti/ asti varṇatvam sāmānyam iti/
312.04	[(2-2-51)]sāmānyavataḥ dharma-yogaḥ n apunar sāmānyasya
312.05	sāmānyavataḥ dharma-yogaḥ n apunar sāmānyasya iti parihāraḥ/  itaḥ ca
312.06	varṇa-vikāra-anupapattiḥ//
312.07	[2-2-50(52)]nitya-tve avikārāt anitya-tve ca anavasthānāt
312.08	nitya-tve avikārāt anitya-tve ca anavasthānāt/ nityāḥ varṇāḥ
312.09	iti na yuktaḥ vikāraḥ vyāghātāt anityāḥ varṇāḥ iti na yuktaḥ vikāraḥ
312.10	anavasthitānām vikāra-adarśanāt//
312.11	[2-2-51(53)]nityānām api atīndriya-tvāt tad-dharma-vikalpāt ca varṇa-vikārāṇām
312.12	apratiṣedhaḥ
312.13	nityānām api atīndriya-tva-darśanāt tad-dharma-vikalpāt ca varṇa-vikārāṇām
312.14	apratiṣedhah iti vikalpa-samā jātiḥ//
312.15	[2-2-52(54)]anavasthāyi-tve ca varṇa-upalabdhi-vat tad-vikāra-upapattiḥ
312.17	anavasthāyi-tve ca varṇa-upalabdhi-vat tad-vikāra-upapattiḥ iti sādharmya-samā
312.18	jātiḥ  ubhayoḥ api uttaram//
312.19	[2-2-53(55)]vikāra-dharmi-tve nityatva-abhāvāt kāla-antare vikāra-
312.20	upapatti^aḥ ca apratiṣedhaḥ
313.01	vikāra-dharmi-tve nityatva-abhāvāt kāla-antare vikāra-upapatti^aḥ ca
313.02	apratiṣedhaḥ/ nityāḥ vikriyante iti vyāghātaḥ doṣaḥ/ upalabhyamānasya
313.03	ca i-kārasya yatva-anupapatti^aḥ varṇa-upalabdhi-vat iti asambaddham/  itaḥ ca varṇa-
313.04	vikāra-anupapattiḥ//
313.05	[2-2-54(56)]prakṛti-aniyamāt varṇa-vikārāṇām
313.06	prakṛti-aniyamāt/ vikāre prakṛtīnām niyamaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti/
313.07	i-kāra-ya-kārayoḥ tu niyamaḥ tasmāt aniyamāt na vikriyante iti//
313.08	[2-2-55(57)]aniyame niyamāt na aniyamaḥ
313.09	aniyame niyamāt na aniyamaḥ/ sūtra-arthaḥ aniyamaḥ na asti iti//
313.10	[2-2-56(58)]niyama-aniyama-virodhāt aniyame niyamāt ca apratiṣedhaḥ
313.12	niyama-aniyama-virodhāt aniyame niyamāt ca apratiṣedhaḥ/ anujñāta-
313.13	pratiṣiddhayoḥ eka-tva-anupapatti^aḥ iti virodhaḥ sūtra-arthaḥ tasmāt anupalabhyamāna-
313.14	pṛthak-dharmi-viśeṣāṇām ādeśaḥ iti/ yadi ete varṇāḥ vikriyeran tataḥ
313.15	pṛthak-vikāra-lakṣaṇa^bhyaḥ dhramin^ḥ upalabhyeta na tu upalabhyate tasmāt na vikriyante
313.16	iti/  atha vikriyante evam vikāraḥ bhavati//
313.17	[2-2-57(59)]guṇa-antara-āpatti-upamarda-hrāsa-vṛddhi-leśa-śleṣa^bhyaḥ tu vikāra-
313.18	upapatti^aḥ varṇa-vikāraḥ
313.19	guṇa-antara-āpatti-upamarda-hrāsa-vṛddhi-leśa-śleṣa^bhyaḥ tu vikāra-upapatti^aḥ santi
313.20	varṇa-vikārāḥ/ sūtra-arthaḥ guṇa-antara-āpatti-ādi^aḥ ādeśaḥ iti/ ayam ca
314.01	vikāra-ādeśa-upadeśa-vikāraḥ bhāṣya-kārena eva samyac nirūpitaḥ iti
314.02	sūtra-artha-mātram nirūpitam iti//
314.03	[2-2-58(60)]te vibhakti-antāḥ padam
314.04	te vibhakti-antāḥ padam/ te varṇāḥ yathā-darśanam vikṛtāḥ vibhakti-antāḥ
314.05	pada-saṃjñakāḥ bhavanti/ artha-pratyayaḥ tarhi na prāpnoti/ na na prāpnoti/
314.06	antya-varṇa-pratyayāt/ pūrva-varṇa-pratisandhāna-pratyaya-apekṣāt artha-pratyayaḥ iti/
314.07	vibhaktiḥ dvayī nāmikī ca ākhyātikī ca/ nāmikī su-ādyaḥ
314.08	ākhyātikī tip-ādyaḥ / tayā viśiṣyamāṇam padam dvedhā bhavati/
314.09	nāma ca ākhyātam ca abhidheyasya kriyā-antara-yogāt/ viśiṣyamāṇa-
314.10	rūpaḥ sabdaḥ nāma yathā brāhmaṇaḥ iti/ kriyā-kāraka-samudāyaḥ
314.11	kāraka-saṃkhyā-viśiṣṭaḥ kriyā-kāla-yoga-abhidhāyi-kriyā-pradhānam ākhyātam
314.12	pacati iti yathā/ yadi dvayī vibhaktiḥ upasarga-nipātāḥ tarhi na pada-saṃjñakāḥ
314.13	/ na na asti antarbhāvāt upasarga-nipātāḥ nāmnā saṃgṛhītāḥ/
314.14	yasmāt āha avyayāt lopaḥ iti te sup-anta-tvāt tena eva saṃgṛhītāḥ iti/
314.15	padena vyavahāraḥ iti padārthaḥ cintyate nāma-padam ca adhikṛtya vyavahāraḥ
314.16	iti nāma-padam cintyate/ nāmnaḥ ca adhikāraḥ vyāpaka-tvāt yasmāt nāmnā
314.17	sarvaḥ eva padārthaḥ vyāpyate tasmāt go^ḥ iti asya padasya arthaḥ cintyate na padāt
314.18	artha-adhigatiḥ viśeṣe anavasthānāt/ na hi pade uccarite kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ
314.19	gamyate tasmāt padam avācakam/ na padasya viśeṣa-aviṣaya-tvāt/ kaḥ evam
314.20	āha padena viśeṣaḥ gamyate api tu sāmānya-vartti padam viśeṣa-vartti
315.01	vākyam na ca sāmānya-abhidhānāt anarthakam bhavitum arhati iti/ na sāmānyasya
315.02	vyavahāra-viśeṣa-aviṣaya-tvāt/ sarvaḥ upadeṣṭṛ^ḥ viśeṣa-pratipatti-artham
315.03	padam uccārayati/ na ca sāmānye paiṣaḥ na sampratipattiḥ tasmāt padam
315.04	avācakam eva iti/ na sāmānya-viṣaya-tve sati viśeṣa-śruti^aḥ niyāmaka-tvāt/
315.05	yadi padam avācakam syāt na ca pada-śravaṇāt sāmānyam gamyeta/ yad avācakam
315.06	na tataḥ sāmānyam viśeṣaḥ vā gamyate yathā arthāt gamyate ca padāt tad
315.07	sāmānyam sāmānya-śruti^aḥ viśeṣa-śrutiḥ niyāmikā bhavati/ yathā go^ḥ iti
315.08	sāmānya-adhigati^i tiṣṭḥati gacchati iti viśeṣa-śruti^ā gamyate iti/
315.09	katham punar iyam viśeṣa-śrutiḥ tiṣṭḥati gacchati iti/ na eva iyam kevalāt viśeṣa-
315.10	śrutiḥ kim tu pūrva-pada-apekṣayā viśeṣa-pratyaya-hetu-tvāt viśeṣa-śrutiḥ iti ucyate/
315.11	bhavatu vā padāt eva ubhayam etasmāt eva vā go-śabdāt ubhayam gamyate
315.12	kriyā ca tad-sādhanam ca pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvasya aniyamena yadā ayam go-śabdaḥ
315.13	gotva-nimittaḥ gotva-yogāt go^ḥ iti tadā go-śabdāt pradhānam sādhanam
315.14	go^ḥ gamyate/ kriyā tu aṅga-bhūtā viśeṣaka-tvāt/ yadā punar kriyā-pradhānam
315.15	padam tadā go^aḥ bhāvaḥ gotvam iti/ tadā go^ḥ aṅga-bhūtaḥ viśeṣaka-tvāt gamyate
315.16	pradhānam gotvam iti/ tad evam pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvasya aniyamena gotvam
315.17	iti ca pade go^ḥ iti ca ubhayam gamyate iti//
315.18	tatra-pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvam āśritya tad-arthe [2-2-59]vyakti-ākṛti-jāti-sannidhi^i
315.19	upacārāt saṃśayaḥ/ yasmāt ayam go-śabdaḥ vyakti-ākṛti-jātiṣu avinābhāvena
315.20	vartamānāt prayujyate/ avinābhāva-vṛttiḥ ca sannidhiḥ/
315.21	ataḥ na jñāyate kim vyaktiḥ padārthaḥ uta ākṛtiḥ uta jātiḥ iti//
316.01	[2-2-60(61)]yāśabda-samūha-tyāga-parigraha-saṃkhyā-vṛddhi-apacaya-varṇa-samāsa-
316.02	anubandhānām vyakti^i upacārāt vyaktiḥ
316.03	śabdasya prayoga-sāmarthyāt padārtha-avadhāraṇam iti prayoga-pradarśana-artham
316.04	yāśabda-ādi-sūtram/ na amī yāśabda-ādyaḥ  iti jāti^i prayujyante
316.05	kasmāt abhedāt ekā jātiḥ tasyāḥ viśeṣaṇam anarthakam/ yā go^ḥ tiṣṭḥati iti
316.06	yā go^ḥ niṣaṇṇā iti/ evam śeṣa^su/ ākṛti^aḥ api abhidhāyakam etad na bhavati
316.07	niṣkriya-tvāt ākṛti^aḥ na hi niṣkriyā gacchati tiṣṭḥati bhavati ca iti/
316.08	evam sarvatra/ yasya ca anena viśeṣena tiṣṭhati-ādinā yogaḥ tam go-śabdaḥ
316.09	abhidhātum arhati iti/ kutaḥ viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāvasya eka-viṣaya-tvāt/ yadi
316.10	go-śabdasya tiṣṭhati-śabdasya ca ekam abhidheyam bhavati tataḥ go-śabdena
316.11	dharmi-antarāṇi nirākriyante tiṣṭhati-śabdena va kriyā-antarāṇi iti viśeṣaṇa-
316.12	viśeṣya-bhāva-āpannayoḥ tiṣṭḥati-go-śabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam yujyate
316.13	anyathā na iti dravyam vyaktiḥ iti hi na artha-antaram tiṣṭhati-śabda-sahitena
316.14	go-śabdena vyaktiḥ abhidhīyate iti  tad-pratiṣedha-artham//
316.15	[2-2-61(62)]na tad-anavasthānāt
316.16	na tad-anavasthānāt iti sūtram/ na anena go-śabdena vyakti-mātram
316.17	śuddham ucyate/ yadi ayam vyakti-mātra-abhidhāyakaḥ abhaviṣyat tena yasyām
316.18	kasyām cit vyakti^i pratyayaḥ abhaviṣyat iti sūtra-arthaḥ/  yadi vyaktiḥ na
316.19	padārthaḥ katham yā go^ḥ tiṣṭhati iti ādi-prayogaḥ/ nimittāt atad-bhāve api
317.01	tad-upacāraḥ/ kva punar nimittāt atad-bhāve api tad-upacāraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti
317.02	tasya upapādana-artham sahacaraṇa-ādi-sūtram//
317.03	[2-2-62(63)]sahacaraṇa-sthāna-tādarthya-vṛtta-māna-dhāraṇa-sāmīpya-yoga-sādhanādhipatya^bhyaḥ
317.04	brāhmaṇa-mañca-kaṭa-rāja-saktu-candana-gaṅgā-śāṭakānna-
317.05	puruṣa^su atad-bhāve api tad-upacāraḥ
317.06	atad-bhāve api tad-upacāraḥ iti/ atad-śabdasya tad-śabdena abhidhānam
317.07	upacāraḥ yathā yaṣṭikā-śabdena dravya-viśeṣaḥ abhidhīyate iti yaṣṭikā-śabdāt
317.08	tu punar sāhacaryāt brāhmaṇa-viśeṣaḥ abhidhīyate/ yathā yaṣṭikāḥ
317.09	praveśaya iti/ evam sarvatra/ kim punar atra upacāra-bījam yaṣṭikāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ
317.10	iti/ na hi upacāra-bījam antareṇa upacāraḥ labhyate/ nanu uktam sāhacaryam iti
317.11	sāhacaryam nāma yaṣṭikayā nitya-sambandhaḥ/ yaṣṭikā-sambandhāt ca yaṣṭikāvān
317.12	iti syāt na yaṣṭikā iti/ yaṣṭikāvān iti ca ayam śabdaḥ mukhyaḥ
317.13	eva iti/ tasmāt upacāra-bījam anyad vaktavyam iti/ yaṣṭikāyām tāvat ayam
317.14	yaṣṭikā-śabdaḥ jāti-nimittaḥ yaṣṭikātvam jātiḥ sā yaṣṭikāyām
317.15	vartate tayā yaṣṭikātva-yuktayā yaṣṭikayā brāhmaṇasya yogaḥ sāhacaryāt
317.16	saṃyukta-samavetām jātim brāhmaṇe adhyāropya brāhmaṇam yaṣṭikā iti āha/ evam
317.17	śeṣāṇi upacāra-bījāni svayam utprekṣaṇīyāni iti//
317.18	[2-2-63(64)]ākṛtiḥ tad-apekṣa-tvāt sattva-vyavasthāna-siddhi^aḥ
317.19	yadi tarhi go^ḥ iti padasya na vyaktiḥ arthaḥ astu tarhi ākṛtiḥ
317.20	tad-apekṣa-tvāt sattva-vyavasthāna-siddhi^aḥ/ ākṛtiḥ padārthaḥ kasmāt
318.01	tad-apekṣa-tvāt sattva-vyavasthānāsya go^ḥ aśvaḥ puruṣaḥ iti evam ādi sattva-vyavasthānam
318.02	ākṛtiḥ-apekṣam siddhyati yad-apekṣā ca sattva-vyavasthāna-siddhiḥ saḥ go-
318.03	śabdasya viṣayaḥ iti/ atra api tad eva upasthitam na tad-anavasthānāt iti/
318.04	yasya ca jāti^ā yogaḥ saḥ go-śabdasya viṣayaḥ na ākṛtiḥ jāti-ayogāt
318.05	kasya punar jāti^ā yogaḥ niyata-avayava-vyūhasya avayavinaḥ niyataḥ avayava-vyūhaḥ
318.06	yasya bhavati saḥ niyata-avayava-vyūhaḥ anya-padarthena bahuvrīhiṇā avayavin^ḥ
318.07	abhidhīyate iti//
318.08	[2-2-64(65)]vyakti-ākṛti-yukte api aprasaṅgāt prokṣaṇa-ādīnām mṛd-gavake
318.09	jātiḥ
318.10	astu tarhi jātiḥ padārthaḥ vyakti-ākṛti-yukte api aprasaṅgāt prokṣaṇa-
318.11	ādīnām mṛd-gavake jātiḥ/ yasmāt mṛd-gavake vyakti-ākṛtī staḥ na ca
318.12	tatra prokṣaṇa-ādīni prayujyante/ na hi gām ānaya gām dehī iti mṛd-gavake
318.13	praiṣa-sampratipattī bhavataḥ/ yad-abhāvāt tatra asampratyayaḥ saḥ go-śabdasya arthaḥ
318.14	kasya abhāvāt tatra asampratyayaḥ jāti^aḥ/ atha mṛd-gavake kasmāt gotvam na jātiḥ
318.15	yasmāt mṛd-gavakaḥ go^ḥ na bhavati yaḥ ca go^ḥ na bhavati na tatra gotvam vartate
318.16	yathā aśvaḥ iti/ atha mṛd-gavakaḥ kasmāt go^ḥ na bhavati iti ataḥ eva na bhavati
318.17	yasmāt mṛd-gavakaḥ iti/ na ca ayam yuktaḥ praśnaḥ mṛd-gavakaḥ kasmāt go^ḥ na bhavati
318.18	iti kim kāraṇam pratipatti-bādhita-tvāt bhavāna pi enam artham pratipadyate
318.19	na ayam go^ḥ iti/ pratipatti^ā ca praśnaḥ bādhyate iti/ atha mṛd-gavake
318.20	go-śabdaḥ kutaḥ ākṛti-sāmānyāt citra-ādi-vat iti/ sarvagatā iti cet/
319.01	atha manyase sarva-gatā jātiḥ sā utpadyamānena yathā gavā sambaddhyate
319.02	tathā mṛd-gavakena api iti asti mṛd-gavake gotvam na anabhyupagamāt kena
319.03	sarva-gata-tvam jāti^aḥ abhyupagamyate api tu sva-viṣaye sarvatra vartate
319.04	iti sarva-gatā iti ucyate/ kaḥ punar gotvasya svaḥ viṣayaḥ/ yatra gotvam
319.05	vartate kva punar gotvam vartate yatra gotva-nimittaḥ anuvṛtta-pratyayaḥ
319.06	bhavati/ kva punar anuvṛtta-pratyayam gotvam karoti yad tasya sādhanam kaḥ
319.07	punar nitye go^aḥ sādhana-arthaḥ yad tena vyajyate/ na hi kakuda-ādimat
319.08	artha-vyatirekena gotvasya vyaktiḥ asti/ na hi piṇḍa^bhyaḥ artha-antaram
319.09	gotvam piṇḍa-antarāla^su adarśanāt iti ke cit/ na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ
319.10	piṇḍa-antarāla^su adarśanāt iti bruvāṇaḥ piṇḍa-antarālam paryanuyojyaḥ/
319.11	kim idam piṇḍa-antarālam kim ākāśam āho abhāvaḥ uta dravya-antaram/
319.12	yadi ākāśam na tatra gotvam na hi ākāśam go^ḥ iti/ etena abhāvaḥ
319.13	dravya-antaram ca vyākhyātam/ viśeṣa-pratyayānām anākasmika-tvāt ayam
319.14	ca piṇḍa-pratyaya-vyatireka-bhāj pratyayaḥ upajāyamānaḥ nimitta-antarāt bhavati
319.15	dṛṣṭā khalu piṇḍa-pratyaya-vyatireka-bhājām pratyayānām nimitta-antarāt
319.16	utpattiḥ/ yathā vastra-carma-kambala^su nīla-pratyayaḥ iti/ ākṛti-sāmānyāt
319.17	iti cet/ atha manyase satyam ayam astu piṇḍa-vyatireka-nimittāt
319.18	anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ bhavati/ na punar asya jātiḥ nimittam api tu yad śiras-
319.19	pāṇi-pāda-ākṛti-sāmānyam tasmāt anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ iti/ na piṇḍavat-
319.20	asādhāraṇa-tvāt ākṛti^aḥ yathā eva piṇḍāḥ piṇḍa-antara-vṛttyaḥ  na bhavanti
319.21	tathā ākṛtiḥ api eka-piṇḍa-vartti-tvāt piṇḍa-antara-varttinī na bhavati/
320.01	ākṛti^aḥ piṇḍa-antara-vartti-tvam abhyupagamyate na asti nāmni vivādaḥ na arthaḥ iti/ atha
320.02	piṇḍa-vyatiriktam sāmānyam na abhyupagamyate tathā api eka-sāmānya-nirākaraṇāt
320.03	samāna-pratyaya-bījam anyat vaktavyam syāt/ na hi asati sāmānye samāna-pratyayam
320.04	paśyāmaḥ iti/ asati api sāmānye dṛṣṭaḥ sāmānya-pratyayaḥ iti cet
320.05	atha manyase yathā sāmānya^su asati sāmānya-anatare anuvṛtta-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
320.06	gotva-ādiṣu idam sāmānyam idam sāmānyam iti/ tasmāt anaikāntika-tvāt
320.07	anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ asādhanam artha-antara-nimitta-pratipādanaḥ iti/ na anabhyupagamāt/
320.08	na mayā gotva-ghaṭatva-aśvatva^su nirnimittaḥ sāmānya-pratyayaḥ
320.09	abhyupagamyate tasmāt anuttaram etad/ sūtra-vyāghātaḥ iti cet/ yadi manyase
320.10	sāmānya^su sāmānyam asti iti nanu sūtra-vyāghātaḥ sāmānya-viśeṣa^su
320.11	sāmānya-viśeṣa-abhāvāt tataḥ eva jñānam iti/ na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt
320.12	ayam sūtra-arthaḥ yathā dravyam iti pratyayaḥ dravyatva-viśeṣaṇāt dravye
320.13	bhavati na punar sāmānya-viśeṣa^su evam iti/ na punar ayam sūtra-arthaḥ
320.14	nirnimittaḥ anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ iti/ kim nimittam iti cet atha manyase
320.15	gotva-ādiṣu anuvṛtti-pratyayasya kim nimittam yataḥ sāmānyam iti
320.16	bhavati kutaḥ sāmānyam iti bhavati aneka-artha-samavāyāt/ yathā
320.17	gotvam aneka-artha-samavāyin tathā aśvatva-ādi iti aneka-arthaiḥ tad-samavāyasya samāna-
320.18	tvāt gotva-ādiṣu anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ iti/ pācaka-ādi-śabda-vat iti cet
320.19	atha manyase yathā pācaka-ādi-śabdāḥ anuvṛtti-pratyayāḥ ca bhavanti na ca
320.20	pācakatvam nāma sāmānya-viśeṣaḥ asti tathā go-ādiṣu anuvṛtta-pratyayaḥ iti
320.21	na hetu-artha-aparijñānāt viśeṣa-pratyayānām ākasmika-tvāt iti asya hetu^aḥ
321.01	piṇḍa-pratyaya-vyatiriktasya pratyayasya nimitta-antarāt utpādaḥ iti ayam arthaḥ
321.02	na punar sarvaḥ anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ sāmānyāt eva bhavati iti/ evam sati
321.03	pacana-kriyāyām yad pradhānam sādhanam tad pācaka-śabdena ucyate/ tad ca
321.04	prādhānyam pācaka-antare api asti iti na doṣaḥ/ yadi aneka-anuvṛtti gotvam
321.05	tad kim pratipiṇḍam parisamāpti^ā vartate atha eka-deśena iti/ kim ca ataḥ
321.06	yadi pratipiṇḍam parisamāpti^ā vartate piṇḍa-vat-asādhāraṇa-tvāt na sāmānyam
321.07	bhavitum arhati atha ekadeśena vartate tathā api na ekam anekatra vartate kim
321.08	tu enakam anekatra iti tasmāt pradeśāḥ sāmānyasya vartante iti/ ye
321.09	te ekadeśāḥ sāmānyasya piṇḍa^su pratyekam vartante te kim sāmānya-
321.10	ātmakāḥ uta na iti/ yadi sāmānya-ātmakāḥ ekam ekatra vartate iti
321.11	prāptam na ce ekam anekatra vartamānam sāmānyam iti yuktam vaktum atha
321.12	asāmānya-ātmakāḥ śūnyam abhidhānam sāmānyasya ekadeśāḥ pratyekam vartante
321.13	iti taiḥ api deśaiḥ sāmānyam pūrvavat vartate iti pūrvavat-prasaṅgaḥ/ na ca anyā
321.14	gatiḥ asti tasmāt na aneka-vṛtti sāmānyam iti na vikalpa-anabhyupagamāt
321.15	na ayam vikalpaḥ asti gotvam pratyekam piṇḍa^su parisamāpya vartate atha
321.16	pradeśena vartate iti tasmāt vikalpa-anabhyupagamāt apraśnaḥ ayam kasmāt gotvasya
321.17	avayavi-samudāya-tva-anabhyupagamāt/ na gotvam avayavin na samudāyaḥ
321.18	samudāyasya bhāgaḥ ekadeśa-śabdaḥ avayavinaḥ ca avayave yadā na kim
321.19	kim cit parihāya aśeṣam abhidhīyate tadā avayavinaḥ avayavān apekṣya kṛtsna-śabdaḥ
321.20	samudāye ca samudāyinaḥ apekṣya na ca gotvam avayavin^ḥ na samudāyaḥ
321.21	tasmāt na kṛtsna-ekadeśa-śabdau staḥ na cet tatra etau śabdau staḥ tasmāt gotvam
322.01	kim kṛtsnena vartate uta ekadeśena iti na yuktaḥ praśnaḥ/ uktam ca atra
322.02	ekam anekatra vartate iti pratijānānaḥ na anuyoktavyaḥ/ kasmāt ubhayena
322.03	vyāghātāt iti/ katham tarhi gotvam goṣu vartate āśraya-āśrayi-bhāvena
322.04	kaḥ punar āśraya-āśrayi-bhāvaḥ samavāyaḥ tatra vṛttimat gotvam/ vṛttiḥ samavāyaḥ
322.05	iti iha-pratyaya-hetuḥ-tvāt iti uktam na gotvam go^i ago^i vṛtti-abhāvāt iti
322.06	cet atha manyase yad idam gotvam goṣu anuvṛtti-pratyaya-kāraṇam tad kim
322.07	go^i vartate āhosvit ago^i yadi tāvat go^i prāk gotva-yogāt go^ḥ
322.08	eva asau iti vyartham gotvam/ atha ago^i na aśva-ādi api gotva-yogāt go^ḥ
322.09	prāpnoti/ na ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt na gotvāt anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ iti
322.10	na vikalpa-anabhyupagamāt na go^i gotvam na ago^i prāk gotvāt na asau
322.11	go^ḥ na api ago^ḥ iti/ kim kāraṇam ubhau etau viśeṣya-pratyayau na
322.12	viśeṣaṇa-sambandham antareṇa bhavataḥ na ca prāk gotva-yogāt vastu vidyate na
322.13	avidyamānam go^ḥ iti ago^ḥ iti ca śakyam vyapadeṣṭum yadā eva vastu tadā eva
322.14	gotvena abhisambaddhyate iti anāspadaḥ vikalpaḥ/ etena sattā-sambandhasya
322.15	sat-asat-vikalpaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ na sataḥ sattā-sambandhaḥ na asataḥ/ yadā eva
322.16	tad vastu tadā eva sattayā sambaddham iti sat-asat-āśrayaḥ doṣaḥ anupapannaḥ iti
322.17	tasmāt upapannā jātiḥ/ na ca asau mṛd-gavake iti yad-abhāvāt tatra
322.18	asampratyayaḥ saḥ padārthaḥ iti//
322.19	[2-2-65(66)]na ākṛti-vyakti-apekṣa-tvāt jāti-abhivyakti^aḥ
322.20	na ākṛti-vyakti-apekṣa-tvāt jāti-abhivyakti^aḥ/ jāti^aḥ abhivyaktiḥ ākṛti-vyaktī
322.21	apekṣate vyakti-ākṛtī iti prāpte prādhānyāt ākṛti^aḥ pūrva-nipātaḥ kim punar
323.01	prādhānyam ākṛti-viśeṣaṇa-tvam vyakti^aḥ yasmāt vyakti^ā viśeṣyamāṇā
323.02	ākṛtiḥ jāti^aḥ liṅgam bhavati tasmāt pradhānam ākṛtiḥ na ca agṛhyamāṇāyām
323.03	ākṛti^i vyakti^i ca jāti-mātram śuddham śabdāt gamyate tasmāt na jātiḥ api eva
323.04	padārthaḥ/  na ca vai yathā-arthena na śakyam bhavitum/ kaḥ tarhi padārthaḥ
323.05	vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  tu padārthaḥ//
323.06	[2-2-66(67)]vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  tu padārthaḥ
323.07	tu-śabdaḥ viśeṣaṇa-arthaḥ kim viśiṣyate pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvasya aniyamena
323.08	padārtha-tvam yadā bheda-vivakṣā viśeṣa-gatiḥ ca go^ḥ tiṣṭḥati go^ḥ niṣaṇṇā iti
323.09	tadā vyaktiḥ padārthaḥ aṅgam jāti-ākṛtī jāti^i ākṛti^i ca sthāna-gamana-ādīnām
323.10	abhāvāt yatra sambhavaḥ saḥ padārthaḥ iti/ yadā punar bhedaḥ na vivakṣitaḥ
323.11	sāmānya-gatiḥ ca tadā jātiḥ padārthaḥ yathā go^ḥ na padā sparṣṭavyā iti ākṛti^aḥ
323.12	ca pradhāna-bhāvaḥ utprekṣitavyaḥ iti svayam utprekṣya iti ayam arthaḥ kva punar ākṛti^aḥ
323.13	prādhānyam dṛṣṭam yathā piṣṭakamayaḥ gāvaḥ kriyantām iti/ tad etad bahulam
323.14	prayoga^su pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvaḥ svayam utprekṣyate iti na vyakti-ākṛti-
323.15	jātyaḥ  padārthaḥ iti ke cit kutaḥ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ sat iti etad eva
323.16	vyāpaka-tvāt upādāya cintyate sat iti anena padena punar jātiḥ abhidhīyate
323.17	piṇḍaḥ vā sambandhaḥ vā jātimān vā arthaḥ/ tatra na tāvat jātiḥ abhidhīyante
323.18	bheda-arthaiḥ abhinna-vibhaktika-tvāt yadi ayam jāti-vācakaḥ sat-śadaḥ sat-śabdaḥ bhavati
323.19	sat-dravyam iti bheda-vācinā dravya-śabdena saha śabda-sāmānyādhikaraṇyam śabda; omit na
323.20	prāpnoti na hi bhinna-artha-vācakānām sāmānyādhikaraṇyam paśyāmaḥ na hi
323.21	go-aśvam iti sāmānyādhikaraṇyam dṛṣṭam/ atha dravya-ādi-vṛtti-tvāt sattāyāḥ
324.01	eva dravya-ādi-śabdaiḥ saha sāmānādhikaraṇyam tatha api pāratantryāt sattāyāḥ
324.02	guṇa-tvam guṇa-guṇi-abhidhāyinoḥ ca śabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam na dṛṣṭam
324.03	iti yathā śaṅkhasya śauklyam iti/ etena sambandhaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ sat-śabdaḥ
324.04	piṇḍānām vācakaḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam piṇḍānām ānantyāt na hi
324.05	ekasya śabdasya aneka-dravya-guṇa-prapañcena sambandhaḥ ākhyātum śakyaḥ na ca anākhyāte
324.06	śabda-artha-sambandhe śabdāt artha-pratipattiḥ yuktā svarūpa-mātra-pratīti^aḥ vyabhicārāt
324.07	ca/ sat-śabda-śravaṇāt ca dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi iti pariplavamānā buddhiḥ avatiṣṭḥate/
324.08	na ca yasmāt abhidhānāt pariplavate buddhiḥ tad-abhidhānam iti yuktam vaktum
324.09	tasmāt sat-śabdaḥ bhedānām na vācakaḥ iti/ jātimat-mātra-abhidhāyakaḥ
324.10	api sat-śabdaḥ na bhavati kasmāt asvatantra-tvāt na hi sat-śabdāt tad-
324.11	bhedāḥ ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  gamyante iti tadvat-ghaṭa-ādi-bheda-anākṣepāt sāmānādhikaraṇya-
324.12	abhāvaḥ/ atha vā asvatantra-tvāt iti sat-śabdaḥ prādhānyena sattāyām
324.13	vartate/ tatra vartamānaḥ tadvat iti upacaryate yad ca yatra vartamānam anyatra
324.14	upacaryate na tad tasya abhidhāyakam mañca-śabda-vat iti/ uktam ca atra
324.15	kim uktam tadvatām ānantyāt na sat-śabdena abhidhānam yuktam iti tadvati
324.16	ca na guṇa-sārūpyāt pratyaya-saṅkrātiḥ/ yathā svāmi-śabdasya bhṛtye na
324.17	guṇa-uparāgāt/ yathā nīlaḥ sphaṭikaḥ iti krama-vṛtti-abhāvāt yugapat-asambhavāt
324.18	ca/ ayathā-artha-jñāna-utpatti-prasaṅgāt ca/ tasmāt na jātimat-mātra-
324.19	abhidhāyakaḥ api asādhāraṇa-viśeṣa-abhidhāyakaḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam/
324.20	anyatra pratyaya-abhāva-prasaṅgāt ca/ na ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt anyāpoha-
324.21	kṛt-śrutiḥ iti/ anya-pada-artha-antara-apoham hitvā arthe kurvatī śrutiḥ
325.01	abhidhatte iti ucyate iti/ atra asmābhiḥ vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  padārthaḥ
325.02	iti pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvasya aniyamena vyavasthāpitam ekānta-vādinaḥ ca ete
325.03	doṣāḥ iti/ na ca ete doṣāḥ katham iti yad tāvat sattā-śabdaḥ sattāyāḥ
325.04	na vācakaḥ iti tad na vyāghātāt sattā-śabdaḥ na sattā-vācakaḥ iti
325.05	vyāhatam ucyate/ katham vā tad-avācaka-tve sati sattā-śabdaḥ iti abhidhīyate/
325.06	bhavat-abhiprāyena abhidhīyate iti cet/ atha manyase na eva naḥ siddhānte
325.07	sattā-śabdāḥ santi kim tu bhavadbhiḥ sattā-śabdaḥ dravya-guṇa-karma-śabdāḥ
325.08	iti ca parikalpya vyavahāraḥ kriyate/ tataḥ asmābhiḥ api yuṣmat-abhiprāya-
325.09	anuvidhānena sattā-śabdāḥ iti vācakāḥ upacaryante iti/ na vyāghāta-
325.10	anivṛtti^aḥ/ sat iti evam ādīni padāni loka-prasiddhāni keṣu artha^su
325.11	vartante iti ubhayoḥ vicāraḥ/ vayam tu brūmaḥ sattā-śabdaḥ sattāyām vartate
325.12	tvam punar bravīṣi sattā-śabdaḥ na sattāyām iti/ evam bruvāṇaḥ mayā virodhena
325.13	pratyavasthīyate virodha-uddhāram vā anuktvā ca akṛtvā tvan-matena tu annayaḥ mayā abhidhīyate iti
325.14	bruvāṇaḥ na vyāghātam ativartase/ yad ca idam ucyate tvayā parikalpitāḥ
325.15	sattā-śabdāḥ iti tad api na parikalpya-viṣaya-anabhidhānāt parikalpyasya
325.16	ca viṣayaḥ atathā-bhūtasya tathā-bhāvibhiḥ sāmānye sati adhyāropita-tad-dharmaṇaḥ
325.17	pradhāna-artha-pratipattiḥ yathā sthāṇi^i puruṣaḥ iti/ yadi ca amī śabdāḥ
325.18	parikalpitāḥ bhavanti pradhānam eṣām vācyam na hi niṣpradhānam parikalpyam/
325.19	yad punar etad bhedaḥ arthaiḥ abhinna-vibhaktika-tvāt iti/ tad na samāna-artha-
325.20	aparijñānāt na brūmaḥ sattā-śabdena dravya-śabdasya sāmānādhikaraṇyam
325.21	api tu sattāyāḥ pradhāna-sādhana-vācinā sat-śabdena dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi abhidhīyante
326.01	iti atra guṇa-karma-śabdaiḥ api itaretara-viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāva-āpannaiḥ tāni eva
326.02	evam ca sati yuktam sāmānādhikaraṇyam ubhayoḥ sat-dravya-śabdayoḥ eka-viṣaya-
326.03	tvāt yadā punar ayam sattā-śabdaḥ sattā-pradhānam eva aṅga-bhūtam sādhanam
326.04	abhidhatte na tadā sāmānādhikaraṇyam dravyasya sattā iti bhavati/ tasmāt
326.05	sāmānādhikaraṇya-anupapattiḥ adoṣaḥ vibhakti-bheda-niyamāt guṇa-guṇi-abhidhāyinoḥ
326.06	sāmānādhikaraṇyasya asiddhiḥ sat-dravya-śabdayoḥ iti etad anena
326.07	pratyuktam/ etena sambandhaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ katham iti na sat-śabdena sambandhaḥ
326.08	abhidhīyate api tu sambandhi-śabdena eva sambandhaḥ ca guṇaḥ na sat-śabdaḥ guṇa-
326.09	śabdaḥ na ca guṇa-śabdena dravya-vācinaḥ śabdasya sāmānādhikaraṇyam asti iti/
326.10	yad punar etad ānatyāt na jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti/ kaḥ ca evam āha
326.11	jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti svayam-prayuktām vācas-yuktim bhavān pratiṣedhati/
326.12	yadi tarhi bhedānām vācakaḥ katham sat-śabdena dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi
326.13	abhidhīyate/ na ca ayam bhoḥ sat-śabdaḥ jāti-śabdaḥ api tu bheda-śabdaḥ
326.14	eva ayam bheda-śabdena ca bhedaḥ eva abhidhīyate iti na kim cit bādhyate/ katham
326.15	na bādhyate yadā sat-śabdaḥ ekaḥ bhedānām ānantyam tad-avastham eva/ na ca ekasya
326.16	anekena sambandhaḥ śakyaḥ ākhyātum na ca anākhyāte sambandhe śabda-artha-pratipattiḥ
326.17	yuktā iti na kevalānām anabhidhānāt/ yaḥ hi sat-śabdena aviśeṣaṇāni
326.18	dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi abhidhīyante iti abhidhatte tam prati eṣaḥ doṣaḥ/ asmākam tu
326.19	dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi sattā-viśeṣaṇāni abhidhīyante/ yatra yatra sattā paśyati
326.20	tatra tatra sat-śabdam prayuṅkte ekā ca sattā tad-pratyayasya anuvṛtti^aḥ tasmāt bhedānām
327.01	ānantyam na doṣaḥ/ kaḥ ca ayam evam-bhūtaḥ sādhya-sādhana-bhāvaḥ śabdam
327.02	pakṣayasi ānantyāt iti vastu-dharmam hetu-tvena abhidhatse iti/ atha bhedāḥ eva
327.03	jāti-śabdāḥ vācya-tvena pakṣa^kriyante tathā api na anvayin^ḥ na vyatirekin^ḥ ca
327.04	dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti iti ahetuḥ ānantyāt iti/ atha api ekadeśān pakṣa^kritya ānantyāt
327.05	iti hetuḥ ekadeśaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti ayam api na virodhāt/ evam ca
327.06	sati na kaḥ cit padārthaḥ vācyaḥ na kaḥ cit śabdaḥ vācakaḥ iti vācya-
327.07	vācaka-bhāva-nivṛtti^i viśeṣaṇam anarthakam ete na vācyāḥ ete na vācakāḥ
327.08	iti/ yad punar etad vyabhicārāt iti na vyabhicāra-viṣaya-aparijñānāt/
327.09	vyabhicārin^ḥ hi nāma jñeyaḥ yaḥ sva-viṣaya-tad-jātīya-vṛtti-tve sati
327.10	anyatra vartate na punar ayam sat-śabdaḥ sva-viṣaya-vyatirekena asati vartate/
327.11	tasmāt asthāne vyabhicāra-deśanā iti hetu-bhāva-anabhyupagamāt ca/ kena ca atra
327.12	hetuḥ abhidhīyate dravyam guṇaḥ karman sattvāt iti tasmāt ayuktam etad api
327.13	bheda-vācaka-tva-pratiṣedhāt dravya-sat-śabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti na
327.14	vaktavyam vyāghātāt/ na ca kaḥ cit śabdaḥ dravya-vācakaḥ dravya-sat-śabdayoḥ ca
327.15	sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti vyāhatam/ yad punar etad tadvataḥ na asvatantra-tvāt
327.16	iti na kila sat-śabdena tad-bhedāḥ ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  gamyante tadvat-ghaṭa-ādi-
327.17	bheda-anākṣepāt sāmānādhikaraṇya-abhāvaḥ iti/ tad na ukta-uttara-tvāt/
327.18	ukta-uttaram etad sat-dravya-śabdayoḥ eka-viṣaya-tvāt sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti
327.19	tadvat-ghaṭa-ādi-bheda-anākṣepaḥ iti vā yuktam sat-śabdena sattā-sādhanānām
327.20	dravya-guṇa-karmaṇām ākṣepāt/ sattā-śabdena dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi na ākṣipyante
327.21	na punar sat-śabdena śakyate vaktum sattā-śabdena api guṇa-bhūtāni gamyante
328.01	iti sat iti ca ayam sat-śabdaḥ kaḥ tasya bhidheyaḥ yaḥ asmāt pratīyate saḥ
328.02	tasya arthaḥ iti kaḥ asya arthaḥ vibhajyate sattā tad-yogaḥ artha-antaram iti trayaḥ
328.03	arthaḥ/ tatra pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāva-vivakṣāyām pradhānam sādhanam gamyate aṅgam
328.04	sattā ca tad-yogaḥ ca kim-kṛtaḥ pradhāna-aṅga-bhāvaḥ artha-kṛtaḥ yadā sattā
328.05	kim cit kurute tadā sattā viśeṣaṇam padārtham upādāya kim cit karoti
328.06	tad-pradhānam aṅgam sattā pradhāna-pratyaya-hetu-tvāt/ yasmāt sattāyāḥ padārthaḥ
328.07	viśiṣyate aṅga-hīne pratyaya-nivṛtti^aḥ yatra tad-aṅgam sattā-ādikam na asti na
328.08	tatra sat iti pratyayaḥ/ tad evam tadvat-ghaṭa-ādi-bheda-anākṣepāt iti ayuktam/
328.09	yad punar etad sat-śabdaḥ sattāyām prādhānyena vartate iti tatra vartamānaḥ tadvat
328.10	upacaryate iti tad na sat-śabda-viṣaya-aparijñānāt/ na bhavatā sat-śabdasya
328.11	viṣayaḥ vyajñāyi kaḥ evam āha sat-śabdaḥ sattāyām prādhānyena vartate iti
328.12	yathā ca sat-śabdasya dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi sattā-sādhana-bhāvāt viṣayaḥ iti
328.13	tathā ca uktam/ etena bhedānām ānantyam pratyuktam/ sat-śabdasya viṣayaḥ
328.14	sattā-sādhanam viṣayaḥ ca sattā-sādhana-bhūtaḥ ekaḥ eva iti/ yad punar etad tadvati
328.15	ca na guṇa-sārūpyāt pratyaya-saṅkrāntiḥ yathā svāmi-śabdasya bhṛtyaḥ iti/
328.16	asya arthaḥ svāmi-anukāri-tvāt bhṛtye svāmi-śabdaḥ iti/ etad tu na buddhyāmahe
328.17	pratyayānām saṅkrāntiḥ iti/ yadi svāmi-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam tad na
328.18	pratyaya-saṅkrāntiḥ na hi svāmi-pratyayaḥ bhṛtye bhavati kevalam gurutām
328.19	priyatām vā sampaśyan svāmi-śabdam yugapat-bhūtam tatra prayuṅkte/ yathā ca bhṛtyaḥ
328.20	svāmi-anukāri-tvāt bhṛtyaḥ svāmin^ḥ iti upacaryate na punar evam sattā-śabdaḥ
329.01	kadā cit api dravya-ādiṣu vartate iti/ yadā tu satām bhāvaḥ sattā iti tadā
329.02	satām viśeṣaka-tvāt guṇa-bhūtānām sattā-śabdena abhidhānam viśeṣaka-tvam upacāraḥ/
329.03	etena eva guṇa-uparāgāt yathā nīlaḥ sphaṭikaḥ iti pratyuktam/ yad punar
329.04	etad krama-vṛtti-abhāvāt yugapat-asambhavāt ca iti asya arthaḥ/ sattā-śabdaḥ sattāyām
329.05	kila varti-tvāt tadvati vartate iti/ na ca evam paśyāmaḥ yugapat api na
329.06	sambhavati sattā-pratyayaḥ ca dravya-pratyayaḥ ca na yugapat bhavataḥ/ etad api na ukta-
329.07	uttara-tvāt/ ukta-uttaram etad/ na atra kramaḥ na yugapat-pratyayaḥ sat-pratyayasya
329.08	sādhana-viṣaya-tvāt/ etena ayathā-artha-jñāna-utpatti-prasaṅgāt iti pratyuktam/ evam
329.09	hi kva hetu-pradhāna-nirāsāt vyavasthitam vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  padārthaḥ iti/
329.10	na jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti viśeṣaṇa-anarthakyam na hi kaḥ cit
329.11	śabdaḥ bhavatā bhedānām vacaka-tvena abhyupagamyate viśeṣaṇam nāma viśeṣye
329.12	ca sati sāmānya-pratipatti^i ca bhavati na asya kim cit viśeṣyam asti na ca
329.13	jāti-śabdasya kaḥ cit padārthaḥ vācyaḥ abhyupagamyate tasmāt viśeṣaṇa-anarthakyam/
329.14	virodhāt ca yadi ca ayam jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ abhaviṣyat
329.15	api tarhi jāti-śabdaḥ eva na abhaviṣyat na hi anya-padārtha-vācakānām tad-śabda-
329.16	tvam sambhavati katham vā bheda-vācaka-tve sati jāti-śabdaḥ iti ucyate yad ca
329.17	ucyate na jāti-sabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti tathā tad kaḥ hi svastha-
329.18	ātman^ḥ jāti-śabda-vācyān bhedān abhyupaiti/ yadi ca jāti-śabda-vācyāḥ
329.19	bhedāḥ na bhavanti anya-śabda-vācyāḥ tarhi prāpnuvanti/ atha anya-śabda-
329.20	vācyāḥ api na bhavanti te na jāti-sabda-vācyāḥ na anya-śabda-vācyāḥ
329.21	iti ubhaya-avācya-tve kim asya viśeṣaṇasya sāmarthyam na jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām
330.01	vācakaḥ iti/ ataḥ evam vaktavyam na vācyāḥ bhedāḥ avācakāḥ śabdāḥ
330.02	iti/ atha manyase para-mata-vyapekṣayā etad ucyate/ na jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām
330.03	vācakaḥ iti na anabhyupagamāt na hi kaḥ cit tantrikaḥ asti yaḥ jāti-
330.04	śabda-vācyān bhedān pratipadyate jāti^aḥ api avācaka-tve vyāghātaḥ jāti-śabdaḥ
330.05	iti na hi yaḥ yasya avācakaḥ saḥ tad-śabdaḥ iti śakyam vaktum na
330.06	jāti-śabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti/ kasya tarhi vācakaḥ bheda-vācaka-
330.07	tva-pratiṣedhāt anya-vācaka-tvam āyātam atha anya-vācaka-tvam api na iṣyate vyartham
330.08	viśeṣaṇam jāti-sabdaḥ bhedānām na vācakaḥ iti sarve eva śabdāḥ arthānām
