330.09	na vācakāḥ iti yuktam vaktum/ atha avācakāḥ śabdāḥ avācyāḥ arthāḥ
330.10	iti ayuktam ca etad vyāghātāt avācakāḥ ca śabdāḥ bhavanti avācaka-tvam ca
330.11	śabdānām śabdena eva pratipādyate iti avācaka-tve śabdānām pratijñā-hetu^oḥ
330.12	vyāghātaḥ na hi jāti-sabdaḥ bhedānām vācakaḥ iti pratijnnā-hetuḥ ānantyāt
330.13	iti/ ubhayam ca vacana-ātmakam iti ubhayasya avacana-ātmaka-tvāt vyāghātaḥ/
330.14	śabdān ca avācakān pratipadyamānena loka-pratipādana-upāyaḥ anyaḥ vaktavyaḥ/
330.15	anyathā abhyupagataḥ iti cet atha manyase na mayā śabdāḥ avācakāḥ
330.16	abhyupagamyante na api arthāḥ avācyāḥ api tu yaḥ bhavadbhiḥ vācya-vācaka-nyāyaḥ
330.17	abhyupagamyate saḥ asmābhiḥ nirākriyate/ asmākam tu śabdena anyāpoha-
330.18	lakṣaṇā jātiḥ abhidhīyate iti viśiṣya vaktavyam yadi anyathā śabdāḥ
330.19	arthānām abhidhāyakāḥ bhavanti viśiṣya vaktavyam/ asmad-prakLpta-jāti-
330.20	ādi-vyatirekena jāti-ādi-śabdāḥ na jāti-ādīnām vācakāḥ iti svarūpa-
331.01	anabhidhānāt ca/ na ca samāna-pratyaya-kāraṇa-tā vyatirekena jāti^aḥ svarūpam
331.02	nirdeṣṭum śakyam na ca anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ pratyākhyātum śakyaḥ na ca viśeṣa-
331.03	pratyayena akasmāt śakyam bhavitum yataḥ ayam pratyayaḥ sā jātiḥ iti na asti
331.04	vivādaḥ/ etāvat yathā-śruti-vākye doṣaḥ/ atha jāti-śabdāḥ vācya-tva^na
331.05	bhedāḥ api pakṣa^kriyante/ etasmin api pratijñā-arthe yāvantaḥ ete
331.06	vākya-doṣāḥ te sarve prasajyante evam ekadeśa-pakṣa^karaṇe api viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-
331.07	bhāva-anupapatti^aḥ pūrva-doṣa-anuvṛttiḥ/ yad api idam ucyate dravya-sat-śabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam
331.08	iti tad api na vyāghātāt/ dravya-sat-śabdayoḥ ca sāmānādhikaraṇyam
331.09	na ca bheda-vācakāḥ śabdāḥ iti vyāhatam/ upacārataḥ
331.10	na vyāghātaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase sat-dravya-śabdau etau upacārataḥ
331.11	abhidhīyete katham iti dravya-śabdena adravya-vyudāsaḥ sat-śabdena asat-vyudāsaḥ
331.12	tau etau asat-dravya-vyudāsa-rūpena pravartamānau ekam artham bruvāte iti samānādhikaraṇau
331.13	iti ucyate mukhyataḥ tu na kaḥ cit vidhānataḥ śabdaḥ vācakaḥ
331.14	iti na mukhya-asambhavāt mukhyataḥ sat-dravya-śabda-prayoge sati tena sāmānyāt
331.15	atathā-bhūtaḥ upacāraḥ iti yuktam na tu evam vādinām pradhānam asti na
331.16	ca pradhānam antareṇa upacāraḥ sambhavati na ca anyāpoha-vāde upacāraḥ
331.17	yuktaḥ ubhayoḥ pradhāna-śabda-tvāt/ yathā siṃha-śabdaḥ siṃhe vartate asiṃha-
331.19	iti/ yad punar etad anya-śabda-artha-apohaḥ śabda-arthaḥ iti tad api ayuktam vidhāna-
331.20	śabda-artha-sambhave sati ādyā pratipattiḥ yadi vidhāna-śabda-arthaḥ bhavati
332.01	tataḥ vidhīyamāna-śabda-artha-pratipatti^i satyām tasya anyatra pratiṣedhaḥ iti
332.02	upapannaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ yasya punar vidhīyamānaḥ padārthaḥ na asti tasya ādyām pratipattim
332.03	antareṇa katham pratiṣedhaḥ yāvat ca itaram na pratipadyate tāvat itaram na
332.05	na pratipadyate tāvat bahu-vipratipattiḥ na yuktā yāvat ca gām na pratipadyate
332.06	tāvat ago^i iti ubhaya-pratipatti-abhāvaḥ/ yaḥ ca ayam anyāpohaḥ ago^ḥ na bhavati iti
332.07	go-śabdasya arthaḥ saḥ kim bhāvaḥ atha abhāvaḥ iti/ yadi bhāvaḥ kim go^ḥ ago^ḥ
332.08	iti yadi go^ḥ na asti vivādaḥ atha ago^ḥ go-śabdasya arthaḥ aho śabda-artha-
332.09	kauśalam/ abhāvaḥ tu na yuktaḥ praiṣa-sampratipatti^oḥ aviṣaya-tvāt na hi
332.10	go-śabda-śrāvaṇāt abhāve praiṣaḥ na sampratipattiḥ śabda-arthaḥ ca pratipatti^ā pratīyate
332.11	na ca go-śabdāt abhāvam kaḥ cit pratipadyate iti anyāpohaḥ ca śabda-arthaḥ
332.12	iti ayuktam avyāpaka-tvāt yatra dvairāśyam bhavati tatra itara-pratiṣedhāt tad-itaraḥ
332.13	pratīyate yathā ago^ḥ iti pade go^ḥ pratīyamānaḥ ago^ḥ pratiṣidhyamānaḥ na
332.14	punar sarva-pade etad asti na hi asarvam nāma kim cit asti yad sarva-padena nivartyeta/
332.15	eka-ādi-vyudāsāt vyāpakam iti cet atha manyase eka-ādi-asarvam tad
332.16	sarva-śabdena nivartyate/ tad na sva-artha-apavāda-doṣa-prasaṅgāt/ evam sati eka-ādi-
332.17	vyudāsena pravartamānaḥ sarva-śabdaḥ aṅga-pratiṣedhāt aṅga-vyatiriktasya ca aṅginaḥ
332.18	anabhyupagamāt anarthakaḥ syāt evam sarve samudāya-śabdāḥ ekadeśa-pratiṣedha-
332.19	rūpena pravartamānāḥ samudāyi-vyatirikta-samudāya-anabhyupagamāt anarthakāḥ
332.20	prāpnuvanti iti/ dvi-ādi-śabdānām ca samuccaya-viṣaya-tvāt eka-ādi-pratiṣedhe
333.01	pratiṣidhyamānānām asamuccayāt dvi-ādi-śabdānām anarthaka-tvam kriyā-rūpa-
333.02	tvāt ca apohasya viṣayaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ ago^ḥ na bhavati iti ayam apohaḥ kim go-viṣayaḥ
333.03	atha ago-vuṣayaḥ/ yadi go-viṣayaḥ katham go^aḥ go^i eva abhāvaḥ atha
333.04	ago-viṣayaḥ iti manyase katham anya-viṣayāt apohāt anyatra pratipattiḥ na hi
333.05	khadire chadyāne palāśe chidā bhavati/ atha ago^aḥ go^i pratiṣedhaḥ
333.06	go^ḥ ago^ḥ na bhavati iti kena go^aḥ agotvam prasaktam yad pratiṣidhyate iti katham
333.07	vā go^i go-pratipattim antareṇa pratiṣedhaḥ na hi uttara-padārtha-pratipattim
333.08	antareṇa pratiṣedham paśyānaḥ/ uktam ca atra ādyā pratipattiḥ na prāpnoti iti
333.09	ādi-pratipattim ca antareṇa na pratiṣedhaḥ iti/ itaḥ ca na yuktaḥ apohaḥ
333.10	vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ yaḥ ayam ago^aḥ apohaḥ go^i saḥ kim vyatiriktaḥ āhosvit
333.11	avyatiriktaḥ iti/ yadi vyatiriktaḥ kim āśritaḥ nāśritaḥ vā iti/
333.12	yadi āśritaḥ āśrita-tvāt guṇaḥ iti go-śabdena guṇaḥ abhidhīyate na go^ḥ iti
333.13	go^ḥ tiṣṭhati sāmānādhikaraṇyam na prāptam/ atha anāśritaḥ kena arthena
333.14	ṣaṣṭhī go^aḥ apohaḥ iti/ atha avyatiriktaḥ go^ḥ eva asau iti na kim cit kṛtam
333.15	bhavati iti/ yam ca apohaḥ prativastu ekaḥ anekaḥ iti vā vaktavyaḥ/ yadi ekaḥ
333.16	aneka-go-sambandhin^ḥ tadā gotvam tad iti atha anekaḥ tataḥ piṇḍa-vat ānantyāt
333.17	ākhyāna-anupapatti^aḥ artha-pratyayaḥ na yuktaḥ/ idam ca tāvat praṣṭavyaḥ jāyate bhavān
333.18	kim ayam apohaḥ vācyaḥ atha avācyaḥ iti/ yadi vācyaḥ anaikāntikaḥ śabda-arthaḥ
333.19	anyāpohaḥ śabdasya arthaḥ iti/ anavasthā vā atha anapoha-vyudāsa-rūpena
333.20	abhidhīyate apohaḥ tasya api anyaḥ iti anavasthā/ atha avācyaḥ anya-śabda-artha-
333.21	apoham karoti iti vyāhatam yadi ca śabdasya apohaḥ na abhidheya-arthaḥ
334.01	abhidheya-artha-vytirekena sva-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ atha saḥ eva asya sva-arthaḥ tathā api
334.02	vyāhatam anya-śabda-artha-apoham sva-arthe kurvatī śrutiḥ abhidhatte iti ucyate iti
334.03	tasya vākyasya ayam arthaḥ tadānīm bhavati anyat anabhidhānaḥ abhidhatte iti/
334.04	anekam iti ca asya padasya dvi-ādi-viṣaya-tvāt sāmānya-adhigati^i viśeṣaḥ
334.05	āśrayitavyaḥ na hi asati viśeṣa-āśrayaṇe sāmānya-śabdāt viśeṣa-pratipattiḥ
334.06	yuktā iti nīla-utpala-śabdayoḥ ca pradhāna-tvāt viśeṣaṇa-viśeṣya-bhāva-anupapatti^i viśeṣaṇasya
334.07	pūrva-nipātaḥ iti ubhayoḥ pradhāna-tvāt na prāptiḥ/ etena rāja-puruṣa-
334.08	śabdau vyākhyātau/ samānādhikaraṇa-arthaḥ ca anyāpoha-vādinā vācyaḥ/ yasya
334.09	ca anyāpohaḥ śabda-arthaḥ tena anīla-anutpala-vyudāsau katham samānādhikaraṇau
334.10	iti vaktavyaḥ/ yasya punar vidhīyamānaḥ śabda-arthaḥ tasya jāti-guṇa-viśiṣṭam
334.11	nīla-utpala-śabdābhyām dravyam abhidhīyate jāti-guṇau ca dravye vartete na punar
334.12	anīla-anutpala-vyudāsau tasmāt samānādhikaraṇa-arthaḥ na asti iti/ tad evam anyāpohaḥ
334.13	yathā yathā vikalpyate tathā tathā pramāṇa-vṛttam bādhate iti//
334.14	katham punar jñāyate nānā vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  iti/ adhigata-tvāt
334.15	praśna-anupapattiḥ iti cet adhigatāḥ vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  yāśabda-ādi-
334.16	sūtraiḥ adhigata-tvāt praśna-arthaḥ anupapannaḥ/ na nimitta-traividhye sati tad-viśeṣa-
334.17	viṣaya-jñāpana-artha-tvāt praśnasya go-śabdaḥ uccaran pradhāna-upasarjana-bhāvena
334.18	nimittaḥ eva pratipādayati iti yāśabda-ādi-sūtrāṇām arthaḥ evam ca sati
334.19	tad-viśeṣa-viṣaya-jñāpana-arthaḥ yuktaḥ praśnaḥ katamā tatra vyaktiḥ katamā
334.20	ākṛtiḥ katamā jātiḥ iti vyakti-ākṛti-jātīnām bhedaḥ lakṣaṇa-bhedāt/
334.21	kim punar vyakti^aḥ lakṣaṇam asti vyakti-lakṣaṇa-pratipādana-artham vyaktiḥ guṇa-viśeṣa-
334.22	āśrayaḥ mūrtiḥ iti sūtram//
335.01	[2-2-67(68)]vyaktiḥ guṇa-viśeṣa-āśrayaḥ mūrtiḥ
335.02	tatra vyajyate iti vyaktiḥ indriya-grāhyā iti yad dravyam upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-
335.03	prāptānām sparśa-antānām guṇānām āśrayaḥ tad mūrtitā-avayava-tvāt mūrtiḥ iti
335.04	ca ucyate vyaktiḥ iti ca/ etad tu na buddhyāmahe kim idam avayavi-dravyasya
335.05	lakṣaṇam aho padārtha-tvena trayāṇām prasaktānām jāti-ākṛti-vyavacchedakam
335.06	vyakti-lakṣaṇam iti/ vayam tu brūmaḥ na atra avayavin^ḥ vyavacchedyaḥ api jāti-
335.07	ākṛti-pada-vyatiriktasya vyakti-padasya arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ iti/ sā ca vyaktiḥ yā na
335.08	jātiḥ na akṛtiḥ tasyāḥ anena sūtrena saṃgrahaḥ vyaktiḥ guṇa-viśeṣa-āśrayaḥ mūrtiḥ
335.09	iti/ guṇāḥ ca viśeṣāḥ ca tad-āśrayaḥ ca atra tad-śabda-lopāt guṇa-viśeṣāḥ āśraya-
335.10	śabdena dravyam ucyate karman api guṇa-viśeṣa-śabdāt labhyate guṇa^bhyaḥ viśeṣāḥ
335.11	guṇa-viśeṣāḥ iti/ guṇa-viśeṣa-grahaṇam kim artham guṇa-padārthena saṃgṛhītāyāḥ
335.12	ākṛti^aḥ nirākaraṇa-artham/ ākṛtiḥ saṃyoga-viśeṣaḥ sā ca guṇa-grahaṇena
335.13	saṃgṛhītā iti tad-nirākaraṇa-artham guṇa-viśeṣa-grahaṇam guṇa-viśeṣāḥ iti atra
335.14	dvi-rūpaḥ vigrahaḥ guṇāḥ ca te viśeṣāḥ iti ca guṇa^bhyaḥ viśeṣāḥ iti ca/
335.15	āśraya-śabda-samānādhikaraṇaḥ mūrti-śabdaḥ dravye mūrcchate iti mūrti-
335.16	śabdena dravyam ucyate/ evam ca pratipadam lakṣaṇam sidhyati/ evam ca rūpa-
335.17	ākāśa-ādi-avarodhaḥ yadi pratipadam etad vyakti-lakṣaṇam bhavati tataḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ
335.18	ākāśa-ādyaḥ  ca vyāpyante anyathā tu na lakṣaṇam avyāpakam syāt iti/
335.19	atha vā guṇa-viśeṣāṇām āśrayaḥ iti guṇāḥ ca viśeṣāḥ ca guṇa-viśeṣāḥ
335.20	teṣāṃ āśrayaḥ dravyam tatra mūrcchate iti mūrtiḥ//
336.01	[2-2-68(69)]ākṛtiḥ jāti-liṅga-ākhyā
336.02	ākṛtiḥ jāti-liṅga-ākhyā/ jātiḥ jāti-liṅgāni ca prakhyāyante yathā
336.03	tām ākṛtim vindyāt sā ca na anyā sattva-avayavānām niyatāt vyūhāt niyata-
336.04	vyūhāḥ khalu avayavāḥ jātim anumāpaynati/ śiras-pāṇi-ādimat-ākāra-sāmānyena
336.05	gotvam vyajyate iti śiras-pāṇi-ādīnām vā avayavi-śeṣa-darśanena kadā
336.06	cit punar iyam ākṛtiḥ padārtha-tvam jahāti kva punar jahāti yatra ākṛti-vyakti^ā
336.07	jātiḥ na bhavati yathā mṛd-suvarṇa-rajatam iti ākṛti^i niyamaḥ
336.08	na jāti^i sarva-ākṛtiḥ jātim liṅgayati na punar sarvā jātiḥ ākṛti^ā
336.09	liṅgyate//
336.10	[2-2-69(70)]samāna-prasava-ātmikā jātiḥ
336.11	samāna-prasava-ātmikā jātiḥ/ yathā bhinnāni anekāni vastūni
336.12	itaretarataḥ anuvṛtti-pratyaya-hetu-tvena vyavatiṣṭḥante sā jātiḥ/ yathā imāni
336.13	kuṇḍāni iti anuvartante na imāni kuṇḍāni iti vyāvartante tad ekam sat-nimittam
336.14	anuvṛtti-kāraṇam yad sāmānya-viśeṣaḥ jātiḥ iti/ samāna-pratyaya-utpatti-
336.15	kāraṇam jātiḥ iti jāti^i niyamaḥ na samāna-pratyaya-utpatti^i jātim
336.16	antareṇa api dṛṣṭa-tvāt/ jātim antareṇa api samāna-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ/ yathā
336.17	pācaka-ākiṣu ādiṣu tasmāt vyavasthitam vyakti-ākṛti-jātyaḥ  padārthaḥ iti/ go-
336.18	ādiṣu anuvṛtti-pratyayaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ piṇḍa-vyariktāt liṅgāt bhavati iti viśeṣavat-
336.19	tvāt nīla-ādi-pratyaya-vat iti/ gotaḥ artha-antaram gotvam bhinna-pratyaya-viṣaya-
337.01	tvāt rūpa-sparśa-pratyaya-vat iti/ gotaḥ artha-antaram gotvam vyapadeśa-śabda-viṣaya-
337.02	tvāt caitra-aśva-vat go^aḥ gotva-anuvṛtti-pratyayā bhinna-nimittā viśeṣa-vat
337.03	rūpa-ādi-pratyaya-vat iti//
337.04	saṃśayasya pramāṇānām vicāraḥ tad-vyavasthitiḥ/
337.05	śabdasya tattva-prāmāṇyam padārthāḥ ca iha kīrtitāḥ//
337.06	iti - auddyotakare nyāyavārttike dvitīya-adhyāyasya
337.07	dvitīya-āhnikam//
337.08	samāptaḥ ca atra dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ//338.01	nyāyavārttike
338.02	tṛtīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ/
338.04	yad-viṣayam ca tattva-jñānam saṃsāra-nivṛtti-hetuḥ bhavati tad-ānantaryāt parīkṣyate/
338.05	tad ca ātma-ādi iti ātman^ḥ vicāryate/ kim punar asya vicāryam kim śarīra-indriya-
338.06	manas-buddhi-vyatiriktaḥ āho na iti na dharmi-aprasiddhi^aḥ vyatireka-avyatirekau
338.07	ātma-dharmau sati dharmiṇi dharmaḥ bhavati iti dharmin^ḥ ca atra aprasiddhaḥ na ca aprasiddhe
338.08	dharmiṇi dharmayoḥ vicāraḥ yuktaḥ tasmāt dharmin^ḥ eva tāvat pratipādyatām iti/
338.09	na icchā-ādi-sūtrāt ātma-prasiddhi^aḥ icchā-ādi-sūtre ātma-sadbhāvaḥ pratipāditaḥ
339.01	pratipādita-tvāt anantaraḥ vicāraḥ yuktaḥ iti/ avipratipatti^aḥ na kaḥ cit
339.02	vādin^ḥ ātma-sadbhāve vipratipadyate kim tu viśeṣe vipratipadyate śarīra-mātram
339.03	ātman^ḥ buddhi-ādyaḥ  saṅghātaḥ ātman^ḥ vyatiriktaḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ iyam ca
339.04	dharma-viśeṣa-vipratipattiḥ apratipanna-ātma-sattvānām na yuktā iti yuktaḥ dharma-vicāraḥ
339.05	asattva-pratipādaka-pramāṇa-asambhavāt ca/ na ca ātma-asattva-pratipādakam pramāṇam
339.06	asti iti ataḥ ca sadbhāve na vipratipadyate iti/ na na asti ajāta-tvāt iti eke na asti
339.07	ātman^ḥ ajāta-tvāt śaśa-viṣāṇa-vat iti tatra nāsti-ātman^ḥ iti pade tāvat vyāhanyete
339.08	nāsti-śabda-samānādhikaraṇa ayam ātma-śabdaḥ na ātmanaḥ asattvam pratipādayati
339.09	kim kāraṇam ātman^ḥ iti sattvam abhidhīyate nāsti iti tasya pratiṣedhaḥ yad ca
339.10	yatra pratiṣidhyate tad tasmāt anyatra asti yathā nāstinā samānādhikaraṇaḥ
339.11	ghaṭa-śabdaḥ na ghaṭa-abhāvam pratipādayitum śaknoti api tu deśa-kāla-viśeṣe
339.12	pratiṣedhati na asti ghaṭaḥ iti/ deśa-viśeṣe vā pratiṣedhaḥ gehe na asti iti
339.13	kāla-viśeṣe vā pratiṣedhaḥ idānīm na asti iti prāc na asti ūrdhvam na asti iti
339.14	sarvaḥ ca ayam pratiṣedhaḥ na anabhyupagata-ghaṭa-sattvasya yuktaḥ / tathā na asti ātman^ḥ iti
339.15	kim ayam deśa-viśeṣe pratiṣidhyate atha kāla-viśeṣe iti/ yadi tāvat deśa-
339.16	viśeṣe pratiṣiddhaḥ saḥ ātmani na yuktaḥ adeśa-tvāt ātmanaḥ na ca deśa-
339.17	viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt ātman^ḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ bhavati/ atha ayam bhavatām abhiprāyaḥ
339.18	śarīram ātman^ḥ na bhavati iti kasya vā śarīram ātman^ḥ yam prati pratiṣedhaḥ
339.19	śarīre na asti ātman^ḥ iti evam pratiṣedhaḥ iti cet kasya vā śarīre ātman^ḥ yam
339.20	prati pratiṣedhaḥ kva tarhi ayam ātman^ḥ na kva cit ātma^ḥ kim ayam na asti eva na
340.01	na asti viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt kā iyam vācas-yuktiḥ na ca śarīre na ca anyatra
340.02	na ca na asti eṣā eṣā vacas-yuktiḥ yad yathā-bhūtam tad tathā nirdiśyate na
340.03	ca ayam ātman^ḥ kva cit api iti tasmāt tathā eva nirdeśaḥ na ca kāla-viśeṣa-
340.04	pratiṣedhaḥ api yuktaḥ ātmani traikālyasya anabhivyakti^aḥ na hi
340.05	ātmani traikālyam abhivyajyate nitya-tvāt nityaḥ ātman^ḥ iti etad upariṣṭāt
340.06	pūrva-abhyasta-sūtre pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ/ tasmāt na kāla-viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ api
340.07	ātma-pratikṣepam ca kurvāṇena ātma-śabdasya viṣayaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ na hi ekam
340.08	padam nirathakam paśyāmaḥ/ atha api śarīra-ādi-viṣayam ātma-śabdam pratipadyethāḥ
340.09	evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ katham iti na asti ātma^ḥ iti vākyasya
340.10	tadānīm ayam arthaḥ bhavati śarīra-ādyaḥ  na santi iti/ atha yam bhavantaḥ
340.11	ātmānam kalpayanti saḥ na asti iti na vayam ātmānam kalpayāmaḥ kalpanā
340.12	hi nāma atathā-bhūtasya tathā-bhāvibhiḥ sāmānyāt tad-dharma-āropena tad-
340.13	pratyaya-viṣaya-tvam na ca ātmānam evam-bhūtam pratipadyāmahe yam bhavantaḥ ātmānam
340.14	kalpayantīti ca bruvāṇaḥ bhavān praṣṭavyaḥ jāyate katham vayam ātmānam kalpayāmaḥ
340.15	iti/ kim sattvena atha asattvena vā yadi sattvena kim asataḥ satā
340.16	sādharmyam yena kalpanā-viṣayaḥ ātman^ḥ ātma-sāmānyam ca anātmanaḥ bruvatā
340.17	ātma-abhyupagataḥ bhavati iti na hi asataḥ satā sāmānyam asti iti/ atha
340.18	śarīra-ādi-viṣayam ahaṅkāram ātmani kalpayitvā viparyasyati evam śarīra-
340.19	ādi-vyatirikta-ahaṅkāra-viṣaya-sattva-abhyupagamāt anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ atha
340.20	manyase eka-padasya na avaśyam arthena bhavitavyam iti yathā śūnyam tamas iti
341.01	tad na vyāghāta-anivṛtti^aḥ śūnya-śabdasya tāvat ayam arthaḥ yasya rakṣitṛ^ḥ dravyasya
341.02	na vidyate tad dravyam śvabhyaḥ hita-tvāt śūnyam iti ucyate tamas-śabdasya api anupalabdhi-
341.03	lakṣaṇa-prāptāni dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi viṣayaḥ yatra yatra asannidhiḥ tejasaḥ
341.04	tatra tatra dravya-ādi tamas-śabdena ucyate/ tamas-śabdaḥ ca anarthakaḥ iti
341.05	bruvāṇaḥ sva-siddhāntam bādhate caturṇām upādeya-rūpa-tvāt tamasaḥ iti tasmāt
341.06	na anarthakam padam iti//
341.07	na asti ātman^ḥ iti ca evam bruvāṇaḥ siddhāntam bādhate/ katham iti rūpam bhadantaḥ
341.08	na aham vedanā saṃskāraḥ vijñānam bhadantaḥ na aham iti evam etad bhikṣaḥ rūpam
341.09	na tvam vedanā saṃskāraḥ vijñānam vā na tvam iti/ te ete skandhāḥ rūpa-
341.10	ādyaḥ  aham-viṣaya-tvena pratiṣiddhāḥ viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ ca ayam na sāmānya-pratiṣedhaḥ
341.11	ātmānam ca anabhyupagacchatā sāmānyam eva pratiṣeddhavyam na aham na eva tvam
341.12	asi iti atha ekaśaḥ pratiṣedhena samudāya-viṣayaḥ ahaṅkāraḥ iti/ tathā api
341.13	rūpa-ādi-skandha-pañcaka-vyatirekena samudāyaḥ vaktavyaḥ ahaṅkāra-viṣayaḥ tad-vyatiriktam
341.14	ca samudāyam ahaṅkāra-viṣayam abhyupagacchatā saṃjñā bhidyate ātman^ḥ
341.15	samudāyaḥ iti atha avyatiriktam eka-pratyayaḥ na prāpnoti aham iti/ na hi
341.16	bahuṣu eka-śabdam paśyāmaḥ/ na ca rūpa-ādi-skandhāḥ ekaśaḥ samuditāḥ vā
341.17	ātman^ḥ iti ahaṅkāra-pratyayaḥ atasmin tad iti prāptaḥ/ bhavatu mithyā-pratyayaḥ
341.18	kim naḥ bādhyate iti katham na bādhyate yadā samyac-pratyaya-anukāriṇaḥ
341.19	mithyā-pratyayāḥ bhavanti na ca ātmānam anabhyupagacchatā tathāgata-darśanam arthavat-
342.01	tāyām vyavasthāpayitum śakyam na ca idam vacanam na asti sarva-abhisamaya-sūtre
342.02	abhidhānāt/ tasmāt na asti ātman^ḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ siddhāntam bādhate iti/
342.03	tathā bhāram vaḥ bhikṣu^āḥ deṣayiṣyāmi bhāra-hāram ca bhāraḥ pañca-skandhāḥ
342.04	bhāra-hāraḥ ca pudgalaḥ iti/ yaḥ ca ātman^ḥ na asti iti saḥ mithyā-dṛṣṭikaḥ bhavati
342.05	iti sūtram//
342.06	yaḥ api ayam hetuḥ ajāta-tvāt iti ayam api rāśi-antara-vyavaccheda-hetu-
342.07	tvāt viruddhaḥ jātam ajātam iti ca vastu-dharmau etau kim punar jātam kim
342.08	ca ajātam iti/ yasya kāraṇavataḥ sattā tad jātam yasy akāraṇavataḥ sattā
342.09	tad ajātam kim kāraṇam nañaḥ janma-pratiṣedha-viṣaya-tvāt ayam khalu nañ
342.10	pravatamānaḥ janmanaḥ pratiṣedham karoti na asya janman vidyate iti/
342.11	tad idam ajātam iti ātmanaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ na bhavati yathā anudakaḥ kamaṇḍaluḥ
342.12	iti/ atha ayam bhāvaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ eva ajātam iti tathā api pratijñā-
342.13	hetu^oḥ eka-tvam ajātam iti ca ayam dharmaḥ na ca dharmaḥ svatantraḥ yuktaḥ
342.14	anyatra samavāyāt yad-tantraḥ ayam dharmaḥ saḥ ātman^ḥ iti viruddhaḥ/ atha dharmaḥ
342.15	asti na dharmin^ḥ iti tathā api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ svatantrasya dharmasya adarśanāt/
342.16	kaḥ ca ayam arthaḥ ajāta-tvāt iti yadi ca ayam arthaḥ na asya janman iti tataḥ asiddham
342.17	janmavān ātman^ḥ kim punar ātmanaḥ janman nikāya-viśiṣṭābhiḥ śarīra-indriya-
342.18	buddhi-vedanābhiḥ apūrvābhiḥ abhisambandhaḥ/ atha ajātatvam akāraṇakatvam tathā na
342.19	kaḥ cit bādhyate viruddhaḥ ca hetuḥ akāraṇakasya nitya-tvāt asattvam sādhayataḥ
342.20	nityatām āpādyate iti viruddhaḥ hetuḥ pratijñā-hetu^oḥ ca avirodhaḥ pratijñāyāḥ
342.21	viṣayaḥ ja asti ātman^ḥ iti hetu^aḥ viṣayaḥ ātyantikaḥ sattā-sambandhaḥ tau etau
343.01	pratijñā-hetū asat-sat-viṣaya-tvāt viruddhau/ etena niruhetuka-tvāt janma-hetu-anupādānāt
343.02	akārya-tvāt akāraṇa-tvāt iti evam ādyaḥ  tulya-doṣāḥ iti pratyuktāḥ//
343.03	yad api idam ucyate śaśa-viṣāṇa-vat iti ayam api asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/
343.04	katham iti śaśa-viṣāṇa-śabdasya sambandha-viṣaya-tvāt sambandha-pratiṣedhaḥ na
343.05	viṣāṇa-pratiṣedhaḥ/ śaśa-viṣāṇa-sambandhaḥ udāharaṇam bhaviṣyati iti saḥ api
343.06	na yuktaḥ kadā cit viṣāṇena śaśasya sambandha-upapatti^aḥ/ loka-virodhaḥ iti
343.07	cet/ atha manyase yadi śaśe viṣāṇam asti nanu lokaḥ viruddhyate iti/
343.08	na viruddhyate kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-pratiṣedha-dvāreṇa laukika-pravṛtti^aḥ/ lokaḥ tāvat
343.09	kāryam kāraṇam vā śaśasya viṣāṇam na asti iti evam pratiṣedhati yathā go^aḥ viṣāṇasya
343.10	ca kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ na ca evam śaśasya viṣāṇasya ca kārya-kāraṇa-
343.11	bhāvaḥ/ na ca kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-pratiṣedhāt asattvam na hi yadi asya na kāryam na
343.12	kāraṇam tad na asti/ yathā devadattasya ghaṭaḥ iti/ idam ca śaśa-viṣāṇam
343.13	na asti iti bruvāṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ kim ayam sāmānya-pratiṣedhaḥ atha viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ
343.14	iti/ yadi sāmānya-pratiṣedhaḥ tad na yuktam aśakya-tvāt śaśasya viṣāṇam
343.15	na asti iti go-ādi-viṣāṇāni api śaśasya na santi iti prāptam etad ca
343.16	aśakyam na hi tāni na santi/ atha viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ kim cit viṣāṇam
343.17	śaśasya pratiṣiddhyate yasya śaśaḥ na kāryam tad ca śaśasya na kāraṇam
343.18	iti saḥ ayam kārya-kāraṇa-sambandhaḥ eva pratiṣidhyate/ kārya-kāraṇa-sambandhaḥ
343.19	ca anyatra dṛṣṭaḥ iha pratiṣidhyate iti na atyanta-asattva-pratipādane dṛṣṭāntaḥ
343.20	bhavati/ etena kha-puṣpa-ādi-asattvam vyākhyātam veditavyam//
344.01	na asti ātman^ḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ iti cet atra api pratijñā-ādi-doṣaḥ dṛṣṭānta-
344.02	doṣaḥ ca pūrvavat yad anupalabdhi^aḥ iti tad api ayuktam/ anupalabdhiḥ asiddhā
344.03	pratyakṣa-ādi-pramāṇa-viṣaya-tvāt ātmanaḥ pratyakṣena tāvat ātman^ḥ upalabhyate
344.04	katham liṅga-liṅgi-sambandha-smṛti-anapekṣam viṣaya-svabhāva-bheda-anuvidhāyi aham iti
344.05	vijñānam rūpa-ādi-jñāna-vat pratyakṣam yad ca api bhavān yukta-saṃśayam pratyakṣam
344.06	pratipadyate tasya kutaḥ pratyakṣa-tvam iti avaśyam bhavatā vijñānam eva liṅga-ādi-
344.07	sambandha-nirapekṣam sva-ātma-saṃvedyam pratipattavyam atha manyase asti ayam aham-
344.08	pratyayaḥ na punar asya ātman^ḥ viṣayaḥ hantaḥ tarhi nirdiśyatām viṣayaḥ/ rūpa-ādiḥ
344.09	viṣayaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase rūpa-ādyaḥ  eva ahaṅkārasya viṣayaḥ tathā
344.10	ca uktam ahaṅkāra-ālambana-utpatti-nimitta-tvāt ātman^ḥ iti ucyate iti tad na asattvāt
344.11	pratiṣedhāt ca/ pratiṣiddhaḥ ahaṅkāraḥ rūpa-ādiṣu rūpam na aham evam etad bhiṣaḥ rūpam
344.12	na tvam iti/ tasmāt rūpa-ādyaḥ  tāvat ahaṅkārasya viṣayāḥ na bhavanti, na ca
344.13	ayam ahaṅkāraḥ rūpa-ādiṣu kadā cit api dṛṣṭaḥ/ na hi bhavati kadā cit aham
344.14	rūpam aham vedanā-ādyaḥ  iti/ nanu bhavati aham gauraḥ aham kṛṣṇaḥ iti na
344.15	bhavati iti brūmaḥ/ katham na hi etasya draṣṭṛ^aḥ etad mama rūpam gauram etad aham
344.16	iti pratyayaḥ bhavati kevalam matup-lopam kṛtvā aham gauraḥ iti ṣaṣṭhī-artham
344.17	nirdiśati/ evam etad na tattvataḥ iti kutaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase matup-
344.18	lopāt ayam ṣaṣṭhī^ā artha-vyapadeśaḥ ṣaṣṭhī-artha-vyapadeśaḥ na tattvataḥ iti kaḥ hetuḥ iti mama-pratyaya-
344.19	samānādhikaraṇasya nirdeśāt yasmin arthe mama-pratyayaḥ asya bhavati tatra
344.20	eva ayam ahaṅkāraḥ api iti mama-pratyaya-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt gamyate matup-lopaḥ
345.01	iti/ mama-pratyaya-samānādhikaraṇaḥ ca ayam aham-pratyayaḥ anyatve dṛṣṭaḥ upakāraka-
345.02	tvāt upakārake vastuni mama-pratyaya-samānādhikaraṇaḥ aham-pratyayaḥ
345.03	dṛṣṭaḥ yaḥ ayam saḥ aham iti uktam ca atra rūpa-ādi-skandha^su aham-pratyayaḥ atasmin
345.04	tad iti pratyayaḥ iti/ tad evam aham-pratyaya-viṣaya-tvāt ātman^ḥ tāvat pratyakṣaḥ
345.05	anumānena api yathā ātman^ḥ upalabhyate tathā uktam icchā-ādi-sūtre iti/ āgamaḥ
345.06	api asti eva tāni etāni pramāṇāni trīṇi eka-viṣaya-tayā pratisandhīyamānāni
345.07	ātmānam pratipādayanti/ na ca pramāṇa-antaram vipratipatti-hetuḥ
345.08	asti tasmāt anupalabdhi^aḥ iti asiddhaḥ hetuḥ sandigdhaḥ ca anupalabdhi^aḥ tri-kāraṇa-
345.09	tvāt anupalabdhi-kāraṇāni trīṇi upalabdha-upalambhanam upalabhyam iti/
345.10	tatra iyam anupalabdhiḥ bhavantī kasya abhāvāt iti sandihyate/ yadi brūṣe
345.11	upalabhyasya abhāvāt iti nanu ayam pratijñā-arthaḥ eva/ na ca pratijñā pratijñām
345.12	sādayati iti anupalabdhiḥ ahetuḥ/ yā ca iyam anupalabdhiḥ ātma-asattva-pratipadanāya
345.13	upādīyate tasyāḥ kaḥ āśrayaḥ/ yadi ātmānam pratipadyase
345.14	vyāhatam bhavati āśrayaḥ ca na asti ca iti/ atha anāśrayaḥ kaḥ sādhana-arthaḥ na
345.15	hi anāśrayaḥ dharmaḥ sādhyam sādhayitum śaknoti/ atha anupalabdhiḥ api na asti
345.16	kaḥ sādhana-arthaḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ iti na hi asādhanam bhavitum arhati iti atha
345.17	kalpitasya anupalabdhiḥ dharmaḥ iti katham kalpitasya iti vācyam kim sattvena
345.18	atha asattvena yadi sattvena na anupalabdhiḥ tasya dharmaḥ na hi sthāṇu^aḥ
345.19	puruṣa-tvena kalpitasya sthāṇu-dharmāḥ pratyavabhāsante atha asattvena kalpitasya
345.20	anupalabdhiḥ dharmaḥ sidhyati anupalabdhiḥ dharmaḥ vṛthā tu kalpanā/ kim
346.01	kāraṇam kalpanāyāḥ atathā-bhāvāt/ kalpanā hi nāma atathā-bhāvaḥ
346.02	asattvena ca ātman^ḥ kalpitaḥ iti asattvena na asti asattvena cet na asti vṛthā
346.03	kalpanā/ kim artham ca ayam ātman^ḥ asattvena kalpyate yadi hetu-artham upapannaḥ
346.04	hetuḥ yad artham upāttaḥ saḥ punar arthaḥ na sidhyati na hi yaḥ puruṣe sthāṇu-
346.05	dharmān adhyāropya hetu-tvena apadiśet tasya asau sthāṇuḥ syāt iti/ tad
346.06	evam api anupalabdhiḥ api ahetuḥ/ rūpa-ādi-skandha-vācaka-śabda-viṣaya-vyatirikta-
346.07	viṣayaḥ ātma-śabdaḥ rūpa-ādi-śabda^bhyaḥ anyatve sati eka-śabda-tvāt ghaṭa-śabda-vat
346.08	iti/ etena aham-pratyayaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ iti cet
346.09	atra uktam yathā guṇa-vyatiriktaḥ guṇin^ḥ iti/ tamas-śabda-ādibhiḥ anaikāntikaḥ
346.10	iti cet/ atha manyase yathā rūpa-ādi-śabda^bhyaḥ anya-tve sati eka-pada-tve ca
346.11	tamas-śabdasya artha-antara-aviṣaya-tvam evam ātma-śabdasya api iti tad na virodhāt/
346.12	tamas-śabdaḥ nirviṣayaḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ siddhāntam bādhate/ upādāya-rūpa-
346.13	tvāt tamasaḥ iti na ca etad asmābhiḥ abhyupagamyate tamas-śabdaḥ nirviṣayaḥ iti
346.14	tamas-śabdasya saviṣaya-tve sūtra-vyāghātaḥ dravya-guṇa-karma-niṣpatti-vaidharmyāt
346.15	bhābhāvaḥ abhāvaḥ tamas iti/ na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt nirākṛta-tejas-sambandhīni
346.16	dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi tamas-śabdena abhidhīyante tasmāt na sūtra-vyāghātaḥ/ apare
346.17	tu jīvavat-śarīram nirātmaka-tvena pakṣayitvā sattvāt iti evam ādikam
346.18	hetum bruvate tad na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ nirātmakam iti kaḥ arthaḥ/ yadi
346.19	tāvat ātmanaḥ anupakārakam iti na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti na hi kim cit ātmanaḥ
346.20	anupakārakam asti/ atha ātma-pratiṣedham ātman^ḥ śarīram na bhavati kasya
347.01	ātman^ḥ śarīram uttara-pada-viṣaya-tvāt ca nisaḥ kim sātmakam iti vācyam
347.02	na hi asati uttara-pade nisaḥ prayogam paśyāmaḥ yathā nirmaśakam iti/ atha
347.03	śarīre ātman^ḥ pratiṣidhyate siddham sādhayati kasya vā śarīre ātman^ḥ
347.04	vidyate/ atha śarīram ātma-sambandhin na bhavati iti punar dṛṣṭāntaḥ na asti iti
347.05	sarve ca ete viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāḥ viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt ca sāmānyam abhyupagamyate
347.06	evam ca sati yad pratiṣeddhavyam tad anujñātam bhavati/ atha ātma-śabdaḥ
347.07	anitya-viṣayaḥ varṇa-ātmaka-tvāt tathā api nitya-śabdena anaikāntikam śarīra-
347.08	ādīnām ātma-śabda-tvāt siddha-sādhanam/ atha śarīra-ādi-vyatirikta-
347.09	viṣayaḥ ātma-śabdaḥ anitya-viṣayaḥ tathā api śarīra-ādi-vyatirikta-viṣaya-
347.10	abhyupagame virodhaḥ/ tad evam yathā yathā ātma-asattvam vicāryate tathā
347.11	tathā nyāyam na sahate iti//
347.12	para-arthāḥ cakṣus-ādyaḥ  saṅghāta-tvāt śayana-āsana-ādi-vat iti saṅghāta-antara-
347.13	arthāḥ iti cet/ atha manyase satyam saṅghātāḥ para-arthāḥ bhavanti te tu
347.14	saṅghāta-antara-arthāḥ yathā śayana-ādyaḥ  iti na saṅghātasya abhyupagame virodhāt
347.15	na hi rūpa-ādi-skandha-vyatiriktam saṅghātam bhavantaḥ pratipadyante na
347.16	ca asati saṅghāta-antare saṅghātānām saṅghāta-antara-artha-tā bhavati/ atha
347.17	pratipadyate vyāhatam bhavati aniṣṭa-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase na
347.18	mayā saṅghāta-antaram pratipadyate api tu asmin hetu^i sati idam aniṣṭam
347.19	bhavati para-arthasya saṅghāta-antara-avinābhāvi-tvāt/ yāvat para-artham bhavati
348.01	tad sarvam saṅghāta-antara-artham dṛṣṭam iti/ na virodhāt anabhyupagamāt ca/ na
348.02	hi kaḥ cit vyatirikta-ātma-vādinaḥ saṅghātaḥ saṅghātasya para-artham karoti/
348.03	tad-ubhaya-pakṣe saṃpratipanne na aniṣṭa-āpādanam dṛṣṭam iti virodhāt ca/ yadi
348.04	vivakṣita-artha-vyatirekena anumānasya avivakṣitam api viṣayaḥ nanu etasyām
348.05	kalpanāyām sarvam anumānam vyāhanyeta/ katham iti yad iṣṭam anumāna-tvena
348.06	tad api na anumānam prāpnoti/ yathā śabda-anitya-tve kṛtakatvam kim
348.07	kāraṇam yathā idam kṛtakatvam anityatvena avinābhūtam anityatvam sādhayati/
348.08	evam duḥkha-śūnya-anātmaka-tvam api sādhayati duḥkha-śūnya-anātmaka-
348.09	tva-vat aśrotra-grāhya-tvam api sādhayiṣyati tataḥ ca na śrotra-grāhyaḥ śabdaḥ
348.10	kṛtaka-tvāt ghaṭa-vat iti atha anumānena bādhyate ayam pratijñā-arthaḥ iti ataḥ
348.11	na hetuḥ/ nanu ayam api saṅghāta-antara-arthāḥ cakṣus-ādyaḥ  iti āgamena anumānena
348.12	ca bādhyate yad ca api bhavān saṅghāta-antaram pratipadyate tatra api anivṛttam
348.13	saṅghātatvam iti tena api saṅghāta-antara-artham bhavitavyam iti evam ca anavasthā/
348.14	na ca enām śaktaḥ kaḥ cit pratipādayitum iti yataḥ eṣā nivartate
348.15	tad asaṃhatam atha asaṃhatam na eva asti asaṃhata-anabhyupagame saṅghāta-anupapatti^aḥ
348.16	vyāghātaḥ na hi kadā cit asaṃhata-pratiṣedhe saṅghātaḥ sidhyati/
348.17	atha anumānam na eva asti iti paraḥ pratipadyate saḥ katham na pratipadyate iti
348.18	praṣṭavyaḥ yadi anumāna-bādita-tvāt anumānam na asti atinyāyaḥ anumānena ca
349.01	bādhyate anumānam ca na asti iti atīndriya-artha-adhigatiḥ ca katham yadi
349.02	anumānam na asti katham atīndriya-arthaḥ gamyate/ āgamāt atīndriya-artha-adhigatiḥ
349.03	bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam āgama-vyatirikta-viṣaya-tvāt kasmāt anumānasya
349.04	āgama-vyatiriktaḥ viṣayaḥ yathā stanayitnu-śabdasya hetu^i, na hi
349.05	tatra pratyakṣam na āgamaḥ iti tad evam ātman^ḥ asti iti samadhigatam tasmin
349.06	vyapadeśasya ubhayathā siddhi^aḥ saṃśayaḥ kaḥ punar ayam vyapadeśaḥ kriyā-karaṇayoḥ
349.07	kartṛ^ā sambandha-abhidhānam vyapadeśaḥ darśanam kriyā karaṇam cakṣus karaṇasya
349.08	kriyāyāḥ kartṛ^ā sambandhaḥ cakṣus^ā paśyati iti/ saḥ ca ayam vyapadeśaḥ dvedhā
349.09	bhavati avayavena samudāyasya yathā mūlaiḥ vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati iti atra samudāya-
349.10	śabdena avayavin^ḥ vṛkṣaḥ abhidhīyate iti/ ekadeśena ca ekadeśa-antarāṇām
349.11	yad bhinna-jātīyaiḥ ārabhyate tatra hi ekadeśa-darśanāt ekadeśa-antarāṇi pratipadyante
349.12	tatra api ca vyapadeśaḥ stambhaiḥ prāsādaḥ dhriyate iti anyena
349.13	ca anyasya vyapadeśaḥ/ paraśunā vṛścati iti paraśuḥ karaṇam vṛścatiḥ
349.14	kriyā tābhyām kartṛ^aḥ abhisambandhaḥ paraśunā vṛścati iti/ asti ca ayam
349.15	iha api vyapadeśaḥ cakṣus^ā paśyati iti/ kim anyena anyasya vyapadeśaḥ
349.16	atha vā avayavena api avayavinaḥ atha ekadeśena ekadeśa-antarāṇām it kriyā-
349.17	karaṇa-karmaṇām vā kartṛ^ā sambandhaḥ vyapadeśaḥ cakṣus^ā vṛkṣam paśyati iti
349.18	kriyā-karaṇa-karmaṇām kartṛ^ā sambandhaḥ asti ca ayam iha api vyapadeśaḥ
349.19	śarīrena sukha-duḥkham anubhavati tatra na jñāyate kim tattvam iti
349.20	anyena ayam anyasya vyapadeśaḥ iti tattvam// katham
350.01	[3-1-1]darśana-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt
350.02	darśana-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt/ dṛśyate anena iti darśanam cakṣus
350.03	spṛśati anena ātman^ḥ spraṣṭavyam artham iti sparśanam tvac-indriyam ucyate/
350.04	darśanena dṛṣṭam artham sparśanena pratyabhijānāti yam adrākṣam tam spṛśāmi
350.05	yam asprākṣam tam paśyāmi iti eka-viṣayau etau pratyayau pratisandhīyete pratisandhānam
350.06	ca nāma smṛti^ā saha eka-viṣaya-tvam/ darśana-sparśana-pratyayayoḥ ghaṭa-ādi-
350.07	viṣaya-tvāt asambandhaḥ iti cet/ atha manyase yau etau darśana-sparśana-pratyayau
350.08	tau ghaṭa-ādi-viṣayau na vyatirekam ātmanaḥ vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt gamayate
350.09	iti/ na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt/ darśana-sparśana-pratyayau eka-kartṛkau iti
350.10	sūtra-arthaḥ/ yadi ca anuṣaktam guṇa-vyatirekam api eka-viṣaya-tvena sādhayataḥ na
350.11	kaḥ cit doṣaḥ/ guṇa-vyatirekaḥ asiddhaḥ iti cet uktam etad yathā guṇa-
350.12	vyatiriktaḥ guṇin^ḥ iti/ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt pratisandhānam iti cet
350.13	ukta-uttaram etad icchā-ādi-sūtre iti/ akārya-kāraṇa-bhūtānām ca pratisandhāne
350.14	anekāntaḥ bhāvasya bhavitṛ-apekṣa-tvāt vā iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ dṛṣṭiḥ
350.15	darśanam spṛṣṭiḥ sparśanam iti bhāva-rūpau etau pratyayau bhavitṛ^am
350.16	pratipādayataḥ/ na hi bhāvam anādhāram paśyāmaḥ utpattivat karma-
350.17	ādhāraḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam karmaṇi asati darśanāt asati api
351.01	karmaṇi smṛti^aḥ bhāvaḥ bhavati iti/ na ca vinaṣṭam karman bhāvasya ādhāraḥ
351.02	yuktaḥ tataḥ yad-ādhāraḥ bhāvaḥ saḥ ātman^ḥ/ darśana-sparśana-grahaṇayoḥ rūpa-ādi-
351.03	viṣaya-tvāt na ghaṭa-ādi-viṣaya-tvam na rūpa-sparśa-anupalabdhi^i tad-viśiṣṭa-
351.04	pratyaya-darśanāt yadā ayam anupalabhyamāna-rūpa-ādikam vastu upalabhate tadā asya
351.05	tad-viśiṣṭaḥ pratyayaḥ ūpajāyate yathā nīla-ādi-uparisthita-sphaṭike pratyayaḥ
351.06	rūpa-ādi-anupalabdhi^i bhavati rātri^i ca valākāyām śukla-ādi-rūpa-
351.07	agrahaṇāt pakṣi-pratyayaḥ tasmāt rūpa-sparśa-vyatirikte ghaṭa-pratyayaḥ pratisandhānāt ca
351.08	vyatirikta-nimitta-tvam na hi nānā-viṣayau pratyayau pratisandhīyete
351.09	asti ca pratisandhānam yam adrākṣam tam spṛśāmi iti yam asprākṣam tam paśyāmi
351.10	iti tasmāt eka-viṣaya-tvam saṅghāta-kartṛkau darśana-sparśana-viṣayau pratyayau
351.11	bhaviṣyate iti na yuktam saṅghātasya nānātva-abādhanāt/ saṅghātaḥ
351.12	saṃhanyamāna-tantraḥ bhavati iti saṃhanyamānam ca anekam anekam ca pratisandhāna-
351.13	pratyaya-kāraṇam na yuktam nānā-kartṛkāṇām nānā-viṣayāṇām ca
351.14	pratyayānām apratisandhānāt/ eka-indriya-pratyayau iti cet/ atha manyase
351.15	yad eva indriyam darśana-pratyayasya kāraṇam tad eva sparśana-pratyayasya api iti/
351.16	tad na aviṣaya-tvāt/ cakṣus khalu sva-viṣayam pratisandhātum arhati iti
351.17	yam adrākṣam saḥ eva ayam arthaḥ iti na punar sparśam indriya-antara-viṣaya-tvāt
351.18	iti/ indriya-antara-vaiyarthyam vā ekaikam eva indriyam aneka-viṣaya-grahaṇam pratisandhatte
351.19	indriya-antara-utpādaḥ vyarthaḥ//
352.01	[3-1-2]na viṣaya-vyavasthānāt
352.02	na viṣaya-vyavasthānāt cetanāni indriyāṇi viṣaya-vyavasthānāt
352.03	viṣaya-vyavasthānāt iti asya ayam arthaḥ karaṇa-viṣaya-niyamāt iti/ sati
352.04	cakṣus^i rūpa-grahaṇam bhavati asati na bhavati/ yad ca yasmin sati
352.05	bhavati asatina bhavati tasya tad/ sati ca cakṣus^i rūpa-grahaṇam bhavati
352.06	tasmāt rūpa-grahaṇam cakṣus^aḥ/ evam śeṣa^su api/ evam sati kim anyena cetanena
352.07	iti cet na sandigdha-tvāt ahetuḥ/ kim ayam indriyāṇām viṣaya-niyamaḥ
352.08	cetana-tvāt āho cetana-upakaraṇa-tvāt iti sandihyate/ cetana-upakaraṇa-
352.09	tve api sati indriyāṇām etad bhavitum arhati yathā pradīpasya sadbhāve rūpa-grahaṇam
352.10	bhavati asati na bhavati/ na ca rūpa-grahaṇam pradīpasya viparyaya-prasādhakaḥ
352.11	ca ayam hetuḥ viṣaya-vyavasthānāt iti/  viparyaya-sādhaka-tva-jñāpana-artham ca
352.12	tad-vyavasthānāt eva ātma-sadbhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ iti sūtram//
352.13	[3-1-3]tad-vyavasthānāt eva ātma-sadbhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ
352.14	yasmāt eva etāni indriyāṇi vyavasthita-viṣayāṇi ataḥ eva avyavasthita-viṣayena
352.15	anyena bhavitavyam/ yadi hi kim cit indriyam aneka-artha-grāhya-vyavasthita-
352.16	viṣayam syāt iti kaḥ tataḥ anyam cetanam anumātum śaknuyāt caitanyam tu
352.17	ātmanaḥ svātantrye sati avyavasthānāt cakṣus-ādi-vat iti na acetanaḥ ātman^ḥ
352.18	asvātantrya-prasaṅgāt cakṣus-ādi-vat iti/ anvaya-abhāvāt ayuktam iti cet atha
352.19	manyase ātmanaḥ caitanya-sādhanasya avyavasthānasya na anvayaḥ asti na ca anvayam
353.01	antareṇa hetuḥ siddhyati iti na viparyaya-sambandhasya avyabhicārāt na ayam
353.02	anvayin^ḥ hetuḥ api tu vyatirekin^ḥ hetuḥ tatra ca vyatireka-sambandha-
353.03	avyabhicāraḥ sāmarthyam/ yad ca acetanam tad sarvam vyavasthita-viṣayam dṛṣṭam
353.04	iti/ pṛthivī-ādi-nitya-tva-sādhane gandhavat-tva-ādi-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet atha
353.05	manyase yadi vyatirekin^ḥ hetuḥ bhavati anvayam antareṇa atha pṛthivī-ādi-nitya-
353.06	tve sādhye gandhavat-tvam kasmāt na hetuḥ/ na hetuḥ ubhaya-vyāvṛtti^aḥ yasmāt gandhavat-
353.07	tvam nityāt ca anityāt ca vyāvartate na punar avyavasthānam evam tasmāt avyavasthānam
353.08	hetuḥ na gandhavat-tvam iti//
353.09	itaḥ ca śarīra-ādi-vyatiriktaḥ ātman^ḥ na śarīra-ādi-saṅghāta-mātram//
353.10	[3-1-4]śarīra-dāhe pātaka-abhāvāt
353.11	śarīra-dāhe pātaka-abhāvāt/ śarīra-grahaṇena śarīra-indriya-buddhi-vedanā-
353.12	saṅghātaḥ prāṇi-bhūtaḥ gṛhyate/ śarīram prāṇi-bhūtam dahataḥ prāṇi-hiṃsā-
353.13	kṛtam pāpam pātakam iti ucyate tasya abhāvaḥ akartṛ^aḥ phalena sambandhaḥ kartṛ^aḥ
353.14	ca anabhisambandhaḥ iti/ kasmāt anabhyupagata-artha-antara-ātmanaḥ anyaḥ śarīra-
353.15	indriya-buddhi-vedanā-saṃghātaḥ kartṛ^ḥ anyaḥ ca tad-phala-sambhoktṛ^ḥ iti/ tad evam
353.16	akṛta-kṛta-abhyāgama-vināśa-doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ aniṣṭaḥ ca tasmāt kartṛ^ḥ bhoktṛ^ḥ
353.17	ca yaḥ saḥ ātman^ḥ iti/ tad idam sūtram yasya ātman^ḥ na asti tasya ayam doṣaḥ
353.18	iti doṣa-pratipādana-artham na sādhana-artham iti hetu-phala-bhāvāt smṛtivat-
353.19	kartṛ-bhoktṛ-vyavahāraḥ yathā hetu-phala-bhāvena vyavasthitāyām santati^i
354.01	yasyām eva bhāvanā upajāyate tasyām eva santati^i smṛtiḥ bhavati iti evam
354.02	yad kāya-citta-santāna-prabhavam karman tad kāya-citta-santāna-prabhavam phalam iti
354.03	na asti akṛta-abhyāgama-kṛta-nāśa-prasaṅgaḥ/ saḥ katham syāt iti yadi anyasyām santati^i
354.04	kṛtam karman anyasyām santati^i phalam dadyāt yadi ca bhavān evam artham
354.05	pratipādayitum śaknoti yaḥ eva karoti saḥ eva bhuṅkte iti ubhayam ca na asti
354.06	tasmāt akṛta-abhyāgama-ādiḥ adoṣaḥ na ukta-uttara-tvāt na santānaḥ nānātvam
354.07	bādhate iti ukta-uttaram etad/ yad punar etad ekasmin adarśanāt iti atra apu uktam
354.08	eka-nimittānām pratyayānām pratisandhānāt iti/ śali-ādi-bīja-vat
354.09	iti cet atha manyase yathā śāli-bījāt aṅkuraḥ upajāyate śāli-
354.10	bīja-vimada avamarda-uttara-kālam nāla-kāṇḍa-ādi-bhāvena bhūta-anugraha-vaśāt śāli-
354.11	bījam prādurbhavati na ca tatra ekam nimittam kim cit anubaddham asti atha ca
354.12	niyamaḥ śali-bīja-pūrvāt aṅkurāt śāli-bījam evam hetu-phala-bhāvena
354.13	vyavasthitāyām santati^i āntare cetasi pariṇāma-viśeṣa-prāptāt karmaṇaḥ
354.14	phalam iti/ tad na bīja-avayava-anuvṛtti^aḥ śāli-bīja-vat iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
354.15	tatra api ye bīja-avayavāḥ te pūrva-vyūha-parityāgena vyāha-antaram āpadyante
354.16	vyūha-antara-āpatti^i ca pṛthivī-dhātuḥ ap-dhātunā saṃgṛhītaḥ āntarena tejasā
354.17	pacyamānaḥ rasa-dravyam nirvartayati saḥ rasaḥ pūrva-avayava-sahitaḥ aṅkura-ādi-bhāvam
354.18	āpadyate, tasmāt bīja-avayava-anuvṛtti^aḥ asiddham idam ucyate yathā śāli-
354.19	bījāt vinaṣṭāt aṅkuraḥ utpadyate atha ca pratisandhānam iti paramāṇu-avastha-
354.20	tvāt bīja-avayavānām yathā-ukta-doṣa-āpattiḥ iti cet atha manyase atha api
355.01	bīja-avayavāḥ pūrva-vyūham parityajanti vyūha-antaram ca āpadyante tasya api
355.02	paramāṇu-avastha-tvāt bījasya paramāṇu-mātram śiṣyate iti/ na ca paramāṇuṣu
355.03	apara-jāti-bhedaḥ vidyate na ca yava-bīja-śāli-bīja-paramāṇūnām kaḥ cit
355.04	viśeṣaḥ yathā anupajāta-viśeṣāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-niyamāt
355.05	tad-pūrvaka-tayā śāli-aṅkuram abhinirvarttayanti tathā mama api kārya-kāraṇa-
355.06	bhāvāt niyamaḥ iti/ na anabhyupagamāt paramāṇu-avasthāni bījāni
355.07	bhavanti iti etad na pratipadyāmahe sarva-avastha-upalabdhi^aḥ yasmāt śāli-ādi-bījam
355.08	ucchūna-avasthām ādim kṛtvā yāvat upāntyam śāli-bīja-kāryam tāvat na kadā
355.09	cit paramāṇu-avastham bhavati yadi tu syāt kadā cit na upalabhyeta yadi
355.10	tarhi āparamāṇu^aḥ vibhāgaḥ na asti kalpa-ādi^i utpattiḥ na syāt paramāṇu-avastha-
355.11	tvāt jagataḥ paramāṇu-avasthe ca jagati na śāli-ādi-jāti-vyaktiḥ asti iti
355.12	punar-utpāde jāti-vyakti-hetu^āḥ paramāṇu^āḥ eva iti abhyupagantavyam/ yathā
355.13	ca kalpa-ādi^i evam paramāṇu-avasthe bīje api iti/ na anena eva ukta-uttara-tvāt/
355.14	anena eva sarva-avastha-upalabdhi^aḥ iti ukta-uttaram etad/ sarga-ādi^i śāli-jāti-
355.15	vyakti-hetuḥ adṛṣṭa-viśeṣaḥ yena adṛṣṭena prāṇinām upabhogāya śāli-ādyaḥ
355.16	nirvartyante tam adṛṣṭa-viśeṣam apekṣamāṇaḥ īśvaraḥ tān tān paramāṇūn tathā
355.17	tathā saṅghātayati yathā yathā śāli-ādi-jāti-vyaktiḥ iti/ etena
355.18	ghaṭa-ādi-pākaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ na paramāṇu-antaḥ vināśaḥ sarva-avastha-upalabdhi^aḥ
355.19	iti/ āmadhyāt pāka-anupapattiḥ iti cet atha manyase yadi
355.20	kārya-kāraṇa-dravye pacyete āmadhyāt pākaḥ na prāpnoti pācakena tejasā
356.01	aprāpta-tvāt na apratibandhāt bhavet api eṣaḥ doṣaḥ yadi tejaḥ anupraveśam avayavin^ḥ
356.02	pratibadhnāti saḥ tu na pratibadhnāti apratibaddham tejas anupraviśati
356.03	iti na pratibadhnāti iti na hetuḥ asti iti, na na asti parisrava-ādimataḥ
356.04	ghaṭasya upalabdhi^aḥ yasmāt parisrava-ādimataḥ ghaṭasya dravatva-pratibandhaḥ na
356.05	dṛṣṭaḥ apratibandhena dravatvam parisrutim karoti tad ca pratyakṣataḥ upalabhyate/
356.06	yadi ca avayava-anupraveśaḥ dravyasya vināśakaḥ syāt na parisrava-ādimān
356.07	ghaṭaḥ upalabhyeta parisravamāṇa-tvāt bhājana-gatānām apām avasthānam na syāt
356.08	vināśe ca kārya-dravyāṇām vyavahita-avyavahitayoḥ tulya-kāla-upalabdhi-
356.09	prasaṅgaḥ yadi ca anupraviśya dravyam dravya-antaram vyatibhinatti tena cākṣuṣasya
356.10	raśmi^aḥ anupraveśāt sphaṭika-ādi-vinaṣṭam iti vyavahite ca avyavahite ca
356.11	tulya-upalabdhiḥ prāpnoti/ tasmāt śāli-bījam adṛṣṭāntaḥ yasya ca pūrva-
356.12	skandha-nirodha-samakālāni skandha-antarāṇi prādurbhavanti tasya teṣām
356.13	skandha-antarāṇām na karma-nimittaḥ sargaḥ prāpnoti/ akarma-nimittena
356.14	sattva-sargena śubha-aśubha-prāpti-arthā kriyā na prāpnoti sukhin^ḥ syām iti tad-
356.15	kriyā iti cet atha manyase svayam karmāṇi karoti katham aham sukhin^ḥ syām
356.16	duḥkhin^ḥ na syām iti/ tad na ananubhūta-tvāt/ yena sukham anubhūtam sukha-sādhana-
356.17	sambandha-anusmṛtiḥ ca yasya saḥ tad-sādhanam upādatte yasya punar pratikṣaṇam
356.18	pradhvaṃsinaḥ saṃskārāḥ tasya sukha-sādhana-sambandha-anusmṛti^aḥ abhāvāt sukhin^ḥ
356.19	syām iti kriyā-anupapattiḥ mokṣa-arthaḥ ca prayāsaḥ na upapadyate ayatna-
356.20	siddha-tvāt jāta-mātraḥ eva ayam mucyate iti mukti-artham brahmacarya-vāsaḥ vyarthaḥ
357.01	paripṛcchā cana prāpnoti/ tathāgatena bhikṣu^āḥ paripṛchyante kaccit yūyam
357.02	mayā vinītāḥ iti te ca āhuḥ vinītāḥ sma iti/ atīta-anāgata-
357.03	vartamānānām ca anupakārya-tvāt ye tāvat anāgatāḥ saṃskāryāḥ te tāvat
357.04	asattvāt na upakriyante ye api atītāḥ te api evam ye tu vartamānāḥ teṣu saṃskāra-
357.05	ādhānam aśakyam vartamāna-lakṣaṇasya anupakārya-tvāt na hi tasya avasthāna-
357.06	kālaḥ asti yasmin upakriyā syāt/ saṃskārya-saṃskārakayoḥ saha utpādāt
357.07	yuktam idam iti cet na saṃskārya-saṃskārakayoḥ saha utpāde niyama-
357.08	anupapattiḥ iti/ idam saṃskārakam idam saṃskāryam iti niyamaḥ
357.09	na upapadyate atha saṃskārakam apekṣamāṇam saṃskāryam vilakṣaṇa-kāryam karoti
357.10	vilakṣaṇa-kārya-utpādaḥ ca saṃskāraḥ iti ucyate/ na saṃskārya-anabhidhānāt
357.11	idam saṃskārakam idam saṃskāryam iti etad katham na hi yaḥ yasya viśeṣam
357.12	na ādatte saḥ tad-saṃskārakaḥ tad-saṃskāryam iti ca śakyam vyapadeṣṭum atha
357.13	api upadeśena saṃskārāṇām anāgatānām anutpattiḥ kriyate/ na anāgatānām
357.14	anutpatti^aḥ sattvāt/ yā asau anāgatānām anutpattiḥ saṃskārāṇām na asau
357.15	kadā cit na asti iti kim tasyāḥ kriyate tasmāt mukti-arthaḥ brahmacarya-vāsaḥ
357.16	iti anupapannam/ tad evam anabhyupagata-artha-antara-ātmanaḥ doṣaḥ iti//
357.17	[3-1-5]tad-abhāvaḥ sātmaka-pradāhe api tad-nitya-tvāt
357.18	tad-abhāvaḥ sātmaka-pradāhe api tad-nitya-tvāt/ yasya ātman^ḥ nityaḥ
357.19	tasya api hiṃsā-phale na prāpnutaḥ hiṃsā tāvat na prāpnoti nitya-tvāt
358.01	phalam api na ātmanaḥ yuktam anupakārya-tvāt asya ca arthasya jñāpikām
358.02	kārikām udāharanti//
358.03	varṣā-tapābhyām kim vyoman^aḥ carmaṇi asti tayoḥ bhayam/
358.04	carma-upamaḥ cet saḥ anityaḥ kha-tulyaḥ cet asat-phalaḥ//
358.05	tad evam ekasmin mate hiṃsā viphalā aparasmin tu pakṣe anupapannā
358.06	niḥphalā ca iti iyam ca hiṃsā sāṃkhya-pakṣe sambhavati buddhi^aḥ pariṇāmi-
358.07	tvāt kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ pariṇāmi-tvāt ucchidyate saḥ eva ca tad-phalāni
358.09	avasthām na jahāti iti tatra phalam na iṣyate tathā buddhiḥ api pūrva-avasthām na
358.10	jahāti nitya-tvāt iti/ atha manyase pūrva-avasthām jahāti iti nityatvam
358.11	viruddhyate pūrva-avasthā-parityāgena buddhi-nitya-tvam viruddhyate/ atha api avasthā
358.12	bhidyate iti manyase tathā api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ avasthābhyaḥ avāsthāvataḥ ananya-
358.13	tvāt na hi avasthābhyaḥ avasthāvān anyaḥ iti avasthā-bhede avasthāvān
358.14	bhidyate iti/ yaḥ eva bauddhasya pratikṣaṇam dhvaṃsiṣu saṃskāra^su doṣaḥ saḥ
358.15	eva pratikṣaṇa-pariṇāmiṣu api iti/ ṛju-vakratā-vat iti cet atha manyase
358.16	ṛju-vakratā-ādi-bhede yathā aṅguli-dravyam na bhidyate na ca aṅgulītaḥ anye te
358.17	tathā avasthā-bhede buddhi^aḥ abhedah iti na anya-tvāt/ anye ṛjuvakrate/ yuktam ca
358.18	yad anya-bhede anya-abhedaḥ iti na punar avasthābhyaḥ atra avasthāvataḥ anya-tvam
358.19	tasmāt asamaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ kim punar vakratvam ṛjutvam vā guṇaḥ ākuñcana-jam
358.20	vakratvam prasāraṇa-jam ṛjutvam guṇāḥ ca guṇinaḥ artha-antaram iti pratipāditam
359.01	etad/ buddhi^aḥ api evam iti cet atha manayse buddhi^aḥ api ye te avasthe
359.02	bhidyete tayoḥ bhede buddhiḥ na bhidyate iti (anya-avasthātaḥ buddhiḥ) tad na
359.03	siddhānta-virodhāt na bhavatām pakṣe dharma-dharmiṇoḥ bhedaḥ iti bhedam ca abhyupagacchatā
359.04	siddhāntaḥ tyaktaḥ bhavati/ tasmāt yathā ātma-nitya-tve sukha-duḥkha-
359.05	anupabhogaḥ doṣaḥ iti na tatra sukha-duḥkhe kalpyete iti tathā buddhi-
359.06	nitya-tvāt buddhi^i api na kalpanīye samānam nityatvam iti/ atha
359.07	ātmanaḥ nityasya puṇya-pāpābhyām kim kriyate sukha-duḥkhe tābhyām kim kriyate
359.08	pratyayaḥ ye sukha-duḥkhe ātmani tad-viṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ ātmani bhavati
359.09	iti etāvat kriyate saḥ ca pratyayaḥ bhogaḥ/ yad punar etad varṣā-tapābhyām kim
359.10	vyoman^aḥ iti yad eva carmaṇaḥ varṣā-tapābhyām kriyate tad-vyoman^aḥ/ atha
359.11	carmaṇaḥ kim sambandhaḥ yathā carman varṣā-tapābhyām sambaddhyate tathā vyaman api
359.12	varṣā-tapa-sambandhāt carma-vat anityam vyoman iti cet atha manyase yathā carman
359.13	varṣā-tapābhyām sambandhāt vikriyate tathā vyoman varṣa-tapa-sambandhāt vikāriṣyate
359.14	iti cet na anekāntāt varṣā-tapa-sambandhin^ḥ paramāṇuḥ atha ca nityaḥ
359.15	iti anekāntaḥ iti/ carmaṇaḥ ca anityatvam kim varṣā-tapa-sambandhāt atha kāraṇa-
359.16	vibhāgāt atha āśraya-vināśāt iti/ vayam tu brūmaḥ kāraṇavataḥ tad-vibhāgāt
359.17	yuktaḥ vināśaḥ na puanr ākāśasya kāraṇam asti iti ubhaya-abhāvaḥ iti
359.18	tadvatām ca avasthānāt na sambandhaḥ nāśa-hetuḥ yadi ca ayam sambandhah nāśa-hetuḥ
359.19	abhaviṣyat na tadvatām avasthānam abhaviṣyat avatiṣṭhante tu bhāvāḥ yāvat na
360.01	vināśa-kāraṇa-sannipātaḥ iti/ atha manyase varṣā-tapābhyām carmaṇaḥ
360.02	saṅkoca-vikāśau bhavataḥ na tu ākāśasya iti tad na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ
360.03	saṅkoca-vikāśau carmaṇaḥ iti kaḥ arthaḥ kim tāvat alpatva-mahattve uta
360.04	saṃyoga-vibhāgau iti tad na tāvat saṅkoca-vikāśau alpatva-mahattve yāvat-dravya-
360.05	bhāvi-tvāt parimāṇasya yāvat-dravyam hi parimāṇam tad-dravye sati na
360.06	nivartate iti/ atha carmaṇaḥ saṅkoca-vikāśau saṃyoga-vibhāgau iti
360.07	tad api na yuktam eka-tvāt/ na hi ekasya saṃyoga-vibhāgau sambhavataḥ iti
360.08	katham tarhi idam ucyate carmaṇaḥ saṅkocaḥ carmaṇaḥ vikāśaḥ iti carma-
360.09	kāraṇānām avinaśyat-kāryāṇām avayavānām ātapa-sambandhāt parasparena yāḥ
360.10	prāptyaḥ  ārabdha-kāryāṇām dravya-antara-anārambhikāḥ tāḥ itaretara-upaśleṣena
360.11	avayava^su vartamānāḥ tad-eka-artha-samavāyini carmaṇi upacaryante tad-apekṣam ca idam
360.12	ucyate carmaṇaḥ saṅkocaḥ na punar carman saṅkucati iti/ etena tad-avayavānām
360.13	eva udaka-sambandha-apekṣāṇām avinaśyat-kāryāṇām parasparataḥ vibhāgaḥ
360.14	vikāśaḥ iti/ vikriyāyām ca dṛṣṭānta-abhāvāt tad eva vastu avinaśyat-vikriyām
360.15	āpadyate iti na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti/ vikriyā tu padārtha-antara-
360.16	utpādaḥ yathā pūrva-rūpa-ādi-nivṛtti^i rūpa-antara-utpāde vikṛtaḥ ghaṭaḥ iti/
360.17	yadi evam-bhūtaḥ vikāraḥ ātmani api cakāsti tatra api sukha-nivṛtti^i
360.18	duḥkha-prādurbhāvaḥ iti sukha-pratyaya-nivṛtti^i duḥkha-pratyayaḥ iti tasmāt
360.19	nityasya ātmanaḥ sukha-duḥkha-upabhogaḥ iti siddham/  nitya-tvāt ātmanaḥ
360.20	hiṃsā-doṣaḥ aparihāryaḥ na anyathā tad-upapatti^aḥ ātma-nitya-tve api anyathā
360.21	hiṃsā bhavati asya arthasya jñāpanāya na kārya-āśraya-kartṛ-vadhāt iti
360.22	sūtram//
361.01	[3-1-6]na kārya-āśraya-kartṛ-vadhāt
361.02	na brūmaḥ nityasya sattvasya vadhaḥ hiṃsā iti api tu anucchitti-
361.03	dharmakasya sattvasya yad śarīram yāni ca sukha-saṃvitti-sādhanāni
361.04	indriyāṇi asādhāraṇāni teṣām pīḍā vaikalyam vā pramāpaṇam vā hiṃsā iti/
361.05	kutaḥ etad pratipattavyam hiṃsāyāḥ phala-upabhogasya ca ubhaya-pakṣa-
361.06	sampratipanna-tvāt hiṃsā-phala-upabhogaḥ ca ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannaḥ sā iyam hiṃsā
361.07	ekasmin pakṣe sambhavati yasya tāvat pratikṣaṇa-dhvaṃsinaḥ saṃskārāḥ tasya
361.08	kim hiṃsyate nirhetuka-tvāt vināśasya kasya vyāpāraḥ asti/ atha
361.09	vilakṣaṇa-utpatti-nimitta-tvena vyavatiṣṭhamānaḥ paraḥ hinasti iti ucyate/
361.10	nanu upacāraḥ bhavati na bhavataḥ pradhānam asti yena śarīra-utpādakaḥ upacaryate,
361.11	na samāna-tvāt bhavatām api pakṣe na pradhānam hiṃsyate iti
361.12	satyam mama api kārya-āśraya-kartṛ-vadhaḥ hiṃsā iti tulyam/ ayam tu viśeṣaḥ
361.13	yataḥ ekasmin pakṣe yaḥ eva ayam ātman^ḥ śarīra-ādi-sādhanaḥ karman
361.14	karoti saḥ eva tad-phalam upabhuṅkte iti akṛta-abhyāgama-doṣaḥ na asti/
361.15	bhavatām akṛta-abhyāgamaḥ kṛta-praṇāśaḥ ca iti pariśeṣāt ātma-nitya-tve hiṃsā
361.16	iti vyavasthitam tatra kārya-āśraya-śabdena śarīram ucyate tad-nimitta-
361.17	tvāt upabhogasya iti kartṛṇī indriyāṇi tad-sādhana-tvāt atha vā samānādhikraṇa-
361.18	samāsāt śarīram eva kārya-āśraya-kartṛ-śabdābhyām ucyate iti kartṛ^ḥ
361.19	bhoktṛ^ḥ ca ātman/ kim punar idam kartṛtvam bhoktṛtvam vā kim iti
361.20	jñāna-cikīrṣā-prayatnānām samavāyaḥ kartṛtvam sukha-duḥkha-saṃvid-samavāyaḥ
362.01	bhoktṛtvam etad tu na śarīre nimitta-tvāt kartṛ śarīram ucyate
362.02	itaḥ ca deha-ādi-vyatiriktaḥ ātman^ḥ//
362.03	[3-1-7]savya-dṛṣṭasya itarena pratyabhijñānāt
362.04	savya-dṛṣṭasya itarena pratyabhijñānāt/ savyena cakṣus^ā dṛṣṭam itarena
362.05	pratyabhijānāti iti sūtram/ tad ca pratisandhānam pratyabhijñānam smṛti-pūrvaka-
362.06	tvāt pratyabhijñānasya pratisandhānāt ca eka-kartṛtvam pratyayānām sidhyati iti/
362.07	siddha-tvāt anārambhaḥ iti darśana-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt iti etasmin prakaraṇe
362.08	siddhaḥ ātma-vyatirekaḥ siddha-tvāt anārambhyam prakaraṇam iti/ indriya-
362.09	vyatireka-jñāpana-artham iti eke/ eke idam prakaraṇam indriya-vyatireka-jñāpana-
362.10	artham varṇayanti/ tad na tataḥ eva siddhi^aḥ pūrva-prakaraṇena eva indriya-śarīra-
362.11	vyatirekaḥ siddhaḥ iti/ samuccaya-artham iti apare apare tu samuccaya-artham
362.12	savya-dṛṣṭasya itarena pratiabhijñānāt iti hetum bruvate/ yuktaḥ anyaḥ samuccayaḥ
362.13	ayam tu na yuktaḥ virodhāt savya-dṛṣṭasya itarena pratyabhijñānāt iti
362.14	bruvāṇaḥ yuktim bādhate/ kā punar iyam yukti-bādhā aneka-tvāt indriyasya
362.15	yugapad-adhiṣṭhāna-asambhavaḥ aṇu manas anekam cakṣus na ca anu^aḥ manasaḥ
362.16	yugapad anekena cakṣus^ā sambandhaḥ sambhavati atha asambaddham api dvitīyam
362.17	cakṣus-arthān ālocayati evam ca sati dvitīya-cakṣus-vat itara-cakṣus api manasā
362.18	anadhiṣṭhitam eva pravartiṣyate iti vyartham manas prāptam anadhiṣṭhitam ca
362.19	pravartamānam indriyam karaṇatvam bādhate na ca karaṇam anadhiṣṭhitam pravartamānam
362.20	dṛṣṭam iti samagra-asamagrayoḥ ca tulya-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ na hi anadhiṣṭhitam
363.01	indriyam pravartate iti aṇu-tvāt manasā nityam ekam eva sambaddhyate
363.02	iti vikala-avikalayoḥ tulya-upalabdhiḥ prāpnoti prakaraṇa-virodhaḥ ca
363.03	indriya-pañcaka-tvāt iti indriya-pañca-tva-pratipādakam prakaraṇam virudhyate iti/ 3-1-7]
363.04	yadi tarhi ekam indriyam dvitva-upalabdhiḥ katham dvitva-upalabdhi-pratipādana-artham
363.05	na ekasmin nāsā-asthi-vyavahite dvitva-babhimānāt iti sūtram//
363.06	[3-1-8]na ekasmin nāsā-asthi-vyavahite dvitva-babhimānāt
363.07	ekam indriyam dravyam dvi-adhiṣṭhānam abhinnam kaḥ adhiṣṭhāna-arthaḥ taijasasya
363.08	cakṣus^aḥ pārthivena kṛṣṇa-sārena upakāra-vikāra-bheda-anuvidhānam yasmāt
363.09	kṛṣṇa-sāraḥ upakriyamāṇaḥ upakriyate vikriyamāṇe vikriyate tiṣṭhati
363.10	paśyati iti tasmāt asya kṛṣṇa-sāram adhiṣṭhānam ucyate/ etad ca vyākhyātam
363.11	śarīra-sūtre/ tasya antau gṛhyamāṇau dvitva-abhimānam prayojayataḥ yathā
363.12	dīrghasya dravyasya madhye vyavahitasya antau upalabhyamānau dvitva-abhimānam
363.13	prayojayate iti//
363.14	[3-1-9]eka-vināśe dvitīya-avināśāt na ekatvam
363.15	eka-vināśe dvitīya-avināśāt na ekatvam/ ekasmin vnaṣṭe udvṛtte
363.16	vā dvitīyam avatiṣṭhate viṣaya-grahaṇe liṅgam na anyathā tad-utpatti^aḥ
363.17	dvitīyam avatiṣṭhate iti na etad kasya cit pratyakṣam pratyaya-mātram tu
363.18	vinaṣṭe api ekasmin adhiṣṭhāne yad avatiṣṭhate tena bhavati/ na ca adhiṣṭhānam
363.19	indriyam api tu yad tad-anuvidhāyin tad anuvidhatte iti yathā
363.20	bahu-vāta-āyanasya veśmanaḥ eka-pidhānāt itarena grahaṇam iti//
364.01	[3-1-10]avayava-nāśe api avayavi-upalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ
364.02	apare tu avayava-nāśe api avayavi-upalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ iti parihāram varṇayanti/
364.03	tasya uttaram/
364.04	[3-1-11]dṛṣṭānta-virodhāt apratiṣedhaḥ
364.05	dṛṣṭānta-virodhāt apratiṣedhaḥ iti sūtram/ asya arthaḥ/ na kāraṇa-
364.06	dravya-vināśe kārya-dravyam avatiṣṭḥate/ yadi na avatiṣṭhate katham kāraṇa-
364.07	dravya-vināśe kāryam upalabhyate iti/ kaḥ evam āha upalabhyate iti
364.08	api tu bahuṣu avayaviṣu yasya kāraṇāni vibhaktāni tad vinaṣṭam yasya
364.09	na vibhaktāni tad upalabhyate iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
364.10	[3-1-12]indriya-antara-vikārāt
364.11	anumīyate ca ayam deha-ādi-vyatiriktaḥ cetanaḥ ātman^ḥ indriya-antara-
364.12	vikārāt/ kasya cit amlasya phalasya dṛṣṭa-sāhacarye rūpe gandhe vā kena
364.13	cit indriyena gṛhyamāṇe rasana-indriya-vikāraḥ kaḥ punar vikāraḥ rasa-anusmṛti^i
364.14	rasa-garddhi-pravarttitaḥ garddhiḥ tṛṣṇā rasa-tṛṣṇā-pravarttitaḥ danta-antara-
364.15	parisrutābhiḥ ap^bhiḥ rasana-indriyasya saṃplavaḥ sambandhaḥ vikāraḥ iti ucyate
364.16	sā ca iyam smṛtiḥ indriya-caitanye na prāpnoti/ na ca pratikṣaṇa-dhvaṃsiṣu
364.17	saṃskāra^su anya-anubhūtasya anyena asmaraṇāt iti/ indriya-antara-vikāraḥ indriya-
364.18	antara-dharmaḥ saḥ katham vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt ātma-vyatirekam pratipādayati
364.19	na indriya-antara-vykāraḥ ātma-vyatireka-pratipatti-kāraṇam api tu smṛtiḥ
365.01	na hi ekam anubhavitṛ^am antareṇa smṛtiḥ prāpnoti smṛtiḥ ca bhāva-tvāt
365.02	bhavitṛ-apekṣā utpatti-vat iti nyāyaḥ na ca smṛtim antareṇa indriya-vikāraḥ
365.03	sambhavati iti pratyakṣaḥ indriya-antara-vikāraḥ upādīyate ananubhūta-rasasya
365.04	indriya-vikāra-abhāvāt iti vyatireka-hetuḥ//
365.05	[3-1-13]na smṛti^aḥ smartavya-viṣaya-tvāt
365.06	na smṛti^aḥ smartavya-viṣaya-tvāt/ smṛtiḥ nāma dharmaḥ tasyāḥ smartavyaḥ
365.07	arthaḥ viṣayaḥ sā iyam smartavya-artha-sannidhānāt upajāyamānā aparidṛṣṭa-sāmarthyam
365.08	na smartṛ^am pratipādayiṣyati na hi sva-kāraṇa-sannidhānāt utpannaḥ
365.09	aṅkuraḥ aparidṛṣṭa-sāmarthyam kumbha-ādikam artham pratipādayati//
365.10	[3-1-14]tad-ātma-guṇa-sadbhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ
365.11	tad-ātma-guṇa-sadbhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ na smartavya-mātrāt smṛtiḥ utpadyate/
365.12	yadā ayam atītam artham smarati tadā smṛtiḥ anādhārā prāpnoti na ca iyam anādhārā
365.13	yuktā guṇa-tvāt na hi kaḥ cit guṇaḥ anādhāraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti na
365.14	ca iyam indriye sambhavati ananubhūta-tvāt na viṣaye tasya asattvāt na śarīre
365.15	śarīra-guṇānām pratyakṣāṇām sva-ātma-para-ātma-pratyakṣa-tvāt na ca iayam anādhārā
365.16	guṇa-tvāt iti uktam/ na ca iyam na asti na ca ātmani asati asyāḥ sadbhāvaḥ yuktaḥ
365.17	iti/ tebhyaḥ anyasya tad-utpatti^i sāmarthyam avagamyate katham iti anena eva
365.18	pratyuktam/ asti ātmanaḥ sāmarthyam smṛti^aḥ ādhāra-bhāvaḥ na ca iyam anādhārā
365.19	kārya-tvāt sarvam kāryam ādhāravat kṣīra-ādi dṛṣṭam iti/ etena -
366.01	na tad cakṣus^i naḥ rūpe na antarāle tayoḥ sthitam/
366.02	na tad asti na tad na asti yatra tad-niṣṭhitam bhavet//
366.03	iti pratyuktam/ na tad asti na tad na asti iti vyāhatam na ca anayā
366.04	kārikayā vijñānasya āśrayi-bhāvaḥ pratiṣeddhum śakyate/ kim kāraṇam
366.05	viśeṣa-pratiṣedhāt/ na tad cakṣus^i naḥ rūpe iti ādi-viśeṣa-pratiṣedhaḥ/
366.06	atha anāśritam eva vijñānam vyartham vākyam na tad cakṣus^i naḥ rūpe iti
366.07	kasya vā cakṣus^i rūpe vā vijñānam vartate yam prati pratiṣedhaḥ ayam ca
366.08	vijñānasya āśraya-pratiṣedhaḥ vicāryamāṇaḥ ātma-sattvam pratipādayati/
366.09	na hi vijñānasya ātma-sattvam antareṇa vākyam etad arthavat-tāyām vyavatiṣṭhate/
366.10	atha vā ekasya aneka-viṣaya-upalabdhi-pratisandhānam aena sūtrena upadarśyate
366.11	iti ukta-nyāyam/ śeṣam bhāṣye iti//
366.12	[(3-1-15)]aparisaṃkhyānāt ca smṛti-viṣayasya
366.13	[3-1-15(16)]na ātma-pratipatti-hetūnām manasi sambhavāt
366.14	na ātma-pratipatti-hetūnām manasi sambhavāt/ ye ete bhavatā ātma-
366.15	pratipādakāḥ hetu^āḥ upadiṣṭāḥ sarve ete manasi sambhavanti/ evam ca
366.16	na manas-vyatiriktaḥ ātman^ḥ sidhyati//
366.17	[3-1-16(17)]jñātṛ^aḥ jñāna-sādhana-upapatti^aḥ saṃjñā-bheda-mātram
366.18	jñātṛ^aḥ jñāna-sādhana-upapatti^aḥ saṃjñā-bheda-mātram/ yathā jñātṛ^am abhyupagacchatā
366.19	jñāna-sādhanāni cakṣus-ādīni pratipadyante tathā mantṛ^am pratipadyamānena
367.01	mati-sādhanam abhyupeyam yad ca tad-mati-sādhanam tad manas iti
367.02	saṃjñā-bheda-mātram/ atha mantṛ^ḥ niḥsādhanaḥ matim karoti evam ayam
367.03	jñātṛ^ḥ niḥsādhanaḥ jñānam kariṣyati iti sarva-indriya-vilopa-prasaṅgaḥ//
367.04	[3-1-17(18)]niyamaḥ ca niranumānaḥ
367.05	niyamaḥ ca niranumānaḥ/ yaḥ ayam niyamaḥ āśrīyate matiḥ asādhanā
367.06	na punar jñāptiḥ iti niyamaḥ niranumānaḥ jñaptivat tu mati^aḥ samādhana-tve
367.07	anumānam asti sukha-ādyaḥ  ca rūpa-ādibhyaḥ viṣaya-antaram iti tad-viṣaya-tvāt
367.08	upalabdhi^aḥ sādhanena bhavitavyam na hi kim cit saviṣayam jñānam asādhanam
367.09	dṛṣṭam rūpa-ādi-jñāna-vat iti niyamaḥ ca niranumānaḥ sukha-ādi-vijñānāni
367.10	niḥsādhanāni bhavantu mā bhūvan rūpa-ādi-viṣayāṇi iti yadi sarvam
367.11	vijñānam samādhanam ucyate manasi api karaṇa-antaram prāpnoti viṣaya-tvāt/
367.12	om iti ucyate asti manasi karaṇam kim punar tad yena manas adhigamyate
367.13	kena ca manas adhigamyate ayugapad-jñāna-utpatti^ā yasya tu manas pratyakṣam
367.14	bhavati tasya yogaja-dharma-anugṛhītaḥ ātma-manas-saṃyogaḥ karaṇam yogi-dharmāṇām
367.15	ca acintya-viṣaya-tvāt katham te manas gṛhṇanti iti avicāraṇīyam etad/
367.16	etena ātma-jñānam pratyuktam/ ātma-manas-saṃyogasya kāraṇa-bhāvāt iti/ evam
367.17	tāvat ātmanaḥ astitva-vyatirekau siddhau bahutvam ca ataḥ eva darśana-sparśanābhyām
367.18	eka-artha-grahaṇāt na anya-dṛṣṭam anyaḥ smarati iti śarīra-dāhe pātaka-
367.19	abhāvāt iti/ sā iyam sarva vyavasthā śarīra-bhede sati sambhavati iti//
368.01	kim punar ayam deha-ādi-saṅghātāt anyaḥ nityaḥ atha anityaḥ iti etasmin
368.02	arthe ubhayathā dṛṣṭa-tvāt iti saṃśayam upapādayati anupapanna-rūpaḥ ca ayam
368.03	saṃśayaḥ/ ātma-astitva-vyatireka-hetubhiḥ śarīra-bhede api abhede ātmani
368.04	siddhaḥ siddha-tvāt prakaraṇam anārabhyam iti/ na anārabhyam katham janma-prabhṛti
368.05	yāvat prāyaṇam etasmāt ekaḥ sidhyati na punar deha-vināśāt ūrdhvam bhavati
368.06	iti etad sidhyati etasya arthasya jñāpana-artham pūrva-abhyasta-smṛti-anubandhāt jātasya
368.07	harṣa-bhaya-śoka-sampratipatti^aḥ iti sūtram//
368.08	[3-1-18(19)]pūrva-abhyasta-smṛti-anubandhāt jātasya harṣa-bhaya-śoka-sampratipatti^aḥ
368.10	asya arthaḥ/ jātaḥ khalu ayam kumārakaḥ viṣaya-adhigama-asamartha^su
368.11	indriya^su harṣa-bhaya-śokān pratipadyamānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ smita kampa-rudita-anumeyān
368.12	te ca smṛti-anubandhāt utpadyante smṛti-anubandhaḥ ca na antareṇa pūrva-
368.13	śarīram iti/ tatra janman nikāya-viśiṣṭābhiḥ śarīra-indriya-buddhi-vedanābhiḥ
368.14	sambandhaḥ/ abhipreta-viṣaya-prārthanā-prāpati^i sukha-anubhavaḥ harṣaḥ/
368.15	aniṣṭa-viṣaya-sādhana-upanipāte tad-jihāsu^aḥ hāna-aśakya-tā bhayam/ iṣṭa-
368.16	viṣaya-viyoge sati tad-prāpti-aśakya-prārthanā śokaḥ/ tad-anubhavaḥ sampratipattiḥ/
368.17	eka-viṣaya-aneka-vijñāna-utpādaḥ abhyāsaḥ eka-ākara-viṣayaḥ vā/
368.18	yathā śālyaḥ  anena abhyastāḥ iti/ pratyakṣa-buddhi-nirodhe tad-anusandhāna-viṣayaḥ
368.19	pratyayaḥ smṛtiḥ/ tad-anugṛhītaḥ tad-anusandhāna-viṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ tad-bhāva-
368.20	viṣayaḥ pratyabhijñānam/ anubandhaḥ bhāvanā-ākhyaḥ smṛti-hetuḥ saṃskāraḥ/
369.01	iṣṭa-viṣaya-anusmaraṇāt nayana-ādi-prasādaḥ smitam/ aniṣṭa-viṣaya-anusmaraṇāt
369.02	tad-sādhana-jihāsa-anuṣṭhāna-lakṣaṇaḥ hasta-ādi-vikṣepavataḥ aśru-vimācana-
369.03	sahitaḥ śabda-viśeṣaḥ ruditam/ smita-rudite vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt asādhanam/
369.04	atha manyase smita-rudite bālya-avasthāyām bhavataḥ na ca bālya-
369.05	avasthā ātmanaḥ tasmāt vyadhikaraṇa-tvāt asādhanam iti/ na śoka-ādimat-
369.06	ātmavatī iti bālya-avasthāyāḥ sādhya-tvāt/ śoka-ādimat-ātmavatī
369.07	bālya-avasthā iti sādhyam smita-rudita-ādimat-tvāt iti/ bālya-avasthā vayas-
369.08	dharmaḥ yauvana-avasthā-vat/ etena smṛti-saṃskāra-anubhava-pūrva-śarīra-sambandha-
369.09	vat ātmavat-tvam vyākhyātam/ katham iti yathā smita-ruditavat-tvena
369.10	śoka-ādimat-ātmavatī bālya-avasthā sādhyate evam śoka-ādimat-ātmavat-tvena
369.11	smṛtimat-ātmavatī bālya-avasthā sādhyā tathā smṛtimat-ātmavat-tvena
369.12	saṃskāravat-ātmavatī bālya-avasthā sādhyā saṃskāravat-ātmavat-tvena pūrva-anubhavavat-
369.13	ātmavatī pūrva-anubhavavat-ātmavat-tvena pūrva-śarīra-sambandhavat-ātmavatī
369.14	sādhyā sarvatra yauvana-avasthā dṛṣṭāntaḥ sā hi smita-ruditavatī
369.15	bhavati iti śoka-ādimat-ātmavatī ca iti sarvatra vaktavyam//
369.16	[3-1-19(20)]padma-ādiṣu prabodha-sammīlana-vikāra-vat tad-vikāraḥ
369.17	padma-ādiṣu prabodha-sammīlana-vikāra-vat tad-vikāraḥ/ anitye api
369.18	vikāra-darśanāt anekāntaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ kaḥ punar ayam prabodhaḥ kim ca
369.19	sammīlanam iti/ padma-pattra-avayava-vibhāgaḥ avinaśyat-kāryaḥ prabodhaḥ/
369.20	padma-pattra-avayavānām ārabdha-kāryāṇām yāḥ punar parasparena prāptyaḥ
370.01	tad sammīlanam iti/ na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ padma-ādiṣu prabodha-sammīlana-
370.02	vikāra-ādi-vat tad-vikāraḥ iti kim ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhana-pakṣaḥ uta
370.03	anaikāntika-pakṣaḥ iti/ yadi sādhana-pakṣe hetum antareṇa dṛṣṭānta-mātram
370.04	na sādhanam iti asādhanam dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ atha dūṣaṇam tad kim dṛṣṭa-kāraṇa-
370.05	pratyākhyānam uta kāraṇa-antara-upapādanam atha ātmanaḥ utpatti-nirodha-
370.06	kāraṇa-anumānam atha ākasmika-tvam iti/ tad yadi tāvat dṛṣṭa-kāraṇa-
370.07	pratyākhyānam tad ayuktam dṛṣṭena viśeṣita-tvāt yauvana-ādi-avasthāsu
370.08	smita-ruditavat-tvam śoka-ādimat-ātmavat-tvam ca dṛṣṭam iti/ dṛṣṭena smita-
370.09	ruditavat-tvena śoka-ādimat-ātmavat-tvam bālya-avasthāyām gamyate iti na
370.10	doṣaḥ/ atha kāraṇa-antara-upapādanam tad api na yuktam ataḥ eva
370.11	dṛṣṭena viśeṣita-tvāt iti/ atha ātmanaḥ utpatti-nirodha-kāraṇa-anumānam
370.12	tad na yuktam dravyasya sataḥ sarvadā amūrta-tvāt akāraṇaḥ ātman^ḥ ākāśa-
370.13	ādi-vat iti/ ātmānan ca kṛtakam pratipadyamānena kāraṇam asya
370.14	vācyam kutaḥ kāryasya kāraṇavat-tvāt sarvam kāryam kāraṇavat dṛṣṭam iti/ na uṣṇa-śīta-varṣa-kāla-nimitta-tvāt pañca-ātmaka-vikārāṇām
370.19	na ayam ākasmikaḥ padma-ādi-prabodha-sammīlana-vikāraḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/
371.01	evam ca sati dṛṣṭāntena na kim cit pratiṣidhyate pañca-ātmaka-vikārāṇām
371.02	iti/ na pañca-ātmaka-kāraṇāni padmāni iti api tu pañcānām
371.03	bhūtānām anugrahe sati bhavanti iti pañca-ātmakāni iti ucyante/ vastutaḥ tu na
371.04	pañca-ātmakam kim cit asti iti upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ//
371.05	[3-1-21(22)]preti-āhāra-abhyāsa-kṛtāt stanya-abhilāṣāt
371.06	preti-āhāra-abhyāsa-kṛtāt stanya-abhilāṣāt/ jāta-mātrasya vatsasya
371.07	pravṛtti^ā stanya-abhilāṣaḥ gamyate tatra pravṛtti-unneyaḥ stanya-abhilāṣaḥ tena
371.08	smṛtiḥ tathā saṃskāraḥ tena anubhavaḥ tena pūrva-śarīram iti pūrvavat prayogaḥ/
371.09	kim artham punar idam sūtram ārabhyate yadā ayam artha pūrva-abhyasta-sūtre avagataḥ/
371.10	sāmānyataḥ adhigatasya viśeṣa-jñāpana-artham sūtram iti adoṣaḥ  pravṛtti-
371.11	mātrasya anaikāntika-tva-jñāpana-artham ayasaḥ ayaskānta-abhigamana-vat tad-upasarpaṇam
371.12	iti sūtram//
371.13	[3-1-22(23)]ayasaḥ ayaskānta-abhigamana-vat tad-upasarpaṇam
371.14	na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ kim idam ayasaḥ ayaskānta-abhigamanam sanimittam
371.15	āho nirnimittam iti/ yadi sanimittam tad kena gamyate
371.16	niyamena ayas^ī ayaskāntam eva upasarpanti na tu loṣṭa-ādīn na ca
371.17	loṣṭa-ādyaḥ  upasarpanti saḥ ayam kārya-niyamāt kāraṇa-niyamaḥ gamyate/
371.18	etena ākasmikatvam pratiṣiddham/ tad idam ayasām upasarpaṇam kim dṛṣṭa-kāraṇa-
372.01	pratyākhyāna-artham upanyastam atha vā kāraṇa-antara-upadarśana-artham atha ātmani
372.02	utpatti-nirodha-anumānam iti pūrvavat-prasaṅgaḥ//
372.03	[3-1-23(24)]na anyatra pravṛtti-abhāvāt
372.04	ākasmikatva-pratiṣedha-artham ca na anyatra pravṛtti-abhāvāt iti sūtram
372.05	[3-1-24(25)]vīta-rāga-janma-adarśanāt
372.06	nityaḥ ātman^ḥ vīta-rāga-janma-adarśanāt/ na hi kaḥ cit jāta-
372.07	mātraḥ vīta-rāgaḥ jāyate vītta-rāgāṇām janma-adarśanāt sarāgaḥ
372.08	jāyate iti gamyate/ janman vyākhyātam/ tataḥ kim rāgasya pūrva-anubhūta-
372.09	viṣaya-anucintanam yoniḥ na ca viṣaya-avagama-asamartha^su indriya^su
372.10	rāgaḥ sambhavati na ca smṛtim antareṇa viṣaya-anucintanam yuktam
372.11	pūrva-anubhūta-viṣay-prārthanā saṃkalpaḥ adṛṣṭāt iti cet/ atha manyase na
372.12	pūrva-śarīra-yogaḥ rāgāt gamyate api tu adṛṣṭāt rāgah iti/ na abhiprāya-
372.13	aparijñānāt na evam abhiprāyaḥ kāraṇa-niyamena rāgaḥ api tu pūrva-
372.14	śarīra-sambandha-pratipādanam sūtra-arthaḥ/ na ca adṛṣṭāt rāgaḥ iti bruvatā
372.15	tad pratiṣidhyate iti kim cit uktam tanmaya-tvāt rāgaḥ iti viṣaya-abhyāsaḥ
372.16	khalu ayam bhāvanā-hetuḥ tanmaya-tvam ucyate jāti-viśeṣāt ca rāga-viśeṣaḥ iti
372.17	karman khalu idam jāti-viśeṣasya nirvartakam tādarthyāt tād-śabdam labhyate
372.18	vīraṇa-ādi-vat//
372.19	[3-1-25(26)]saguṇa-dravya-utpatti-vat tad-utpattiḥ
373.01	saguṇa-dravya-utpatti-vat tad-utpattiḥ iti anaikāntika-pakṣe sūtram/ na ukta-
373.02	uttara-tvāt ukta-uttaram etad//
373.03	[3-1-26(27)]na saṃkalpa-nimitta-tvāt rāga-ādīnām
373.04	na saṃkalpa-nimitta-tvāt rāga-ādīnām iti/ atra api pūrvavat saṃkalpa-
373.05	smṛti-saṃskāra-pūrva-anubhava-pūrva-śarīra-vat ātmavat-tvāni yojyāni iti/ evam
373.06	ca anādiḥ saṃsāraḥ apavargantaḥ sidhyati iti vyavasthitam etad asti ātman^ḥ
373.07	vyatiriktaḥ nityaḥ ca iti//
373.08	ātma-anantaram śarīram avasara-prāptam parīkṣyate/ atha vā anādiḥ cetanasya
373.09	śarīra-yogaḥ iti śarīram parīkṣyate/ tasmin parīkṣyamāṇe
373.10	ātma-adhikāraḥ ca parisamāptaḥ bhavati/ kim punar asya parīkṣyam kim
373.11	ghrāṇa-ādi-vat eka-prakṛti atha nānā-prakṛti iti vipratipatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ/ śrūyate
373.12	khalu atra vipratipattiḥ tatra idam tattvam//
373.13	[3-1-27(28)]pārthivam guṇa-antara-upalabdhi^aḥ
373.14	mānuṣam śarīram pārthivam iti/ mānuṣam iti kim artham viśiṣyate
373.15	loka-antara-śarīrāṇi na pārthivāni iti yuktam viśeṣaṇam gandhavat-tvāt
373.16	paramāṇu-vat gandhavān paramāṇuḥ eka-ātmakah dṛṣṭaḥ gandhavat ca śarīram
373.17	tasmāt eka-ātmakam eka-svabhāvam iti/ na tu idam ap-ādibhiḥ asaṃpṛktayā pṛthivī^ā
373.18	ārabdham ca iṣṭa-indriya-artha-āśraya-bhāvena kalpyate iti bhūta-saṃsargaḥ avipratiṣiddhaḥ/
373.19	evam tarhi pārthiva-āpya-taijasam tad-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ [3-1-29]niḥśvāsa-ucchvāsa-upalabdhi^aḥ
374.01	cāturbhautikam, [3-1-30]gandha-kleda-pāka-vyūha-avakāśa-dāna^bhyaḥ pāñcabhautikam
374.02	iti pratyuktam//
374.03	tatra sannihitānām tad-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ iti tad idam aneka-bhūta-prakṛti
374.04	śarīram arasam agandham arūpam asparśam ca prakṛti-anuvidhānāt syāt iti
374.05	bhāṣyam/ tasya vyākhyānam pṛthivī-udakābhyām ārabhyamāṇam agandham kāraṇa-
374.06	gandhasya ekasya anārambhaka-tvāt/ pṛthivī-analābhyām ārabhyamāṇam agandham arasam ca
374.07	kāraṇa-gandha-rasayoḥ kevalayoḥ anārambhaka-tvāt/ pṛthivī-analābhyām agandham
374.08	arasam ca arūpam ca vāyu^aḥ agandha-rasa-rūpa-tvāt/ pṛthivī-ākāśābhyām agandham
374.09	arasam arūpam asparśam ca ākāśe gandha-ādi-abhāvāt sarvatra samānaḥ nyāyaḥ
374.10	kāraṇa-guṇasya kevalasya anārambhaka-tvāt/ jala-analābhyām agandham ca arasam ca
374.11	jala-vāyubhyām agandham ca arasam ca arūpam ca jala-ākāśābhyām agandham arasam arūpam
374.12	asparśam ca tejas-anilābhyām agandhan ca arasam ca arūpam ca anala-ākāśābhyām
374.13	agandham arasam arūpam asparśam ca anila-ākāśābhyām evam eva bhū-jala-analaiḥ
374.14	agandham eva bhū-jala-anilaiḥ evam eva bhū-jala-ākśaiḥ evam eva pṛthivī-anila-analaiḥ
374.15	agandham arasam ca pṛthivī-anala-ākāśaiḥ evam eva pṛthivī-anila-ākāśaiḥ agandham arasam
374.16	arūpam ca jala-anila-analaiḥ agandham arasam ca jala-anala-ākāśaiḥ evam eva jala-
374.17	anila-ākāśaiḥ agandham ca arasam ca arūpam ca anala-anila-ākāśaiḥ evam eva pṛthivī-
374.18	udaka-tejas-vāyubhiḥ agandham pṛthivī-udaka-jvalana-ākāśaiḥ evam eva avani-jala-
374.19	ākāśaiḥ evam eva pṛthivī-anila-jvalana-ākāśaiḥ agandham arūpam ca jala-anila-
375.01	anala-ākāśaiḥ evam eva pṛthivī-udaka-tejas-vāyu-ākāśaiḥ agandham kāraṇa-gūṇasya ekasya
375.02	anārambhaka-tvāt iti vācyam eka-kāraṇaka-tve tu satata-utpatti-
375.03	anupapattī nityatva-prasaṅgaḥ ca iti uktam//
375.04	[3-1-31(29)]śruti-prāmāṇyāt ca
375.05	śruti-prāmāṇyāt ca/ sūryam te cakṣus spṛṇomi iti asya mantrasya ante
375.06	pṛthivī^ām te śarīram iti sā iyam smṛtiḥ kāraṇāt kārya-utpattiḥ/ yad uktam
375.07	bhavati sūryaḥ cakṣus-kāraṇam pṛthivī śarīrasya iti antya-iṣṭa-kāle mantraḥ
375.08	sūryam te cakṣus gacchatu pṛthivī śarīram iti/ asya api mantrasya arthaḥ
375.09	yad yasmāt āyātam tad tasmin eva pralayam gacchati prakṛti^i vikārasya pralaya-
375.10	abhidhānam/ pralaya-śabdena ca kārya-śūnyam kāraṇam ucyate na punar
375.11	kāryasya kāraṇe layaḥ pralayaḥ evam ca asat utpadyate sanniruddhyate iti
375.12	ayam arthaḥ uktaḥ bhavati//
375.13	[3-1-32(30)]kṛṣṇa-sāre sati upalambhāt vyatiricya ca upalambhāt
375.14	saṃśayaḥ
375.15	atha idānīm indriyāṇi prameya-kramena vicāryante/ kim āvyaktikāni
375.16	āhaṃkārikāṇi āho bhautikāni iti saṃśaya-kāraṇa-pratipādana-
375.17	artham sūtram//
375.18	kṛṣṇa-sāre sati upalambhāt vyatiricya ca upalambhāt saṃśayaḥ/ kṛṣṇa-sāram
375.19	bhautikam tasmin anupahate viṣaya-upalabdhiḥ kṛṣṇa-sāra-vyatirekena viprakṛṣṭa-
375.20	deśa-avasthitasya viṣayasya upalabdhiḥ sā iyam vyatireka-upalabdhiḥ abhautika-
376.01	dharmaḥ tad evam ubhaya-dharma-upalabdhi^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti/ kṛṣṇa-sāram eva cakṣus tasmin
376.02	sati bhāvāt rūpa-grahaṇasya yasmāt idam rūpa-grahaṇam sati kṛṣṇa-āre bhavati
376.03	asati na bhavati/ yad ca yasmin sati bhavati asati na bhavati tasya
376.04	tad iti/ yathā kārya-dravyasya rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti/ na pradīpa-ādibhiḥ anekāntāt
376.05	pradīpe sati rūpa-upalabdhiḥ bhavati na ca pradīpasya rūpa-upalabdhiḥ
376.06	iti anekāntaḥ/ yasya kṛṣṇa-sāram cakṣus tasya sannikṛṣṭa-viprakṛṣṭayoḥ
376.07	tulya-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ kṛṣṇa-sāram na viṣayam prāpnoti prāpti-aviśeṣāt
376.08	sannikṛṣṭa-viprakṣṭayoḥ tulya-upalabdhiḥ prāpnoti/ viṣaya^bhāvāt iti
376.09	cet atha manyase sannikṛṣṭaḥ asya viṣayaḥ bhavati viprakṛṣṭaḥ na viṣayaḥ
376.10	evam ca na tulya-upalabdhiḥ iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt pratyakṣa-sūtre iti/
376.11	abhautikāni iti apare
376.12	[3-1-33(31)]mahat-aṇu-grahaṇāt
376.13	mahat-aṇu-grahaṇāt/ mahat iti mahattaram mahattamam ucyate aṇu iti
376.14	aṇutaram aṇutamam iti tad idam ubhayam cakṣus^i upalabhyamānam bhautikatvam
376.15	cakṣus^aḥ bādhate bhautikam hi yāvat bhavati tāvat eva vyāpnoti abhautikam
376.16	tu vyāpaka-tvāt sarva-sambaddham na bhautika^su pradīpa-ādiṣu dṛṣṭa-tvāt
376.17	bhautikāḥ pradīpa-ādyaḥ  mahat-aṇu^oḥ prakāśakāḥ bhavanti iti anekāntaḥ
376.18	pradīpa-ādi-anabhyupagame vā mahat-aṇu-prakāśaka-tvam cakṣus^aḥ eva iti asādhāraṇa-
376.19	tvāt ahetuḥ bhautika-abhautikayoḥ nivṛtti^aḥ/ nanu ca abhautike jñāne
376.20	mahat-aṇu-prakāśaka-tvam dṛṣṭam na dṛṣṭam na hi buddhi^ā mahat-aṇunī prakāśyete
377.01	api tu prakāśaḥ buddhiḥ na prakāśanam iti avadhāritasya arthasya hāna-
377.02	upādāna-upekṣā-buddhīnām sādhanam buddhiḥ iti na abhautikam mahat-aṇu-prakāśakam
377.03	asti nanu manas vidyate satyam na punar tad bhautikam na api abhautikam
377.04	iti/ etena ātman^ḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ na bhautikaḥ na abhautikaḥ iti yadi
377.05	manas na abhutikam yad uktam bhautikāni indriyāṇi abhautikam manas iti
377.06	tad avyāhatam na abhautika-arthasya abhūta-ātmaka-paryāya-tvāt abhautikam manas iti
377.07	abhūta-ātmakam manas iti yāvat uktam bhavati/ mukhyataḥ tu manas na bhautikam
377.08	na abhautikam it na indriye api samāna-tvāt indriyam abhautikam iti
377.09	yāvat uktam bhavati abhūta-ātmakam iti/ abhūta-ātmakam vyāpakam ca indriyam
377.10	pratipadyamānaḥ idam paryanuyojyaḥ vyavahita-artha-grahaṇam kasmāt na bhavati kim
377.11	kāraṇam vyāpaka-tvāt indriyasya na kuḍya-ādi^aḥ āvaraṇa-sāmarthyam asti iti vṛttiḥ
377.12	pratiṣidhyate iti cet atha manyase satyam vyāpakam indriyam tasya tu
377.13	puruṣa-artha-hetunā kṣobhyamānasya mahā-hradāt budbudāḥ iva niḥsaranti astāḥ
377.14	kuḍya-ādibhiḥ pratiṣidhyante iti na vṛtti-vyatirekena indriya-sattve
377.15	pramāṇa-abhāvāt yā iyam viṣaya-grahaṇa-ātmikā vṛttiḥ tām tyaktvā tad-vyatiriktam
377.16	indriyam iti kim pramāṇām na ca aprāmāṇikaḥ arthaḥ śakyaḥ
377.17	pratipattum na ca pratiṣidhyamāna-apratiṣidhyamānayoḥ ekatvam ekatve
377.18	vā anarthakyam vṛttiḥ pratiṣidhyate niścarati iti ca/ avyatirekāt ca tad-
377.19	utpatti-vināśa-dharmakam yadi vṛtti-avyatirekin indriyam yathā vṛtti^aḥ utpāda-
377.20	vināśau evam indriyasya api prāpnutaḥ/ vṛtti^aḥ vyaktiḥ na utpattiḥ iti cet
378.01	atha manyase na mayā vṛtti^aḥ utpattiḥ abhyupagamyate api tu vyaktiḥ na
378.02	nirodhaḥ vināśaḥ api tu tirobhāvaḥ iti na utpatti-viśeṣa-tvāt vyaktiḥ
378.03	utpatti^aḥ viśeṣaḥ/ katham iti na anupajāta-viśeṣasya vyaktiḥ iti/ atha
378.04	anupajāta-viśeṣam vyajyate iti manyase nityam vyaktiḥ syāt/ etena
378.05	vināśaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ tirobhāvaḥ iti vidyamānam na kim cit niruddham
378.06	na hi anivṛtta-viśeṣasya agrahaṇam bhavati/ na ca sarva-nitya-vādinā grahaṇa-
378.07	agrahaṇe yukte viśeṣasya anupajananāt na grahaṇam viśeṣasya anupacaryāt na agrahaṇam
378.08	iti/ nityam vyāpakam ca indriyam abhyupagacchataḥ kāraṇa-arthaḥ hīyate/
378.09	kāraṇam nāma yasya anantaram yad bhavati kāraṇam ca nityam kāryam ca nityam
378.10	iti kim kasya anantaram kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ ca katham tayoḥ nitya-tvāt/
378.11	kāraṇa-śabda-arthaḥ ca vācyaḥ/ nanu karoti iti kārakam satyam karoti iti
378.12	kārakam na punar nitya-vādinaḥ kim cit kartavyam asti na ca asati kartavye
378.13	kāraka-artham paśyāmaḥ/ vyakti^i kāraka-arthaḥ iti cet vyakti^i ca tulyam
378.14	vyaktiḥ api vyaṅgyavat-nityā iti vyakti^i api na kāraka-arthaḥ asti yugapad-aneka-
378.15	vijñāna-prasaṅgāt ca yadi ca vṛttiḥ vṛttimataḥ na anyā bhavati vṛttimataḥ
378.16	avasthānāt vṛttīnām avasthānam iti yugapad-aneka-vijñāna-prasaṅgaḥ vṛtti-aneka-
378.17	tve ca ekam indriyam anekam prāpnoti vṛttibhyaḥ ananya-tvāt/ atha mā bhūt
378.18	indriya-bhedaḥ iti vṛttīnām tarhi ekatvam prāpnoti vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ ananya-
378.19	tvāt/ atha mā bhūt ayam doṣaḥ iti ubhayam na iṣyate bhedaḥ tarhi vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ
378.20	iti na ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt ayuktam vyāpakam indriyam nityam
379.01	ca iti/  mahat-aṇu-grahaṇasya ca anyathā-siddhi^aḥ ahetuḥ yaḥ ayam hetuḥ mahat-aṇu-
379.02	grahaṇāt abhautikāni indriyāṇi iti ayam anyathā-siddhiḥ na mahat-aṇu-grahaṇa-
379.03	mātrāt abhautikatvam vyāpakatvam indriyāṇām śakyam pratipattum kasmāt idam
379.04	yasmāt raśmi-artha-sannikarṣa-viśeṣāt mahat-aṇu^oḥ grahaṇam//
379.05	[3-1-34(32)]raśmi-artha-sannikarṣa-viśeṣāt tad-grahaṇam
379.06	cakṣus-raśmi^aḥ arthasya ca sannikarṣa-viśeṣāt mahat-aṇu^oḥ grahaṇam bhavati/ tad ca
379.07	sannikarṣa-mātrāt sāmānya-grahaṇam sannikarṣa-viśeṣāt viśeṣa-grahaṇam/ kaḥ
379.08	puanr sannikarṣasya viśeṣaḥ bhūyas-avayava-sannikarṣa-anugrahaḥ yasmāt ayam sannikarṣaḥ
379.09	viśeṣa-pratipatti-hetuḥ bhūyas-avayava-saṃyogaiḥ anugṛhyate saḥ ayam avayava-
379.10	antara-saṃyoga-apekṣaḥ avayavi-indriya-sannikarṣaḥ sannikarṣa-viśeṣaḥ iti ucyate saḥ ca
379.11	raśmi-artha-sannikarṣa-viśeṣaḥ aṇu-mahatoḥ tulyaḥ bhavati iti anyathā mahat-aṇu^oḥ grahaṇam
379.12	siddhyati/ raśmi-artha-sannikarṣaḥ ca āvaraṇa-liṅgaḥ kuḍya-ādi-vyavahitānām aprakāśa-
379.13	rūpa-tvāt aprāpya-kāri-tve tu na kuḍya-ādi^aḥ āvaraṇa-sāmarthyam asti iti asti
379.14	cākṣaṣaḥ raśmiḥ yathā pradīpa-raśmiḥ iti  āvaraṇa-anumeya-tve sati idam āha
379.15	[3-1-35(33)]tad-anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ
379.16	tad-anupalabdhi^aḥ ahetuḥ/ na cākṣaṣaḥ raśmiḥ vidyate iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ katham
379.17	iti/ upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāpta-tvāt anumāna-anupapattiḥ yad khalu upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-
379.18	prāptam na upalabhyate tad na asti yathā ghaṭa-ādi ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ mahat-aneka-dravyavattva-
379.19	rūpavattvāni santi iti upalabhyante ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  tathā mahat-anekadravyavattva-rūpavān
379.20	cākṣuṣaḥ raśmiḥ kasmāt pratyakṣataḥ na upalabhyate iti mahattvam tāvat
380.01	kāraṇa-mahattva-bahutva-pracaya^bhyaḥ anekadravyavattvam api kāraṇa-bahutvāt eva
380.02	rūpa-sparśavat hi tejas iti na arūpam tad/ evam aśeṣa-upalabdhi-kāra-sannidhāne
380.03	sati yad na upalabhyate tena gamyate na asti iti//
380.04	[3-1-36(34)]na anumīyamānasya pratyakṣataḥ anupalabdhiḥ abhāva-hetuḥ
380.05	na anumīyamānasya pratyakṣataḥ anupalabdhiḥ abhāva-hetuḥ/ yad pratyakṣataḥ
380.06	na upalabhyate tad anumānena upalabhyamānam na asti iti ayuktam yathā candramasaḥ
380.07	para-bhāgaḥ pṛthivī^āḥ ca adhas-bhāgaḥ pratyakṣa-lakṣaṇa-prāpti^i api na pratyakṣataḥ upalabhyate
380.08	anumānena ca upalabdhi^aḥ na tau na staḥ kim punar anumānam arvāc-
380.09	bhāga-vat ubhaya-pratipattiḥ tathā cākṣuṣasya raśmi^aḥ kuḍya-ādi-āvaraṇam anumānam
380.10	sambhavati iti/ apare tu mahat-anekadravyavattvāt rūpavattvāt ca upalabdhiḥ iti upalabdhi^i
380.11	niyamam varṇayanti/ na upalabhyamānaḥ iti kim uktam bhavati na
380.12	yuktaḥ yatra yatra mahat-anekadravyavattva-rūpāṇi santi tad tad upalabhyate iti
380.13	api tu yad yad upalabhyate tatra tatra mahat-anekadravyavattva-rūpāṇi santi iti/
380.14	evam tarhi idam sūtram na upalabdhi^aḥ kāraṇa-pratipādakam sat-svabhāvāt iti
380.15	satsu mahat-aneka-dravya-rūpa^su upalabdhiḥ na bhavati iti na etāṇi upalabdhi-kāraṇam
380.16	iti
380.17	[3-1-37(35)]dravya-guṇa-dharma-bhedāt ca upalabdhi-niyamaḥ
380.18	dravya-guṇa-dharma-bhedāt ca upalabdhi-niyamaḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/  kasmāt
380.19	tarhi cākṣuṣaḥ raśmi^aḥ na upalabhyate iti upalabdhi-kāraṇa-abhāvāt iti/
381.01	na etāvat eva upalabdhi-kāraṇam yad mahat-aneka-dravya-rūpāṇi api tu rūpa-grahaṇāt
381.02	rūpa-viśeṣah abhidhīyate na rūpa-mātram evam ca sūtram//
381.03	[3-1-38(36)]aneka-dravya-samavāyāt rūpa-viśeṣāt rūpa-upalabdhiḥ
381.04	aneka-dravya-samavāyāt rūpa-viśeṣāt rūpa-upalabdhiḥ iti/ atra
381.05	rūpa-viśeṣa-grahaṇena rūpa-dharmaḥ udbhava-samākhyaḥ abhidhīyate na rūpatvam rūpa-
381.06	antarāt rūpam viśinaṣṭi api tu udbhavaḥ viśeṣaka-tvāt viśeṣaḥ iti ucyate yathā
381.07	brāhmaṇa-viśeṣaḥ iti na brāhmaṇatvam brāhmaṇa-viśeṣaḥ evam samāna-jātīya-
381.08	viśeṣaka-tvam yad tad viśeṣaḥ iti ucyate udbhavaḥ ca kārya-gamyaḥ yasya abhāvāt viṣakta-
381.09	avayavam āpyam dravyam hemante gṛhyate taijasam ca grīṣme yasya abhāvāt
381.10	pradīpa-raśmiḥ upalabhyate āditya-raśmiḥ ca saḥ udbhavaḥ nāma viśeṣaḥ saḥ
381.11	tasmin nāyane raśmi^i rūpa-viśeṣaḥ na asti iti ataḥ cākṣuṣaḥ raśminā upalabhyate
381.12	dṛṣṭaḥ ca tejasaḥ dharma-bhedaḥ/ caturvidham ca tejas bhavati udbhūta-rūpa-sparśam
381.13	yathā āditya-raśmiḥ/ udbhūta-rūpam anudbhūta-sparśam yathā pradīpa-raśmiḥ/ ubhayam
381.14	ca pratyakṣam rūpasya udbhūta-tvāt/ udbhūta-sparśam anudbhūta-rūpam yathā vāri-sthitam
381.15	tejas/ anudbhūta-rūpa-sparśam yathā nāyanam tejas/ ubhayam ca apratyakṣam
381.16	rūpasya anudbhūti^aḥ//
381.17	[3-1-39(37)]karma-kāritaḥ ca indriyāṇām vyūhaḥ puruṣa-artha-tantraḥ
381.18	karma-kāritaḥ ca indriyāṇām vyūhaḥ puruṣa-artha-tantraḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ rūpa-
381.19	sparśa-anabhivyaktiḥ ca vyavahāra-prakLpta-arthā yadi nāyanaḥ raśmiḥ udbhūta-sparśaḥ
382.01	bhavet tena dṛśya-viśeṣe aneka-raśmi-sannidhāne sati dravyam dahyeta aneka-
382.02	raśmi-sannipāte ca sati vyavahita-tvāt dravyasya anupalabdhi^ā bhavitavyam/
382.03	atha manyase yathā āditya-raśmi-sambaddhe arthe nāyanaḥ raśmiḥ na vyavadhīyate evam
382.04	raśmi-antara-sannipāte api iti/ tad na vyatibhidya-artha-grāhaka-tvāt vyatibhidya-
382.05	āditya-raśmim tad-sambaddhena dravyena sambaddhyate udbhūta-rūpa-sparśavat-tve ca
382.06	cakṣus^aḥ yasya pūrvam sannipatitam cakṣus tad-itarena vyavahitam api na artham
382.07	gṛhṇīyāt/ atha aneka-raśmi-sannipāte sati samāna-jātīya-dravya^bhyaḥ
382.08	dravya-antaram raśmiḥ utpadyate iti/ evam sati samagra-asamagra-cakṣus^oḥ tulya-upalambhaḥ
382.09	prāpnoti/ na ce etad iṣṭam anupalabdhi^aḥ iti vyavahāra-prakLpti-artham ca nāyanasya
382.10	raśmi^aḥ anudbhūta-rūpa-sparśavat tvam iti/ sarva-dravyāṇām viśva-rūpaḥ vyūhaḥ puruṣa-
382.11	artha-kāritaḥ iti/ karman ca dharma-adharma-rūpam cetanasya upabhoga-artham iti//
382.12	[(3-1-38)]avyabhicārāt ca pratīghātaḥ bhautika-dharmaḥ
382.13	avyabhicārin^ḥ tu pratīghātaḥ bhautika-dharmaḥ/ bhautikam cakṣus
382.14	kuḍya-ādibhiḥ pratīghāta-darśanāt ghaṭa-ādi-vat iti/ apratīghātāt abhautikam
382.15	iti cet atha manyase yadi pratīghātāt bhautikam apratīghātāt
382.16	abhautikam dṛṣṭaḥ ca apratīghātaḥ kāca-abhra-paṭala-sphaṭika-antarita-upalabdhi^aḥ
382.17	na anekāntāt pradīpa-raśmi-vat bhautikasya apratīghātaḥ yathā pradīpa-raśmi^aḥ
382.18	iti/ sthāli-ādiṣu ca pācakasya tejasaḥ apratīghātāt iti  upapadyate
382.19	ca anupalabdhiḥ kāraṇa-bhedāt//
