383.01	[3-1-40(39)]madhyandina-ulkā-prakāśa-anupalabdhi-vat tad-anupalabdhiḥ
383.02	madhyandina-ulkā-prakāśa-anupalabdhi-vat tad-anupalabdhiḥ/ yathā upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-
383.03	prāptasya madhyandina-ulkā-prakāśasya nimittāt agrahaṇam abhibhavāt
383.04	tathā upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptasya cākṣuṣasya raśmi^aḥ agrahaṇam nimittāt rūpasya anudbhūti^aḥ
383.05	iti/ madhyandina-ulkā-prakāśaḥ na upalabhyate iti āditya-prakāśāt/
383.06	āditya-prakāśa-abhibhavāt iti ayuktam sarva-raśmivat-tva-prasaṅgāt/
383.07	evam sati sarvam loṣṭa-ādi-raśmi-vat prāpnoti/ atha loṣṭa-ādi-raśmyaḥ
383.08	kasmāt na upalabhyante iti anuktaḥ brūyāt āditya-raśmi-abhibhavāt iti ayuktam
383.09	tad etad uttara-dvārakam sūtram//
383.10	[3-1-41(40)]na rātri^i api anupalabdhi^aḥ
383.11	na rātri^i api anupalabdhi^aḥ/ yadi loṣṭa-ādi-raśmi^āh syuḥ diva-āditya-prakāśa-
383.12	abhibhavāt ca na upalabhyante iti rātri^i tarhi upalabhyeran/ rātri^i api
383.13	na upalabhyante vyañjaka-abhāvāt iti cet na hi yad yasya abhibhāvakam tad
383.14	tasya abhivyañjakam iti/ katham na prāpnoti loṣṭa-ādi-raśmīnām upalambhaḥ
383.15	anumānataḥ ca na upalabhyate loṣṭa-raśmiḥ iti api-śabdāt gamyate/ tad evam
383.16	sarva-pramāṇa-nivṛtti^aḥ na vidyate loṣṭa-raśmiḥ na punar evam cākṣuṣaḥ raśmiḥ niranumānaḥ
383.17	kuḍya-ādi^ah āvaraṇasya sāmarthyāt iti  tasya vidyamānasya bāhya-
383.18	prakāśa-anugrahāt viṣaya-upalabdhi^aḥ anabhivyaktitaḥ anupalabdhiḥ//
383.19	[3-1-42(41)]bāhya-prakāśa-anugrahāt viṣaya-upalabdhi^aḥ anabhivyaktitaḥ
383.20	anupalabdhiḥ
383.21	dṛṣṭānta-sthāne eva etad sūtram kim uktam bhavati yad khalu bāhya-prakāśam
384.01	apekṣate tasya anupalabdhiḥ rūpa-anabhivyaktitaḥ iti anudbhūti^aḥ yathā viṣakta-
384.02	avayavasya api atra dravyasya rūpa-anudbhūti^aḥ agrahaṇam  tathā cākṣuṣaḥ raśmiḥ bāhya-
384.03	prakāśa-anugraham apekṣate tasmāt asya api rūpasya anudbhūti^aḥ agrahaṇam iti/
384.04	kasmāt punar nāyanasya raśmi^aḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ abhibhavaḥ na kāraṇam ucyate iti
384.05	na ucyate//
384.06	[3-1-43(42)]abhivyakti^i ca abhibhavāt
384.07	abhivyakti^i ca abhibhavāt/ yad udbhūta-rūpam bāhya-prakāśa-anugrahaṇam ca
384.08	na apekṣate tad abhibhūyate yathā madhyandina-ulkā-prakāśaḥ anudbhūta-rūpaḥ ca
384.09	nāyanaḥ raśmiḥ bāhya-prakāśa-anugrahaṇam ca apekṣate yad anudbhūta-rūpam tad na abhibhūyate
384.10	yathā tad eva viṣakta-avayavam āpyam dravyam/ yad ca bāhya-prakāśa-anugraha-apekṣam
384.11	udbhūta-rūpam api tad na abhibhūyate yathā ghaṭa-ādi-dravyam iti saḥ ayam ubhaya-
384.12	viṣayaḥ abhibhavaḥ nāyana-raśmi^i anupapannaḥ iti vipratipatti-viṣayaḥ/ kṛṣṇa-
384.13	sāram raśmimat dravya-tve sati rūpa-upalabdhi^i niyatasya sādhana-aṅgasya
384.14	nimitta-tvāt pradīpa-vat iti/ atha vā raśmimat-cakṣus dravya-tve sati
384.15	niyata-tve ca sati sphaṭika-ādi-vyavahita-artha-prakāśaka-tvāt pradīpa-vat//
384.16	[3-1-44(43)]naktañcara-nayana-rasmi-darśanāt ca
384.17	naktañcara-nayana-raśmi-darśanāt ca iti dṛṣṭānta-sūtram/ mānuṣam cakṣus raśmimat
384.18	aprāpti-svabhāva-tve sati rūpa-ādi-upalabdhi-nimitta-tvāt naktañcara-cakṣus-vat
384.19	iti/ jāti-bhedāt indriya-prabhedaḥ iti cet atha manyase yathā viḍālatvam
384.20	jātiḥ vṛṣadaṃśe vartate na manuṣye evam raśmimat-viḍālasya eva cakṣus bhaviṣyati
385.01	na mānuṣasya iti na āvaraṇa-sāmarthyāt sati etasmin jāti-bhede yathā vṛṣadaṃśa-
385.02	prabhṛtīnām kuḍya-ādibhiḥ rāsmyaḥ  āvriyante tathā manuṣyāṇām api iti
385.03	samānam eva iti itaḥ ca bhautikāni indriyāṇi pratighāti-tvāt ghaṭa-vat
385.04	itivat bhūtam śrotram dravya-tve sati bāhya-prāpta-artha-prakāśaka-tvāt
385.05	ghrāṇa-ādi-vat iti  raśmi-artha-sannikarṣa-grahaṇāt tad-grahaṇam ayuktam anyathā api grahaṇāt
385.07	[3-1-45(44)]aprāpya-grahaṇam kāca-abhra-paṭala-sphaṭika-antarita-upalabdhi^aḥ
385.09	aprāpya-grahaṇam kāca-abhra-paṭala-sphaṭika-antarita-upalabdhi^aḥ/ tṛṇa-ādi-
385.10	sarva-dravyam kāce abhra-paṭale vā pratihanyate/ yadi cākṣuṣaḥ raśmiḥ
385.11	prāpta-artha-prakāśakaḥ syāt kāca-abhra-paṭala-eka-antarite prakāśakaḥ na syāt
385.12	asti tu tasmāt na prāpya-kārin cakṣus iti/ ataḥ eva abhautikam prāpya-
385.13	kāri-tvam bhautika-dharmaḥ iti  na kuḍya-antarita-anupalabdhi^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ iti//
385.14	[3-1-46(45)]kuḍya-antarita-anupalabdhi^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ
385.15	aprāpya-kāri-tve indriyasya na kuḍya-ādi-āvaraṇa-sāmarthyam asti iti uktam/
385.16	śeṣam bhāṣye//
385.17	[3-1-47(46)]apratighātāt sannikarṣa-upapattiḥ
385.18	apratighātāt sannikarṣa-upapatti^aḥ/ na kācaḥ abhra-paṭalam vā
385.19	raśmim pratibadhnāti saḥ apratihanyamānaḥ vyatibhidya arthena sambadhyate/
386.01	yad ca manyeta na bhautikasya asti apratīghātaḥ iti sarvam hi bhautikam
386.02	pratīghāta-dharmakam iti tad na//
386.03	[3-1-48(47)]āditya-raśmi^aḥ sphaṭika-antarite api dāhye avighātāt
386.05	āditya-raśmi^aḥ sphaṭika-antarite api dāhye avighātāt/ na
386.06	āditya-raśmi^aḥ avighātāt sphaṭika-antarite api avighātāt dāhye api avighātāt/
386.07	avighātāt iti pada-abhisambandhāt vākya-bhedaḥ/ na ekam
386.08	vākyam aneka-artham prativākyam ca artha-bhedaḥ iti śeṣam bhāṣye//
386.09	kaḥ ayam avighātaḥ avyuhyamāna-avayava-dravya-anupravesśaḥ/ yasya dravyasya
386.10	avayavāḥ na vyuhyante tasya antara-avayavaiḥ avyuhyamānasya yaḥ abhisambandhaḥ saḥ
386.11	avighātaḥ iti/ antarvyavasthitasya vā dravyasya vā avyuhyamāna-avayava-dravyasya
386.12	bahiravasthita-dravya-prāptiḥ dṛṣṭam kalaśe niṣaktānām apām bahir-śīta-sparśa-
386.13	grahaṇam na hi guṇasya asvatantrasya guṇinam antareṇa bahir-nirgamanam
386.14	yuktam iti/ tatra parispandaḥ tiryak-gamanam parisravaḥ pātaḥ iti//
386.15	[3-1-49(48)]na itaretara-prasaṅgāt
386.16	na itaretara-prasaṅgāt/ itara-dharmaḥ itaratra prayujyate itara-dharmaḥ
386.17	ca itaratra yadi avighātaḥ cakṣus-raśmi^aḥ sphaṭika-ādibhiḥ kuḍya-ādibhiḥ api
387.01	prāpnoti kuḍya-ādibhiḥ vā pratīghātaḥ sphaṭika-ādibhiḥ api prāpnoti  na eṣaḥ
387.02	doṣaḥ//
387.03	[3-1-50(49)]ādarśa-udakayoḥ prasāda-svābhāvyāt rūpa-upalabdhi-vat tad-
387.04	upalabdhiḥ
387.05	ādarśa-udakayoḥ prasāda-svābhāvyāt rūpa-upalabdhi-vat tad-upalabdhiḥ/ ādarśa-
387.06	udakayoḥ prasādaḥ rūpa-viśeṣaḥ saḥ ca svaḥ bhavati niyamāt/ kaḥ
387.07	punar ayam rūpa-viśeṣaḥ dravya-antara-asaṃyukta-dravya-samavāyaḥ tasya vā rūpa-
387.08	upalambhana-sāmarthyam svaḥ dharmaḥ saḥ ca svabhāvataḥ tasya yā vidyamānatā
387.09	udaka-ādiṣu tad-svābhāvyam prasāda-svabhāva-tvāt ādarśa-udaka-ādiṣu nayana-
387.10	raśmiḥ pratihanyate saḥ ca pratihataḥ pratinivṛtti^i sva-mukha-ādinā
387.11	sambadhyate tasya ca agra-sambandhāt yad abhimukham agram tad abhimukham mukha-ādi
387.12	paśyati iti yathā agrataḥ avasthitasya puruṣasya iti ādarśa-mukha-grahaṇam anukramena
387.13	tad api āśu-bhāvat na vibhāvyate ādarśa-rūpa-anugrahaṇāt tad-anurañcitaḥ
387.14	pratyayaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
387.15	[3-1-51(50)]dṛṣṭa-anumitānām hi niyoga-pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ
387.16	dṛṣṭa-anumitānām hi niyoga-pratiṣedha-anupapattiḥ/ pramāṇasya tattva-
387.17	viṣaya-tvāt dṛṣṭa-anumitāḥ khalu ime dravya-dharmāḥ yathā-bhūtāḥ bhavanti tathā-
387.18	bhūtāḥ eva pramāṇena pratipādyante imau ca bhavatā niyoga-pratiṣedhau
387.19	deśitau kva viṣaye syātām na ca etad yuktam na hi yathā dhūmena agni-
387.20	pratipattiḥ tathā udaka-pratipattiḥ api bhavatu iti/ na ca udaka-pratipattiḥ dhūmena
388.01	bhavati iti agni-pratipattiḥ api na yuktā arthāt niyuñjānaḥ bhavān upekṣaṇīyaḥ
388.02	pratighāta-apratighātayoḥ khalu upalabdhi-anupalabdhī vyavasthāpike vyavahita-
388.03	upalabdhi^ā anumīyate sphaṭika-ādibhiḥ apratīghātaḥ vyavahita-anupalabdhi^ā
388.04	ca kuḍya-ādibhiḥ pratīghātaḥ īt/ yadi prāpya-kārin cakṣus bhavati
388.05	atha kasmāt añjana-śalāka-ādi na upalabhyate na indriyena asambandhāt
388.06	indriyena sambaddhāḥ arthāḥ upalabhyante na ca añjana-śalāka-ādi indriyena
388.07	sambaddham adhiṣṭhānasya anindriya-tvāt raśmiḥ indriyam na adhiṣṭhānam na
388.08	raśminā añjana-śalākā sambaddhā iti//
388.09	evam tāvat bhautikāni indriyāṇi iti samarthitam kim punar ekam
388.10	indriyam āho anekam iti/ eke tu//
388.11	[3-1-52(51)]sthāna-anya-tve nānā-tvāt avayavi-nānā-sthāna-tvāt ca saṃśayaḥ
388.13	sthāna-anya-tve nānā-tvāt avayavi-nānā-sthāna-tvāt ca saṃśayaḥ/ eke tu
388.14	sthāna-anya-tve nānā-tvam dṛṣṭam yathā bahūnām ghaṭānām iti/ ekasya ca
388.15	nānā-sthāna-tvam yathā avayavinaḥ iti/ anupapanna-rūpaḥ ca ayam saṃśayaḥ/ katham
388.16	iti yadi tāvat evam kriyate sthāna-anya-tve nānātva-ekatva-darśanāt iti
388.17	tadā ekatve sthāna-anya-tvasya adarśanāt na samāna-dharmaḥ/ atha nānā-sthāna-tve
388.18	sati eka-aneka-tva-darśanāt saṃśayaḥ iti/ tadā dravyam nānā-sthānam anekam na
388.19	kim cit dṛṣṭam iti na samāna-dharma-tvam yad ghaṭa-ādi anekam tad na nānā-
389.01	sthānam api tu sthāna-anya-tvam tasya saḥ ayam saṃśayaḥ ubhayathā anupapannaḥ
389.02	indriya^su sthāna^su tu yuktaḥ kim nānā-sthānāni uta nānā-sthānam
389.03	indriya^su tu na sthāna-nānā-tvāt sambhavati na ca nānā-sthāna-tvāt iti
389.04	śarīra-vyatireki-tvāt sattvāt ca saṃśayaḥ/ śarīra-vyatiriktam ubhayathā
389.05	dṛṣṭam ekam anekam ca yathā ākāśam ghaṭa-ādi ca/ sat ca ubhayathā dṛṣṭam ekam anekam
389.06	ca tad evam ubhayathā indriya^su śarīra-vyatirekaḥ sattvam ca tena sandihyate
389.07	ekam indriyam kim punar tad//
389.08	[3-1-53(52)]tvac-avyatirekāt
389.09	tvac-avyatirekāt/ kaḥ punar ayam avyatirekaḥ sarva-adhiṣṭhāna-sambandhaḥ/
389.10	na hi na kim cit indriyam na tvacā prāptam sati bhāvaḥ vā na hi asatyām
389.11	tvaci kim cit artha-grahaṇam bhavati tasmāt tvac ekam indriyam/ na indriya-
389.12	antara-artha-anupalabdhi^aḥ iti loka-virodhaḥ ekam indriyam iti bruvāṇaḥ
389.13	lokam viruṇaddhi vidyamāne tvac-indriye sparśa-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ indriya-
389.14	antara-arthāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  andha-ādibhiḥ upalabhyeran na tu upalabhyante tasmāt na ekam
389.15	indriyam iti/ yad api avyatirekāt iti tad api na anekāntāt anekena
389.16	ca anindriyena sarvāṇi indriya-adhiṣṭhānāni pṛthivī-ādinā prāptāni iti anekāntaḥ/
389.17	avayava-upaghātāt iti cet atha bhavet etad tvac-avayavaḥ kaḥ cit
389.18	upahataḥ bhavati tad-upaghātāt andha-ādibhiḥ na upalabhyante rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti/
389.19	yathā tvac-avayava-viśeṣena eva dhūma-upalabdhiḥ yaḥ cakṣus^i tvac-avayava-viśeṣaḥ tad-
390.01	upabhātāt dhūma-sparśa-anupalabdhiḥ iti/ na vyāghātāt/ tvac-avayava-viśeṣena
390.02	dhūma-upalabdhi-vat rūpa-ādi-upalabdhiḥ iti bruvatā yad uktam ekam indriyam iti
390.03	tad vyāhatam bhavati prāc ekam indriyam tvac iti upagamya idānīm tvac-avayava-
390.04	viśeṣāḥ rūpa-ādi-grāhakāḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ pratiṣedhyam abhyanujānāti na
390.05	hi avayavi-anatiriktāḥ avayavāḥ iti/ yān ca avayavān rūpa-ādi-grāhakān
390.06	tvacaḥ manyante te kim indriya-ātmakāḥ uta na iti kim ca ataḥ yadi indriya-
390.07	ātmakāḥ na ekam indriyam atha indriya-ātmakāḥ na bhavanti na tarhi indriya-
390.08	grāhyāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti  sūtrena ca abhisambandhaḥ//
390.09	[3-1-54(53)]na yugapad-artha-anupalabdhi^aḥ
390.10	na yugapad-artha-anupalabdhi^aḥ/ yasya ekam indriyam tasya yugapad arthānām upalabdhiḥ
390.11	prasajyate/ katham iti ātman^ḥ manasā sambadhyate manas indriyena
390.12	indriyam sarva-arthaiḥ sannihitaiḥ iti/ ātma-indriya-manas-artha-sannikarṣa^bhyaḥ
390.13	yugapad-upalabdhyaḥ  prādus syuḥ iti/ ne eka-indriya-grāhyāṇām yugapad-upalabdhi-
390.14	prasaṅgāt yathā eka-indriya-vādinaḥ yugapad-aneka-artha-sannidhāne sati yugapad-
390.15	upalabdhiḥ doṣaḥ bhavati tathā aneka-indriya-vādinaḥ api eka-indriya-grāhya^su
390.16	yugapad-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ yaḥ eva atra parīhāraḥ saḥ eva mama api bhaviṣyati
390.17	iti/ atha ayam aduṣṭaḥ pakṣaḥ adeśyam tarhi etad yugapad-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ
390.18	iti/ atra ke cit parihāraṃ varṇayanti bubhutsā-bhedāt na yugapad-
390.19	upalabdhiḥ iti mama api evam yadi bubhutsā-bhedāt na yugapad-upalabdhiḥ labhyate
390.20	mama api na bubhutsā-bhedaḥ daṇḍa-vāritaḥ iti/ satyām ca bubhutsāyām
391.01	ayugapad-upalabdhi^i nyāyaḥ anyaḥ avadhāraṇīyaḥ iti/ karaṇa-tvāt iti anye - anye
391.02	tu bruvate parihāram karaṇa-tvāt iti/ karaṇasya vilāyam dharmaḥ
391.03	adhiṣṭhitam api anekām yugapad-kariyām na śaknoti kartum andhiṣṭhitam
391.04	ca na pravartate iti tulyam/ yasya api ekamindriyam tasya api karaṇatvam na
391.05	nivāryate iti tasmāt yugapad-artha-anupalabdhiḥ iti adoṣaḥ ayam iti/ evam
391.06	bruvāṇaḥ eka-indriya-vādin^ḥ vikalpya paryanuyojyaḥ/ yad tad ekam indriyam
391.07	sarva-artham iti manyase tad kim prāpya-kārin āho na iti kim ca ataḥ/ yadi
391.08	prāpya-kāri kim tad iti vaktavyam yadi tvac tvacā prāptānām rūpāṇām
391.09	agrahaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ/ atha aprāptam rūpam gṛhṇāti sparśa-ādiṣu api evam prasaṅgaḥ/
391.10	atha sāmikāri-indriyam kim cit prāptam gṛhṇāti kim cit ca aprāptam evam
391.11	sati karaṇa-dharma-atikramaḥ/ bhavatu karaṇa-dharma-atikramaḥ kim naḥ bādhyate
391.12	iti/ etasmin pakṣe na yugapad-upalabdhi-prasaṅgāt iti sūtram/ yathā
391.13	aneka-indriya-vādinaḥ eka-indriya-grāhya^su yugapad-upalabdhi-parasaṅge karaṇa-tvāt iti
391.14	parihāraḥ saḥ asya na bhavati karaṇa-dharma-atikramāt vyāhatam ca etad
391.15	karaṇam prāpya-kārin aprāpya-kārin ca iti/ asāhacaryāt ca na tvac sāhacaryam
391.16	nāma yasya ekam viṣaya-grahaṇam tasya dvitīyam api iti andha-badira-ādi-abhāva-prasaṅgaḥ//
391.17	[3-1-55(54)]vipratiṣedhāt ca na tvac ekā
391.18	vipratiṣedhāt ca na tvac ekā/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ eka-upaghāte tad-vināśe vā
391.19	sarva-anupalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ atha ekam indriyam bhavati tad-vighāte vināśe vā
392.01	sarva-anupalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ āvaraṇa-anupapatti^aḥ/ yadi tvac ekam indriyam bhavati
392.02	na āvaraṇasya sāmarthyam asti iti/ viprakṛṣṭa-avasthita-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ dūra-antika-
392.03	artha-anuvidhānam ca upalabdhi-anupalabdhi^oḥ na syāt aprāpya-kāri-tve tvac-
392.04	indriyasya antike grahaṇam dūre ca agrahaṇam iti etad na syāt/  pratiṣedhāt ca
392.05	nānātva-siddhi^i sthāpanā-hetuḥ api upādīyate pratiṣedhāt nānātvam sidhyati iti/
392.06	tad na buddhyāmahe pratiṣedhāt ekatva-sādhanam na syāt nānātvam katham sidhyati
392.07	iti na asādhanā kriyā iti ekatvam ca pratiṣeddhum katham śakyate pratiṣedhāt
392.08	ekatva-sādhanam anumānam nivartate na punar ekatvam anekatvam vā nivartate
392.09	iti/ katham tarhi ayam granthaḥ grāhyaḥ pratiṣedhāt ca anantaram nānātva-siddhi^i
392.10	sthāpanā-hetuḥ api upādīyate indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt/ atha vā pratiṣedhāt ca
392.11	nānātva-siddhi^i iti pratiṣedha-hetunā ekatva-pratiṣedhe kṛte arthāt nānātvam
392.12	siddhyati iti ayam arthaḥ/ katamaḥ punar asau pratiṣedha-hetuḥ yugapad-artha-upalabdhi-
392.13	prasaṅgāt iti ayam anena ekatvam pratiṣiddham tad evam avīta-siddhi^i vīta-
392.14	darśana-artham idam ucyate//
392.15	[3-1-56(55)]indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt
392.16	indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt iti sūtram/ arthaḥ prayojanam iti evam ādi
392.17	bhāṣye/ idam ca sūtram na katham cana nyāyena sambadhyate/ katham iti
392.18	vidhīyamānaḥ arthaḥ sādhyaḥ pañca-indriyāṇi iti indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt iti asambaddham
392.19	atha prayojana-pañca-tvam tad api evam eva/ atha grahaṇāni pakṣa^kriyante
392.20	tatra api aikāntikaḥ hetuḥ na asti iti/ etena viṣayāḥ vyākhyātāḥ/
393.01	idam tu sūtram evam syāt rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-śabda^su nānā-sādhana-kriyaḥ kartṛ^ḥ
393.02	eka-viṣaya-avasiti^i viṣaya-antara-siddhi^i karaṇa-antara-apekṣi-tvāt yasya
393.03	kartṛ^aḥ anekaḥ viṣayaḥ tasya anyatama-viṣaya-avasāye viṣaya-antara-siddhi^i
393.04	karaṇa-antara-apekṣi-tvam dṛṣṭam yathā aneka-śilpa-paryavadātasya puruṣasya
393.05	kriyā-avasāyāt kriyāyām karaṇa-antara-apekṣi-tvam/ tathā ca rūpa-ādi-anyatama-
393.06	viṣaya-avasāye viṣaya-antara-siddhi^i sādhana-antara-apekṣi-tvam asti
393.07	tasmāt rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-śabda^su nānā-sādhana-kriyaḥ kartṛ^ḥ//
393.08	[3-1-57(56)]na tad-artha-bahu-tvāt
393.09	na tad-artha-bahu-tvāt/ asya yathāśruti-utthānam asya ca arthaḥ indriya-pañcatva-
393.10	virodhaḥ/ na virodhāt artha-bahu-tvāt indriya-bahu-tvam iti bruvāṇaḥ yad
393.11	abhyupagatam ekam indriyam iti tad bādhate na asādhanāt na mayā artha-bahu-tvāt
393.12	indriya-bahu-tvam sādhyate yataḥ me virodhaḥ syāt api tu bhavatā indriya-artha-
393.13	pañca-tvāt pañca-indriyāṇi iti yad sādham uktam tasya mayā virodhaḥ deśyate
393.14	yadi indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt pañca-indriyāṇi bhavanti indriya-artha-bahu-tvāt bahūni
393.15	indriyāṇi prasajyante iti/  asya uddhārakam sūtram//
393.16	[3-1-58(57)]gandhatva-ādi-avyatirekāt gandha-ādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ
393.17	gandhatva-ādi-avyatirekāt gandha-ādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ/ gandhatva-ādibhiḥ sāmānyaiḥ
393.18	kṛta-vyavasthāḥ gandha-ādyaḥ  na sva-karaṇa-vyatirekena karaṇa-antarāṇi
393.19	prayojayanti yāvatsu gandhatvam vartate tāvatām eka-sādhana-sādhya-tvāt yaḥ
394.01	punar aparaḥ jāti-bhedaḥ iṣṭa-aniṣṭa-upekṣaṇīya-tva-ādiḥ na asau karaṇa-antaram
394.02	prayojayati evam sarvatra/ śeṣam bhāṣye/  yadi sāṃānyam saṃgrāhakam
394.03	prāptam indriyāṇām viṣayatva-avyatirekāt ekatvam//
394.04	[3-1-59(58)]viṣayatva-avyatirekāt ekatvam
394.05	asya arthaḥ avirodhaḥ eva na ukta-uttara-tvāt indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt iti asya
394.06	arthena ukta-uttaram etad/  asya ca arthasya jñāpana-artham na buddhi-lakṣaṇa-adhiṣṭhāna-
394.07	gati-ākṛti-jāti-pañcatva^bhyaḥ iti sūtram//
394.08	[3-1-60(59)]na buddhi-lakṣaṇa-adhiṣṭhāna-gati-ākṛti-jāti-pañcatva^bhyaḥ
394.09	tatra buddhyaḥ  eva lakṣaṇāni iti asya arthaḥ varṇitaḥ indriya-artha-pañca-tvāt
394.10	iti/ nānā-indriyam bhinna-adhiṣṭhāna-tvāt yasya adhiṣṭhānam bhinnam tad
394.11	anekam dṛṣṭam yathā ghaṭa-ādiṣu adhiṣṭhāna-bhedaḥ tathā indriyāṇām asti tasmāt
394.12	nānā iti/ adhiṣṭhāna-bhedaḥ asiddhaḥ iti cet na andha-badhira-ādi-abhāva-
394.13	prasaṅgāt/ yasya punar adhiṣṭhāna-bhedaḥ tasya bhede eka-adhiṣṭhāna-vināśe adhiṣṭhāna-
394.14	antara-āśrayasya avasthānam iti na doṣaḥ/ gati-bhedāt iti asya bhinna-
394.15	gati-tvāt iti prayogaḥ prasaṅgaḥ pūrvavat/ ākṛtiḥ parimāṇam iyattā/
394.16	sva-sthāna-parimāṇāni ghrāṇa-rasana-sparśanāni/ cakṣus bahis-naḥsṛtam niḥsṛtam viṣaya-
394.17	vyāpin tad-mahat/ śrotram tu ākāśam tad-adhiṣṭhāna-niyamena pravartate dharma-
394.18	adharma-saṃhitayā iṣṭa-aniṣṭa-upekṣaṇīya-śabda-sādhana-bhūtayā karṇa-śaṣkulī^ā yaḥ
395.01	ākāśasya sambandhaḥ tad-sambandha-anuvidhāyin ākāśam na vavara-antarena
395.02	asya ādinā śabdam upalakṣayati na anyatra iti tad-upakāra-pratīkāra-bhedāt
395.03	vā upakriyate pratikriyate ca iti na ca punar ākāśam nitya-tvāt upakriyate
395.04	na pratikriyate iti/ prakṛṣṭa-aprakṛṣṭa-śrotra-bhedaḥ api ataḥ eva/ jātiḥ
395.05	iti yonim pracakṣate/ pañca khalu imā indriya-yonyaḥ  pṛthivā-ādīni
395.06	bhūtāni indriyāṇām yonyaḥ  iti yoniḥ tādātmyam na punar tad-kārya-tvam
395.07	ākāśe sambhavati nitya-tvāt/  katham punar jñāyate bhūta-prakṛti-indriyāṇi
395.08	na avyakta-prakṛtīni iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt na avyakta-prakṛtīni indriyāṇi iti
395.09	ukta-uttaram etad//
395.10	[3-1-61(60)]bhūta-guṇa-viśeṣa-upalabdhi^aḥ tādātmyam
395.11	bhūta-guṇa-viśeṣa-upalabdhi^aḥ tādātmyam/ bhūta-guṇa-viśeṣaḥ gandha-rasa-rūpa-
395.12	sparśa-śabdāḥ viśeṣaka-tvāt viśeṣāḥ iti ucyate/ yathā gandhavattva-ādinā
395.13	pṛthivā-ap-ādibhyaḥ bhidyate rasa-ādibhiḥ ap-ādyaḥ  iti/ dṛṣṭaḥ hi bāhyānām
395.14	ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannānām pṛthivī-ādi-bhūtānām sva-guṇa-abhivyakti-niyamaḥ
395.15	asti ca ghrāṇa-ādibhiḥ api gandha-ādi-abhivyakti-niyamaḥ tena bhūta-guṇa-
395.16	viśeṣa-upalabdhi^aḥ bhūta-prakṛtīni indriyāṇi iti/ kim kim ātmakam iti yena
395.17	yad guṇa-abhivyaktiḥ/ tatra pārthivam ghrāṇam gandha-abhivyakti-hetu-tvāt bāhya-
395.18	pārthiva-dravya-vat/ evam śeṣa^su api//
395.19	gandha-ādyaḥ  pṛthivī-ādi-guṇāḥ iti uddiṣṭam uddeśaḥ ca niyoga-vikalpa-
396.01	samuccayaiḥ samānaḥ tatra niyogaḥ ekaika-guṇa-tvam vikalpaḥ kasya cit ekaḥ
396.02	kasya cit dvau iti evam ādiḥ samuccayaḥ sarvaḥ sarvatra ataḥ viśeṣaṇa-artham
396.03	gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdānām sparśa-paryantāḥ pṛthivī^āḥ ap-tejas-vāyūnām pūrvam
396.04	pūrvam apohya ākāśasya uttaraḥ niyama-arthe sūtre//
396.05	[3-1-62(61)]gandha-rasa-rūpa-sparśa-śabdānām sparśa-paryantāḥ pṛthivī^āḥ
396.06	[3-1-63(62)]ap-tejas-vāyūnām pūrvam pūrvam apohya ākāśasya uttaraḥ
396.07	catvāraḥ pṛthivī^ām ekaikaśaḥ apakarṣaḥ uttara^su sparśa-paryantānām iti
396.08	vibhakti-vipariṇāmaḥ tena kim kṛtam bhavati sparśa-paryāntānām paryantānām viniyuktānām
396.09	yaḥ uttaraḥ saḥ ākāśasya iti ākāśasya uttaraḥ śabdaḥ/ uttaraḥ
396.10	iti ayam tarap-nirdeśaḥ na yuktaḥ dvayoḥ dṛṣṭa-tvāt iha punar prakṛṣṭa-vāci-tve
396.11	sati uttamaḥ iti syāt/ na ayam no tarap-nirdeśaḥ api tu para-abhidhānam etad
396.12	yad uktam bhavati paraḥ iti tad uktam bhavati uttaraḥ iti/ tantram vā sparśasya
396.13	vivakṣita-tvāt bhavatu vā tarap-nirdesaḥ nanu uktam uttamaḥ iti prāpnoti na
396.14	sparśasya vivakṣita-tvāt gandha-ādibhyaḥ paraḥ sparśaḥ sparśāt ayam paraḥ iti
396.15	yāvat uktam bhavati tāvat uktam bhavati uttaraḥ iti  na sarva-guṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ//
396.16	[3-1-64(63)]na sarva-guṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ
396.17	na ayam guṇa^su viniyogaḥ sādhuḥ kasmāt pārthivena ghrāṇena pārthivānām
396.18	gandha-ādīnām upalambha-prasaṅgāt evam āpyena rasanena trayāṇām
396.19	taijasena cakṣus^ā dvayoḥ iti  katham tarhi ātma-guṇāḥ viniyoktavyāḥ//
397.01	[3-1-65(64)]ekaika-śyena-uttara-uttara-guṇa-sadbhāvāt uttara-uttarāṇām tad-
397.02	anupalabdhiḥ
397.03	ekaika-śyena-uttara-uttara-guṇa-sadbhāvāt uttara-uttarāṇām tad-anupalabdhiḥ yasmāt
397.04	ekaika-guṇāni bhūtāni tasmāt eka-guṇa-grahaṇam iti ekaika-śyenaḥ iti
397.05	sautraḥ nirdeśaḥ katham tarhi aneka-guṇāni bhūtāni gṛhyante iti/
397.06	saṃsargāt tu aneka-guṇa-grahaṇam iti/ saṃsṛṣṭā ap-ādibhiḥ pṛthivā tasmāt
397.07	aneka-guṇāḥ gṛhyate iti evam śeṣa^su saṃsargaḥ tu apratiṣiddhaḥ mithas
397.08	pañcānām ca iti/  niyamaḥ tarhi na prāpnoti pṛthivī^ām catvāraḥ na itara^su
397.09	saṃsargasya aniyamāt iti/ na na prāpnoti kasmāt viṣṭam hi aparam parena//
397.10	[3-1-66(65)]viṣṭam hi aparam parena
397.11	viṣṭam ap-ādinā parena aparam na aparena pṛthivī-ādinā param ap-ādi/
397.12	viṣṭatvam saṃyoga-viśeṣaḥ/  na pārthiva-āpyayoḥ pratyakṣa-tvāt iti/
397.13	[3-1-67(66)]na pārthiva-āpyayoḥ pratyakṣa-tvāt
397.14	na iti tri-sūtrīm anantara-uktām anena pratyācaṣṭe/ yasya ekaika-guṇāni
397.15	bhūtāni tasya taijasam eva dravyam rūpavat-tvāt pratyakṣam prāpnoti/
397.16	na pārthivam āpyam vā arūpa-tvāt/ asya sūtrasya vikalpataḥ anekaḥ
397.17	sūtra-arthaḥ kalpyate iti bhāṣye varṇitam  tad eva nyāya-viruddham pravādam
397.18	pratiṣidhya na sarva-guṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ iti deśitam tad samādhīyate
398.01	[3-1-68(67)]pūrva-pūrva-guṇa-utkarṣāt tad tad pradhānam
398.02	pūrva-pūrva-guṇa-utkarṣāt tad tad pradhānam tasmāt na sarva-guṇa-upalabdhiḥ ghrāṇa-
398.03	ādīnām indriyāṇām pūrvam pūrvam indriyam parasmāt parasmāt pradhānam/
398.04	kā pradhānatā catur-guṇatva-ādiḥ kaḥ guṇa-utkarṣaḥ kha-guṇa-abhivyakti-sāmarthyam
398.05	yena guṇena yad dravyam utkṛṣyate saḥ tad-jātīya-abhivyañjaka-tvāt utkṛṣṭaḥ
398.06	bhavati yathā bāhyāṇām dravyānām iti/ asti ca indriyāṇām api sva-
398.07	guṇa-abhivyakti-niyamaḥ tasmāt na sarva-guṇa-upalabdhiḥ/ yaḥ punar gandha-guṇa-
398.08	tvāt ghrāṇam gandha-grāhakam iti pratijānīte tasya sarva-guṇa-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ
398.09	kasmāt punar na sarvam pārthivam ghrāṇam iti vyavasthā-niyama-jñāpana-artham/
398.10	tad-vyavasthānam tu bhūyastvāt iti sūtram//
398.11	[3-1-69(68)]tad-vyavasthānam tu bhūyastvāt
398.12	kim punar idam bhūyastvam artha-nirvṛtti-samarthasya pravibhaktasya dravyasya
398.13	saṃsargaḥ puruṣa-saṃskāra-kāritaḥ bhūyastvam dṛṣṭaḥ hi prakarṣe bhūyastva-śabdaḥ
398.14	yathā loke prakṛṣṭaḥ viṣayaḥ bhūyas^ḥ iti ucyate/ yathā ca pṛthak-artha-kriyā-
398.15	samarthāni puruṣa-saṃskāra-upagrahāt viṣa-oṣadhi-maṇi-prabhṛtīni dravyāṇi
398.16	nirvartyante iti  sva-guṇāt na upalabhante indriyāṇi kasmāt iti cet
398.17	kena kāraṇena svāni gandha-ādīni indriyāṇi na upalabhante iti//
398.18	[3-1-70(69)]saguṇānām indriya-bhāvāt
399.01	saguṇānām indriya-bhāvāt saha guṇena indriyam bhavati nirguṇam tu
399.02	na indriyam ataḥ na gṛhṇāti  yadi punari indriyasya sahakārin^ḥ ca gandhaḥ
399.03	syāt grāhyaḥ ca iti//
399.04	[3-1-71(70)]tena eva tasya agrahaṇāt ca
399.05	tena eva tasya agrahaṇāt ca yadi indriyam sva-gandham gṛhṇīyāt tena asau
399.06	indriya-gandhaḥ na indriya-grāhyaḥ syāt gandham ca gṛhṇat-indriyam ātmānam gṛhṇīyāt
399.07	ātman^ḥ hi asya gandhaḥ iti/ na ca ātma-sādhanam karaṇam asti iti dṛṣṭānta-
399.08	virodhaḥ na ca etad pratyakṣam yadvat ghrāṇa-gandhaḥ ghrāṇa-grāhyaḥ iti/
399.09	kasmāt punar idam na deśyate ghrāṇena ghrāṇam kasmāt na gṛhyate iti/
399.10	na deśyate adṛṣṭa-tvāt na dṛṣṭam ghrāṇena ghrāṇasya grahaṇam iti ataḥ na
399.11	deśyate iti tulyam ghrāṇena svasya gandhasya agrahaṇam iti etad api na adeśanīyam/
399.12	sva-guṇāt na upalabhante indriyāṇi kasmāt iti tulyaḥ
399.13	hi ubhayatra hetu-abhāvaḥ//
399.14	[3-1-72(71)]na śabda-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ
399.15	na śabda-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ sva-guṇāt na upalabhante indriyāṇi iti etad ayuktam/
399.16	sva-guṇaḥ upalabhyate ākāśena śabdaḥ iti/  na punar śrotram saśabdam
399.17	indriyam iti yathā itarāṇi saguṇāni indriyāṇi ne evam ākāśam iti/
399.18	kasmāt śabdasya itara-guṇa-vaidharmyāt ākāśasya itara-dravya-vaidharmyāt asya arthasya
399.19	jñāpana-artham tad-upalabdhiḥ itaretara-dharma-vaidharmyāt iti sūtram//
399.20	[3-1-73(72)]tad-upalabdhiḥ itaretara-dharma-vaidharmyāt
400.01	na śabdaḥ śabdasya vyañjakaḥ na ākāśam saguṇam indriyam iti/ kim
400.02	punar idam śrotram ākāśam pariśeṣāt/ tatra ātman^ḥ tāvat na śrotram kartṛ-
400.03	tvāt śrotṛ^ḥ ātman^ḥ na śrotram/ manas śrotram bhaviṣyati iti api na
400.04	manasaḥ śrotra-tve badhira-ādi-abhāvaḥ sarva-viṣaya-tvāt ca manasaḥ sarva-viṣayam ca
400.05	śrotram prāpnoti/ pṛthivī-ādi api na śrotram ghrāṇa-ādi-bhāvena viniyogāt
400.06	ghrāṇa-ādi-viniyuktāḥ pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  śrotra-bhāve ca ghrāṇa-ādīnām tad-vaikalye
400.07	śabda-anupalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ/ tvacaḥ śrotra-bhāve badhira-ādi-abhāvaḥ sarva-
400.08	upaghāte ca prāyaṇam/ diś-kālayoḥ srotra-bhāve ca śabdasya anya-guṇa-tvāt
400.09	agrahaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ/ tad-guṇa-tvam iti cet nāmni vivādaḥ yasya śabdaḥ
400.10	guṇaḥ tad ākāśam bhavatā saṃjñā-mātram bhidyate tau diś-kālau iti/
400.11	ākāśa-pratyākhyānam vā anya-guṇa-tvāt śabdasya yadi śabdaḥ diś-kāla-guṇaḥ
400.12	bhavati ākāśam tarhi pratyākhyātam/ na hi śabdam antareṇa ākāśa-asti-tve
400.13	liṅgam iti yathā diś-kālayoḥ para-apara-ādi liṅgam iti na ca
400.14	dravya-antaram śiṣyate na ca guṇānām śrotra-bhāvaḥ yuktaḥ na ca karmaṇām
400.15	na ca sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāyānām sāmarthya-anupalabdhi^aḥ na ca idam śrotram
400.16	na asti śabda-upalabdhi-liṅgam śiṣyate ca ākāśam tasmāt ākāśam śrotram iti//
400.17	iti - auddyotakare nyāyavārttike tṛtīyasya adhyāyasya
400.18	ādyam āhnikam//401.01	tṛtīye adhyāye
401.02	dvitīyam āhnikam/
401.03	namas parama-ātmane/
401.04	parīkṣitāni indriyāṇi arthāḥ ca buddhi^aḥ idānīm parīkṣā-kramaḥ/ sā
401.05	kim anityā nityā vā iti dharma-vicāraḥ sattvasya adhigata-tvāt tasyām ca
401.06	saṃśayam upapādayati//
401.07	[3-2-1]karma-ākāśa-sādharmyāt saṃśayaḥ
401.08	karma-ākāśa-sādharmyāt saṃśayaḥ/ ubhābhyām sādharmyam asparśavat-tvam
401.09	viśseṣaḥ upajanana-apāya-dharmaka-tvam viparyayaḥ ca buddhi^i na upalabhyate tataḥ
401.10	saṃśayaḥ/ anupapanna-rūpaḥ khalu ayam saṃśayaḥ sarva-śarīrin^āṃ pratyātma-
401.11	vedanīya-tvāt sarva-śarīrin^ām pratyātma-vedanīyam etad yad uta anityā
401.12	buddhiḥ iti traikālya-vyakti^aḥ ca buddhi^i traikālyam vyajyamānam dṛṣṭam jānāmi
401.13	jñāsyāmi ajñāsiṣam iti/ na ca upajanana-apāya-avantarena traikālya-vyaktiḥ na
401.14	hi anupajanana-apāya-dharma^su ākāśa-ādiWu traikālyam vyajyate na ca viśeṣa-
401.15	darśane sati yuktaḥ saṃśayaḥ tasmāt saṃśaya-prakriyā-anupapattiḥ iti/ śeṣam
401.16	bhāṣye/  dṛṣṭi-pravāda-upālambha-artham prakaraṇam na ca buddhi^aḥ anityatvam pratipādyam
402.01	api tu parasya darśanam nivartanīyam iti/ evam hi paśyantaḥ
402.02	pravadanti saṃkhyā nityā buddhiḥ iti sādhanam ca pracakṣate viṣaya-
402.03	pratyabhijñānāt iti//
402.04	[3-2-2]viṣaya-pratyabhijñānāt
402.05	yam pūrvam ajñāsiṣam tam imam jānāmiti jñānayoḥ samane arthe
402.06	pratisandhānam avasthitāyāḥ buddhi^aḥ upapannam/ buddhi-nānā-tve pratyabhijñāna-
402.07	anupapattiḥ yathā puruṣa-antara-buddhiṣu//
402.08	[3-2-3]sādhyasama-tvāt ahetuḥ
402.09	sādhyasama-tvāt ahetuḥ/ yathā eva buddhi^aḥ nitya-tvam sādhyam tathā eva buddhi^i pratyabhijñānam
402.10	api sādhyam kasmāt cetana-dharmasya karaṇe anupapatti^aḥ puruṣa-
402.11	dharmaḥ khalu ayam jñānam darśanam upalabdhiḥ bodhaḥ iti/ cetanaḥ hi pūrva-jñātam
402.12	artham pratyabhijānāti iti tasya eva tasmāt hetu^aḥ nitya-tvam yuktam/ kriyā-anādhāra-
402.13	tvāt karaṇasya/ na hi karaṇam kriyāyāḥ ādhāraḥ bhavati upalabdhiḥ
402.14	ca kriyā sā ca na karaṇe varttitum arhati iti/ sva-kriyā-ādhāra-
402.15	tvāt ayuktam iti cet atha manyase sarvam kārakam sva-kriyāyāḥ ādhāraḥ
402.16	bhavati iti ataḥ ayuktaḥ kriyā-anādhāraḥ iti satyam bhavati sva-kriyā-ādhāraḥ
402.17	na punar sva-kriyātaḥ karaṇa-vyapadeśaḥ/ sva-kriyāsu sarvam kārakam kartṛ
402.18	bhavati karaṇa-bhāvam ca āśritya buddhi^i vicāraḥ tasmāt ayuktam etad/ sarva-
402.19	kartṛ-tva-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet yadi punar sva-kriyā-nimittakaḥ kartṛ-vyapadeśaḥ sarvam
402.20	kārakam kartṛ prāptam satyam etad na abhyupagamāḥ eva doṣa-tvena deśyante iti/
402.21	kartṛ-karaṇa-ādi-vyapadeśaḥ ca katham pradhāna-kriyā-apekṣaḥ kartṛ-karaṇa-ādi-vyapadeśaḥ
403.01	yad artham karaṇam upādīyate sā pradhāna-kriyā tasyām ca sādhana-bhāve
403.02	vivakṣite bhede vā avivakṣite kārakam iti anena śabdena abhilapyante
403.03	kartṛ-ādyaḥ  yadā tu bhedaḥ vivakṣitaḥ tadā kartṛ-karaṇa-karma-śabda-vācyāni
403.04	iti/ kāraka-śabdena api sāmānyena abhidhīyamāna^su karaka^su kāraka^su yaḥ
403.05	svagataḥ viṣayaḥ itaretara-vyāvṛttaḥ saḥ abhidhīyante anyasya abhāvāt
403.06	pradhāna-kriyā-apekṣam tu kārakāṇām kartṛ-ādi-abhidhānam iti/ tatra yad
403.07	itara-aprayojyam itara-kāraka-prayoktṛ ca tad kartṛ evam śeṣa^su kāraka-śabdāḥ
403.08	pūrvavat vaktavyāḥ/ yadi anya-kāraka-aprayojya-tvam kartṛ-tvam nanu ayam api kāraka-
403.09	upādānena prayujyate na prayujyate phalasya prayojaka-tvāt phalam kartṛ^am
403.10	prayojayati na kārakam karaṇa-ādi na ca phalam kārakamiti teṣām
403.11	tannāntarīyakam upādānam/ na hi karaṇa-ādi kāraka-upādānam antareṇa
403.12	kriyā sambhavati iti tannāntarīyaka-tvāt kārakāṇi upādīyante iti/
403.13	tasmāt cetana-dharmaḥ karaṇe na sambhavati iti upetya pratyabhijñānam antaḥkaraṇe
403.14	tasya eka-aneka-vyāvṛtti^aḥ asādhāraṇa-tā/ na hi pratyabhijñānam ekatve dṛṣṭam na
403.15	nānātve iti pratyabhijñānena svarūpa-avadhāraṇe ca sati viruddhaḥ yadā
403.16	ca idam pratyabhijñānam nirūpyate tadā buddhi-bhedam pratipādayati pratyabhijñānam
403.17	hi nāma ādya-pratyakṣa-virodhe dvitīya-darśane prāk-āhita-saṃśkāra-
403.18	abhivyakti^i smṛti-pūrvam tṛtīyam darśanam/ na ca buddhi-abheda-vādinaḥ
403.19	dvitīyam api tāvat darśanam asti kutaḥ tṛtīyam iti/ pratyaya-bhedaḥ iti
404.01	cet atha manyase avasthitāyāḥ buddhi^aḥ pratyayāḥ tirobhavanti vyajyante ca/
404.02	tatra ādya-darśana-tirobhāve pratyaya-antara-āvirbhāve ca pratyabhijñānam iti tad na
404.03	yuktam tad-vyatirekena buddhi-svabhāva-anavadhāraṇāt/ yām tām pratyaya-vyatiriktām
404.04	buddhim bhavantaḥ kalpayanti tasyāḥ kaḥ svabhāvaḥ iti vaktavyam/
404.05	asmākam tu pratyaya-mātram buddhiḥ artha-grahaṇam hi buddhiḥ iti/ darśana-dṛṣṭasya
404.06	sparśanena pratyabhijñānāt nānātve pratyabhijñānam iti viruddhaḥ pradīpa-
404.07	antara-dṛṣṭasya pradīpa-antarena iti/ yadi buddhiḥ adhyavasyati atha buddhi^i
404.08	vartamānena jñānena cetanaḥ kim karoti cetayate iti kaḥ cetayate
404.09	yadi yasya adhyavasāyaḥ saḥ cetayate ciruddham etad/ atha ātman^ḥ cetayate katham
404.10	buddhi^i vartamānena pratyayena ātman^ḥ cetayate na hi anyatra vartamānayā kriyayā
404.11	anyasya tadvat-tā bhavati/ atha puruṣaḥ cetayate buddhiḥ jānīte iti na idam
404.12	jñānāt artha-antaram ucyate yāvat uktam bhavati cetayate tāvat uktam bhavati jānīte
404.13	iti/ buddhiḥ jñāpayati iti cet atha manyase buddhiḥ jñāpayati ātman^ḥ
404.14	jānīti iti addhā jānīte ca puruṣaḥ buddhiḥ jñāpayati iti/ adhyavasāya-
404.15	cetanayoḥ ca svabhāva-bhedaḥ vaktavyaḥ bhavatā api ekatvam vaktavyam deśa-kāla-
404.16	svabhāva-abhedāt yasmāt avideśam avikālam ca buddhi^ā adhyavasitam ātman^ḥ api adhyavasyati/
404.17	svabhāva-abhedaḥ asiddhaḥ iti cet atha manyase satyam abhinna-
404.18	kālam abhinna-deśam ca buddhi^ā adhyavasitam ātman^ḥ api adhavasyati na punar yaiḥ acetana-
404.19	ātman^ḥ eva adhyavasāyaḥ iti/ na pratiśarīram aneka-puruṣa-prasaṅgāt yadi anyaḥ cetayitṛ^ḥ
404.20	anyaḥ ca adhyavasātṛ^ḥ bhavati anyaḥ pratijānīte anyaḥ paśyati anyaḥ
404.21	śṛṇoti mantṛ^ḥ iti evam ādiṣu puruṣa-antarāṇi khalu imāni draṣṭṛ^ḥ śrotṛ^ḥ
405.01	mantṛ^ḥ iti na ekasya ete dharmāḥ iti/ atra bhavatā kim vaktavyam abhinna-arthāḥ
405.02	ete śabdāḥ iti etad vaktavyam abhinna-arthāḥ tad ete śabdāḥ bhavanti nanu
405.03	atra api arthaḥ na bhidhyate cetanaḥ adhyavasātṛ^ḥ mantṛ^ḥ boddhṛ^ḥ iti tasmāt ayuktam
405.04	etad buddhiḥ adhyavasyati ātman^ḥ cetayate iti/ yadi punar idam pratyabhijñānam
405.05	manasaḥ nityatva-sādhakam syāt na yuktam evam bhavitum manasaḥ
405.06	nityatva-abhyupagamāt abhyupagamyate manasaḥ nityatvam iti tatra sādhanam
405.07	anarthakam anekāntāt ca na pratyabhijñānāt karaṇa-ekatvam sidhyati dṛṣṭam
405.08	karaṇa-bhede api pratyabhijñānam jñātṛ^aḥ eka-tvāt darśana-dṛṣṭasya sparśanena
405.09	pratyabhijñānāt pradīpa-dṛṣṭasya pradīpa-antarena iti/  yad tu idam manyate
405.10	buddhi^aḥ avasthitāyāḥ yathā-viṣayam vṛttyaḥ  prādurubhavanti vṛttiḥ ca vṛttimataḥ
405.11	na anyā iti tad ca na yugapad-agrahaṇāt
405.12	[3-2-4]na yugapad-agrahaṇāt
405.13	vṛttimataḥ avasthānāt vṛttiavasthānam iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
405.14	[3-2-5]apratyabhijñāne ca vināśa-prasaṅgaḥ
405.15	apratyabhijñāne ca vināśa-prasaṅgaḥ vṛttīnām apagame vṛttimataḥ api
405.16	apagamaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/  avibhu ca ekam manas paryāyena indriyaiḥ sambaddhyate
405.17	iti//
405.18	[3-2-6]krama-vṛtti-tvāt ayugapad-grahaṇam
405.19	krama-vṛtti-tvāt ayugapad-grahaṇam/ vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ nānā-tvāt iti
405.20	sūtra-arthaḥ/ vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ eka-tve prādurbhāva-tirobhāvayoḥ abhāvaḥ iti//
406.01	[3-2-7]apratyabhijñānam ca viṣaya-antara-vyāsaṅgāt
406.02	apratyabhijñānam ca viṣaya-antara-vyāsaṅgāt iti vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ bhede
406.03	sati upapadyate iti/ ekatve tu anarthakaḥ vyāsaṅgaḥ iti  vibhu-tve ca antaḥkaraṇasya
406.04	paryāyena indriyaiḥ saṃyogaḥ//
406.05	[3-2-8]na gati-abhāvāt
406.06	na gati-abhāvāt/ prāptāni antaḥkaraṇena indriyāṇi iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ vyāpitve
406.07	ca yugapad-aneka-indriya-sambandhāt yugapad-grahaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ ca doṣaḥ/ aṇu^i
406.08	api manasi eka-indriya-grāhya^su yugapad-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet karaṇa-tvāt na
406.09	yugapad grahaṇam/ mama api evam kasmāt na parihāraḥ na karaṇa-bhede dṛṣṭa-
406.10	tvāt/ yāni khalu abhinnāni karaṇāni tāni ekena eva adhiṣṭhāyakena
406.11	adhiṣṭhitāni yugapad-pravartamānāni dṛṣṭāni/ yathā ubhaya-hasta-adhiṣṭhite
406.12	vāsyau pravartete tathā indriyāṇi vyāpakena antaḥkaraṇena adhiṣṭhīyante iti
406.13	yugapad-pravṛttiḥ prasaktā tad-prasakti^i ca yugapad-grahaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ iti/
406.14	gati-abhāvāt ca vibhunaḥ antaḥkaraṇasya prāpti-arthasya gamanasya abhāvāt na
406.15	ca ayugapad-pratyaya-utpatti^i pramāṇam asti yena pramāṇena pratiṣiddham api
406.16	pratyaya-ayaugapadyam pratipadyeta yathā katham cit pratiṣiddham api pratipadyate
406.17	tathā cakṣus^aḥ gatiḥ pratiṣiddhā kena cit sannikarṣa-viprakarṣayoḥ tulya-
406.18	kāla-grahaṇāt yasmāt sannikṛṣṭam pāṇim viprakṛṣṭam candramasam ekasmin
406.19	kāle cakṣus^ā pratipadyate tasmāt na gatimat cakṣus iti vipratiṣiddhā api
406.20	satī gatiḥ vyavadhāna-pratighātena anumīyate iti/ na ca yugapad-pratyaya-
407.01	utpatti^i pramāṇam asti saḥ ayam na antaḥkaraṇe vivādaḥ na tasya nitya-tve/
407.02	kutaḥ tarhi vivādaḥ tasya vibhu-tve tad ca pramāṇataḥ anupalabdhi^aḥ na asti/ na
407.03	hi antaḥkaraṇasya vibhutva-pratipādakam pramāṇam asti ekam antaḥkaraṇam nānā
407.04	ca imāḥ cakṣus-vijñāna-ādi-ātmikāḥ vṛtti^āh iti/ etad ca vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ
407.05	eka-tve anupapannam yasya punar vṛtti-vṛttimatoḥ nānā-tvam tasya antaḥkaraṇa-
407.06	sādhanaḥ cakṣus-ādi-sādhanaḥ ca ātman^ḥ arthān upalabhate iti yuktam/ etena
407.07	viṣaya-antara-vyāsaṅgaḥ pratyuktaḥ/ puruṣaḥ jānīyte na antaḥkaraṇam iti
407.08	kaḥ punar ayam vyāsaṅgaḥ abhīṣṭa-viṣayaḥ aneka-vijñāna-utpādaḥ saḥ ca
407.09	puruṣasya na antaḥkaraṇasya yaḥ punar ayam sannidhāna-asannidhāna-lakṣaṇaḥ antaḥkaraṇasya
407.10	vyāsaṅgaḥ saḥ anujñātah iti/  ekam antaḥkaraṇam nānā vṛttyaḥ
407.11	iti na etad nyāya-vat-saṃcakṣāṇakaḥ pratipattum arhati yathā eva antaḥkaraṇam ekam
407.12	tathā vṛttiḥ iti/ dṛṣṭa-virodhaḥ iti cet atha manyase yadi ca antaḥkaraṇāt
407.13	na vṛttiḥ bhidyate nanu dṛṣṭam virudhyate rūpam rasaḥ gandhaḥ sparśaḥ
407.14	iti dṛṣṭaḥ pratyaya-bhedaḥ na syāt na na syāt viṣaya-bheda-anuvidhānāt
407.15	vṛttiḥ abhinnā api viṣaya-bhedam anuvidhīyamānā bhinnā iva khyāti yathā upadhāna-
407.16	bhedam anuvidhīyamānam sphaṭika-ādi-dravyam bhinnam iva prakāśate/
407.17	asya ca arthasya jñāpana-artham sphaṭika-anyatva-abhimāna-vat tad-anyatva-abhimānaḥ
407.18	iti sūtram//
407.19	[3-2-9]sphaṭika-anyatva-abhimāna-vat tad-anyatva-abhimānaḥ
407.20	na anekāntāt/ ubhayathā khalu ayam nānātva-abhimānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ nānā-
407.21	bhūta^su gandha-ādiṣu abhinne ca sphaṭke iti yathāśruti-sūtre hetu-abhāvaḥ
407.22	dṛṣṭānta-mātra-tvāt dṛṣṭānta-mātram etad na atra hetuḥ asti iti/ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ
408.01	yaḥ ayam dṛṣṭāntaḥ sphaṭika-vat iti vikalpaḥ atra kim ayam sphaṭikaḥ
408.02	upadhāna-bhedena bhidyate uta na iti kim ca ataḥ yadi bhidyate na dṛṣṭāntaḥ
408.03	atha na bhidyate nānātva-abhidhānaḥ katham yadi upadhāna-bhedāt bhinnam upadhānam
408.04	iti katham pratipadyase yadi pratyaya-bhedāt vyāghātaḥ abhinnā vṛttiḥ
408.05	pratyayaḥ ca bhinnaḥ iti vyāghātaḥ pratyaya-paryāya-tvāt vṛtti^aḥ/ atha bhavatām
408.06	vṛttīnām bhede kim pramāṇam jñānānām kramena upajana-apāya-darśanam kramena
408.07	jñānāni upajāyante apayanti ca iti dṛśyate eka-aneka-viṣaya-tvāt ca pratyaya-
408.08	nānātva-abhidhānasya eka-aneka-viṣaya-adhigatiḥ katham ekasmin ca viṣaye
408.09	nānātva-abhidhānaḥ sphaṭike dṛṣṭaḥ nānā-bhūta^su ca gandha-ādiṣu nānātva-
408.10	abhimānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tena nānātva-abhimānasya ubhayathā dṛṣṭa-tvāt idam ekam
408.11	idam anekam iti na yuktaḥ pratyayaḥ iti/ yaḥ ca ayam abhinne sphaṭike
408.12	bheda-pratyayaḥ saḥ api pratyay-bhedam antareṇa na yuktaḥ/ na hi pratyaya-
408.13	abheda-vādinaḥ sphaṭike nīla-ādi-pratyaya-bhedaḥ yuktaḥ iti sphaṭike
408.14	nīla-ādi-pratyayaḥ asambandhāt na yuktaḥ na hi nīlam dravyam cakṣus-sannikṛṣṭam
408.15	iti/ atha sannikṛṣṭam tad eva nīlam na sphaṭikaḥ iti sphaṭika-samānādhikaraṇaḥ
408.16	sphaṭike nīla-pratyayaḥ na yuktaḥ/ na nairantarya-darśanāt
408.17	nairantaryena avasthite sphaṭika-nīla-dravye cakṣus^ā sannikṛśyete tad-sannikarṣāt
408.18	nīla-guṇa-ādhyāropena sphaṭike mithyā-pratyayaḥ nīlaḥ iti yathā
408.19	sāntara^su antarāla-adarśanāt saṃyukta-pratyayaḥ iti/ yasya ca pratyayaḥ na
408.20	bhidyate tasya trīṇi pramāṇāni iti śāstram vyāhanyate na hi
408.21	pratyaya-abhede pramāṇabhedaḥ sambhavati iti/ viṣaya-bhedāt tritvam iti cet
409.01	atha manyase viṣayaḥ tredhā bhinnaḥ tena anurañjitaḥ pratyayaḥ tredhā eva bhavati
409.02	tad na yuktam pramāṇa-abhede viṣaya-bheda-anadhigati^aḥ/ na hi pramāṇa-bhedam
409.03	antareṇa viṣaya-bhedaḥ adhigantum śakyate iti/ viṣaya-tādātmyāt iti cet
409.04	atha manyase viṣayaḥ eva asau tathā vyavasthitaḥ tādātmyāt tathā ākhyāti iti
409.05	tad na pramāṇa-vaiyarthyāt evam sati vyartham pramāṇam na viṣaya-bhedam pramāṇa-
409.06	bhedaḥ avagamayati viṣayaḥ tu tādātmyāt tathā vyavatiṣṭhate iti yathā adhyavasyati
409.07	tathā cetayate iti ca vyāghātaḥ/ aprakāravat-tvāt prakāravati
409.08	etad yuktam aprakāre na yuktam buddhiḥ tam pratyayam upalabhate iti ca virodhaḥ
409.09	adhikaraṇa-ādheyayoḥ abhedāt yaḥ pratyayaḥ saḥ buddhiḥ iti adhikaraṇa-ādheyayoḥ
409.10	abhedaḥ/ evam ca sati buddhi-stham pratyayam upalabhate iti vyāhatam//
409.11	sphaṭika-anyatva-abhimāna-vat tad-anyatva-abhimānaḥ iti etad amṛṣyamāṇaḥ
409.12	kṣaṇika-vādin^ḥ āha/ sphaṭike api aparāpara-utpatti^aḥ kṣaṇika-tvāt vyaktīnām ahetuḥ//
409.13	[3-2-10]sphaṭike api aparāpara-utpatti^aḥ kṣaṇika-tvāt vyaktīnām
409.14	ahetuḥ
409.15	[3-2-11]niyama-hetu-abhāvāt yathā-darśanam abhyanujñā
409.16	sphaṭikasya abhedena avasthitasya anyatva-abhimānaḥ iti ayuktam kṣaṇika-
409.17	tvāt vyaktīnām kṣaṇikatvam upacaya-apacaya-prabandha-darśanāt/ yasmāt śarīra-
409.18	ādiṣu pakti-nirvṛttam rasa-dravyam pratikṣaṇam upacīyate vā apacīyate ca
409.19	upacayāt vyaktīnām utpādaḥ apacayāt vyakti-nirodhaḥ tau ca utpāda-nirodhau
409.20	tasya kāla-antarena dṛṣṭau ataḥ avagamyate pratikṣaṇam upacīyate ca apacīyate
410.01	ca śarīram iti/ tasya khalu pratikṣaṇam upacaya-apacayau na
410.02	dṛṣṭau tasya kāla-antarena api na bhavataḥ yathā bhūmi^aḥ apacyamānāyāḥ
410.03	pākajānām iti/ pacyamāna-bhūmi-pākaja-vat ca pratikṣaṇam viśeṣaḥ iti/
410.04	asya prayogaḥ pratikṣaṇam śarīram anyat ca anyat ca bhavati bāhya-pratyaya-abhede
410.05	sati ante viśeṣa-darśanāt pacyamāna-bhūmi-pākaja-vat apacyamāna-bhūmi-
410.06	pākaja-vat ca iti/  tad idam upacaya-apacaya-prabandha-darśanam śarīre dṛṣṭa-tvāt śarīrasya
410.07	anyatvam sādhayatu na punar anupalabhyamāna-upacaya-apacaya-viśeṣāṇām
410.08	sphaṭika-ādīnām etasmāt anyatvam sidhyati iti ahetuḥ/ yatra upalabdhiḥ tasya
410.09	anyatvam iti cet atha manyase na eva asmābhiḥ upacya-apacaya-prabandha-darśanena
410.10	sarva-bhāvānām kṣaṇika-tvam sādhyate api tu yatra tad asti tasmin
410.11	kṣaṇika-tvam iti/ satyam yasya yasya upacaya-apacaya-prabandhaḥ upalabhyate tasya
410.12	tasya anyatvam iti na tu kṣaṇika-tvam upacaya-apacaya-prabandha-darśanam ca anyathābhavat-
410.13	ahetuḥ kṣaṇika-tve/ katham anyathā pūrva-avayavāḥ āhāra-avayava-
410.14	sahitāḥ pūrva-vyūha-parityāgena vyūha-antaram āpadyamānāḥ śarīra-antaram
410.15	utpādayanti iti yuktaḥ bhedaḥ iti ahetuḥ eṣaḥ/ tulyam pakṣa-pratijñānam iti
410.16	cet atha manyase pūrva-āhāra-avayava-upacaya-yoge sati śarīra-avayavāḥ
410.17	śarīra-antaram ārabhante pūrva-vyūha-parityāgena na punar kṣaṇika-tvāt anyat
410.18	jātam iti atra kaḥ hetuḥ iti/ na asādhanāt na mayā āhāra-upacaya-
410.19	apacaya-yogāt śarīrasya anyatā sādhyate api tu bhavatā viśeṣa-darśanāt śarīrasya
410.20	anyatvam abhyadhāyi tataḥ ca kṣaṇikatvam iti viśeṣa-darśanam kṣaṇikatva-
411.01	sādhanam tad asmābhiḥ viśeṣa-darśanam anyathābhavat kṣaṇika-tvena sādhanam iti
411.02	etāvat-mātrakam ucyate na sādhanam abhidhīyate iti/  atha avaśyam sādhanam
411.03	vaktavyam idam brūmaḥ na utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ//
411.04	[3-2-12]na utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ
411.05	utpatti-kāraṇam vināśa-kāraṇam ca upacaya-apacayau valmīka-ghaṭa-ādīnām
411.06	dṛṣṭau iti tad-ubhayam kṣaṇika-tve na syāt/ upacayaḥ iti ca vṛddhiḥ
411.07	upacitaḥ go^ḥ iti apacayaḥ iti hrāsaḥ apacitaḥ go^ḥ iti ubhayam
411.08	ca etad avasthita-vastu-viṣayam na hi apūrvam jāyamānam varddhate iti ucyate na api
411.09	vinaśyat pacitamiti ucyate upacaya-apacaya-utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ
411.10	iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ atha vā kāraṇam iti ādhāraḥ abhidhīyate kāryam
411.11	ādheyam na ca kṣanika-tve ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvaḥ sambhavati na ca ādhāra-
411.12	ādhāra-bhāvam antareṇa kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ tasmāt kārya-kāraṇa-bhāva-upalabdhi^aḥ
411.13	akṣaṇika-tvam iti/ asya prayogaḥ kārya-kāraṇe eka-kāla-anubhāvinī
411.14	ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvāt kṣīra-kuṇḍa-vat iti/ kṣīram kuṇḍe vartate tad ca
411.15	kuṇḍa-kālam anubhavati tathā ca kāryam kāraṇe vartate tasmāt tad api
411.16	kāraṇa-kālam anubhavati na ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvasya asiddha-tvāt iti cet atha
411.17	manyase asiddhaḥ kārya-kāraṇayoḥ ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvaḥ kāryasya anādhāra-tvāt/
411.18	na eva hi naḥ kim cit kāryam ādhāravat dṛṣṭam iti/ na anekāntāt
411.19	na ayam ekāntaḥ sarvam kāryam anādhāram iti/ yathā ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipatti^ā
411.20	rūpam kāryam ca ādhāravat ca sparśaḥ tad-āśrayaḥ iti ca vyāghātaḥ/ yadi
411.21	ca rūpam anāśritam bhavati sparśaḥ tad-āśrayaḥ iti vyāhatam/ anena ca
412.01	rūpa-dṛṣṭāntena yāvat yāvat samāna-kāla-tayā sādhyate sarvam tad eka-
412.02	kāla-anubhāvin bhavati iti siddhaḥ kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ/ kṣaṇika^su api
412.03	nāśa-utpādayoḥ eka-kāla-tvāt tulā-antayoḥ unnamana-avanamana-vat iti cet
412.05	vināśa-samakālam kārya-bhāvāt/ yadā kāraṇam vinaśyati tadā
412.06	kāryam utpadyate vinaśyat ca kāraṇam asti kāraṇa-vināśena abhinna-kālaḥ
412.07	kārya-utpādaḥ yathā tulā-antayoḥ nāma-unnāmau iti/ na hetu-artha-aparijñānāt/
412.08	na brūmaḥ kṣaṇika-tve kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ na asti api
412.09	tu ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvaḥ bhinna-kāla-tvāt kārya-kāraṇayoḥ na yuktaḥ iti kāryam
412.10	ca anādhāram iti na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti asmad-pakṣe tu kāryam ādhāravat iti asti
412.11	dṛṣṭāntaḥ rūpa-ādiḥ iti/ tulā-ādhārasya ca vyāpāre nāma-unnāma-
412.12	abhyupagamāt yugapad-bhāva-anupapattiḥ eka-tvāt/ yadi tulām
412.13	adhikṛtya bravīṣi tadā ekam karman bhramaṇa-ākhyam eka-tvāt yugapad-bhāvaḥ
412.14	na yuktaḥ na hi ekam yugapad-bhavati iti/ atha avayava-karmaṇī tayoḥ api
412.15	kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvena anabhyupagamāt kim kena sambadhyate atha yugapad-bhāva-
412.16	mātram vivakṣitam iti yugapad-bhāvaḥ kāraṇa-yaugapadyāt yāvatām arthānām
412.17	kāraṇāni yugapad-bhavanti tāvatām ekasmin kāle bhavaḥ iti nāma-
412.18	unnāmau asambaddhau kāraṇa-yaugapadyam tu gurutva-prayatna-saṃyogāḥ unnati^aḥ avanati^aḥ
412.19	api gurutvavat-dravya-saṃyoga-anugṛhītaḥ rajju-tulā-saṃyogaḥ atha api na
412.20	karma-artha-antaram saṃyogam vā paraḥ pratipadyate tasya api uktam uttaram saṃyogaḥ
413.01	api artha-antaram iti saṃyoga-kāraṇa-tvena ca karman api artha-antaram iti/ yathā
413.02	ca bhāvānām utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇam upalabhyate na evam sphaṭikasya na
413.03	ca anupalabhyamānam śakyam abhyanujñātum iti/  kṣīra-vināśe kāraṇa-anupalabdhi-
413.04	vat dadhi-utpatti-vat ca tad-upapattiḥ//
413.05	[3-2-13]kṣīra-vināśe kāraṇa-anupalabdhi-vat dadhi-utpatti-vat ca
413.06	tad-upapattiḥ
413.07	yathā kṣīrasya vināśa-kāraṇam anupalabhyamānam abhyanujñāyate dadhnaḥ
413.08	ca utpatti-kāraṇam evam sphaṭika-ādi-vyaktīnām upalabhyamāne utpatti-
413.09	vināśa-kāraṇe abhyanujñeye iti//
413.10	[3-2-14]liṅgataḥ grahaṇāt na anupalabdhiḥ
413.11	liṅgataḥ grahaṇāt na anupalabdhiḥ/ yad liṅgataḥ upalabhyate na tad na
413.12	asti iti/ viparyayaḥ ca sphaṭika-ādi^i na hi sphaṭika-ādi-vyaktīnām aparāpara-
413.13	utpatti^i liṅgam asti iti/ na na asti śīta-uṣṇa-sparśa-bhedasya nānā-tve
413.14	sādhana-tvāt śīta-uṣṇa-sparśa-bhedaḥ nānā-tve dṛṣṭaḥ yathā agni^i udake ca
413.15	asti ayam śīta-uṣṇa-sparśa-bhedaḥ sphaṭike satyam asti saḥ tu nimitta-antarāt bhavati
413.16	kim punar nimitta ap-tejas-avayava-anupraveśaḥ udaka-avayava-anupraveśāt śītaḥ
413.17	tejas-avayava-anupraveśāt ca uṣṇaḥ iti/ ap-tejas-avayava-anupraveśaḥ na yuktaḥ
413.18	alpatara-bahutara-anupraveśe hetu-abhāvāt/ hemante hi ākāśe paryuṣitānām
413.19	kāṣṭha-bhūmi-aśma-lohānām śaityasya uttara-uttaraḥ viśeṣaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tathā punar
414.01	grīṣma-ātapa-santāpitānām teṣām eva auṣṇyasya/ na hi teṣu alpatara-behutara-praveśe
414.02	kāraṇam paśyāmaḥ tasmāt svagata-mahābhūta-viśeṣa-apekṣāṇi āsādita-bāhya-nimittāni
414.03	bhūtāni eva tathā tathā utpadyante iti yuktam/ na yuktam na
414.04	hi ap-tejas-avayava-anupraveśena bhūta-svabhāvaḥ nivāryate iti bhūta-svabhāvasya
414.05	ubhaya-pakṣa-sāmyāt asādhanam bhūta-svabhāvaḥ iti viruddhaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ yadi avayava-
414.06	anupraveśam na pratipadyase khara-sneha-uṣṇa-iraṇa-ātmakāni bhūtāni iti
414.07	vyāhanyate/ katham iti yad kaṭhinam tad ekasmin kāle uṣṇam api
414.08	bhavati iti yad ca uṣṇam tad kaṭhinam gacchati iti tad eka-svabhāvakam vastu
414.09	dvi-svabhāvakam tri-svabhāvakam ca prāpnoti/ yasya punar avayava-anupraveśaḥ tasya eṣaḥ
414.10	doṣaḥ na asti anyatra avasthitasya saṃyukta-samavetasya auṣṇasya grahaṇāt
414.11	iti/ viruddhaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ katham ekasya viśeṣasya abhyupagamāt ekam
414.12	vastu ekasmin kāle kaṭhinam uṣṇam gacchati ca iti tri-svabhāvakam tataḥ ca
414.13	viśeṣa-darśanāt iti ayam hetuḥ siddhāntam abhyupetya tad-virodhin^ḥ viruddhaḥ iti
414.14	viruddhaḥ bhavati/ atha manyase na viśeṣa-mātram nānātvam pratipādayati api
414.15	tu virodhin^ḥ viśeṣaḥ nānā-tve sādhanam iti/ na abhiprāya-aparijñānāt
414.16	vayam api na brūmaīḥ viśeṣa-mātram nānātva-sādhanam api tu virodhin^ḥ viśeṣaḥ
414.17	itaretara-vyāvṛttaḥ lakṣaṇa-tvena upapannaḥ saḥ viruddhaḥ yadi aviruddhaḥ syāt na
414.18	lakṣaṇam syāt eka-vṛtti-tve vā lakṣaṇa-trayasya tad tad vastu kim abhidhīyate/
414.19	yadi tāvat pṛthivī tad na yuktam auṣṇyasya darśanāt atha tejas tad api
414.20	na yuktam kāṭhinya-darśanāt/ etena vāyuḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ saḥ ayam hetuḥ bhūta-
414.21	bhūta-lakṣaṇam bādhate nānātva-sādhanam vā na bhavati/ etena jale tāpa-grahaṇam
415.01	pratyuktam/ tatra api auṣṇya-snigdhatā-iraṇatā ca yugapad bhavati iti/ jale agni-
415.02	rūpa-grahaṇa-prasaṅgaḥ tu na yuktaḥ abhibhavāt/ tad na siddhānta-aparijñānāt na ayam
415.03	siddhāntaḥ jala-rūpena agni-rūpam abhibhūyate iti api tu tejasaḥ cātur-
415.04	vidhyāt tatra na rūpa-grahaṇam catur-vidham tejas iti etad prasādhitam
415.05	indriya-prakaraṇe iti/ yā ca iyam pratijñā anyaḥ ca anyaḥ ca sphaṭikaḥ iti,
415.06	asyāḥ ca kaḥ arthaḥ yadi tāvat anyatva-mātram tadā siddha-sādhanam bhavati
415.07	kumbha-ādibhyaḥ anyaḥ sphaṭikaḥ iti/ atha sphaṭika-antarāt anyaḥ iti
415.08	tad api tādṛś eva/ atha pūrva-sphaṭikāt anyaḥiti katamaḥ asau pūrva-sphaṭikaḥ
415.09	yātaḥ ayam anyaḥ iti/ vyavacchedasya aśakya-tvāt anabhidhānam yathā
415.10	ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  tebhyaḥ anyaḥ ananya-vicāraḥ anya-ananya-vicāraḥ ghaṭa-ādi^aḥ yuktaḥ na
415.11	punar iha pūrva-sphaṭika-antaram ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam yataḥ vartamānam anya-tvena
415.12	sādhyate atha ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam pūrva-sphaṭika-antaram vyartham sādhanam atha
415.13	vartamānam sphaṭikam abhyupagamya yad atītam sphaṭika-antaram tad anyat iti
415.14	sādhyate/ evam sati śīta-uṣṇa-sparśa-bhedaḥ na tad-dharmaḥ vartamāna-sphaṭika-
415.15	dharma-tvāt vartamānāt ca sphaṭikāt anyat atītam iti kena etad na pratipannam/
415.16	atha tad eva anyat iti vyāghātaḥ tad eva anyat ca iti/ evam yathā yathā anyatvam
415.17	vicāryate tathā tathā siddha-sādhanam bhavati siddhāntam ca bādhate/
415.18	atha ayugapad-kāla-pratyayānām ālambanam sphaṭikaḥ na bhavati iti pratijñā-arthaḥ/
415.19	etasmin api pratijñā-arthe na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti/ pradīpaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ pradīpaḥ
415.20	ayugapad-kāla-pratyayānām anālambanam sat-ādi-dharmāḥ ca iti vidyate dṛṣṭāntaḥ/
415.21	na asiddha-tvāt asiddham etad ayugapad-kāla-pratyaya-anālambanāḥ pradīpa-
416.01	ādyaḥ  teṣām aneka-kṣaṇa-avasthānāt pradīpasya tāvat sva-kāraṇa-sattā-sambandha-
416.02	kālaḥ sāmānya-abhivyakti-kālaḥ avayava-karma-kālaḥ tad-anantaram vibhāga-
416.03	kālaḥ tad-anantaram tad-saṃyoga-vināsa-kālaḥ tataḥ dravya-nāśaḥ iti/
416.04	yathā pradīpaḥ evam sarve bhāvāḥ sva-kāraṇa-sattā-sambandha-abhivyakti-tad-virodhi-
416.05	āśraya-vināśa-anuvidhānena vinaśyanti/ akāraṇasya vināśa-
416.06	anabhyupagamāt na hi naḥ kim cit niḥkāraṇam vinaśyati vinaśyat-avastha-
416.07	dravya-karma-vat etad syāt na karmaṇaḥ abhyupagame virodhāt anabhyupagamāt ca/
416.08	vināśa-hetu-abhāvāt iti cet atha manyase na vināśa-kāraṇam asti
416.09	tasmāt utpanna-mātraḥ eva ayam bhāvaḥ vinaśyati iti na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/
416.10	vināśa-hetuḥ na asti iti bruvāṇaḥ paryanuyoktavyaḥ kim akāraṇa-tvāt vināśaḥ
416.11	na asti uta akāraṇa-tvāt nityaḥ iti/ bhavatām pakṣe akāraṇam dvedhā nityam
416.12	asat ca asmākam tu nityam eva/ yadi akāraṇa-tvāt nityaḥ vināśaḥ
416.13	kāryasya utpādaḥ na prāpnoti utpannasya ca bhāvasya vināśena saha
416.14	avasthānam iti ca doṣaḥ/ tataḥ ca bhāvānām abhāva-avirodhāt atyantam avasthānam
416.15	iti/ atha asan vināśaḥ evam api sarva-nitya-tvam vināśa-abhāvāt/ yadi
416.16	ca asan vināśaḥ vinaśyati iti asati vināśe pratyayaḥ na prāpnoti na asatyām
416.17	gati^i gacchati iti bhavati/ atha avināśi-tvāt akāraṇaḥ vināśaḥ iti
416.18	manyase vināśaḥ na vinaśyati iti kutaḥ etad/ vinaṣṭānām punar anutpatti^aḥ
416.19	iti cet atha manyase yadi vināśaḥ vinaśyeta vinaṣṭaḥ punar
416.20	utpadyate/ na yuktam etad na hi vināśa-abhāva-abhāvaḥ yataḥ asya vināśāt
417.01	bhavet api tu kāraṇavān bhāvaḥ tasya yadā kāraṇam bhavati
417.02	tadā utpādaḥ iti api ca vināśaḥ kāraṇavān ca na vinaśyati abhāva-tvāt
417.03	bhāva-dharmaḥ eṣaḥ yad kāraṇavat tad vinaśyati iti abhāvaḥ tu akāraṇaḥ api
417.04	vinaśyati it yathā prāgabhāvaḥ kāraṇavān api na vinaśyati yathā
417.05	pradhvaṃsābhāvaḥ/ yadi kāraṇavān vināśaḥ nanu janakam vināśakam
417.06	prāpnoti yaḥ eva agni-saṃyogaḥ pākajānām utpādayitṛ^ḥ saḥ eva pākaja-
417.07	antara-utpāde pūrva-pākajān ucchinatti iti janakam vināśakam prāptam
417.08	na anavarodhāt na idam bhavatā kāśyapīyam darśanam vyajñāyi na hi naḥ
417.09	yaḥ eva agni-saṃyogaḥ pākajān utpādayati saḥ eva ucchinatti api tu agni-
417.10	saṃyoga-antaram pūrva-rūpa-ādi ucchinatti samantara-kālāt rūpa-ādīn ārabhate
417.11	tataḥ tāni api anyaḥ tāni api anyaḥ iti evam janakasya vināśakatva-prasaṅgaḥ na asti/
417.12	atha manyase janaka-sarūpam api vināśakam na bhavati na na bhavati
417.13	dṛṣṭaḥ hi abādi-saṃyogāt aṅkurasya utpādaḥ saḥ eva abādi-saṃyogaḥ tad-vināśakaḥ
417.14	iti/ na hi dṛṣṭe anupapannam nāma yaḥ ca akāraṇa-vināśe doṣaḥ saḥ aparihāryaḥ
417.15	iti ataḥ evam ucyate janaka-sarūpam vināśakam iti/ evam tāvat
417.16	anyatve sādhye doṣaḥ uktaḥ/ ye tu kṣaṇikatvam sādhayanti viśeṣa-darśanāt
417.17	vināśa-hetu-abhāvāt ca tān prati idam ucyate kṣaṇikāḥ saṃskārāḥ iti
417.18	bruvāṇaḥ kṣaṇika-śabda-artham praṣṭavyaḥ kim uktam bhavati kṣaṇikāḥ iti
417.19	yadi vināśinaḥ iti siddha-sādhanam atha āśu-vināśinaḥ iti tadā
418.01	viśeṣaṇam siddhāntavirodhin/ atha utpanna-pradhvaṃsinaḥ iti etad api
418.02	tādṛś eva/ utpannāḥ pradhvaṃsinaḥ iti siddha-sādhanam/ atha utpannaḥ
418.03	vinaṣṭaḥ iti ekaḥ kālaḥ/ utpatti-vināśau eka-kālau iti/ tena
418.04	yathā anutpannasya utpattiḥ evam anutpannasya vināśaḥ iti prāptam/ atha
418.05	utpannam vinaśyati iti etasmin api pakṣe utpattivat-vināśaḥ kāraṇavān
418.06	yathā utpattiḥ kriyā-tvāt kādācitka-tvāt sakāraṇā tathā vināśa-kāraṇam
418.07	anubhavati tataḥ vinaśyati iti akāraṇe ca vināśe doṣaḥ
418.08	uktaḥ/ yaḥ ca ayam matu-arthīyaḥ kṣaṇikā kṣaṇikaḥ iti saḥ katham yadi nirukta-
418.09	nyāyena kṣayaḥ kṣaṇaḥ iti kṣaṇaḥ asya asti iti kṣaṇikaḥ īt tad na yuktam
418.10	kāla-bhedāt yadā kṣayaḥ na tadā kṣayin^ḥ iti bhinna-kālayoḥ na matu-arthīyaḥ
418.11	dṛṣṭaḥ atha punar bhāvaḥ eva anantareṇa vināśena viśiṣyamāṇaḥ kṣaṇikaḥ
418.12	iti ucyate tathā api tena eva tad eva tadvat na bhavati iti na yuktaḥ matu-arthīyaḥ/
418.13	atha kṣaṇa-avasthiti-kālāḥ kṣaṇikāḥ iti sarva-antyam kālam kṣaṇa-tvena
418.14	pratipadya tatra ye avatiṣṭhante te kṣaṇikāḥ iti/ etad api na yuktam/
418.15	saṃjñā-mātra-tvena kālasya abhyupagamāt/ pañca-bhikṣu^āḥ saṃjñā-bheda-mātram
418.16	iti śāstram/ na hi saṃjñā-mātram vastu-viśeṣaṇa-tvena yuktam/
418.17	kṣaṇikāḥ it ca pratijñām kṛtvā na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti iti uktam pradīpa-ādi^aḥ
418.18	asiddha-tvāt/ ye api hetu^āḥ viśeṣa-darśanāt iti evam ādyaḥ  te api asiddhāḥ
418.19	anyathā-siddhāḥ viruddhāḥ vā bhavanti iti ahetu^āḥ upetya vā viśeṣavat-tvam
419.01	vartamāna-ekakṣaṇa-viśeṣavat-tvena anaikāntikam ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipatti^ā yaḥ eva
419.02	kṣaṇaḥ vartamānaḥ saḥ viśeṣavān bhavati ekaḥ ca iti anekāntaḥ/ ante viśeṣa-
419.03	darśanāt iti ca viśeṣaṇa-anarthakyam anta-artha-anabhidhānāt katamasmin
419.04	ante viśeṣa-darśanāt iti ca vaktavyam atha antaḥ vināśaḥ na etasmin
419.05	viśeṣa-darśanam asti atha antimam vastu eva anta-śabda-vācyam evam sati eksamin
419.06	viśeṣa-darśanam iti viruddhaḥ hetuḥ yaḥ api ayam hetuḥ darśana-adarśanāt
419.07	iti asya arthaḥ nīyamānasya kila pradīpa-ādi^aḥ dravyasya deśa-antare utpādaḥ
419.08	deśa-antare nirodhaḥ tad-kṛte darśana-adarśane staḥ ataḥ ca devadatta-ādi^aḥ gacchataḥ
419.09	darśana-adarśanāt ataḥ tasya api pratikṣaṇam utpāda-vināśau iti/ na etad nyāyam
419.10	devadatta-ādi^aḥ draśana-adarśanayoḥ anya-nimitta-tvāt saṃyoga-vibhāga-kṛte
419.11	darśana-adarśane na utpāda-vināśa-kṛte yasmin deśe saṃyogaḥ vinaṣṭaḥ
419.12	tasmin saṃyogin^ḥ na gṛhyate yasmin utpannaḥ tasmin gṛhyate iti yad yogāt
419.13	hi dravye iha-buddhiḥ bhavati tad-bhāva-abhāvayoḥ tadvat-tvāt iti/ atha saṃyoga-
419.14	vibhāgau na vidhyete tasya api saṃyogaḥ artha-antaram bhavati iti pratipāditam/
419.15	etena vibhāgaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ tad evam saṃyoga-vibhāga-utpatti^aḥ dravyasya
419.16	darśana-adarśane bhavataḥ na anyathā iti/ yad punar etad pradīpasya desa-antare utpādaḥ
419.17	iti etad api na buddhyāmahe/ katham iti yad tāvat bhavatām pakṣe vastu-utpannam
419.18	yatra tatra eva tad dhvaṃsate yad utpatsu tad anāhita-saṃskāram asattvāt deśa-antarena
419.19	utpattum arhati iti/ atha anāhita-saṃskārasya api deśa-antare utpādaḥ
419.20	na kim cit vastu svabhinna-deśam utpadyate/ atha kāraṇam saṃskṛtam iti
420.01	tad-ādheya-vaśāt kāryam api tathā-bhūtam eva utpadyate iti tulyam kāryena
420.02	yathā eva kārye saṃskāraḥ ādhātum aśakyaḥ tathā kāraṇe api iti tulyam
420.03	nityam bhavatām pakṣe bhinna-deśena kāryena utpattavyam abhinna-deśena vā/
420.04	etena eva atulya-pātaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ atulya-pātāt kṣaṇika-tvam iti na
420.05	kila ākāśe patataḥ loṣṭa-ādi^aḥ pratibandhakam kim cit asti yad-anuvidhānena
420.06	loṣṭa-ādikam kim cit cirena patati kim cit āśviti tasmāt kṣaṇika-
420.07	tvāt kāḥ cid avasthāḥ tādṛśyaḥ utpadyante yāsām na bhavati adhas santāna-
420.08	utpatti^i sāmarthyam punar ca tādṛśyaḥ utpadyante yāsām bhavati iti etad api
420.09	anyathā-siddham eva tulyānām gurutvavatām dravyāṇām ekasya gurutva-prayatna-kṣepāḥ
420.10	kāraṇam ekasya gurutva-mātram ekasya gurutva-prayatnau iti/ tad evam kāraṇa-
420.11	bheda-anuvidhāyīni gurutvavanti dravyāṇi cira-āśu-pāta-anuvidhāna-bhāñji
420.12	bhavati iti ahetuḥ atulya-pātaḥ/ yad api idam iṣu^aḥ ca āpatanam bhavet iti saṃskāra-
420.13	ekatva-vādinaḥ doṣaḥ ayam api saṃskāra-ekatva-anabhyupagamena pratyuktaḥ/
420.14	yathā ca anekaḥ saṃskāraḥ iti tathā uktam pāṇi-nimitta-praśleṣa-sūtre iti/
420.15	ekaḥ ca saṃskāraḥ yadā kadā cit nimitta-bhedāt paṭūni karmāṇi karoti
420.16	kadā cit mandāni iti gurutva-vat draṣṭavyam yathā ekam gurutvam sahakāri-
420.17	saṃyogāt mandam pātam karoti tad eva pratibandha-apagame paṭum karoti/
420.18	dṛṣṭaḥ hi grāvṇaḥ ākāse muktasya paṭuḥ pātaḥ mandaḥ apām iti
420.19	tad evam atulya-pātaḥ api ahetuḥ/ atha akṣaṇika-tve kim pramāṇam uktam
420.20	ādhāra-ādheya-bhāvāt eka-kāla-anubhāvīni kārya-kāraṇe kuṇḍe badara-vat iti
421.01	ataḥ ca vipratipannāḥ ayugapad-kālāḥ pratyayāḥ eka-viṣayāḥ avyutthāyin
421.02	tad-pratyaya-sāmānādhikaraṇye sati samāna-śabda-vācya-tvāt vartamāna-ekakṣaṇa-
421.03	aneka-puruṣa-pratyaya-vat yathā vartamāna-kṣaṇa-viṣayāṇām aneka-puruṣa-
421.04	pratyayānām avyutthāyi-tad-pratyaya-sāmānādhikaraṇye sati samāna-śabda-vācyānām
421.05	eka-viṣaya-tvam tathā ca avyutthāyi-tad-pratyaya-samānādhikaraṇāḥ
421.06	samāna-śabda-vācyāḥ ca vipratipannāḥ ayugapad-kālāḥ pratyayāḥ te api eka-
421.07	viṣayāḥ iti pratyātmam na aneka-kartṛkāḥ pratyayāḥ apratisandhāna-prasaṅgāt
421.08	devadatta-ādi-pratyaya-vat iti/ rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-pratyayāḥ eka-aneka-nimittāḥ
421.09	mayā iti pratisandhānāt kṛta-samayānām bahūnām vartamāna-ekakṣaṇa-aneka-
421.10	vijñāna-vat iti śvastana-adyatana-vijñānāni eka-santati^i eka-aneka-nimittāni
421.11	mayā iti pratisandhānāt pūrvavat śāstra-artha-pratyayāḥ eka-aneka-nimittāḥ
421.12	pratisandhānāt pūrvavat nānā-viṣayāṇām ca nānā-kartṛkāṇām ca pratyayānām
421.13	apratisandhānāt iti pūrvavat eva nyāyaḥ iti//
421.14	[3-2-15]na payasaḥ pariṇāma-guṇa-antara-prādurbhāvāt
421.15	na payasaḥ pariṇāma-guṇa-antara-prādurbhāvāt/ na evam kṣīram vinaśyati
421.16	na api dadhi utpadyate api tu avasthitasya dravyasya pūrva-dharma-nivṛtti^i dharma-
421.17	antaram utpadyate iti eke varṇayanti/ apare tu avasthitasya dravyasya guṇa-
421.18	nivṛtti^i guṇa-antaram utpadyate iti/ saḥ khalu ayam eka-pakṣa^bhāvaḥ iti
421.19	kaḥ upamāna-arthaḥ ubhayatra dravyam avatiṣṭhate ekasya tirobhāva-abhivyaktī
421.20	ekasya vināśa-prādurbhāvau iti upamāna-arthaḥ  ubhayatra uttaram vyūha-antarāt dravya-
421.21	antara-utpatti-darśanam pūrva-dravya-nivṛtti^aḥ anumānam//
422.01	[3-2-16]vyūha-antarāt dravya-antara-utpatti-darśanam pūrva-dravya-nivṛtti^aḥ anumānam
422.03	śeṣam bhāṣye/ yad punar etad ekasya tirobhāva-abhivyaktī ekasya vināśa-
422.04	prādurbhāvau iti tad na pūrva-avastha-aparityāgāt/ yadi vināśa-utpādau
422.05	nivṛtti-prādurbhāvau atha api abhivyakti-anabhivyaktī sarvathā pūrva-avasthām jahāti
422.06	dravyam iti na hi anapavṛktasya pūrva-avasthātaḥ abhivyakti-anabhivyaktī bhavataḥ
422.07	vināśa-prādurbhāvau vā iti/ tasmāt ayuktam avasthitasya pariṇāmaḥ iti/
422.08	abhyanujñāya ca niḥkāraṇam kṣīra-vināśam niṣkāraṇam ca dadhi-utpādam//
422.09	[3-2-17]kva cit vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ kva cit ca upalabdhi^aḥ anekāntaḥ
422.11	kva cit vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ kva cit ca upalabdhi^aḥ anekāntaḥ/ kṣīra-
422.12	dadhi-vat niḥkkāraṇau vināśa-utpādau sphaṭika-vyaktīnām na punar kumbha-
422.13	ādi-vat sakāraṇau iti na atra hetuḥ asti iti niradhiṣṭhānam ca dṛṣṭānta-
422.14	vacanam/ kaḥ adhiṣṭhāna-arthaḥ āśraya-arthaḥ kim uktam bhavati niradhiṣṭḥānam
422.15	iti/ dharmin^ḥ na asti iti yāvat uktam bhavati na hi sphaṭikasya utpāda-
422.16	vināśau pratikṣaṇam upalabhyete yataḥ iyam cintā bhavet kim etau sakāraṇau
422.17	āhosvit akāraṇau iti yathā anabhyupagata-śabda-tattvam prati kaḥ cit
422.18	brūyāt kṛtaka-tvāt anityaḥ śabdaḥ tādṛś etad iti dharmi-asiddhi^aḥ ca dṛṣṭānta-
422.19	abhāvaḥ iti ca samānam/ abhyanujñāya ca sphaṭikasya utpāda-vināśau
423.01	yaḥ atra sādhakaḥ tasya abhyanujñānāt apratiṣedhaḥ ubhau etau dṛṣṭāntau kṣīra-
423.02	dadhi-vat kumbha-ādi-vat tatra kim kumbha-ādiḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ bhavatu atha kṣīra-dadhinī
423.03	iti/ tatra kumbha-ādi-vat iti yuktam kumbha-ādi-utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇasya
423.04	dṛṣṭa-tvāt kṣīra-dadhi-vat tu na yuktam anumānataḥ grahaṇāt iti siddham
423.05	anityā buddhiḥ iti/ itaḥ ca anityā buddhiḥ guṇa-tva sati pratyakṣāyāḥ pratyakṣa-tayā
423.06	vyāpaka-dravya-samavāyāt śabda-vat jātimat-tve sati asmad-ādi-pratyakṣa-tvāt
423.07	āśrita-tvāt śabda-vat karaṇa-bhāve sati ayogi-pratyakṣa-tvāt śabda-vat eva//
423.08	sā tu iyam buddhiḥ kasya guṇaḥ iti etad cintyate na guṇa-bhāva-asiddhi^aḥ
423.09	anitya-tve sati acākṣuṣa-pratyakṣa-tvāt guṇa-bhāvaḥ sannikarṣa-utpatti^aḥ saṃśsayaḥ
423.10	iti/ tatra ayam viśeṣaḥ//
423.11	[3-2-18]na indriya-arthayoḥ tad-vināśe api jñāna-avasthānāt
423.12	na indriya-arthayoḥ tad-vināśe api jñāna-avasthānāt/ bhavati khalu indriye
423.13	arthe ca vinaṣṭe jñānam adrākṣam iti adrākṣam iti etad na indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt
423.14	smṛti-tvāt smṛtiḥ eṣā na indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt bhavitum arhati smṛti^i api
423.15	tulyam yathā jñātṛ^am antareṇa jñānam na bhavati evam anubhavitṛ^i naṣṭe anubhavitṛ^am
423.16	antareṇa smṛtiḥ ayuktā iti/  astu tarhi manas jñānam guṇaḥ
423.17	[3-2-19]yugapad-jñeya-anupalabdhi^aḥ na manasaḥ
423.18	yugapad-jñeya-anupalabdhi^aḥ na manasaḥ/ tad khalu yugapad-jñeya-anupalabdi^ā
423.19	samadhigatam manas na ataḥ tasya guṇaḥ jñānam viśeṣaṇa-upādānāt antaḥkaraṇa-
423.20	bahutva-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet atha manyase yad khalu yugapad-jñeya-anupalabdhi^ā
423.21	samadhigatam manas na tasya guṇaḥ jñānam iti evam bruvāṇena bahūni
424.01	antaḥkaraṇāni pratipannāni bhavanti na hi asati antaḥkaraṇa-bahu-tve viśeṣaṇam
424.02	arthavat iti/ na sarvasya indriyasya manana-nimitta-tvāt sarvābhindriyam sarvam indriyam
424.03	manana-nimitta-tvāt manas iti ucyate/ evam ca sati viśeṣaṇam yuktam
424.04	yugapad-jñeya-anupalabdhi^ā yad samadhigatam manas na tasya guṇaḥ jñānam iti
424.05	evam api viśeṣaṇam vyartham anyasya anabhyupagamāt/ yadi tasya antaḥkaraṇasya
424.06	na bhavati anyasya tarhi sādhanasya jñānam guṇaḥ iti prāptam
424.07	anyasya anabhyupagamāt/ anyasya upapatti^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ anavasthita-tvāt pramāṇa-
424.08	prameya-bhāvasya yadā ayam yogin^ḥ puruṣa-antarena puruṣa-antaram paricchinatti
424.09	tadā puruṣa-antara-paricchede puruṣa-antaram sādhanam iti tattva-jñāna-guṇam
424.10	bhavati iti yuktam viśeṣaṇam iti/ tasmāt na tasya guṇaḥ jñānam iti/ kasya
424.11	tarhi jñasya vaśi-tvāt vaśin^ḥ jñātṛ^ḥ avaśyam karaṇam iti na ca ayam jñātṛ^i
424.12	niyamaḥ jñātṛ^ḥ vaśin^ḥ eva vaśyaḥ api bhavati iti/ acetane hi niyamaḥ
424.13	acetanam tu sarvam vaśyam iti/ vaśyam manas acetana-tvāt ghrāṇa-ādi-vat iti/
424.14	jñāna-guṇa-tve vā saṃjñā-bheda-mātram cittam ca antaḥkaraṇam jñāna-guṇam ca iti
424.15	saṃjñā-mātram bhidyate/ yathā ca jñātṛ^aḥ jñāna-sādhanam indriyam evam mantṛ^ḥ mati-
424.16	sādhanena avaśyam bhavitavyam iti/ ubhayoḥ cetana-tve pratyaya-vyavasthā-anumānam
424.17	na syāt vibhu ca antaḥkaraṇam jñāna-guṇam iti sarva-indriyaiḥ yugapad-upalabdhi-
424.18	prasaṅgaḥ//
424.19	[3-2-20]tad-ātma-guṇa-tve api tulyam
424.20	[3-2-21]indriyaiḥ manasaḥ sannikarṣa-abhāvāt tad-anutpattiḥ
424.21	tad-ātma-guṇa-tve api tulyam/ yasya ātman^ḥ vibhuḥ ca jñāna-guṇaḥ ca tasya api
425.01	sarva-indriyaiḥ sambandhasya apratiṣedhāt yugapad-upalabdhi-prasaṅgaḥ  na prasaṅgaḥ kāraṇa-
425.02	antara-bhāvāt indriya-artha-sannikarṣa-vat indriya-manas-sannikarṣaḥ api kāraṇam
425.03	iti/ tasya ayaugapadam aṇu-tvāt manasaḥ iti/  yadi punar ātma-indriya-artha-
425.04	sannikarṣāt eva jñānam utpadyet utpadyeta kim syāt/
425.05	[3-2-22]na utpatti-kāraṇa-anapadeśāt
425.06	na utpatti-kāraṇa-anapadeśāt/ asmāt eva utpadyatām iti na kāraṇam
425.07	apadiśyate iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ ātma-indriya-artha-sannikarṣāt jñānam utpadyate
425.08	iti/ ayugapad-utpatti^i vā na kāraṇa-antaram apadisyate iti videha-
425.09	pratyaya-anutpatti^i vā yadā indriyam arthena bahis-avasthitena sambaddhyate
425.10	ātma-sambandhaḥ api tatra asti iti kasmāt videha-pratyayaḥ na bhavati iti vaktavyam
425.11	antaḥkaraṇa-pratyākhyāne ca kevalam ātman^ḥ smṛti-ādi-kāraṇam iti
425.12	kasmāt na yugapad-utpattiḥ smṛtīnām yadā ca indriyam ātman^ḥ ca arthena yugapad-
425.13	sambaddhau tadā kim indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ kāraṇam uta ātma-artha-
425.14	sannikarṣaḥ uta ātma-indriya-artha-sannikarṣaḥ iti kāraṇa-anapadeśaḥ  vināśa-
425.15	kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca avasthāne tad-nityatva-prasaṅgaḥ//
425.16	[3-2-23]vināśa-kāraṇa-anupalabdhi^aḥ ca avasthāne tad-nityatva-prasaṅgaḥ
425.18	tad-ātma-guṇa-tve api tulyam iti etad anena samuccīyante yadi ātma-guṇaḥ
425.19	buddhiḥ bhavati vināśa-kāraṇa-abhāvāt nityatvam asyāḥ prasajyeta katham iti
426.01	guṇānām dvai vināśa-hetū āśraya-abhāvau virodhi-guṇa-prādurbhāvaḥ ca tatra
426.02	nitya-tvāt ātmanaḥ anupapannaḥ pūrvaḥ virodhin^ḥ ca gunaḥ na asti nityatva-
426.03	prasaṅgaḥ//
426.04	[3-2-24]anityatva-grahaṇāt buddhi^aḥ buddhi-antarāt vināśaḥ śabda-vat
426.05	anityatva-grahaṇāt buddhi^aḥ buddhi-antarāt vināśaḥ śabda-vat/ anityā buddhiḥ
426.06	iti etad pratipāditam/ tatra asyāḥ buddhi-antarāt vā saṃskārāt vā vināśaḥ
426.07	iti pratipattavyam/ na dve saha iti cet atha manyase na dve buddhī saha
426.08	bhavataḥ yataḥ parayā pūrvā nivartyate tad ca na yugapad-utpatti-niṣedhāt
426.09	yugapad-utpattiḥ pratiṣidhyate na sahabhāvaḥ na hi buddhi^oḥ yugapad-bhāve kim cit
426.10	virudhyate iti labdha-ātmakena ca kāryena kāraṇa-vināśa-abhyupagamāt/
426.11	yadā ca kāryam labdha-ātmakam bhavati tadā kāraṇam vināśayati śabda-vat/
426.12	yathā labdha-ātmakaḥ kāryaḥ śabdaḥ kāraṇam śabdam nirmāṣṭi tathā buddhiḥ
426.13	api iti/ antyayoḥ na syāt iti cet atha manyase yadi pūrvau buddhi-śabdau
426.14	parābhyām vināsyete antyayoḥ buddhi-śabdayoḥ katham vināśa-kāraṇam
426.15	iti/ yathā ca antyau buddhi-śabdau vinā nimittāt vinasyataḥ evam anyat
426.16	api kāryam iti/ na sparśavat-dravya-saṃyogāt na tad-utpattiḥ sparśavat-dravya-
426.17	saṃyogena antya-śabdaḥ virudhyate/ ye kuḍya-ādibhiḥ kāraṇasya saṃyogāḥ te
426.18	eka-artha-samavāyinam śabdam pratibadhnanti atah atyantam aśrutiḥ śabdasya
426.19	iti/ antyā buddhiḥ sthiti-hetu-abhāvāt vinaśyati iti kālāt vā saṃskārāt
427.01	katham kālāt yau antyasya janmanaḥ sthiti-hetū dharma-adharmau tayoḥ
427.02	abhāvāt antyā buddhiḥ vinasyati iti kasmāt punar evam ucyate na punar
427.03	ahetukaḥ vināśaḥ abhyupagamyate iti na abhyupagamyate ukta-doṣāt/ nirhetuke
427.04	vināśe uktaḥ doṣaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅgam nirākurvadbhiḥ iti/ yadi kāraṇasya
427.05	ayaugapadyāt buddhīnām ayugapad-utpattiḥ yatra kāraṇasya yaugapadyam asti
427.06	tatra yugapad-utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ yathā smṛtiṣu/ atra tāvat ekaḥ parihāraḥ
427.07	karaṇasya eka-kriyā-nirvṛtti^i sāmarthyāt na hi karaṇam ekasmin kāle
427.08	anekām kriyām nirvatayitum śaktam iti pariccheda-tvāt ca smṛtīnām
427.09	jñāna-vat yugapad-utpattiḥ ayugapad-utpattiḥ iti/  apare tu kāraṇasya ayaugapadyam upapādayiṣyantaḥ
427.10	jñāna-samaveta-ātma-pradeśa-sannikarṣāt manasaḥ smṛti-utpatti^aḥ na yugapad-
427.11	utpattiḥ iti āha//
427.12	[3-2-25]jñāna-samaveta-ātma-pradeśa-sannikarṣāt manasaḥ smṛti-utpatti^aḥ na
427.13	yugapad-utpattiḥ
427.14	etad tu na samyac iti pratipādayitum āha/ na antaḥśarīra-vṛtti-
427.15	tvāt manasaḥ//
427.16	[3-2-26]na antaḥśarīra-vṛtti-tvat manasaḥ
427.17	antaḥśarīre manas vartate iti kaḥ vṛtti-arthaḥ/ na tāvat āśraya-
427.18	āśrita-bhāvaḥ na hi manas kva cit āśritam na api vṛttiḥ sva-kārya-
427.19	sāmarthyam bahiskārya-darśanāt manasā adhiṣṭhitam cakṣus niḥsṛtya rūpa-ādi
427.20	grāhayati cakṣus^oḥ visarāru^oḥ manasaḥ kāryam tad-bhāva-bhāvi-tvāt na
428.01	ca anyā gatiḥ sambhavati tasmāt na antaḥśarīra-vṛtti manas iti/ atra
428.02	brūmaḥ na āśraya-āśrita-bhāvaḥ manasaḥ vṛttiḥ na api kārya-pratilambhaḥ api
428.03	tu na aśarīrasya manasaḥ kāryam iti manasaḥ vṛtti-arthaḥ/ na hi aśarīram
428.04	manas puruṣasya artham karoti iti/  sādhya-tvāt ahetuḥ//
428.05	[3-2-27]sādhya-tvāt ahetuḥ
428.06	aśarīram manas puruṣa-artha-asamartham iti sādhyam etad  smarataḥ śarīra-
428.07	dhāraṇa-upapatti^aḥ na pratiṣedhaḥ//
428.08	[3-2-28]smarataḥ śarīra-dhāraṇa-upapatti^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ
428.09	smaraṇa-śarīra-dhāraṇe yugapad-bhavataḥ iti dṛṣṭam dṛṣṭam ca sarvena
428.10	pratipattavyam cira-niścarite ca manasi smarataḥ śarīra-dhāraṇam na
428.11	syāt/  na tad-āśu-gati-tvāt manasaḥ//
428.12	[3-2-29]na tad-āśu-gati-tvāt manasaḥ
428.13	ubhayam sidhyati smaraṇam dhāraṇam ca/  na smaraṇa-kāla-aniyamāt
428.14	[3-2-30]na smaraṇa-kāla-aniyamāt
428.15	aniyataḥ smṛti-kālaḥ ataḥ ayuktam etad/ śarīra-saṃyoga-anapekṣam
428.16	ca ātma-manas-saṃyogam smṛti-kāraṇam bruvataḥ śarīrasya upabhoga-āyatana-tvam
428.17	nivartate/ tataḥ tad-nivṛtti^i śarīra-utpatti-vaiyarthyam iti//
428.18	[3-2-31]ātma-preraṇa-yadṛcchā-jñatābhiḥ ca na saṃyoga-viśeṣaḥ
429.01	apare tu ātma-preraṇa-yādṛcchā-jñatābhiḥ ca na saṃyoga-viśeṣaḥ iti
429.02	parihāram bruvate//
429.03	[3-2-32]vyāsakta-manasaḥ pāda-vyathanena saṃyoga-viśeṣena samānam
429.05	[3-2-33]praṇidhāna-liṅga-ādi-jñānānām ayugapad-bhāvāt yugapad-asmaraṇam
429.06	[(3-2-34)]prātibha-vat tu praṇidhāna-ādi-anapekṣe smārte yaugapadya-prasaṅgaḥ
429.08	etad vyāsakta-manasaḥ pāda-vyathanena saṃyoga-viśeṣena samānam bhavati
429.09	iti aparihāraḥ/ karma-adṛṣṭam upabhoga-artham niyāmakam bhavati iti na yuktam
429.10	samāna-tvāt/ tasmāt pūrvaḥ eva parihāraḥ na antaḥśarīra-vṛtti-tvāt
429.11	manasaḥ iti/  yadi tarhi jñāna-samaveta-ātma-sannikarṣa-ayaugapadyāt iti
429.12	parihāraḥ na iṣyate prāptaḥ smṛtīnām yugapad-utpādaḥ kāraṇasya yaugapadyāt
429.13	iti/ na apekṣā-kāraṇasya ayugapad-bhāvāt/
429.14	yataḥ praṇidhāna-ādi-ātma-manas-saṃyogasya sahakāri-kāraṇam tad na yugapad-
429.15	bhavati iti ayugapad-utpattiḥ smṛtīnām/ yadi praṇidhāna-ādi-apekṣa-ātma
429.16	manas-saṃyogaḥ na yugapad-smṛti-kāraṇam yāḥ smṛtyaḥ  praṇidhāna-ādi-sannidhānam
429.17	antareṇa bhavanti tāsām yaugapadyam syāt na anabhyupagamāt/ na
429.18	hi praṇidhāna-ādi-anapekṣam smārtam asti sataḥ smṛti-hetu^aḥ saṃvedanāt/
429.19	prātibhena samāna-abhimānaḥ ayam iti prātibham tarhi yugapad-bhavitum
430.01	arhati/ na hi tasya asannihitam kāraṇam iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt/
430.02	ukta-uttaram etad na hi karaṇasya aneka-kriyā-nirvṛtti-sāmarthyam asti
430.03	prātibham ca anekam jñānam bhavat ekasmin arthe bhavet atha anekasmin/ na tāvat
430.04	ekasmin vaiyarthyāt/ na anekasmin sāmarthya-abhāvāt/ na ca ayam niyamaḥ
430.05	pratyayānām yaugapadyam na bhavati iti jñātṛ^aḥ vikaraṇa-dharmaṇaḥ deha-nānātve
430.06	pratyayānām yaugapadyāt iti/ yadi ca jñāna-samaveta-ātma-pradeśa-asannikarṣāt
430.07	na smṛtīnām yugapad-utpattiḥ ye saṃskārāḥ ekadeśāḥ teṣu avasthita-
430.08	śarīrasya aneka-jñāna-samavāyāt ekadeśe yugapad-aneka-artha-smaraṇam syāt/ na
430.09	ca etad asti it ataḥ ca aparīhāraḥ iti/ avasthita-śarīrasya saṃskārāḥ
430.10	samāna-deśāḥ iti ayuktam/ ātma-padeśānām adravya-antara-tvāt/ evam sati
430.11	sarva-saṃskārāṇām samāna-deśa-tve pratyaya-yaugapadya-prasaṅgaḥ aparihāryaḥ/
430.12	saṃskāra-pratyāsatti^ā smṛti-yaugapadyam na bhavati iti kā pratyāsattiḥ na
430.13	hi saṃskārāṇām sannikṛṣṭa-viprakṛṣṭa-bhāvaḥ asti samāna-deśa-tvāt/ na
430.14	brūmaḥ sannikṛṣṭatā pratyāsattiḥ api saṃskārasya sahakāri-kāraṇa-samavadhānam
430.15	pratyāsattiḥ śabda-vat yathā śabdāḥ santāna-vartinaḥ sarve eva
430.16	ākāśe samavayanti samāna-deśa-tve api yasya upalabdhi^aḥ kāraṇāni santi
430.17	saḥ upalabhyate na itare tathā saṃskāra^su api iti/  puruṣasya jñānam icchā-
430.18	ādyaḥ  antaḥkaraṇasya iti kasya cit darśanam tad pratiṣidhyate jñasya icchā-dveṣa-nimitta-
430.19	tvāt ārambha-nivṛtti^oḥ//
430.20	[3-2-34(35)]jñasya icchā-dveṣa-nimitta-tvāt ārambha-nivṛtti^oḥ
431.01	jñātṛ^aḥ eva icchā-ādyaḥ  iti sūtra-arthaḥ. yadi antaḥkaraṇasya icchā-ādyaḥ
431.02	syuḥ na pratyakṣāḥ syuḥ na hi icchā-ādyaḥ  anyatra vartamānāḥ anyasya pratyakṣāḥ
431.03	bhavitum arhanti iti. nityam ca icchā-ādyaḥ  apratyakṣāḥ syuḥ manas guṇānām
431.04	atīndriya-tvāt. ārambha-nivṛtti^oḥ ca pratyak-ātmani dṛṣṭa-tvāt paratra
431.05	anumānam//
431.06	[3-2-35(36)]tad-liṅga-tvāt icchā-dveṣayoḥ pārthiva-ādya^su apratiṣedhaḥ
431.07	tad-liṅga-tvāt icchā-dveṣayoḥ pārthiva-ādya^su apratiṣedhaḥ/ pravṛttinivṛttibhyām
431.08	sāmānādhikaraṇyāt śarīra-ādiṣu aprasaṅgaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
431.09	[3-2-36(37)]paraśu-ādiṣu ārambha-nivṛtti-darṣanāt
431.10	paraśu-ādiṣu ārambha-nivṛtti-darśanāt anekāntaḥ vākya-arthaḥ//
431.11	[3-2-37(38)]niyama-aniyamau tu tad-viśeṣakau
431.12	niyama-aniyamau tu tad-viśeṣakau/ prayujyamāna-bhūta-viṣaya-tvam pravṛtti-
431.13	nivṛtti^oḥ niyamaḥ yāni bhūtāni prayujayante teṣu pravṛtti-nivṛttī
431.14	bhavataḥ na sarveṣu iti/ yadi punar jñatvāt bhūtānām pravṛtti-nivṛttī syātām
431.15	bhūta-mātre syātām yathā gurutva-ādibhyaḥ yāni gurutva-ādimanti
431.16	bhūtāni tāni sva-guṇāt patanti iti niyamaḥ ca dṛṣṭaḥ tasmāt na
431.17	bhūtāni cetanāni iti eka-śarīre ca jñātṛ-bahu-tvam niranumānam iti/
431.18	yasya bhūtāni cetanāni tasya eka-śarīre bahūni bhūtāni iti bahu^āḥ
431.19	cetanāḥ syuḥ/ bhavantu bahu^āḥ cetanāḥ kim naḥ bādhyate iti cet na
431.20	bahutve pratyaya-vyavasthāna-anumānam bhavet avyavasthānam tu paśyāmaḥ
432.01	pratyayānām pratisandhānāt sarvaḥ pratyayaḥ pratisandhīyamānaḥ ekasmin
432.02	kārya-kāraṇa-saṅghāte dṛṣṭa iti. anyathā-abhidhānāt ca hita-ahita-prāpti-
432.03	parīhāra-arthaḥ parispandaḥ pravṛtti-nivṛttī bhavatā tu kriyā-mātram
432.04	kriyā-uparamam ca āśritya tad-liṅga-tvāt icchā-dveṣayoḥ pārthiva-ādya^ṣu apratiṣedhaḥ
432.05	iti uktam yathā-vidhe ca pravṛtti-nivṛttī prayujyamāna-bhūta-ādhāras tena
432.06	tathā-bhūte bhūta-mātre bhavataḥ iti  bhūta-indriya-manasām samānaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ
432.07	manas tu udāharaṇa-mātram//
432.08	[3-2-38(39)]yathā-ukta-hetu-tvāt pāratantryāt akṛta-abhyāgamāt ca na
432.09	manasaḥ
432.10	yathā-ukta-hetu-tvāt pāratantryāt akṛta-abhyāgamāt vca na manasaḥ/ yathā-ukta-
432.11	hetu-tvāt iti darśana-sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt iti evam ādīnām hetūnām
432.12	apratiṣedhāt/ pāratantryāt iti paratantryāṇi paratantrāṇi śarīra-indriya-manas^
432.13	dhāraṇa-preraṇa-vyūhana-kriyāsu prayatna-vaśāt pravartante caitanye punar svatantrāṇi
432.14	syuḥ/ akṛta-abhyāgamāt ca cetana-tvāt śarīra-ādīnām taiḥ kṛtam
432.15	karman puruṣena bhujyate iti akṛta-kṛta-āgama-nāśa-doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ acetana-tve
432.16	tu tad-sādhanasya sva-kṛta-karma-phala-upabhogaḥ iti  atha ayam siddha-upasaṅgrahaḥ
432.17	pariśeṣāt yathā-ukta-hetu-upapatti^aḥ ca//
432.18	[3-2-39(40)]pariśeṣāt yathā-ukta-hetu-upapatti^aḥ
432.19	[3-2-40(41)]smaraṇam tu ātmanaḥ jña-svābhāvyāt
432.20	ātmanaḥ guṇaḥ jñānam iti prakṛtam/ yathā-ukta-hetu-upapatti^aḥ iti
433.01	ātma-pratipādakānām hetūnām apratiṣedhāt/ pariśeṣāt iti śarīra-
433.02	indriya-manasām pratiṣedhāt anyasya ca śiṣyamāṇasya asambhavāt ātmanaḥ ca
433.03	śiṣyamāṇa-tvāt tasya guṇaḥ jñānam iti/ upapatti^aḥ iti vā svatantraḥ
433.04	hetuḥ ātma-nitya-tve iti kasya upapatti^aḥ saṃsāra-apavargayoḥ ūpapatti^aḥ iti evam
433.05	ādi bhāṣye/  smṛti-hetūnām ayaugapadyāt ayugapad-smaraṇam iti uktam/ atha
433.06	kebhyaḥ smṛtiḥ utpadyate iti smṛti-kāraṇānām ayaugapadya-pratipādana-artham
433.07	praṇidhāna-ādi sūtram//
433.08	[3-2-41(42)]praṇidhāna-nibandha-abhyāsa-liṅga-lakṣaṇa-sādṛśya-parigraha-āśraya-
433.09	āśrita-sambandha-ānantarya-viyoga-eka-kārya-virodha-atiśaya-prāpti-
433.10	vyavadhāna-sukha-duḥkha-icchā-dveṣa-bhaya-arthitva-kriyā-rāga-dharma-adharma-nimitta^bhyaḥ
433.12	evam upapannam anityā buddhiḥ iti//
433.13	anityāyām ca buddhi^i utpanna-apavargi-tvāt kāla-antara-avasthita-
433.14	tvāt ca saṃśayaḥ utpanna-apavargiṇī buddhiḥ śabda-vat āho kāla-antara-
433.15	sthāyinī kumbha-vat iti utpanna-apavargiṇī iti yuktam kaḥ punar ayam arthaḥ
433.16	utpanna-apavargiṇī iti anyebhyaḥ api vināśibhyaḥ āśutaram vinaśyati iti na
433.17	tu ayam arthaḥ utpatti-anantaram dhvaṃsate iti/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ āśutara-
433.18	vināśinī buddhiḥ jātimat-tve sati asmad-ādi-pratyakṣa-tāyām vyāpaka-dravya-
433.19	samavāyāt śabda-vat pratikṣaṇam apūrva-utpatti^i karaṇa-tve sati pratyartha-prakāśaka-
434.01	tvāt yasya pratikṣaṇam apūrva-utpanna-padārtha-prakāśaka-tvam karaṇa-tve sati
434.02	tasya āśutara-vināśi-tvam dṛṣṭam yathā pradīpasya pratikṣaṇam apūrva-utpannān
434.03	pradhvaṃsinaḥ padārthān buddhiḥ pratyartha-niyamena prakāśayati tasmāt iyam
434.04	api āśutara-vināśinī iti/ atha vā āśutara-vināśinī buddhiḥ guṇa-tve
434.05	sati abāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣa-tvāt sukha-vat iti/ asya arthasya jñāpana-artham karma-
434.06	anavasthāyi-grahaṇāt iti sūtram//
434.07	[3-2-42(43)]karma-anavasthāyi-grahaṇāt
434.08	yadi tarhi anavasthāyi-padārtha^su anavasthitā buddhiḥ atha yadā
434.09	buddhi-sthitam kumbha-ādikam upalabhyate tadā kim kumbha-vat kāla-antara-avasthāyinī
434.10	uta āśuvidhvaṃsinī iti/ avasthita-grahaṇe api pratyakṣa-nivṛtti^aḥ
434.11	pradhvaṃsinī yadā api avasthitam kumbha-ādikam upalabhate tadā api santānena eva
434.12	buddhyaḥ  nivartante kasmāt vyavahite pratyakṣa-nivṛtti^aḥ yadi kumbha-vat kāla-
434.13	antara-avasthāyinī buddhiḥ abhaviṣyat vyavahite api pratyakṣam avāsthāsyat
434.14	na tu avatiṣṭhate tasmāt na kāla-antara-avasthāyinī iti/ smṛti^aḥ avatiṣṭhate
434.15	iti cet yadi buddhiḥ dhvaṃsinī syāt smṛtiḥ na syāt na hi buddhi-
434.16	bhede smṛtim paśyāmaḥ iti yathā puruṣa-antara-buddhiṣu asti ca smṛtiḥ
434.17	tasmāt avatiṣṭḥate buddhiḥ iti na ataḥ eva anavasthānāt na hi buddhi^i avasthitāyām
434.18	smṛtiḥ sambhavati smṛtiḥ nāma pratyakṣa-buddhi-nirodhe tad-pūrvakaḥ
434.19	viṣaya-pratisandhiḥ yathā idam adrākṣam iti etad ca na pratyakṣa-buddhi^i avasthitāyām
434.20	yuktam iti anyataḥ ca tad-bhāvāt smṛtiḥ aliṅgam iti yā iyam smṛtiḥ buddhi^i
434.21	avasthāna-liṅga-tvena abhyupagamyate na iyam liṅgam anyataḥ tad-bhāvāt avatiṣṭhate hi
435.01	buddhi-jaḥ saṃskāraḥ iti tataḥ praṇidhāna-ādi-nimitta-apekṣāt smṛtiḥ
435.02	iti/ hetu-abhāvaḥ iti cet atha manyase saṃskārāt smṛtiḥ na buddhi-
435.03	avasthānāt iti atra kaḥ hetuḥ smṛtiḥ eva yadi avatiṣṭhate buddhiḥ smṛtiḥ na
435.04	syāt//
435.05	[3-2-43(44)]avyakta-grahaṇam anavasthāyi-tvāt vidyut-sampāte rūpa-
435.06	avyakta-grahaṇa-vat
435.07	[3-2-44(45)]hetu-upādānāt pratiṣeddhavya-abhyanujñā
435.08	avyakta-grahaṇam anavasthāyi-tvāt vidyut-sampāte rūpa-avyakta-grahaṇa-vat/
435.09	yadi anavasthāyinī buddhiḥ avyaktasya grahaṇam prāpnoti yathā vidyut-
435.10	prakāśitānām avyakta-grahaṇam iti  na virodhāt avyakta-grahaṇāt avasthāyinī
435.11	buddhiḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ anavasthāyinī buddhiḥ iti yad pratiṣedhyam
435.12	tad abhyanujānāsi tasya ca abhyanujñānāt yad uktam kāla-antara-avasthāyinī
435.13	buddhiḥ iti tad bādhitam bhavati avyakta-grahaṇasya ca anya-nimitta-tvāt na
435.14	avyakta-grahaṇāt buddhi-anavasthānam siddhyati/ yad tad vyaktam avyaktam ca grahaṇam na
435.15	buddhi^aḥ avasthāna-anavasthānābhyām api tu grahaṇa-hetu^ah bhedāt iti tad-asambhavāt ca
435.16	na avyaktam nāma grahaṇam asti kasmāt artha-grahaṇa-mātra-tvāt buddhi^aḥ artha-
435.17	grahaṇa-mātram buddhiḥ/ tad ca artha-grahaṇam sāmānyataḥ viśeṣataḥ ca yad ca
435.18	sāmānya-viṣayam tad sāmānyam prati vyaktam evam viśeṣa-viṣayam evam
435.19	tadvat-viṣayam iti/ tad idam avyakta-grahaṇam deśitam katham ekasmin
435.20	viṣaye buddhi-anavasthāna-kāritam syāt koka-virodhaḥ iti cet yadi
436.01	tarhi vyakta-avyakta-grahaṇe na syātām yaḥ ayam vyavahāraḥ stoka^su vyaktam
436.02	paśyāmi iti saḥ katham na anyathā tad-upapatti^aḥ/ na ayam sāmānya-viśeṣa-
436.03	tadvatsu vyakta-avyakta-grahaṇa-bhedaḥ api tu dharmiṇaḥ sāmānyataḥ adhigatasya
