436.04	viśeṣataḥ upalipsā-dvārakam avyakta-grahaṇam iti/ anekāntāt ca na ayam ekāntaḥ
436.05	asti yad viṣaya-grahaṇa-hetu^aḥ grāhyasya ca anavasthānam tatra avyakta-grahaṇam//
436.06	[3-2-45(46)]na pradīpa-arcis^aḥ santati-abhivyakta-grahaṇa-vat tad-grahaṇam
436.08	na pradīpa-arcis^aḥ santati-abhivyakta-grahaṇa-vat tad-grahaṇam/ anavasthāyi-
436.09	tve api yathā pradīpa-arcis^ām sanatatyaḥ  vartamānānām grāhya-anavasthānam
436.10	grahaṇa-anavasthānam ca bhavati bhavati ca vyaktam grahaṇam arcis^ iti yathā
436.11	arcis^su tathā anyatra api iti vyavasthitam kṣaṇikā buddhiḥ iti/  śarīra-guṇaḥ
436.12	cetanā sati śarīre bhāvāt asati ca abhāvāt iti/ yad ca yasmin sati
436.13	bhavati asati ca na bhavati tasya tad iti vijñāyate yathā rūpa^ādyaḥ
436.14	iti/ na sati bhāvāt śarīra-guṇa-tvam cetanāyāḥ siddhyati
436.15	sati bhāvasya anya-guṇa-tve api darśanāt/ na tāvat saṃyoga-vibhāgas-saṃskārāḥ
436.16	karma-guṇāḥ atha ca sati karmaṇi bhāvaḥ asati ca abhāvaḥ iti/
436.17	saṃyoga-vibhāga-śabda^su ca satsu śabdaḥ bhavati atha ca na tad-guṇaḥ iti
436.18	tad-guṇa-tvam ca tatra upalabdhi^ā siddhyati yadi atra vartamānam upalabhyate rūpa-
436.19	ādikam tad tasya guṇaḥ iti/ atha sati bhāvāt iti anena śarīre cetana-
436.20	upalabdhim brūyāt tasya api asiddha-tā doṣaḥ na hi śarīre cetanā upalabhyate
437.01	cetanā-mātram tu upalabhyate tad ca sandigdham kim śarīre uta anyasmin iti
437.02	upetya śarīre cetanā-upalabdhim kim śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā uta dravya-antara-
437.03	guṇaḥ iti dravye sva-guṇa-para-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ saṃśayaḥ iti ukta-artham sūtram//
437.04	[3-2-46(47)]dravye sva-guṇa-para-guṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ saṃśayaḥ
437.05	na śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā yāvat dravya-bhāvi-tvāt rūpa-ādīnām dṛṣṭānta-
437.06	sūtram//
437.07	[3-2-47(48)]yāvat dravya-bhāvi-tvāt rūpa-ādīnām
437.08	vaidharmyena ye śarīra-guṇāḥ bhavanti te yavat-śarīram vartante yathā
437.09	rūpa-ādyaḥ  na tu yavat-śarīram cetanā vartate tasmāt na tad-guṇaḥ iti/
437.10	sādharmyena vā na tad-guṇaḥ iti yathā apsu uṣṇatā iti/ saṃskāra-vat iti
437.11	cet atha manyase yathā saṃskāraḥ śarīra-guṇaḥ na ca yavat-śarīram
437.12	vartate tathā cetanā api iti na kāraṇa-anucchedāt saṃskārasya na śarīra-
437.13	mātram kāraṇam tasya nodanā-ādi-kāraṇa-sannidhāne tad-abhāve bhāva-abhāvau
437.14	yuktau kāraṇa-sannidhānavataḥ ayuktam etad iti/ cetanā-nimittasya api asannidhānam
437.15	iti cet atha manyase yathā eva saṃskārasya kāraṇam asannihitam evam
437.16	cetanā-kāraṇam api iti tad ayuktam vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ yad śarīre cetanā-
437.17	utpatti-kāraṇam tad kim śarīra-stham uta dravya-antara-stham śarīra-stham api
437.18	kim yavat-śarīra-bhāvi uta naimittikam iti/ yadi yavat-śarīra-
437.19	bhāvi na kadā cit cetanā-hīnam śarīram upalabhyeta nimittasya sannihita-
437.20	tvāt atha naimittikam yad śarīre cetanā-utpatti^aḥ nimittasya kāraṇam
438.01	tatra api cetanāvat-prasaṅgaḥ/ atha dravya-antara-stham dravya-antara-vṛtti-nimittam
438.02	śarīre cetanām karoti na dravya-antara^su iti atra api niyama-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ/
438.03	dravya-antara-stham ca nityam anityam vā anityam api kāla-antara-
438.04	avasthāyin kṣaṇa-pradhvaṃsin vā iti cetanā-vat prasaṅgaḥ/  yad ca manyeta śyāma-
438.05	ādi-guṇam ca dravyam bhavati na ca yāvat-dravya-bhāvinī śyāmatā iti tad ca na
438.06	pākaja-guṇa-antara-utpatti^aḥ//
438.07	[3-2-48(49)]na pākaja-guṇa-antara-utpatti^aḥ
438.08	na atyantam śyāmatā-uparame rūpa-antara-utpattiḥ cetanā-uparamaḥ tu atyantaḥ
438.09	iti/  itaḥ ca pratidvandvi-siddhi^aḥ pākajānām apratiṣedhaḥ
438.10	[3-2-49(50)]pratidvandvi-siddhi^aḥ pākajānām apratiṣedhaḥ
438.11	yāvatsu dravya^su pūrva-guṇa-pratiṣedhaḥ tāvatsu pratidvandvinaḥ guṇa-antarasya
438.12	darśanam yathā śyāma-nivṛtti^i rakta-ādīnām iti/ na punar śarīre
438.13	cetanā-viparītam guṇa-antaram upalabhyate cetanā-mātra-uparamaḥ tu atyantaḥ iti
438.14	atha manyase śarīre api acetanatvam nāma guṇa-antaram iti tad na svarūpataḥ
438.15	anirdeśāt yad tad acetanatvam nāma kim tad cetanā-viparītam dharma-bhūtam vastu
438.16	uta cetanā-vipratiṣedha-mātram iti yadi viparītam cetanā-vat saṃvedyam
438.17	syāt atha cetanā-vipratiṣedha-mātram tad na guṇaḥ iti//
438.18	[3-2-50(51)śarīra-vyāpi-tvāt
438.19	itaḥ ca na śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā śarīra-vyāpi-tvāt śarīra-vat śarīra-
439.01	avayavāḥ cetanā-utpatti^ā vyāpyante/ kaḥ vyāpti-arthaḥ na kaḥ cit śarīra-avayavaḥ
439.02	cetanā-hīnaḥ bhavati iti sarvasya cetanā-nimitta-tvāt śarīra-vat śarīra-avayavāḥ
439.03	cetanāḥ prāpnuvanti/ bhavantu śarīra-avayavāḥ cetanāḥ na pratyaya-vyavasthā-
439.04	prasaṅgāt śarīra-vat śarīra-avayavāḥ cetanāḥ iti pratyaya-vyavasthānam syāt
439.05	na śarīra-avayavāḥ cetanāḥ keśa-nakha-ādiṣu anupalabdhi^aḥ iti dṛṣṭānta-sūtram//
439.06	[3-2-51(52)]na keśa-nakha-ādiṣu anupalabdhi^aḥ
439.07	[3-2-52(53)]tvac-paryanta-tvāt śarīrasya keśa-nakha-ādiṣu aprasaṅgaḥ
439.08	tvac-paryanta-tvāt śarīrasya keśa-nakha-ādiṣu aprasaṅgaḥ saṃyogi-dravyam keśa-
439.09	nakha-ādyaḥ  na tu śarīra-avayavāḥ iti tasmāt na ayam prasaṅgaḥ iti/  itaḥ ca
439.10	na śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā śarīra-guṇa-vaidharmyāt//
439.11	[3-2-53(54)]śarīra-guṇa-vaidharmyāt
439.12	dvaye śarīra-guṇāḥ bhavanti bāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  atīndriyam
439.13	ca gurutvam vidha-antaram tu cetanā na bāhyakaraṇa-pratyakṣā susaṃvedya-tvāt
439.14	na atīndriyā manas-viṣaya-tvāt tasmāt na śarīra-guṇaḥ iti  na rūpa-
439.15	ādīnām itaretara-vaidharmyāt//
439.16	[3-2-54(55)]na rūpa-ādīnām itaretara-vaidharmyāt
439.17	yathā itaretara-vidharmāṇaḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  na śarīra-guṇa-tvam ativartante tathā
439.18	rūpa-ādi-vaidharmye sati cetanā śarīra-guṇa-tvam na hāsyati//
439.19	[3-2-55(56)]aindriyaka-tvāt rūpa-ādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ
439.20	aindriyaka-tvāt rūpa-ādīnām apratiṣedhaḥ/ dvaye śarīra-guṇāḥ bhavanti
440.01	iti uktaḥ sūtra-arthaḥ/ rūpa-ādīnām itaretara-vaidharmyam ananusaṃhitam ca avyāvṛttam
440.02	ca iti vaidharmya-mātram anvayavyatirekin tu cetanayāḥ śarīra-guṇatva-sādhanam na
440.03	bhavati iti tasmāt asamam etad/ ye hetu^āḥ anena prakaraṇena labhyante teṣām
440.04	prayogaḥ na śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā nimitta-antara-abhāve virodhi-guṇa-adarśane
440.05	ca sati ayāvat-dravya-bhāvi-tvāt apsu auṣṇya-vat iti śarīra-vyāpi-tvāt iti
440.06	tu na sādhanam kim tu śarīre cetanām bruvataḥ doṣaḥ aneka-cetanatva-prasaṅgaḥ
440.07	na śarīra-guṇaḥ cetanā bāhyakaraṇa-apratyakṣa-tvāt sukha-ādi-vat iti/ atha idam
440.08	prakaraṇam nirṇīta-artham kasmāt punar ārabhyam iti bahudhā parīkṣyamāṇam tattvam
440.09	niścitataram bhavati iti ataḥ ārabhyate iti parīkṣitā buddhiḥ iti//
440.10	manas avasara-prāptam parīkṣyate tad pratiśarīram ekam anekam iti vā
440.11	vicāre jñāna-ayaugapadyāt ekam manas//
440.12	[3-2-56(57)]jñāna-ayaugapadyāt ekam manas
440.13	ayugapad-jñānāni bhavanti iti pratyakṣam etad ca manasaḥ eka-tvam sādhayati/
440.14	katham iti yadi bahūni manas^ syuḥ pratīndriyam manasaḥ sambandhaḥ
440.15	iti yugapad-aneka-artha-sannidhāne yugapad-aneka-jñānāni bhaveyuḥ yad tu eka-
440.16	indriya-grāhya^su artha^su vijñānānām ayugapad-bhāvaḥ tad na manasaḥ eka-tve liṅgam
440.17	anyataḥ tad-bhāvāt anyathā hi karaṇa-tvāt tad bhavati iti uktam//
440.18	[3-2-57(58)]na yugapad-aneka-kriyā-upalabdhi^aḥ
440.19	na yugapad-aneka-kriyā-upalabdhi^aḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/  asya uttara-dvārakam
440.20	sūtram//
441.01	[3-2-58(59)]ālāta-cakra-darśana-vat tad-upalabdhiḥ āśu-sañcārāt
441.02	ālāta-cakra-darśana-vat tad-upalabdhiḥ āśu-sañcārāt/ yathā ālātasya
441.03	bhramataḥ vidyamānaḥ kramaḥ na upalabhyate tathā buddhīnām āśu-bhāvāt vidyamānaḥ
441.04	kramaḥ na upalabhyate na ca asti ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannā yugapad-utpattiḥ
441.05	yayā bahutvam manasaḥ pratipadyemahi iti//
441.06	[3-2-59(60)]yathā-ukta-hetu-tvāt ca aṇu
441.07	yathā-ukta-hetu-tvāt ca aṇu yaḥ eva eka-tve hetuḥ manasaḥ uktaḥ anena eva aṇu
441.08	manas avasātavyam iti/  manasaḥ khalu bhoḥ sendirya-śarīre vṛttiḥ
441.09	śarīre vicāryamāṇe manas eva vicāritam bhavati ataḥ śarīram punar
441.10	vicāryate/ kim ayam śarīra-sargaḥ karma-nirapekṣaiḥ bhūtaiḥ ārabhyate āhosvit
441.11	sāpekṣaiḥ iti vipratipatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ/ śrūyate khalu atra vipratipattiḥ
441.12	tatra idam tattvam pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt tad-utpattiḥ//
441.13	[3-2-60(61)]pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt tad-utpattiḥ
441.14	pūrva-śarīre yā pravṛttiḥ vāc-buddhi-śarīra-ārambha-lakṣaṇā tad-pūrva-kṛtam tasya
441.15	phalam tad-janitau dharma-adharmau tayoḥ anubandhaḥ ātma-samavetayoḥ avasthānam
441.16	vipāka-kālasya aniyamāt aniyataḥ vipāka-kālaḥ iha amutra jāti-
441.17	antare ca iti tena avasthitena prayukta^bhyaḥ bhūta^bhyaḥ tasya śarīrasya utpattiḥ na
441.18	svatantra^bhyaḥ kasmāt punar karman dharma-adharma-saṃjñakam utpatti-samakālaṃ phalam na
442.01	dadāti/ kaḥ evam āha na dadāti iti vipāka-kālasya aniyamāt yadā
442.02	aśeṣa-kāraṇa-sannidhānam bhavati sannidhāne api yadā kāraṇāni apratibaddhāni
442.03	bhavanti tadā dadāti eva yadā punar na dadāti tadā vipacyamāna-
442.04	karma-atiśaya-pratibandhāt yaḥ vipacyamānaḥ karma-atiśayaḥ bhavati
442.05	yasya dharma-adharma-samākhyātaḥ anupabhukta-phalaḥ tena pratibandhāt vidyamānam
442.06	api karma-phalam na dadāti. yāni vā prāṇi-antarāṇi tasya karmaṇaḥ
442.07	samāna-upabogāni teṣām vipacyamānaiḥ karma-āśayaiḥ pratibandhāt na sarvadā
442.08	phalam yāni vā prāṇi-antarāṇi karma-bhāgīni tasya karmaṇaḥ tad-karmabhiḥ
442.09	pratibandhāt tasya vā karmaṇaḥ sahakāri-dharma-adharma-lakṣaṇam nimittam na asti
442.10	iti ataḥ phalasya anārambhaḥ tasya vā sahakāriṇaḥ pratibandhāt sattva-antara-karmaṇām
442.11	vā sahakāriṇām pratibandhāt na sarvadā phalam dharma-adharmau prayacchataḥ
442.12	iti/ durvijñeyā ca karma-gatiḥ sā na śakyā manuṣya-dharmaṇā avadhārayitum
442.13	ākṛti-mātram tu uktam/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ karma-tantrāṇi
442.14	bhūtāni śarīram ārabhante na nirapekṣāṇi iti/ ayam nyāyaḥ abhidhīyate
442.15	puruṣa-viśeṣa-guṇa-prerita-bhūta-pūrvakam śarīram kārya-tve sati puruṣa-artha-kriyā-
442.16	sāmarthyāt yad puruṣa-artha-samartham tad puruṣa-viśeṣa-guṇa-prerita-bhūta-pūrvakam dṛṣṭam
442.17	yathā ratha-ādi puruṣa-viśeṣa-guṇena prayatnena preritaiḥ bhūtaiḥ ārabhyamāṇam puruṣa-
442.18	artha-kriyā-samartham dṛṣṭam tathā ca śarīram tasmāt idam api sāpekṣaiḥ bhūtaiḥ
442.19	ārabhyate sukha-duḥkha-nimitta-tvāt ghaṭa-ādi-vat iti/ itaḥ ca kārya-tvāt
442.20	ratha-ādi-vat bāhyakaraṇa-grāhya-tve sati rūpa-ādimat-tvāt ghaṭa-ādi-vat eva/
442.21	atra nāstikaḥ evam āha bhūta^bhyaḥ mūrti-upādāna-vat tad-upādānam//
443.01	[3-2-61(62)]bhūta^bhyaḥ mūrti-upādāna-vat tad-upādānam
443.02	yathā karma-nirapekṣa^bhyaḥ bhūta^bhyaḥ nirvartyāḥ mūrtyaḥ  sikatā-śarkarā-gaurikāñcana-
443.04	sargah api iti puruṣa-artha-kriyā-sāmarthyāt iti etasya hetu^aḥ anaikāntika-dvārakam
443.05	sūtram/ sādhana-pakṣe dṛṣṭānta-mātra-tvāt asiddham  atha pakṣaḥ karma-nirapekṣāṇi
443.06	bhūtāni śarīram ārabhante puruṣa-artha-kriyā-sāmarthyāt sikatā-
443.07	ādi-vat iti/ etad ca na sādhyasama-tvāt/
443.08	[3-2-62(63)]na sādhyasama-tvāt
443.09	yathā eva śarīra-sargaḥ karma-nirapekṣaiḥ bhūtaiḥ ārabhyate iti sādhyam tathā
443.10	sikatā-ādīnām akarma-nimittaḥ sargaḥ sādhyate iti/ sikatā-ādīnām api
443.11	karma-nimittaḥ sargaḥ iti etasmāt eva hetu^aḥ sidhyati iti/ yathā ca ubhaya-
443.12	pakṣa-sampratipannaḥ ratha-ādi-dṛṣṭāntaḥ puruṣa-viśeṣa-guṇa-prerita-bhūta-pūrvakaḥ asti
443.13	na tathā akarma-nimitta-sarga-pakṣe dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti iti  viṣamaḥ ca ayam upanyāsaḥ
443.14	bhūta^bhyaḥ mūrti-upādāna-vat iti/ na utpatti-nimitta-tvāt mātā-pitṛ^oḥ//
443.15	[3-2-63(64)]na utpatti-nimitta-tvāt mātā-pitṛ^oḥ
443.16	mātā-pitṛ-śabdena lohita-retas^ī bīja-bhūte gṛhyete tatra pitṛ^oḥ
443.17	putra-phala-anubhavanīye karmaṇī sattvasya garbha-vāsa-anubhavanīyam karmam  saha mātṛ-
443.18	garbha-āśaye śarīra-utpattim prayojayati tatra ca utpannam bīja-anuvidhānam bīja-
443.19	anuvidhānāt ca yad-jātīyau tasya pitṛ^au ca tad-jātīyaḥ sambhavati iti//
443.20	[3-2-64(65)]tathā āhārasya
443.21	tathā āhārasya utpatti-nimitta-tvāt iti prakṛtam/ etad api sikatā-
444.01	ādibhiḥ vaidharmyam eva ucyate āśita-pītam āhāraḥ tasya pākāt rasa-dravyam
444.02	nirvartyate tad-mātṛ-śarīre upacīyate garbha-śarīre ca upahitam
444.03	kalala-ādi-bhāvena upacīyamānam karma-sāpekṣaiḥ bhūtaiḥ pāṇi-ādi-bhāvam āpadyate/
444.04	yadi punar karma-nirpakṣāṇi bhūtāni syuḥ sthāli-ādi-gatam iva annam
444.05	pacyeta na tu idam asti tasmāt sāpekṣāṇi iti  prāpti^i ca aniyamāt//
444.06	[3-2-65(66)]prāpti^i ca aniyamāt
444.07	karma-nirapekṣāṇi bhūtāni śarīram ārabhante iti sarvaḥ dampatī^oḥ
444.08	saṃyogaḥ putra-ādi-phalakaḥ syāt/ karma-sāpekṣa^su bhūta^su upapannaḥ niyama-abhāvaḥ
444.09	iti/  sarva-ātmabhiḥ sambandhāt sādhāraṇa-vigrahavattva-prasaṅgaḥ/ niyama-hetu-
444.10	abhāvāt iti cet atha manyase śarīram sarva-ātmabhiḥ sambandhyate saṃyogajena
444.11	saṃyogena kriyājena vā tatra śarīra-ādi-gataḥ na kaḥ cit niyama-hetuḥ
444.12	iti yena śarīram eva tasya ātmanaḥ vyavatiṣṭheta na ca puruṣa-gataḥ viśeṣaḥ
444.13	kaḥ cit vidyate yena śarīram ekasya puruṣasya upabhoga-sādhanam bhavet asti
444.14	ca niyamaḥ tasmāt vaktavyaḥ niyama-hetuḥ iti/ asya uttara-dvārakam sūtram
444.15	śarīra-utpatti-nimitta-vat saṃyoga-utpatti-nimittam karman
444.16	[3-2-66(67)]śarīra-utpatti-nimitta-vat saṃyoga-utpatti-nimittam karman
444.18	yena eva karmaṇā śarīram utpādyate tad eva saṃyogam niyamayati
444.19	akarma-nimitte tu śarīra-sarge yathā-ukta-doṣaḥ iti/ tad-sthatā kutaḥ iti
444.20	cet atha manyase yadi karman niyāmakam śarīra-sargasya atha karmaṇi
445.01	kutaḥ niyamaḥ iti na sva-saṃyoga-utpatti-niyamāt yaḥ yasya ātmanaḥ
445.02	saṃyogaḥ tasmāt yad karman upajāyate tad tasya bhavati iti/ saṃyoge kutaḥ
445.03	niyamaḥ iti yathā eva karmaṇi paryanuyogaḥ na vyāvartate tathā saṃyoge
445.04	api iti na manas-niyama-hetu-tvāt yasya ātmanaḥ yad manas tena yaḥ saṃyogaḥ
445.05	janyate saḥ tasya bhavati iti manasi api tulyam manasaḥ tarhi sarva-ātmabhiḥ
445.06	sambandhāt kim niyāmakam iti karman eva yad manas yadīyena karmaṇā
445.07	upanibaddham tadīyam tad bhavati iti/ sarga-ādi^i katham iti cet sarga-ādi^aḥ anabhyupagamāt
445.08	adeśyam anādiḥ saṃsāraḥ pūrva-abhyasta-sūtre pratipāditam etad
445.09	ādimati saṃsāre eṣaḥ deśyaḥ na anādi^i iti//
445.10	[3-2-67(68)]etena aniyamaḥ pratyuktaḥ
445.11	etena aniyamaḥ pratyuktaḥ/ kaḥ punar ayam niyamaḥ yathā ekasya ātmanaḥ
445.12	śarīram tathā sarva-ātmanam iti niyamaḥ anyasya anyathā anyasya anyathā iti
445.13	aniyamaḥ bhedaḥ vyāvṛttiḥ dṛṣṭaḥ śarīra-bhedaḥ praṇinām anekarūpaḥ saḥ ca
445.14	karma-nirapakṣaiḥ bhūtaiḥ śarīra-ārambhe na yuktaḥ karmaṇām bhedāt śarīrāṇi
445.15	bhidyante iti yuktam upapannaḥ ca tad-viyogaḥ karma-kṣaya-upapatti^aḥ/ dve
445.16	śarīrasya prakṛtī vyaktā ca avyaktā ca/ tatra avyaktāyāḥ karma-samākhyātāyāḥ
445.17	prakṛti^aḥ upabhogāt prakṣayaḥ prakṣīṇa^e ca karmaṇi vidyamānāni bhūtāni
445.18	na śarīram utpādayanti iti upapannaḥ apavargaḥ karma-nirapekṣa^su kasya
445.19	prakṣayāt mokṣamāṇāḥ apavṛjyeran bhūta-lakṣaṇāyāḥ prakṛti^aḥ anucchedāt//
445.20	[3-2-68(69)]tad adṛṣṭa-kāritam iti cet punar tad-prasaṅgaḥ apavarge
446.01	adṛṣṭakāritam iti cet atha manyase adṛṣṭa-kāritā śarīra-
446.02	utpattiḥ adṛṣṭa-śabdena adarśanam ucyate adarśanm adṛṣṭamiti sarga-ādi^i pradhānam
446.03	puruṣa-arthena hetunā pravartate tad tu pravartamānam mahat-ādi-bhāvena śarīram
446.04	utpādyati utpanne śarīre draṣṭṛ^ḥ dṛśyam paśyati iti/ dṛśyam ca dvedhā
446.05	viṣayaḥ nānātvam ca prakṛti-puruṣayoḥ dṛṣṭena pradhānam carita-artha-tvāt na
446.06	pravartate iti tad evam adarśanam śarīra-sarga-hetuḥ tad-bhāva-bhāvi-tvāt iti
446.07	etasmin khalu adarśane punar tad-prasaṅgaḥ apavarge/ yā ca prāc pravṛtti^aḥ darśana-
446.08	nivṛtti-vat nivṛttiḥ darśana- adarśana-abhimatā yā ca uttara-kālam nivṛtti^aḥ sarge na etayoḥ adarśanayoḥ
446.09	kaḥ cit viśeṣaḥ adarśana-nimittā cet śarīra-utpattiḥ bhavati tena yathā
446.10	prāc nānātva-darśanāt pradhānam puruṣa-artham pravartate tathā apavṛkte api pravarteta
446.11	na ca evam abhyupagamyate tasmāt na adarśanam śarīra-sarge kāraṇam iti/ carita-artha-
446.12	tayā viśeṣaḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam na carita-arthānām acarita-arthānām
446.13	ca bhūtānām punar ārambha-darśanāt ādyena śarīrena rūpa-ādi-upalabdhi^aḥ kṛta-
446.14	tvāt dvitīya-ādi-śarīram śabda-ādi-upalabdhi-sādhanam na syāt bhavati ca
446.15	śabda-ādi-upalabdhi-sādhanam dvitīya-ādi-śarīram tena gamyate carita-arthāni api
446.16	bhūtāni punar śarīram ārabhante iti/ puruṣa-arthena ca hetunā punar
446.17	śarīrāṇi ārabhante iti puruṣa-arthaḥ ca puruṣa-pradhāna-nānātva-darśanam tasya
446.18	ca akāraṇāt anarthakaḥ śarīra-ārambhaḥ bhavet iti/ didṛkṣā-viśeṣaḥ adarśanam
446.19	iti cet atha manyase na adarśanam darśana-abhāvaḥ kim tu didṛkṣā-darśanam
446.20	iti ucyate sā ca apavṛkte pradhāne didṛkṣā na bhavati iti na prāc
446.21	pravṛtti^aḥ tad-abhāvāt yāvat pradhānam mahat-ādi-bhāvena na pariṇamate tāvat
447.01	didṛkṣā na asti katham kāraṇam bhaviṣyati pravṛtti^aḥ iti sarva-śaktimat-tvāt
447.02	tadā api asti iti cet atha manyase sarvāḥ kāraṇa-śaktyaḥ  pradhāne vidyante
447.03	tad idam ātma-bhūtābhiḥ kāraṇa-śaktibhiḥ sarva-śakti-mat-pradhānam sarva-śaktimat-
447.04	tvāt prāc api pravṛtti^aḥ pradhāne didṛkṣā asti iti na apavarga-abhāva-prasaṅgāt
447.05	didṛkṣā-vat nānātva-darśanam api asti iti apavarga-abhāvaḥ vidyamāne ca nānātva-darśane
447.06	pradhānasya pravṛttiḥ ayuktā yadā ca nānātva-darśanam tadā api didṛkṣā na
447.07	vyāvartate na hi sat-ātmānam jahāti iti vaḥ pakṣaḥ/ didṛkṣāyām ca satyām
447.08	kutaḥ apavargaḥ didṛkṣā-nānātva-darśane ca viruddhe katham ekasmin kāle
447.09	bhavataḥ/ ajñānam adarśanam iti ca bruvāṇaḥ vikalpataḥ paryanuyojyaḥ kim
447.10	jñāna-abhāvaḥ ajñānam uta mithyā-jñānam iti kim ca ataḥ yadi abhāvaḥ apavargaḥ na
447.11	prāpnoti prāc pravṛtti^aḥ apavṛkte ca pradhāne jñāna-abhāvasya tulya-tvāt/ atha
447.12	mithyā-jñānam tad an yuktam prāc pravṛtti^aḥ tad-abhāvāt katham abhāvaḥ buddhi-
447.13	dharmaka-tvāt apavṛkte ca pradhāne na buddhiḥ iti katham tad-dharmaḥ ajñānam
447.14	bhaviṣyati iti satkāryavāda-abhyupagamāt sarvadā asti iti bruvāṇaḥ mokṣam
447.15	bādhate/ katham tattva-jñānasya sarvadā-bhāvāt tattva-jñāna-mithyā-jñānayoḥ ca
447.16	saha anavasthānāt pravṛtti^aḥ tādātmyam ayuktam/ na hi bhavatām pakṣe kim
447.17	cit asat bhavati sat ca ātmānam jahāti iti sarva-arthānām sambhavāt pradhānam
447.18	kim artham pravartate iti vaktavyam/ atha abhivyakti-nimittām pravṛttim pratipadyethāḥ
447.19	sā api abhivyaktiḥ prāc pravṛtti^aḥ satī aho asatī iti pūrvavat-prasaṅgaḥ
447.20	atha prāc anupalabdham paścāt upalabhate kim ūpajāta-viśeṣam uta anupajāta-viśeṣam
447.21	iti/ yadi viśeṣa-upajananāt paścāt upalabhyate vyāhatam bhavati anupajāta
448.01	viśeṣaḥ kasmāt prāc na upalabhyate iti vaktavyam satkāryavāda-
448.02	abhyupagame ca sphūrjatā api na viśeṣa-upajanaḥ śakyaḥ pratipādayitum na
448.03	ca viśeṣa-upajana-pratyākhyāne vastunnaḥ upalabdhi-anupalabdhi-viṣaya-tvam śakyam
448.04	avagamayitum karma-nimitte tu sarge darśana-artham bhūtāni śarīram
448.05	puruṣa-guṇa-preritāni ārabhante iti yuktam na hi anutpanne śarīre draṣṭṛ^ḥ
448.06	nirāyatanaḥ dṛśyam paśyati iti/ apare tu adṛṣṭam paramāṇu-guṇam varṇayanti
448.07	teṣām api punar tad-prasaṅgaḥ apavargaḥ iti paramāṇu-guṇasya śarīra-utpatti-
448.08	nimitta-tvāt punar tad-prasaṅgaḥ apavargaḥ iti//
448.09	[3-2-69(70)]manas karma-nimitta-tvāt ca saṃyoga-ādi-anucchedaḥ
448.10	manas karma-nimitta-tvāt ca saṃyoga-avyucchedaḥ/ paramāṇu^āḥ sva-guṇāt
448.11	adṛṣṭāt saṃhanyante saṃhatāḥ ca dvyaṇuka-ādi-prakaraṇe śarīram utpādayanti
448.12	tad manas sva-guṇāt adṛṣṭāt ūpasarpati iti/ upasarpaṇa-hetu^aḥ adṛṣṭasya nitya-tvāt
448.13	kim-kṛtam apasarpaṇam iti/ karma-āśaya-kṣaye karma-āśayāt apasarpaṇam iti yuktam
448.14	prāyaṇam ubhaya-hetuḥ manas-vṛttiḥ adṛṣṭaḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam ekasya
448.15	jīvana-prāyaṇa-hetutva-anupapatti^aḥ  nityatva-prasaṅgaḥ ca prāyaṇa-anupapatti^aḥ
448.16	[3-2-70(71)]nityatva-prasaṅgah ca prāyaṇa-anupapatti^aḥ
448.17	nityatva-prasaṅgaḥ iti prāyaṇa-anupapattim brūmaḥ tad idam uktam upasarpaṇa-
448.18	hetu^aḥ adṛṣṭasya anitya-tvāt apasarpaṇa-hetu^aḥ ca adṛṣṭasya abhāvāt yādṛcchike tu
448.19	prāyaṇe prāyaṇa-bhedaḥ na syāt//
448.20	[3-2-71(72)]aṇu-śyāmatā-nityatva-vat etad syāt
449.01	aṇu-śyāmatā-nityatva-vat etad syāt iti parihāram varṇayanti/ yathā
449.02	kila aṇu^aḥ śyāmatā nityā atha api saṃyogena pratiṣidhyate evam aṇu-
449.03	manas^oḥ adṛṣṭaḥ samyac-jñānena pratiṣidhyate iti/  etad ca na akṛta-abhyāgama-
449.04	prasaṅgāt iti//
449.05	[3-2-72(73)]na akṛta-abhyāgama-prasaṅgāt
449.06	anupapanna-pramāṇasya abhyupagamaḥ akṛta-abhyāgamaḥ iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ yathā-
449.07	śruti vā sūtra-arthaḥ akṛtasya karmaṇaḥ upabhoga-prasaṅgāt iti/ etad savistara-
449.08	uktam bhāṣye//
449.09	ātman^ḥ śarīram karaṇam arthaḥ buddhiḥ manas tathā/
449.10	yad yathā vastu tattvena tad tathā iha upapāditam//
449.11	iti auddyotakare nyāyasūtravārttike tṛtīya-adhyāyasya
449.12	dvitīyam āhnikam//
449.13	tṛtīyah adhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ//450.01	nyāyavārttike
450.02	caturthaḥ adhyāyaḥ
450.03	manasaḥ anantaram pravṛttiḥ avasara-prāptā parīkṣitavyā iti ataḥ āha/
450.04	[4-1-1]pravṛttiḥ yathā-uktā
450.05	pravṛttiḥ yathā-uktā tathā parīkṣitā iti arthaḥ sūtrasya/ kim punar pravṛtti^aḥ
450.06	parīkṣitam iti yāvat dharma-adharma-āśrayam parīkṣitam pūrvā pravṛtti^aḥ parīkṣā/
450.07	tatra pravṛttiḥ dvedhā bhavati kāraṇa-rūpā kārya-rūpā ca/ kāraṇa-rūpā
450.08	pravṛttiḥ vāc-buddhi-śarīra-ārambhaḥ iti/ kārya-rūpā dharma-adharma-samākhyā
450.09	duḥkha-ādi-sūtre varṇitā/ tatra kāraṇa-rūpāyāḥ pravṛtti^aḥ viṃśatidhā bhedaḥ
450.10	kārya-rūpā tu pravṛttiḥ eka-dravyā abhyudayasya pratiniyamāt avasthitā
450.11	vyāpaka-kālasya niyamāt ātma-samavāyāt guṇa-bhūtā anityā
450.12	prāyaṇa-ādi-darśanāt/ tad uktam vipacyamāna-karma-āśaya-prakṣaye prāyaṇam iti
450.13	janmanaḥ kāraṇam pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt tad-utpattiḥ iti saṃsāra-apavarga-
450.14	hetu-bhūta bhūtā tad-bhāve saṃsāraḥ tad-abhāve apavargaḥ iti evam ādi uktam//
450.15	pravṛtti^aḥ anantarāḥ tarhi doṣāḥ iti abhidhīyantām ity ataḥ āha tathā
450.16	doṣāḥ//
450.17	[4-1-2]tathā doṣāḥ
450.18	uktāḥ iti/ buddhi-samāna-āśraya-tvāt ātma-guṇāḥ saṃsārasya anādi-tvāt
451.01	anādinā pravṛttena pravartante samyac-jñānāt ca mithyā-jñāna-nivṛtti^i
451.02	nivartante iti utpāda-nirodha-dharmakāḥ kārya-tve sati indriya-antara-pratyakṣa-
451.03	tvāt acākṣuṣa-pratyakṣa-tvāt ca guṇāḥ/  pravartanā-lakṣaṇāḥ doṣāḥ iti uktam
451.04	tathā ca māna-ādyaḥ  pravartayanti iti lakṣaṇa-sāmyāt te api upasaṃkhyeyāḥ iti
451.05	na upasaṃkhyāynate saṃgṛhīta-tvāt kena saṃgrahaḥ pravartanā-lakṣaṇāḥ doṣāḥ
451.06	iti anena eva/ teṣām tu saṃgṛhītānām rāga-dveṣa-moha-artha-antara-bhāvāt//
451.07	[4-1-3]tad trairāśyam rāga-dveṣa-moha-artha-antara-bhāvāt
451.08	teṣām doṣāṇām tryaḥ  rāśyaḥ  tryaḥ  pakṣāḥ sambhavanti rāga-pakṣaḥ
451.09	kāmaḥ matsaraḥ spṛhā tṛṣṇā lobhaḥ iti/ dveṣa-pakṣaḥ krodhaḥ īrṣyā
451.10	asūyā drohaḥ amarṣaḥ iti/ moha-pakṣaḥ mithyā-jñānam vicikitsā mānaḥ
451.11	prasādaḥ iti/ kāmaḥ matsaraḥ iti ādi kim paryāya-vacanam āho
451.12	artha-antara-bhāvaḥ iti/ kiṃ ca ataḥ yadi paryāya-vacanam tryaḥ  rāśyaḥ
451.13	iti ayuktam/ na hi paryāya-śabda^su rāśitvam sambhavati na hi indra-
451.14	śakra-purandara-śabdānām rāśi-arthaḥ kaḥ cit sambhavati/ artha-antara-bhāve
451.15	viśeṣaḥ vācyaḥ iti/ artha-antara-bhāvaḥ iti brūmaḥ/ tatra kāmaḥ
451.16	strī-gataḥ abhilāṣaḥ yā strī-gatā prārthanā sā kāmaḥ iti/ evaṃ ca āhuḥ
451.17	na akāmayamānaḥ maṇḍayate iti/ akṣīyamāṇa-vastu-aparityāga-icchā
451.18	matsaraḥ yad vastu dīyamānam upabhujyamānam vā na kṣīyate tad aparityāga-icchā
451.19	matsaraḥ/ yathā rājakīya-upādānāt mā udakam pā iti/ avastu-ādāna-icchā
452.01	spṛhā yad vastu svam na bhavati tasya yā ādāna-icchā sā spṛhā/ punarbhava-
452.02	pratisandhāna-hetu-bhūtā tṛṣṇā yā punarbhava-prārthanā sā tṛṣṇā/
452.03	pramāṇa-viruddha-para-dravya-apahāra-icchā lobhaḥ pramāṇa-viruddham para-dravya-ādānam
452.04	kurvāṇaḥ lubdhaḥ iti ucyate/ eṣām ca sāmānyam rāgaḥ sāmānyena viśeṣaḥ
452.05	saṃgṛhītaḥ iti śarīra-indriya-adhiṣṭhāna-vaikṛtya-hetuḥ krodhaḥ saṃjāte
452.06	yasmin śarīra-indriya-adhiṣṭḥānāni vikṛtāni bhavanti saḥ krodhaḥ iti/
452.07	sādhāraṇe vastuni para-abhiniveśa-pratiṣedha-icchā īrṣyā yad aparigṛhītam
452.08	sādhāraṇam vastu tasmin yaḥ para-abhiniveśa-pratiṣedha-abhiprāyaḥ sā īrṣyā/ para-
452.09	guṇa-akṣamatā asūyā yā para-guṇān śrutvā akṣamatā upajāyate sā asūyā/
452.10	para-apakāra-icchā drohaḥ/ aśaktasya param prati apacikīrṣā sā drohaḥ apakāra-
452.11	asahiṣṇutā amarṣaḥ/ asya ca dveṣaḥ sāmānyam tena saṃgrahaḥ viparyaya-
452.12	jñānam mithyā-jñānam atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ iti/ saṃśayaḥ vicikitsā
452.13	kasmāt iti tathā vidyamāna-avidyamāna-guṇa-adhyāropena ātma-utkarṣa-
452.14	pratyayaḥ mānaḥ āho aham asmi iti/ śaktasya kartavya-akaraṇam pramādaḥ
452.15	yad śaktaḥ sva-kartavyam na karoti saḥ pramādaḥ iti/ asya ca sāmānyam mohaḥ
452.16	tena saṃgrahaḥ iti taḥ trairāśyāt na upasaṃkhyāyante iti/ kutaḥ etad
452.17	lokataḥ tad-avagati^aḥ/ lokataḥ ete kāma-ādyaḥ  evambhūta^su abhiprāya^su
452.18	prayujyante na abhiprāya-mātre na hi bhikṣām prārthayamānaḥ kāmayate
452.19	iti evam bhavati iti/ lakṣaṇasya tarhi abhedāt tritvam anupapannam
453.01	iti ekam rāga-dveṣa-mohāḥ lakṣaṇa-abhedāt rāga-bheda-vat iti/ na anupapannam
453.02	rāga-dveṣa-moha-artha-antara-bhāvāt sati api lakṣaṇasya abhede āntargaṇikam
453.03	bhedakam lakṣaṇam asti tad upapatti^aḥ tritvam/ yathā rāga-lakṣaṇena saṃgṛhītānām
453.04	kāma-ādīnām iti/ na ca sāmānyam ekatve kāraṇam tathā ca sāmānyasya
453.05	eva abhāvaḥ syāt yadi rāga-dveṣa-mohānām ekatvam syāt samānam
453.06	etad lakṣaṇam iti etad eva na syāt iti na hi ekasya samānam bhavati iti/ kim
453.07	punar āntargaṇikam lakṣaṇam iti sapti-lakṣaṇaḥ rāgaḥ yaḥ viṣaya^su abhiṣaṅgaḥ
453.08	saḥ rāgaḥ amarṣa-lakṣaṇaḥ dveṣaḥ asahiṣṇutā duḥkhasya duḥkha-
453.09	sādhanānām ca dveṣaḥ mithyā-pratipatti-lakṣaṇaḥ mohaḥ yathā avasthita-
453.10	viṣaye ayathābhāva-pratipattiḥ yā etad ca rāga-dveṣa-mohānām vyavacchedakam
453.11	lakṣaṇam ātma-pratyakṣam iti//
453.12	[4-1-4]na eka-pratyanīka-tvāt
453.13	ekam rāga-dveṣa-mohāḥ eka-pratyanīka-tvāt vibhāga-vat iti/ yathā
453.14	vibhāgaḥ ekena eva saṃyogena viruddhyamānaḥ ekaḥ bhavati tathā tattva-
453.15	jñānena ekena viruddhyante rāga-ādyaḥ  tasmāt ekam rāga^ādyaḥ  iti//
453.16	[4-1-5]vyabhicārāt ahetuḥ
453.17	vyabhicārāt ahetuḥ eka-pratyanīkāḥ ca rūpa-ādyaḥ  eka-agni-saṃyoga-
453.18	virodhinaḥ na ca eṣām ekatvam iti anaikāntikaḥ ayam eka-yoni-tvāt ekam
454.01	rāga-ādyaḥ  śabda-vat iti api anena eva pratyuktam/ eka-yonyaḥ  rūpa^ādyaḥ  na
454.02	ca eṣām ekatvam iti  sati ca artha-antara-bhāve//
454.03	[4-1-6]teṣām mohaḥ pāpīyān na amūḍhasya itara-utpatti^aḥ
454.04	teṣām mohaḥ pāpīyān pāpatamaḥ pāpiṣṭhaḥ iti vā prāpte
454.05	dvau dvau abhipretya pāpīyān iti ucyate rāga-mohayoḥ mohaḥ pāpīyān
454.06	dveṣa-mohayoḥ mohaḥ pāpīyān iti/ kasmāt na amūḍhasya itarau bhavataḥ mūḍhaḥ
454.07	kupyati mūḍhaḥ rajyate mūḍhaḥ muhyati iti/ tattva-jñānāt ca moha-nivṛtti^i
454.08	rāga-ādi-nivṛttiḥ iti eka-pratyanīkatā yasmāt tattva-jñānāt nivṛtte mohe
454.09	na rāga-dveṣau bhavataḥ ataḥ eka-pratyanīkatā na punar eka-tvāt/
454.10	evam ca kṛtvā duḥkha-ādīnām uttara-uttara-apāye tad-anantara-abhāvāt apavargaḥ
454.11	iti sidhyati/  prāptaḥ tarhi
454.12	[4-1-7]nimitta-naimittka-bhāvāt artha-antara-bhāvaḥ doṣa^bhyaḥ
454.13	nimitta-naimittka-bhāvāt artha-antara-bhāvaḥ doṣa^bhyaḥ/ adoṣaḥ
454.14	mohaḥ doṣa-nimitta-tvāt rūpa-ādi-vat iti//
454.15	[4-1-8]na doṣa-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt mohasya
454.16	na doṣa-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt na kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvena padārthānām
454.17	tathābhāvaḥ atathābhāvaḥ vā sidhyati kim tu lakṣaṇasya abhedāt iti
454.18	doṣa-lakṣaṇam ca mohe asti iti doṣaḥ mohaḥ//
455.01	[4-1-9]nimitta-naimittika-upapatti^aḥ ca tulya-jātīyānām apratiṣedhaḥ
455.03	nimitta-naimittika-upapatti^aḥ ca tulya-jātīyānām apratiṣedhaḥ tulya-
455.04	jātīyāyām aneka-vidha-vikalpaḥ nimitta-naimittika-bhāvaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti/
455.05	yathā buddhir buddhi-antara-nimittam buddhi-jātīyā ca iti/ turī-ādyaḥ  dravyasya
455.06	nimittam dravya-jātīyāḥ nodanā-abhighāta-saṃyukta-saṃyogāḥ saṃskāraḥ ca guṇāḥ
455.07	iti parīkṣitāḥ doṣāḥ iti//
455.08	doṣa-anantaram pretyabhāvaḥ tasyāḥ siddhar siddhiḥ ātmanaḥ nitya-tvāt nityaḥ
455.09	ātman^ḥ na jāyate na miryate ubhayam ca pretyabhāvaḥ mṛtvā punarjanman
455.10	tatra ayam siddha-artha-anuvādaḥ/
455.11	[4-1-10]ātma-nitya-tve pretyabhāva-siddhiḥ
455.12	ātma-nitya-tve pretyabhāva-siddhiḥ/ nityaḥ san ātman^ḥ praiti jāyate
455.13	ca iti kim uktam bhavati mriyate iti pūrva-utpannābhiḥ śarīra-indriya-buddhi-
455.14	vedanābhiḥ viyujyate iti/ kim ca uktam jāyate iti apūrva-utpannābhiḥ nikāya-
455.15	viśiṣṭābhiḥ śarīra-indriya-buddhi-vedanābhiḥ sambaddhyate iti/ na punar
455.16	janma-maraṇe ātmani utpāda-nirodhau nitya-tvāt nityaḥ ātman^ḥ iti
455.17	pūrva-abhyasta-sūtre pratipāditam. yasya punar ātmanaḥ utpāda-nirodhau
455.18	janma-maraṇe tasya akṛta-abhyāgama-kṛta-nāśa-doṣa-prasaṅgaḥ iti uktam śarīra-
456.01	dāhe pātaka-abhāvād iti sūtram varṇayabhiḥ/  katham utpattiḥ iti cet
456.02	kim-dharmakāt kāraṇāt śarīra-indriya-mahābhūta-ādi vyaktam utpadyate
456.03	iti//
456.04	[4-1-11]vyakta-avyaktānām pratyakṣa-prāmāṇyāt
456.05	vyakta-avyaktānām pratyakṣa-prāmāṇyāt/ kim punar vyaktam upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-
456.06	prāptam rūpa-ādi-yuktam dravyam tad-sāmānyāt paramāṇu-lakṣaṇam api pṛthivī-
456.07	ādi-kāraṇam vyaktam iti ucyate/ kim sāmānyam rūpa-ādi-yogaḥ rūpa-ādi-
456.08	sāmānya-yogāt paramāṇu^āḥ vyaktāḥ iti ucyante iti rūpa-ādi-yukta^bhyaḥ
456.09	paramāṇubhyaḥ ghaṭa-ādi go-ādi vyaktam utpadyate iti/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ
456.10	pratyakṣa-prāmāṇyāt rūpa-ādimatām utpattiḥ iti na anekāntāt na ayam
456.11	ekāntaḥ rūpa-ādimadbhyaḥ rūpa-ādimat-utpattiḥ iti arūpāt api saṃyogāt
456.12	rūpa-ādimat utpadyamānam ghaṭa-ādi go-ādi dṛṣṭam iti. na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt
456.13	na ayam sūtra-arthaḥ rūpa-ādimataḥ eva rūpa-ādimat utpadyate iti api
456.14	tu rūpa-ādimat-sāmagrī-pūrvakam go-ādi vyaktam iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ evam ca
456.15	na anekāntaḥ asti na hi arūpāyāḥ sāmagrī^āḥ rūpa-ādimat kāryam iti//
456.16	[4-1-12]na ghaṭād ghaṭa-aniṣpatti^aḥ
456.17	na ghaṭād ghaṭa-aniṣpatti^aḥ/ sajātīyāt sajātīyam utpadyate iti
457.01	matvā pratiṣedhaḥ na sūtra-artha-aparijñānāt iti na brūmaḥ sarvam sarvasya
457.02	kāraṇam iti yaḥ ca sarvam sarvasya kāraṇa-tvena abhyupeyāt saḥ evam pratyavastheyaḥ
457.03	na ghaṭāt ghaṭaḥ utpadyamānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti/  api tu
457.04	[4-1-13]vyaktāt ghaṭa-niṣpatti^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ
457.05	vyaktāt ghaṭa-niṣpatti^aḥ apratiṣedhaḥ/ yad api tad ghaṭa-ādi vyaktam utpadyate
457.06	tad api vyaktāt eva kapāla-ādi^aḥ vyaktāt ca utpadyamānam ghaṭa-ādi na kimcit
457.07	bādhate iti/ rūpa-ādimat-sāmagrī-pūrvakam śarīra-ādi vyaktam darśana-
457.08	sparśanābhyām eka-artha-grahaṇāt ghaṭa-vat iti śabda-vat vā vaidharmyena tad etad tattvam/
457.09	ataḥ param prāvādukānām darśanāni upanyasya kāni cit pratiṣidhyante
457.10	kāni ciat upagamyante iti/ tatra tāvat
457.11	[4-1-14]abhāvāt bhāva-utpattiḥ na anupamṛdya prādurbhāvāt
457.12	[4-1-15]vyāghātāt aprayogaḥ
457.14	abhāvāt bhāva-utpattiḥ na anupamṛdya prādurbhāvāt/ abhāvād bhāvaḥ jāyate
457.15	iti kasya cit darśanam tatra nyāyam bruvate na anupamṛdya prādurbhāvāt iti na
457.16	kila avinaṣṭe kāraṇe kāryam utpadyate iti/ yathā bīja-vināśa-uttara-kālam
457.17	aṅkuraḥ prādurbhavati yadi bīja-vināśaḥ aṅkura-kāraṇam na abhaviṣyat avinaṣṭe
457.18	api bīje 'ṅkuraḥ abhaviṣyat na ca evam asti tasmāt bīja-vināśaḥ aṅkura-
457.19	kāraṇam iti  upamṛdya prādurbhavati iti vyāghātaḥ yad upamṛdnāti tad asti
458.01	na hi asat upamarda-kāraṇam iti yad ca prādurbhavati tad na asti na hi
458.02	sat utpadyate iti tad idam upamṛdnāti prādurbhavati ca iti vyāhatam ucyate
458.03	kaḥ vyāghātaḥ saha-asambhavaḥ yadi upamṛdnāti na jāyate atha jāyate
458.04	na upamṛdnāti//
458.05	[4-1-16]na atīta-anāgatayoḥ kāraka-śabda-prayogāt
458.06	na atīta-anāgatayoḥ kāraka-śabda-prayogāt/ atīte anāgate ca
458.07	kāraka-śabdaḥ pravartamānaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ atīte tāvat bhinnaṃ kumbham anuśocati
458.08	bhinnasya kumbhasya kapālāni abhūt kumbhaḥ iti/ anāgate
458.09	api putraḥ janiṣyate janiṣyamāṇam putram abhinandati janiṣyamāṇasya
458.10	putrasya nāma karoti ajātāḥ putrāḥ sthaviraṃ tāpayanti iti bahulam
458.11	prayogāḥ bhāktāḥ iti/ evam ayam prādurbhaviṣyan aṅkuraḥ upamṛdnāti iti ucyate
458.12	kaḥ punar upamarda-arthaḥ ānantarya-sāmarthyāt anantara-utpatti-arthaḥ yasmāt
458.13	bīja-upamardāt anantaram ayam prādurbhavati iti/ atra utkam vyāghātāt iti
458.14	na ajātena upamardaḥ na upamarda-samarthaḥ jāyate iti/ yad api idam ucyate
458.15	atīte anāgate ca kāraka-śabdāḥ prayujyante iti n aprayogaḥ vāryate
458.16	api tu asataḥ kāraṇāt utpattiḥ na yuktā iti kāraka-śabdānām ca traikālikaḥ
458.17	prayogaḥ tad uktam prāmāṇya-sāmānya-cintāyām vṛkṣaḥ tiṣṭhati iti yathā/
458.18	yad punar etad ānantarya-sāmarthyāt upamṛdya prādurbhavati iti ucyate iti na
458.19	bīja-nāśaḥ aṅkura-kāraṇam api tu vyāhata-vyūhānām bīja-avayavānām pūrva-
459.01	vyūha-nivṛtti^i vyūha-antaram utpadyate iti vyūha-antarāt aṅkuraḥ āvirbhavati iti/
459.02	na ca avinaṣṭe pūrva-vyūhe vyūha-antarena śakyate bhavitum iti ānantaryam
459.03	prādurbhāva-arthaḥ/ evam ca bīja-upādānam yuktam iti/  asya ca arthasya
459.04	jñāpana-artham sūtram//
459.05	[4-1-17]na vinaṣta^bhyaḥ aniṣpatti^aḥ
459.06	na vinaṣta^bhyaḥ aniṣpatti^aḥ iti/ na hi bīja-vināśaḥ aṅkurasya
459.07	kāraṇam api tu bīja-avayavāḥ pūrva-vyūha-parityāgena iti arthaḥ sūtrasya//
459.08	[4-1-18]krama-nirdeśāt apratiṣedhaḥ
459.09	krama-nirdeśāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ pūrvam bīja-vināśaḥ paścāt aṅkura-utpattiḥ iti
459.10	sūtra-arthaḥ/ abhāvaḥ cet aṅkura-utpatti-kāraṇam syāt sarvam sarvasmāt utpadyeta/
459.11	abhāvasya nirviśeṣa-tvāt/ na hi śāli-bīje vinaṣṭe kā cit anvaya-
459.12	śaktiḥ asit ananvitam ca kāryam sarvābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ utpadyeta anvitam tu
459.13	dṛṣṭam tasmāt na abhāvaḥ kāraṇam iti sūtra-arthaḥ/  aparaḥ idānīm āha//
459.14	[4-1-19]īśvaraḥ kāraṇam puruṣa-karma-aphalya-darśnāt
459.15	īśvaraḥ kāraṇam puruṣa-karma-aphalya-darśnāt/ puruṣaḥ ayam samīhamānaḥ
459.16	na avaśyam samīha-aphalam āpnoti tena gamyate para-adhīnam puruṣasya karma-
459.17	phala-avadhānam iti/ nirapekṣaḥ cet puruṣaḥ karma-phala-bhoge samarthaḥ syāt
460.01	na kasya cit aphalā kriyā bhavet kaḥ cit duḥkham kuryāt iti/ ubhayam
460.02	ca dṛṣṭam tasmāt īśvaraḥ kāraṇam iti//
460.03	[4-1-20]na puruṣa-karma-abhāve phala-aniṣpatti^aḥ
460.04	na puruṣa-karma-abhāve phala-aniṣpatti^aḥ iti/ īśvaraḥ cet kāraṇam syāt
460.05	puruṣa-karma-antarena pi sukha-duḥkha-upabhogau syātām tataḥ ca karma-lopaḥ
460.06	anirmokṣaḥ ca/ īśvarasya eka-rūpa-tvāt eka-rūpā kriyā iti/ atha īśvaraḥ kāraṇa-
460.07	bheda-anuvidhānena kāryam nirvartayati yad apekṣate tad na karoti iti prāptam
460.08	na hi kulālaḥ daṇḍa-ādi karoti evam karma-sāpekṣaḥ cet īśvaraḥ jagat-
460.09	utpatti-kāraṇam syāt karmaṇi īśvaraḥ na īśvaraḥ syāt//
460.10	[4-1-21]tad-kārita-tvāt ahetuḥ
460.11	tad-kārita-tvāt ahetuḥ/ na brūmaḥ karma-ādi-anapekṣaḥ īśvaraḥ kāraṇam
460.12	iti/ api tu puruṣa-karman īśvaraḥ anugṛhṇāti kaḥ anugraha-arthaḥ/
460.13	yad yathā-bhūtam yasya yadā vipāka-kālaḥ tad tathā tadā viniyuṅkte
460.14	iti/ yaḥ punar īśvaram karma-anapekṣam kāraṇa-tvena pratipadyate tasya anirmokṣatva-
460.15	ādi-doṣaḥ/ sāpekṣe tu īśvare yathā-uktaḥ na doṣaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/
460.16	tad-kārita-tvāt evam bruvatā nimittakāraṇam īśvaraḥ iti upagatam bhavati/
460.17	yad ca nimittam tad itarayoḥ samavāyikāraṇa-asamavāyikāraṇayoḥ anugrāhakam/
460.18	yathā turī-ādi tantūnām tad-saṃyogānām ca iti/ īśvaraḥ cet
460.19	jagataḥ nimittam jagataḥ sākṣāt upādāna-kāraṇam kim uktam pṛthivī-ādi
461.01	paramasūkṣmaṃ paramāṇu-saṃjñitam dravyam iti vyakta-kāraṇa-abhyupagame tu sati
461.02	nimitta-viśeṣa-vipratipatti^i īśvara-prakriyā/ yasmāt nimittakāraṇe
461.03	vipratipadyante ke cit kālaṃ ke cit īśvaram ke cit prakṛtam iti tad evam
461.04	nimitta-viśeṣa-vipratipatti^i kim nyāyam iti/ īśvaraḥ iti nyāyam tatra
461.05	hi pramāṇāni avighāten pravartante iti/ astitva-asiddhiḥ iti cet
461.06	atha manyase siddhe īśvara-astitve kāraṇa-antara-nirākaraṇam nimittakāraṇa-
461.07	bhāvaḥ ca siddhyet tattva-siddham tasmāt ayuktam iti/ na ataḥ
461.08	eva tad-upapatti^aḥ iti yena eva nyāyena īśvarasya kāraṇa-tvam sidhyati
461.09	tena eva astitvam iti na hi avidyamānam kāraṇam iti/ kaḥ punar īśvarasya
461.10	kāraṇa^tve nyāyaḥ ayam nyāyaḥ abhidhīyate pradhāna-paramāṇu-karmāṇi
461.11	prāk pravṛtti^aḥ buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitāni pravartante acetana-tvāt vāsī-ādi-
461.12	vat iti yathā vāsī-ādi buddhimatā takṣṇā adhiṣṭhitam acetana-tvāt
461.13	pravartate tathā pradhāna-paramāṇu-karmāṇi acetanāni pravartante tasmāt
461.14	tāni api buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitāni iti tatra pradhāna-kāraṇikāh tāvat
461.15	puruṣa-artham adhiṣṭhāyakam pradhānasya varṇayanti puruṣa-arthena prayuktam pradhānam
461.16	pravartate puruṣa-arthaḥ ca dvedhā bhavati śabda-ādi-upalabdhiḥ guṇa-puruṣa-antara-darśanam
461.17	ca iti tad ubhayam pradhāna-pravṛtti^aḥ vinā na bhavati iti na prāk pravṛtti^aḥ tad-
461.18	abhāvāt yāvat pradhānam mahat-ādi-bhāvena na pariṇamate tāvat na
461.19	śabda-ādi-upalabdhiḥ asti na guṇa-puruṣa-antara-upalabdhiḥ iti hetu-abhāvāt
461.20	pradhāna-pravṛttiḥ ayuktā atha astinā sat-ātmānam lambhate na sat niruddhyate
462.01	iti evam ca sati vidyamānaḥ puruṣa-arthaḥ pradhānam pravartayati iti na
462.02	puruṣa-arthāya pradhānasya pravṛttiḥ na hi loke yad yasya bhavati saḥ tad-artham
462.03	punar yatate iti/ satatam ca pravṛttiḥ prāpnoti kāraṇasya sannihita-tvāt
462.04	iti puruṣa-arthaḥ pravṛtti^aḥ kāraṇam iti puruṣa-arthasya  nitya-tvāt satatam
462.05	pravṛtti^ā bhavitavyam iti/ atha vidyamānaḥ api na pravartayati na tarhi
462.06	puruṣa-arthaḥ kāraṇam iti yasya abhāvāt pradhānam na pravartate yasya ca
462.07	bhāvāt pravartate tad kāraṇam iti/ atha vidyamānaḥ pratibandhāt na pravartayati
462.08	pratibandha-apagamasya aśakya-tvāt satatam apravṛttiḥ yad pratibandha-kāraṇam puruṣa-
462.09	arthasya  tasya apagamaḥ kartum aśakyaḥ na sat-ātmānam jahāti iti pratibandhakasya
462.10	nitya-tvāt nityam apravṛtti^ā bhavitavyam/ yadā bhavantaḥ sattva-
462.11	rajas-tamasāṃ sāmya-avasthām prakṛtim varṇayanti sā kutaḥ nivartate iti
462.12	vaktavyam/ na ca anivṛttāyām sāmya-avasthāyām vaiṣamyena śakyam bhavitum/
462.13	atha aṅga-aṅgi-bhāvasya aniyamāt vaiṣamyam bhavati atra api bhavantam paryanuyuñjmahe/
462.14	katham sāmyena avasthitam adhikam hīnam ca bhavati na apūrva-upacayaḥ
462.15	vidyate na pūrva-hānam asti iti yān ca śabda-ādīn prāk-anupalabdha-svarūpān
462.16	puruṣaḥ upalabhate buddhiḥ upalambhayati te kim upajāta-viśeṣāḥ uta
462.17	anupajāta-viśeṣāḥ iti/ yadi upajāta-viśeṣāḥ upalabhyante iti vyāhatam
462.18	bhavati na asat-ātmānam labhate iti/ atha anupajāta-viśeṣāḥ eva upalabhyate
462.19	tathā api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ pradhānam puruṣa-arthaḥ pravartayati iti/
462.20	saḥ ayam pradhāna-vādaḥ yāvat yāvat vicāryate tāvat tāvat pramāṇa-vṛttam
462.21	bādhate iti/ ye paramāṇūn puruṣa-karma-adhiṣṭhitān jagataḥ kāraṇa-
462.22	tvena varṇayanti tān prati idam ucyate paramāṇavaḥ pravartante iti satatam
463.01	pravṛtti^ā bhavitavyam/ atha kāla-viśeṣa-apekṣāḥ pravartante pramāṇubhiḥ
463.02	kālaḥ vyākhyātaḥ yathā acetana-tvāt paramāṇu^āḥ buddhimantam adhiṣṭḥātṛ^am
463.03	apekṣante tathā kālaḥ api iti na hi tatra acetanatvam nivartate
463.04	iti/ kṣīra-ādi-vat acetanasya api pravṛttiḥ iti cet yathā apatya-bharaṇa-artham
463.05	kṣīra-ādi^aḥ acetanasya api pravṛttiḥ evam paramāṇu^āḥ api acetanāḥ puruṣa-artham
463.06	pravartiṣyante iti/ tad na yuktam sādhyasama-tvāt yathā eva paramāṇu^āḥ
463.07	svatantrāḥ pravartante iti sādhyam tathā kṣīra-ādi-acetanam svatantram pravartate
463.08	iti/ yadi kṣīra-ādi svatantram pravarteta mṛta^ṣu api pravarteta na tu
463.09	pravartate ataḥ avagamyate buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitam tad api na ca ayam hetuḥ
463.10	tasmāt na pravartate evam yāvat yāvat acetanam pravartate tāvat sarvam cetana-
463.11	adhiṣṭhitam iti/ ayam aparaḥ hetuḥ buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭḥitam
463.12	mahābhūta-ādi vyaktam iti sukha-duḥkha-ādi-nimittam bhavati rūpa-ādimat-
463.13	tvāt turī-ādi-vat iti/ dharma-adharmau buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitau puruṣasya
463.14	upabhogam kurutaḥ karaṇa-tvāt vāsī-ādi-vat iti/ ātman^ḥ eva adhiṣṭhātṛ^ḥ
463.15	dharma-adharmayor bhaviṣyati iti cet yasya etau dharma-adharmau saḥ eva adhiṣṭhātṛ^ḥ
463.16	bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam prāk kāya-karaṇa-utpatti^aḥ tad-asambhavāt yāvat
463.17	kāya-karaṇa-saṅghātaḥ na upajāyate puruṣasya tāvat ayam ajñaḥ upalabhyān api
463.18	tāvat rūpa-ādīn na upalabhate kutaḥ anupalabdhau dharma-adharmau upalapsyate iti/
463.19	yadi puruṣaḥ svatantraḥ pravartate na duḥkham kuryāt na hi kaḥ cit
464.01	ātmanaḥ duḥkham icchati iti/ yaḥ ca ātmanaḥ aṅga-upaghātam śiras-cheda-ādi vā
464.02	karoti tad-vaikalye prāyaṇe vā hita-buddhiḥ pravartate iti/ yadi
464.03	punar dharma-adharmābhyām eva adhiṣṭhitāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ pravarteran na yuktam etad
464.04	acetana-tvāt na hi kim cit acetanam svatantram adhiṣṭhāyakam dṛṣṭam iti abhyupagamya
464.05	api ca dharma-adharmayoḥ paramāṇu-pravṛtti-sāmarthyam na karaṇasya
464.06	kevalasya kiryā-nivṛtti^i asāmarthyāt na hi karaṇam kevalam kriyām
464.07	nirvartayat upalabhyate/ atha paramāṇu-apekṣābhyām dharma-adharmābhyām kriyate
464.08	tad api na yuktam adṛṣṭa-tvāt na hi karma-karaṇābhyām kriyām janyamānām
464.09	kva cit api paśyāmaḥ iti ātman^ḥ kartṛ^ḥ bhaviṣyati iti uktam etad ajña-tvāt
464.10	iti/ akāraṇa-utpattiḥ bhaviṣyati iti na yuktam adṛṣṭa-tvāt iti/ na
464.11	ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ karmāṇi
464.12	ca pravartante iti/ kriyā-anāveśāt akāraṇam iti cet atha manyase
464.13	ye khalu kartṛ^āḥ bhavanti te kriyā-āviṣṭhāḥ kulāla-ādyaḥ  iti kriyā-
464.14	rahitaḥ ca īśvaraḥ tasmāt akāraṇam iti na vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ īśvaraḥ niṣkriyaḥ
464.15	iti kām kriyām adhikṛtya ucyate/ dvayī hi naḥ kriyā utkṣepaṇa-ādikā
464.16	ca ākhyāta-śabda-vācyā ca/ yadi ākhyāta-śabda-vācyām adhikṛtya ucyate
464.17	tadā asiddhaḥ hetuḥ svātantrya-abhyupagamāt svātantryam hi bhagavati
464.18	nityam asti/ kim punar svātantryam anyakāraka-aprayojya-tvam itara-kāraka-
464.19	aprayojaka-tvam ca tad uktam kārakāni varṇayadbhiḥ iti/ atha utkṣepaṇa-ādikām
464.20	adhikṛtya ucyate niṣkriyaḥ iti tadā anekāntaḥ kriyāvat ca kāraṇam dṛṣṭam
465.01	niṣkriyam ca iti kadā cit dravyāṇi uparata-kriyāṇi dravyam ārabhante
465.02	saṃyogāt nivṛtte karmaṇi saṃyoga-upakaraṇāni dravyāṇi dravyam ārabhante
465.03	iti niṣkriyāṇām ārambhaḥ/ yadā ca yugapad bahūni dravyāṇi saṃhanyante
465.04	tadā asādhāraṇa-kārya-vyāvṛtta^bhyaḥ saṃyoga^bhyaḥ ekam eva dravyam utpadyate
465.05	eka-avayava-vibhāge tu dravya-nivṛtti^i śeṣāṇi dravya-antarāṇi dravyam utpādayante
465.06	iti niṣkriyāṇām ārambhaḥ/ kāni cit punar kriyāvanti
465.07	ārabhante yadā antara-karma-jāt saṃyogāt nivṛtte karmaṇi itarasmin dravye
465.08	karma-nivṛtti-samakālam eva dravyam utpadyate tadā kriyāvatā dravyena ārambhāt
465.09	kriyāvatām ārambhaḥ virodhaḥ ca utkṣepaṇa-ādikāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ anabhyupagamāt
465.10	iti/ na kāraṇam īśvaraḥ vikalpa-anupapatti^aḥ/ kartṛ^ḥ cet īśvaraḥ kim
465.11	sāpekṣaḥ karoti uta nirapekṣaḥ iti kim ca ataḥ yadi sāpekṣaḥ yena
465.12	karoti tasya akartṛ^ḥ evam anyatra api prasaṅgaḥ/ tad api sādhanam yena karoti
465.13	tasya akartṛ^ḥ iti/ atha ayam kim cit anapekṣya karoti tadvat anyatra api prasaṅgaḥ/
465.14	atha ayam sarvam anapekṣya karoti evam api puruṣa-karma-aphalam bhavet anirmokṣaḥ
465.15	ca prasajyeta yaḥ ca akarma-nimitte sarge doṣaḥ saḥ iha api prasajyate
465.16	iti nirapekṣa-kartṛ-tvasya anabhyupagamāt dharma-adharma-viphala-tva-ādi-
465.17	doṣaḥ na asti na ca akarma-nimitte sarge doṣaḥ iti/ yena karoti na
465.18	tasya akartṛ^ḥ iti cet na anekāntekāntāt anekāntāt na ayam ekāntaḥ asti yaḥ yena
465.19	karoti saḥ tad na karoti iti yathā aneka-śilpa-paryavadātaḥ puruṣaḥ karaṇa-
466.01	antara-upādānaḥ vāsī-ādi karoti vāsī-ādi-upādānaḥ daṇḍa-ādi
466.02	karoti tad-upādānaḥ ghaṭa-ādi na ca paryāya-kartṛ-tve sati kartṛtvam
466.03	tathā īśvaraḥ api dharma-adharma-upādānaḥ śarīra-sukha-duḥkha-ādi ātma-manas-saṃyoga-
466.04	śuddha-aśuddha-abhisandhi-sādhanaḥ ca dharma-adharmau sukha-duḥkha-smṛti-apekṣaḥ tad-sādhana-
466.05	ādi-apekṣaḥ ca śuddham aśuddham ca abhisandhim iti/ yadā karoti tadā sādhanasya
466.06	akartṛ^ḥ iti cet atha manyase yadā ayam sādhyam yad kim cit dṛṣṭam nirvartayati
466.07	tadā yena sādhayati tasya akartṛ^ḥ prasajyate iti/ na etad evam brūmaḥ
466.08	sarvān arthān ayam ekasmin kāle karoti iti api tu paryāyena paryāya-
466.09	kartṛ-tve ca ayam adoṣaḥ/ yadā ādi^i karoti tasy asādhana-utpattiḥ iti cet
466.10	atha manyase yadi śarīra-ādi-kartṛ-tvam dharma-adharma-ādi-apekṣasya atha yadā ādi^i
466.11	karoti katham tad ādi^aḥ anabhyupagamāt adeśyam etad anādiḥ saṃsāraḥ
466.12	iti pratipāditam etad dharma-adharma-sāphalyam ca evam yadi ca anādi saṃsāraḥ
466.13	ca apekṣaḥ ca kartṛ^ḥ evam prāṇi-antara-samavāyinām dharma-adharmāṇām sāphalyam/
466.14	atha ayam īśvaraḥ kurvāṇaḥ kim artham karoti loke hi ye kartṛ^āḥ bhavanti
466.15	te kim cit uddiśya pravartante idam āpsyāmi idam hāsyāmi ca iti na punar
466.16	īśvarasya heyam asti duḥkha-abhāvāt na upādeyam vaśi-tvāt/ krīḍā-artham
466.17	iti eke eke tāvad bruvante krīḍa-artham īśvaraḥ sṛjati iti nanu etad ayuktam
466.18	krīdā hi nāma rati-artham bhavati vinā krīdayā ratim avindatām na ca
466.19	rati-arthī bhagavān duḥkha-abhāvāt iti/ duḥkhinaḥ ca sukha-upagama-artham
466.20	krīḍanti/ vibhūti-khyāpana-artham iti apare/ jagataḥ vaiśvarūpyam khyāpanīyam
467.01	iti apare manyante/ etad api tādṛś eva na hi vibhūti-khyāpanena kaḥ cit
467.02	atiśayaḥ labhyate na ca asya ākhyāpanena kim cit hīyate iti/ kim-artham
467.03	tarhi karoti tad-svābhāvyāt pravartate iti aduṣṭam yathā bhūmi-ādīni
467.04	dhāraṇā-ādi-kriyām tad-svābhāvyāt kurvanti tathā īśvaraḥ api tad-svābhāvyāt
467.05	pravartate iti pravṛtti-svabhāvakam tattvam iti/ tad-svābhāvyāt satatam
467.06	pravartate iti cet atha manyase yadi pravṛtti-svābhāvakam tattvam pravṛtti-
467.07	nivṛttī na prāpnutaḥ na hi pravṛtti-svabhāvake tattve nivṛttiḥ yujyate iti
467.08	kramen utpattiḥ na prāpnoti tattvasya ekarūpa-tvāt idam idānīm bhavatu idam
467.09	idānīm na bhavatu iti na yuktam na hi ekarūpāt kāraṇāt kārya-
467.10	bhedaḥ paśyāmaḥ iti/ na eṣaḥ doṣaḥ buddhimat-tvena viśeṣaṇāt buddhimat-tattvam
467.11	iti pratipāditam/ buddhimat-tayā ca viśiṣyamāṇam sāpekṣam ca na
467.12	sarvadā pravartate na sarvam ekasmin kāle utpādayati yasya kāraṇa-sānnidhyam
467.13	tad bhavati yada asannihita-kāraṇam tad na bhavati na ca sarvasya yugapad kāraṇa-
467.14	sānnidhyam asti ataḥ sarvasya yugapad-utpādaḥ na yuktaḥ saḥ khalu pravartamānaḥ
467.15	dharma-adharmayoḥ paripāka-kālam apekṣate kāraṇa-antara-utpādam tad-bhāginām
467.16	ca sattvānām tatra sannidhānam tad-bhāgi-sattva-dharma-adharma-aparipākam ca
467.17	tad apratibandham ca iti/ yad tad īśvarasya aiśvaryam kim tad nityam anityam iti/
467.18	yadi anityam tasya kāraṇam vācyam yasya ca anityam aiśvaryam tasya kāraṇa-bhedaḥ
467.19	bhavati aṇima-ādi^aḥ evam anyeṣām api iti anekaḥ īśvaraḥ prasajyate iti/
468.01	atha aneka-tve kim bādhyate iti ekasmin vastuni vyāhata-kāmayor
468.02	īśvarayoḥ pravṛttiḥ na prāpnoti atha ekam itaraḥ atiśete yaḥ atiśete saḥ
468.03	īśvaraḥ na itaraḥ iti/ atha nityam aiśvaryam dharma-vaiyarthyam na tad-dharmāt bhavati
468.04	iti/ nityam iti brūmaḥ/ na ca dharma-vaiyarthyam doṣaḥ tasya yaḥ dharmaḥ
468.05	īśvare na asau tatra aiśvaryam karoti kim tu pratyātma-vṛttīn dharm-adharma-sanicayān
468.06	anugṛhṇāti na ca īśvare dharmaḥ asti iti acodyam etad tad-svabhāva-anavadhāraṇāt
468.07	sandehaḥ īśvaraḥ kim dravyam āho guṇa-ādīnām anyatamaḥ iti/ dravyam
468.08	buddhi-guṇa-tvād dravya-antara-vat buddhimat-tvāt tarhi ātma-antaram iti na
468.09	ātma-antaram guṇa-bhedāt/ tad yathā guṇa-bhede sati pṛthivī-ādyaḥ  na ātmānaḥ
468.10	tathā guṇa-bhinnaḥ īśvaraḥ tasmāt asau api na ātma-antaram iti/ kaḥ punar
468.11	etasya bhedaḥ/ eke tāvat bruvate dharma-jñāna-vairāgya-aiśvaryāṇi-atiśayavanti
468.12	tasmin iti nityatvam atiśayaḥ/ etad tu na buddhyāmahe yathā buddhimat-tāyām
468.13	īśvarasya pramāṇa-sadbhāvah na ca evam dharma-ādi-nitya-tve pramāṇam asti na
468.14	ca aprāmāṇikam pratipattum śakyam atiśayaḥ tu buddhi-nitya-tvam guṇa-bhedaḥ
468.15	tatra hi nityā buddhiḥ saṃkhyā-ādyaḥ  sāmānya-guṇāḥ ṣaṭ-guṇaḥ ākāśa-
468.16	vat īśvaraḥ iti/ atha asya buddhi-nitya-tve kim pramāṇam iti/ nanu idam eva
468.17	buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ pravartante iti buddhimat-tāyām
468.18	etad sādhanam sā punar nityā iti kutaḥ pratyartha-niyama-asambhavāt ye khalu
468.19	pratyartha-niyatāḥ buddhi-bhedāḥ te śarīra-ādi-kāraṇa-sannidhāne sati bhavanti
468.20	na tu iayam pratyartha-niyatā yugapad-aneka-kārya-utpatti-darśanāt yathā sthāvara-
469.01	bhedasya anekasya yugapad-utpādaḥ iti/ saḥ ca pratyartha-niyata-buddhi-bheda^su
469.02	īsvarasya na yuktaḥ saṃkhyā-parimāṇa-pṛthaktva-saṃyoga-vibhāga-buddhyaḥ  eva
469.03	tasya guṇāḥ/ atha buddhimat-tayā īśvarasya śarīra-yogam api pratipadyate/
469.04	tena api pratipadyamānena śarīra-ādyaḥ  nityāḥ anityāḥ vā avaśyam eṣitavyāḥ/
469.05	yadi anityāḥ dharma-adharma-sadbhāvaḥ abhyupeyaḥ tad-abhyupagame ca tad-tantra-
469.06	tvāt īśvaraḥ na īśvaraḥ syāt/ atha nityān śarīra-ādīn kalpayasi
469.07	evam api dṛṣṭa-viparītam kalpitam bhavati dṛṣṭa-viparyayam pratipadyamānena
469.08	buddhiḥ nityatvam pratipattavyam/ atha santāna-vartinīm anekām buddhim
469.09	īśvare pratipadyate evam api na yugapad-utpādaḥ sthāvara-ādīnām prāpnoti
469.10	atha tāḥ santāna-vartinī^āḥ buddhyaḥ  bhavanti/ evam api dṛṣṭa-viparītam
469.11	kalpitam bhavati/ evam ca kalpayatā buddhi-nitya-tvam eva pratipattavyam/
469.12	etāvat ca etat syāt nityā vā santāna-vartinī vā santāna-vartinī
469.13	na yuktā/ yadi guṇa-bhedāt bhedaḥ yad abhinna-guṇam tad ekam prāpnoti yathā
469.14	diś-kālau iti na anekāntāt guṇa-bhedāt nānātvam brūmaḥ na punar guṇa-abhedāt
469.15	ekatvam iti tathā hi abhinna-guṇānām ghaṭa-ādīnām nānātvam iti
469.16	diś-kālayoḥ guṇa-bhede api kārya-bhedāt nānātvam iti/ na ca buddhimat-tayā
469.17	vinā īśvarasya jagat-utpādaḥ ghaṭate iti/ sā ca buddhiḥ sarva-artha-atīta-anāgata-
469.18	vartamāna-viṣayā pratyakṣā na ānumānikī na āgamikī na tatra anumānam
469.19	na āgamaḥ iti jñāna-nitya-tvāt ca na saṃskāraḥ nityam vijñānam īśvarasya iti
470.01	na tatra saṃskāraḥ vidyate iti saṃskāra-abhāvāt buddhi-nitya-tvāt ca na smṛtiḥ
470.02	smṛti-abhāvāt ca na anumānam na duḥkham adharmasya abhāvāt ataḥ eva na vairāgyam
470.03	iti duḥkha-abhāvāt na virajyate iti ataḥ eva na dveṣaḥ duḥkha-bhāvāt iti/
470.04	icchā tu vidyate akliṣṭā avyāhatā sarva-artha^su yathā buddhiḥ iti/ atha
470.05	kim ayam baddhaḥ muktaḥ iti/ na baddhaḥ duḥkha-abhāvāt eva abaddha-tvāt na muktaḥ
470.06	iti bandhavān mucyate iti na ca bhagavati bandhanam asti iti ataḥ na
470.07	muktaḥ iti/ ātma-antarāṇām asambandhāt adiṣṭhātṛtvam anupapannam iti cet
470.08	atha manyase artha-antara-samavāyinaḥ ye dharma-adharmāḥ te sākṣāt īśvarena
470.09	sambaddhyante na pāramparyena na ca anadhiṣṭhitayoḥ dharma-adharmayoḥ pravṛttiḥ yuktā
470.10	iti/ tad ca na aja-sambandha-upapatti^aḥ ajaḥ sambandhaḥ ātma-antarāṇām iti eke
470.11	icchanti na ca etad iha pratiṣidhyate iti apratiṣedhāt upāttaḥ saḥ iti/
470.12	te tu ajam sambandham pramāṇataḥ pratipādayanti vyāpakaiḥ ākāśa-ādibhiḥ sambaddhaḥ
470.13	īśvaraḥ mūrtimat-dravya-sambandhi-tvāt ghaṭa-vat iti yathā ghaṭa-ādi mūrtimatā
470.14	ghaṭa-ādinā sambandhitva-vyāpakaiḥ ākāśa-ādibhiḥ sambaddhyate tathā īśvaraḥ api
470.15	mūrtimat-sambandhin^ḥ iti tasmāt ayam api vyāpakaiḥ ākāśa-ādibhiḥ sambaddhyate
470.16	iti/ saḥ punar ātma-īśvara-sambandhaḥ kim vyāpakaḥ avyāpakaḥ iti artha-abhāvāt
470.17	avyākaraṇīyaḥ eṣaḥ ātma-īśvara-sambandhaḥ asti iti etad eva śakyate vaktum/
470.18	saḥ punar īśvara-ātmānau vyāpnoti na vyāpnoti iti na vyākriyate/ ye api ajam
470.19	saṃyogam na icchanti teṣām api aṇu-manas-saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ asti sambandhaḥ
470.20	yāni pratyātma-manas^i tāni sarvāṇi īśvara-sambaddhāni iti ataḥ sambandha-sambandha-
470.21	upapatti^aḥ ātma-antarāṇi adhitiṣṭhati yathā ātma-hasta-saṃyoga-prayatnābhyām haste
471.01	karman bhavati utpanna-karmakaḥ hastaḥ sandaṃśa-ādinā sambaddhyate tad-sambandhāt
471.02	ayaḥpiṇḍa-ādi adhitiṣṭhati/ yadi tarhi sarga-ādi^i īśvarasya kāraṇa-tve ayam
471.03	nyāyaḥ abhihitaḥ idānīm īśvaraḥ na kāraṇam iti prāptam idānīm api
471.04	saḥ eva nyāyaḥ mṛta-śarīriṇām yau dharma-adharmau tau buddhimat-kāraṇa-
471.05	adhiṣṭhitau iti samānaḥ nyāyaḥ/ buddhimat-kāraṇa-adhiṣṭhitāni svāsu
471.06	svāsu dhāraṇā-ādi-kriyāsu mahābhūtāni vāyu-antāni pravartante acetana-
471.07	tvāt vāsī-ādi-vat/ evam kārya-tvāt tṛṇa-ādīni pakṣa^kṛtya darśana-sparśana-
471.08	viṣaya-tvāt iti vaktavyam/ evam yatra yatra vipratipattiḥ kāryatvam ca
471.09	tad tad anena eva nyāyena anena dṛṣṭāntena vāsī-ādinā pakṣayitvā sādhayitavyam/
471.10	āgamāt ca āgamāt api śrūyate īśvaraḥ kāraṇam/
471.11	ajñaḥ jantuḥ anīśaḥ ayam ātmanaḥ sukha-duḥkhayoḥ/
471.12	īśvara-preritaḥ gacchet svargam vā svabhram eva vā//
471.13	yadā saḥ devaḥ jāgarti tadā idam ceṣṭate jahat/
471.14	yadā svapiti śānta-ātman^ḥ tadā sarvam nimīlati//
471.15	[4-1-22]animittataḥ bhāva-utpattiḥ kaṇṭaka-taikṣṇya-ādi-darśanāt
471.17	aparaḥ idānīm āha/ animittataḥ bhāva-utpattiḥ kaṇṭaka-taikṣṇya-
471.18	ādi-darśanāt/ yathā kaṇṭaka-taikṣṇya-ādi nimittam ca upādāna-vat ca
471.19	tathā śarīra-ādi-sargaḥ api tad idam dṛṣṭānta-sūtram/ kaḥ punar atra nyāyaḥ
471.20	animittāḥ racanā-viśeṣāḥ śarīra-ādyaḥ  saṃsthānavat-tvāt kaṇṭakā-ādi-vat
471.21	iti/ na anupalabdha-nimittānām anumānataḥ nimitta-upalabdhiḥ yasya
472.01	nimittam pratyakṣataḥ na upalabhyate tasya anumānataḥ pratyetavyam kutaḥ
472.02	nimittavat-dravya-sāmānyāt yāni khalu nimittavanti dravyāṇi saṃsthāna-
472.03	viśeṣavanti tāni ghaṭa-ādīni saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat-śarīram kaṇṭaka-ādyaḥ  ca
472.04	tasmāt nimittavantaḥ iti/ uktam ca atra kim uktam pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt
472.05	tad-utpattiḥ iti/ na ca ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat animittam
472.06	dṛṣṭam iti  apare tu  iti
472.07	parihāram varṇayanti//
472.08	[4-1-23]animitta-nimitta-tvāt na animittataḥ
472.09	animittam eva nimittam yataḥ ca utpattiḥ tad nimittam iti/ animitta-
472.10	nimitta-tvāt na animittataḥ utpattiḥ iti  etad ca na nimitta-
472.11	animittayoḥ artha-antara-bhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ//
472.12	[4-1-24]nimitta-animittayoḥ artha-antara-bhāvāt apratiṣedhaḥ
472.13	anyat nimittam anyat ca nimitta-pratyākhyānam iti na ca pratyākhyānam
472.14	eva pratyākhyeyam bhavati nimittam iti artha-abhyanujñā animittam iti
472.15	tasya pratiṣedhaḥ abhyanujñā-pratiṣedhayoḥ ca ekatvam ayuktam iti/ saḥ khalu ayam
472.16	vādaḥ akarma-nimittaḥ sattva-sargaḥ iti etasmāt na bhidyate iti/ abhede tu
472.17	tad-pratiṣedhena eva pratiṣiddhaḥ boddhavyaḥ kaṇṭaka-ādīnām ca animittam janman iti
472.18	bruvāṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ/ kim kaṇṭaka-mātrasya animittam janman uta sarvasya iti/ yadi
472.19	kaṇṭaka-mātrasya animittam janman tad-śeṣena dṛṣṭāntena śakyam pratipādayitum
473.01	nimitta-vat iti/ atha sarvam animittam iti ayam pakṣaḥ tathā api vyāghātaḥ
473.02	pratipādya-pratipādaka-nyāyasya abhyupagamāt sarvam ca animittam pratipādayasi
473.03	ca iti vyāghātaḥ/ vākya-upādānāt ca vyāghātaḥ animittataḥ bhāva-utpattiḥ
473.04	kaṇṭaka-taikṣṇya-ādi-vat iti vākyam pratipādakam upādīyate na ca nimittam
473.05	asti iti vyāghātaḥ/ animittataḥ bhāva-utpattiḥ iti sanimittā bhāva-
473.06	utpattiḥ iti ca vākye yadi anayoḥ artha-bhedam pratipadyase vākya-bhedāt artha-bheda-
473.07	pratipattiḥ iti vyāhatam bhavati animittā bhāva-utpattiḥ iti/ atha
473.08	na vākya-bhedena artha-bhedam pratipadyase vākya-viśeṣa-upādānam vyāhatam
473.09	yad kim cit vākyam upādeyam iti animittām bhāva-utpattim pratipadyamānena
473.10	sarva-loka-vyavahāraḥ ucchinnaḥ bhavati animittā bhāva-utpattiḥ iti ca
473.11	bhāva-mātra-pakṣa^karaṇena dṛṣṭāntaḥ bhavati/ atha śarīra-ādi-ekadeśam pakṣayasi
473.12	tathā api anyatara-dharma-asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat-tvasya vidyamāna-
473.13	tvāt animittatvasya ca abhāvāt iti/  anye tu manyante sarvam
473.14	anityam utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvāt//
473.15	[4-1-25]sarvam anityam utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvāt
473.16	kim anityam nāma yasya kadā cit bhāvaḥ tad anityam utpatti-dharmakam
473.17	anutpannam na asti vināśa-dharmakam ca vinaṭṣam na asti kim punar sarvam
473.18	bhautikam śarīra-ādikam abhautikam ca buddhi-ādi tad ubhayam utpatti-vināśa-
473.19	dharmakam iti vijñāyate iti/ tasmāt sarvam anityam it sūtrasya
473.20	dṛṣṭānta-artha-tvāt tasya sarva-antarbhāvāt udāharaṇa-abhāvaḥ/ sarvam anityam
474.01	iti brūṣe dṛṣṭānta-abhāvaḥ sarvasya pakṣa^kṛta-tvāt na sādhyam udāharaṇam
474.02	yuktam utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvāt iti ca ayam hetuḥ avyāpakaḥ/ yadi sarvam
474.03	pakṣayitvā utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvam upādatse paramāṇu-ākāśa-ādiṣu
474.04	tad-guṇa^su ca keṣu cit sāmānya-ādiṣu na utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvam asti iti
474.05	hetuḥ avyāpakaḥ  na anityatā-nitya-tvāt
474.06	[4-1-26]na anityatā-nitya-tvāt
474.07	sarvam anityam eva iti bruvāṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ kim sarvasya anityatā nityā
474.08	āhosvit anityā iti/ yadi anityatā nityā na tarhi sarvam anityam atha
474.09	anityā anityatāyāḥ abhāvāt sarvam anityam iti vyāhataḥ hetuḥ//
474.10	[4-1-27]tad-anityatvam agni^aḥ dāhyam vināśya anuvināśa-vat
474.11	tad-anityatvam agni^aḥ dāhyam vināśya anuvināśa-vat/ yathā agni^aḥ dāhyam
474.12	vināśya anuvinaśyati evam anityatā api sarvam vināśya anuvinaśyati/
474.13	ayam ca khalu vādaḥ sarvam anityam iti nityam vyācaṣṭe//
474.14	[4-1-28]nityasya apratyākhyānam yathā-upalabdhi-vyavasthānāt
474.15	nityasya pratyākhyānam na yuktam yathā-upalabdhi-vyavasthānāt yatra yatra
474.16	ayam hetuḥ upalabhyate tatra tatra hetu-sāmarthyāt anityatvam pratipadyāmahe yatra
474.17	punar ayam hetuḥ na asti teṣām anityatvam katham pratipattavyam/ atha akāraṇakam
474.18	anityam pratipadyase vyāhataḥ tarhi hetuḥ utpatti-vināśa-dharmaka-tvāt iti
474.19	anityam iti bruvāṇena avaśyam nityam abhyupeyam nañaḥ prasajya-paryudāse
475.01	pratiṣedha-viṣaya-tvāt ayam nañ pravartamānaḥ prasajya-pratiṣedhena pravartate
475.02	paryudāsena vā tathā ca uttara-pada-siddhaḥ yadi nityam na bhavati iti anityam
475.03	yad anyatra bhavati tad anyatra pratiṣidhyate iti siddham uttara-padam/ atha
475.04	nityāt anityam iti tathā api uttara-padena bhavitavyam na asati uttara-pada-arthe
475.05	tasya anyatvam siddhyati iti anityatā-bhāvāt ca sarvam anityam iti na doṣaḥ
475.06	anityatvam nāma dharmaḥ saḥ asati dharmiṇi na bhavati iti evam ca sati
475.07	sarvasya anityatvam na doṣaḥ iti/ atha punar utpatti-dharmakena anityena siddham
475.08	śeṣam sandihyamāna-anityatvam viparīta-nityatvam ca sattvāt anityam iti
475.09	sādhayet tasya api uttara-pada-siddhi^ā nityatva-siddhiḥ iti pratijñā-doṣāt vākyam
475.10	nivartate iti/  ayam aparaḥ ekāntaḥ//
475.11	[4-1-29]sarvam nityam pañca-bhūta-nitya-tvāt
475.12	sarvam nityam pañca-bhūta-nitya-tvāt/ bhūta-mātram idam sarvam tāni ca
475.13	nityāni bhūta-uccheda-anupapatti^aḥ sarvam nityam iti//
475.14	[4-1-30]na utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ
475.15	na utpatti-vināśa-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ/ utpatti-kāraṇam bhāvānām upalabhyate
475.16	vināśa-kāraṇam ca tad-ubhayam nityatve na syāt na hi nityam nāma
475.17	jāyate vinaśyati iti//
475.18	[4-1-31]tad-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt apratiṣedhaḥ
476.01	tad-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ yad idam utpadyate vinaśyati iti ca
476.02	manyase na tad-bhūta-lakṣaṇa-hīnam gṛhyate bhūta-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt sarvam nityam
476.03	iti/ sarvam anityam iti ca bruvāṇaḥ na kaḥ cit jāyate vinaśyati iti
476.04	pratipadyate tad-pratipatti^i ca hita-ahita-prāpti-parihāra-arthaḥ asya parispandaḥ
476.05	vyarthaḥ āpadyeta sarvasya ca pakṣa^karaṇāt dṛṣṭānta-abhāvaḥ vākya-upādānam
476.06	ca vyāhatam/ yad idam vākyam para-pratipādanāya upādīyate sarvam nityam
476.07	pañca-bhūta-nitya-tvāt iti anena vākyena ayam kim karoti kim asiddham sādhayati
476.08	uta siddham nivartayati/ yadi tāvat asiddham sādhayati kā iyam
476.09	siddhiḥ vākyena pratipādyate yadi vijñānam vyāhatam bhavati sarvam nityam
476.10	vijñānam ca jāyate iti/ atha pratipattiḥ na kriyate kā tarhi
476.11	siddhiḥ yā pravartamānam karoti avaśyam kārakena apūrvam kim cit niṣpādyam
476.12	tad niśpatti^aḥ vyāghātaḥ kārakatvam vā nivartate na hi nityasya kim cit
476.13	kartavyam asti/ atha siddham nivartayati iti pakṣaḥ āśrīyate etasmin api
476.14	pakṣe nityam nivartate ca iti vyāghātaḥ/ atha tirobhavati iti tirobhāve
476.15	api ayam apūrva-utpattiḥ pūrva-vastu-vināśaḥ vā abhyupeyaḥ iti sarvathā na
476.16	vyāghātāt mucyase yad prāk-anupalabdha-rūpam paścāt upalabhyate tatra avaśyam apūrva-
476.17	utpattiḥ vā pūrva-vastu-vināśaḥ vā abhyupeyaḥ iti sarvathā na vyāghātāt
476.18	mucyate yad punar etad bhūta-lakṣaṇa-avarodhāt sarvam nityam iti na anyathā
476.19	tad-upapatti^aḥ bhūta-lakṣaṇa-yogaḥ anyathā sambhavati iti//
477.01	[4-1-32]na utpatti-tad-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ
477.02	utpatti-tad-kāraṇa-upalabdhi^aḥ kāraṇa-samāna-guṇa-utpattiḥ ca dṛṣyate kāraṇam
477.03	ca dṛśyate na ca etad ubhayam nitya-viṣayam na hi nityasya utpattiḥ na api
477.04	nityasya kāraṇam asti asti ca etad ubhayam tasmāt kāraṇa-samāna-guṇam
477.05	kāryam utpādayati iti kāraṇa-samāna-guṇam kāryam utpadyate iti kāraṇa-
477.06	samāna-guṇa-utpāde ca kārya-bhūta-lakṣaṇa-avarodhaḥ siddhyati iti/ vibhakta-nyāyam
477.07	ca etad vyakta-avyaktānām ca avivakṣā prāmāṇyāt iti etasmin sūtre/
477.08	avyāpakaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ pañca-bhūta-nitya-tvāt iti katham karma-ādīnām avyāpti^aḥ
477.09	karma-buddhi-śabda-ādyaḥ  pañca-bhūta-nitya-tvāt iti anena vyāptāḥ iti jñātṛ^aḥ
477.10	prayatna-darśanāt ca vyāghātaḥ utpatti-vināśa-prayuktasya jñātṛ^aḥ prayatnaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ
477.11	sarva-nitya-tve ca na yuktaḥ prasiddhaḥ ca avayavin^ḥ tad-dharman^ḥ utpatti-vināśa-dharman^ḥ
477.12	ca avayavin^ḥ pratipāditaḥ ataḥ ayuktam etad/ katham avyāpakaḥ anekāntaḥ pakṣasya
477.13	anta-dvaye avasthānāt pakṣaḥ hi sarvam hi nityam iti tad ca sarvam bhūta-
477.14	lakṣaṇam anyathā ca tad-pakṣa-anta-dvaye avasthānāt na sarvam bhūta-lakṣaṇa-
477.15	yuktam iti anekāntaḥ/ svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-vat mithyā-upalabdhiḥ iti cet atha
477.16	manuṣe yad idam utpadyate vinaśyate ca iti tad na tattvataḥ asti asati
477.17	etasamin abhimānaḥ bhavati utpannam vinaṣṭam iti yathā na svapne viṣayāḥ
477.18	santi atha ca viṣaya-abhimānaḥ asti iti na pramāṇa-abhāvāt yad idam
477.19	go-ādi ghaṭa-ādi-utpatti-vināśe yuktam upalabhyate etad na asti iti na asattva-pratipādakam
477.20	pramāṇam abhidhīyate na ca aprāmāṇikam śakyam pratipattum yathā
478.01	ca citta-vyatirekiṇaḥ viṣayāḥ tathā upariṣṭāt vakṣyāmaḥ/ yadi svapna-viṣaya-
478.02	abhimānaḥ atha bhūta-upalabdhiḥ api svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-vat prasajyate/
478.03	pṛthivī-ādi-abhāve sarva-vyavahāra-vilopaḥ iti cet atha manyase
478.04	pṛthivī-ādīni bhūtāni na sanit nanu pṛthivī-ādi-āśrayaḥ vyavahāraḥ
478.05	ucchidyeta tad-itaratra samānam/ yadi vyavahāra-uccheda-bhayāt bhūtāni pratipadyante
478.06	tataḥ eva utpatti-vināśau api pratipadyasva na hi utpatti-vināśau
478.07	antareṇa kaḥ cana vyavahāraḥ siddhyati iti nityānām dravyāṇām atīndriya-
478.08	tvāt aviṣaya-tvāt ca utpatti-vināśayoḥ yad nityam tad atīndriyam aviṣayaḥ
478.09	ca utpatti-vināśayoḥ tena svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-vat tad-abhimānaḥ iti ayuktam/
478.10	utpatti-vināśa-abhimānaḥ iti bruvatā utpatti-vināśayoḥ viṣayaḥ vaktavyaḥ/
478.11	na ca sarva-nitya-vādinām utpatti-vināśayoḥ viṣayaḥ asti viṣayam ca pratyācakṣāṇena
478.12	tad-anukārin^ḥ viparyaya-pratyayaḥ heyaḥ iti/  apare tu sarvam
478.13	nityam iti etad anyathā varṇayanti vyavasthite tasya upādānasya dharma-mātram
478.14	nivartate dharma-mātram āvirbhavati yad ca nivartate tad-nivṛttam api asti iti
478.15	yad ca āvirbhavati tad prāk api āvirbhāvāt asti//
478.16	[4-1-33]na vyavasthā-anupapatti^aḥ
478.17	na vyavasthā-anupapatti^aḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ yad ca nivartate tad nivṛttam
478.18	api asti iti bruvāṇaḥ bhavān praṣṭavyaḥ bhavati kim uktam bhavati
478.19	yadi brūṣe pūrvam upalabdham paścāt na upalabhyate iti/ atha sataḥ anupalabdhiḥ
478.20	kim-kṛtā yadi āvaraṇa-ādi-kṛtā tadā āvaraṇa-ādi upalabhyeta uktam ca atra yad pūrvam
479.01	anupalabdham paścāt upalabhyate tasya apūrva-viśeṣa-utpādaḥ pūrva-viśeṣa-pracyavaḥ
479.02	abhyupeyaḥ iti tad-abhyupagame ca vyāghātaḥ yad ca uktam yad ca āvirbhavati tad
479.03	prāk api āvirbhavāt asti tad na vyāghātāt āvirbhavati vidyate ca iti
479.04	vyāhatam/ atha āvirbhāvaḥ abhivyaktiḥ tathā api pūrva-doṣa-anivṛttiḥ abhivyaktiḥ
479.05	asatī bhavati iti/  ayam aparaḥ ekāntaḥ//
479.06	[4-1-34]sarvam pṛthak-bhāva-lakṣaṇa-pṛthak-tvāt
479.07	sarvam pṛthak-bhāva-lakṣaṇa-pṛthak-tvāt/ bhāvasya lakṣaṇam śabdaḥ lakṣyate
479.08	anena bhāvaḥ sarvaḥ bhāva-samākhyā-śabdaḥ aneka-viṣayaḥ yathā kumbhaḥ iti/
479.09	bhāva-samākhyā-śabdaḥ anekasmin avayava-samūhe vartate iti yathā kumbha-śabdaḥ/
479.10	evam anye api śabdāḥ iti/ asya prayogaḥ kumbha-śabdaḥ aneka-viṣayaḥ
479.11	eka-pada-tvāt senā-śabda-vat iti/ pada-śravaṇāt aneka-artha-avagati^aḥ yasmāt
479.12	pada-śruti^aḥ anekaḥ arthaḥ avagamyate yathā senā iti//
479.13	[4-1-35]na aneka-lakṣaṇaiḥ eka-bhāva-niṣpatti^aḥ
479.14	na aneka-lakṣaṇaiḥ eka-bhāva-niṣpatti^aḥ/ aneka-lakṣaṇaiḥ iti madhya-pada-
479.15	lopin^ḥ samāsaḥ aneka-vidha-lakṣaṇaiḥ iti/ gandha-ādibhiḥ ca guṇaiḥ budhna-ādibhiḥ
479.16	ca avayavaiḥ sambaddhaḥ ekaḥ bhāvaḥ utapadyate ataḥ śabdāt eka-avagati^i śeṣaḥ
479.17	anuṣaktaḥ avagamyate iti guṇa-vyatirekaḥ guṇin^ḥ iti avayava-vyatiriktaḥ ca avayavin^ḥ
479.18	iti vibhakta-nyāyam etad/ yad punar eka-pada-tvāt aneka-viṣayaḥ kumbha-ādi-śabdaḥ
479.19	iti tad na dṛṣṭānta-abhāvāt na hi kaḥ cit śabdaḥ aneka-viṣayaḥ asti
479.20	senā-ādi-śabdānām aneka-viṣaya-tvena asiddhi^aḥ yathā ca eka-viṣayaḥ senā-ādi-
480.01	śabdaḥ tathā uktam iti/ sarvasya pakṣa^karaṇāt dṛṣṭānta-abhāvaḥ ādhāra-ādheya-
480.02	anuṣaktānām rūpa-ādīnām avayavinaḥ ca grahaṇāt anyathā asiddhiḥ nañaḥ
480.03	prasajya-pratiṣedha-paryudāsa-viṣaya-tvena avasthānāt ca viruddhaḥ anekam iti ayam
480.04	nañaḥ prayogaḥ saḥ ca ayam prasajya-pratiṣedha-paryudāsa-viṣaya-tvena pratiṣedhāt
480.05	prakalpyate/ yadi tāvat prasajya-pratiṣedhaḥ ekaḥ na bhavati iti yad ekatra
480.06	pratiṣiddhyate tad anyatra bhavati evam ca vyāghātaḥ/ atha paryudāsa-pakṣaḥ
480.07	ekasmāt anyat anekam iti evam api ekam abhyupagatam bhavati na hi asati ekasmin
480.08	tasmāt anyat anekam siddhyati iti/  itaḥ ca
480.09	[4-1-36]lakṣaṇa-vyavasthānāt eva apratiṣedhaḥ
480.10	tad-lakṣaṇa-vyavasthānāt eva apratiṣedhaḥ/ na hi kaḥ cit ekaḥ bhāvaḥ iti
480.11	ayuktaḥ pakṣaḥ kasmāt lakṣaṇa-pravyavasthāpanāt na hi kumbhaḥ iti ukte
480.12	anekaḥ arthaḥ avagamyate/ katham na avagamyate eka-vacana-anta-tvāt eka-vacana-antaḥ
480.13	eṣaḥ śabdaḥ kumbhaḥ iti/ na bahuṣu eka-vacanam yuktam praiṣa-sampratipatti^oḥ
480.14	eka-viṣaya-tvāt kumbham ānaya iti praiṣaḥ eka-viṣayaḥ bhavati/ itaraḥ api
480.15	kumbha-śabdāt pratipadya ekam kumbham ānayati te ete praiṣa-sampratipattī eka-
480.16	viṣayei jñāpayataḥ kumbha-śabdena ekaḥ arthaḥ abhidhīyate iti/ yān tu rūpa-ādīn
480.17	avayavān pratipadyase saḥ na śabda-arthaḥ kim tu anuṣaṅgaḥ tad-nāntarīyaka-tvāt
480.18	iti samūha-bhedasya ca anavasthānāt yatra avasthānam tad ekam go-ādi-ghaṭa-
481.01	ādi-dravyāṇi samuditāni pratipadyamānena samūhaḥ abhypeyate saḥ ca ayam
481.02	samūhaḥ iyanti dravyāni etāni ghaṭa-ādi-bhāvena avasthitāni iti na
481.03	vyavatiṣṭhate asya bhedaḥ alpatara-tama-tvena yad etad param alpam tad abhedyam tataḥ
481.04	nivartate iti/ yataḥ ca ayam bhedaḥ nivartate tad ekam/ ekam ca pratyācakṣāṇena
481.05	ekam api pratyākhyeyam eka-samuccayāt anekasya/ atha manyase
481.06	yantam abhedyam paramāṇum pratipadyase saḥ rūpa-ādīnām samudāyaḥ iti catvāri
481.07	vā dravyāṇi pṛthivī-ādīni samudhitāni paramāṇuḥ iti/ etasmin
481.08	vai darśane ye rūpa-ādyaḥ  samuditāḥ te paramāṇuḥ iti yad paramāṇu^i rūpam
481.09	saḥ kasya samudāyaḥ iti vaktavyam/ evam śeṣa^su guṇa^su catvāri dravyāṇi
481.10	samuditāni paramāṇuh iti bruvāṇena catuṣṭayasya samuccaya-nimitta-tvāt
481.11	yad ekam pṛthivī-ādi saḥ kasya samudāyaḥ iti vaktavyam/ atha anantam
481.12	samudāyam pratipadyase aṣṭau dravyāṇi samuditāni paramāṇuḥ iti
481.13	śāstram vyāhatam bhavati/ kāma-iṣṭa-dravyakaḥ aṇuḥ śabdaḥ iti eka-anupapatti^i
481.14	na aneka-upapattiḥ iti etad heyam/ yaḥ ca ayam hetuḥ samūhe bhāva-śabda-prayogāt
481.15	iti ubhayataḥ vyāghātāt na kim cit katham ubhayataḥ na kaḥ cit ekaḥ
481.16	bhāvaḥ samūhe bhāva-śabda-prayogāt iti pratijñā-hetu^oḥ vyāghātaḥ/ yasmāt
481.17	eka-artha-anupapatti^i na samūhaḥ upapadyate iti samūham ca āśritya ekam pratyācakṣāṇena
481.18	samūhaḥ eva pratyākhyātaḥ bhavati iti saḥ ayam ubhayataḥ vyāghātāt
481.19	yad-kim-cana-vādaḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
482.01	atha ayam aparaḥ ekāntaḥ//
482.02	[4-1-37]sarva-bhāvaḥ bhāva^su itaretara-abhāva-siddhi^aḥ
482.03	sarva-bhāvaḥ bhāva^su itaretara-abhāva-siddhi^aḥ/ yāvat bhāva-jātam tad sarvam
482.04	abhāvaḥ kasmāt bhāva^su itaretara-abhāva-siddhi^aḥ/ asat-pratyaya-pratiṣedhābhyām
482.05	bhāva-śabda-sāmānādhikaraṇyāt sarvam abhāvaḥ anutpanna-pradhvasta-paṭa-vat sarvaḥ
482.06	bhāva-śabdaḥ asat-samānādhikaraṇaḥ pratiṣedha-samānādhikaraṇaḥ ca pradhvasta-paṭa-
482.07	vat yathā na asti paṭaḥ iti katham na asti kadā ca na asti iti
482.08	sthālī-ādi-ātmanā anutpadyamānaḥ dhvastaḥ ca iti/ saḥ ayam ghaṭa-śabdaḥ asat-pratyaya-
482.09	samānādhikaraṇaḥ ghaṭasya atyanta-sattvam pratipādayati/ tathā ca sarve
482.10	bhāvāḥ asat-pratyaya-samānādhikaraṇāḥ tasmāt sarvam abhāvaḥ iti/  pratijñā-
482.11	padayoḥ pratijñā-hetu^oḥ ca vyāghātaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyam abhinna-vibhaktimat-
482.12	tvam eka-vibhakti-uccāraṇam iti bhāva-abhyupagamāt tad-śabda-abhyupagamāt ca
482.13	vidhi^aḥ/ yad sarvam iti manyase tad abhāvaḥ iti na asati tad-pratyayena
482.14	bhavitum śakyam/ na ca bhāva-pratyayena abhāvaḥ iti ca bhāvaḥ pratiṣidhyate
482.15	na asati uttara-padārthe nañaḥ prayogaḥ iti prasajya-pratiṣedha-paryudāsābhyām
482.16	pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ yathā aneka-pade anitya-pade ca evam sarva-pade ca abhāva-pade ca
482.17	vyāghātaḥ ūhyaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ sarva-asattve vibhakti-svabhāva-avadhāraṇam
482.18	vaktavyam/ yadi ca sarvam abhāvaḥ vibhaktiḥ kim-svabhāvikā iti vaktavyam
482.19	etad/ asatyām ca vibhakti^i abhinna-vibhaktika-tvam sāmānādhikaraṇyam
482.20	iti vyāhatam/ asat-pratyaya-pratiṣedha-sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti brūṣe
483.01	sarvam abhāvaḥ iti ca vyāvartayasi na hi asat adhikaraṇam bhavati/ adhikaraṇam
483.02	nāma yad yatra vartate tad ca abhāve na asti iti sūtrena abhisambandhaḥ//
483.03	[4-1-38]na svabhāva-siddhi^aḥ bhāvānām
483.04	na svabhāva-siddhi^aḥ bhāvānām iti/ svena bhāvena bhāvāḥ bhavanti iti
483.05	sūtra-arthaḥ/ kim punar anena sūtrena kriyate pūrva-sūtra-virodhaḥ codyate/ katham
483.06	iti bhāva^su itaretara-abhāva-siddhi^aḥ iti abhāvaḥ abhāvatā abhidhīyate tathā
483.07	ca abhidadhānaḥ svabhāvataḥ abhāvān abhyupaiṣi viśeṣaṇa-vaiyarthyam ca bhāva^su
483.08	itaretara-abhāva-siddhi^aḥ iti sarva-asattve hi evam eva vaktavyam na santi bhāvāḥ
483.09	na punar evam vaktum yuktam itarat itarat na bhavati iti itarat iti
483.10	ca ayam vidhāna-śabdaḥ na ca sarvasya abhāve vidhīyamānam vastu sambhavati
483.11	yad itara-śabda-vācyam syāt iti/ itara-vācya-tām ca abhyupagamya itarat itaratra
483.12	pratiṣidhyate iti/ svabhāva-siddhiḥ abhyupagatā bhavati kaḥ ca svaḥ bhāvaḥ
483.13	bhāvānām dravya-ādīnām sat-ādi sāmānyam kriyāvat-ādiḥ viśeṣaḥ sparśa-
483.14	paryantāḥ pṛthivī^āḥ pratyekam anantaḥ bhedaḥ sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāya-ādīnām
483.15	sāmānyaḥ vaiśeṣikaḥ ca antargaṇikaḥ anantaḥ bhedaḥ iti/ saḥ ayam abhāvasya
483.16	nirupākhya-tvāt sampratyāyakaḥ svabhāva-bhedah na syāt asti tu asau
483.17	tasmāt sarvam abhāvaḥ iti/ atha vā svabhāva-siddhi^aḥ iti go^ḥ iti prasajyamāne
483.18	na abhāvaḥ pratayate kim tu jāti-viśiṣṭam dravyam yadi ca sarvam abhāvah
483.19	syāt go^ḥ iti prayukte abhāvaḥ gamyeta tasmāt na sarvam abhāvaḥ iti/
484.01	atha vā svabhāva-siddhi^aḥ iti asan go^ḥ aśva-ātmanā anaśvaḥ go^ḥ ago^ḥ go^ḥ iti
484.02	kasmāt na ucyate iti avacanāt go^ḥ iti ago^ḥ go^ḥ iti ukte vyāhatam
484.03	bhavati yadi vyāghāta-bhayāt ago^ḥ go^ḥ iti na ucyate hanta tarhi siddhaḥ
484.04	abhāvaḥ/ katham tarhi ayam pratiṣedhah ago^ḥ aśvaḥ iti/ vyatireka-pratiṣedhe
484.05	bhāvena asat-pratyayasya sāmānādhikaraṇyam iti yathā na santi kuṇḍe
484.06	vadarāṇi iti kuṇḍa-vadarayoḥ sambandhaḥ pratiṣiddhyate na kuṇḍa-vadaram abhāvaḥ//4-1-38]
484.07	[4-1-39]na svabhāva-siddhiḥ āpekṣika-tvāt
484.08	na svabhāva-siddhiḥ āpekṣika-tvāt/ na svabhāva-siddhiḥ āpekṣikī yataḥ
484.09	na svena ātmanā kim cit siddham iti/ yathā dīrgha-hrasvei para-aparei ca iti//
484.10	[4-1-40]vyāhata-tvāt ayuktam
484.11	vyāhata-tvāt ayuktam/ yadi hrasva-apekṣā-kṛtam dīrgham hrasvam āpekṣikam
484.12	atha dīrgha-apekṣā-kṛtam hrasvam dīrgham anāpekṣikam paraspara-apekṣayoḥ ca yad-
484.13	apekṣayā itarat jāyate tad-anāpekṣikam iti ubhayoḥ abhāvaḥ ekasya abhāvāt
484.14	iti/ apekṣāyām anapekṣāyām ca dravyayoḥ na bhedaḥ yavatī eva dravye
484.15	apekṣamāṇe tāvatī eva anapekṣamāṇe api na hi anyataratra bhedaḥ/
484.16	atiśaya-grahaṇam katham yadi dravyayoḥ dvayoḥ abhedaḥ apekṣamāṇayoḥ anapekṣamāṇayoḥ
484.17	dīrgha-hrasva-pratyayaḥ tarhi na prāpnoti nana prāpnoti dvayoḥ grahaṇe
484.18	atiśayasya grahaṇāt dvayoḥ tāvat dravyayoḥ pratyartha-niyatei dvei hi eva buddhī
484.19	bhavataḥ ataḥ ekasmin vidyamānam atiśayam gṛhṇāti ekasmin vidyamānam
484.20	ca hrasvatvam saḥ ayam ubhaya-darśane pratisandhatte pratisandhāna-pratyaya-jā tu asya
485.01	buddhiḥ utpadyate asmāt dīrgham adas asmāt adas hrasvam iti na punar vastunaḥ utpāda-
485.02	anutpādau iti/ svabhāva-siddhi^i ca asatyām apekṣā na prāpnoti/ yadi
485.03	svabhāva-siddham antareṇa apekṣā-bhedāt dīrgha-ādi-bhedāḥ bhavanti sarvam apekṣya sarvasya
485.04	sarvatra tathā-bhūtaḥ pratyayaḥ syāt na ca rūpa-rasa-gandha-sparśa-pratyaya^su apekṣā-sāmarthyam
485.05	asti na ca parimaṇḍalau paramāṇū upalabhamānaḥ īśvarah paramāṇum
485.06	apekṣya paramāṇu-antare dīrgha-hrasva-ādi-pratyayān karoti ataḥ na apekṣā-
485.07	kṛṭāḥ pratyayāḥ iti/ sarvam abhāvaḥ iti sarvathā ca ayam vādaḥ vyāhataḥ/
485.08	kutaḥ vyāghātaḥ ādi^i tāvat pramāṇa-utpatti-anutpattī sarvam abhāvaḥ
485.09	iti bruvāṇaḥ pramāṇam paryanuyojyaḥ/ yadi brūte vyāhatam bhavati atha
485.10	na abhidhatte arthaḥ asya na sidhyati pramāṇa-abhāvāt/ sarvam abhāvaḥ iti vākyam
485.11	tasya yadi abhidheyam artham pratipadyate pūrvavat vyāghātaḥ atha na pratipadyate
485.12	varṇa-uccāraṇa-mātram anarthakam iti/ sarvam abhāvaḥ iti ca vākyasya
485.13	pratipādayitṛ^am pratipattṛ^am ca yadi pratipadyate pūrvavat vyāghātaḥ
485.14	sarvam abhāvaḥ iti sarvam bhāvaḥ iti ca vākyei yadi anayoḥ artha-bhedam pratipadyate
485.15	vyāhatam bhavati atha na pratipadyate viśeṣa-upādānam vyartham saḥ ayam
485.16	sarva-abhāva-vādaḥ yāvat yāvat vicāryate tāvat tāvat upapattim na sahate iti/
485.17	atha ime saṃkhyā-ekānta-vādāḥ/ sarvam ekam sat-aviśeṣāt iti evam ādi bhāṣye/
485.18	teṣām pratiṣedha-dvārakam sūtram//
485.19	[4-1-41]saṃkhyā-ekānta-asiddhiḥ kāraṇa-anupapatti-upapattibhyām
486.01	saṃkhyā-ekānta-asiddhiḥ kāraṇa-anupapatti-upapattibhyām/ saṃkhyā-ekāntānām
486.02	asiddhiḥ kāraṇasya pratijñā-artha-vyatirekāt sarvam ekam iti pratijñām
486.03	kṛtvā yadi pratijñeya-vyatiriktam sādhanam bravīti ekāntaḥ na siddhyati
486.04	tad ca sādhanam pratijñāyāḥ ca arthaḥ iti dvaitam evam dvaita-ādiṣu api iti/ atha
486.05	pratijñeya-vyatiriktam sādhanam na asti evam api ekāntaḥ asya vā na siddhyati
486.06	sādhana-abhāvāt na sādhyam sādhanam iti//
486.07	[4-1-42]na kāraṇa-avayava-bhāvāt
486.08	na kāraṇa-avayava-bhāvāt/ kāraṇasya sādhya-avayava-tvāt iti ayam
486.09	sūtra-arthaḥ/ sādhya-avayava-sādhanam evam ca sati sādhya-vyatiriktam sādhanam
486.10	na uktam bhavati na ca asādhanā siddhiḥ iti//
486.11	[4-1-43]niravayava-tvāt ahetuḥ
486.12	niravayava-tvāt ahetuḥ/ sādhya-avayavaḥ eva kāraṇam iti ayam ahetuḥ
486.13	niravayava-tvāt pratijñā-arthasya sarvam ekam iti etasmin pratijñā-arthe na
486.14	kim cit apavṛjyate anapavargena sarvam pakṣa^kṛtam iti sarvasya niravayavasya
486.15	pakṣa^karaṇāt na hetuḥ asti na ca sādhyam hetuḥ bhavitum arhati iti/ ātmani
486.16	ca kriyā viruddhyate yad tad-pratipādyam tad eva pratipādakam iti na yuktam na
486.17	karman karaṇam bhavitum arhati iti/ te khalu ime saṃkhyā-ekāntāḥ yadi viśeṣa-
486.18	kāritasya artha-bhedasya pratyākhyānena vartante mithyā-vādāḥ bhavanti pratyakṣa-
486.19	anumāna-āgama-virodhi-tvāt/ pratyakṣaḥ ayam go-ādi-ghaṭa-ādi-bhedaḥ anumānataḥ
486.20	api gamyate anyat anyena anumīyate āgamataḥ api gamyate pratipannaḥ
487.01	pratipādayitṛ^ḥ apratipannaḥ pratipādyaḥ iti/ atha abhyanujñānena
487.02	vartante tathā api ekāntatvam jahati/ atha sāmānya-kāritaḥ abhedaḥ viśeṣa-
487.03	kāritaḥ ca bhedaḥ iti pratipadyate tathā api na kim cit bādhyate/ na ca
487.04	bhedam antareṇa sāmānyam labdha-avakāśam iti sāmānyam pratipadyamānena
487.05	bhedaḥ abhyupagantavyaḥ bhedam ca pratyācakṣāṇena sāmānyam api pratyākhyeyam
487.06	viśeṣa-anādhārasya sāmānyasya aviṣaya-tvāt te khalu ime saṃkhyā-ekāntāḥ
487.07	tattva-jñāna-prativivekāya parīkṣitāḥ iti//
487.08	pretyabhāva-anantaram phalam uddiṣṭam tasmin/
487.09	[4-1-44]sadyas kāla-antare ca phala-niṣpatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ
487.10	sadyas kāla-antare ca phala-niṣpatti^aḥ saṃśayaḥ/ kā cit kriyā
487.11	sadyas-phalā yathā pacati dogdhīti tasyāḥ sadyas phala-modana-payasī
487.12	kriyā-anantaram bhavati bhavataḥ iti/ kā cit kāla-antara-phalā yathā kṛṣati
487.13	vapati iti tasyāḥ kāla-antare phalam na kriyā-anantaram sasya adhigamaḥ iti/
487.14	asti ca iyam kriyā agnihotram juhuyāt iti tasyāḥ phale sandehaḥ
487.15	kim iyam sadyas-tāpa-ādi-phalā uta kāla-antara-phalā//
487.16	[(4-1-45)]na sadyas kāla-antara-upabhogya-tvāt
487.17	na sadyas kāla-antara-upabhogya-tvāt/ svargaḥ phalam śrūyate coditayā
487.18	ca kriyayā na anarthikayā śakyam bhavitum na ca ayam tāpa-ādi-kāmaḥ
488.01	agnihotram juhoti tena avagamyate tām havana-ādi-kriyām apekṣamāṇāt
488.02	ātma-manas-sambandhāt viśuddhena abhisandhinā anugṛhītāt ātmani dharmaḥ
488.03	utupadyate iti/ saḥ ca kāla-antare aprtibaddhyamāna-śaktiḥ deśa-ādi-anugṛhītaḥ
488.04	phalam dadāti tad ca patite tasmin dehe bhavati deha-antara-
488.05	āpannasya ataḥ sadyaḥ iti/
488.06	[4-1-45(46)]kāla-antarena aniṣpattiḥ hetu-vināśāt
488.07	kāla-antarena aniṣpattiḥ hetu-vināśāt/ vyāvṛtte havane vyāvṛtte ca
488.08	pūrva-śarīre deha-antare phalam śrūyate tad na vinaṣṭāt kāraṇāt yuktam iti/
488.09	[4-1-46(47)]prāk niṣpatti^aḥ vṛkṣa-phala-vat tad syāt
488.10	prāk niṣpatti^aḥ vṛkṣa-phala-vat tad syāt/ na brūmaḥ vinaṣṭāt kāraṇāt
488.11	kāryam utpadyate api tu agnihotra-havana-ādi-lakṣaṇayā kriyayā
488.12	yathā uktena vidhinā dharmaḥ iti uktam tasmāt phalam iti yathā
488.13	phala-arthinā vṛkṣasya mūla-seka-ādi-karman kriyate na ca mūla-sekāt vinaṣṭāt
488.14	phalena yuktam bhavitum api tu mūla-seka-ādi-karma-kṛtam bhoktṛ^aḥ
488.15	karma-apekṣam pṛthivī-ādi-dhātum anugṛhṇāti saḥ upagṛhītaḥ āntarena
488.16	tejasā pacyamānaḥ rasa-dravyam abhinirvartayati tad rasa-dravyam vṛkṣam āphalāt
488.17	sadyas upasarpati tasmāt parṇa-ādi-phalam nirvartate/ evam ca sati vinaṣṭāt
488.18	mūla-sekāt phala-nirvṛttiḥ na ca mūla-seka-ādi-vaiyarthyam iti/
488.19	tad idam prāk-niṣpatti^aḥ//
489.01	[4-1-47(48)]na asat na sat na sat asat sat-asatoḥ vaidharmyāt
489.02	na asat na sat na sat asat sat-asatoḥ vaidharmyāt/ prāk niṣpatti^aḥ utpatti-dharmakam
489.03	na asat upādāna-niyamāt asattva-samāne upādāna-niyamaḥ na
489.04	prāpnoti sat api na bhavati vidyamānasya utpattiḥ vyāhatā iti asti
489.05	ca utpadyate ca iti vyāhatam/ na sat asat sat-asatoḥ vaidharmyāt sat iti
489.06	artha-abhyanujñā asat iti tasya pratiṣedhaḥ abhyanujñā-pratiṣedhayoḥ
489.07	sāmānādhikaraṇyam anupapannam/ tasmāt na sat asat api na apy ubhaya-viparītam
489.08	kāryam yujyate tasya svarūpa-anirdeśāt iti/ na hi tasya svarūpam śakyam
489.09	nirdeṣṭum iti/  prāk utpatti^aḥ utpatti-dharmakam asat iti addhā/ satyam iti
489.10	kasmāt//
489.11	[4-1-48(49)]utpāda-vyaya-darśanāt
489.12	utpāda-vyaya-darśanāt/ vastunaḥ prāk-anupalabdhi-rūpasya paścāt-upalabdhiḥ
489.13	yā saḥ asat utpāde sati yujyate vināśaḥ ca upalabdha-rūpasya punar anupalabdhiḥ
489.14	iti/ na ca etad sarva-nitya-vādinaḥ na hi sat-pakṣaḥ utpādaḥ
489.15	na ca vināśaḥ iti utpāda-vināśau ca pratyācakṣāṇena lokaḥ heyaḥ/
489.16	atha lokaḥ ayam pravartamānaḥ kim-artham pravartate nanu ca ayam pravartate
489.17	idam āpsyāmi idam hāsyāmiti satyam evam pravartate na punar sat-kārya-
490.01	vādinaḥ kim cit heyam upādeyam vā vidyate pratijñā-ādīnām ca avayavānām
490.02	upādānam vyartham utpāda-vyaya-pratyākhyānāt na kim cit jāyate na
490.03	kim cit vinaśyati iti avayavānām upādānam vyartham yadi sat-kārya-
490.04	pratipatti-artham pratipatti^aḥ vidyamāna-tvāt vyartham upādānam na hi yadi asya
490.05	loke bhavati saḥ tad-upādānam upādatte iti/ atha ajñāna-tirobhāva-artham
490.06	upādānamiti etad api tādṛś eva/ na hi anupajāta-viśeṣasya ajñāna-
490.07	nivṛttiḥ yuktā iti/ atha abhivyakti-artham upādānam iti kā iyam abhivyaktiḥ nāma
