490.08	yā upādānena kriyate yadi kāryam vyāhatam/ atha kārya-dharmaḥ
490.09	tathā api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ/ atha upalabdhiḥ kārya-viṣaya-abhivyaktiḥ sā
490.10	kriyate iti na mucyate vyāghātāt/ atha kāraṇa-ātmanā avasthitasya
490.11	kārya-ātmanā avasthānam abhivyaktiḥ evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ kārya-ātmanā
490.12	avasthānam asat bhavati/ atha kāraṇasya saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ abhivyaktiḥ
490.13	saṃsthāna-viśeṣaḥ abhūtvā bhavati iti vyāghātaḥ/ atha asti vyartham upādānam
490.14	atha kāraṇasya svalakṣaṇa-puṣṭiḥ abhivyaktiḥ/ na apūrva-abhyāgama-abhāve
490.15	svalakṣaṇa-puṣṭi-śabda-artham paśyāmaḥ iti vyartham upādānam svalakṣaṇa-puṣṭiḥ ca
490.16	pūrvam abhūtvā paścāt bhavati iti vyāghātāt na mucyase/ evam yena yena
490.17	kalpanā-abhivyaktiḥ abhidhīyate tena tena sat-kāryam bādhate iti/  yat punar
490.18	etad na asat upādāna-niyamāt//
490.19	[4-1-49(50)]buddi-siddham tu tad asat
490.20	buddi-siddham tu tad asat/ na ayam sattvāt upādāna-niyamaḥ api tu sāmarthyāt
491.01	idam anena vākyam nirvartayitum na idam anena iti evam buddhi-siddham kāryam
491.02	kṛtvā yad yasya utpatti^e samartham tad tasya upādīyate na sarvam sarvasya
491.03	na sarvasmāt sarvam utpadyamānam dṛṣṭam iti/ atha utpatti-niyamena kārya-
491.04	niyamena kārya-kāraṇa-niyamam pratipadyate tena api kārya-kāraṇa-śabdayoḥ
491.05	arthaḥ vācyaḥ kim uktam bhavam kāraṇam iti kim uktam ca bhavati kāryam
491.06	iti/ nanu kartoti kāraṇam kriyate iti kāryam/ yadi abhivyaktiḥ
491.07	pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ atha vidyamāna^su api karoti-arthaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti manyase
491.08	yathā keśān kuru pṛṣṭham kuru iti na tad-vidyamānānām hi vacana-aviśeṣaḥ
491.09	keśānām avidyamānaḥ kriyate paścāt pṛṣṭasya mala-apagamaḥ avidyamānaḥ
491.10	iti na vidyamānam kāraṇa-artham jātu api paśyāmaḥ iti tasmāt asat etad/
491.11	yadi hi asat kriyate iti kharasya viṣāṇam kim na kriyate iti/ kaḥ
491.12	evam āha na kriyate iti yad tad kharasya viṣāṇam tad kriyate iti/
491.13	atha kharaḥ kasmāt viṣāṇasya kāraṇam na bhavati iti etad na jānīmaḥ
491.14	kasmāt na bhavati iti na punar kharena viṣāṇam kriyamāṇam dṛṣṭam iti
491.15	ataḥ avasīyate/ etena śaśa-viṣāṇam vyākhyātam/ na tad-asattvāt na
491.16	kriyate kim tu kāraṇa-abhāvāt iti/ na ca asattvam utpatti^i hetuḥ api
491.17	tu sataḥ anutpatti^aḥ asat utpadyate iti asattvāt khara-viṣāṇam na utpadyate
491.18	iti bruvāṇaḥ sva-siddhāntam bādhate na hi bhavat-pakṣe khara-viṣāṇam na asti
491.19	sattve ca asattve ca samāne yasya kāraṇam asti tad utpadyate iti sat-kārya-
491.20	vādinaḥ ca evam sarva-loka-vyavahāraḥ ucchidyate iti uktam na apūrvam jāyate na
492.01	pūrvam vinaśyati iti/ atha asat-kārye kim pramāṇam na sattve na ca asattve
492.02	anumānam asti dharmiṇi avipratipatti^aḥ kva tarhi vivādaḥ yad ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannam
492.03	tasya dharma^su tad ubhaya-pakṣa-sampratipannāḥ tantu^āḥ tān prati pakṣāḥ
492.04	vibhajyante/ eke tāvat bruvate tantu-mātram paṭaḥ iti/ apare tu tantu^āḥ
492.05	saṃsthāna-viśeṣena avasthitāḥ iti/ apare tu tantu^āḥ eva kārya-ātmanā avatiṣṭhante
492.06	iti/ apareṣām dharma-adharma-āvirbhāva-tirobhāvau iti/ apare
492.07	tu śakti-viśiṣṭāḥ tantu^āḥ paṭaḥ iti āhuḥ/ tad evam vibhakta^su pakṣa^su tantu-
492.08	mātra^su tad-vyatirekaḥ sādhayitavyaḥ saḥ ca upapāditaḥ iti/ ye api
492.09	saṃsthāna-viśeṣena avasthitān tantūn paṭaḥ iti varṇayanti tān prati sādhanam
492.10	prāk upalabdhi-kālāt saṃsthāna-viśeṣa-śūnyāḥ tantu^āḥ tad-kāraṇa-tvāt turī-
492.11	ādi-vat iti/ etena kārya-ātmanā avatiṣṭhante iti vyākhyātam/ tathā
492.12	dharma-antara-āvirbhāva-tirobhāvau iti ataḥ eva śakti-viśeṣa-avasthitāḥ tantu^āḥ
492.13	iti bruvāṇaḥ kim cit api na bādhate iti tān prati na sādhanam ucyate
492.14	iti/ yaḥ eva arthaḥ buddi-siddhaḥ tad asat iti saḥ eva arthaḥ śakti-viśeṣā-avasthitāḥ
492.15	tantu^āḥ iti sarva^su ca eva pakṣa^su na apūrva-utpādam antareṇa paṭa-buddhiḥ
492.16	sambhavati iti vyāghātaḥ anivāryaḥ/ vidyamāna-abhivyakti-arthāḥ tantu^āḥ tad-arthinā
492.17	niyamena upādānāt khanitra-ādi-vat vidyamānasya abhivyaktiḥ asati iti
492.18	abhivyakti-vat kārye api prasaṅgaḥ sarvam ca ukta-uttaram etad/ yad punar etad
493.01	upādāna-niyamāt iti upādānam kāraṇam na ca sati kāraṇa-artham
493.02	paśyāmaḥ iti pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ yad api khanitra-ādi-vat iti na ca khanitra-
493.03	ādi-udaka-artham upādīyate api tu āvaraṇa-vigama-artham āvaraṇa-vigamaḥ ca
493.04	vibhāgaḥ saḥ ca abhūtvā bhavati iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ atha vibhāgam na
493.05	pratipadyeta saḥ api apratipanna-vibhāgaḥ vibhāga-pratyaya-utpatti-kāraṇam paryanuyojyaḥ/
493.06	atha aprāpti-mātrāt vibhāga-pratyayam pratipadyate sā api aprāptiḥ kim
493.07	prāpti^aḥ abhāvaḥ uta prāpti^aḥ anyā iti kim ca ataḥ prāpti^aḥ abhāvaḥ aprāptiḥ
493.08	sā aprāptiḥ kutaḥ bhavati iti vaktavyam/ yadi prāpti-virodha-nimittam
493.09	tad vibhāgaḥ iti na abhāvaḥ vibhāgasya yad yatra pratiṣidhyate tad anyatra
493.10	bhavati iti uktam/ aprāptiḥ ca dvayī saṃśleṣaḥ saṃśliṣṭānām vibhāgaḥ ca/ tatra
493.11	yaḥ saṃśliṣṭānām vibhāgaḥ saḥ vibhāgaḥ iti tam ca khanitra-ādīni
493.12	kurvanti/ atha prāpti^aḥ anyā aprāptiḥ sā api dvayī tatra api pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ/
493.13	atha bhinna-deśa-utpattim vibhāgam pratipadyeta bhinna-deśe ime utpanne
493.14	iti viśeṣaṇa-upādānāt na bhinna-deśa-utpattiḥ vibhāgaḥ iti/ utpanne
493.15	utpannam iti bhinna-deśa-tayā bhinnam iti vibhāgāt vibhaktam iti
493.16	tāni etāni bheda-pratyaya-nimittāni pratyaya-bheda-anumeyāni na hi ime
493.17	paryāya-śabdāḥ bhinnam utpannam vibhaktam ca iti/ bhinna-deśa-utpattti^aḥ ca pratiṣiddha-
493.18	tvāt na bhinna-deśa-utpattiḥ vibhāgaḥ iti/ kṣaṇika-tvāt vibhāgasya
493.19	vibhāga-pratyaya-utpattiḥ na yuktā iti cet na sāmānya-abhivyakti-saṃyoga-
493.20	vināśābhyām eva pratyaya-utpatti-kāla-anubhavāt/ pūrvam tāvat sva-kāraṇāt
494.01	utpannaḥ vibhāgaḥ sāmānyam vyanakti sāmānya-abhivyakti-uttara-kālam vibhāga-
494.02	pratyayam karoti saṃyogam ca vināśayati saṃyoga-vināśa-uttara-kālam saṃyogāt
494.03	dvi-ātmakāt vinaśyati iti/ tad evam aneka-kṣaṇa-anubhavāt utpanna-mātraḥ
494.04	vibhāgaḥ dhvaṃsate iti asiddham/ tad evam vyavasthitam etad asat utpadyate iti//4-1-49(50)]
494.05	[4-1-50(51)]āśraya-vyatirekāt vṛkṣa-phala-utpatti-vat iti ahetuḥ
494.06	āśraya-vyatirekāt vṛkṣa-phala-utpatti-vat iti ahetuḥ/ yad ca mūla-seka-ādi
494.07	karman yad ca parṇa-ādi-phalam tad ubhayam vṛkṣa-āśrayam karman tu iha karma-phalam ca anyatra
494.08	iti āśraya-vyatirekāt vṛkṣa-phala-utpatti-vat iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ//
494.09	[4-1-51(52)]prīti^aḥ ātma-āśraya-tvāt apratiṣedhaḥ
494.10	prīti^aḥ ātma-āśraya-tvāt apratiṣedhaḥ iti/ āśraya-vyatirekah asiddhaḥ iti
494.11	sūtra-arthaḥ/ yatra eva karman tatra eva karma-phalam iti//
494.12	[4-1-52(53)]na putra-strī-paśu-paricchada-hiraṇya-anna-ādi-phala-nirdeśāt
494.14	na putra-strī-paśu-paricchada-hiraṇya-anna-ādi-phala-nirdeśāt/ putra-ādi
494.15	phala-tvena nirdiśyate na putra-ādi-ātmani vartate iti//
494.16	[4-1-53(54)]tad-sambandhāt phala-niṣpatti^aḥ teṣu phala-vat upacāraḥ
494.17	tad-sambandhāt phala-niṣpatti^aḥ teṣu phala-vat upacāraḥ/ putra-ādi-sambandhāt
494.18	phalam prītiḥ bhavati tad-kāraṇa-tvāt putra-ādi phala-śabdena abhidhīyate
495.01	yathā anna-sādhanāḥ prāṇāḥ annam vai prāṇāḥ iti/ upacāraḥ ca prāṇa-
495.02	abhāvāt loka-prayukta-vākya-anuvidhānena kriyate iti/
495.03	phala-anantaram duḥkham uddiṣṭam lakṣitam ca bādhanā-lakṣaṇam duḥkham iti/
495.04	tad kim sarva-lokasya sukhasya pratyākhyānam āhosvit anyaḥ kalpaḥ
495.05	iti/ anyaḥ iti āha katham na vai sarva-loka-sākṣikam sukham śakyam pratyākhyātum/
495.06	ayam tu janma-maraṇa-prabandha-anubhava-nimittāt duḥkhāt nirvaṇṇasya
495.07	duḥkha-bhāvana-upadeśaḥ katham nāma ayam sarvam duḥkham iti bhāvayet bhāvayan ca
495.08	virajyeta viraktaḥ mucyeta iti/ katham punar ayam sarvam duḥkham iti
495.09	bhāvayati sarve sattva-nikāyāḥ sarvaḥ punarbhavaḥ sarvam utpatti-sthānam
495.10	duḥkha-sāhacaryāt duḥkham iti uktam ṛṣibhiḥ duḥkha-bhāvanam upadiśyate tatra
495.11	ca ahetuḥ upādīyate//
495.12	[4-1-54(55)]vividha-bādhanā-yogāt duḥkham eva janma-utpattiḥ
495.13	vividha-bādhanā-yogāt duḥkham eva janma-utpattiḥ iti/ tatra jāyate
495.14	iti janman śarīra-indriya-buddhyaḥ  vyapadiśyante śarīra-ādīnām prādurbhave
495.15	utpattiḥ janman utpattiḥ janma-utpattiḥ vividhā ca bādhanā hīnā
495.16	madhya-utkṛṣṭā ca iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ anena abhiprāyena duḥkha-upadeśaḥ na
495.17	sukham pratyākhyātam//
495.18	[4-1-55(56)]na sukhasya api antarāla-niṣpatti^aḥ
495.19	na sukhasya api antarāla-niṣpatti^aḥ/ yasmāt bādhanā-antarāle sukham utpadyamānam
495.20	pratyakṣa-dṛṣṭam iti/  itaḥ ca
496.01	[4-1-56(57)]bādhanā-nivṛtti^aḥ vedayataḥ paryeṣaṇa-doṣāt apratiṣedhaḥ
496.02	bādhanā-nivṛtti^aḥ vedayataḥ paryeṣaṇa-doṣāt apratiṣedhaḥ/ sukhasya duḥkha-
496.03	uddeśena iti prakaraṇāt yasmāt ayam vedayan idam me sukha-sādhanam idam me
496.04	duḥkha-sādhanamiti sukha-sādhanam āptum duḥkha-sādhanam hātum prayatate sukha-
496.05	sādhanānām prāpti^e ca asya yatamānasya aneka-vidhāḥ -vidhaḥ tāpaḥ anupaplavate tataḥ
496.06	tāpa-anubhavāt sarvam duḥkham iti ucyate na sukhasya āvirbhāvāt iti/
496.07	ayam eva ca arthaḥ muninā ślokena varṇitaḥ//
496.08	kāmam kāmayamānasya yadā kāmaḥ samṛdhyati/
496.09	atha enam aparaḥ kāmaḥ kṣipram eva prabādhate// iti evam ādi/
496.10	[4-1-57(58)]duḥkha-vikalpe sukha-abhimānāt ca
496.11	duḥkha-vikalpe sukha-abhimānāt ca/ yaḥ ca sukha-artham yatamānasya duḥkha-
496.12	vikalpaḥ bhavati tam sukha-aṅga-bhāvāt sukham iti abhimanyamānaḥ punar tad
496.13	upādatte punar punar nivartate iti na saṃsāram ativartate tasyāḥ sukha-saṃjñāyāḥ
496.14	pratipakṣaḥ upadeśaḥ ayam kriyate iti sarvam duḥkham iti/ yadi evam
496.15	kasmāt duḥkham janman iti na ucyate saḥ ayam evam vaktavye yad āha duḥkham eva iti
496.16	tena sukha-abhāvam jñāpayati yad api tad-antarāle sukham upajāyate tad api
497.01	duḥkham eva duḥkha-anuṣaṅgāt na abhāvāt iti janma-vinigraha-arthī yaḥ vai
497.02	khalu ayam eva śabdaḥ janmanaḥ anena vinigraham śāsti sarvam duḥkham iti
497.03	bhāvayan duḥkha-sādhanāni na upādatte anupādadānaḥ vimucyate iti/
497.04	duḥkha-uddeśa-anantaram apavargaḥ sampratyākhyāyate//
497.05	[4-1-58(59)]ṛṇa-klṣśa-pravṛtti-anubandhāt apavarga-abhāvaḥ
497.06	ṛṇa-klṣśa-pravṛtti-anubandhāt apavarga-abhāvaḥ iti anena sūtrena ṛṇa-anubandhāt
497.07	na asti apavargaḥ/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ anubandhaḥ sadā karaṇīyatā na ṛṇa-anubandhāt
497.08	kadā cit mucyate janma-prabhṛti yāvat prāyaṇam iti kleśa-anubandhāt
497.09	apavargaḥ na asti/ kleśa-anubaddhaḥ eva ayam jāyate klṣśa-anubaddhaḥ eva ayam
497.10	mriyate iti/ pravṛtti-anupabandhāt apavargaḥ na asti na kadā cit ayam na karman
497.11	karoti  yad tāvat ṛṇa-anubandhāt iti//
497.12	[4-1-59(60)]pradhāna-śabdena anupapatti^aḥ guṇa-śabdena anuvādaḥ nindā-
497.13	praśaṃsa-upapatti^aḥ
497.14	pradhāna-śabdena anupapatti^aḥ guṇa-śabdena anuvādaḥ nindā-praśaṃsa-upapatti^aḥ iti/
497.15	na ayam pradhāna-śabdaḥ pratyādeya-dāna-pratideya-grahaṇa-asambhavāt/ yatra khalu
497.16	ekaḥ pratyādeyam dadāti itaraḥ pratideyam gṛhṇāt tatra ayam
497.17	rṇa-śabdaḥ pradhānam bhavati anyatra bhāktaḥ iti tasmāt guṇa-śabdena ayam
498.01	anuvādaḥ iti yathā agniḥ māṇavakaḥ iti tatra agni-śabdaḥ dahana-ādi-samarthe
498.02	tejas-viśeṣe vartate saḥ tatra vartamānaḥ tad-sāmānyāt atathā-bhūte māṇavake
498.03	prayujyate agniḥ māṇavakaḥ iti na punar agni-śabdaḥ māṇavaka-samānādhikaraṇaḥ
498.04	dahana-ādi-samartham māṇavakam pratipādyati api tu ye agni^aḥ
498.05	piṅgala-pāṭavatva-ādyaḥ  dharmāḥ tad-sambandhinam māṇavakam pratipādayati
498.06	tad-dvārakaḥ ca prayujyamānaḥ ayam śabdaḥ guṇa-upakaraṇa-tvāt gauṇaḥ bhavati/
498.07	tathā ca ṛṇa-śabdaḥ prayukta-upamam ca idam vākyam ṛṇavān jāyate iti
498.08	upamā atra luptā draṣṭavyā ṛṇavān jāyate iti/ kaḥ upamāna-arthaḥ
498.09	asvātantryam ṛṇavān yathā asvatantraḥ evam ayam jāyamānaḥ karmasu
498.10	asvatantraḥ vartate iti/ jāyamānaḥ iti ca ayam guṇa-śabdaḥ eva na
498.11	mukhyaḥ sambhavati na hi mātṛtaḥ kumāre jāyamāne lohita-pāṇi-
498.12	pāda-āsye adhyayana-ādi-karma-upapattiḥ iti ataḥ gṛha-sthe jāyamāne iti/
498.13	kim punar gṛha-sthasya jāyamānena sāmānya-kriyā-sambandhaḥ agnihotra-
498.14	ādi-havana-kriyā-sambandha-sāmānyāt gṛha-sthaḥ jāyate iti/ yathā
498.15	mātṛtaḥ kumārakaḥ jāyamānaḥ śarīra-ādibhiḥ sambaddhyate evam agnihotra-
498.16	havana-ādi-kriyā-sambandha-sāmānyena jāyate iti/ kasmāt śaktasya
498.17	arthinaḥ karmabhiḥ adhikārāt śaktaḥ ca arthin^ḥ ca karmabhiḥ adhikriyate mātṛtaḥ
498.18	kumārake jāyamāne na śaktiḥ na ca arthitā iti ubhayam tu gṛha-sthe
498.19	jāyamāne tasmāt gṛha-sthaḥ jāyamānaḥ abhidhīyate iti/
499.01	arthitvasya apariṇāme jarā-marya-vāda-upapattiḥ yad ca idam brāhmaṇam ājarasam
499.02	karmabhiḥ sambandham śāsti tad anyathā sambhavati iti arthitvasya apariṇāme
499.03	jarā-marya-vāda-upapattiḥ/ yadā arthitva-pariṇāmaḥ yadā arthitvam pariṇatam
499.04	bhavati tadā anena agnihotram san nyasanīyam āyuṣas-turīya-bhāgam pravrajya
499.05	ayuktam āha jarā iti yāvat jīva-saṃyoge hi jarayā havā iti anarthakam
499.06	yasmāt ayam śaktasya bāhya-śaktiḥ vihitā sati arthitve anyena havanam
499.07	śāsti yadā punar arthitvam vipariṇatam tadā sanyāsaḥ abhidhīyate iti/
499.08	kasmāt punar etad evam ucyate sati arthitve agnihotra-havanam iti karma-vidhi^i
499.09	kāma-saṃyoga-śruti^aḥ yasmāt sarvasmin karmaṇi kāma-vidhiḥ śrūyate svarga-
499.10	kāmaḥ iti yathā upadiṣṭa-artham vidvān ca upadeśa-viṣayaḥ yaḥ ca upadiṣṭam artham
499.11	vijānāti tam prati upadeśaḥ kriyate na gāyanaḥ badhira^su pravartate na
499.12	narttakaḥ andha^su na ca upadeśa-vijñānam bālake asti tasmāt na tam prati upadeśaḥ
499.13	na bhidyate ca laukikāt laukikaḥ api tāvat aparīkṣakaḥ na
499.14	jāta-mātram kumārakam evam brūyāt adhīsva yajasva brahmacaryam cara iti/ kutaḥ
499.15	evam ṛṣiḥ upapanna-anavadya-vādin^ḥ brūyāt iti/ atha api vihitam vā anūdyeta
499.16	kāmāt vā arthaḥ kalpyete vihita-anuvacanam nyāyam tatra anupapanna-
499.17	pramāṇakasya arthasya abhidhānam kāmataḥ prakalpyeta yaḥ hi arthaḥ pramāṇataḥ
499.18	yāvat abhidhānam na sambhavati tasya yad abhidhānam tatra kāmataḥ prakalpanam
500.01	sambhavati yathā jāyamānaḥ kumārakaḥ ṛṇavān bhavati iti upapanna-
500.02	pramāṇasya abhidhānam anuvacanam yathā jāyamāna-śabdaḥ gṛhasthaḥ iti/
500.03	katham avagamyate gṛhasthaḥ iti yajña-sādhana-tvāt yasmāt yajamānaḥ
500.04	yajña-aṅgam bhavati sādhane tu prayatna-vyāpāraḥ dṛṣṭaḥ na phale yathā pāka-
500.05	sādhana^su kāṣṭha-ādiṣu na pāke na loṣṭa-ādiṣu sādhana^su/ śeṣam bhāṣye/
500.06	katham punar idam gamyate phala-arthinaḥ etad brāhmaṇam bhavati iti/
500.07	[(4-1-61)]adhikaraṇāt ca vidhānam vidya-antara-vat
500.08	[4-1-60(62)]samāropaṇāt ātmani apratiṣedhaḥ
500.09	samāropaṇāt ātmani apratiṣedhaḥ/ yasmāt nivṛtte arthitve samāropaṇam/
500.10	vidhīyate/
500.11	[4-1-61(63)]pātra-caya-anta-anupapatti^aḥ phala-abhāvaḥ
500.12	pātra-caya-anta-anupapatti^aḥ phala-abhāvaḥ/ yasmāt nivṛtte arthitve pravrajitānām
500.13	pātra-caya-antāni karmāṇi na kriyante yadi ca idam brāhmaṇam
500.14	aviśeṣena pravartate tena sarvatra pātra-caya-āntāni karmāṇi prasajyante
500.15	iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/  yad punar etad kleṣa-santati^aḥ avicchedāt iti/
500.16	[4-1-62(64)]suṣuptasya svapna-adarśane kleṣa-abhāva-vat apavargaḥ
500.17	suṣuptasya svapna-adarśane kleṣa-abhāva-vat apavargaḥ/ yathā suṣuptasya svapna-
500.18	adarśane kleṣa-anubandhaḥ vicchidyate sukha-duḥkha-sādhana-anubandhaḥ ca tathā muktasya
501.01	api iti tad ca yoginaḥ muktasya rūpam udāharanti iti yad api pravṛtti-anubandhāt
501.02	iti/
501.03	[4-1-63(65)]na pravṛttiḥ pratisandhānāya hīna-kleśasya
501.04	na pravṛttiḥ pratisandhānāya hīna-kleṣasya/ vidyamānā pravṛttiḥ
501.05	kleṣam antareṇa pratisandhānāya na bhavati iti dharma-adharma-kāraṇam na bhavati
501.06	iti yāvat uktam syāt pratisandhiḥ tu pūrva-janma-nivṛtti^i punarjanman tad ca
501.07	tṛṣṇā-kāritam/ etad ca uktam vīta-rāga-janma-adarśanāt iti sūtre/ karma-
501.08	vaiphalya-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet na karma-vipāka-pratisaṃvedanasya apratyākhyānāt na
501.09	brūmaḥ vidyamāne karmaṇi mucyate iti api tu karmāṇi ante
501.10	janmani vipacyante/
501.11	[4-1-64(66)]na kleśa-santati^aḥ svābhāvika-tvāt
501.12	na kleśa-santati^aḥ svābhāvika-tvāt/ na upapadyate kleśa-santati^aḥ vicchedaḥ
501.13	kutaḥ kleṣa-santati^aḥ svābhāvika-tvāt/ anādiḥ iyam kleśa-santatiḥ
501.14	svabhāvataḥ pravṛttā na ca asau ucchettum śakyā iti/  tatra eke parihāram
501.15	varṇayanti/
501.16	[4-1-65(67)]prāk-utpatti^aḥ abhāva-anityatva-vat svābhāvike api anityatvam
501.18	prāk-utpatti^aḥ abhāva-anityatva-vat svābhāvike api anityatvam/ yathā prāk-
502.01	utpatti^aḥ abhāvaḥ anādiḥ atha ca bhāvena nivartyate evam anādiḥ kleśa-
502.02	santatiḥ atattva-jñasya tattva-jñānena virotsyate iti/
502.03	[4-1-66(68)]aṇu-śyāmatā-nityatva-vat vā
502.04	aṇu-śyāmatā-nityatva-vat vā yathā aṇu^aḥ śyāmatā anādiḥ ca anityā
502.05	ca evam kleśa-santatiḥ api iti/ sataḥ khalu dharmaḥ nityatvam anityatvam ca iti
502.06	nitya-anityatve avadhārayadbhiḥ uktam tattva-bhāve bhāktam iti/ kā punar
502.07	iha bhaktiḥ nityam akāraṇam akāraṇaḥ ca prāgabhāvaḥ anityam bhūtva
502.08	na bhavati na ca prāgabhāva-abhāve sati bhavati etad sāmānyam yad punar
502.09	etad anādiḥ aṇu-śyāmatā iti hetu-abhāvāt ayuktam anutpatti-dharmakam anityam
502.10	na atra hetuḥ asti  ayam tu samādhiḥ/
502.11	[4-1-67(69)]na saṅkalpa-nimitta-tvāt ca rāga-ādīnām
502.12	na saṅkalpa-nimitta-tvāt ca rāga-ādīnām/ karma-nimitta-tvāt itaretara-
502.13	nimitta-tvāt ca iti ca-arthaḥ/ anubhūta-viṣaya-prārthanā-saṅkalpaḥ iti uktam/
502.14	mithyā-saṅkalpa^bhyaḥ rañjanīya-kopanīya-mohanīya^bhyaḥ rāga-ādyaḥ  prādurbhavanti/
502.15	karman ca nikāya-nivartakam saṅkalpa-apekṣam rāga-ādīn janayati/
502.16	dṛṣṭaḥ hi kaḥ cit sattva-nikāyaḥ rāga-bahulaḥ yathā pārāvatā-ādiḥ/
502.17	kaḥ cit krodha-bahulaḥ yathā sarpa-ādiḥ/ kaḥ cit moha-bahulaḥ yathā
502.18	jagara-ādiḥ/ yadi karman rāga-ādīn nirvartayeta sarvadā rāga-ādibhiḥ
503.01	bhavet iti anirmokṣa-prasaṅgaḥ na kāraṇa-vaikalyāt saṅkalpa-ādi-apekṣam karman
503.02	rāga-ādi-kāraṇam na nirapekṣam iti/ sukha-ādīnām api tarhi karma-
503.03	kāraṇaka-tvam na prāptam na nirapekṣa-tvāt karma-sukha-ādiṣu kartavya^su
503.04	saṅkalpāt na apekṣate api tu sva-kāraṇa-sannidhāna-apekṣam sukha-ādi karoti/
503.05	yathā utkṣapaṇa-ādi-karman nodanā-adi-apekṣam saṃskāram karoti anapekṣam tu
503.06	saṃyoga-vibhāgau iti/ paraspara-nimitta-tvam ca rāga-ādīnām dṛṣṭam raktaḥ
503.07	muhyati raktaḥ kupyati kupitaḥ muhyati rajyate ca mūḍhaḥ kupyati
503.08	rajyate ca iti/ yad api idam ucyate anādiḥ kleśa-santatiḥ iti tad na aviśeṣāt
503.09	yathā anādiḥ kleśa-santatiḥ evam ādhyātmikāḥ bhāvāḥ sarve anādinā
503.10	prabandhena pravartante na jātu anutpanna-pūrvam kim cit utpadyate na ca evam
503.11	kim cit anutpatti-dharmakam pratijñāyate anutpanna-pūrvam tu tattva-jñānam
503.12	kevalam utpadyate na anyaḥ kaḥ cit ādhyātmikaḥ bhāvaḥ iti//
503.13	iti - auddyotakare nyāyavārttike caturthasya adhyāyasya
503.14	ādyam āhnikam//504.01	caturthe adhyāye
504.02	dvitīyam āhnikam/
504.03	namaḥ parama-ātmane/
504.04	tad idam tattva-jñānam utpadyāmanam kim viṣaya-mātre bhavati āhosvit viṣaya-
504.05	viśeṣe iti/ viṣaya-mātre tāvat aśakyam tattva-jñānena bhavitum kutaḥ
504.06	viṣayāṇām ānantyāt viṣaya-viśeṣe api tattva-jñāna-viṣaya-tvena yaḥ avatiṣṭhate
504.07	saḥ vibhajya vacanīyaḥ/ tatra viṣaya-antarāṇām ātma-ādi-prameya-vyatirekiṇām
504.08	nirākaraṇam prameya-viśeṣa-viṣaya-tvāt sūtrasya yad tad ātma-ādi-sūtram na tena
504.09	prameya-mātram abhidhīyate prameya-mātra-abhidhāne hi ātma-ādi-grahaṇam vyartham
504.10	api tu prameya-viśeṣaḥ yaḥ tattva-jñāna-viṣaya-tvena vyavatiṣṭhamānaḥ saṃsāra-
504.11	viccheda-hetuḥ bhavati yaḥ ca moha-viṣaya-tvāt saṃsāram pratanoti saḥ tattvataḥ
504.12	jñeyaḥ iti/ tasmin ayam praśnaḥ kim khalu yāvantaḥ viṣayāḥ tāvatsu
504.13	tattva-jñānam pratyekam utpadyate atha kva cit utpadyate iti/ na tāvat pratyekam
504.14	ātma-ādi^aḥ ānantyāt kim idam ānantyam iyattā-pratyaya-aviṣaya-tvam/
504.15	atha kva cit utpadyate yatra na utpadyate tattva-jñānam tatra anivṛttaḥ mohaḥ
504.16	yatra mohaḥ tatra rāga-dveṣau iti rāga-dveṣa-mohāḥ bandhanam iti anirmokṣa-
504.17	prasaṅgaḥ/ atha manyase anya-viṣayam tattva-jñānam anya-viṣayam moham nivartayati
504.18	evam ca sati apavargaḥ prāṇi-mātre prāpnoti na hi kaḥ cit prāṇin^ḥ
505.01	kasya cit arthasya tattvam na vā iti na moha-artha-aparijñānāt na tattva-jñānasya
505.02	anutpatti-mātram mohaḥ api tu mithyā-jñānam mohaḥ tad ca mithyā-jñānam yasmin
505.03	viṣaye pravartamānam saṃsāra-bījam bhavati saḥ viṣayaḥ tattvataḥ jñeyaḥ iti/ kim
505.04	punar tad mithyā-jñānam anātmani ātma-pratyayaḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
505.05	[4-2-1]doṣa-nimittānām tattva-jñānāt ahaṅkāra-nivṛttiḥ
505.06	doṣa-nimittānām tattva-jñānāt ahaṅkāra-nivṛttiḥ/ śarīra-ādi-duḥkha-antam
505.07	prameyam doṣa-nimittam tad-viṣaya-tvāt mithyā-jñānasya tad-viṣayam tattva-jñānam
505.08	utpannam tad-viṣayam ahaṅkāram nivartayati samāne viṣaye tayoḥ virodhāt/
505.09	sarvam ca etad uktam dvitīya-sūtre iti/
505.10	[4-2-2]doṣa-nimittam rūpa-ādyaḥ  viṣayāḥ saṅkalpa-kṛtāḥ
505.11	doṣa-nimittam rūpa-ādyaḥ  viṣayāḥ saṅkalpa-kṛtāḥ/ kāmāḥ viṣayāḥ
505.12	indriya-arthāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  ucyante/ tatra te viṣayāḥ mithyā-saṃkalpyamānāḥ
505.13	rāga-ādīn janayanti kaḥ punar eṣām tu mithyā-saṃkalpaḥ asādhāraṇa-
505.14	pratyaya-utpatti-nimitta-tvena vyavasāyaḥ mama eva ete iti/ tān asādhāraṇa-tayā
505.15	praticakṣīta na ete mama daiva-caura-agni-dāyāt asādhāraṇāḥ iti/ evam
505.16	etasya praticakṣāṇasya yaḥ vyavasāyaḥ tad-viṣayaḥ mohaḥ saḥ nivartate
505.17	iti/ tad-nivṛtti^i adhyātmam śarīra-ādīn prasañcakṣīta/ kim punar
505.18	prasaṃkhyānam śarīra-ādiṣu na ete ātmānaḥ iti vyatireka-darśanam prasaṃkhyānam/
506.01	saḥ ayam dhyātmam bahis ca vivikta-cittaḥ viharan muktaḥ iti ucyate/ etad ca
506.02	tam prāhuḥ jīvan eva vidvān saṃharṣa-āyāsābhyām mucyate iti/  ataḥ param
506.03	kā cit sañjñā bhāvayitavyā kā cit heyā iti upadiśyate/ na artha-nirākaraṇam
506.04	na artha-upādānam kim tu yathā-vyavasthite eva viṣaye kim ict bhāvanīyam
506.05	kim cit nirākaraṇīyam iti/
506.06	[4-2-3]tad-nimittam tu avayavi-abhimānaḥ
506.07	tad-nimittam tu avayavi-abhimānaḥ/ teṣām doṣāṇām nimittam
506.08	avayavi-abhimānaḥ/ tatra avayavin^ḥ iti sañjñā bhāvanīyā strī-sañjñā heyā
506.09	sapariṣkārā puruṣasya pariṣkāraḥ bandhanam strī^aḥ puruṣa-sañjñā
506.10	sapariṣakārā iti/ tatra api ca dve sañjñā^i nimitta-sañjñā artha-vyañjana-sañjñā
506.11	ca/ nimitta-sañjñā danta-oṣṭham iti danta-ādiṣu dantatva-ādi-nibandhanā
506.12	sañjñā/ artha-vyañjana-sañjñā ca ittham dantāḥ ittham oṣṭhau iti ittham iti
506.13	adhyāropena ucyamānā sañjñā mohaḥ saḥ ca rāga-ādi-kāraṇam iti
506.14	heyaḥ na punar avayavin^ḥ nirākāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ tathā-bhāvāt iti//
506.15	atha idānīm artham nirākariṣyatā avayavini upapādyate/
506.16	[4-2-4]vidyā-avidyā-dvaividhyāt saṃśayaḥ
506.17	vidyā-avidyā-dvaividhyāt saṃśayaḥ/ sat-asatoḥ upalambhāt dvi-vidhā
506.18	vidyā sat-asatoḥ anupalambhāt avidyā dvi-vidhā eva/ evam ca avayavin^ḥ yadi upalabhyate
507.01	atha na upalabhyate na katham cana saṃśayāt mucyate iti/ tatra
507.02	tāvat uktam na vidyā-avidyā-dvaividhyam saṃśayasya kāraṇam upalabdhi-anupalabhi^oḥ
507.03	pūrva-pada-viśeṣaṇa-tvāt/
507.04	[4-2-5]tad-asaṃśayaḥ pūrva-hetu-prasiddha-tvāt
507.05	tad-asaṃśayaḥ pūrva-hetu-prasiddha-tvāt/ tasmin avayavini saṃśayaḥ na
507.06	yuktaḥ pūrva-uktānām hetūnām apratiṣedhāt tatra ye avayavinaḥ artha-antara-bhāve
507.07	hetu^āḥ uddiṣṭāḥ te na śakyāḥ pratiṣeddhum iti/
507.08	[4-2-6]vṛtti-anupapatti^aḥ api tarhi na saṃśayaḥ
507.09	vṛtti-anupapatti^aḥ api tarhi na saṃśayaḥ/ na asti avayavin^ḥ iti tad vibhajate
507.11	[4-2-7]kṛtsna-ekadeśa-avṛtti-tvāt avayavānām avayavi-abhāvaḥ
507.12	kṛtsna-ekadeśa-avṛtti-tvāt avayavānām avayavi-abhāvaḥ/ avayavāḥ avayavini
507.13	varteran avayavin^ḥ vā avayava^su/ avayavāḥ avayavini vartamānāḥ
507.14	kṛtsnena vartante ekadeśena vā/ tatra tāvat avayavāḥ avayavnini
507.15	kṛtsnena vartante avayava-avayavinoḥ parimāṇa-bhedāt/ alpa-parimāṇaḥ
507.16	avayavaḥ mahā-parimāṇaḥ ca avayavin^ḥ iti alpa-parimāṇena mahā-
508.01	parimāṇasya avyāptiḥ eka-dravyaḥ avayavaḥ prāpnoti eka-avayavi-vṛtti-tvāt/
508.02	na ca eka-dravyam dravaym avinaśyat ādhāram asti na api avayavin^ḥ ekadeśena vartate na
508.03	hi asya anye avayavāḥ ekadeśa-bhūtāḥ santi tasmin api ca ekadeśena vartamānaḥ
508.04	avayavaḥ kim ekadeśena vartate kṛtsnena vā iti pūrvavat prasaṅgaḥ/
508.05	atha yava^su avayavin^ḥ vartate
508.06	[4-2-8]teṣu ca avṛtti^aḥ avayavi-abhāvaḥ
508.07	teṣu ca avṛtti^aḥ avayavi-abhāvaḥ/ na tāvat pratyavayavam kṛtsnaḥ vartate
508.08	tayoḥ parimāṇa-bhedāt avayavinaḥ ca eka-dravya-tā-prasaṅgāt/ eka-avayava-
508.09	vṛtti-tvāt eka-dravyaḥ avayavin^ḥ prāpnoti eka-dravyaḥ ca avayavin^ḥ ekena dravyena
508.10	ārabhyate iti satata-utpatti-prasaṅgaḥ/ kim ca eka-dravya-tvāt dvi-aṇukam
508.11	ekasmin paramāṇu^i vartate iti kāraṇa-vibhāga-vināśa-abhāvāt
508.12	nityam prāpnoti utpattimat ca nityam ca iti na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti/ atha api avayavin^ḥ
508.13	ekadeśena vartate tathā api avayavini ārambhaka-avayavi-avyatirikta-
508.14	avayavāh prāpnuvanti ye avayava^su vartante iti/
508.15	[4-2-9]pṛthak ca avayava^bhyaḥ avayavi-avṛtti^aḥ
508.16	pṛthak ca avayava^bhyaḥ avayavi-avṛtti^aḥ/ na api avayava-vyatirekena anyatra vartate
508.17	agrahaṇāt nityatva-prasaṅgāt avayava-vyatirekena anyatra vartamānaḥ upalabhyeta
508.18	nityaḥ ca syāt anādhāram dravyam nityam iti/
509.01	[4-2-10]na ca avayavin^ḥ avayavāḥ
509.02	na ca avayavin^ḥ avayavāḥ/ na ca avayavānām dharma-mātram avayavin^ḥ kasmāt
509.03	dharmasya avayavinaḥ dharmibhiḥ avayavaiḥ pūrvavat sambandhaḥ anupapannaḥ na ca atra
509.04	dharmibhyaḥ avayava^bhyaḥ avayavin^ḥ dharmaḥ pṛthak upalabhyate nityatva-prasaṅgāt iti
509.05	ca samānam/ avayavin^ḥ ca ekadeśena avayave vartate iti avayava-samūha-
509.06	mātram avayavin^ḥ prāpnoti/ ekasmin ca avayave ekadeśena vartamānaḥ avayavin^ḥ
509.07	eka-avayava-upalambhāt ekadeśena upalabdhi^i tad-sthānaḥ avayavin^ḥ gṛhyeta na
509.08	tu eka-tantu-darśane paṭa-upalabdhiḥ bhavati tad evam mukta-saṃśayaḥ abhidhīyate
509.09	na asti avayavin^ḥ iti/  yad tāvat avayavāḥ avayavini vartante iti tad na
509.10	anabhyupagamāt na kāraṇam kārye vartate api tu kāraṇe kāryam iti/
509.11	yad punar etad avayavin^ḥ avayava^su vartamānaḥ ekadeśena vartate kṛtsnena vā
509.12	vartate iti tad na/
509.13	[4-2-11]ekasmin bheda-abhāvāt bheda-śabda-prayoga-anupapatti^aḥ apraśnaḥ
509.15	ekasmin bheda-abhāvāt bheda-śabda-prayoga-anupapatti^aḥ apraśnaḥ/ kṛtsnam
509.16	iti ca ekadeśaḥ iti ca bheda-viṣayau etau śabdau na ca etau ekasmin
509.17	upapannau anekasya aśeṣatā kṛtsna-śabdasya arthaḥ aśeṣasya kasya cit abhidhānam
509.18	ekadeśa-śabdasya na ca etad iha upapadyate iti/ tasmāt na avayavini
509.19	kṛtsna-śabdaḥ na api ekadeśa-śabdaḥ iti/
510.01	[4-2-12]avayava-antara-abhāve api avṛtti^aḥ ahetuḥ
510.02	avayava-antara-abhāve api avṛtti^aḥ ahetuḥ/ avayava-antara-abhāvāt iti/
510.03	yadi avayava-vyatiriktam avayava-antaram pratipadyethāḥ tathā api avayavaḥ avayave
510.04	vartate na avayavin^ḥ iti ahetuḥ avayava-antara-abhāvāt na ekadeśena vartate iti
510.05	evam ca anekatra vartate iti pratijānānaḥ na anuyoktavyaḥ ubhayena
510.06	vyāghātāt iti uktam/ yadi avayavin^ḥ na ekadeśena vartate na kṛtsne vartate
510.07	atha katham vartate iti vṛttiḥ avayava^su āśraya-āśrayi-bhāvaḥ samavāya-
510.08	ākhyaḥ sambandhaḥ saḥ katham bhavati iti yasya yataḥ anyatra ātma-lābha-anupapattiḥ
510.09	tad tatra eva vartate iti na khalu kāraṇa-dravya^bhyaḥ anyatra
510.10	kārya-dravyam ātmānam labhate iti/ viparyayaḥ tu kāraṇa-dravya^su na kāraṇāni
510.11	kārya-dravya^su ātmānam labhante iti/ katham nitye āśraya-āśrayi-
510.12	bhāvaḥ anitya^su darśanāt siddham nitya^su yathā anityāḥ niyata anityāni yatraupalabhyante
510.13	tatra vartante tathā nityāni api yatra upalabhyante tatra vartante
510.14	iti etad samānam anityānām nityaiḥ rūpa-antara-anirdeśāt iti cet
510.15	atha manyase yadi vyatiriktaḥ avayavin^ḥ bhavati tasya avayava-rūpa-ādi-vyatirekena
510.16	avayavinaḥ rūpa-antaram nirdeṣṭavyam yathā acitrāḥ tantu^āḥ paṭa-
510.17	mātram ārabhante iti na upalabhyamāna-rūpa-ādi-ādhāra-tvāt yad avayavinaḥ
510.18	rūpam upalabhyate tad asya rūpam yathā avayavasya kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvāt ca
511.01	viruddhaḥ avayavinaḥ rūpam nirdiśyatām iti bruvāṇena avayava-avayavinau
511.02	tāvat abhyupagatau tad-abhyupagamāt ca viruddhaḥ etāvān eva ayam vivādaḥ
511.03	abhyupeta-hāniḥ/ upetya citra-vivādam brūmaḥ paṭasya citram rūpam iti/
511.04	anekatva-prasaṅgaḥ iti cet atha manyase citram rūpam avayavinaḥ abhyupagacchatā
511.05	aneka-rūpaḥ avayavin^ḥ upagataḥ bhavati na ca ekam dravyam aneka-rūpam
511.06	na api anekasmin ekam iti/ na citra-śabdasya anekaika-viṣaya-tvāt
511.07	citra-śabdena anaikam ekam ca abhidhīyate anekam ca citram rūpam citrāṇi rūpāṇi
511.08	iti na ekasmin dṛṣṭa-tvāt/ na hi ekasmin citram dṛṣṭam iti
511.09	na abhyupeta-hāni^aḥ/ ekam citram iti anabhyupagata-aneka-citram iti heyam
511.10	eka-citra-samuccayaḥ hi aneka-citram iti atha anekam acitram citram iti
511.11	ucyate evam api anivṛttaḥ vyāghātaḥ acitram citram iti yathā aśuklam
511.12	śuklam iti/ atha acitrāṇi tantu-rūpāṇi saṃhatya paṭe citram rūpam
511.13	utpādayanti iti pakṣe āśrīyate evam ca etasmin kim cit bādhyate
511.14	paṭa-antare prasaṅgaḥ citra-pratyayasya yasya paṭasya ekam citram pārśvam dvitīyam
511.15	avicitram tena yathā ekasmin pārśve tam paṭam paśyataḥ citra-buddhiḥ bhavati
511.16	evam paṭa-antare api citra-paṭa-buddhi^ā bhavitavyam bhavatā eva idam uktam yasya
511.17	paṭasya ekam pārśvam citram saḥ tu paṭaḥ na citraḥ pārśvasya apaṭa-tvāt/ citra-
511.18	pratyayaḥ tatra kasmāt na iti cet citra-acitrābhyām pārśvābhyām paṭaḥ ārabhyamāṇaḥ
511.19	citraḥ paṭaḥ iti yathā paṭa-antare citra-pratyayaḥ evam citra-
512.01	pratyayaḥ ghaṭa-antare api prasaktaḥ na prasaktaḥ ekasya citra-rūpasya anārambhaka-
512.02	tvāt ekasmin pārśve citram rūpam tad katham avayavini rūpa-ādi-
512.03	antaram ārapsyate iti/ etāvat ca śakyam vaktum avayava-rūpābhyām avayavi-
512.04	rūpa-antaram ārabhyate iti tathā-upalabdhi^aḥ/ yadi anya-rūpaḥ avayavin^ḥ syāt
512.05	na upalabhyate na ca avayava-rūpāt avayavinaḥ grahaṇam yuktam vāyu^aḥ api upalabdhi-
512.06	prasaṅgāt yadi anya-rūpāt anyasya upalabdhiḥ bhavati vāyu^aḥ api upalabdhiḥ
512.07	prāpnoti na ca etad asti/ tasmāt svarūpāt avayavin^ḥ upalabhyate ubhaya-avayava-
512.08	darśana-pratisandhānāt ca avayavini rūpa-upalabdhiḥ bhavati na ca evam sati arūpaḥ
512.09	avayavin^ḥ iti tasmāt avayavi-abhimānaḥ pratiṣidhyate sapariskāra-rūpaḥ
512.10	na avayavin^ḥ iti yathā rūpa-ādiṣu mithyā-saṃkalpaḥ na rūpa-ādyaḥ  iti/ ayam
512.11	ca arthaḥ muninā ślokena darśitaḥ/
512.12	indriya-arthāḥ hi anarthāya yadi syuḥ avikalpitāḥ/
512.13	sarvaḥ anarthena sajyeta caran indriya-gocaraḥ// iti/
512.14	sarva-agrahaṇam avayavi-asiddhi^aḥ iti avasthitaḥ api edam atha idamāha/
512.15	[4-2-13]keśa-samūhe taimirika-upalabdhi-vat tad-upalabdhiḥ
512.16	keśa-samūhe taimirika-upalabdhi-vat tad-upalabdhiḥ/ yathā ekaikaḥ keśaḥ
512.17	taimirikena na upalabhyate keśa-samūhaḥ tu upalabhyate tathā ekaikaśaḥ
512.18	aṇuḥ na upalabhyate aṇu-samūhaḥ tu upalabhyate iti tad idam aṇu-samūha-viṣaya-
512.19	grahaṇam iti/
513.01	[4-2-14]sva-viṣaya-anatikramena indriyasya paṭu-manda-bhāvāt viṣaya-
513.02	grahaṇasya tathā-bhāvaḥ na aviṣaye pravṛttiḥ
513.03	sva-viṣaya-anatikramena indriyasya paṭu-manda-bhāvāt viṣaya-grahaṇasya tathā-
513.04	bhāvaḥ na aviṣaye pravṛttiḥ/ na hi indriyam prakṛṣyamāṇam sva-viṣayam atikrāmati
513.05	na hi cakṣus prakṛṣyamāṇam rasam gṛhṇāti na apakṛṣyamāṇam
513.06	sva-viṣayāt pracyavate api tu sva-viṣaye eva indriyasya prakarṣa-apakarṣa-
513.07	apekṣāṇi paṭu-manda-bhāvena grahaṇāni bhavanti iti/ tatra paṭu-grahaṇam
513.08	sāmānya-viśeṣa-tadvatām grahaṇam iti mandam tu grahaṇam sāmānya-mātra-
513.09	ālocanam iti saḥ ayam taimirikaḥ kañ cit kaḥ cit cakṣus-viṣayam keśam na gṛhṇāti
513.10	keśa-samūham ca gṛhṇāti ubhayam hi ataimirikasya cakṣuṣaḥ viṣayaḥ
513.11	iti/ paramāṇu^āḥ tu atīndriyāḥ darśana-viṣaya-tvam na pratipadyante saṃhatāḥ
513.12	aindriyakāḥ asaṃhatāḥ atīndriyāḥ iti mahān vyāghātaḥ/ anupajāta-
513.13	viśeṣāṇām darśana-viṣaya-tva-anupapatti^aḥ tasmāt utpadyate avayavin^ḥ yaḥ
513.14	darśanasya viṣayaḥ iti/ sañcaya-mātram darśanasya viṣayaḥ iti cet
513.15	atha manyase darśanasya samuccaya-mātram viṣayaḥ samuccitāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ
513.16	darśanasya viṣaya-tām upayānti iti na samuccayasya saṃyoga-bhāvāt/
513.17	samuccayaḥ nirūpyamāṇaḥ paramāṇu-saṃyogaḥ saṃyogaḥ ca upalabhyamāna-āśrayaḥ
513.18	upalabhyate bhavati iti hi idam anena saṃyuktam iti yaḥ ca viṣayaḥ upalabhyate
513.19	tasya anupalabdhi^i yad ca āvaraṇam kāraṇam upalabhyate na tu paramāṇu^āḥ
514.01	upalabdhi-lakṣaṇa-prāptāḥ tasmāt na teṣām āvaraṇa-ādi^aḥ agrahaṇa-kāraṇāt agrahaṇam
514.02	iti//
514.03	[4-2-15]avayava-avayavi-prasaṅgaḥ ca evam āpralayāt
514.04	avayava-avayavi-prasaṅgaḥ ca evam āpralayāt/ yaḥ ca avayavinaḥ avayava-vṛtti-
514.05	vikalpāt abhāvaḥ uktaḥ saḥ avayavasya avayava^su prasajyamānaḥ pralayāt vā
514.06	nivarteta niravayavāt vā paramāṇutaḥ nivarteta/ katham iti yathā avayavin^ḥ
514.07	avayava^su vartamānaḥ pratyekam sarva-ātmanā vā ekadeśena vā varteta iti
514.08	uktam/ tathā avayavaḥ avayava^su vartamānaḥ tad-avayavaḥ api avayava^su iti evam yāvat
514.09	paramāṇuḥ iti yāvat vā pralayaḥ anivṛttiḥ vā iti/ sarvathā upalabdhi-viṣayaḥ
514.10	na asti upalabdhi-viṣayam ca āśritya ayam vādaḥ kim avayavin^ḥ ekadeśena vartate
514.11	uta sarva-ātmanā vartate iti/ saḥ ayam āśrayam vyāghnan ātma-vyāghātāya
514.12	kalpate iti/
514.13	[4-2-16]na pralayaḥ aṇu-sadbhāvāt
514.14	na pralayaḥ aṇu-sadbhāvāt/ na ca asti pralayaḥ aṇu-sadbhāvāt/ ayam
514.15	khalu vibhajyamāna-bhedaḥ tāvat vartate yāvat paramāṇuḥ iti paramāṇuḥ
514.16	nāma vibhāge alpatara-prasaṅgasya yataḥ na alpīyaḥ tatra avasthānāt/ loṣṭasya
514.17	vibhajyamāna-avayavasya alpataram alpataram alpatamam uttaram uttaram bhavati saḥ ca ayam alpatara-
514.18	prasaṅgaḥ yataḥ nivartate yataḥ na alpīyaḥ asti tam paramāṇum
514.19	pracakṣmahe iti/  atha na paramāṇu-antaḥ vibhāgaḥ tena
515.01	[4-2-17]param vā truṭi^aḥ
515.02	param vā truṭi^aḥ/ avayava-vibhāgasya ānantyāt truṭiḥ ameyaḥ prāpnoti
515.03	saṃkhyā-parimāṇa-gurutvaiḥ tāvat parimāṇaḥ truṭiḥ iyat atra gurutvam vartate
515.04	iti iyantaḥ ca paramāṇu^āḥ saṃhatāḥ truṭi-bhāvam āpadyante iti na avadhāraṇam
515.05	yuktam/ kim kāraṇam yathā eva aneka-paramāṇu-saṃhataḥ hi mahān saṃkhyā-
515.06	parimāṇa-gurutvaiḥ ameyaḥ tathā truṭiḥ api bhedasya ānantyāt prasajyate atha
515.07	pralaya-antaḥ vibhāgaḥ saḥ api na yujyate vibhāgasya vibhajyamāna-
515.08	hāna-anupapatti^aḥ na pralaya-antaḥ vibhāgaḥ saḥ ca ayam vibhāgaḥ vibhajyamāna-
515.09	ādhāraḥ vibhajyamānaḥ ca na asti vibhāgaḥ asti iti vyāghātaḥ/
515.10	vibhajyamāna-avayavam paramāṇum pratipadyamānena sāvayavaḥ paramāṇuḥ
515.11	pratipattavyaḥ/ sāvayava-tve paramāṇu-śabdasya arthaḥ vācyaḥ kim uktam bhavati
515.12	paramāṇuḥ iti/ ye ca paramāṇu^aḥ avayavāḥ te kim samāna-parimāṇāḥ uta
515.13	bhinna-parimāṇāḥ iti/ yadi samāna-parimāṇāḥ avayava-avayavi-bhāvaḥ
515.14	durupapādaḥ na hi vayam samāna-parimāṇānām kva cit avayava-avayavi-bhāvam
515.15	paśyāmaḥ/ atha bhinna-parimāṇāḥ na paramāṇuḥ pratiṣidhyate kāryam idam
515.16	paramāṇuḥ iti kṛtvā pratiṣidhyate/
515.17	atha idānīm ānupalabdhikaḥ sarvam na asti iti manyamānaḥ āha/
515.18	[4-2-18]ākāśa-vyatibhedāt tad-anupapatti^aḥ
515.19	ākāśa-vyatibhedāt tad-anupapatti^aḥ/ ākāśena paramāṇuḥ vyatibhinnaḥ
515.20	vyatibhedāt anityaḥ ghaṭa-vat iti/
516.01	[4-2-19]ākāśa-asarvagata-tvam vā
516.02	[4-2-20]antar bahis ca kārya-dravyasya kāraṇa-antara-vacanāt akārye
516.03	tad-abhāvaḥ
516.04	ākāśa-asarvagata-tvam vā/ atha paramāṇu^aḥ antar-ākāśam na asti asarvagatam
516.05	tarhi ākāśam prasajyate antar-paramāṇūnām asambandhāt/ ākāśa-vyatibhedāt
516.06	anityaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti abhidadhānaḥ vyatibheda-artham praṣṭavyaḥ kim uktam
516.07	bhavati ākāśa-vyatibhedāt iti/ yadi paramāṇu-ākāśena sambandhaḥ
516.08	vyatibhedaḥ tadā atra na kim cit bādhyate na hi ākāśa-sambandhāt
516.09	paramāṇu^aḥ nityatvam anityatvam prasajyate iti/ atha sambandha-mātram eva
516.10	anityatā-kāraṇam brūyāt tathā api ākāśa-grahaṇam vyartham sambandha-mātrasya
516.11	anityatā-sādhaka-tvāt na hi kṛtakatvasya sādhana-bhāve devadatta-kṛtakatva-ādi
516.12	yuktam uktam saṃyoga-mātram ca uttara-sūtrena anūdyate carita-artham saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ
516.13	iti tad etad eva punar ucyate iti/ tasmāt na ayam vyatibheda-arthaḥ/
516.14	atha paramāṇu-antar-sambandhaḥ vyatibhedaḥ saḥ api na yuktaḥ akārya-tvāt
516.15	paramāṇu^aḥ antar bahis iti kāryasya kāraṇa-antara-vacanam akāryaḥ ca
516.16	paramāṇuḥ tasmāt na antar bahis iti/ atha paramāṇu-avayava-sambandhah ākāśena
516.17	vyatibhedaḥ etad api tādṛś eva paramāṇu^aḥ akārya-tvāt na tasya avayavāḥ iti/
516.18	atha paramāṇu-avayavānām vibhāgaḥ vyatibhedaḥ saḥ api akārya-tvena eva
516.19	pratyuktaḥ/ kāryasya avayavāḥ vibhajyante akāryaḥ ca paramāṇuḥ iti/
516.20	tasmāt na avayava-vibhāgaḥ upetya paramāṇu^aḥ avayavān na tad-vibhāgasya ākāśam
517.01	kāraṇam karma-jaḥ hi vibhāgaḥ tasya ākāśam api kāraṇam iti na
517.02	nyāyaḥ asti ākāśam ca vibhāga-hetu-tvena pratipadyamānaḥ sarva-bhāvānām
517.03	anavasthānam pratipadyeta na sannihite vibhāge hetu^i ākāśe bhāvānām
517.04	avasthānam yuktam/ sat api ākāśam nimitta-antaram apekṣate tad-vyatirekena
517.05	ākāśasya api tad-utpatti^i asti sāmarthyam iti pramāṇa-abhāvaḥ/ atha antar-
517.06	śuṣiram vyatibhedaḥ etad api na asti sarvataḥ vyavahitasya yasya madhye
517.07	avayavāḥ na santi tad śuṣiram na ca paramāṇu^aḥ avayavāḥ santi kutaḥ
517.08	śuṣiram vyatibhedaḥ/ na ca anyā gatiḥ asti tasmāt ākāśa-vyatibhedāt
517.09	iti śūnyam abhidhānam/ yad punar etad ākāśam asarvagatam prasajyeta
517.10	antar-paramāṇunā asambandhāt iti na sarvagata-artha-aparijñānāt na ayam sarvagata-
517.11	arthaḥ yad na asti tena sambandhaḥ iti api tu yad mūrtimat tena sarvena
517.12	sambandhah iti sarvagatatva-arthaḥ/ na ca paramāṇu^aḥ madhyam asti tasmāt na
517.13	tena asambandhāt ākāśam asarvagatam prasajyate iti/
517.14	[4-2-21]sarva-saṃyoga-śabda-vibhavāt ca sarvagatam
517.15	sarva-saṃyoga-śabda-vibhavāt ca sarvagatam/ yasmāt sarva-mūrtimadbhiḥ
517.16	saṃyogāḥ vibhavanti ākāśe tad-āśrayāḥ bhavanti śabdāḥ sva-kāraṇa-
517.17	sannidhānāt upajāyamānāḥ sarve eva ākāśam āśrayante iti sarvagatam
517.18	ākāśam/
518.01	[4-2-22]avyūha-aviṣṭambha-vibhutvāni ca ākāśa-dharmāḥ
518.02	avyūha-aviṣṭambha-vibhutvāni ca ākāśa-dharmāḥ/ sarpatā kriyāvatā
518.03	dravyena ākāśam na vyūhyate sarpataḥ ca kriyāvataḥ dravyasya kriyā-hetu guṇam
518.04	na pratibadhnāti kasmāt asparśa-tvāt yad sparśavat bhavati tad vyūhyate
518.05	pratibadhnāti ca na tu evam-dharmakam ākāśam tasmāt apratighāti iti sah bhavān
518.06	sāvayave sparśavati dravye dṛṣṭam dharmam viparītena āśaṅkitum arhati iti/ uktam
518.07	ca atra yadi paramāṇuḥ vibhajyate paramāṇuḥ na syāt/ kim kāraṇam
518.08	vibhāge alpatara-prasaṅgasya yataḥ na alpīyaḥ tatra avasthānam iti/ vināśam ca
518.09	paramāṇu^aḥ bhavan kāraṇa-vināśāt vā kalpyeta kāraṇa-vibhāgāt vā ubhayam
518.10	ca na asti paramāṇu^aḥ akārya-tvāt iti uktam/ tasmāt na ākāśa-vyatibhedāt
518.11	anityatvam iti/
518.12	[4-2-23]mūrtimatām ca saṃsthāna-upapatti^aḥ avayava-sadbhāvaḥ
518.13	mūrtimatām ca saṃsthāna-upapatti^aḥ avayava-sadbhāvaḥ/ na avayavāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ
518.14	mūrtimat-tvāt iti saṃsthānavat-tvāt ca sāvayavāḥ iti/ saṃsthānavat
518.15	sāvayavam dṛṣṭam ghaṭa-ādi-saṃsthānavantaḥ paramāṇu^āḥ tasmāt
518.16	sāvayavāḥ iti/
518.17	[4-2-24]saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ
518.18	[4-2-25]anavasthā-kāri-tvāt anavasthā-anupapatti^aḥ ca apratiṣedhaḥ
518.19	saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ/ sāvayavatvam saṃyogi-tvāt iti sūtra-arthaḥ/ nanu idam
518.20	sūtram saṃsthānavat-tvāt iti anena eva carita-artham saṃyoga-viśeṣasya saṃsthānavat-tvena
519.01	abhidhānāt/ na ca carita-artham avayava-saṃyoga-viśeṣasya saṃsthānavat-tvena
519.02	abhidhānāt saṃyoga-mātram ca saṃyoga-śabdena abhidhīyate iti na doṣaḥ/
519.03	tatra mūrtiḥ nāma avyāpi-dravya-ṣaḍ-vidham parimāṇam aṇu-mahat-dīrgham hrasvam
519.04	paramahrasvam paramāṇu iti/ saṃsthānam nāma pracaya-ākhyaḥ saṃyogaḥ saṃyogaḥ
519.05	aprāpti-pūrvakā prāptiḥ iti/ yad tāvat mūrtimat-tvāt sāvayavāḥ iti
519.06	tad na anaikāntāt/ yaḥ paramāṇu^aḥ avayavaḥ saḥ mūrtimān ca niravayavaḥ
519.07	ca iti anekāntaḥ/ atha mūrtimat-tvāt tasya api avayavāḥ santi evam sati
519.08	truṭiḥ ameyaḥ prāpnoti gurutva-saṃkhyā-parimāṇaiḥ iti uktam/ atha tāvat
519.09	mūrtimat vibhajyate yāvat antaḥ iti antyaḥ niravayavaḥ iti vācyam
519.10	atha antaḥ vibhāgaḥ saḥ na yuktaḥ vibhāgasya vibhajyamānaiḥ vinā anupapatti^aḥ
519.11	iti/ etāvat ca etad kalpaḥ iti syāt paramāṇu-antaḥ vibhāgaḥ pratyaya-
519.12	antaḥ vā anantaḥ vā yadi paramāṇu-antaḥ anaikāntaḥ vyāhatam bhavati
519.13	paramāṇuḥ mūrtimān ca na ca sāvayavaḥ iti/ kaḥ punar atra vyāghātaḥ
519.14	niravayavam mūrtimantam ca paramāṇum pratipadyase sāvayavaḥ iti ca bravīṣi
519.15	pratipatti^ā vyāhanyate anante pralaya-ante ca vyāghātaḥ truṭi^aḥ ameya-tva-prasaṅgaḥ
519.16	vibhāgasya ca anādhāra-tva-prasaṅgaḥ sāvayavaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti ca pratijñā-padei
519.17	vyāhatei/ katham iti sāvayava-śabdasya arthaḥ samāna-jātīya-ārabdham samāna-
519.18	jātīya-āśritam avayavaḥ tad-ādhāraḥ iti sāvayavaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti bruvatā
519.19	kārya-viśeṣaḥ paramāṇuḥ ca iti uktam bhavati/ kārya-viśeṣaḥ paramāṇuḥ ca iti
519.20	vyāhatam/ atha eka-paramāṇu-pūrvaka-tvam paramāṇu^aḥ pratipadyase tathā ca na
520.01	paramāṇuḥ sāvayavaḥ iti na asya avayavāḥ santi kim tu kṛtakaḥ paramāṇu-
520.02	pūrvaka-tvāt/ etad ca na dṛṣṭānta-abhāvāt ekam kāraṇam iti ca
520.03	dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti ekena ca ārabhyamāṇasya paramāṇu^aḥ na kāraṇa-sāmagrī-apekṣaṇam
520.04	asti iti na prāgabhāvaḥ yuktaḥ yasya prāgabhāvaḥ na asti tasya ca utpādah
520.05	na yuktaḥ/ atha eka-śabda-pūrvaka-tve sati śabda-ādīnām prāgabhāva-ādi pratipadyate
520.06	tad api asiddham na ekam kāraṇam asti āśraya-ādi-apekṣaḥ śabdaḥ śabda-
520.07	antaram karoti iti pratipadya api ca eka-paramāṇuḥ pūrvakatvam paramāṇu^ah kaḥ
520.08	sāvayava-arthaḥ katamaḥ tatra avayavaḥ katamaḥ tatra avayavin^ḥ iti/ atha kāraṇam
520.09	eva avayavaḥ iti na kārya-paramāṇu-kāle kāraṇa-paramāṇuḥ asti iti
520.10	sāvayava-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ/ mūrtimat-tvāt sāvayavaḥ paramāṇuḥ iti bruvāṇaḥ
520.11	vikalpataḥ punar paryanuyojyaḥ kā iyam mūrtiḥ yayā mūrti^ā mūrtimān
520.12	paramāṇuḥ iti satyām ca mūrti^i kim artha-antaram anartha-antaram vā paramāṇu^aḥ
520.13	mūrtiḥ iti yadi rūpa-ādi-viśeṣaḥ mūrtiḥ na bhavat-pakṣe tayā mūrti^ā
520.14	mūrtimān kaḥ cana na ca rūpa-ādi-vyatiriktam pramāṇena paramāṇum pratipadyate
520.15	sarva-apakarṣa-prāptāḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  eva paramāṇuḥ na ca rūpa-ādibhiḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ
520.16	rūpa-ādimantaḥ na ca anartha-antara-bhāve matub-vyutpādam paśyāmaḥ nanu
520.17	dṛṣṭaḥ anartha-antara-bhāve api matub-pratyayaḥ yathā hastimatī senā iti
520.18	na dṛṣṭaḥ iti brūmaḥ/ yathā ca senā-anartha-antaram tathā uktam iti/ asati
520.19	ca artha-antara-bhūte paramāṇu^i asatyām ca mūrti^i sāvayavāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ
520.20	mūrtimat-tvāt iti vākyasya arthaḥ rūpa-ādyaḥ  rūpa-ādimantaḥ rūpa-ādimat-
520.21	tvat iti/ etena paṭaḥ amūrtimat-tvāt sāvayavaḥ iti pratyuktam/ na
520.22	ca rūpa-ādi-vyatiriktam paṭam pratipadyase na mūrtimat-tvam/ atha ca mūrtimat
521.01	tvāt sāvayavaḥ paṭaḥ iti ca bravīṣi vyatireka-abhyupagame vyāghātaḥ
521.02	anabhyupagame nārthaḥ siddhyati iti dṛṣṭānta-ubhaya-dharma-siddha-tvāt na paṭe
521.03	sāvayavatvam mūrtimattva-arthaḥ iti/ etena sāvayavāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ
521.04	saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ iti vyākhyātam/ saṃyogasya abhyupagame vyāghātaḥ anabhyupagame
521.05	paramāṇu-tvāt iti hetu-arthaḥ saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat-tvam tu asiddham yaḥ
521.06	eva arthaḥ sāvayava-tvāt iti saḥ eva arthaḥ saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat-tvāt iti/ atha
521.07	saṃsthānam asarvagata-dravya-parimāṇam pratipadyethāḥ evam sati mūrtimat-tvena
521.08	saṃsthānavat-tvam carita-artham iti pṛthak vaktavyam mūrtimat-tvāt saṃsthāna-viśeṣavat-
521.09	tvat iti/ uktam ca atra vibhāge alpatara-prasaṅgasya yataḥ na alpīyaḥ tatra
521.10	avasthānāt aṇu-avayavasya ca aṇutara-tva-prasaṅgāt aṇu-kārya-pratiṣedhaḥ iti/
521.11	yad punar etad saṃyoga-upapatti^aḥ madhye san aṇuḥ pūrva-aparābhyām sambadhyamānaḥ
521.12	sāvayavaḥ ayam eva arthaḥ kārikayā gīyate/
521.13	ṣaṭkena yugapad-yoge paramāṇu^aḥ ṣaḍ-aṃśatā/
521.14	ṣaṇṇām samāna-deśa-tve piṇḍaḥ syāt aṇu-mātrakaḥ// iti
521.15	deśa-tvāt sambandhānām/ atha samāna-deśāḥ sarve saṃyogāḥ ṣaṇṇām
521.16	paramāṇūnām piṇḍaḥ paramāṇu-mātrakaḥ prāpnoti/ yadi dve dve dravyei
521.17	adhikṛtya abhidhīyate tataḥ api deśāḥ paramāṇūnāṃ sambandhinam
522.01	paramāṇum adhikṛtya abhidhīyate tataḥ anekaḥ saṃyogaḥ samāna-deśaḥ iti na
522.02	kim cit bādhyate/ yad punar etad samāna-deśāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ iti
522.03	tad na anabhyupagamāt deśāḥ eva tāvat paramāṇu^i na santi iti kutaḥ
522.04	samāna-deśāḥ bhaviṣyanti/ na ca kim cit dravyam samāna-deśam asti iti asiddhaḥ
522.05	dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ nanu ca kārya-kāraṇei saṃyoga-samāna-deśei yathā ghaṭaḥ paṭena
522.06	sambaddhyate tantunā tad-aṃśunā ca tad-svāśrayena iti ādi/ etad anabhyupagamena
522.07	pratyuktam/ samāna-deśāḥ tatra saṃyogāḥ iti na kārya-kāraṇei na ca
522.08	saṃyogāḥ api dve dve adhikṛtya iti ṣaṇṇām samāna-deśa-tvāt iti vācyam
522.09	na dravyam samāna-deśa-tāyām vyavatiṣṭhate na saṃyoga-samāna-deśa-tāyām iti/
522.10	yad punar etad diś-deśa-bhedaḥ yasya asti tasya ekatvam na yujyate kaḥ evam āha
522.11	diś-deśa-bhedaḥ asti iti diś-deśa-bhedāḥ ca diśaḥ saṃyogāḥ parikalpitān ca
522.12	diś-deśa-bhedān kalpayitvā paramāṇu^aḥ diś-deśa-bhedaḥ abhyupagamyate mukhyaḥ tu
522.13	na diś-deśa-bhedaḥ na api paramāṇu^aḥ bhedaḥ paramāṇuḥ diśā sambaddhyate
522.14	iti/ etāvat mātram vidyate etad ca na kim cit bādhate chāyā-āvṛtī
522.15	tarhi na prāpnutaḥ paramāṇu^aḥ adeśa-tvāt iti na deśāvat-tvāt chāyā-āvṛtī
522.16	kim tarhi mūrtimat sparśavat-tvāt mūrtimat-sparśavat-dravyam dravya-antaram
522.17	āvṛṇoti kim idam āvṛṇoti sva-sambandhitvena itarasya sambandham
522.18	pratiṣedhati iti/ chāyā tu tejas-paramāṇu^aḥ āvaraṇāt mūrtimatā
522.19	paramāṇunā tejas-paramāṇuḥ āvriyate yatra ca asya āvaraṇam tatra chāyā iti
522.20	virala-tejas-sambandhīni dravya-guṇa-karmāṇi chāyā iti abhidhīyate sarvataḥ
523.01	vyāvṛtta-tejas-sambandhīni tu tāni tamas-sañjñakāni iti/ tad evam
523.02	chāyā-āvṛti^oḥ anyathā-siddha-tvāt ahetuḥ/ etena kriyāvat-tvāt sparśavat-tvāt
523.03	dravya-antara-ārambhaka-tvāt kriyā-kāraṇa-saṃskāra-pratyaya-tvāt paratva-aparatvavat-
523.04	tvāt iti evam ādi pratyuktam/ katham iti yathā mūrtimat-tvāt sāvayavāḥ
523.05	iti etasmin vākye pratijñā-doṣāḥ hetu-doṣāḥ tathā sarva^su eteṣu pakṣa^su
523.06	para-pakṣa-abhyupagata^su hetuṣu yathā-sambhavam viruddha-asiddha-anaikāntika-ādi-
523.07	doṣa-bhedāḥ vaktavyāḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ ye tu kriyāvat-tva-ādibhiḥ
523.08	paramāṇūnām anityatvam sādhayanti taiḥ kriyāvat-tvam vyañjakam vā kārakam
523.09	vā avaśyam abhyupagantavyam/ yadi kriyāvat-tvām anityatvasya kārakam yad akriyam
523.10	tad nityam prāpnoti/ atha janman kriyā iti abhidhīyate tadā anityāḥ paramāṇu^āḥ
523.11	janmavat-tvāt iti hetu-arthaḥ janmavat-tvam asiddham paramāṇūnām iti/
523.12	atha kriyāvat-tvam vyañjakam ucyate vyañjaka-tve api anyataḥ anityatvam paramāṇūnām
523.13	vaktavyam/ na hi ayam vyañjakasya dharmaḥ yad avyaṅgyam vastu kuryāt api tu
523.14	anyataḥ bhūtam hetuḥ vyanakti na hi pradīpaḥ asantam artham janayitvā prakāśayati
523.15	para-siddha-utkṣepaṇa-ādi-kriyā-abhyupagame viruddhaḥ/ etena ghaṭa-ādi-
523.16	dṛṣṭāntaḥ vyākhyātaḥ/ kriyāvat-tvam ca kriyā-samavāyaḥ tad-abhyupagame
523.17	viruddhaḥ anabhyupagame anityaḥ paramāṇuḥ paramāṇu-tvāt iti hetu-arthaḥ/
523.18	matub^aḥ ca artha-antare dṛṣṭa-tvāt viruddhaḥ anartha-antare dṛṣṭānta-abhāvaḥ/
523.19	evam śeṣāṇi vākyāni vikalpya yathā-sambhavam doṣāḥ vaktavyāḥ iti/
523.20	atha para-pakṣa-siddhān etān abhyupaiti katham para-pakṣa-siddhāḥ atha na
524.01	pramāṇatah katham svayam anupalabdhaḥ dharmaḥ para-pratipādanāya upādīyate
524.02	iti//
524.03	yad idam bhāvān buddhiḥ āśritya viṣayāḥ santi iti manyase mithyā-
524.04	buddhyaḥ  etāḥ yadi hi tattva-buddhiḥ syuḥ buddhi^ā vivecane kriyamāṇe
524.05	buddhi-viṣayāṇāṃ yāthārthyam upalabhyeta//
524.06	[4-2-26]buddhi^ā vivecanāt tu bhāvānām yāthātmya-anupalabdhiḥ tantu-
524.07	apakarṣaṇe paṭa-sadbhāva-anupalabdhi-vat tad-anupalabdhiḥ
524.08	buddhi^ā vivecanāt tu bhāvānām yāthātmya-anupalabdhiḥ tantu-apakarṣaṇe
524.09	paṭa-sadbhāva-anupalabdhi-vat tad-anupalabdhiḥ/ ye ete buddhi-viṣayāḥ go^ādyaḥ
524.10	ghaṭa-ādyaḥ  na ete tattvataḥ santi kasmāt buddhi^ā vivicyamānānām bhedaśaḥ
524.11	grahaṇāt/ yathā ayam tantuḥ ayam tantuḥ iti buddhi^ā tantuṣu vivicyamāna^su
524.12	na paṭaḥ kaḥ cit asti yaḥ paṭa-buddhi^aḥ viṣayaḥ syāt evam aṃśuṣu buddhi^ā
524.13	vivicyamāna^su evam tad-avayava^su tāvat yāvat paramāṇuḥ paramāṇu^āḥ api
524.14	bhāgaśaḥ vivicyamānāḥ tāvat yāvat pralayaḥ iti/ tad evam sarvasya asattve
524.15	go-ādi-buddhyaḥ  ghaṭa-ādi-buddhyaḥ  mithyā-buddhyaḥ  iti//
524.16	[4-2-27]vyāhata-tvāt ahetuḥ
524.17	vyāhata-tvāt ahetuḥ/ buddhi^ā vivecanāt tu bhāvānām sarva-bhāva-anupapattiḥ
524.18	iti ayuktam kasmāt vyāghātāt kaḥ vyāghātaḥ saha-asambhavaḥ
525.01	yadi buddhi^ā vivecanam bhāvānām na sarva-bhāva-anupapattiḥ/ atha sarva-
525.02	bhāva-anupapattiḥ na buddhi^ā vivecanam sarva-bhāvānām iti sarva-bhāvānām anupapattiḥ
525.03	iti bruvāṇaḥ pramāṇam paryanuyojyaḥ yadi pramāṇam bravīti
525.04	vyāhatam bhavati atha na bravīti arthaḥ asya na siddhyati pramāṇa-abhāvāt/
525.05	atha aprāmāṇikī siddhiḥ sarva-bhāva-utpattiḥ kasmāt na siddhyati yaḥ ca
525.06	sarva-bhāvānām abhāvaḥ bhāva^su itaretara-apekṣa-siddhi^aḥ iti etasmin vāde doṣaḥ
525.07	uktaḥ saḥ iha api draṣṭavyaḥ iti/  yad punar etad yadi vyatiriktam
525.08	paṭa-ādi-dravyam syāt tantuṣu buddhi^ā vivicyamāna^su pṛthak upalabhyeta iti//
525.09	[4-2-28]tad-āśraya-tvāt apṛthak-grahaṇam
525.10	tad-āśraya-tvāt apṛthak-grahaṇam/ kāraṇa-dravya-āśritam kāryam tasmin na
525.11	pṛthak upalabhyate iti viparyaye yadi pṛthak-grahaṇāt yatra kārya-kāraṇa-bhāvaḥ
525.12	āśraya-āśrita-bhāvaḥ ca na asti tatra pṛthak-grahaṇam iti//
525.13	[4-2-29]pramāṇataḥ ca artha-pratipatti^aḥ
525.14	pramāṇataḥ ca artha-pratipatti^aḥ/ yad asti tathā ca yad na asti yathā ca
525.15	tad sarvam pramāṇataḥ upalabdhi^ā siddhyati iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
525.16	[4-2-30]pramāṇa-anupapatti-upapattibhyām
525.17	[4-2-31]svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-vat ayam pramāṇa-prameya-abhimānaḥ
525.18	svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-vat ayam pramāṇa-prameya-abhimānaḥ/ yathā svapne
525.19	viṣayāḥ na santi atha ca viṣaya-abhimānaḥ evam na pramāṇāni na
525.20	prameyāṇi santi atha ca pramāṇa-prameya-abhimānaḥ/
526.01	[4-2-32]māyā-gandharva-nagara-mṛga-tṛṣṇikā-vat vā
526.02	[4-2-33]hetu-abhāvāt asiddhiḥ
526.03	[4-2-34]smṛti-saṅkalpa-vat ca svapna-viṣaya-abhimānaḥ
526.04	māyā-gandharva-nagara-mṛga-tṛṣṇikā-vat vā/ na pramāṇa-abhāvāt svapna-anta-
526.05	vat avidyamāna^su viṣaya^su abhimānaḥ iti atra na hetuḥ ucyate iti
526.06	svapna-ante ca asantaḥ viṣayāḥ iti kaḥ hetuḥiti/ khyātiḥ iti cet
526.07	ayam jāgrat-avasthā-upalabdhānām viṣayāṇām citta-vyatirekiṇām asattve
526.08	hetuḥ khyātiḥ svapna-vat iti na dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyasama-tvāt/ ye ete
526.09	svapna-avasthāyām viṣayāḥ khyānti na te citta-vyatiriktāḥ iti atra
526.10	kaḥ hetuḥ/ pratibuddhena anupalambhāt na santi iti atha manyase yasmāt
526.11	pratibuddhena na upalabhyante tasmāt na santi iti na viśeṣaṇa-upādānāt/ ye
526.12	pratibuddhena upalabhyante te santi iti prāptam vyartham vā viśeṣaṇam pratibuddhena
526.13	anupalambhāt iti/ yadi ca upalabhyamānam jāgrat-avasthāyām viṣayam
526.14	asantam manyase atha cittam asti iti atra kaḥ hetuh iti viparyaye ca
526.15	sāmarthya-abhāvāt ahetuḥ jāgrataḥ anupalabdhiḥ iti/ yadi upalabdhiḥ sattva-sādhanam
526.16	tataḥ anupalabdhiḥ asattvam sādhayati viparyaye hi sāmarthyam dṛṣṭam iti/
526.17	na citta-vyatirekiṇaḥ viṣayāḥ grāhya-tvāt vedanā-ādi-vat iti yathā vedanā-
526.18	ādi-grāhyam na citta-vyatiriktam tathā viṣayāḥ api vedanā sukha-duḥkhei
526.19	cittam jñānam iti sukha-duḥkhābhyām ca anyam jñānam iti asiddhaḥ dṛṣṭāntaḥ
527.01	sukha-duḥkhei grāhyei grahaṇam jñānam iti grāhya-grahaṇa-bhāvāt anyatvam iti/
527.02	atha abhinnam vijñānam vedanāyāḥ tathā api grāhyam ca gṛhītiḥ ca ekam iti
527.03	na dṛṣṭāntaḥ asti/ na hi karman ca kriyā ca ekam bhavati iti/ atha
527.04	ekatvam pramāṇa-vṛttam anapakṣya pratipadyethāḥ tathā api catvāraḥ skandhāḥ iti
527.05	śāstra-vyāghātaḥ/ atha catuṣṭvam na pratipadyate dṛṣṭam vijñāna-mātram eva abhyupagamyate
527.06	saḥ api dṛṣṭam vijñāna-bhedam anuyoktavyaḥ bāhyasya ādhyātmikasya ca
527.07	vijñāna-bheda-hetu^aḥ abhāvāt/ katham vijñāna-bhedaḥ iti svapna-vat vijñāna-
527.08	bhedam yadi pratipadyate saḥ api dṛṣṭa-anubhūtānām bhāvānām bhāvanā-vaśena
527.09	vijñāna-bhedam pratipādayitavyaḥ/ atha svapna-pakṣe bhāvānām bhāvanā-vaśāt
527.10	vijñāna-bhedam pratipadyeta saḥ api bhāvya-bhāvakayoḥ bhedena pratyavastheyaḥ
527.11	na abhinnam bhāvyam bhāvakam ca iti ye ca ete svapna-ādi-pratyayāḥ pura-vimāna-
527.12	udyāna-yāna-ādi-bheda-anuvidhāyinaḥ te mithyā-pratyayāḥiti mithyā-pratyayānām
527.13	ca jāgrat-avasthā-pratyaya-sāmānyāt bhāvaḥ mama api sarve mithyā-
527.14	pratyayāḥ bhaviṣyanti iti bruvāṇaḥ pradhānam anuyoktavyaḥ na ca niḥpradhānam
527.15	viparyaya-pratyayam paśyāmaḥ iti citta-vyatirekiṇam viṣayam apratipadyamānaḥ
527.16	sādhana-dūṣaṇa-svabhāvam paryanuyoktavyaḥ/ yadi bāhya-svabhāvakam vyāghātakam
527.17	atha citta-svabhāvakam na cittena paraḥ pratipadyate iti arthaḥ na siddhyati/
527.18	na hi itara-svapnam anākhyātam itaraḥ vijānāti iti/ atha śabda-ākāram cittam
528.01	pratipadyate tena api śabda-ākāram cittam iti ākāra-arthaḥ vaktavyaḥ/
528.02	ākārah hi nāma pradhāna-vastu-sāmānyāt atasmin tad iti pratyayaḥ/ na
528.03	ca bhavat-pakṣe śabdaḥ vidyate iti śabda-ākāram cittam iti nirabhidheyam
528.04	vākyam citta-vyatiriktam viṣayam apratipadyamānaḥ jāgrat-svapna-avasthāyām
528.05	bhedam paryanuyojyaḥ jāgrat-avasthāyām viṣayāḥ na santi svapna-avasthāyām api iti
528.06	iyam svapna-avasthā iyam jāgrat-avasthā iti kutaḥ etad dharma-adharma-vyavasthā na
528.07	prāpnoti yathā svapna-avasthāyām agamya-ādi-gamanāt adharma-utpattiḥ na bhavati evam
528.08	jāgrat-avasthāyām api na syāt/ atha siddha-upaghāta-anupaghātau bhedam
528.09	janayate iti pratipadyeta tad api tādṛś eva siddha-upaghātaḥ ca iti saḥ vaikṛtya-
528.10	hetuḥ iti katham avagamyate/ atha vijñānasya spaṣṭām aspaṣṭām
528.11	ca bhedam pratipadyeta viṣayam antareṇa jñānasya tucchatā aspaṣṭatā vaktavyā/
528.12	asati arthe vijñāna-bhedaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ iti cet atha manyase yathā tulya-
528.13	karma-vipāka-utpannāḥ pretāḥ pūya-pūrṇām nadīm paśyanti na tatra nadī asti
528.14	na pūyam na hi ekam vastu-aneka-ākāram bhavitum arhati dṛṣṭaḥ ca vijñāna-bhedaḥ
528.15	ke cit tām eva jala-pūrṇām paśyanti ke cit rudhira-pūrṇām iti ataḥ avasīyate
528.16	yathā adhyāse nimitta-apekṣam asati bāhye nimitte vijñānam eva tathā
528.17	utpadyate iti/ na vyāghātāt asati bāhye vijñānam eva tathā iti
528.18	bruvāṇaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ jāyate katham tathā iti/ yadi rudhira-ākāram vijñānam
528.19	rudhiram tarhi vaktavyam kim rudhiram iti evam jala-ākāram nadī-ākāram
528.20	ca vaktavyam pūya-pūrṇām paśyanti iti ca vākyasya padāni pratyekam vicāryamāṇāni
529.01	rūpa-ādi-skandhāḥ bhāvaiḥ nirviṣayāṇi bhavanti deśa-ādi-niyamaḥ ca
529.02	na prāpnoti ekasmin deśe nadīm pūya-pūrṇām paśyanti na deśa-antara^su
529.03	asati arthe niyama-hetuḥ vaktavyaḥ yasya punar vidyamānam kena cit ākārena
529.04	vyavasthitam tasya śeṣaḥ mithya-pratyayaḥ iti yuktam/ mithyā-pratyayāḥ ca
529.05	bhavantaḥ na pradhānam bādhante iti pūya-ādi-pratyayānām pradhānam vaktavyam
529.06	iti yathā pūya-ādi-pratyayānām evam māyā-gandharva-nagara-mṛga-tṛṣṇā-salilānām
529.07	iti karmaṇah vāsanā anyatra phalam anyatra kalpyate iti/ asya arthaḥ
529.08	yatra eva kila karman tatra eva kila phalena bhavitavyam yasya citta-vyatirekiṇaḥ
529.09	viṣayāḥ tasya anyatra karman anyatra karma-phalam iti vyadhikaraṇei karma-phalei
529.10	bhavataḥ iti/ tad na anabhyupagamāt na mayā karma-phalei vyadhikaraṇei abhyupagamyete
529.11	ātmani karman tatra eva phalam iti adoṣaḥ/ madīyāt cittāt artha-
529.12	antaram viṣayāḥ sāmānyām viśeṣavat-tvāt santāna-antara-citta-vat pramāṇa-
529.13	gamya-tvāt kārya-tvāt anitya-tvāt dharma-pūrvaka-tvāt ca iti/
529.14	evam ca sati//
529.15	[4-2-35]mithyā-upalabdhi-vināśaḥ tattva-jñānāt svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-
529.16	praṇāśa-vat pratibodhe
529.17	mithyā-upalabdhi-vināśaḥ tattva-jñānāt svapna-viṣaya-abhimāna-praṇāśa-vat
529.18	pratibodhe/ sthāṇu^i puruṣa-adhyavasāyah mithyā-upalabdhiḥ tattva-jñānena ca
530.01	mithyā-adhyavasāyaḥ saḥ nivartate na arthaḥ sthāṇu-puruṣa-lakṣaṇaḥ na hi asau sthāṇuḥ na
530.02	bhavati iti yathā svapna-upalabdhānām ye adhyavasāyāh te jāgrat-avasthā-upalabdhi^ā
530.03	nivartyante na arthaḥ viṣaya-sāmānya-lakṣaṇaḥ iti/ śeṣam bhāṣye//
530.04	[4-2-36]buddhi^ah ca evam nimitta-sadbhāva-upalambhāt
530.05	buddhi^ah ca evam nimitta-sadbhāva-upalambhāt/ mithyā-buddhi^aḥ nimittam
530.06	asti kim punar tad-sāmānya-darśanam avidyamāna-viśeṣa-adhyāropaḥ
530.07	iti/ mithyā-buddhim pratipadyamānena tasya animittam vaktavyam nimittam
530.08	ca pratipadyamānena arthaḥ abhyupeyaḥ iti//
530.09	[4-2-37]tattva-pradhāna-bhedāt ca mithyā-buddhi^aḥ dvaividhya-upapattiḥ
530.10	tattva-pradhāna-bhedāt ca mithyā-buddhi^aḥ dvaividhya-upapattiḥ tattvam sthāṇuḥ iti
530.11	pradhānam puruṣaḥ iti tattva-pradhānayoḥ alope mithyā-buddhiḥ bhavati iti/ śeṣam
530.12	bhāṣye/  doṣa-nimittānām tattva-jñānāt ahaṅkāra-nivṛttiḥ iti uktam/
530.13	atha tattva-jñānam katham utpadyate iti//
530.14	[4-2-38]samādhi-viśeṣa-abhyāsāt
530.15	samādhi-viśeṣa-abhyāsāt/ śeṣam bhāṣye/ vyāpaka-tvāt antaḥkaraṇasya
530.16	pratyāhāraḥ na yuktaḥ iti cet atha manyase vyāpakam antaḥkaraṇam
530.17	tad katham pratyāhriyate iti na ukta-uttara-tvāt/ yathā na vyāpakam antaḥkaraṇam
530.18	tathā uktam adhastāt/ sva-aṅga-gati-īkṣaṇa-upapattiḥ iti cet atha
531.01	manyase yasya api antaḥkaraṇam aṇu tasya api sva-aṅga-gati-vīkṣaṇam na prāpnoti
531.02	yugapad haste cakṣuṣi vā sannidhānāt śarīra-ātma-manas-saṃyoga-yauga-padyāt
531.03	prayatna-darśanayoḥ yugapad-utpatti^aḥ sva-ātma-śarīra-sambandhaḥ ca ātmanaḥ sambandhaḥ ca
531.04	yugapad bhavati tatra ātma-manas-śarīra-sambandhāt sva-aṅga-gatiḥ ātma-manas-
531.05	sambandhāt cakṣuṣaḥ preraṇam tathā sva-aṅga-gatiḥ prayatnāt bhavati iti darśanam
531.06	cakṣus-raśmi^aḥ arthasya ca sannidhānāt iti/ na ca ātmanaḥ pradeśāḥ santi iti
531.07	acodyam pradeśavati ca ātmani anyena ātma-pradeśena manas sambadhyate anyena
531.08	śarīram iti sva-aṅge gati-īkṣaṇa-ādi-doṣaḥ yasya punar niṣpradeśaḥ eva asau
531.09	tasya eṣaḥ doṣaḥ na bhavati iti śarīra-sambandhinā ātmanā manas-sambandhaḥ iti//
531.10	[4-2-39]na artha-viśeṣa-prābalyāt
531.11	na artha-viśeṣa-prābalyāt/ na upapadyate samādhi-viśeṣa-abhyāsaḥ abubhutsamānasya
531.12	api buddhi-utpatti^aḥ yathā stanayitnu^aḥ śabda-prabhṛtiṣu iti//
531.13	[4-2-40]kṣud-ādibhiḥ pravartanāt ca
531.14	kṣud-ādibhiḥ pravartanāt ca/ kṣud-pipāsābhyām ca anivṛttaḥ api
531.15	buddhyaḥ  eva utpadyante iti na yuktaḥ samādhi-viśeṣaḥ//
531.16	[4-2-41]pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt tad-utpattiḥ
531.17	pūrva-kṛta-phala-anubandhāt tad-utpattiḥ/ pūrva-kṛtaḥ pūrva-śarīra-abhyastaḥ
532.01	samādhi-viśeṣaḥ tasya phalam dharmaḥ tad-kṛtaḥ punar samādhi-viśeṣaḥ iti
532.02	samādhi-viśeṣāt ca bāhya-viṣaya-abhibhavaḥ laukikasya api tāvat samādhi-
532.03	viśeṣa-avasthitasya bāhya-viṣaya-upanipāte buddhyaḥ  na utpadyante kim punar
532.04	yoginaḥ iti/  yadi bāhya-viṣaya-upanipāte artha-jñānāni prādurbhavanti
532.05	apavarge api evam prasaṅgaḥ yuktaḥ ca apavarge bāhya-viṣaya-viśeṣa-upanipātaḥ
532.06	vidyate iti buddhi-utpāda-prasaṅgaḥ//
532.07	[4-2-42]araṇya-guhā-pulina-ādiṣu yoga-abhyāsa-upadeśaḥ
532.08	[4-2-43]apavarge api evam-prasaṅgaḥ
532.09	[4-2-44]na niṣpanna-avaśyam-bhāvi-tvāt
532.10	na niṣpanna-avaśyam-bhāvi-tvāt/ sati śarīre bāhya-viṣayaḥ jñāna-
532.11	utpatti-kāraṇam na anyathā iti sūtra-arthaḥ//
532.12	[4-2-45]tad-abhāvaḥ ca apavarge
532.13	tad-abhāvaḥ ca apavarge/ tasya śarīra-ādi^ah upalabdhi-kāraṇasya abhāvaḥ apavarge
532.14	iti/ tasmāt sarva-duḥkha-mokṣaḥ apavargaḥ iti/ yasmāt sarvam duḥkha-bījam
532.15	duḥkham ca apavarge vicchidyate tasmāt sarva-duḥkhena vimuktir apavargaḥ//
532.16	[4-2-46]tad-artham yama-niyamābhyām ātma-saṃskāraḥ yogāt ca adhyātma-
532.17	vidhi-upāyaiḥ
533.01	tad-artham yama-niyamābhyām ātma-saṃskāraḥ yogāt ca adhyātma-vidhi-upāyaiḥ/
533.02	tasya apavargasya adhigamāya yama-niyama-abhyāsa-saṃskāraḥ//
533.03	[4-2-47]jñāna-grahaṇa-abhyāsaḥ tad-vidyaiḥ ca saha saṃvādaḥ
533.04	prajñāna-grahaṇāt ca abhyāsaḥ tad-vidyaiḥ ca saha saṃvādaḥ jñāna-grahaṇam śāstra-grahaṇam
533.05	tad-vidyā-yatnam upāyam buddhyante iti tad-vidyā iti avibhakta-artham vacanam//
533.06	[4-2-48]tam śiṣya-guru-sabrahmacāri-viśiṣṭa-śreyaḥ arthabhiḥ anasūyubhiḥ
533.07	abhyupeyāt
533.08	tam śiṣya-guru-sabrahmacāri-viśiṣṭaḥ iti evam ādi sūtram etad tu
533.09	nigadena eva nīta-artham//
533.10	[4-2-49]pratipakṣa-hīnam api vā prayojana-artham arthi-tve
533.11	pratipakṣa-hīnam api vā prayojana-artham arthi-tve tam abhyupeyāt iti vartate//
533.12	[4-2-50]tattva-adhyavasāya-saṃrakṣaṇa-artham jalpa-vitaṇḍā^ī bīja-praroha-saṃrakṣaṇa-
533.13	artham kaṇṭaka-śākha-āvaraṇa-vat
533.14	tattva-adhyavasāya-saṃrakṣaṇa-artham jalpa-vitaṇḍā^ī bīja-praroha-saṃrakṣaṇa-artham
533.15	kaṇṭaka-śākha-āvaraṇa-vat iti/ anutpanna-tattva-jñānena etad kartavyam iti
533.16	sūtra-arthaḥ/  vidyā-nirveda-ādibhiḥ ca parena avajñāyamānasya/
534.01	[4-2-51]tābhyām vigṛhya kathanam
534.02	tābhyām jalpa-vitaṇḍābhyām vigṛhya iti vijigīṣayā na tattva-bubhutsayā iti
534.03	vidyā-pālana-artham ca etad na lābha-pūja-ākhyāti-artham iti//
534.04	pravṛtti-doṣa-sambandhaḥ pretyabhāvaḥ prapañcitaḥ/
534.05	phalam duḥkham vimuktiḥ ca caturthe parikīrtitāḥ//
534.06	iti auddyotakare nyāya-sūtra-vārttike caturtha-adhyāyasya
534.07	dvitīyam āhnikam//
534.08	caturthaḥ adhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ//
